### [1] [Beyond the Last Layer: Global Inter-Layer Aggregation for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00017)
*Wenbin Shen,Zhichao Lian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GILA方法，通过层间自注意力聚合冻结预训练编码器多层全局表示，提升AI生成图像检测与跨数据集泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型快速发展导致检测器面临生成器迭代快、数据分布变化大、跨数据集泛化差等挑战；现有基于预训练视觉编码器的方法主要使用最后一层全局表示，忽视中间层有用信息。

Method: GILA从冻结编码器不同深度提取全局表示，通过层间自注意力建模各层表示间交互，并自适应聚合上下文化表示；方法不依赖特定预训练目标，且无需微调预训练编码器。

Result: 在3种不同预训练目标的视觉骨干、10个已知生成器和3个外部数据集上评估，GILA更充分利用多层信息，提升检测性能与跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 多层全局表示的层间交互与自适应聚合是提升AI生成图像检测及跨域泛化的有效途径，且具备良好通用性与免微调优势。

Abstract: The rapid development of generative models has made AI-generated images increasingly close to real images. It has also brought several challenges to detectors, including rapid generator updates, large changes in data distribution, and limited cross-dataset generalization. Many existing methods based on pretrained vision encoders primarily use the global representation from the last layer, potentially leaving useful information from intermediate layers underexplored. To address this issue, we propose Global Inter-Layer Aggregation (GILA). GILA extracts global representations from different depths of a frozen encoder, models interactions among layer representations through inter-layer self-attention, and adaptively aggregates the resulting contextualized representations. The method is not tied to a single pretraining objective and does not require fine-tuning the pretrained encoder. We evaluated GILA on three vision backbones with different pretraining objectives, ten seen generators, and three external datasets. The experimental results show that GILA makes better use of multi-layer information in pretrained encoders and improves detection performance and cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [2] [Intervenable Concept Dynamics for Video Activity Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00020)
*Patrick Knab,David Steinmann,Giovanni De Felice,Felix Divo,Udo Schlegel,Wolfgang Stammer,Sascha Marton,Christian Bartelt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TRACE-CBM，将概念瓶颈模型扩展到活动预测：用稀疏时空图精炼概念、递归预测未来概念状态，并支持推理时编辑概念/活动/边以检验可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBM）仅从观测输入中锚定概念，无法刻画对象、动作及其交互随时间变化导致的概念激活演化，因此不支持对未来概念状态的预测，难以用于持续活动的视频预测任务。

Method: TRACE-CBM（Temporal Relational Activity Concept Evolution）：1）以稀疏时空图对观测到的概念激活进行精炼；2）递归预测未来概念状态，每次状态转移以先验活动分布为条件；3）用共享的活动分类器解码观测状态与预测状态；4）推理阶段支持无需参数更新地编辑概念值、活动激活与学到的图边。

Result: 在多个数据集上，观测活动 Top-1 准确率较基于概念的方法提升 1.2–8.5 个百分点；H3 预测 Top-1 准确率与匹配的黑盒预测器差距仅 1.9–2.2 个百分点。干预实验显示，概念节点编辑可纠正 25.6–34.4% 的初始错误 H3 预测且不翻转原本正确的预测；概念引导在最多 90.0% 的情况下达到指定目标活动。

Conclusion: 将概念瓶颈框架扩展至时间预测是可行的：概念状态的可演化建模在保持接近黑盒预测器精度的同时，提供了可检查、可编辑的时空关系依赖，并具备有效的因果式干预与引导能力。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models route predictions through human-understandable concepts, making their intermediate states inspectable and editable. Extending this model family to forecasting requires modeling how concept activations evolve over time as objects, actions, and their interactions change, yet existing CBMs ground concepts only in observed inputs and do not forecast their future evolution. We study this problem using videos of ongoing activities and forecast future activities through a named-concept state. We propose TRACE-CBM (Temporal Relational Activity Concept Evolution), which first refines observed concept activations with sparse spatio-temporal graphs. It then recursively predicts future concept states, conditioning each transition on the prior activity distribution, and decodes both observed and predicted states with a shared activity classifier. At inference time, the model supports editing concept values, activity activations, and learned edges without parameter updates, exposing the relational spatio-temporal dependencies used for forecasting. Across multiple datasets, TRACE improves observed-activity Top-1 accuracy over concept-based methods by 1.2-8.5 percentage points and attains H3 forecast Top-1 accuracy within 1.9-2.2 points of matched black-box forecasters. Intervention experiments show concept-node edits correct 25.6-34.4% of initially incorrect H3 forecasts without flipping initially correct ones, and concept steering achieves the requested target activity in up to 90.0% of cases.

</details>


### [3] [Diagnosing Prototype Estimation in Few-Shot Class-Incremental Learning: Training-Free Geometry Selection and Unlabeled-Query Prototype Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00022)
*Md Tanvir Hossain Saikat,Md Akib Haider,Md. Tahmid Islam Tomal,Md Hasanul Kabir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文对冻结特征少样本类增量学习(FSCIL)进行回顾性匹配反事实分解，指出新类原型估计的有限样本噪声是残差差距主因，据此提出仅用支撑集的 ShrinkSel 选择器与批量直推式 QPR 原型校正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结视觉编码器下原型头在FSCIL中已具竞争力，但残差差距究竟源于噪声新类均值，还是源于分类器重拟合带来的后续变化，此前缺乏因果式拆分证据；作者要定位这一瓶颈。

Method: 在同一特征上构建回顾性匹配反事实：固定余弦打分，先将每个五样本新类均值替换为全数据均值，再拟合线性探针，以有序列分解量化均值替换的贡献；辅以样本数与逐类诊断检验有限样本机制，并用仅基类编码器作对照。方法上提出 ShrinkSel——仅在支撑集上对原型半径与协方差收缩进行选择，其族以共享LDA为精确回退端点；并给出查询原型校正(QPR)，用门控无标签查询更新新类方向同时保持已选几何。

Result: 16个强编码器—数据集对上，均值替换解释该有序列差距的主体；ShrinkSel 在其中15对上优于LDA 0.04–1.52个点，并在Aircraft上选中精确LDA端点。CLIP ViT-B/16上QPR带来1.21–2.03点提升，且逐查询自排除下收益仍保持，说明增益不依赖自包含；代价是可测量的基类精度损失，且支撑充足或类群极小时增益减弱。

Conclusion: 冻结特征FSCIL的核心约束是可用于原型估计的证据量（样本支持度），而非仅分类器拟合方式；在该证据受限设定下，支撑集驱动的几何选择与利用无标签查询的原型校正可有效弥合残差差距。

Abstract: Frozen visual encoders make prototype heads competitive in few-shot class-incremental learning (FSCIL), but it remains unclear how much of the residual head gap is sensitive to noisy novel-class means rather than to the subsequent changes introduced by classifier refitting. On identical features, we construct a retrospective matched counterfactual: first replace each five-shot novel mean with its complete-data counterpart under fixed cosine scoring, then fit a linear probe. Across sixteen strong encoder dataset pairs, mean replacement accounts for the majority of this ordered gap. Shot and classwise diagnostics support a finite-sample mechanism, whereas a weaker base-only encoder does not satisfy the same criterion. This diagnosis motivates ShrinkSel, a support-only selector over prototype radius and covariance shrinkage whose family contains shared LDA as an exact fallback. Across sixteen evaluated pairs, it improves LDA on fifteen by 0.04-1.52 points and selects the exact LDA endpoint on Aircraft. We further introduce Query Prototype Rectification (QPR), a batch-transductive extension that uses gated unlabeled queries to update novel directions while preserving the selected geometry. On CLIP ViT-B/16, QPR adds 1.21-2.03 points; exact query-wise self-exclusion retains the benefit, showing that the gains do not require self-inclusion. The update repairs scarce novel prototypes at a measurable base-class cost and weakens with plentiful supports or very small cohorts. Thus, the evidence available for prototype estimation is a central constraint in frozen-feature FSCIL.

</details>


### [4] [FORCE: Factorized Object Relations for Counterfactual Editing in Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00023)
*Suraj K. Sood,Yugyung Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FORCE 是一个从原始视频无监督学习成对物体交互表示的世界模型，将每对物体的关系编码为显式潜变量并用于反事实编辑，实验表明其瓶颈在于感知定位精度而非关系动力学本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型能预测场景演化，但难以被"审问"：给定一个预测视频的模型，无法方便地询问"若两个物体间的某种交互不同会怎样"。作者希望获得可干预、可编辑的交互表示，且编辑不依赖模拟器参数映射。

Method: 1) 通过冻结的自监督特征上的 slot attention 抽取物体；2) 关系编码器从短观测窗口推断每对物体的关系潜变量；3) 交互头将潜变量转为力；4) rollout 期间该潜变量保持固定，使编辑持续生效而非被重算；5) 支持关系的删除、替换、迁移与引入，所有编辑直接作用于模型内部量。在可获真实编辑结果的模拟环境中评估，用基于效应的分数与模拟器在同一操作下的响应进行对比。

Result: 在留出片段上，基于视频的编辑与模拟器响应的一致性分数为 0.19–0.37；若以精确物体位置训练与评分，同一架构可达 0.57–0.85。

Conclusion: 差距不源于学习到的关系动力学，而源于感知：编辑上限受限于从视频中定位物体的精度，而编辑所需的精度略超出当前以物体为中心的感知能力；提升物体定位精度是让基于视频的反事实世界模型可行的明确方向。

Abstract: World models predict how scenes evolve, but they are difficult to interrogate: given a model that forecasts a video, one cannot easily ask what would have happened had a particular interaction between two objects been different. We present Factorized Object Relations for Counterfactual Editing (FORCE), a world model that infers pairwise interactions from raw video without supervision and represents each as an explicit latent per object pair, so that a relation can be removed, changed, transferred or introduced directly. Objects are extracted by slot attention over frozen self-supervised features; a relation encoder infers each pair latent from a short observation window, and an interaction head turns it into a force. The latent is held fixed across the rollout, so an edit persists rather than being recomputed, and every edit acts on the model's own quantities with no mapping to simulator parameters. We evaluate in a simulated environment where the true outcome of an edit is available, scoring the change each edit induces against the change the simulator produces under the same operation. On held-out clips, FORCE's edits made from video agree with the simulator's response at 0.19 to 0.37 on our effect-based score; trained and scored on exact object positions instead, the same architecture reaches 0.57 to 0.85. The gap therefore lies not in the learned relational dynamics but in perception: what bounds an edit is how precisely objects can be located from video, and the precision editing needs lies just beyond what current object-centric perception delivers. Closing that gap is a concrete target for making counterfactual world models work from video.

</details>


### [5] [Covariance-aware Scoring: A Geometric Correction for Vision-Language Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00024)
*Lakpa Tamang,Saman Forouzandeh,Kamal Berahmand,Wei Shao,Hamid Beigy,Mahdi Jalili*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CARe，用目标特征二阶矩诱导的 Mahalanobis 几何替代环境空间余弦相似度进行测试时适应评分，理论上与共享协方差高斯下的 Bayes 最优线性判别方向一致，实验上在各向异性越强的数据集增益越大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CLIP 类视觉-语言模型的 TTA 方法（特征缓存、原型估计、在线统计量累积）最终都以环境嵌入空间中的余弦相似度对测试特征与类代表打分。特征分布各向异性时，少数高方差方向主导该得分，而这些方向未必具有判别性，构成共享决策规则的局限。

Method: CARe：以目标域特征二阶矩诱导的 Mahalanobis 几何替代环境几何，即用协方差感知的评分取代余弦评分；环境空间评分作为各向同性特例纳入统一框架。

Result: 理论上在共享协方差高斯模型下，协方差感知决策方向与 Bayes 最优线性判别方向一致，并证明其潜在收益随特征各向异性增强而增大。实验在两个骨干网络、标准与细粒度分布偏移基准上均优于基座余弦评分；增益在特征最各向异性的数据集上最大，在特征趋于各向同性时消失。

Conclusion: 决策几何（而非仅类代表估计）是 TTA 中被忽视的关键设计维度；协方差感知评分是低成本且理论可解释的改进，其有效性可由特征各向异性程度预测。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) enables vision--language models such as CLIP to adapt to distribution shifts without labels or access to source data. Existing methods adapt through feature caching, prototype estimation, or the online accumulation of feature statistics, but most ultimately score a test feature against a class representative in the ambient embedding space, typically through cosine similarity. We identify this shared decision rule as a limitation under anisotropic feature distributions, which means when a small number of directions account for a large fraction of feature variance, the score can be dominated by high-variance directions that are not necessarily discriminative. In light of this, we propose CARe, a covariance-aware scoring for TTA that replaces the ambient geometry with a Mahalanobis geometry induced by the target feature second moment. Theoretically, we show that under a shared-covariance Gaussian model the covariance-aware decision direction coincides with that of the Bayes-optimal linear discriminant, with ambient-space scoring as the isotropic special case, and establish that the potential benefit of covariance-aware scoring grows with feature anisotropy. Empirically, we show that CARe improves adaptation accuracy over its base cosine scoring on both standard and fine-grained distribution-shift benchmarks across two backbones. Consistent with our analysis, the gains are largest on datasets whose features are most anisotropic and vanish as the features approach isotropy.

</details>


### [6] [Edge-Optimized YOLOv11n Architecture for Offline Recognition of Nigerian Sign Language Alphanumeric Gestures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00025)
*Adeleke Adeniyan,Abimbola H. Afolayan,Olanrewaju V. Johnson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对尼日利亚低资源移动设备，构建轻量级YOLO11n实时手语识别系统，在自建11,208张图像数据集上取得Precision 0.9975、mAP 0.9942，并实现安卓端离线实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撒哈拉以南地区聋人包容面临区域性障碍，现有识别模型依赖重型算力，而当地常用手机内存仅1–4 GB，难以运行标准模型，亟需低资源硬件上的实时手语识别方案。

Method: 采用轻量YOLO11n目标检测框架；构建含11,208张原始图像的尼日利亚手语基准数据集（33个静态类别、5名签名者），按签名者划分训练S1–S3、验证S4、测试S5，空间增强仅限训练集（扩增至23,509张，验证/测试各1,681张）；引入量化感知压缩目标、结合平方Frobenius范数惩罚的损失函数、量化背景复杂度与自遮挡的空间密度遮挡指数，以及关联FLOPs、内存带宽与芯片时钟频率的延迟刻画方程。

Result: 在真实安卓硬件上使用未量化FP32权重测试，平均推理延迟405.5–1,759.4 ms；Precision 0.9975，Recall 0.9991，mAP 0.9942。

Conclusion: 结果支持受限移动设备上的离线运行；已通过基于CameraX的移动应用实现实用部署，可实时采集手势并即时以文本显示识别结果。

Abstract: Deaf inclusion across sub-Saharan society faces regional hurdles, with Nigeria as the core empirical focus. Existing recognition models demand heavy computational infrastructure. However, standard mobile phones used locally rarely exceed 1 GB to 4 GB of RAM. This study addresses constraints through a lightweight YOLO11n object-detection framework. The system provides real-time Nigerian Sign Language recognition for low-resource hardware. A benchmark dataset containing 11,208 raw gesture images was established. The data spans 33 static classes collected from five signers. Partitioning isolates signers S1 through S3 for training. Signer S4 provides validation data. Signer S5 is reserved for testing. Spatial augmentations are restricted strictly to the training set. This yields 23,509 training images. The validation and test sets each maintain 1,681 images across varied environments. The framework integrates a quantization-aware compression objective. Loss functions merge with squared Frobenius-norm penalties to govern weight distortion. A Spatial-Density Occlusion Index quantifies background complexity and self-occlusion. A latency-profiling equation relates floating-point operations and memory bandwidth to chipset clock speed. Performance testing on physical Android hardware utilizes unquantized FP32 weights. Mean inference latencies span 405.5 ms to 1,759.4 ms. Measured against test data, the algorithm attains a Precision of 0.9975. Recall reaches 0.9991. Mean Average Precision is 0.9942. Outcomes support offline functionality on constrained mobile devices. Practical deployment operates through a mobile utility using CameraX. The application captures gestures in real time. Recognized signs display as text instantly.

</details>


### [7] [The Margin Knows the Background: Uneven Abstention in Frozen Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00026)
*Satwik Sai Prakash Sahoo,Bibhor Puhan,Shubhankar Mahanta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究冻结视觉语言模型中选择性预测的置信分数如何导致群体覆盖偏差，将分数分解为平均与差值两个分量，发现分数选择的效果依赖数据集，且"分数推动群体在其排序中的位移"比"群体可辨识性"更能解释覆盖差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一置信阈值会不成比例地拒绝某些群体的样本，而现有工作不清楚是何种分数、以及分数的哪个成分携带群体信息并造成这种覆盖不平衡。

Method: 在OpenAI CLIP、LAION CLIP、SigLIP上，将margin与最大logit等一族分数用最高两个类别logit的平均值与差值表示，分析哪个分量携带群体信息；证明任一阈值下群体覆盖相等等价于分数与群体成员独立；引入随机化margin基线检验覆盖差距的下降是否超过加噪声的效果；分数选择在测试前固定；使用Waterbirds与NICO++，含配对背景替换实验。

Result: Waterbirds上，margin在80%覆盖下造成的覆盖差距是最大logit的2.5–5.5倍，群体信号位于差值分量，配对背景替换显示背景会移动该信号，但最大logit会提高整体错误；NICO++上最大logit反使最差上下文错误升高5.4–6.6个百分点，且对OpenAI CLIP相对随机基线无改进；两数据集上"分数将群体沿其排序下推的幅度"与覆盖差距的相关性（Spearman 0.88/0.92）高于单纯的群体可辨识性（0.61/0.65）。

Conclusion: 同一分数在不同数据集上的群体覆盖表现并不一致，因此需将群体覆盖与选择性风险结合、并对照随机化基线进行评估。

Abstract: Selective prediction allows a model to abstain on uncertain inputs, but a single confidence threshold can disproportionately reject examples from particular groups. We study how confidence scores produce this imbalance in frozen vision-language models (OpenAI CLIP, LAION CLIP, SigLIP). We express a family of scores, including the margin and maximum logit, through the average and the difference of the two highest class logits, and ask which carries group information. We show that equal group coverage at every threshold is equivalent to independence between the score and group membership. A randomized margin baseline tests whether a reduction in coverage disparity beats what adding noise achieves. With score choices fixed before testing, the effect of choice depends on the dataset. On Waterbirds, the margin creates coverage gaps 2.5 to 5.5 times larger than maximum logit at 80% coverage, its group signal is in the difference component, and paired background swaps show the background moves it, although maximum logit increases overall error. On NICO++, maximum logit instead increases worst-context error by 5.4 to 6.6 points and offers no improvement over the randomized baseline for OpenAI CLIP. Across both datasets, how far a score pushes a group down its ranking tracks the coverage gap (Spearman 0.88, 0.92) better than plain group identifiability (0.61, 0.65). These results explain why a score that abstains more evenly on one dataset may be less effective on another and motivate judging group coverage and selective risk together against randomized baselines.

</details>


### [8] [SFA: A Statistical Physics-Inspired Diffusion Transformer for Efficient and High-Quality Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00027)
*Zixuan Huang,Tianhao You,Guozhang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出尺度无关注意力（SFA），以均值池化+计数校正+共享softmax构建空间层级，实现O(N log N)注意力并在高分辨率扩散Transformer中以更优FID超越全注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在图像生成中表现优异，但在高分辨率下加速注意力同时保持生成质量仍是重大挑战。

Method: 受统计物理与自然界无标度律启发，SFA保留细粒度局部交互，同时在逐级更粗的尺度上汇总远距离区域；具体通过均值池化、token计数校正与跨尺度共享softmax构建无标度空间层级，使前向与反向均为O(N log N)复杂度，并给出Triton实现。

Result: 262,144 token下相较FlashAttention前向提速357.3×、反向265.6×。ImageNet 256×256上PixelFlow从零训练40 epoch，SFA在五种注意力机制（含全注意力）中生成质量最佳，FID-50k由13.25降至12.51，该优势持续至80 epoch；在FFHQ上优于其他高效注意力方法；SD 3.5 Medium替换注意力后在匹配微调预算下保持全注意力质量；预训练PixelFlow微调结果类似。

Conclusion: 无标度结构可作为高效高质量扩散Transformer的有效设计原则。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers have achieved remarkable success in image generation, but accelerating attention while preserving generation quality remains a major challenge at high resolutions. Inspired by statistical physics and the widespread occurrence of scale-free laws in nature, we introduce Scale-Free Attention (SFA), which preserves fine local interactions while summarizing distant regions at progressively coarser scales. For $N$ tokens, mean pooling, token-count correction, and a shared softmax over a scale-free spatial hierarchy give SFA $O(N\log N)$ complexity in both forward and backward passes. Our Triton implementation achieves $357.3\times$ forward and $265.6\times$ backward attention speedups over FlashAttention at 262,144 tokens. After 40 epochs of PixelFlow training from scratch on ImageNet at $256\times256$ resolution, SFA achieves the best generation quality among five attention mechanisms, including full attention. Fréchet inception distance evaluated on 50,000 generated samples (FID-50k) improves from 13.25 for full attention to 12.51, and the advantage over full attention persists through 80 epochs. SFA also achieves the best generation quality among the compared efficient-attention methods on Flickr-Faces-HQ (FFHQ). Beyond training from scratch, Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium with attention replaced by SFA retains the generation quality of its full-attention counterpart under matched fine-tuning budgets. We obtain similar results when fine-tuning pretrained PixelFlow on ImageNet. SFA establishes scale-free structure as an effective design principle for efficient, high-quality diffusion transformers.

</details>


### [9] [A Systematic Analysis of Live Intraoperative Surgical Expert Narrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00028)
*Arda Mamur,Matthias Seibold,Christoph Laux,Florian Wanivenhaus,Philipp Fürnstahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 分析13例内镜腰椎减压手术（13.2 h）的术者现场口述，构建无标注流程将德语语音转为带时间戳与手术实体的术序线索；37.9%语句为有效线索，密集覆盖器械、解剖与动作，但存在术语识别错误（18.0%）与时间错位（器械请求提前中位8.2 s）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术视频理解中人工标注是主要瓶颈；术者现场口述无需人工标注、可规模化，但其信息内涵尚未被刻画。

Method: 两名资深脊柱外科医生分析13例内镜腰椎减压术（13.2 h），采用时间同步的近场麦克风德语音频；构建无标注流水线：词级语音识别→手术术语错误的确定性纠正→语句融合→基于LLM的英译→相关性过滤，输出带时间戳、标注所引用手术实体与言语行为的术序线索，并对器械请求进行人工核验。

Result: 3,171条语句中1,201条（37.9%）保留为术序线索，57.6%为非术序内容被剔除；线索引用器械60.7%、解剖48.0%、手术动作57.1%，98.5%至少含其一；39%（472/1,201）为指令性言语，主要为器械请求与命令；467条人工核验的器械请求全部先于器械入镜，中位提前8.2 s；18.0%的保留线索存在领域术语转写错误。

Conclusion: 现场口述是描述器械、解剖、动作与团队协作的密集、无标注监督信号，但噪声大且相对所指事件存在时间位移，下游安全应用前必须对该位移建模。

Abstract: Manual annotation remains the principal bottleneck in surgical video understanding. Live intraoperative narration by the operating surgeon is a scalable alternative that requires no manual annotation, but the information it contains has not been characterized. We analyzed 13 endoscopic lumbar spine decompression procedures (13.2 h) by two senior spine surgeons, using time-synchronized close-mic German audio. An annotation-free pipeline (word-level speech recognition, deterministic correction of surgical-term errors, utterance fusion, LLM-based translation to English, and relevance filtering) converted speech into time-stamped procedural cues labelled with the surgical entities they reference and their speech act. Of 3,171 utterances, 1,201 (37.9%) were retained as procedural cues, while 57.6% were non-procedural and removed. Retained cues referenced instruments (60.7%), anatomy (48.0%) and surgical actions (57.1%), and 98.5% carried at least one of these. 39% of cues (472 of 1,201) were directives, mainly instrument requests and commands. All 467 manually verified instrument requests preceded the instrument's appearance in the field, by a median of 8.2 s. Domain-term transcription corruption affected 18.0% of retained cues. Live narration is thus a dense, annotation-free supervisory signal describing instruments, anatomy, actions and team coordination, but it is noisy and temporally displaced from the events it names, and this displacement must be modelled before safe downstream use.

</details>


### [10] [DiA: Directional Adapter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00029)
*Ashish Kumar Singh,Prakash Chandra Chhipa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiA：在CLIP上构建参数高效的方向性适配器，将时序建模解耦为因果与反因果两支，以深度可分离时序卷积在紧凑瓶颈空间中实现，门控融合，在七个基准上取得单模态SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度动作识别依赖动作的时序展开过程，视觉相似类别差异常体现在准备/执行/完成阶段；均匀聚合的时序上下文是次优的。目标为：少可调参数、计算高效、仅用视频单一模态。

Method: 基于CLIP的DiA适配器：将时序特化表述为因果（过去→现在）与反因果（未来→现在）两个方向，分别建模；两方向均在紧凑瓶颈空间中使用深度时序卷积以控制参数与算力，并经可学习融合（门控）组合。

Result: 7个公开基准上单视频模态方法中达到SOTA，仅约3.6M可调参数；相比先前方法具有更高吞吐与更低推理时延。

Conclusion: 方向性、深度可分离的时序适配是兼顾精度—算效—单模态约束的有效方案，验证了非对称时序建模对细粒度动作识别的价值。

Abstract: Fine-grained action recognition often depends on how an action unfolds over time rather than on appearance alone. Visually similar classes may differ in preparation, execution, or completion phases, making uniformly aggregated temporal context suboptimal. We study this problem under a practical objective: i) improving recognition performance with few tunable parameters, ii) being compute efficient, and iii) using only the readily available video modality. Motivated by this missing temporal observation and the accuracy--compute efficiency--unimodality objective, we propose Directional Adapter (DiA), a parameter-efficient adapter on top of CLIP. DiA formulates temporal specialization into causal and anti-causal directions, allowing past-to-present and future-to-present cues to be modeled differently. To keep this directional modeling parameter- and compute-efficient, both directions use depth-wise temporal convolutions in a compact bottleneck space and are combined through a learnable fusion. DiA achieves state-of-the-art performance among methods using a single video input modality across seven public benchmarks, with only $\sim$3.6M tunable parameters, while also delivering higher throughput and lower inference latency than prior methods. Code is available at https://github.com/prakashchhipa/DiA-Directional-Adapter.

</details>


### [11] [Monocular Badminton Speed Estimation with Air Drag and Court Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00031)
*Diwen Huang,Sarvesh Madullapalli,Ishan Pendyala,Oliver Chen,Kevin Li,William Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文利用羽毛球空气阻力提供的强减速线索并结合已知场地几何，从智能手机单目视频估计羽毛球速度：以场地线标定相机，对观测球位与帧时间戳做鲁棒非线性最小二乘拟合重力-阻力轨迹，并比较自由单目拟合与约束穿越球网平面的拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目相机无法仅凭图像区分“近处慢速物体”与“远处快速物体”，而羽毛球所受空气阻力提供强减速线索，且球场几何已知，可用来消解单目尺度与深度歧义，从而实现单相机速度估计。

Method: 对智能手机视频用场地线进行相机标定；以观测的羽毛球位置和帧时间戳，通过鲁棒非线性最小二乘拟合含重力与空气阻力的轨迹模型；设置两种拟合：自由单目拟合与附加“轨迹穿越球网平面”约束的拟合。

Result: 在两个录制会话的51次击球（吊球、平抽、半场杀球、杀球）上评估：相对基于阻力的立体参考，网平面约束拟合将速度差中位数从13.3%降至8.6%；对不使用阻力模型、由三角化位置差分得到的第二参考，网平面约束拟合的中位差亦更小。

Conclusion: 结果支持将场地几何与飞行动力学相结合，用于单相机羽毛球速度估计，且穿越球网平面的几何约束能有效提升单目测速精度。

Abstract: A single camera cannot distinguish between a slowly moving object nearby and a rapidly moving object in the distance without additional information. For a shuttlecock, air drag provides a strong deceleration cue that can be combined with known court geometry. We fit a gravity-and-drag trajectory to smartphone video and compare a free monocular fit with a fit constrained to cross the net plane. Camera calibration uses court lines, and the trajectory is fitted to observed shuttle positions and frame timestamps by robust nonlinear least squares. We evaluated 51 badminton shots from two recording sessions, spanning drop shots, drives, half-smashes and smashes. The net-plane fit reduced the median absolute speed difference from a drag-based stereo reference from 13.3% to 8.6%. A second reference, obtained by differentiating triangulated positions without a drag model, also gave a smaller median difference for the net-plane fit. The results support combining court geometry with flight dynamics for badminton speed estimation from a single camera.

</details>


### [12] [Gaussifier: Fast 2D Gaussian Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00032)
*Yunlai Zhou,Yu Yin,Michael W. Jenkins,Yehe Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gaussifier 是一种轻量级前馈框架，实现2D高斯分解，在 Kodak-24/DIV2K 上达到 40.73/38.61 dB PSNR，速度超过 60 FPS，无需逐图微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D高斯渲染速度极快（数千FPS），但拟合单张图像需数秒优化，限制了离线使用；现有前馈方法虽快，但精度不足且依赖逐图微调。作者希望兼顾高精度与实时性。

Method: 从逐图拟合数据中学习高斯中心密度图；采用等质量Voronoi划分，使每个Voronoi单元约含等量密度以放置高斯；再用无状态、可复用的网络进行数次迭代精化输出。

Result: Kodak-24/DIV2K 上 PSNR 为 40.73/38.61 dB，速度 >60 FPS。相同高斯数量下，较所有前馈基线提升 3.0–15.6 dB；以更少高斯数量仍领先最强基线 2.5–3.5 dB；相同数量下，需逐图拟合的基线达到其 PSNR 需 26–326 倍时间。

Conclusion: Gaussifier 使高保真2D高斯图像足够快且轻量，可用于实时应用；作者开源代码、权重及基于 WebGPU 的浏览器演示。

Abstract: 2D Gaussians render images at thousands of frames per second. However, fitting an image takes seconds of optimization. This confines 2D Gaussians to offline use. Feedforward methods are faster but less accurate without per-image fine-tuning. We present Gaussifier, a lightweight feedforward framework for 2D Gaussian decomposition. We learn a density map of Gaussian centers from per-image fitting data. Equal-mass Voronoi places Gaussians so that each Voronoi cell holds approximately equal density. A stateless, reusable network then refines the output in a few iterations. Gaussifier reaches 40.73/38.61\,dB PSNR on Kodak-24/DIV2K at over 60 FPS. At the same Gaussian count, it exceeds every feedforward baseline by 3.0 to 15.6\,dB in PSNR. With fewer Gaussians than any baseline, it still leads the strongest one by 2.5 to 3.5\,dB. At the same count, baselines with their own per-image fitting need 26 to 326 times longer to reach its PSNR. Gaussifier makes high-fidelity 2D Gaussian images fast and light enough for real-time applications. We release code, weights and a browser-based WebGPU demo at https://github.com/Gaussifier/gaussifier.

</details>


### [13] [Multimodal Cross-Condition Learning for Temporal Sentence Grounding in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00033)
*Allen He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MCCL（多模态交叉条件学习）框架，通过Video Prefix Adapter将池化视觉上下文转为连续token以条件化冻结的DistilBERT，并与Sentence Conditioned Adapter配合实现"视觉→语言→视觉"的有序双向条件交互，在Charades-STA上双向条件相较最强单向模式提升1.6–2.3 mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序句子定位需要既能保留查询相关证据的视频表示，又能反映其被解读时所处视觉上下文的句子表示。现有端到端方法仅通过句子条件化的视觉自适应满足前者，而语言表示仍与视频无关，缺乏视觉对语言的反馈。

Method: 提出MCCL：(1) VPA（Video Prefix Adapter）把池化视觉上下文映射为连续token序列，作为前缀条件化冻结的DistilBERT编码器；(2) SCADA（Sentence Conditioned Adapter）实现句子对视觉的条件化；(3) 二者串联形成视觉→语言→视觉的有序条件交换。系统研究两种视频骨干、LSTM与Mamba时序模块及四种条件模式，在两个数据集上评估。

Result: 在Charades-STA全部四种设置下，双向条件较更强的单向模式提升1.6–2.3 mIoU；ActivityNet Captions呈现不同模式：双向对I3D–Mamba有益，而C3D下视频→文本单向条件更强。

Conclusion: 将单向视觉自适应扩展为可配置的多模态条件框架，并在对应基线上报告了描述性增益；条件方向的最优选择依赖骨干与数据集，需按配置调优。

Abstract: Temporal sentence grounding requires a video representation that preserves query-relevant evidence and a sentence representation that reflects the visual context in which it is interpreted. Prior end-to-end grounding addresses the first requirement through sentence-conditioned visual adaptation, but leaves the language representation independent of the video. This work proposes Multimodal Cross-Condition Learning (MCCL). Its Video Prefix Adapter (VPA) converts pooled visual context into a sequence of continuous tokens that condition a frozen DistilBERT encoder. Combined with the Sentence Conditioned Adapter (SCADA), VPA supports an ordered exchange from vision to language and back to vision. A comprehensive study examines two video backbones, LSTM and Mamba temporal modules, and four conditioning modes on two datasets. Bidirectional conditioning exceeds the stronger one-way mode by 1.6--2.3 mIoU points in all four Charades-STA settings. ActivityNet Captions exhibits a different pattern: bidirectionality helps I3D--Mamba, whereas video-to-text conditioning is stronger with C3D. The study extends one-way visual adaptation into a configurable multimodal framework and reports descriptive gains over the corresponding baselines.

</details>


### [14] [A Trustworthy Clinical Decision Support Framework for Brain Tumor MRI Classification: Integrating Attention-Guided Learning, Explainable AI, and Out-of-Distribution Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00034)
*Arjun Kumar Gupta,Harminder Pal Monga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出整合分类、可解释性、OOD输入筛查与实时部署的脑肿瘤MRI诊断框架：InceptionResNetV2+CBAM分类四类，Grad-CAM可视化，两阶段OOD机制，并落地为NeuroScan AI网页应用，测试准确率96.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤深度学习诊断仅有高分类精度不足以实用，还需可理解的预测、对不合适输入的识别能力以及可用于临床的系统化部署。

Method: 以InceptionResNetV2为骨干并引入CBAM注意力模块，对MRI进行胶质瘤、脑膜瘤、垂体瘤、无肿瘤四分类；用Grad-CAM生成可视化解释；设计两阶段OOD机制——先做灰度图检查，再结合Mahalanobis距离、softmax置信度与预测熵筛查异常输入；各组件集成于实时Web应用NeuroScan AI。

Result: 在Mendeley脑肿瘤MRI数据集（12,064张）上取得98.70%验证准确率、96.73%测试准确率、macro-average AUC 0.9965；OOD流程在100张图像初步基准上达99%检测准确率；Grad-CAM可视化在四类中均与临床公认肿瘤位置定性对应。

Conclusion: 该工作将分类、可解释性、输入验证与部署统一于单一框架，但仍需更大规模的临床与OOD验证。

Abstract: Deep learning models for brain tumor diagnosis need to do more than achieve high classification accuracy. For practical use, they should also provide understandable predictions, recognize unsuitable inputs, and work within a usable clinical system. This paper presents a framework for classifying brain MRI into four categories: glioma, meningioma, pituitary tumor, and no tumor. The model uses InceptionResNetV2 with a Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM), while Grad-CAM provides visual explanations of its predictions. A two-stage out-of-distribution (OOD) mechanism screens unsuitable inputs using an initial grayscale check followed by Mahalanobis distance, softmax confidence, and predictive entropy. These components are brought together in NeuroScan AI, a real-time web application. Evaluated on a Mendeley brain tumor MRI dataset of 12,064 images, the framework achieved 98.70% validation accuracy, 96.73% test accuracy, and a macro-average AUC of 0.9965. The OOD pipeline achieved 99% detection accuracy on a preliminary 100-image benchmark. Grad-CAM visualizations showed qualitative correspondence with clinically established tumor locations across all four classes. Overall, the work brings classification, explainability, input validation, and deployment into one framework, while recognizing that broader clinical and OOD validation is still required.

</details>


### [15] [Selective Positional Allocation for Noise-Robust Query Retrieval in Small-Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00035)
*Yangchen Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPAR检测框架，将位置先验视为可分配资源，在小目标检测中显著提升精度并大幅精简模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空与遥感图像中微小目标检测的瓶颈更多来自位置先验在前景/背景界定不清区域的分布方式，而非骨干网络的表征能力。

Method: 核心为选择性位置嵌入SPE（可解释嵌入+激活条可视化）；编码分支MOHFE融合热图调制的位置信号与类别、框级证据生成紧凑多尺度特征；解码分支HIDQ用前景门控在查询解码前剔除背景主导候选；DPSConv以双路径互补采样增强检索表征。热图监督由现有检测目标导出，仅训练时使用，无额外标注与推理开销。

Result: 在DOTA、DIOR、VisDrone、PASCAL VOC、NWPU VHR-10、AITOD上验证：较基线最高提升9.1个百分点，解码器由8层减至3层，参数量减少59.4%（66.3M vs 163M）。

Conclusion: 以位置线索的"选择性分配与检索"重构检测流程，可在不增加推理成本的前提下显著提升小目标检测性能并实现轻量化。

Abstract: In aerial and remote-sensing imagery, detecting tiny targets is frequently hampered less by the representational capacity of the backbone network than by the manner in which positional priors are distributed across ill-defined foreground and background regions. We introduce SPAR (Selective Positional Allocation and Retrieval), a detection framework that re-conceptualizes positional cues as an allocatable resource: they are intensified at semantically meaningful locations while responses driven by scene clutter are actively attenuated. At the heart of SPAR lies Selective Positional Embedding (SPE), an interpretable embedding mechanism paired with an activation-bar visualization that facilitates direct inspection and hyper-parameter tuning. The encoder branch, termed MOHFE, fuses heatmap-modulated positional signals with category-specific and box-level evidence to yield more compact multi-scale features; the decoder branch, termed HIDQ, employs a foreground gating operation to discard background-dominated candidates prior to query decoding. To cope with the extreme sparsity of small-object evidence, we additionally design DPSConv (Dual-Path Sampling Convolution), which augments retrieval-oriented representations through two geometrically complementary sampling routes. Notably, all heatmap supervision is derived from existing detection objectives and consumed exclusively during training, requiring no auxiliary heatmap annotations and leaving inference as an ordinary forward pass with no additional gradient overhead. Comprehensive evaluations on DOTA, DIOR, VisDrone, PASCAL VOC, NWPU VHR-10, and AITOD demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed design: relative to the baseline, SPAR attains an improvement of up to 9.1 percentage points, shortens the decoder from eight layers to three, and reduces model parameters by 59.4% (66.3M versus 163M).

</details>


### [16] [From Single-Shot QA to Continuous Perception: Revisiting the Energy Composition and Optimization Boundary of Edge VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00036)
*Yiting Duan,Miaoxin Wang,Aoqing Guo,Bo Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在连续感知负载（N=10、稀疏输出）下，视觉预填充能耗占总能耗的92–97%，且其由视觉编码器的架构效率而非模型规模决定；而解码（逐token）能耗随规模单调增长。两者构成相互独立的能耗定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘VLM能耗结论多在单次推理（single-shot）负载下得出，与真实连续感知场景不符，可能低估图像编码开销并误导优化方向，故需重新刻画连续感知下的能耗结构。

Method: 在Jetson AGX Thor与Orin上，对三类架构家族的9个VLM进行profiling，共采集4,508条记录，并用纯文本任务作校准以分离视觉部分开销；在N=10的连续感知与稀疏输出条件下测量能耗分解。

Result: 得到两条相互独立的能耗定律：每输出token的解码能耗随模型规模单调增长；视觉预填充能耗由视觉编码器架构效率主导、与规模无显著关系。连续感知+稀疏输出时视觉预填充能耗占比达92–97%。

Conclusion: 边缘VLM能耗应以连续感知为基准评估；据此提出视觉帧复用、自适应输出截断、任务感知路由与编码器吞吐优化等部署优化策略。

Abstract: Visual-prefill energy conclusions for edge vision-language models (VLMs) are commonly drawn under single-shot workloads; we revisit them under continuous perception. Profiling nine VLMs across three architecture families on Jetson AGX Thor and Orin (4,508 records, calibrated with a pure-text task), we find two mutually independent energy laws: the per-output-token decode cost grows monotonically with model scale, whereas the visual-prefill (image-encoding) cost is dominated by visual-encoder architectural efficiency, not scale. Under continuous perception ($N{=}10$) with sparse output, visual-prefill energy reaches 92--97\% of total. We derive optimization strategies---visual frame reuse, adaptive output truncation, task-aware routing, and encoder throughput optimization---for edge VLM deployment.

</details>


### [17] [Deep Learning for Detecting Building Setback Rule Violations in Urban Planning: A Case Study on Bangladesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00037)
*Farzana Tasnim,Faria Yasmin,Md. Tauseef-Ur-Rahman,Amina Islam,Tamzid Bin Tariq,Yuki Bhuiyan,Muhammad Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较四种机器学习方法，用街景照片对达卡建筑退界（setback）状况进行三分类（合规/临界/违规），微调ResNet18在1,275张测试集上取得最高65.4%准确率（macro-F1 0.50），但各模型均难以识别临界类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 达卡快速城市化导致建筑退界违规普遍，压缩人行道并阻碍紧急通行；负责建筑管控的RAJUK主要依赖人工现场检查，难以规模化。作者希望探索自动化街景筛查的可行性，并为南亚监管场景建立参考性能基线。

Method: 构建372张带地理标签的街景标注数据集（覆盖达卡16个社区），经增强扩展至5,968张用于训练；比较四种方法：MLP、基于冻结ResNet50嵌入的随机森林、基于冻结ResNet18嵌入的SVM，以及微调ResNet18 CNN；在1,275张图像测试集上评估。

Result: 微调CNN准确率65.4%（macro-F1 0.50）最优，其次为随机森林59.9%（macro-F1 0.44）与SVM 58.2%（macro-F1 0.45）。所有模型对合规类识别可靠，但因视觉模糊性对临界"moderate"类表现较差。

Conclusion: 微调CNN在退界合规自动筛查中具相对优势，但整体性能仍受限于小规模数据与临界类别的视觉歧义；结果为南亚监管环境下的自动化退界合规筛查确立了参考基线，未来需扩充数据与改进边界样本判别。

Abstract: Dhaka's rapid urban growth has made building setback violations common, narrowing footpaths and blocking emergency access. RAJUK, responsible for building control in the Dhaka Metropolitan Area, enforces setback rules mainly through manual field inspection, which scales poorly. We present a systematic comparison of four machine learning approaches for classifying building setback conditions from street-level photographs: negative (compliant), moderate (borderline), and positive (violation). We built a labeled dataset of 372 geo-tagged images from sixteen Dhaka neighborhoods and expanded it with 5,968 augmented copies for training. We trained and evaluated a multi-layer perceptron (MLP), a Random Forest ensemble on frozen ResNet50 embeddings, a support vector machine on frozen ResNet18 embeddings, and a fine-tuned ResNet18 CNN. On a 1,275-image test set, the fine-tuned CNN achieved 65.4% accuracy (macro-F1 0.50), followed by Random Forest at 59.9% (macro-F1 0.44) and SVM at 58.2% (macro-F1 0.45). All models classify the compliant class reliably but struggle with the borderline moderate class due to visual ambiguity. These results establish reference performance levels for automated setback-compliance screening in a South Asian regulatory setting.

</details>


### [18] [Beyond Black Boxes: An Ontology Based Vision Framework for Transparent Fruit Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00039)
*M. J. Marapperuma,B. V. K. I. Vidanage*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种融合实时视觉与OWL语义知识库的混合式果实病害检测框架，通过知识引导图像处理流程，无需大规模标注数据即可实现可解释诊断，在MangoFruitDDS上F1达85.00%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的植物病原识别方法虽准确，但依赖大规模标注数据集，且为黑箱模型，缺乏灵活性与透明度，不利于及时、经济地减少作物损失。

Method: 构建基于Web本体语言（OWL）的果实病害综合本体，形式化编码症状表型、光谱表型（HSV）与形态表型等植物病理学知识；由知识引导的图像处理流水线在专家规则下完成诊断，无需大规模训练数据。本体经计算验证的结构稳健性与实用性评估。

Result: 本体评估总体得分7.4/10；在公开数据集MangoFruitDDS上取得F1 85.00%、精确率85.00%、召回率85.23%，与VGG16等深度学习模型表现相当。

Conclusion: 知识驱动方法可在无大规模标注数据条件下实现准确、透明且可扩展的自动化果实病害诊断，具备与深度学习模型竞争的性能。

Abstract: Efficiency and reliability in the timely identification of phytopathogens would help minimize crop loss in agricultural sectors but existing deep learning based methods, although accurate, would require a large annotated dataset and are black-boxed in their operation, thus, restricting flexibility and transparency. The current paper presents a hybrid detection framework that is able to overcome these drawbacks through the unified real-time vision with a semantic knowledge base. Web Ontology Language (OWL) was used to develop a comprehensive ontology of the disease of fruits that formally codifies phytopathological knowledge, such as symptom phenotype, spectral phenotype (HSV), and morphological phenotype. Such knowledge-guided configuration guides the image processing pipeline, making it possible to diagnose using this knowledge under expert specified rules without a large training dataset. The ontology underwent a computationally verified test of its structural soundness and commonsensical practical usefulness and scored an overall of 7.4/10. On the public set of MangoFruitDDS, the diagnostic performance was an F1 Score of 85.00%, precision of 85.00%, and a recall of 85.23%. These findings are very competitive and similar to other known deep learning models like the VGG16 that prove that the knowledge-driven approach provides an accurate, transparent, and scalable solution on automated fruit disease diagnosis.

</details>


### [19] [Real-Time Obstacle Detection and Distance Estimation on Smartphones for Visually Impaired and Distracted Pedestrians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00040)
*Abtin Zandi,Sarvin Nami,Roza Ganjipour,Bardia Sabagh Kermani,Amirreza Tarabkhah,Hamed Farbeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于Kotlin的Android应用，用重训练的YOLOv8检测18类城市障碍物，经剪枝、量化与早退优化后通过NNAPI部署，中端机推理延迟由约3.2s降至0.39–0.48s（推荐配置0.42–0.48s，召回0.81）；结合ARCore或单目焦距测距，无需LiDAR/深度相机，为视障者与"低头族"提供TTS与振动多模态实时预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球至少22亿人存在视力障碍，其中数千万为重度视力丧失；传统助行工具（白手杖、导盲犬）成本高、能力有限且难以普及。同时，公众场所中智能手机分心导致与车辆碰撞、绊倒和跌落风险上升。因此需要一种轻量、基于智能手机、兼顾无障碍性与低延迟的解决方案，同时服务两类用户。

Method: 以Kotlin开发Android应用；将YOLOv8检测器在18类常见城市障碍物上重训练；采用剪枝、量化（INT8）和早退分支进行模型优化；通过Android NNAPI部署，可在具备条件时将推理分派到GPU、NPU或DSP；结合ARCore或单目焦距估计进行距离估计，仅需单颗手机摄像头，不使用LiDAR或深度相机；向视障用户提供TTS语音引导、向分心行人提供振动提示的多模态反馈。

Result: 在中端智能手机上，优化使平均推理延迟从现成YOLOv8n的约3.2s降至0.39–0.48s；推荐配置（INT8量化+早退）运行于0.42–0.48s，同时保持0.81的召回率。距离估计仅依赖单目相机即可工作。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过剪枝、量化、早退与NNAPI硬件加速的组合，YOLOv8可在消费级手机上实现近实时的城市障碍物检测，配合单目测距与多模态反馈，可作为一种低成本、无需专用传感器的可及性辅助方案，同时服务视障人士与智能手机分心行人。

Abstract: According to the World Health Organization, at least 2.2 billion people worldwide live with visual impairment, including tens of millions with severe vision loss. Traditional aids such as white canes or guide dogs remain costly, limited in capability, and inaccessible to many. At the same time, smartphone distraction in public spaces has been linked to elevated risks of collisions with vehicles, trips, and falls. These dual challenges motivate lightweight, smartphone-based solutions that combine accessibility with low latency. We present a Kotlin-based Android application that uses a YOLOv8 detector, retrained to recognize eighteen common urban obstacle classes, to deliver real-time obstacle alerts for both user groups. The detector was optimized with pruning, quantization, and early-exit branches, and deployed through the Android Neural Networks API (NNAPI), which can dispatch inference to GPU, NPU, or DSP accelerators when available. On a mid-range smartphone, these optimizations reduced average inference latency from about 3.2 s for the off-the-shelf YOLOv8n to 0.39-0.48 s; the recommended configuration (INT8 quantization with early exit) runs in 0.42-0.48 s while retaining a recall of 0.81. The application combines the optimized detector with ARCore-based or monocular focal-length distance estimation, so it works with a single smartphone camera and requires no LiDAR, depth camera, or other specialized sensor. Multimodal feedback, including Text-to-Speech guidance for visually impaired users and vibration alerts for distracted pedestrians, supports use across different contexts.

</details>


### [20] [Think Before You Locate: Clinical Chain-of-Though Supervision for Sequential Medical Image Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00041)
*Adinath Madhavrao Dukre,Govinda Kolli,Yifan Lu,Ziyun Zou,Azmine Toushik Wasi,Sara Atito,Behzad Bozorgtabar,Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Muhammad Awais,Imran Razzak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MedChain构建18.2万条筛选后的图像级推理理由，覆盖8类序列医学grounding任务与10种影像模态，并以dCoT-SFT微调Qwen3.5，在MedSG-Bench上刷新SOTA（27B：77.24 mIoU、85.20 Acc@0.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床定位依赖跨时点、跨切片、跨视角/模态的比较，而现有序列医学grounding的视觉语言模型仅用边界框监督；框只说明目标在哪里，无法解释为何该图像中的该区域能回答问题。因此研究显式、图像接地的推理能否提升定位，以及哪些任务受益。

Method: 构建MedChain语料（182,573条过滤后的rationale，8种序列grounding任务、10种影像模态）：先由医学VLM（FlemingVL-38B）生成描述式草稿理由，再由多模态精炼器（Qwen3.5-397B-A17B）结合图像、问题与标注框重写为框条件的、显式跨图像推理的解释；在此基础上对Qwen3.5进行dCoT-SFT（审慎式思维链监督）微调，并额外尝试dCoT-SFT后接IoU奖励的GRPO。

Result: 在4B/9B/27B三个规模上均达到MedSG-Bench新SOTA，27B取得77.24 mIoU与85.20 Acc@0.5，较最强基线提升4.69/5.49点；固定4B checkpoint的配对比较中，dCoT-SFT较answer-only SFT提升3.56 mIoU（95% CI 3.13–4.00）。增益主要来自变化检测与目标跟踪，三类外观匹配任务性能下降；用其他任务的rationale替换模型自身rationale使mIoU下降12.2点，说明定位强依赖推理上下文；dCoT-SFT后加GRPO仅带来小幅且依赖任务的增益（经多随机种子对照实验分析）。

Conclusion: 显式图像接地的推理监督可有效提升序列医学grounding，但收益具有任务异质性：对需要跨图像时序/运动推理的任务有效，对外观匹配类任务反而有害；定位性能高度依赖与任务匹配的推理上下文；仅靠结果级强化学习（IoU奖励）难以在dCoT-SFT之上进一步显著提升。

Abstract: Clinicians rarely localize a finding from one image alone. They compare current and prior scans, follow structures across slices, and match regions across views or modalities. Yet vision-language models for sequential medical grounding usually learn only from bounding boxes. Boxes show where the target is, but they do not explain why that region in that image answers the question. We study whether explicit, image-grounded reasoning can improve localization, and which tasks benefit from it. We introduce method, whose core is MedChain, a corpus of 182,573 filtered rationales spanning eight sequential grounding tasks and ten imaging modalities. A medical VLM (FlemingVL-38B) first drafts a caption-like rationale, and a multimodal refiner (Qwen3.5-397B-A17B) then re-examines the images, question and annotated box to write a box-conditioned explanation with explicit cross-image reasoning. We fine-tune Qwen3.5 on these rationales with deliberative chain-of-thought supervision (dCoT-SFT). Across 4B, 9B, and 27B models, dCoT-SFT sets a new state of the art on MedSG-Bench. The 27B model reaches 77.24 mIoU and 85.20 Acc@0.5, improving over the strongest prior model by 4.69 and 5.49 points. In a paired comparison using fixed 4B checkpoints, dCoT-SFT improves over answer-only SFT by 3.56 mIoU (95% CI 3.13--4.00). The gains mainly come from change detection and object tracking, while performance drops on three appearance-matching tasks. Replacing a model's own rationale with a rationale from another task reduces mIoU by 12.2 points, showing that localization depends strongly on the reasoning context. Finally, adding GRPO with an IoU reward after dCoT-SFT gives only small and task-dependent gains, which we analyze through controlled multi-seed development experiments.

</details>


### [21] [Diagnosing Zero-Shot Forgetting in CLIP Fine-Tuning: Attention Drift Characterizes Adaptation, Representation Drift Predicts Transfer Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00042)
*Ruize Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究能否利用微调期间已可得的无标签目标图像来预判CLIP微调造成的零样本能力损失，比较了注意力熵、线性CKA、嵌入漂移与相对权重变化四种信号，发现CKA适合跨配置比较、注意力熵仅适用于单一配置内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP在下游任务上微调通常降低其在其他任务上的零样本精度，而量化这一损失一般需要带标签的迁移基准，成本较高。作者希望验证该损失能否在微调过程中直接从已有的无标签目标图像中被提前预测。

Method: 在EuroSAT与Oxford-IIIT Pets上用全量微调（Full FT）和LoRA微调ViT-B/32，覆盖4个学习率×3个随机种子，并补充ViT-B/16网格；以DTD、GTSRB为留出验证目标；以CIFAR-100、Food-101、ImageNet-R的零样本保持率衡量遗忘。比较四种信号：CLS token注意力熵、与预训练模型的线性CKA、嵌入漂移、相对权重变化，并进行分块（block）分解分析。

Result: 固定配置内，注意力熵变化与遗忘强相关（平均|ρ|=0.860），但其符号依赖数据集、适配方法与骨干，合并后相关性降至0.361；分块分析显示符号几乎完全由第7–12块决定。CKA以ρ=0.960对运行按保持率排序，且在六个epoch中的第一个epoch后即已确立（ρ=0.959）。在留出目标上CKA达ρ=0.95，分块解释也正确预测了两个数据集注意力变化的符号。

Conclusion: 支持一条简单的决策规则：CKA适用于跨配置比较，注意力熵仅在单一配置内具有信息量。作者指出证据局限于单一模型族与较短的训练日程。

Abstract: Fine-tuning CLIP on a downstream task typically reduces its zero-shot accuracy on other tasks, and quantifying this loss ordinarily requires a labeled transfer benchmark. We study whether the loss can be anticipated from unlabeled target images that are already available during fine-tuning. Four signals are compared: the entropy of CLS token attention, linear centered kernel alignment (CKA) with the pretrained model, embedding drift, and relative weight change. ViT-B/32 is adapted by full fine-tuning (Full FT) and by LoRA on EuroSAT and Oxford-IIIT Pets over four learning rates and three seeds, with an additional ViT-B/16 grid; DTD and GTSRB are held out as confirmation targets, and forgetting is measured as zero-shot retention on CIFAR-100, Food-101, and ImageNet-R. Within a fixed configuration, the change in attention entropy is strongly associated with forgetting (mean $|ρ|=0.860$). Its sign, however, depends on the dataset, the adaptation method, and the backbone, and the pooled correlation falls to 0.361. A block decomposition shows that this sign is determined almost entirely by blocks 7 to 12. CKA ranks runs by retention at $ρ=0.960$, and the ranking is already established after the first of six epochs ($ρ=0.959$). On the held-out targets, CKA reaches $ρ=0.95$, and the block account correctly predicts the sign of the attention change for both datasets. These results support a simple decision rule: CKA is suited to comparisons across configurations, whereas attention entropy is informative only within a single configuration. The evidence is limited to one model family and short training schedules.

</details>


### [22] [Out-of-distribution AI-generated image detection with zero-phase deconvolution for residual whitening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00043)
*Yusuf Yigit Pilavcı,Antoine Mallet,Vincent Itier,Jan Butora,Jérémie Boulanger,Patrick Bas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以白化图像噪声残差（去卷积预处理）去除生成器指纹，缓解取证偏置，从而提升AI生成图像检测在分布外生成器上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器在分布外测试中鲁棒性差：训练集未包含的生成器上性能显著下降。作者将此归因于图像来源（真实或生成）之间的"取证偏置"，即生成器特有的指纹引入的相关噪声模式，导致检测器过拟合到特定生成器而非通用的生成痕迹。

Method: 以去除生成器特异性指纹为目标重新设计训练流程；该流程可用一个去卷积（deconvolution）预处理操作近似实现，其作用是对图像噪声残差的空间分布进行白化（whitening），以消除引发泛化损失的相关噪声模式；随后在经白化处理的残差上端到端训练CNN检测器。

Result: 白化噪声残差空间分布可显著提升检测器对训练中未见生成器图像的检测表现；在相同设置下，基于白化残差训练的端到端CNN相比使用原始图像训练，平均检测性能提升12.6%。

Conclusion: 抑制生成器相关指纹、白化噪声残差是一种简单有效的预处理手段，能明显改善AI生成图像检测的跨生成器泛化性；作者认为该训练过程可由去卷积预处理近似，为缓解取证偏置提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Current AI-generated image detection methods struggle in out-of-distribution testing conditions because detectors are usually not robust when evaluated on generators not included in the training set. This work explores a way to mitigate the forensic bias between sources of images -whether real or generated -by removing generator-specific fingerprints. This new training process can be approximated by pre-processing involving a deconvolution operation designed to remove correlated noise patterns inducing loss of generalization. Indeed, we show that whitening the spatial distribution of the image noise residuals can significantly improve the performance of detectors on images coming from generators unseen during training. In particular, an end-to-end CNN model trained on whitened image residuals improves detection by 12.6% on average compared to a training involving original images.

</details>


### [23] [SparseWAM: Driving World-Action Models are Worth 16 Register Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00044)
*Yixiang Yang,Lizhong Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Zhiwen Chen,Jie Ling,Qiankun Yu,Xingliang Liu,Yang Lei,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SparseWAM 提出在每帧仅16个token的稀疏寄存器空间中做世界模型预测，保留部署期"先想象后行动"能力，同时将延迟降至164ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频生成器的世界动作模型（WAM）在推理时需生成稠密未来视频，延迟过高；高效变体在部署时丢弃世界分支，丧失测试时想象能力；即使用patch级DINO/JEPA特征替代稠密视频隐变量，仍存在token序列长、编码大量决策无关信息的问题。

Method: 提出SparseWAM：以冻结DINOv2特征压缩得到每帧仅16个token的稀疏寄存器空间，并通过联合"规划+打分"预训练使该空间聚合决策相关信息；在该空间内使用模态解耦的Mixture-of-Transformers与因果注意力掩码，使动作token可条件于想象出的未来寄存器，从而在部署时保留显式"先想象后行动"推理；空间紧凑性还支持低成本多模态采样。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1上取得92.6 PDMS，NAVSIM v2上90.1 EPDMS，NavHard上39.6，延迟仅164ms，约为DriveVA的1/5。

Conclusion: 稀疏寄存器空间可在大幅降低推理延迟的同时保留世界模型的测试时想象能力，验证了该表示在自动驾驶规划中的高效性与有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) transfer scene-dynamics priors to planning by jointly forecasting future scenes and ego trajectories. However, WAMs built on video generators must generate dense future videos at inference, incurring prohibitive latency, while efficient variants discard the world branch at deployment and thus forgo test-time imagination. Even when dense video latents are replaced with patch-level DINO or JEPA features, these representations still involve long token sequences and encode decision-irrelevant information. We therefore propose SparseWAM, which forecasts in a sparse register space of only 16 tokens per frame, compressed from frozen DINOv2 features and shaped by joint planning-and-scoring pretraining to concentrate decision-relevant information. Within this space, SparseWAM employs a modality-decoupled Mixture-of-Transformers with a causal attention mask that lets action tokens condition on imagined future registers, retaining explicit imagine-then-act reasoning at deployment. Its compactness further enables low-cost multimodal sampling. As a result, our SparseWAM achieves 92.6 PDMS on NAVSIM v1, 90.1 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, and 39.6 on NavHard at only 164 ms latency, roughly 1/5 that of DriveVA, demonstrating the efficiency of the sparse register space. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [24] [Faces or Models? Where the Age Gap in Facial Expression Recognition Comes From](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00045)
*Jessica Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究在FACES数据库上评测了5个开源面部表情识别模型，发现所有模型在老年面孔上准确率下降11.7–15.2个百分点，且该差距主要源于最终分类层的训练方式而非特征表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面部表情识别系统日益用于养老照护场景，但其在老年面孔上的准确性鲜有测量，存在对老年人群可靠性的评估空白。

Method: 在FACES数据库（171名19–80岁成人、2,052张摆拍照片）上评测5个开源FER模型，覆盖3种架构（ResNet-18、POSTER++、QCS）×2个训练集（RAF-DB、AffectNet）；同时设置人类评分者与仅用面部几何特征的分类器作为对照，并控制架构、图像质量、裁剪与表情强度；最后仅重训练最终分类层。

Result: 所有模型在老年面孔上损失11.7–15.2个百分点；人类评分者损失8.6点，几何分类器损失8.3点；老年人摆拍时愤怒、恐惧、快乐表情的个体差异更大。AffectNet训练模型把老年中性面孔误判为悲伤或愤怒的比例达62%，RAF-DB训练模型则在恐惧、厌恶、愤怒上损失更多。仅重拟合最终分类层后，差距由11–21点降至4点以内。

Conclusion: 模型所学特征能够表征老年面孔，年龄相关性能下降的根源在于最终分类层的训练方式，而非特征提取能力，提示可通过轻量层重训练缓解年龄偏差。

Abstract: Facial expression recognition (FER) systems are increasingly used in elder care, yet their accuracy on older faces is rarely measured. This study evaluated five open-source FER models on the FACES database, which holds 2,052 posed photographs of 171 adults aged 19 to 80. The models cross three architectures (ResNet-18, POSTER++ and QCS) with two training datasets (RAF-DB and AffectNet). All models lost accuracy on older faces by 11.7 to 15.2 percentage points. Older faces were harder for people too: human raters lost 8.6 points, a classifier using only facial geometry lost 8.3, and for anger, fear, and happiness older adults differed more from one another when posing. On specific expressions the models fell much further than people did. AffectNet-trained models misread older neutral faces as sad or angry 62% of the time. RAF-DB-trained models lost fear, disgust, and anger instead. The gap persisted after controlling for architecture, image quality, cropping, and expression intensity. When only the final classification layer was refitted on older faces, the gap shrank from 11 to 21 points to 4 points or less, suggesting that the models' learned features can represent older faces and that the deficit lies in how the final layer was trained.

</details>


### [25] [Circumpolar Wall-to-Wall Detection of Retrogressive Thaw Slumps with Scarce Labels and Extreme Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00046)
*Yili Yang,Heidi Rodenhizer,Robb Young,Brendan M. Rogers,Marlena Bartkus,Stefano Potter,Greg Fiske,Scott Zolkos,Wenwen Li,Chia-Yu Hsu,Luigi Marini,Todd C. Nicholson,Benjamin C. Maglio,Jennifer Watts,Anna Liljedahl,Susan M. Natali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文面向永久冻土冻融滑塌(Retrogressive thaw slumps, RTS)这一"稀有目标"检测问题，构建并系统评估了深度学习检测系统，用4160万张4.8米PlanetScope影像图块覆盖2068万平方公里的环极区，并通过172次受控实验考查了各因素对精度的影响，最终产出含19,068个高置信度检测的环极RTS地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTS是冻土退化中最活跃的地表形变之一，剧烈冻融过程可使冻土解冻对气候变暖的贡献约翻倍，且已威胁北极三分之一的基础设施。尽管环极RTS制图进展迅速，但已发布清单仅覆盖冻土区的一部分；同时RTS仅占影像图块的0.1–0.5%，标注成本高昂，因此检测器必须按稀有目标问题来设计与评估。

Method: 用4160万张4.8米PlanetScope图块（2068万平方公里）开展环极制图；训练集含22,259个图块、其中1,718个含2,609个RTS实例；通过172次受控实验进行调优，比较卷积网络与多模态融合、基础模型编码器、掩码自编码器预训练等替代方案；并在真实稀有比例0.1–0.5%下，于留出生态区做一次性测试，提出稀有目标的评估协议。

Result: 卷积网络在所有对比方案中表现最佳——多模态融合、基础模型编码器、MAE预训练均以更高算力得到相近或更差结果；标注数据对性能提升最大，基础模型的优势仅在标签稀缺时出现。真实比例0.1–0.5%下，最终模型PR-AUC为0.812–0.855，目标精度0.854，F1为0.644；全覆盖部署得到19,068个高置信度检测，抽样精度按尺寸等级为0.54–0.90。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于：提出面向稀有目标的评估协议、给出稀缺标签条件下决定精度的因素的受控分析，以及由经充分验证的模型生成的环极RTS地图；说明在稀有目标任务中，卷积网络配合充足高质量标注仍具竞争力。

Abstract: Retrogressive thaw slumps (RTS) are among the most dynamic ground deformations caused by permafrost thaw. Abrupt thaw processes such as RTS could roughly double the contribution of permafrost thaw to climate warming, and already threaten a third of Arctic infrastructure. Circumpolar RTS mapping has advanced quickly, yet released inventories together cover only part of the permafrost region. RTS occupy only 0.1-0.5% of image tiles and labels are costly, so a detector must be designed and evaluated as a rare-target problem. We therefore developed a deep learning system and mapped 20.68 M square kilometres of circumpolar permafrost using 41.6 M tiles of 4.8 m PlanetScope imagery. Training used 22,259 tiles with 2,609 RTS instances in 1,718 of them, and the model was tuned through 172 controlled experiments. A convolutional network matched every alternative tested, including multimodal fusion, foundation-model encoders and masked-autoencoder pretraining, each at far more compute for similar or worse results. Labelled data moved performance most, and a foundation model's advantage appeared only when labels were scarce. Tested once on held-out ecoregions at the real-world prevalence of 0.1-0.5%, the final model achieved an area under the precision-recall curve of 0.812-0.855, an object precision of 0.854 and F1 of 0.644. Deployed wall-to-wall, the model produced a circumpolar map of 19,068 high-confidence detections whose sampled precision ranges from 0.54 to 0.90 by size class. We present an evaluation protocol for rare targets, a controlled account of what governs accuracy under scarce labels, and the circumpolar RTS map the validated model produced.

</details>


### [26] [TopoMix: Continuous Spatial Mixing for Long-Tailed Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00047)
*Yefan Wang,Yusen Wu,Zihui Ling,Jianyuan Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TopoMix，一种无训练、用连续空间变化掩码替代固定全局/硬替换混合的数据增强方法，在长尾识别基准上稳定优于代表性混合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于混合（mixing）的增强依赖固定全局比例或硬性空间替换，缺乏局部变化空间；在长尾识别中少数类对增强样本质量尤为敏感，该缺陷更突出。

Method: 由低分辨率随机控制网格经双三次插值（bicubic interpolation）与随机非线性整形（stochastic non-linear shaping）生成平滑、空间连续变化的掩码，用该掩码对两张图像做逐元素混合并对标签进行重加权；不引入可训练参数或辅助网络（training-free）。

Result: 在标准长尾基准上，TopoMix在各代表性混合类基线上取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 平滑空间混合是一种对长尾识别有效的增强策略，且以极简的免训练方式实现输入空间中更灵活的混合。

Abstract: Most mixing-based augmentation methods improve generalization through a simple idea: combine two training samples with a fixed global ratio or a hard spatial replacement. While effective, this design leaves little room for local variation, which becomes a more visible limitation in long-tailed recognition, where minority classes are especially sensitive to the quality of augmented samples. We propose TopoMix, a training-free augmentation method that replaces fixed mixing with continuous spatial mixing. TopoMix constructs a smooth spatially varying mask from a low-resolution random control grid, bicubic interpolation, and stochastic non-linear shaping, and uses this mask for element-wise image mixing and label reweighting. Without introducing trainable parameters or auxiliary networks, TopoMix offers a simple way to make mixing more flexible in the input space. Experiments on standard long-tailed benchmarks show that it consistently improves over representative mixing-based baselines, indicating that smooth spatial mixing is an effective augmentation strategy for long-tailed recognition.

</details>


### [27] [Keyframes First, Motion Second: Revisiting Compressed-Domain Video Captioning under Limited Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00048)
*Asok B K,Ashim Nepal,Loknath Regmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在有限监督条件下，压缩域视频描述中关键帧表征（尤其微调SigLIP 2池化头）远比运动/残差分支更有效，且仅解码I帧更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoCap类压缩域描述器需从零训练运动与残差编码器，消耗边缘设备稀缺的解码时间与监督信号；作者探究在有限监督下运动分支是否值得付出该代价。

Method: 在VATEX 2,500个训练片段上做受控实验：固定冻结的SigLIP 2关键帧编码器、GPT-2解码器与训练计划，仅改变解码器视觉token（34种配置），用配对bootstrap置信区间与三随机种子报告留出测试集CIDEr；并在MSVD完整划分、CoCap原生视频编码下复验。

Result: 从零训练的运动编码器无可测收益（CoCap式动作token CIDEr变化-0.55，95%CI[-2.3,+1.2]，其余六种运动用法亦无益）；用48倍描述样本预热的运动模型平均+1.3 CIDEr但跨种子不显著，且仅在关键帧token冻结时成立。微调SigLIP 2注意力池化头+5.4 CIDEr，1–8个此类token在每个种子上均优于16个CoCap式token，每片段解码7.3 ms（对比44–114 ms）。MSVD上该读出方式领先14.2 CIDEr，动作token无可靠增益。

Conclusion: 有限监督下应优先投资关键帧表征，运动信息次之；简单高效的关键帧读出可在精度与延迟上同时取代繁重的运动分支。

Abstract: Compressed-domain video captioners such as CoCap read the H.264 bitstream directly: a pretrained encoder reads the keyframes (I-frames), while motion and residual encoders, trained from scratch, turn the codec's motion vectors and residuals into action tokens. This motion branch costs decoding time and supervision, both scarce on edge devices. We ask when it pays off under limited supervision. In a controlled study on 2,500 VATEX training clips, we fix a frozen SigLIP 2 keyframe encoder, a GPT-2 caption decoder and the training schedule, vary only the visual tokens the decoder receives (34 configurations), and report held-out test CIDEr with paired bootstrap intervals and three seeds. Motion encoders trained from scratch bring no measurable benefit: CoCap-style action tokens change CIDEr by -0.55 (95% CI [-2.3, +1.2]), and six other uses of motion do not help. Warm-started from a model trained on 48x the caption samples, they add +1.3 CIDEr on average, though not significantly across seeds, and only while the keyframe tokens stay frozen. Reading the keyframes better matters more: fine-tuning SigLIP 2's own attention-pooling head adds 5.4 CIDEr, and one to eight such tokens beat the 16 CoCap-style tokens on every seed while decoding only I-frames (7.3 ms instead of 44-114 ms per clip). The same holds on MSVD's full split in CoCap's own video encoding, where the readout leads by 14.2 CIDEr and action tokens bring no reliable gain, also with clean single-reference motion vectors. Under limited supervision, keyframes come first and motion second.

</details>


### [28] [Background Bias Assessment and Mitigation in Plant Disease Detection Using EfficientNet-B0 with Image Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00049)
*Majed Molhi,Nishi Gupta,Ali Algumaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统量化植物病害检测模型中的背景偏置，提出原图/仅叶片/仅背景三条件评估协议，发现EfficientNet-B0在PlantVillage上原图98.97%、仅叶片96.62%，而仅背景仍达23.58%，BBC系数0.762表明性能主要依赖叶片特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准数据集上超过99%的准确率可能掩盖模型利用虚假背景相关（环境捷径）而非真实病理特征的脆弱性，亟需可量化的背景偏置评估手段。

Method: 采用两阶段训练框架：基于EfficientNet-B0的迁移学习，并在优化过程中对背景信息进行操控；构建原图、仅叶片、仅背景三条件评估协议，并定义背景偏置系数（BBC）进行定量验证。

Result: PlantVillage（20,638张图像、15类病害）上，原图准确率98.97%，仅叶片96.62%，仅背景骤降至23.58%；BBC=0.762，说明预测性能主要依赖叶片相关特征而非背景失真。

Conclusion: 即便是先进模型也可能利用环境捷径，背景偏置评估对农业AI的可部署性至关重要；所提方法为面向实际部署的农业AI奠定基础，代码、模型与评估流程均已公开。

Abstract: Plant disease detection using deep learning has achieved remarkable accuracies exceeding 99% on benchmark datasets. However, these metrics may mask a critical vulnerability: models may be taking advantage of false background correlations instead of learning real pathological features. This study presents a systematic investigation of background bias in plant disease detection through a two-stage training framework with quantitative validation. We propose a three-condition evaluation protocol testing models on original, leaf-only, and background-only images to directly measure background dependency. Our approach combines standard transfer learning using EfficientNet-B0 and manipulation of background information during optimization. Experimental results on the PlantVillage dataset (20,638 images, 15 disease classes) show that while our model achieves 98.97% accuracy on the original images and maintains 96.62% accuracy on leaf-only images, the performance drops to 23.58% on background-only images. The calculated background bias (BBC) coefficient of 0.762 indicates that a large part of the model's predictive performance depends on leaf-related features rather than background distortions. This research offers clear evidence that even with the most advanced models, environmental shortcuts can be leveraged, and this highlights the importance of bias evaluation. The approach that has been used has laid a foundation for the development of agricultural AI that is ready for deployment. The implementation, models, and evaluation process are all publicly available.

</details>


### [29] [ReactionBench: A Human-Anchored Benchmark for Modeling Reactions to Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00050)
*Yibo Zhao,Ao Qu,Xuan Jiang,Jiahao Xu,Keane Ong,Hang Jiang,Zhaofeng Wu,Dingyi Zhuang,Yihong Tang,Kaichen Zhou,Jinhua Zhao,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReactionBench，一个以人为锚点的视频理解基准，用于评测多模态模型能否将观察者的反应与其刺激语境对应起来，涵盖反应强度排序、反应-刺激匹配与反应依据解释三项能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态视频基准多评估事件、时序推理或画面中的人物，情感与观众反应数据集则多聚焦情绪标签、聚合反应或仅从刺激本身做预测；"模型能否将观察者反应锚定到对应刺激语境"这一问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建ReactionBench：从31,834个同步刺激-反应片段（433小时、八个领域）的候选池中筛选，经人工标注获得3,257个时刻的14,894条评分；冻结评测集含250个强度画像、112个匹配题、200个理由题及447条盲写人类参考。评测三项互补能力：视频内反应强度排序、反应与时间对齐刺激的匹配、解释与该反应相关的具体刺激线索。

Result: 在七个多模态模型上，反应特定对齐仍具挑战性：四选一刺激-反应匹配最佳模型准确率仅50.9%，GPT-5在反应替换下无可检测的性能损失；性能对评测协议亦敏感。

Conclusion: 现有模型难以完成反应特定的视频理解与刺激锚定，且结果受评测协议影响；ReactionBench为该方向提供了人本锚定的评测基准。

Abstract: Understanding what happens in a video is not sufficient for socially responsive multimodal systems: such systems also aim to relate what a person observes to how that person responds. Existing video benchmarks largely evaluate events, temporal reasoning, or people depicted in the video, while affective and viewer-response datasets often focus on emotion labels, aggregate responses, or prediction from the stimulus alone. This leaves an underexplored question: can current multimodal models ground an observed viewer reaction in the stimulus context associated with it? We introduce ReactionBench, a human-anchored benchmark for reaction-specific video understanding. Built from a candidate pool of 31,834 synchronized stimulus-reaction clips spanning 433 hours and eight domains, ReactionBench evaluates three complementary abilities: ranking the intensity of a viewer's reactions within a video, matching a reaction to its temporally aligned stimulus, and explaining the concrete stimulus cues linked to that reaction. Human annotation provides 14,894 ratings over 3,257 moments, and the frozen evaluation comprises 250 intensity profiles, 112 matching items, and 200 rationale items with 447 blind-written human references. Across seven multimodal models, reaction-specific grounding remains challenging: the best model reaches 50.9% accuracy on four-way stimulus-reaction matching, while GPT-5 shows no detectable loss under reaction substitution. Performance is also sensitive to evaluation protocol. Project page: https://yibozh.github.io/ReactionBench-page/

</details>


### [30] [SIMIT: Self-Improving Vision-Language Models via Imagination at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00053)
*Mehmet Onurcan Kaya,Desmond Elliott,Dim P. Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIMIT：让视觉语言模型在回答无标签测试查询前，先"自造"由答案反推图像生成的（问题,答案,图像）三元组作为练习数据，通过多阶段验证与难度筛选、自适应预算分配，实现免标注、免外部模型的测试时自我提升，支持上下文学习（SIMIT-ICL）与权重更新（SIMIT-FT）两种形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在无标注、无外部模型的给定测试集上提升性能。现有测试时方法存在两类局限：一是延长推理或重复采样但不改变模型；二是用多候选答案的多数一致或模型自判正确性作为奖励更新模型，但这类奖励会强化既有错误。

Method: 核心思想是"想象一个解已知的相似问题"——模型无需正确解出目标查询也能构造有用的练习题。具体先指定答案、再生成对应图像，合成多样化的（问题,答案,图像）三元组；结合原生图像生成与可扩展技能库（文档、图表、示意图等结构化视觉）以覆盖多模态任务；流水线中用多阶段验证检查图像是否支持所指定答案，并用难度过滤保留有信息量的样本；自适应预算对模型置信度较低的查询分配更多样本。所得数据可用于上下文内改进（SIMIT-ICL）或权重内改进（SIMIT-FT）。

Result: 在17个多样化基准上，SIMIT 优于现有自我提升方法；最佳配置相对 BAGEL-7B 取得 +7.20% 的平均相对增益，免训练的 SIMIT-ICL 亦达到 +6.98%。

Conclusion: 以"答案优先、图像后生成"的可控合成数据替代易强化错误的自我奖励信号，是测试时自我提升的有效途径，且无需标注与外部模型，在免训练与权重更新两种设置下均取得一致收益。

Abstract: We introduce SIMIT, a test-time self-improvement framework in which a vision-language model creates its own query-specific training data before answering an unlabeled test query. Our goal is to improve performance on a given target test set without annotations or external models. To pursue this goal, some test-time methods improve answers through longer reasoning or repeated sampling but leave the model unchanged. Others update the model using rewards based on majority agreement among multiple candidate answers or the model's own judgments of correctness, but such rewards can reinforce existing mistakes. SIMIT instead imagines similar problems whose solutions it controls: a model need not correctly solve the target query to construct useful practice examples. It synthesizes diverse (question, answer, image) triplets by specifying each answer before realizing its image. To support diverse multimodal tasks, SIMIT combines native image generation with an extensible skill library for structured visuals, including documents, charts, and diagrams. Within this pipeline, multi-stage verification checks that images support their assigned answers, while difficulty filtering retains informative examples. Adaptive budgeting further targets this practice by allocating more samples to queries the model is less confident in answering. The resulting data can improve the model in-context (SIMIT-ICL) or in-weight (SIMIT-FT). Across 17 diverse benchmarks, SIMIT outperforms existing self-improvement methods. Its best configuration achieves a +7.20% mean relative gain over BAGEL-7B, while training-free SIMIT-ICL achieves +6.98%.

</details>


### [31] [Auditing GeoFM Evaluation for Field-Extent Segmentation: Label Proxies, Baselines, and When Frozen Features Match Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00054)
*Santosh Pant*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文用同一批单日期Sentinel-2像素，量化标签代理与基线选择如何影响GeoFM在农田边界分割上的评测结论，发现ESA WorldCover代理会显著抬高AUROC，而在真实耕地标签上从零训练的U-Net在多数区域优于Prithvi冻结线性探针；冻结解码器与全量微调在ε=0.02下大多TOST等价，肯尼亚为共同失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GeoFM评测在农田边界分割任务中普遍使用耕地覆盖代理标签（cropland proxy）并以逐像素光谱基线作对比，这些做法可能导致对模型排序的误判，因此需系统审计标签与基线选择对结论的影响。

Method: 在六个国家使用完全相同的单日期Sentinel-2像素；对比多边形字段标签与ESA WorldCover耕地代理标签；比较随机森林、从零训练U-Net、Prithvi冻结线性探针；在相同解码器下对比Prithvi与TerraMind的冻结骨干与全量微调（十个匹配随机种子，TOST等价检验，ε=0.02 AUROC）；并构建30组跨区域迁移矩阵，辅以AUROC与AP评价。

Result: 改用WorldCover代理后随机森林AUROC由0.55–0.82升至0.79–0.96；真实标签上U-Net达0.89–0.98 AUROC，在6个区域中5个高于Prithvi冻结线性探针；12个区域×模型单元中9个TOST等价，肯尼亚为共同失败案例（正样本约0.1%像素），全量微调更优；U-Net在完整30组迁移矩阵上领先；排除肯尼亚的探索性分析中冻结解码器优于微调版本，冻结TerraMind与U-Net无AUROC差异；Prithvi冻结解码器参数量约为全量微调的1/110。

Conclusion: 解读GeoFM排名前必须审计标签与基线，并应先测试冻结解码器再假定全量微调有益；结论限于单日期、逐像素田地范围分割，不适用于地块边界或实例勾绘。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation-model (GeoFM) benchmarks for agricultural field-extent segmentation often rely on cropland land-cover proxies and compare against per-pixel spectral baselines. We measure how these choices affect conclusions using identical single-date Sentinel-2 pixels from six countries. Replacing polygon-derived field labels with ESA WorldCover cropland raises random-forest AUROC from 0.55-0.82 to 0.79-0.96, showing that the proxy creates a more spectrally separable task. On the actual field-extent labels, a from-scratch U-Net reaches 0.89-0.98 AUROC and has higher point AUROC than a Prithvi frozen linear probe in five of six regions. We then compare a frozen backbone and full fine-tuning using the same decoder for Prithvi and TerraMind, with ten matched seeds. At $\varepsilon=0.02$ AUROC, 9 of 12 region$\times$model cells are TOST-equivalent. Kenya is the common failure case: positives cover roughly 0.1% of pixels, and full fine-tuning performs better for both models. Cross-region results are less favorable to frozen models. On AUROC and average precision (AP), the U-Net leads the full 30-transfer matrix, while an exploratory analysis excluding Kenya finds that both frozen decoders outperform their fine-tuned counterparts; no AUROC difference is detected between frozen TerraMind and the U-Net in that subset. For Prithvi, a frozen decoder trains about $110\times$ fewer parameters than full fine-tuning. These results argue for auditing labels and baselines before interpreting GeoFM rankings, and for testing a frozen decoder before assuming that full fine-tuning will help. Our conclusions are limited to single-date, per-pixel field-extent segmentation, not parcel-boundary or instance delineation.

</details>


### [32] [From Clips to Streams: A Unified Framework for Streaming Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00055)
*Hoang Quan Dang,Xin Shen,Yanbin Liu,Xin Yu,Ling Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文正式定义流式手语翻译（Streaming SLT）任务，并提出首个端到端框架 StreamSLST，通过并行解码联合完成连续视频中的手语分割与翻译，在真实与合成流式基准上稳定超越级联方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有手语翻译（SLT）研究多聚焦于预先切分好的片段，与真实交流中连续不断的手语流相脱节。将问题从片段扩展到流需要同时发现并翻译语言事件，而传统"先分割再翻译"的级联流程存在误差传播问题，因此需要端到端方案与相应评测基准。

Method: 提出 StreamSLST：基于可变形 Transformer（Deformable Transformer）的端到端框架，采用并行解码策略在单次前向中联合优化时间分割与翻译，避免级联误差传播；引入句子感知滑动窗口采样器（Sentence-aware Sliding Window Sampler）将连续流裁剪为可训练单元以支持长视频可扩展训练；并设计三模态视觉-语言预训练（Tri-modal Visual-Language Pretraining）阶段，在联合优化前对齐姿态动态与文本语义。

Result: 在 BOBSL、How2Sign 两个真实流式数据集以及新建的合成基准 Streaming-Phoenix-2014T、Streaming-CSL-Daily 上评估，StreamSLST 一致优于参数匹配的"分割后翻译"级联流程；分析表明联合优化学到的时序支撑区间更宽且对翻译更有效，简单的跨度扩展或重缩放无法复现。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了连续手语理解的首个稳健基准，证明了端到端联合分割-翻译范式的优势，为现实可及的手语交流接口奠定基础，并公开了代码与数据。

Abstract: Sign Language Translation (SLT) research has typically focused on pre-segmented clips, a constraint that disconnects models from the continuous reality of real-world communication. To bridge the gap from clips to streams, we formally define Streaming SLT, a realistic task demanding the joint discovery and translation of linguistic events from continuous, untrimmed video inputs. To address this challenge, we propose StreamSLST, the first end-to-end framework for Streaming Sign Language Segmentation and Translation via Deformable Transformer architecture. Unlike cascaded approaches that suffer from error propagation, StreamSLST employs a parallel decoding strategy to jointly optimize temporal segmentation and translation in a single pass. To enable scalable training on long-form videos, we incorporate a Sentence-aware Sliding Window Sampler that crops continuous streams into trainable units. We further design a Tri-modal Visual-Language Pretraining stage that aligns pose dynamics with textual semantics before joint optimization. To support comprehensive evaluation, we conduct experiments on two real-world streaming datasets, BOBSL and How2Sign, together with two newly constructed synthetic streaming benchmarks, Streaming-Phoenix-2014T and Streaming-CSL-Daily. The results demonstrate that StreamSLST consistently outperforms a matched cascaded segmentation-then-translation pipeline. Interestingly, our analysis reveals that joint optimization learns wider, translation-effective temporal support that simple expansion or rescaling of the annotated spans fails to reproduce. Our work establishes the first robust benchmark for continuous sign language understanding and paves the way for accessible real-world communication interfaces. The source code and data are available at https://github.com/streamsl/StreamSLST.

</details>


### [33] [SemaNorm: Stain Normalization via Semantic and Morphology Alignment for Robust Cross-Hospital Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00056)
*Subhankar Chattoraj,Sawon Pratiher,Arijit Ghosh,Prashant Khare,Writankar Kundu,Karan Vishwakarma,Preha Handa,Kirti Rashmi,Hubert Konik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自监督染色规范化方法SemaNorm，通过基础模型一致性与细胞核结构损失同时保证语义与形态，跨医院分类OOD准确率提升5.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 染色变异是计算病理中的主要域偏移来源，但现有归一化方法多只优化颜色一致性，未显式保持形态与诊断语义。

Method: SemaNorm采用自监督染色规范化：从强扰动输入重建干净图像块，避免配对迁移、对抗不稳定和恒等坍缩；并结合冻结病理基础模型一致性损失与可微的细胞核结构损失。

Result: 在Camelyon17-WILDS与NCT-CRC-HE-100K上，通过跨医院肿瘤分类、细胞图形态指标及独立基础编码器的语义稳定性评估：OOD准确率较最强归一化基线提升5.9%，较无归一化提升13.5%，形态保持最佳。

Conclusion: 以形态与语义感知为目标进行优化，可作为仅关注颜色的染色归一化的原则性替代方案。

Abstract: Stain variability remains a major source of domain shift in computational pathology, yet existing normalization methods largely optimize color consistency without explicitly preserving morphology or diagnostic semantics. We propose SemaNorm, a self-supervised stain canonicalization stain normalizer to recover clean patches from strongly perturbed inputs, avoiding paired transfer, adversarial instability, and identity collapse. It further combines a frozen pathology foundation-model consistency loss with a differentiable nuclei-structure loss to preserve semantic and cellular organization. SemaNorm is evaluated across Camelyon17-WILDS and NCT-CRC-HE-100K using cross-hospital tumor classification, cell-graph morphology metrics, and semantic stability measured with an independent held-out foundation encoder. SemaNorm improves out-of-distribution accuracy by 5.9% over the strongest normalization baseline and by 13.5% over no normalization, while achieving the best morphology preservation. Independent foundation-model and cell-graph analyses further confirm improved semantic and structural stability. These results establish morphology- and semantics-aware optimization as a principled alternative to color-only stain normalization.

</details>


### [34] [CausalPrune: Counterfactual Causal Token Pruning for Efficient Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00058)
*Pengcheng Zheng,Chaoning Zhang,Ya Wen,Jiaxin Huang,Jiaquan Zhang,Caiyan Qin,Kuien Liu,Sheng Zheng,Pietro Liò,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出因果剪枝框架CausalPrune，用反事实因果估计视觉token重要性，实现高效LVLM推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs中过多视觉token带来巨大计算开销，现有token剪枝方法仅基于相关性统计估计重要性，无法区分真正因果决定模型输出的token与伪相关的token。

Method: 将LVLM推理建模为结构因果模型(SCM)，提出因果token重要性分数(CTIS)，通过干预每个视觉token估计平均处理效应(ATE)；推导CTIS的高效一阶近似，并引入后门调整以缓解潜在跨模态混杂；进一步用逐层因果中介分解，将token重要性拆分为经中间隐状态的直接效应与间接效应，实现跨层渐进剪枝。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5、Qwen2.5-VL等多种LVLM架构上，保持相当甚至更优的多模态性能，同时显著降低FLOPs和prefill延迟，持续优于现有剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 以反事实因果视角替代相关性估计进行视觉token剪枝，为LVLM高效推理提供了有理论依据且实用的方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in multimodal understanding, but their practical deployment is still limited by the substantial computation introduced by excessive visual tokens. Visual token pruning offers an effective solution by removing redundant visual tokens during inference. However, existing methods typically estimate token importance from correlational statistics, which cannot distinguish tokens that causally determine the model output from those spuriously associated with it. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{CausalPrune}, a counterfactual causal token pruning framework for efficient LVLMs. We formulate LVLM inference as a Structural Causal Model and introduce the Causal Token Importance Score (CTIS), which estimates the Average Treatment Effect of intervening on each visual token. To make causal estimation practical, we derive an efficient first-order approximation of CTIS and incorporate backdoor adjustment to mitigate latent cross-modal confounding. Within this causal formulation, layer-wise causal mediation decomposes token importance into direct and indirect effects through intermediate hidden states, enabling progressive pruning across transformer layers in a principled manner. Extensive experiments on various LVLM architectures, such as LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen2.5-VL, demonstrate that CausalPrune maintains comparable or even superior multimodal performance while achieving substantial reductions in FLOPs and prefill latency, consistently outperforming existing pruning methods.

</details>


### [35] [Not All Minorities Are Equal: Fairness Outcomes in Private Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00059)
*Cohen Archbold,Usman Hassan,Sen-ching Samson Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV
