TL;DR: 研究差分隐私（DP）训练对生成式人脸模型在人口群体与视觉属性表征上的公平性影响，发现更强隐私保护可能不成比例地损害部分少数群体的质量与效用，且该效应无法仅由数据表征比例解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DP 为 AI 系统中的个体隐私提供形式化保障，但其对生成模型公平性的影响尚不清楚；作者希望厘清隐私保护训练如何改变人脸生成模型中各人口群体与视觉属性的表征。

Method: 在两个人脸图像数据集上，采用多个不同隐私预算（隐私强度）水平进行生成模型训练，系统比较不同人口群体与视觉属性在生成质量与效用上的变化，并检验数据表征比例与退化程度之间的关系。

Result: 更强的隐私约束会不成比例地降低部分代表性不足群体的生成质量与效用，表明隐私可能放大既有差异；但表征比例并不能解释全部效应——若干高代表性属性同样出现显著退化，而部分低代表性属性反而较为稳健。

Conclusion: 隐私保护学习的影响不能仅由数据表征比例解释，可能取决于所学信息的特性及其交互作用；评估生成模型时需同时考虑隐私与公平性。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) provides formal guarantees for protecting individuals in artificial intelligence systems, but its consequences for fairness in generative models remain poorly understood. We investigate how privacy-preserving training affects the representation of demographic groups and visual attributes in generative models of human faces. Across two facial image datasets and multiple privacy levels, we find that stronger privacy can disproportionately degrade the quality and utility of some underrepresented groups, suggesting that privacy can amplify existing disparities. However, representation alone does not explain these effects: several well-represented attributes also experience substantial degradation, while some underrepresented attributes remain comparatively robust. These findings show that the impact of privacy-preserving learning cannot be explained solely by data representation and may depend on the characteristics and interactions of the information being learned. Our results highlight the need to consider both privacy and fairness when evaluating generative models.

</details>


### [36] [DDEL: Closed-Loop Mutual Enhancement of Dataset Distillation and Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00061)
*Weiqi Zhang,Yongquan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DDEL框架，将集成学习的行为反馈到数据集蒸馏过程中，形成闭环迭代，并在三个医学影像任务上给出初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流程中数据集蒸馏（DD）与集成学习（EL）被顺序组合：蒸馏出的数据集合固定不变，集成模型仅在此基础上学习，二者缺乏交互；作者希望让集成模型的训练行为反过来影响蒸馏过程。

Method: DDEL闭环框架：每一轮先训练集成模型，在源数据上度量损失与预测不一致性（disagreement），用这些信号指导下一轮的数据集更新；具体实现采用分布匹配（distribution matching）作为蒸馏方法与同质三成员集成（homogeneous three-member ensemble）。

Result: 在ISIC 2019皮肤病变分类、I-SPY1治疗响应预测、NSCLC-Radiomics两年预后二分类三项任务上，在与开环基线相同的每类图像预算下，DDEL分别达到平衡准确率0.3471、平均ROC-AUC 0.5120与0.5597；I-SPY1上的差异较小，且ISIC的训练轨迹显示增加轮数并不稳定带来提升。

Conclusion: 结果为DD-EL反馈这一研究方向提供了初步证据，但方法的计算开销与鲁棒性仍需受控评估。

Abstract: Dataset distillation (DD) reduces a training set to a small set of examples, while ensemble learning (EL) combines predictions from several models. When applied in sequence, the distilled set remains fixed as the ensemble learns. We introduce DDEL, a framework that feeds ensemble behavior back into distillation. Each round trains the ensemble, measures loss and prediction disagreement on the source data, and uses these signals to guide the next data update. We implement this loop with distribution matching and a homogeneous three-member ensemble.
  Preliminary experiments cover skin lesion classification (ISIC 2019), treatment-response prediction (I-SPY1), and binary two-year prognosis classification (NSCLC-Radiomics). With the same images-per-class budget as the open-loop comparison, DDEL reaches balanced accuracy 0.3471 on ISIC and mean ROC-AUC 0.5120 and 0.5597 on I-SPY1 and NSCLC. The I-SPY1 difference is small, and the ISIC trajectory shows that more rounds do not consistently improve performance. These results offer initial evidence for studying DD-EL feedback, with its computational cost and robustness still requiring controlled evaluation.

</details>


### [37] [CarDiff: Anatomically Aware Dental Caries Synthesis using Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00062)
*Sanyam Jain,Bruna Neves de Freitas,Alexandros Iosifidis,Ruben Pauwels*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CarDiff是一个用于全景牙科X光片中龋齿合成的半监督框架，结合配对掩膜-图像学习与增强掩膜自监督生成，引入解剖感知条件与语义一致性约束，在MLUA-DC1000上取得最佳FID(90.76)与LPIPS(0.48)，并作为分割增广使平均Dice提升最高6.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙科影像标注数据有限，且龋齿类别（尤其少数类）样本不足，亟需可控的合成数据增广手段。

Method: 提出半监督龋齿合成框架CarDiff：1) 配对掩膜-图像学习与基于增强掩膜的自监督生成相结合；2) 在病灶区域与周围结构间引入解剖感知条件（anatomy-aware conditioning）；3) 通过分割引导监督与对抗式解剖对齐实现语义一致性约束。

Result: 在MLUA-DC1000数据集上，CarDiff在对比方法中取得最优FID(90.76)与LPIPS(0.48)；在七种分割架构的下游增广评测中，采用5倍增广训练集使平均Dice最高提升6.5%，中等及组合病灶类别增益更明显。

Conclusion: 解剖一致的扩散合成在有限监督条件下对牙科X光片增广具有实用价值。

Abstract: Synthetic medical image generation can help address limited annotated data in dental imaging, particularly for under-represented caries classes. In this work, we present CarDiff, a semi-supervised framework for dental caries synthesis in panoramic radiographs. The method combines paired mask-image learning with self-supervised generation from augmented masks, while incorporating anatomy-aware conditioning between lesion regions and surrounding structures. Semantic consistency is enforced through segmentation-guided supervision and adversarial anatomical alignment. Experiments on the MLUA-DC1000 dataset show that CarDiff achieves improved synthesis quality compared with existing semantic synthesis baselines, obtaining the best Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) (90.76) and Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity (LPIPS) (0.48) scores among evaluated methods. We further assess the generated radiographs for downstream segmentation augmentation across seven architectures. Using a 5x augmented training set improves average Dice score by up to 6.5%, with larger gains observed for medium and combined lesion classes. These results suggest that anatomically consistent diffusion synthesis may be useful for dental radiograph augmentation under limited supervision. The code is available at https://github.com/s4nyam/CarDiff.

</details>


### [38] [Where Generated Floor Plans Stop: Encoding Practice Acceptance Rules to Evaluate Machine Learning Floor Plan Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00063)
*Yiliang Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将某建筑事务所平面布局软件中的验收规则编码为六项固定顺序的检查，用其对四个已发表的平面生成器及RPLAN、MSD两个建成语料库进行评估，发现以邻接度衡量的主流指标与实际可建造/可验收性存在显著背离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平面生成器的评价主要依据生成房间邻接关系与请求的匹配度（如Compatibility），这一度量缺乏建筑学知识，无法反映真实设计实践中的可接受标准，因此需要引入来自实际生产软件的验收规则来检验生成平面。

Method: 将某建筑事务所生产用布局软件中的平面验收规则编码为六项按固定顺序执行的检查，平面在首个未通过的检查处被报告；对两条无建筑规范规定的房间形状限值进行校准，使每条限值单独拒绝5%的建成公寓。另制定迁移规范，记录哪些检查可由公开数据承载。随后将检查器应用于四个已发表生成器和RPLAN、Modified Swiss Dwellings（MSD）两个建成语料库，并设置三项测量控制以验证结论稳健性。

Result: 生成器在不同检查处被拦截。仅房间比例检查一项即拒绝33.7%的House-GAN++平面（限于检查器可读取并可建造者），而RPLAN仅为5.0%，该差距在三项测量控制下依然存在。在5%误拒率下，MSD公寓通过全部六项检查的比例比House-GAN++高5.5至23.5个百分点（取决于计数口径）。在Compatibility邻接度量上得分最高的两个生成器通过率最低，原因在于其平面表示方式；而在通过率最高的两个生成器内部，该邻接度量几乎无法预测哪些平面能通过。

Conclusion: 基于验收规则的检查器揭示了标准邻接度量所遗漏的问题，即邻接匹配度与实际建筑可接受性之间存在脱节；检查器已开源发布。但论文明确声明，通过检查并不等同于实践中被业主或事务所真正接受。

Abstract: Machine learning floor plan generators are proposed as aids to design decisions, yet they are judged mainly by how closely a generated plan's room adjacencies match the request, a measure without architectural knowledge. We encode an architecture practice's floor plan acceptance rules, taken from its production layout software, as six checks applied in a fixed order, report each plan at the first check it fails, and calibrate the two room-shape limits, which no building code states, so that each alone rejects 5 per cent of built apartments. A transfer specification records which checks public data can carry. Applied to four published generators and two built corpora, RPLAN and Modified Swiss Dwellings (MSD), the checks stop the generators at different points. A room proportion check alone refuses 33.7 per cent of the House-GAN++ plans the checker can read and build, against 5.0 per cent of RPLAN, a gap that survives three measurement controls. At the 5 per cent rate, MSD apartments pass all six checks 5.5 to 23.5 points more often than House-GAN++, depending on the plans counted. The two generators that score best on the adjacency measure, Compatibility, pass least, because of how they represent plans, and within the two that pass most it barely predicts which plans pass. The checks thus expose what the standard measure misses; the checker is released. Passing is not shown to mean acceptance in practice.

</details>


### [39] [OpenMidnight: Closing the Performance Gap Between Open and Proprietary Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00064)
*Ratna Sagari Grandhi,Anis Chihoub,Ryan Kim,Daniel Z. Kaplan,Chao Feng,Anish Dulal,Leema Krishna Murali,Rohitkumar Datchanamourty,Nevasini Sasikumar,Debojyoti Das,Ahmed Hassayoune,Rhizo Nymph,Anas Zafar,Connor Lane,Benjamin Warner,Hassan Muhammad,Tanishq Mathew Abraham,Paul S. Scotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenMidnight 1.1 是 Midnight 的开源复现与改进版本，基于TCGA约1.2万张公开全切片图像训练，仅需约320 H100 GPU小时（较Midnight降低34倍），在THUNDER基准上平均分割Dice达69.7（最高），在14个模型中排名第四并超过Midnight，同时公开代码、预处理流程与模型权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理基础模型依赖专有数据、算力开销大，且训练代码与预处理流程不公开，导致难以独立验证与后续方法开发；作者希望通过开源复现与精简，降低复现门槛并验证在有限算力与公开数据下能否得到有竞争力的模型。

Method: 以DINOv2预训练权重初始化编码器，在TCGA约12,000张公开全切片图像上做病理预训练，总耗时约320 H100 GPU小时；在THUNDER的16个分类与4个分割数据集上评测，并开展消融实验，分析初始化方式、数据规模、模型尺寸与组织构成对下游性能的影响。

Result: 平均分割Dice为69.7，为所有模型中最高；在14个模型中综合排名第四，优于Midnight；在部分任务上达到或超过用更大数据集训练的模型。消融实验给出初始化、数据量、模型规模与组织成分对下游性能的定量影响。

Conclusion: 在公开数据与大幅缩减算力的条件下仍可获得有竞争力的病理基础模型，说明预训练初始化与训练配方设计至关重要；开源训练代码、预处理流水线与权重有助于可复现研究及后续模型开发。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models offer transferable representations for a range of downstream tasks in computational pathology, but reliance on proprietary data, substantial compute requirements, and limited availability of training code and preprocessing pipelines hinder independent validation and further methodological development. We present OpenMidnight 1.1, an open-source replication and refinement of Midnight trained on approximately 12,000 publicly available whole-slide images from The Cancer Genome Atlas. Using an encoder initialized with pretrained DINOv2 weights, OpenMidnight 1.1 uses approximately 320 H100 GPU-hours for pathology pretraining, a 34-fold reduction relative to the compute reported for Midnight. Across 16 classification and four segmentation datasets in THUNDER, OpenMidnight 1.1 achieves the highest mean segmentation Dice (69.7) and ranks fourth among 14 models, ahead of Midnight, with performance that matches or exceeds models trained on substantially larger datasets in several tasks. Our ablation studies additionally examine how initialization, data volume, model size, and tissue composition affect downstream performance, providing empirical evidence to inform the development of future training recipes. Our release of the training code, preprocessing pipeline, and model weights lowers barriers to reproducible research and further development of competitive pathology foundation models.

</details>


### [40] [BreastXAgent: A lightweight agentic explainability framework for breast density classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00065)
*Muhammad Turab,Raja Vavekanand,Knooz Fatima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BreastXAgent 是一个轻量级智能体框架，将预训练乳腺密度分类器、Grad-CAM 视觉解释、LangGraph 路由、LLM 报告生成与基于规则的护栏结合，生成受控、忠实的非诊断性报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习乳腺密度分类模型通常只输出标签与置信度，缺乏可解释性；直接让 LLM 生成解释则容易出现对 Grad-CAM 证据和临床结论的过度断言（overclaim），存在安全风险。

Method: 框架由五部分组成：冻结的预训练分类器、Grad-CAM 可视化解释、LangGraph 路由控制流程、LLM 报告生成、基于规则的护栏（guardrails）。对比实验包含无约束 LLM 报告、严格提示词、严格提示+LangGraph 路由以及完整 BreastXAgent 等配置，在 160 张乳腺摄影图像上评估安全性通过率、过度断言数量与效用得分。

Result: 无约束 LLM 报告产生 75% 的 Grad-CAM 过度断言与 62.5% 的临床过度断言；严格提示与 LangGraph 路由提升了安全性但未完全消除违规；完整 BreastXAgent 达到 100% 安全通过率、零过度断言，并获得最高效用分数。

Conclusion: 智能体式护栏机制可在不修改底层分类器的前提下，实现安全、结构化的解释生成，为医学影像模型的可解释输出提供可控方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models for breast density classification typically return only a label and confidence score. This paper presents BreastXAgent, a lightweight agentic framework that combines a pretrained classifier, Grad-CAM visual explanation, LangGraph-based routing, LLM report generation, and rule-based guardrails to produce controlled, faithful, non-diagnostic reports. On 160 mammography images, uncontrolled LLM reporting produced 75% Grad-CAM overclaims and 62.5% clinical overclaims. Strict prompting and LangGraph routing improved safety but did not eliminate violations. The full BreastXAgent achieved 100% safety pass rate, zero overclaims, and highest utility score. Results demonstrate that agentic guardrails enable safe, structured explanation generation without modifying the underlying classifier.

</details>


### [41] [Comparing Monocular and Multi-View 3D Pedestrian Reconstruction Strategies for Urban Intersection Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00066)
*Kauan Mariani,Matheus Carvalho,Joel Perca,João Rulff,Jorge Poco*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 比较两类从多相机路口数据获取行人运动描述符（3D姿态、度量轨迹、身高与步速）的策略：单目学习式流水线与标定多视角几何流水线，并在无3D真值条件下用无参考指标评估其权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市路口行人运动分析对交通安全评估、出行规划与智慧城市感知至关重要，需依赖物理可信的运动描述。但固定路侧相机存在斜视远距离视角、频繁遮挡，且通常缺少3D姿态真值与跨相机身份标注，因此需要明确不同重建策略的适用性。

Method: 在同一多相机路口数据集上对比两条流水线：(1) 单目学习式流水线，结合文本提示分割、跟踪与学习式3D人体估计；(2) 标定多视角几何流水线，结合2D姿态估计、跟踪、跨相机关联与三角化。因缺少3D真值，采用面向路口分析的无参考评价准则：行人覆盖率、人体测量学合理性、步速分布、计算开销与失效模式。

Result: 两类策略呈现互补权衡：学习式流水线部署更简单、覆盖行人更多，但存在尺度歧义与系统性身高压缩；几何流水线能以更低代价获得度量重建，但依赖标定、会丢弃仅被单相机观测到的行人，且在密集人群中可能产生严重离群值。

Conclusion: 归纳出面向目标分析任务选择重建策略、并按任务需求配置相机基础设施的实用指南。

Abstract: Understanding how pedestrians move and interact at urban intersections is central to traffic-safety assessment, mobility planning, and smart-city sensing. Such analyses rely on physically grounded descriptions of motion: 3D body pose, metric trajectories, and pose-derived descriptors such as stature and walking speed. Recovering these from fixed roadside cameras is difficult: viewpoints are oblique and long-range, occlusions are frequent, and neither ground-truth 3D pose nor cross-camera identity labels are typically available. We compare two strategies for deriving pedestrian motion descriptors from a multi-camera intersection dataset: a monocular learned pipeline that combines text-prompted segmentation, tracking, and learned 3D body estimation, and a calibrated multi-view geometric pipeline that combines 2D pose estimation, tracking, cross-camera association, and triangulation. Because 3D ground truth is unavailable, we evaluate both with reference-free criteria relevant to intersection analysis: pedestrian coverage, anthropometric plausibility, walking-speed distributions, computational cost, and failure modes. The two strategies exhibit complementary trade-offs. The learned pipeline is simpler to deploy and covers more pedestrians, but suffers from scale ambiguity and systematic height compression; the geometric pipeline yields metric reconstructions more efficiently, but requires calibration, discards pedestrians seen by only one camera, and can produce severe outliers in dense crowds. We distill these findings into practical guidance for selecting a reconstruction strategy and configuring camera infrastructure according to the target analysis task.

</details>


### [42] [MedICL: Modality-Anatomy-Aware Visual In-Context Learning for Medical Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00067)
*Ji-jun Park,Soo-joon Choi,Seungmin Han,Haeun Kwon,Jiwon Jeong,Taeyang Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedICL：一种免训练的医学大视觉语言模型自适应方法，通过"策展"示例而非重训练模型来实现视觉上下文学习，在三个医学VQA数据集上显著超越零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学LVLM的免训练适配是部署刚需，但视觉ICL会把模态与风格不匹配的示例注入医学语境，使冻结模型学到错误的作答风格。

Method: 三个组件：MADS选择模态-解剖对齐且覆盖互补解读维度的示例；W2C用弱检索信号策展，并以强验证投票校验示例本身（而非模型自身输出）；ECC用W锚定的示例集作答，按异常感知签名聚合，冲突时重新作答一次。

Result: 在LLaVA-Med零样本协议下，VQA-RAD、SLAKE、PathVQA上开放式问题提升+11~+14点、封闭式提升+7~+14点；零训练成本下恢复VQA-RAD微调开放式性能的71%；对偏移或损坏的示例库保持稳健。

Conclusion: 示例质量（策展）是医学视觉ICL的关键瓶颈，精心筛选演示即可在无训练成本下大幅逼近微调性能并具备鲁棒性。

Abstract: Training-free adaptation of medical vision-language models (LVLMs) is a deployment requirement, yet visual in-context learning (ICL) injects modality- and style-mismatched demonstrations into medical contexts, teaching a frozen model the wrong answering style. We present MedICL, which curates demonstrations instead of retraining the model. MADS selects modality-anatomy aligned demonstrations that cover complementary interpretation dimensions; W2C curates them with weak retrieval signals and a strong verification vote that checks demonstrations rather than the model's own outputs; ECC answers with W anchored demonstration sets, aggregates by abnormality-aware signatures, and re-answers once on conflict. On VQA-RAD, SLAKE, and PathVQA under the LLaVA-Med zero-shot protocol, MedICL improves the strongest zero-shot baseline by +11 - +14 points on open-ended and +7 - +14 on closed questions, recovers 71% of fine-tuned open-ended performance on VQA-RAD at zero training cost, and stays robust to shifted or corrupted demonstration banks.

</details>


### [43] [SAMP: Self-Adaptive Margin Prioritization For Robust Diffusion Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00069)
*Yuhao Xue,Jiuan Zhou,Yu Cheng,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAMP是一个自适应扩散模型水印框架，通过全局边界调节动态调整嵌入强度、通过脆弱性敏感分配提高易损水印维度的优化优先级，兼顾鲁棒性与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型降低了高保真伪造图像的门槛，内容溯源变难。现有水印方法在去噪全程使用固定嵌入强度，或对所有水印维度一视同仁，导致部分维度已可靠时仍引入不必要失真，而脆弱维度又得不到足够强化。因此需要同时自适应调整去噪过程中的嵌入强度与各水印维度的优化优先级。

Method: 提出SAMP框架，包含两部分：全局边界调节（global margin regulation）依据目标码字与竞争码字之间的间隔，并结合常见攻击影响的估计来调整嵌入强度；脆弱性敏感分配（vulnerability-sensitive allocation）识别易被局部扰动削弱的水印维度，并赋予其更高的优化优先级。

Result: 平均信息恢复准确率达0.993；相比SOTA方法，PSNR提升16.8%，LPIPS降低47.1%。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了SAMP在实现可靠且高质量扩散模型水印方面的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have made high-fidelity visual generation widely accessible, while also lowering the barrier to producing convincing visual forgeries and complicating content provenance. Existing watermarking methods typically use a fixed embedding strength throughout denoising or treat all watermark dimensions equally. As a result, they may introduce unnecessary distortion when some watermark dimensions are already reliable, while failing to sufficiently reinforce those that are more vulnerable to subsequent perturbations. Therefore, robust watermarking requires adapting both the embedding strength during denoising and the optimization priority across watermark dimensions. To address this problem, we present SAMP, a self-adaptive framework consisting of global margin regulation and vulnerability-sensitive allocation. Global margin regulation adjusts the embedding strength according to the separation between the target codeword and competing codewords, together with the estimated effects of common attacks. Vulnerability-sensitive allocation identifies watermark dimensions that are more easily weakened by local perturbations and gives them higher optimization priority. Experimental results demonstrate that SAMP achieves an average information recovery accuracy of 0.993. Compared with state-of-the-art methods, SAMP improves PSNR by 16.8% and reduces LPIPS by 47.1%. These results validate the effectiveness of SAMP for reliable and high-quality diffusion watermarking.

</details>


### [44] [How Reliable Are Object Detection Models Beyond Clear Weather? A Deployment-Oriented Robustness Study for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00071)
*Munshaibur Rahman Mahin,Isha Das,Most. Sonia Islam,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,M. F. Mridha,Md. Jakir Hossen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在统一源域评估协议下，对轻量/中尺度单阶段、两阶段与Transformer检测器在雾、雨、雪、夜间条件下的跨天气鲁棒性进行评测，并用性能下降率、天气鲁棒性指数（WRI）与ECE校准误差刻画退化程度与置信度可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知需在非晴昼环境下保持可靠检测，但现代检测器在仅源域、无适应条件下的跨天气鲁棒性缺乏系统刻画，现有评测缺少统一协议与兼具性能-校准双维度的度量。

Method: 仅在晴昼驾驶场景训练，在未见雾、雨、雪、夜间条件下无适应测试；覆盖轻量与中尺度单阶段、两阶段及Transformer检测器，统一协议下以Performance Drop(%)、WRI及车/公交/卡车/行人等类级敏感性分析评估，并以ECE度量域偏移下的置信度校准。

Result: 恶劣条件性能一致下降，夜间退化最高达62.90%；公交类对环境变化最敏感，车辆相对稳定；YOLO类检测器校准失配最低，DETR鲁棒性最差（WRI=0.6859）。

Conclusion: 检测器跨天气鲁棒性与置信度可靠性存在显著架构差异，需将类级敏感性与校准误差纳入安全关键智能车辆感知的部署评估。

Abstract: Autonomous driving perception systems must maintain reliable object detection under diverse environmental conditions, not only in clear daytime scenes. However, the cross-weather robustness of modern detectors remains insufficiently characterised under source-only evaluation. This study evaluates representative object detection architectures trained exclusively on clear daytime driving scenes and tested without adaptation under unseen fog, rain, snow, and night conditions. The evaluation covers lightweight and medium-scale one-stage, two-stage, and transformer-based detectors within a unified protocol. Robustness is assessed using performance Drop (%), a Weather Robustness Index (WRI), and class-level sensitivity analysis for critical road participants, including cars, buses, trucks, and persons. Prediction confidence reliability under environmental domain shift is measured using Expected Calibration Error (ECE). Results reveal consistent performance degradation in adverse conditions, reaching 62.90% at night. Buses show the highest sensitivity to environmental variability, while cars remain comparatively stable across weather transitions. Comparisons indicate that YOLO-based detectors exhibit the lowest calibration mismatch, reflecting stronger agreement between predicted confidence and empirical detection correctness. In contrast, DETR achieves the lowest robustness score, with a WRI of 0.6859, indicating greater vulnerability to cross-weather domain shift. These findings offer deployment-oriented insights into detector robustness and confidence reliability for safety-critical intelligent vehicle perception systems.

</details>


### [45] [CRaFT-XDet: Causal and Robust Fusion Transformer for Dual-Energy X-ray Prohibited Item Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00072)
*Moinul Hossain,Munshaibur Rahman Mahin,Taskin Khaleque,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,M. F. Mridha,Md. Jakir Hossen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出因果建模与物理先验结合的X射线违禁品检测框架CRaFT-XDet，将衰减驱动的模态变化下检测重构为不变表征学习，在SIXray上达0.9389 mAP@0.5，并具跨扫描仪泛化与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线安检图像存在严重目标重叠、扫描仪相关的域偏移，以及杂乱行李构成带来的伪相关；现有检测器依赖外观驱动监督，鲁棒性与跨扫描仪泛化受限。

Method: 提出扫描仪不变因果检测形式化（定理1），证明在划分扫描仪环境下最小化因果不变性正则（CIR）可近似干预分布P(Y|do(Xobj))；框架包含（i）因果特征解耦抑制上下文捷径，（ii）物理motivated正则约束材质一致嵌入，（iii）基于Transformer的跨能量交互建模；模型29.2M参数、89.5 GFLOPs、345 FPS（1.7ms）。

Result: SIXray五折交叉验证达0.9389 mAP@0.5、0.8021 mAP@0.5:0.95；无域自适应下在OPIXray、HiXray保持较强跨数据集性能；类别无关二分类威胁检测召回率93.92%、平均IoU 0.9031。

Conclusion: 因果不变学习与物理先验可有效提升双能X射线违禁品检测的鲁棒性、跨扫描仪泛化与安全关键场景可靠性，同时满足实时部署需求。

Abstract: Automated prohibited item detection in X-ray security imagery is fundamentally constrained by severe object overlap, scanner-dependent domain shifts, and spurious correlations induced by cluttered baggage composition. Existing detectors largely rely on appearance-driven supervision, limiting their robustness and cross-scanner generalisation. We introduce CRaFT-XDet, a causality-grounded and physics- motivated detection framework that reformulates X-ray threat detection as invariant representation learning under attenuation-driven modality variation. Our approach is grounded in a Scanner-Invariant Causal Detection formalisation (Theorem 1), which establishes that minimising the proposed Causal Invariance Regularisation (CIR) objective across partitioned scanner environments approximates the interventional distribution P (Y | do(Xobj)) under standard identifiability assumptions, providing a principled causal formulation for dual-energy X-ray detection. CRaFT-XDet integrates: (i) causal feature disentanglement to suppress context-induced shortcuts; (ii) physics-motivated regularisation to enforce material-consistent embeddings; and (iii) transformer-based cross-energy interaction modelling for structured reasoning in cluttered scenes. With 29.2 M parameters, 89.5 GFLOPs, and 345 FPS (1.7 ms inference), CRaFT-XDet is real-time deployable. Trained on SIXray with five-fold cross-validation, the framework achieves 0.9389 mAP@0.5 and 0.8021 mAP@0.5:0.95. Without domain adaptation, CRaFT-XDet maintains strong cross- dataset performance on OPIXray and HiXray, indicating robust generalization across heterogeneous scanner distributions. In category-agnostic binary threat detection, the model attains 93.92% recall and 0.9031 mean IoU, highlighting reliability in safety-critical screening where missed detections are costly.

</details>


### [46] [When frequency domain classification is superior to spatial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00073)
*Yanir Harel,Shalom Rosner,Ronit D. Gross,Tal Halevi,Shilo Glam,Ella Koresh,Ido Kanter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文论证频域分类精度下降的根源在于缺乏局部特征，并据此构建频域+空域混合模型以提升分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像分类模型主要依赖局部特征与特征提取进行预测，在空域表现良好，但在频域表现不佳，尽管两种表示包含相同信息。作者希望解释这一性能差距的成因。

Method: 通过频域局部数据交换实验（与逆变换后空域精度对比）以及使用含全局特征的数据集进行验证；在此基础上构建混合模型，将仅在频域训练的模型与仅在空域训练的模型组合；在ImageNet-100、WikiArt、DTD、FMD、PSVRT、KTH-TIPS2-b上训练并评估VGG与ViT架构。

Result: 频域局部交换数据显著降低其逆变换空域精度，甚至低于直接频域精度，而频域精度保持相对稳定；包含空域全局特征的数据集在频域取得更高精度；混合模型获得更高分类准确率。

Conclusion: 频域精度下降源于局部特征的缺失，而非信息量的差异；结合频域与空域模型的混合策略可有效提升分类性能。

Abstract: Modern image classification models largely rely on local characteristics and feature extraction for their predictions. This focused approach works well in the spatial domain, but reportedly falls short in the frequency domain, even though both representations contain the same information. This study demonstrates that the significant reduction in accuracy in the frequency domain stems from the lack of local features. Locally swapping data in the frequency domain significantly decreases its inverse spatial domain accuracy, even below that of the frequency domain; however, the frequency domain accuracy remains relatively constant. Moreover, the use of datasets containing global features in the spatial domain yields higher accuracy in the frequency domain. Using these results, a hybrid model is constructed, combining one model trained only in the frequency domain and another trained only in the spatial domain to achieve higher classification accuracy. The proposed visual geometry group (VGG) and vision transformer (ViT) architectures are trained on the ImageNet-100, WikiArt, DTD, FMD, PSVRT, and KTH-TIPS2-b datasets and evaluated.

</details>


### [47] [When Morphs Look Better: Face Image Quality as a Training-Free Signal for Morphing Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00074)
*Hao Peng,Fu Bo,Mahmoud Elshirbeny,Guray Ozgur*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估17种FIQA方法在20个morphing攻击集上的MAD性能，主张预先固定质量分数方向而非事后翻转，发现分数分离度大不等于检测性能好，且性能强烈依赖攻击类型与数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FIQA被提出作为无需morph专属训练的MAD信号，但既有工作常在翻转质量度量能降低误差时才翻转，掩盖了morph获得更高质量的失败案例，缺乏对FIQA作为MAD信号的公平、可解释评估。

Method: 在FRLL与FEI两个数据集的20个morphing攻击集（含5个扩散模型攻击集）上评估覆盖所有FIQA类别的17种方法；将每个质量分数直接作MAD决策分数，并依据各方法定义预先固定分数方向，不做事后翻转。

Result: FRLL上DifFIQA(R)平均D-EER为11.42%，与CLIB-FIQA、eDifFIQA(L)一同明显优于其余方法；扩散攻击上多达11/17种方法给morph更高质量，且最大反向分离对应最差检测结果；GraFIQs(L)在DCMorph与Arc2Morph上最佳但在landmark与GAN类攻击上表现差；FEI最低平均D-EER为39.75%，跨数据集阈值不可迁移。

Conclusion: FIQA作MAD信号时，质量分数分离度不能预测检测性能，方向一致性需显式检验；性能高度依赖攻击类型，跨数据集泛化与阈值迁移存在严重局限。

Abstract: Face image quality assessment (FIQA) estimates the utility of a face image for automated face recognition and has been proposed as a morphing attack detection (MAD) signal that requires no morph-specific training. We evaluate 17 FIQA methods from all current FIQA categories on 20 morphing attack sets from two datasets, FRLL and FEI, including five diffusion-based attack sets. Each quality score is used directly as a MAD decision score. Unlike prior work, which inverted a quality measure when this lowered its error, we fix the score direction of each method in advance from its definition, so that cases in which morphs receive higher quality than bona fide samples remain visible. On FRLL, DifFIQA(R) achieves a mean D-EER of 11.42%, and together with CLIB-FIQA and eDifFIQA(L) clearly outperforms all other FIQA methods. A strong separation of bona fide and morph scores does not imply good detection performance: on the diffusion-based attacks, up to 11 of 17 methods assign higher quality to morphs than to bona fide samples, and the largest reversed separation in the study coincides with the worst detection result of the study. Performance also depends strongly on the attack: GraFIQs(L), the best detector of DCMorph and Arc2Morph on FRLL, performs poorly on the landmark-based and GAN-based attacks. On FEI, the lowest mean D-EER is 39.75%, and a threshold set on the bona fide data of one dataset does not transfer to the other.

</details>


### [48] [CRAFT: Corrective and Robust Multi-Agent Framework for Text-to-Parametric CAD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00076)
*Mohammed Musthafa Rafi,Anushrut Jignasu,Mahdi Saraeian,Chinmay Hegde,Aditya Balu,Adarsh Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRAFT是一个无训练的、基于多智能体的"文本→参数化OpenSCAD程序"生成框架，以JSON中间表示贯穿携带符号化参数表达式，结合语义理解、参数规划、编译、渲染、自纠错与多视图组件级验证，在无配对数据条件下产出可编辑的建模程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-CAD方法存在"数据依赖 vs 输出保真"的权衡：监督方法需大规模文本-模型配对数据；零样本提示策略脆弱，且易丢失下游编辑与定制所需的参数化结构。作者希望在不依赖任务特定训练的前提下，同时获得较好的几何质量与更强的可编辑参数结构。

Method: 提出CRAFT多智能体框架，核心为JSON中间表示，在整个生成流程中携带符号化参数表达式，使模型保持参数化、可编辑。流程整合：语义理解、参数化规划、编译、渲染、自我纠正，以及组件级验证，并构成分层恢复机制，利用多视图视觉反馈检测并修复几何错误。全程无任务特定训练。

Result: 在NopSCADlib、ABC、Slice-100K三个基准上，CRAFT在感知质量与几何质量上与直接零样本基线相当（competitive），同时暴露出显著更多的可编辑参数化结构。

Conclusion: 通过符号化参数JSON中间表示加多智能体纠错与视觉验证的分层流程，可在零训练条件下兼顾输出保真与参数化可编辑性，为可访问且可定制的机械CAD生成提供可行路径。

Abstract: Text-to-CAD generation has the potential to make mechanical CAD more accessible, but existing approaches face a trade-off between data dependence and output fidelity. Supervised methods rely on large paired text-model datasets, while zero-shot prompting strategies are often brittle and tend to lose the parametric structure needed for downstream editing and customization. We present CRAFT, a corrective and robust multi-agent framework that produces parametric OpenSCAD programs from natural language without task-specific training. At its core is a JSON intermediate representation that carries symbolic parametric expressions throughout the generation process, so the resulting models remain editable and retain their parametric structure. CRAFT combines semantic understanding, parametric planning, compilation, rendering, self-correction, and component-level verification within a layered recovery process, using multi-view visual feedback to detect and repair geometric errors. Across NopSCADlib, ABC, and Slice-100K, CRAFT is competitive with direct zero-shot baselines in perceptual and geometric quality while exposing substantially more editable parametric structure.

</details>


### [49] [AegisBench: Auditing Aerial Search-and-Rescue Person Detection Under the Conditions It Is Deployed In](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00077)
*Amogh Vinaykumar,Prem Kamasani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AegisBench 用四类灾害、九种物理动机 corruption（各三档severity）在 HERIDAL 与 SARD 上评测 Faster R-CNN、YOLOv11、RT-DETR 共 168 种条件，揭示低光照下 recall 归零的“静默失效”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机人员检测已被应急管理机构采用，但其基准均在平静晴昼采集，而实际部署场景为洪水、野火、风暴与夜间。论文旨在量化这种“基准—任务”间的鲁棒性差距，提供可审计的评测工具。

Method: 构建 AegisBench：基于 4 个灾害族设计 9 种物理动机 corruption，每类 3 个 severity 且单调对应可声明的图像统计量或物理量；应用于 HERIDAL、SARD 两个航空搜救数据集；三种架构迥异的检测器（两阶段 Faster R-CNN、单阶段 YOLOv11、DETR 系 RT-DETR）由统一 evaluator 在 168 种条件下评测，置信阈值在干净验证集上冻结以模拟实际部署；辅以无阈值 mAP、三种训练种子与 300 张真实夜间无人机图像验证，并做温和低光照微调实验。

Result: 鲁棒性高度不均衡。低光照为极端情形：4.5 档曝光损失使三个检测器在两个数据集上 recall 精确为 0，该结论在无阈值 mAP 与三个种子下均成立；在 300 张真实夜间图像上也仅检出 759 人中的 2 人。幸存检测基本仍落在目标上，故失效是静默的——空帧与“系统看不见人”的帧外观相同。温和低光照微调在留出 severity 上仅恢复 0.06–0.19 recall，对其他条件有架构相关的代价，且不迁移到真实夜间图像。空间类 corruption 在两数据集上排序不同，作者无法仅用飞行高度解释，因其参数随帧尺寸而非人员尺寸缩放。

Conclusion: 现有航空人员检测器在灾害与夜间条件下鲁棒性不足且失效静默，晴昼基准严重高估实战能力；AegisBench 作为审计工具公开 corruption 引擎、评测流水线与逐条件结果。

Abstract: Drone-based person detection is being adopted by emergency-management agencies to find missing people faster than ground teams can. The benchmarks behind that adoption were collected in calm, clear daylight, while the missions that trigger deployment happen in floods, wildfires, storms, and at night. AegisBench measures that gap. It applies nine physically motivated corruptions from four disaster families, each at three severities monotone in a declared image statistic or physical quantity, to two aerial search-and-rescue datasets, HERIDAL and SARD. Three architecturally distinct detectors (Faster R-CNN, YOLOv11, RT-DETR) are scored across 168 conditions by one shared evaluator, at confidence thresholds frozen on clean validation data as a fielded system would run. Robustness is highly uneven. Low light is the extreme case: a 4.5-stop exposure loss drives recall to exactly zero for all three detectors on both datasets, a result that holds under threshold-free mAP and across three training seeds, and is mirrored on 300 real night drone images, where 2 of 759 people are found. Surviving detections largely stay on target, so the failure is silent: an empty frame looks the same as one whose occupants the system cannot see. Fine-tuning on milder low-light levels recovers 0.06 to 0.19 recall at the held-out severity, at an architecture-dependent cost on other conditions, and does not transfer to real night imagery. Spatial corruptions rank differently on the two datasets, which we cannot attribute to altitude alone because their parameters scale with frame size rather than person size. We release the corruption engine, evaluation pipeline, and all per-condition results as an audit instrument for anyone evaluating these systems.

</details>


### [50] [From Zero to OCR: A Data-Centric Framework for Unsupported Low-Resource Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00078)
*Faizan Iqbal,Haq Nawaz Malik,Hafsah Pervaiz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个数据为中心的端到端OCR构建框架，仅用无标注文本语料和数字字体生成合成训练数据，在无人支持的低资源文字Kashmiri（波斯-阿拉伯Nastaliq）上实现词级识别0.39% CER，并公开模型、代码与285,864条合成文本行数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多低资源文字缺乏标注文档图像，且通用合成数据难以保留文字特有的视觉结构（如复杂连写），导致现有OCR引擎（六种常用引擎均不支持Kashmiri）无法覆盖这些脚本，需要一种无需人工标注图像的可复用建库流程。

Method: 1) 合成数据引擎：按正确的复杂文字整形规则渲染词、行、页图像并自动派生标签；2) 模型：YOLOv8词级检测器 + 以CTC训练的卷积循环识别器；3) 评估：分阶段门控协议（stage-gate），在引入检测前先隔离识别误差；4) 关键改进来自渲染保真度、保留变音符号的裁剪边距与非极大值抑制参数调优；行级识别因CER过高（41%–79%）转向词级建模。

Result: 词级CER：留出词0.39%、合成页面10.0%、浏览器渲染数字页面13.5%；参考基线：排版书籍页面18.45%、退化历史文档49.8%。三个行级识别器（含一个从阿拉伯语迁移的模型）CER在41%–79%之间停滞。

Conclusion: 仅凭无标注语料与字体即可构建可用OCR系统，渲染保真度与词级建模是低资源复杂文字OCR的关键；论文释放模型、推理代码与285,864张合成Kashmiri文本行图像，并给出可复用的分步流程。

Abstract: Optical character recognition (OCR) remains inaccessible to many low-resource scripts because annotated document images are unavailable and synthetic training data can fail to preserve script-specific visual structure. We present a data-centric framework that builds end-to-end OCR for such scripts from only an unannotated text corpus and digital fonts, without manually annotating a single training image. A synthetic data engine renders word, line and page images with correct complex-script shaping and automatically derived labels; a YOLOv8 word detector and a convolutional recurrent recognizer trained with connectionist temporal classification learn from these images; and a stage-gate evaluation protocol isolates recognition error before detection is introduced. We demonstrate the framework on Kashmiri, a Perso-Arabic Nastaliq script with approximately 6.8 million speakers that none of six widely used OCR engines supports. Three line-level recognizers, including one transferred from Arabic, stalled between 41% and 79% character error rate (CER), motivating a word-level formulation; subsequent improvements came primarily from rendering fidelity, diacritic-preserving crop margins and tuned non-maximum suppression. The resulting system achieves 0.39% CER on held-out words, 10.0% on synthetic pages and 13.5% on browser-rendered digital pages, and establishes reference results of 18.45% on typeset book pages and 49.8% on degraded historical documents. We publicly release the trained models, the inference code and a synthetic dataset of 285,864 Kashmiri text-line images, and we describe a step-by-step procedure for building OCR for other unsupported scripts.

</details>


### [51] [Faithful Where You Look: Anchor-Based Evaluation Hides Text Fabrication in Image-Conditioned Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00079)
*Yujia Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出，现有图像条件视频模型的文本保真度评测只在“锚点”（标注区域）上比对，因而掩盖了大部分失败；作者在周边区域发现大量无对应参考的伪造可读文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像条件视频模型被用于展示真实地点，其输出是否忠实于所给参考地点是一个实际问题。已有文本保真度基准只在锚点（如任务相关的招牌标注区域）上做与参考的比对，作者认为这一评测选择本身隐藏了大部分失败现象。

Method: 以48张东京店面街景参考图为条件，驱动来自3家厂商的4个模型生成视频；分别在标注招牌（锚点）与周边区域（参考中其余文本）测量文本保真度，并与无模型对照基线比较。引入区域级“伪造文本”判定：生成文本区域在参考中无对应物；由两名盲评者评定并报告一致性（kappa）。在两家此前未见过的厂商上做预注册复测，并与VBench-I2V基于参考的一致性指标对比；另在伦敦做拉丁字母预注册复现（两轮）。

Result: 锚点处四个模型均接近无模型对照（锚点文本保真度0.90–0.94，对照0.996），彼此无法可靠区分；周边区域有36–54%的生成文本区域在参考中无对应，对照仅1.6%，尽管所有提示词均禁止新增可读文本。盲评者一致性kappa=0.83–0.96；预注册复测在两家新厂商上分别得到42%与56%；VBench-I2V的参考一致性得分在本样本中未见能追踪该现象。被伪造的文本多为参考中本无文本或文本不可读处的“似可读文本”，此类形式被字符串匹配指标丢弃。伦敦复现第一轮未达标，独立第二轮达标。

Conclusion: 锚点式评测会系统性低估图像条件视频模型的文本忠实度失败；应把周边区域与“无对应参考的新增可读文本”纳入评测，并采用不依赖字符串匹配的判定方式。作者公开代码、标注、逐视频得分、评分与全部预注册材料。

Abstract: Image-conditioned video models are used to show real places, so whether their output stays faithful to the place they were shown is a practical question. The text benchmarks we found that check fidelity against a reference do so at an anchor - an annotated region, typically the sign the task is about. We show that this choice hides most of the failure. We conditioned four models from three vendors on 48 street-level references of Tokyo storefronts and measured text fidelity at the annotated sign and in the periphery, the other text the reference covers. At the anchor, all four models are close to a no-model control (anchor text fidelity 0.90-0.94 against 0.996) and are not reliably separated from one another. In the periphery, 36-54% of generated text regions have no counterpart in the reference, against 1.6% for the control, although every prompt forbade new readable text. Two blinded raters agree on which regions are fabricated (kappa = 0.83-0.96), the result held in preregistered rounds on two vendors whose videos the measure had never seen (42% and 56%), and VBench-I2V's reference-based consistency scores show no evidence of tracking it in our sample. Most fabricated text is legible-looking text where the reference shows none or cannot be read - a form that string-matching metrics discard, which is why our own first measure missed it. A preregistered Latin-script replication in London missed its bar in a first round and met it in an independent second round. We release code, annotations, per-video scores, ratings and every preregistration. Code and data: https://github.com/geoywang/refanchor-bench

</details>


### [52] [Sequential Transfer Learning for Plant Disease Recognition Across Perennial Crop Domains Using MobileNetV2-CBAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00080)
*Ropkathaa Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究验证了经CBAM增强的轻量级MobileNetV2表征可在多年生作物病害识别任务间顺序迁移复用：核桃基线准确率75.76%，加CBAM后84.85%，依次迁移至腰果90.67%、咖啡100.00%、芒果99.50%，在异质六作物基准ABCGMP上取得88.44%准确率、81.49%平衡准确率与0.9949 ROC-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多局限于单一源域到目标域的迁移评估，缺乏对病害识别表征能否在多个不同多年生作物域间"渐进复用"及其随目标域异质性上升而变化的系统刻画。

Method: 采用MobileNetV2嵌入CBAM注意力模块的轻量框架；以核桃为源域，按核桃→腰果→咖啡→芒果顺序做序贯迁移，每阶段复用前一模型表征；最终在异质的ABCGMP六作物基准上验证，各阶段均使用独立测试集与互补分类指标评估。

Result: 核桃基线75.76%→加入CBAM后84.85%→腰果90.67%→咖啡100.00%→芒果99.50%；ABCGMP六作物基准达88.44%准确率、81.49%平衡准确率、0.9949 ROC-AUC。序贯迁移中预测能力基本保持，但多作物异质性引入可测量的性能约束（准确率与平衡准确率差距明显，提示类别不平衡影响）。

Conclusion: 轻量注意力增强的共享表征具备跨多个多年生作物域的序贯知识复用能力，但域异质性扩大时性能存在上限；研究将迁移评估从孤立源-目标对扩展到多域序列，为作物病害识别的可扩展模型复用提供了经验依据。

Abstract: Purpose: This study investigates whether a learned disease-recognition representation can be progressively reused across distinct perennial crop domains and how its predictive behaviour changes as target-domain heterogeneity increases.
  Design/methodology/approach: A lightweight MobileNetV2 framework enhanced with the Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) was evaluated through sequential transfer from Walnut to Cashew, Coffee and Mango, followed by validation on the heterogeneous ABCGMP six-crop benchmark. Each stage used the preceding model representation and was evaluated on an independent test set using complementary classification measures.
  Findings: Test accuracy increased from 75.76% for the Walnut baseline to 84.85% after CBAM integration, followed by 90.67% on Cashew, 100.00% on Coffee and 99.50% on Mango. The final ABCGMP evaluation achieved 88.44% accuracy, 81.49% Balanced Accuracy and 0.9949 ROC-AUC. The results show that substantial predictive capability was retained through successive crop transfers, while broader multi-crop heterogeneity introduced a measurable performance constraint.
  Originality: The study provides an empirical characterisation of sequential knowledge reuse across multiple perennial crop domains using a common lightweight attention-enhanced representation, extending evaluation beyond isolated source-to-target transfer.

</details>


### [53] [HistGramBench: Measuring Structural Recovery When Multimodal Models Redraw Historical Mathematical Diagrams as TikZ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00081)
*Minh Quang Tran,Carter He,Cameron Bloch,Zitai Huang,Thien Truong Vo,Trong Nguyen Tran,Isaac Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HistGramBench基准，含1551–1933年间8种语言95部著作的2,507幅几何图，用双模型一致性事实校验重绘程序，评估六个低成本AI模型，最佳仅恢复约五分之三的事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字图书馆中十六至二十世纪数学书籍的字母标注图仅以扫描图像存在，内容不可检索；将图重绘为简短绘图程序本可记录点线圆关系，但印刷图版缺少可比对的源代码，因而缺乏衡量此类工具是否可用的评测基准。

Method: 构建HistGramBench：2,507幅图、95部著作、8种语言、1551–1933年。对每幅图，由来自不同供应商的两个AI模型就读者可从图中获取的事实（如哪些线平行、两线交点位置）达成一致，并依据模型自身生成的绘图程序产出的图形校验这些事实；据此评测六种低成本AI模型。

Result: 最佳模型恢复约五分之三（≈60%）的事实；模型遗漏的事实大多来自始终未能生成图形的程序；平行与相切关系最难重现。

Conclusion: HistGramBench为历史学家与图书馆员提供了量化手段，用于判断此类工具何时能使老旧数学图实现可检索。

Abstract: Digital libraries preserve mathematical books printed from the sixteenth to the twentieth century, but the lettered diagrams in them, the figures on which Euclidean proofs depend, remain scanned images whose content cannot be searched. Redrawing such a diagram as a short drawing program would record which points lie on which lines and circles. But a printed plate offers no source code against which the redrawing could be checked. We introduce HistGramBench, a benchmark of 2,507 diagrams from 95 works printed between 1551 and 1933 in eight languages. For each diagram, two AI models from different providers agree on the facts a reader takes from the figure, such as which lines are parallel or where two lines meet, and every redrawing is checked against those facts on the model's own drawing. We evaluate six affordable AI models. The best recovers about three fifths of these facts, and most of what the models miss comes from programs that never produce a drawing. Parallels and tangencies are the hardest relations to reproduce. HistGramBench gives historians and librarians a way to measure when such tools can make old mathematical diagrams searchable.

</details>


### [54] [AdaptAD: Foundation Model Adaptation with Missing Modality Robustness for Alzheimer's Disease Staging Across the Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00084)
*Dawa Chyophel Lepcha,Sophie A. Martin,Pierrick Coupé,Shabbir Syed Abdul*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaptAD用LoRA微调冻结的ImageNet预训练ViT-B/16，将T1加权MRI表示为经内侧颞叶的16个2.5-D切片，并以缺失感知融合器整合可选形态学特征，在ADNI与OASIS-3上实现CN/MCI/AD全连续期分期，且不依赖FreeSurfer形态学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单一结构MRI上完成从认知正常到轻度认知障碍再到痴呆的全AD连续期分期仍有困难：3-D网络在小样本上易过拟合，且FreeSurfer形态学数据在实际临床中常不可得。

Method: 提出AdaptAD：将配准后的T1扫描解析为穿过内侧颞叶的16个解剖定位2.5-D切片；对冻结的ImageNet预训练ViT-B/16采用低秩适配（LoRA），仅训练8900万参数中的350万；形态学特征以token形式输入，缺失时用可学习的[MISSING] token替代，并由缺失感知堆叠器融合两路专家。预注册协议下在ADNI 597名受试者（29%无FreeSurfer）相同5折划分上比较10种方法，采用Holm校正的配对bootstrap检验；锁定模型在OASIS-3 993名受试者上一次性测试。

Result: ADNI上三分类宏平均AUC为0.745（95% CI 0.709–0.781），排名第一并显著优于全参数微调、ShaSpec和3-D CNN；OASIS-3上达0.780（0.748–0.814），在基于CDR的标签上（0.804）显著优于所有3-D与图像-表格方法。LoRA以少25倍的可训练参数达到与全微调相当的性能；去除形态学后外部宏AUC变化小于0.003。疾病评分在各诊断组内与MMSE、CDR-SB相关，并对MCI向痴呆进展具有分层能力（log-rank p<0.001）；显著性图集中于内侧颞叶与脑室周围区域。

Conclusion: 参数高效的2-D基础模型可仅凭常规T1扫描、无需形态学即可完成AD全连续期分期，为记忆门诊部署提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Staging Alzheimer's disease (AD) across its continuum, from cognitively normal (CN) through mild cognitive impairment (MCI) to dementia from a single structural MRI remains difficult because 3-D networks overfit small cohorts, and FreeSurfer morphometry is often unavailable. We present AdaptAD, which reads a registered T1-weighted scan as 16 anatomically placed 2.5-D slices through the medial temporal lobe and adapts a frozen ImageNet-pretrained ViT-B/16 with low-rank adaptation (LoRA), training 3.5 M of 89 M parameters. Morphometry enters as tokens when present, or as a learned [MISSING] token, and a missing-aware stacker fuses both experts. Under a pre-registered protocol, ten methods were compared for three-class CN/MCI/AD staging on identical 5-fold splits of 597 ADNI participants, 29% without FreeSurfer, with Holm-corrected paired bootstrap tests. The locked model was tested once on 993 OASIS-3 participants (988 scored by every method). On ADNI, AdaptAD achieved a three-class macro-AUC of 0.745 (95% CI 0.709 to 0.781), ranking first and significantly exceeding full fine-tuning, ShaSpec and a 3-D CNN. On OASIS-3 it reached 0.780 (0.748 to 0.814), and on the CDR-based label (0.804) it significantly outperformed every 3-D and image-tabular method. LoRA matched full fine-tuning with 25 times fewer trainable parameters, and removing morphometry changed external macro-AUC by less than 0.003. The disease score tracked MMSE and CDR-SB within diagnostic groups and stratified MCI-to-dementia progression (log-rank p < 0.001), while saliency concentrated in medial temporal and periventricular regions. A parameter-efficient 2-D foundation model can therefore stage the full AD continuum from a routine T1 scan without morphometry, offering a scalable route to memory-clinic deployment.

</details>


### [55] [Rethinking Feature Perturbation for Single-Source Domain Generalization: A Representation-Space Analysis and Spectral Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00091)
*Badr-Eddine Benelmostafa,Sohaib Baroud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出四维表示空间描述符（特征位移、关系位移、任务区域比例、子空间偏差）作为分析特征扰动机制的经验框架，并以非学习的傅里叶相位扰动方法DISP为案例验证可控扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单源域泛化（SSDG）中特征扰动普遍以任务性能评价，而非其所诱导的表示变化，缺乏对扰动机制的刻画与可解释性。

Method: 构建由特征位移D_F、关系位移D_R、任务区域比例Θ_Task、子空间偏差Θ_Subspace组成的四维表示空间描述符；在两个含风格与天气偏移的目标检测设定下刻画现有扰动机制的几何轮廓；并以DISP（对相位幅值ρ、通道耦合κ、频域支撑M显式控制的非学习傅里叶相位扰动）为案例研究其控制量与描述符的关系。

Result: 观察到不同扰动机制呈现不同的几何轮廓，且较强的域外（OOD）配置集中于描述符空间的有界区域内；DISP在实验中取得较强的OOD性能。

Conclusion: 结果不构成普适最优，但表明可在无目标域访问条件下定位与更强SSDG性能相关的区域；显式扰动控制可通过表示空间描述加以分析，推动更可解释、可控与可检验的特征扰动研究。

Abstract: Feature perturbation is widely used in single-source domain generalization (SSDG), yet it is typically evaluated by task performance rather than by the representation changes it induces. We investigate whether perturbations can be compared through measurable descriptors of these changes. We introduce a four-dimensional representation-space description comprising feature displacement $D_F$, relational displacement $D_R$, a task-region ratio $Θ_{\mathrm{Task}}$, and subspace deviation $Θ_{\mathrm{Subspace}}$. We use these descriptors as an empirical analysis framework, not as a universal geometry, to characterize existing perturbation mechanisms. Across two object-detection settings involving style and weather shifts, we observe distinct geometric profiles and a concentration of relatively strong out-of-domain (OOD) configurations within a bounded region of the descriptor space. These findings do not establish a universal optimum but motivate investigating whether regions associated with stronger SSDG performance can be identified without target-domain access. As a case study, we introduce Dual-Invariant Spectral Perturbation (DISP), a non-learned Fourier-phase perturbation with explicit controls over phase magnitude $ρ$, channel coupling $κ$, and frequency support $\mathcal{M}$. We empirically examine how these controls affect the proposed descriptors. DISP achieves strong OOD performance in our experiments, illustrating how explicit perturbation controls can be analyzed through a representation-space description. We hope this viewpoint can contribute to more interpretable, controllable, and ultimately testable approaches to feature perturbation in SSDG.

</details>


### [56] [OCRia-1 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00092)
*Aman Rangapur,Antonio Jimeno Yepes,Daniel Bustamante,Nutchapol Dendumrongsup,Sebastián G. Acosta,Pierre-Loïc Doulcet,Simon Suo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OCRia-1 is a 4B document-parsing VLM achieving state-of-the-art table/chart/text-formatting parsing (mean ParseBench 0.87) with ~4,700 tok/s single-request decoding on one B200 GPU.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional document parsing struggles with merged cells, spanning headers, tables with thousands of cells, chart data, and meaningful text formatting (bold, italics, sub/superscripts), yet these details are business-critical.

Method: Emphasizes data quality and reward design: synthetic pages give exact ground truth for long tables and rich formatting; real pages transcribed via a PDF-text-layer-grounded pipeline; dimension-specific rewards for table reconstruction, chart-data extraction, and formatting fidelity; trained with SFT then RLVR; plus a block-diffusion speculator, quantization and custom kernels for speed.

Result: Highest scores on tables, charts and text formatting among evaluated systems (incl. a 35B model and Mistral OCR 4.1); mean ParseBench score 0.87 vs 0.75 next-best; ~4,700 tok/s (≈9× faster than base model on stock vLLM).

Conclusion: Careful, diversity-focused data curation plus dimension-specific verifiable rewards drive strong parsing accuracy, and system-level optimization yields the fastest published document-parsing VLM in its class.

Abstract: We introduce OCRia-1, a 4B vision-language model (VLM) for document parsing that excels at preserving table structure, extracting chart data and retaining text formatting. OCRia-1 handles content that has traditionally been challenging in document parsing: merged cells and spanning headers, tables with thousands of cells, chart data, and meaningful text formatting such as bold, italics, subscripts and superscripts, preserving details needed for business-critical document analysis. At the time of this report, it achieves the highest scores on tables, charts and text formatting among the systems evaluated, including a 35B model and Mistral OCR 4.1. Its mean ParseBench score over the four dimensions we evaluate is 0.87, against 0.75 for the next-best system.
  We emphasize data quality and reward design in training OCRia-1. Synthetic pages provide exact ground truth for long tables and rich formatting, while real pages are transcribed using a pipeline grounded in the PDF text layer. We attribute OCRia-1's performance primarily to careful data curation that prioritizes quality and diversity over quantity. Dimension-specific rewards evaluate table reconstruction, chart-data extraction and text-formatting fidelity. We train the model on the curated data using supervised fine-tuning (SFT), then refine it through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR).
  To our knowledge, OCRia-1 is the fastest published document-parsing VLM in its class, decoding at about 4,700 tokens per second with a single request on one B200 GPU, about nine times faster than the base model on stock vLLM. This is achieved through a block-diffusion speculator, quantization and custom kernels.

</details>


### [57] [Pano2BIM: A Geometry-Constrained Vision-Language Agent for As-Built BIM Generation from a Floor Plan and a Single 360$^\circ$ Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00097)
*Juan Diego Castaño Molina,Borja García de Soto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Pano2BIM，一个几何约束的VLM智能体，从平面图与单张全景图生成LOD 200的近似IFC4模型，实现快速低成本竣工BIM生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生需要准确、最新的竣工BIM，但生成与维护成本高、耗时；图像方法缺乏结构化BIM信息，VLM难以可靠完成度量层面的物体放置。

Method: VLM识别地标与物体，确定性工具估计相机位姿、通过射线投射计算度量位置并校验空间约束；可选第二阶段用渲染-比较反馈丰富表现模型而不改动主要家具几何。

Result: 单实验室初步评估：Measure流程家具足迹IoU均值0.53（基线0.44），七个固定家具中平均匹配4.0个（基线2.3个）；测量阶段平均3.8分钟、API成本1.63美元/次，完整流程约42分钟、11美元。

Conclusion: 验证了快速、低成本竣工BIM生成的可行性，可服务于反映物理条件变化的数字孪生维护。

Abstract: Digital twins require accurate, up-to-date building information models (BIM) that reflect physical conditions, yet generating and maintaining as-built models remains costly and labor-intensive. Emerging image-based methods can capture visual appearance without inherently producing structured BIM information, while vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with reliable metric object placement. This paper presents Pano2BIM, a geometry-constrained VLM agent that generates approximate IFC4 models at Level of Development (LOD) 200 from a floor plan with known room dimensions and a single 360-degree photograph. The VLM identifies landmarks and objects, while deterministic tools estimate camera pose, compute metric positions through ray casting, and check spatial constraints. An optional second stage enriches the presentation model through render-and-compare feedback without modifying the primary furniture geometry. In a preliminary evaluation on one laboratory, the proposed Measure workflow achieved a mean furniture-footprint intersection over union (IoU) of 0.53, compared with 0.44 for a single-response baseline using identical modeling instructions, and matched an average of 4.0 versus 2.3 of seven fixed furniture elements across three runs. Centroid disagreements were similar for the four elements consistently identified by both approaches. The measurement stage averaged 3.8 minutes and USD 1.63 in API costs per run, while the complete demonstrated workflow required approximately 42 minutes and USD 11. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of rapid, low-cost as-built BIM generation, with potential applications in maintaining digital twins that reflect changing physical conditions.

</details>


### [58] [A Novel Lightweight Multi-Scale Branching Encoder for Few-Shot Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00099)
*Oriod Malo,Alexandre Xavier Falcão,Laurent Najman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Sum12 多尺度分支编码器，用 7 条并行卷积分支（核尺寸组合之和为 12）替换 ProtoNet 基础编码器的嵌入阶段，在不显著增加计算开销的前提下提升小样本分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本学习（FSL）中每类仅有少量标注样本，而原型网络（ProtoNet）的嵌入质量直接决定分类效果。作者希望在几乎不增加计算成本的前提下改进嵌入阶段，兼顾局部与更大范围的空间结构表征，并适配低数据、资源受限的视觉识别场景。

Method: 设计轻量卷积编码器 Sum12：7 条并行分支、各分支卷积核尺寸之和为 12，在统一的多尺度特征空间中融合局部与较宽空间结构。在 Omniglot、CIFAR-FS、FC100、miniImageNet 上按受控 FSL 协议评估，与原始 ProtoNet 编码器及 Inception 风格分支变体对比；并开展消融实验（融合策略、空洞卷积、分支组成、感受野预算），另在 MedMNIST 2D 基准上测试常规监督医学图像分类。

Result: 在上述实验中，Sum12 相对基础 ProtoNet 编码器总体提升了低样本（low-shot）分类性能，同时保持紧凑的模型规模；在 MedMNIST 2D 上也展示了其作为常规监督分类骨干的表现。

Conclusion: Sum12 编码器适用于低数据视觉识别与资源受限的智能成像应用。但面向目标嵌入式平台的完整端到端验证，以及已观察到的实现差异的排查，仍属未来工作。

Abstract: Few-Shot Learning (FSL) addresses classification scenarios in which only a small number of labeled examples are available for each class. This paper presents the Sum12 Multi-Scale branching encoder, a lightweight convolutional architecture designed to improve the embedding stage of Prototypical Networks without substantially increasing computational cost. The encoder uses seven parallel convolutional branches whose kernel-size combinations sum to 12, allowing local and broader spatial structures to be represented within a unified Multi-Scale feature space. The proposed architecture was evaluated on Omniglot, CIFAR- FS, FC100, and miniImageNet under controlled few-shot learning protocols and compared with the original Prototypical Network encoder and Inception-inspired branch variants. Additional ablation studies have investigated fusion strategies, dilated convolutions, branch compositions, and receptive field budget. Beyond few-shot learning, the encoder was also tested on the MedMNIST 2D benchmark to assess its behavior in conventional supervised medical image classification. Across these experiments, Sum12 generally improved the low-shot classification performance with respect to the basic ProtoNet encoder while preserving a compact model size. These properties suggest that the proposed encoder may be suitable for low-data visual recognition and resource-constrained intelligent imaging applications. However, complete end-to- end validation on the target embedded platform, including resolution of the observed implementation discrepancies, remains future work.

</details>


### [59] [Demystifying Prompt Stealing Attacks against Synthetic Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00102)
*Junyan Huang,Sanskar Agmain,Chen Gong,Yan Pang,FNU Suya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究视频提示词窃取攻击：仅靠恢复提示词无法忠实重建公开视频，作者提出V-PSA，结合恢复的caption与DDIM/Null-Text Inversion构造可复用输入，实现约0.9的视频相似度并支持受控编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创作者公开示例视频用于宣传售卖，但可能被攻击者用于未授权复现与定制；且现有评估若只考察提示词恢复，会低估攻击者的重建能力。

Method: 将captioning与prompt优化适配为视频PSA，发现仅恢复提示词保真度有限（初始噪声变化、独立生成器均导致空间/时间实现差异）；提出V-PSA：用恢复的caption与DDIM inversion在独立生成器下构造参考轨迹，再用Null-Text Inversion优化逐步无条件嵌入以减小重建偏差；修改caption时该输入可引导保持布局与运动的编辑。构建含12,000视频-提示词对、3个受害生成器、4类提示词来源的基准。

Result: 目标与重建视频相似度约0.9，显著优于仅提示词基线的0.6；支持多种保持目标结构的受控变体；在图像PSA中引入inversion-derived输入亦提升重建效果。

Conclusion: 评估仅局限于提示词恢复会低估攻击者复现公开生成输出的能力，需将重建保真度与可控编辑纳入安全评估。

Abstract: Creators publicly showcase generated content to advertise and sell their work, but these examples may also enable unauthorized reproduction and customization. We study whether an adversary can derive reusable inputs from a single public video for faithful reconstruction and controlled editing, without access to the original prompt or generator. We adapt captioning and prompt optimization approaches to video prompt stealing attacks (PSAs) and find that prompt recovery alone provides limited reconstruction fidelity. Even the original prompt produces different spatial and temporal realizations when the initial noise changes. Using an independent generator enlarges the reconstruction gap, as reusing the original prompt and seed can still produce substantially different videos. We propose V-PSA, which uses a recovered caption and DDIM inversion to construct a reference trajectory under an independent generator. Null-Text Inversion then optimizes per-step unconditional embeddings to reduce deviations from this trajectory during reconstruction. The resulting inputs also guide generation when the caption is modified, supporting edits that change selected content while preserving the target's layout and motion. We construct a benchmark containing 12,000 video-prompt pairs from three victim generators and four prompt sources. V-PSA achieves video-to-video similarity around 0.9 between target and reconstructed videos, substantially improving over prompt-only baselines with similarity score of 0.6, and supports multiple controlled variations that preserve the target's structure. Additional experiments show that augmenting prior image PSAs with inversion-derived inputs also improves reconstruction. Our results show that evaluations restricted to prompt recovery can underestimate an attacker's ability to reconstruct public generative outputs.

</details>


### [60] [Detector-Integrated Multi-Cue Association for Occlusion-Aware UAV Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00105)
*Shu-Hung Chou,Trong-An Bui,Wei-Ming Chen,Hsin-Piao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出检测器集成多线索跟踪框架：在YOLOv8n颈部挂接轻量投影头，经ROIAlign单次前向同时输出检测与嵌入，再以运动、IoU、外观多线索加权关联，UAVDT上MOTA 28.33、HOTA 31.16、IDF1 38.18，82.59 FPS，IDSW较DeepSORT降低58.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机影像中车辆目标小、外观相似且频繁遮挡，易产生ID切换；DeepSORT类外观跟踪器依赖独立ReID网络，逐目标推理拖慢运行速度，且小目标/遮挡目标的外观特征不可靠。

Method: 检测器集成式多线索跟踪框架。在YOLOv8n检测器neck处附加轻量投影头，通过ROIAlign提取目标嵌入，单次前向传播同时产出检测框与嵌入，省去独立ReID网络的逐目标推理；关联阶段将运动距离、IoU距离与外观距离融合为多线索代价，权重由消融实验确定，并辅以低分检测救援（low-score rescue）与外观记忆保护机制。

Result: UAVDT基准上MOTA 28.33、HOTA 31.16、IDF1 38.18，速度82.59 FPS；三项指标均为对比跟踪器中最优，IDSW相对DeepSORT减少58.4%。

Conclusion: 将外观嵌入提取内嵌到检测器中可实现精度与速度的兼顾：在保持实时性（82.59 FPS）的同时提升跟踪精度并显著抑制ID切换，说明"检测-嵌入一体化+多线索关联"是UAV小目标MOT的有效方案。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) imagery is challenging because vehicles are small, visually similar, and frequently occluded, which leads to identity switches (IDSW). Appearance-based trackers such as DeepSORT rely on a standalone re-identification (ReID) network, whose per-target inference limits runtime speed, while the appearance of small or occluded vehicles remains unreliable. This paper proposes a detector-integrated multi-cue tracking framework. Lightweight projection heads are attached to the neck of the YOLOv8n detector to extract target embeddings through ROIAlign, producing detections and embeddings in a single forward pass. A multi-cue association cost then combines motion, IoU, and appearance distances with weights determined through ablation, complemented by low-score rescue and appearance-memory protection. On the UAVDT benchmark, the proposed method achieves a MOTA of 28.33, a HOTA of 31.16, and an IDF1 of 38.18 at 82.59 FPS, the best among the compared trackers in all three metrics, and reduces IDSW by 58.4\% compared with DeepSORT.

</details>


### [61] [Dynamic Merging Mutually-Distilled Multi-View Networks for RGB-Event-based Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00107)
*Futian Wang,Fan Zhang,Xiao Wang,Yuhe Ding,Shiao Wang,Jingxi Huang,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于多视角教师网络权重互蒸馏的事件-帧相机多模态融合方法，先在特征级与logit级做互知识蒸馏整合多种事件表示，再以动态分层模型合并策略在参数级融合，构建统一RGB-Event模型，在多个事件数据集上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-Event融合范式把事件流转换为帧、体素或张量后再学习时空信息，仅利用了事件数据有限的表示能力；且标准训练流程未能充分利用学习过程中产生的多样模型参数。

Method: 多编码器分别提取不同类型事件表示；两阶段框架：第一阶段在特征级与logit级进行互知识蒸馏（互蒸馏利用多视角教师网络权重）；第二阶段对第一阶段模型参数执行动态分层模型合并，得到统一的多模态RGB-Event表示模型（模型参数级融合）。

Result: 在多个基于事件的（event-based）数据集上的实验结果表明，所提框架取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过互蒸馏整合多种事件表示并在模型参数层面融合，可突破单一事件表示的容量限制、充分利用训练中的参数多样性，实现更优的RGB-Event多模态融合。

Abstract: Multi-modal fusion algorithms combining conventional frame-based cameras and event cameras have recently attracted substantial research attention. Typically, existing approaches convert event streams into frames, voxels, or tensors, and then learn the required spatiotemporal information from these transformed event representations for fusion with conventional frame-camera data. Obviously, such paradigms only exploit the limited representational capacity of event data. Moreover, standard training pipelines fail to fully leverage diverse model parameters obtained throughout the learning process. In this paper, we propose a novel mutual distillation method leveraging multi-view teacher network weights to better integrate multiple event representations and achieve model-parameter level fusion. Specifically, we employ multiple encoders to extract different types of event representations and perform mutual knowledge distillation at both the feature-level and logit-level in this process. Afterwards, we perform a dynamic hierarchical model merge strategy on the model parameters from the first stage to yield a unified multi-modal RGB-Event representation model. Experimental results across multiple event-based datasets demonstrate that our proposed framework achieves significant performance improvements.

</details>


### [62] [A New Formulation of the Elastica Model with Application to Image Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00110)
*Zuoqiang Shi,Xue-Cheng Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于曲线二值表示的Euler elastica新表述：曲率可由径向核卷积-R_ε*φ/(C_0ε)近似、无需φ的导数，能量化为加权全变分（ROF推广），并用FFT+split Bregman/ALM求解，在二值图像修复中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euler elastica能量在曲线建模中十分成功，但传统level-set或相场表示下曲率项导致能量非线性、非凸（level-set情形还不可微），最小化困难。

Method: 采用曲线的±1二值表示φ；核心是曲率的极简公式：沿曲线Γ有 κ≈-1/(C_0ε)(R_ε*φ)+O(ε)，其中R_ε为半径ε球内支撑的径向核、C_0为显式常数。该式不含φ的导数，卷积用FFT计算。由此能量成为加权全变分，形式上推广ROF模型；同一曲率公式还可导出相场型模型。算法上将FFT与split Bregman迭代（本文问题中即增广拉格朗日法ALM）结合，子问题为共轭梯度求解的线性系统或显式逐点公式。

Result: 数值实验（二值图像修复/inpainting）表明所提算法有效。

Conclusion: 新表述避免了导数与不可微性，保持曲率项的几何精度O(ε)，将elastica最小化转化为可高效FFT/ALM求解的加权TV问题，为曲线与界面的建模与操作提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Curves and interfaces play a central role in computer vision and image processing, and Euler's elastica energy is one of the most successful tools to model and manipulate them. The elastica energy is usually computed through a level-set or a phase field representation of the curve, in which the curvature term makes the energy nonlinear, non-convex and, in the level-set case, non-differentiable, so that its minimization is notoriously difficult. In this paper, we propose a new formulation of the elastica model based on a binary representation of curves. The key ingredient is a very simple formula for the curvature: if $φ$ is the $\pm1$ binary function associated with a curve $Γ$, then along $Γ$ the curvature is given by $-\frac{1}{C_0ε}\left(R_ε*φ\right)+O(ε)$, where $R_ε$ is a radial kernel supported in a ball of radius $ε$ and $C_0$ is an explicit constant. No derivative of $φ$ is involved and the convolution is evaluated by FFT. The resulting energy is a weighted total variation, so that formally the new formulation is a generalization of the well-known ROF model; a phase field type model is obtained from the same curvature formula. Combining FFT with the split Bregman iteration, which for the present problem coincides with an augmented Lagrangian method (ALM), we obtain efficient algorithms whose subproblems are either linear systems solved by conjugate gradient or explicit pointwise formulas. Numerical experiments on the inpainting of binary images are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [63] [SpatialVec: A Unified Representation Learning Framework for Geospatial Objects with Boundary-Aware Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00111)
*Samya Acharja,Kanchan Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialVec：一种边界感知的空间表征学习框架，通过自适应采样几何边界及周边区域并借助几何重建训练Transformer编解码器，为点、折线、多边形生成紧凑、统一且几何感知的嵌入，显著提升复杂拓扑关系的保留与下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间编码方法难以保留细粒度拓扑信息，尤其对折线、多边形等复杂几何体的交互；对边界结构和局部空间上下文刻画不足，导致边界附近拓扑敏感关系在嵌入空间中表达不足，进而影响下游空间推理任务。

Method: 提出SpatialVec框架：对几何边界及其周边区域进行自适应采样，捕获细粒度几何结构；采用基于Transformer的编码器-解码器，通过几何重建任务进行训练，输出点、折线、多边形的紧凑、几何感知、统一的表征。

Result: 在两个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，SpatialVec显著提升了涉及多边形交互的困难拓扑关系的保留能力，并在下游预测与分类任务上明显优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 边界感知的自适应采样与重建式训练可有效弥补现有空间编码在拓扑细节上的不足，为GeoAI中的拓扑感知空间推理提供统一且高效的几何表征方案。

Abstract: Spatial representation learning is essential for Geospatial Artificial Intelligence (GeoAI) applications, enabling machine learning models to capture the geometry, location, and spatial relationships of geographic entities such as points, polylines, and polygons. Existing spatial encoding methods often struggle to preserve fine-grained topological information, particularly for interactions involving complex geometries such as polylines and polygons. These approaches inadequately capture boundary structure and local spatial context, causing topology-sensitive relationships near geometry boundaries to be underrepresented in the learned embedding space. Consequently, accurately modeling complex spatial interactions remains a significant challenge for downstream spatial reasoning tasks. To fill the gap, we introduce SpatialVec, a boundary-aware spatial representation learning framework that adaptively samples geometry boundaries and their surrounding regions, capturing fine-grained geometric structures critical for topology-aware spatial reasoning. A transformer-based encoder-decoder trained through geometry reconstruction produces compact, geometry-aware, and unified representations for points, polylines, and polygons. Empirical evaluation on two real-world datasets shows that SpatialVec substantially improves the preservation of challenging topological relationships involving polygonal interactions and outperforms state-of-the-art baselines significantly on downstream prediction and classification tasks.

</details>


### [64] [Predicting Cognitive Profiles from Structural MRI using Spatial Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00113)
*Taslima Ferdaus Shuva,Md. Tanvir Rahman,Risala Tasin Khan,Mohammad Ali Moni,ASM Ashraf Mahmud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出空间-拓扑图神经网络（ST-GNN），在标准化空间图上限制消息传递以融合脑形态与协变量，预测四种认知表型，优于线性基线与无拓扑深度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从结构神经影像预测认知能力受制于脑形态的高维性与几何复杂性；传统方法将区域形态学展平为表格特征，无法显式表征空间关系。

Method: 将区域形态学投影到群体水平的标准化空间图上，将消息传递限制在既定相邻区域；在两个大型儿童队列（N=2,378–2,424）上预测四种认知表型；通过消融实验比较稀疏几何拓扑（K近邻）、全局特征缩放与全连接网络、区域归一化方案；并用Integrated Gradients做特征归因分析。

Result: ST-GNN有效融合空间形态与系统性协变量，性能超过线性基线和无拓扑深度模型；消融显示稀疏KNN拓扑与保持绝对形态尺度的全局特征缩放优于全连接与区域归一化方案；Integrated Gradients识别出与神经解剖证据一致的形态特征，如工作记忆对应小脑体积、执行功能对应上额叶表面积。

Conclusion: 标准化结构MRI可为任务特异性认知预测提供有用信息，所揭示的架构归纳偏置（计算路由与形态特征归因的差异）具备可迁移性，为基于MRI的神经发育评估奠定基础。

Abstract: Predicting cognitive abilities from structural neuroimaging remains challenging due to the high dimensionality and geometric complexity of brain morphology. Conventional models typically flatten regional morphometry into tabular features, failing to explicitly represent spatial relationships. We propose a Spatial-Topological Graph Neural Network (ST-GNN) to predict four distinct cognitive phenotypes across large pediatric cohorts (N = 2,378 to 2,424). By projecting regional morphometry onto a population-level standardized spatial graph, ST-GNN restricts message passing to defined neighboring regions. Evaluations demonstrate that ST-GNN effectively fuses spatial morphology with systemic covariates, outperforming linear baselines and topology-free deep models. Ablations reveal that imposing a sparse geometric topology (K-nearest neighbors) and utilizing global feature scaling - which preserves absolute morphometric scale - outperform fully connected networks and region-normalized approaches. These choices provide transferable architectural inductive biases, revealing differences between computational routing and morphometric feature attribution. Integrated Gradients analysis identified distinct morphological features consistent with neuroanatomical evidence, such as cerebellar volume for working memory and superior frontal surface area for executive function. These findings suggest standardized structural MRI provides useful information for task-specific cognitive prediction, establishing a foundation for MRI-based neurodevelopmental assessment.

</details>


### [65] [Brain-Behavior Modeling from Multimodal MRI using Opportunistic Pretraining and Common-Cohort Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00114)
*Md. Tanvir Rahman,Taslima Ferdaus Shuva,Nabil Anan Orka,Asaduzzaman Khan,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出并评估了一种两阶段机会性深度集成方法，利用基线ABCD研究中不完整的影像模态覆盖来预测流体、晶体和总体认知能力，通过扩展预训练和Ridge融合获得适度的预测提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整的影像覆盖会限制多模态认知预测的样本量。作者希望利用不完整/部分观测的队列（各模态可用样本不同）进行模型开发，从而扩大可用数据规模。

Method: 使用ABCD基线数据，为结构/弥散、静息态、任务态MRI分别构建含协变量的独立神经网络模型；先在各模态可用的最大子队列（至多9,686人）上预训练，再迁移至共同训练队列，最后用Ridge回归融合（三模态队列4,216人）。采用家庭感知的五折交叉验证，与单一模态及仅协变量模型对比，并进行消融与梯度归因分析。

Result: 两阶段训练在27项比较中的24项提升了测试R²（中位增益0.0081）；融合较最强单模态模型提升平均R² 0.0051–0.0333，平均绝对误差降低0.0067–0.0976。任务+静息融合在流体（R²=0.2222）和总体认知（0.3665）上最优，结构+静息在晶体认知上最优（0.3706），相对仅协变量模型分别提升0.0805、0.1180、0.0890。消融显示扩展预训练贡献了大部分改善；梯度归因揭示了目标依赖的输入模式。

Conclusion: 结果支持利用部分观测队列进行模型开发，融合增益虽为适度但稳定。该方法在外部队列验证前，有望用于MRI辅助的认知表型刻画。

Abstract: Incomplete imaging coverage limits sample sizes for multimodal cognitive prediction. We evaluated a two-stage opportunistic deep ensemble using baseline Adolescent Brain Cognitive Development Study data to predict fluid, crystallized, and total cognition. Separate neural models processed structural/diffusion, resting-state, and task-based MRI data with covariates. Models were pretrained on eligible modality cohorts (up to 9,686 participants), adapted to common training cohorts, and fused via Ridge regression (trimodal cohort: 4,216 participants). Five-fold family-aware cross-validation compared this approach against constituent and covariate-only models. Two-stage training increased mean test coefficient of determination (R^2) in 24 of 27 comparisons (median gain: 0.0081). Fusion increased mean R^2 by 0.0051-0.0333 over the strongest constituent and reduced mean absolute error by 0.0067-0.0976. Task-plus-resting-state and trimodal ensembles reduced absolute error across all targets (nominal p <= 0.0108). Task-plus-resting-state fusion achieved the highest mean R^2 for fluid (0.2222) and total cognition (0.3665), while structural-plus-resting-state led for crystallized cognition (0.3706), yielding R^2 gains of 0.0805, 0.1180, and 0.0890 over covariate-only references. Ablations showed expanded pretraining drove most improvements. Gradient-based attribution identified target-dependent input patterns. These findings support using partially observed cohorts for model development to achieve modest fusion gains. Pending external validation, this approach may support MRI-informed cognitive phenotyping.

</details>


### [66] [DTP-OPSD: Reinforcement Learning with Distractor-Aware Temporal-Privilege On-Policy Self-Distillation for Temporal Video Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00120)
*Zhaoyu Chen,Yongwei Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DTP-OPSD 用"干扰物感知的时间特权在线自蒸馏"弥补 GRPO 在难样本上全零回报导致的无梯度问题，仅加 300 条难样本即在三个 TVG 基准上取得开源 8B 模型最佳 mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TVG 中同一视频常含重复/相似/相反事件，难例上 GRPO 采样的整组 rollout 均未命中目标、tIoU 全为 0，组内相对优势全为零，策略梯度信号消失，模型无法从这些困难样本中学习。

Method: 采用共享权重的教师—学生框架：学生看原始视频并 on-policy 生成回复；教师看"特权视频"，其中目标窗口用红色满帧边框标注、同一视频内的干扰窗口用蓝色满帧边框标注。蒸馏损失由教师对学生生成的前缀进行 token 级 Jensen-Shannon 散度稠密监督。自适应门控：当教师不优于学生时抑制蒸馏；当教师更优但学生全部 rollout 的 tIoU 为 0 时加强蒸馏。另通过 VLM 筛选加人工校验构建紧凑的高干扰训练集（300 条）。

Result: 在 Charades-TimeLens、ActivityNet-TimeLens、QVHighlights-TimeLens 上 mIoU 分别为 55.93、54.77、66.71，为对比开源 8B 模型中最优；相同数据下较纯 GRPO 在全部 12 项指标上提升，R1@0.7 分别提升 1.28、1.62、1.78 点。

Conclusion: 在 GRPO 奖励塌缩的难例上，以时间窗口标注作为特权信息的 on-policy 自蒸馏可提供有效的稠密替代监督，且配合少量高质量难负样本即可显著提升 TVG 定位性能。

Abstract: Temporal Video Grounding (TVG) aims to localize the start and end times of an event described by a language query in a video. The task becomes particularly challenging when the same video contains multiple repeated, similar, or opposite events. On these hard examples, all responses sampled by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) may miss the target and receive zero temporal Intersection-over-Union (tIoU). Their group-relative advantages are then all zero, leaving no useful policy gradient signal. To address this, we propose DTP-OPSD, a reinforcement learning framework with distractor-aware temporal-privilege on-policy self-distillation. The student observes the clean video and generates responses on-policy, while a shared-weight teacher observes a privileged video in which the target window is marked by red full-frame borders and same-video distractor windows by blue full-frame borders. For the distillation loss, the teacher evaluates student-generated prefixes and provides dense token-level Jensen-Shannon divergence supervision. This supervision remains informative even when GRPO provides no policy gradient signal. We suppress distillation when the teacher does not outperform the student and strengthen it for groups where the teacher performs better but all student rollouts receive zero tIoU. We also construct a compact hard-interference training set through VLM screening and human verification. With only 300 additional post-training examples, DTP-OPSD achieves the highest mIoU among the compared open-source 8B models on Charades-TimeLens, ActivityNet-TimeLens, and QVHighlights-TimeLens, reaching 55.93, 54.77, and 66.71, respectively. Compared with Pure GRPO trained on the same data, DTP-OPSD improves all 12 reported metrics, with R1@0.7 gains of 1.28, 1.62, and 1.78 points on the three benchmarks. Code will be released at https://github.com/nian1125/DTP-OPSD

</details>


### [67] [CDBM: Complexity-Diversity Framework for Building Morphology Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00122)
*Evgenii Maslov,Alexandra Vabnits,Denis Karpov,Alexey Morozov,Anastasia Antsiferova,Art Prosvetov,Valentin Khrulkov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出12个可解释的建筑体量描述符CDBM，衡量几何复杂性与多样性，在大规模OSM数据上计算并与3DBM、LION潜变量在六项任务上对比，CDBM在真实-生成判别和人类偏好预测上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有建筑体量表征（如3DBM、形状潜变量）在可解释性和对几何细节分布不均性的刻画上不足，且缺乏对生成模型输出质量的多任务系统性评测基准。

Method: 面向竖直拉伸表示的体量，构造12个可解释描述符，从一维、二维元素上度量几何细节表达强度（complexity）及其分布不均性（diversity）；在482,241,306栋OSM建筑上计算，并在任务特定样本上与3DBM、LION形状潜变量对比，用梯度提升与逻辑回归评测功能、区域、上下文相关性、真实-生成判别、人类偏好及几何条件生成六项任务。

Result: 梯度提升下CDBM在区分生成体量与实测体量、匹配人类偏好两项上最佳；逻辑回归下在真实感上与3DBM持平；其余三项任务3DBM领先。作为生成目标，描述符揭示模型难以生成表达强烈且异质的形态。

Conclusion: CDBM并非替代而是补充现有指标，提供可解释的复杂性-多样性视角，可用作生成任务的诊断目标。

Abstract: We introduce Complexity-Diversity Building Metrics (CDBM), twelve interpretable descriptors for building massings represented as vertical extrusions. They measure how strongly geometric detail is expressed (complexity) and how unevenly it varies across one- and two-dimensional elements (diversity). Computed for 482,241,306 OpenStreetMap buildings and compared with 3D Building Metrics (3DBM) and the LION shape latent on task-specific samples, CDBM is tested on function, region, contextual relevance, real-generated discrimination, human preference and geometry-conditioned generation. With gradient boosting, CDBM best separates generated from mapped massings and best matches human preference; with logistic regression it matches 3DBM on realism. 3DBM leads on the other three tasks. As generation targets, the descriptors show that models struggle to produce strongly expressed and heterogeneous forms. CDBM complements existing indicators.

</details>


### [68] [SketchBloom: Training-Free Vector Sketch Outpainting with Stroke-Level Style Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00245)
*Quang-Thinh Nguyen,Duy-Khang Do,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SketchBloom：免训练的稀疏草图外扩框架，结合预训练栅格外扩、风格迁移引导与逐实例矢量优化，输出可编辑SVG贝塞尔笔画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏草图外扩在保持轮廓连贯性与笔画风格方面仍具挑战，现有基于优化的矢量化方法缺乏针对草图外扩的合成设计。

Method: 输入草图、场景提示与可选参考风格；自适应潜空间混合平衡内容轮廓与参考纹理，无需重训；由参考导出的笔画词表初始化轮廓与DINO注意力引导的笔画，分别针对结构与纹理优化，再经强调轮廓的乘性融合。整个流程不训练任务特定网络。

Result: 在LineArtOut-1K（1,024组输入与风格组合）上，当所有方法对同一外扩栅格矢量化时，SketchBloom在感知、语义与结构保真度上优于所评估的矢量基线；单张NVIDIA H200上每对约需6分钟。该协议评估矢量合成而非端到端外扩或参考风格优劣。

Conclusion: 提出一种面向草图外扩的免训练矢量合成方案，可生成可编辑SVG；评测范围限于矢量合成环节。

Abstract: Outpainting sparse sketches while preserving contour coherence and stroke style remains challenging. We present SketchBloom, a training-free framework that combines pretrained raster outpainting and style-transfer guidance with per-instance vector optimization. Given a sketch, a scene prompt, and an optional reference style, it produces an editable Scalable Vector Graphic (SVG) composed of Bezier strokes. Adaptive latent blending balances content contours and reference texture without model retraining. A reference-derived stroke vocabulary initializes contour- and DINO-attention-guided strokes, which are optimized separately for structure and texture before contour-emphasizing multiply fusion. As in prior optimization-based vector sketching, no task-specific network is trained; our contribution is the synthesis design specialized to sketch outpainting. On LineArtOut-1K, comprising 1,024 input and style combinations, SketchBloom improves perceptual, semantic, and structural fidelity over the evaluated vector baselines when all methods vectorize the same outpainted raster. This protocol evaluates vector synthesis rather than end-to-end outpainting or reference-style superiority. Runtime is approximately six minutes per pair on a single NVIDIA H200 GPU. Project page: https://khangcntn123.github.io/SketchBloom/
