</details>


### [69] [SketchForge: Training-Free Scene-to-Vector Sketching via Decoupled Structure and Texture Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00253)
*Quang-Thinh Nguyen,Duy-Khang Do,Hien Trinh,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SketchForge: a training-free per-instance test-time optimization of Bezier curves that converts a raster image into a resolution-independent, stroke-editable SVG sketch, decoupling structural contour strokes from texture strokes via vector-native differentiable rendering and multiply-blend compositing.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Raster-to-vector conversion faces a dilemma: raster-space rendering pipelines are hard to edit at the stroke level, while generation-from-scratch vectorization methods struggle to faithfully preserve both structural contours and tonal texture of an arbitrary input image.

Method: A training-free framework requiring no extra training or fine-tuning. Strokes are seeded by a contour- and attention-guided initialization, then refined by a vector-native differentiable rendering pipeline that decouples structural contour strokes from free-form texture strokes under dedicated objectives, and are composited via multiply-blend layering.

Result: Extensive experiments show SketchForge consistently outperforms existing vector-based baselines in structural fidelity and generation quality.

Conclusion: SketchForge provides a resolution-independent, stroke-editable SVG sketch generation path that unifies fidelity to the input with stroke-level editability, without any training.

Abstract: Converting a raster image into a resolution-independent, stroke-editable vector sketch while faithfully preserving both structural contours and tonal texture remains challenging: raster-space rendering pipelines are difficult to edit at the stroke level, while generation-from-scratch vectorization methods struggle to remain faithful to an arbitrary input image. We present SketchForge, a training-free framework that converts an input raster image into a resolution-independent SVG sketch through per-instance test-time optimization of Bezier curves, requiring no additional training or fine-tuning. Strokes are seeded via a contour and attention-guided initialization, then refined by a vector-native differentiable rendering pipeline that decouples structural contour strokes from free-form texture strokes under dedicated objectives, composited via multiply-blend layering. Extensive experiments show that SketchForge consistently outperforms existing vector-based baselines in structural fidelity and generation quality. Project page: https://khangcntn123.github.io/SketchForge

</details>


### [70] [Seeing Less in a Sparse World: Cross-View Geolocalization from Limited Field-of-View Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00288)
*Fahimul Aleem,Shruti Vyas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出大规模全球分布数据集CVW500k（50万+地面-卫星图像对，采用有限视场地面图像）与GeoQueryNet查询式跨视角融合Transformer，以解决跨视角地理定位中数据集地理局限与全景观测不真实的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角地理定位方法受限于缺乏全球多样化训练数据集与基准，已有基准多地理受限，且普遍使用全景地面视图，无法反映真实相机捕获的有限视场图像。

Method: 构建CVW500k数据集：覆盖多样地理区域与环境、超50万地面-卫星图像对，地面端采用有限视场图像。提出GeoQueryNet：基于CLIP视觉编码器并施加参数高效LoRA适配，引入跨视角对齐模块，利用可学习查询token提取跨视角地理一致特征。

Result: 构建了全球分布式大规模数据集并给出更具挑战性的真实设定基准；GeoQueryNet通过查询式跨视角融合与LoRA高效适配实现地面-卫星视角鸿沟的桥接（摘要未给出具体数值指标）。

Conclusion: 更真实（有限视场）且地理多样化的数据与查询式跨视角对齐建模，是推进GNSS拒止/退化环境下跨视角地理定位的有效路径；代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Cross-view geolocalization aims to determine the geographic location of a ground-level image by retrieving its corresponding satellite image, offering a promising alternative for localization in Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS)-denied or degraded environments such as urban canyons, dense forests, and disaster scenarios. Despite recent progress, existing approaches remain limited by the lack of globally diverse training datasets and benchmarks that are often geographically restricted. Furthermore, most benchmarks rely on panoramic ground views, which do not reflect the limited field-of-view imagery captured by real world cameras.
  To address these challenges, we introduce Cross-View World 500k (CVW500k), a large-scale globally distributed dataset containing over 500,000 ground-satellite image pairs collected across diverse geographic regions and environments. Unlike previous datasets, CVW500k uses limited field-of-view ground images, providing a more realistic and challenging setting for cross-view geolocalization. In addition, we propose GeoQueryNet, a query-based cross-view fusion transformer framework designed to bridge the viewpoint gap between ground and satellite images. The model leverages a CLIP-based visual encoder with parameter-efficient LoRA adaptation and introduces a Cross-View Alignment Module that uses learnable query tokens to extract geographically consistent features across viewpoints. Our code can be found here: https://github.com/Fahim17/Cross_View_World_Dataset.git .

</details>


### [71] [Seeing the Unseen: Unified Visible--Invisible Motion for Physically Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00293)
*Mingyang Bao,Fangda Ye,Xihao Chen,Bobo Li,Jason Li,Shengqiong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"Seeing the Unseen"统一框架，将视频生成分为物理动力学学习与物理先验注入两阶段：用粒子图动力学模拟器PGDS预测三维运动轨迹，再以可见—不可见运动场VIMF表征并将其注入扩散生成器，从而生成视觉连贯且物理一致的视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成方法视觉质量高但物理一致性不足，原因在于缺乏对底层物理过程的显式建模；已有方案或依赖强假设的模拟器、或仅用粗粒度语义引导，且难以有效将物理先验注入生成模型。

Method: 框架分两阶段。其一，提出轻量级粒子图动力学模拟器PGDS，从数据中学习可泛化的物理交互并预测合理的三维运动轨迹；其二，提出可见—不可见运动场VIMF，同时刻画输入帧中可观测的运动与随时间因物体动力学而涌现的运动，作为比传统物理信号更完整、更具结构的运动引导；最后将这些轨迹集成到基于扩散的生成器中。

Result: 大量实验表明，相较强基线方法，本方法在运动准确性、交互一致性上有所提升，并减少了物理伪影。

Conclusion: 通过学习显式物理动力学并以结构化运动场形式注入扩散生成器，可有效提升视频生成的物理一致性，为物理接地（physics-grounded）视频生成提供统一范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation have achieved impressive visual quality, yet they often fail to produce physically consistent dynamics due to the lack of explicit modeling of underlying physical processes. Existing approaches either rely on simulators with strong assumptions or use coarse semantic guidance, and further struggle to effectively inject physical priors into generation models. In this work, we propose Seeing the Unseen, a unified framework for physics-grounded video generation. Our approach decomposes the problem into two stages: learning physical dynamics and injecting them into video synthesis. First, we introduce a lightweight Particle-based Graph Dynamics Simulator (PGDS) that learns generalizable physical interactions from data and predicts plausible 3D motion trajectories. Second, we propose a Visible--Invisible Motion Field (VIMF) that captures both motion observable in the input frame and motion that emerges over time due to object dynamics. This representation enables more complete and structured motion guidance compared to conventional physical signals. By integrating these trajectories into a diffusion-based generator, our method produces videos that are both visually coherent and physically consistent. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved motion accuracy, interaction consistency, and reduced physical artifacts compared to strong baselines.

</details>


### [72] [Déjà Vu: Cycle-Consistent Re-ranking for Semantic Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00328)
*Xiaoran Zhang,Huidong Liu,Yang Liu,Chien-Chih Wang,Jinfa Huang,Lingkai Kong,Bryan Wang,Yan Gao,Hongdong Li,Alex Wong,James S. Duncan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Déjà Vu测试时解码模块，将语义对应重构为"先召回后重排"，仅基于已有相似度矩阵即显著提升多种基线的匹配精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同类别图像间语义等价关键点匹配受外观、视角与尺度变化影响而困难；预训练大模型特征具备强零样本对应能力，但现有方法将最终匹配简化为贪心top-1最近邻，作者发现正确对应在前若干候选中出现频率远高于top-1命中率（30个候选时零样本特征即超过有监督模型的top-1精度），说明瓶颈在于"解码"而非表征能力。

Method: 将语义对应建模为recall-then-rerank：先依据相似度分布的置信度自适应提名候选集（分布模糊时增大、置信时缩小），再用分级循环一致性分数重排——每个候选反向匹配回源图，并按反向匹配落点与原关键点的距离进行惩罚。整个模块仅作用于基础方法已计算出的相似度矩阵，可作为即插即用组件。

Result: 在SPair-71k、其几何子集与PF-Pascal上，在无监督与弱监督两种设定下均稳定提升三个代表性基线，最高增益为SPair-71k上+1.8 PCK@0.10、PF-Pascal上+3.4 PCK@0.15，Keypoint Average Precision下+4.9。

Conclusion: 语义对应的主要限制来自解码策略而非特征表征；通过自适应候选提名与循环一致性重排的轻量测试时模块，可在不改变基础特征的情况下持续提升语义对应性能。

Abstract: We propose \emph{Déjà Vu}, a test-time decoding module for semantic correspondence. Matching semantically equivalent keypoints across images of the same object category is challenging due to variations in appearance, viewpoint, and scale. While features from large pretrained vision models carry strong zero-shot correspondence ability, existing methods reduce final matching to greedy top-$1$ nearest-neighbor selection. We show that the correct correspondence appears among the top-ranked candidates far more often than top-$1$ matching achieves: with $30$ candidates, zero-shot features exceed the top-$1$ accuracy of a supervised model. This reveals a \textit{decoding bottleneck} rather than a limit of the representation. Motivated by this observation, we formulate semantic correspondence as a \textit{recall-then-rerank} problem. Our method first nominates an adaptive candidate set, larger when the similarity distribution is ambiguous and smaller when it is confident, then re-ranks the candidates with a graded cycle-consistency score: each candidate is matched back to the source image and penalized by how far its reverse match lands from the original keypoint. The module operates entirely on the similarity matrix already computed by the base method. It consistently improves three representative baselines on SPair-71k, its geometric subset, and PF-Pascal under both unsupervised and weakly supervised settings, with gains of up to $+1.8$ PCK@0.10 on SPair-71k and $+3.4$ PCK@0.15 on PF-Pascal, and $+4.9$ under Keypoint Average Precision.

</details>


### [73] [SIEVE: Preserving Cross-Image Evidence via Depth-Aware Token Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00351)
*Nitish Shukla,Surgan Jandial,Nhat-Tan Bui,Arun Ross*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIEVE：面向多图VLM的无需训练、单次前向的视觉token压缩框架，跨图像与解码层深度联合分配token，在6.25%预算下于多图、单图与视频基准均取得最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多图VLM处理长视觉序列计算开销大。压缩需满足两点：保留跨图像互补证据、保持不同图像token相互独立。全局选择会使与问题相关的图像被欠代表；早期解码层剪枝在充分多模态处理前就删除相关内容，而现有方法却把大部分预算花在早期层。

Method: 训练无关、单次前向框架。①将每张图池化为紧凑空间网格以降低早期开销；②在等额每图配额下按prompt注意力渐进选择代表token，并将未选token聚合到同图相似代表上（保证每图有代表、图像间token隔离并精炼表示）；③用闭式调度设定各层保留数量，以命中预设平均token预算。

Result: 四个VLM骨干上，6.25%预算下在5个多图与4个单图基准取得最高平均，2个视频基准取得最高均值。LLaVA-OneVision-7B上，6.25%与12.5%预算分别超最强基线9.96与9.03分；12.5%时超未压缩模型2.01分，prefill FLOPs减少6.6倍。

Conclusion: 按图像独立配额+跨层深度联合分配token的压缩策略，优于全局选择与早期层重投入的现有方案，可在极低预算下兼顾多图互补证据保持与计算效率。

Abstract: Multi-image vision-language models (VLMs) incur substantial computation when processing long visual sequences. Compressing these sequences must preserve complementary evidence across images and keep tokens from different images separate. Global selection can underrepresent question-relevant images, and pruning in early decoder layers risks removing relevant content before sufficient multimodal processing, yet existing methods spend most of the budget on these early layers. We propose SIEVE, a training-free, single-pass framework that jointly allocates visual tokens across images and decoder depth. SIEVE first pools each image into a compact spatial grid to reduce early processing cost. It then progressively selects representatives using prompt attention under equal per-image quotas and aggregates unselected tokens into similar representatives from the same image. These operations keep every image represented and its tokens separate from other images, while refining the retained representations. A closed-form schedule sets retention counts to target a prescribed average token budget across decoder layers. Across four VLM backbones, SIEVE achieves the highest average at a 6.25% budget on five multi-image benchmarks and four single-image benchmarks, and the highest mean on two video benchmarks. On LLaVA-OneVision-7B, it outperforms the strongest baseline by 9.96 and 9.03 points at 6.25% and 12.5% budgets, and at 12.5% it exceeds the uncompressed model by 2.01 points with 6.6x fewer prefill FLOPs.

</details>


### [74] [MoRE-GS: Motion-Regime Experts for Future Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00375)
*Shima Salehi,Joshua Peeples*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoRE-GS 是一个因果、渲染感知的动态 3D 高斯场景预测框架，通过四种互补运动专家（持久性、系数空间 Transformer、图卷积网络、带刚性与非刚性约束的结构化 Transformer）的路由组合，实现对同一场景中多种运动机制的外推预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来动态新视角合成需要在同一场景内同时外推多种运动机制，而单一动力学模型难以同时表征静止区域、协同运动、局部交互与关节式轨迹，因此需要一种能按运动机制组合多个预测器的方法。

Method: 仅使用观测到的时间前缀重建高斯表示并冻结，随后通过四个互补专家预测其控制关键点运动：持久性基线、系数空间 Transformer、图卷积网络、带刚性与非刚性运动约束的结构化 Transformer；并采用紧凑路由表在软运动分组与预测时域分箱上分配专家混合权重，同时用持久性门控抑制不可靠外推。

Result: 在 D-NeRF 与 HyperNeRF 场景上，MoRE-GS 相比其他方法取得更优性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，经渲染器校准的组合式互补运动预测器能够有效提升跨异质运动机制的因果动态高斯预测能力。

Abstract: Future dynamic novel-view synthesis requires extrapolating multiple motion regimes within the same scene. A single dynamics model may not simultaneously represent stationary regions, coordinated motion, local interactions, and articulated trajectories. We introduce Motion Regime Expert Routing for Gaussian Splatting (MoRE-GS), a causal, render-aware framework for forecasting dynamic 3D Gaussian scenes. MoRE-GS freezes a Gaussian representation reconstructed exclusively from the observed temporal prefix and forecasts its control-keypoint motion using four complementary experts: persistence, a coefficient-space Transformer, a graph convolutional network, and a structured Transformer with rigid and non-rigid motion constraints. A compact routing table assigns expert mixtures across soft motion groups and forecast-horizon bins, while a persistence gate suppresses unreliable extrapolation. Across D-NeRF and HyperNeRF scenes, MoRE-GS achieves improved performance against other methods, and these results demonstrate that renderer-calibrated composition of complementary motion predictors improves causal dynamic Gaussian forecasting across heterogeneous motion regimes.
  Project page: https://advanced-vision-and-learning-lab.github.io/MoRE-GS-website/

</details>


### [75] [LF-VideoQA: Evaluating Long-Video Understanding with Long-Form Theory-of-Mind Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00383)
*Shaden Shaar,Bradon Thymes,Majd Aldaye,Claire Cardie,Bharath Hariharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LF-VideoQA：基于心理理论（ToM）构建的长篇叙事视频开放式问答数据集，含 94 部电影、6300+ 道需跨平均 23 分钟场景推理的问题，并配问题级评分 rubric；评测显示现有开源 VLM 表现远低于完备水平，召回是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频 QA 数据集多为短答案或多选，把长视频理解简化为“检索”，无法考察跨长时间跨度整合证据的能力；因此需要开放式、需综合多时刻证据的基准。

Method: 选用心理理论（ToM）问题作为任务载体：角色信念依赖其全部所见所闻，天然要求聚合分散证据。数据全自动构建——对剧本标注场景事件及角色的一阶信念与元信念，再由 LLM 生成并验证（问题、答案、rubric）三元组，并以人工研究验证质量；rubric 用于 recall 与 precision 评分。

Result: 评测四类 SOTA 开源 VLM、三种设置：(i) 可访问剧本的 oracle 仍远未给出完整答案；(ii) 最佳非 oracle 模型 recall 仅约为 oracle 一半；(iii) 字幕与说明文字表现不及视频本身，区别于以往长视频基准，说明问题需视觉证据；(iv) recall 是主要瓶颈，答案遗漏大量 rubric 要点。

Conclusion: 长叙事视频的开放式、跨时刻整合式理解对现有模型仍极具挑战，LF-VideoQA 提供了可细粒度评分的诊断性基准，并指出召回不足与视觉证据依赖是关键改进方向。

Abstract: The hardest part of understanding a long video is not pinpointing any one moment but synthesizing many, e.g., answering a question that requires tracking what develops across hours of content and articulating how the pieces fit together. To date, most long-video QA datasets require only a short answer or a multiple-choice selection, oversimplifying the long-video understanding task to retrieval. We introduce LF-VideoQA, a dataset for long-form, open-ended question answering over long narrative videos built on theory-of-mind questions: a character's belief depends on everything they have seen or been told, so each question demands assembling and reasoning from evidence distributed across the video. The dataset contains more than 6,300 questions on 94 recent films; each requires reasoning over scenes spanning an average of 23 minutes and comes with a question-specific rubric for recall and precision scoring. The dataset is created entirely automatically by annotating screenplays with scene events and each character's first-order beliefs and meta-beliefs, then generating (question, answer, rubric) triples through LLM-based question generation and verification; human studies validate their quality. We evaluate four families of state-of-the-art open-source VLMs across three settings and find the task highly challenging: (i) even an oracle with access to the screenplay falls well short of complete answers; (ii) the best non-oracle model attains barely half of the recall level of the oracle; (iii) unlike on prior long-video benchmarks, captions and subtitles underperform the video itself, indicating that the questions need visual evidence; and (iv) recall is the predominant bottleneck, with answers omitting many required rubric claims.

</details>


### [76] [Evaluating SAM-based and Convolutional Architectures in Thin Retinal Vessel Segmentation: Model Capacity, Computational Cost and Foundation Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00396)
*Carlos Eduardo Fontaneli,Cesar Henrique Comin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统量化了plain ViT与CNN在视网膜细血管分割上的差异，发现预训练plain ViT（MedSAM）的细血管中心线召回率与最佳CNN无显著差异，但更小的模型即可达到同等性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中普遍认为plain ViT因粗粒度patch tokenization、弱局部性偏置及需大训练集，在细血管分割上劣于CNN，但该预期从未被系统量化验证。

Method: 在三个视网膜数据集（DRIVE、HRF、FIVES）及一个显微数据集（VessMap）上，对比plain ViT编码器模型（MedSAM）、混合Transformer（LiteMedSAM）与三种不同规模CNN；以细血管中心线召回率与精确率为指标，并考虑计算成本与所需训练轮数。

Result: 预训练MedSAM的细血管中心线召回率在四个数据集上均与最佳CNN无显著差异（-0.6至+2.5个百分点）；经架构搜索但从零训练的紧凑plain ViT未保持该性能。

Conclusion: 对预训练plain ViT而言，结果不支持其会漏检细血管的预期，但相似细血管性能可由远小的模型实现。

Abstract: In retinal vessel segmentation, plain Vision Transformers (ViTs) are commonly expected to perform worse than convolutional neural networks (CNNs) on thin vessels, for reasons such as their coarse patch tokenization, their weak locality bias and their need for large training sets. Although this expectation is widely shared in the literature, how plain ViTs actually compare with CNNs on thin vessels has not been systematically quantified. We address this question by comparing a model with a plain ViT encoder (MedSAM), a hybrid Transformer (LiteMedSAM) and three CNNs of different sizes on three retinal datasets (DRIVE, HRF, FIVES) and, as a second-domain check, a microscopy dataset (VessMap). We measured the centerline recall and precision of thin vessels and compared the models while also considering their computational cost and how many epochs they needed to train. We found that the pre-trained MedSAM had a centerline recall of thin vessels that was not significantly different from that of the best CNN on all four datasets (-0.6 to +2.5 percentage points). A compact plain ViT obtained through architecture search but trained from scratch did not retain this performance. For pre-trained plain ViTs, our results do not support the expectation that they miss thin vessels, although similar thin-vessel performance can be obtained with much smaller models.

</details>


### [77] [TAP-Out: Tracking Any Point in 360° via Outpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00406)
*Finlay G. C. Hudson,James A. D. Gardner,William A. P. Smith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAP-Out 将视频生成式外扩(outpainting)作为世界模型先验，把超出视野(OOV)的点跟踪显式化：先外扩为360°全景视频，再用分割定位目标、生成以物体为中心的透视裁剪，交给现成的帧内跟踪器处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跟踪离开相机视野的点需推理未观测到的场景动态，这与跟踪可见对应点本质不同；已有方法缺乏对OOV推理的显式建模，性能受限。

Method: 离线解耦流水线：给定窄视场输入视频，1) 用生成式视频外扩得到完整360°等距柱状(equirectangular)视频；2) 通过视频物体分割在全景场景中定位目标；3) 合成以物体为中心的透视裁剪，交由标准帧内跟踪器处理。各模块均为现成基础模型，无需任务特定结构修改。

Result: 在TAPVid360基准上达到SOTA，OOV跟踪精度较最佳先前方法提升约3倍；并在出框物体分割与检测上展示灵活性；提供按运动模式、静态/动态内容、幻觉质量的诊断分析，刻画成功与失败条件。

Conclusion: 将外扩视频生成作为世界模型先验、以解耦方式组合基础模型，是解决OOV点跟踪的有效范式，并具备向分割、检测等任务迁移的灵活性。

Abstract: Tracking points that leave the camera's field of view (FoV) requires reasoning about unobserved scene dynamics, a fundamentally different problem from tracking visible correspondences. We present TAP-Out, an offline method that makes this out-of-view (OOV) reasoning explicit by leveraging generative video outpainting as a world-model prior. Given a narrow-FoV input video, TAP-Out outpaints a complete 360° equirectangular video, locates target objects within the panoramic scene via video object segmentation, and synthesises object-centred perspective crops on which standard in-frame trackers operate. This decoupled design composes off-the-shelf foundation models without requiring task-specific architectural modifications. On the TAPVid360 benchmark, TAP-Out achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving OOV tracking accuracy by approximately 3x over the best prior method. We demonstrate its flexibility on out-of-frame object segmentation and detection and we provide detailed diagnostic analyses across motion regimes, static versus dynamic content, and hallucination quality, characterising when and why the approach succeeds or fails.

</details>


### [78] [PAM-Mamba - Efficient Dense Action Detection using Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00423)
*Dhruvraj Singh Rawat,Faegheh Sardari,Armin Mustafa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PAM-Mamba 是一种线性时间（O(T)）的密集动作检测架构，基于 Mamba 与多分支时序编码器，在保持精度的同时大幅降低参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 类方法精度高，但自注意力 O(T²) 复杂度限制其在长未裁剪视频上的可扩展性；需在密集动作检测中兼顾精度与效率。

Method: 1) 门控可学习位置编码（GLPE），以初始化为零的标量门控制位置信号注入；2) Hub-and-Spoke Funnel，在 PAT 非层次多尺度基础上，从原始全分辨率输入向各粗粒度分支建立直接残差；3) Bi-TemBa 双向时序 Mamba，先用浅层时序卷积模块提取局部运动线索，再级联双向 Mamba 扫描，反向扫描精化前向表示，实现线性时间双向上下文；4) 基于不确定性的 Mixup，利用预测熵指导混合伙伴选择。

Result: 采用 PAT 评测实现，取得 26.82（Charades I3D）、34.59（Charades CLIP）、46.56（MultiTHUMOS I3D）、46.92 mAP（MultiTHUMOS CLIP）；参数量 26.1–27.9M，较 PAT 减少 61–63%。

Conclusion: 以 Mamba 替代 Transformer 自注意力，在密集动作检测中实现 O(T) 线性复杂度，并以显著更少参数达到具竞争力精度，验证了高效时序建模方案的可行性。

Abstract: Dense action detection requires precisely localising multiple overlapping actions in long untrimmed videos. Transformer-based methods achieve strong accuracy but their $O(T^2)$ self-attention cost limits scalability. We present PAM-Mamba, a linear-time $O(T)$ architecture built on Mamba with a multi-branch temporal encoder. Gated Learnable Positional Encoding (GLPE) controls positional signal injection via a scalar gate initialised to zero, starting training without added positional signal. A Hub-and-Spoke Funnel extends the non-hierarchical multi-scale design of PAT with a direct residual from the original full-resolution input to each coarse branch, providing each coarse branch with a projection of the input features. Bi-TemBa (Bidirectional Temporal Mamba) first extracts local motion cues with a shallow Temporal Convolution Module, then applies a cascaded bidirectional Mamba scan; the backward pass refines the forward-scanned representation from the opposite direction, giving each temporal position access to context from both directions in linear time. Uncertainty-Based Mixup uses prediction entropy to guide the selection of mixup partners. Using the PAT evaluation implementation, we obtain 26.82 (Charades I3D), 34.59 (Charades CLIP), 46.56 (MultiTHUMOS I3D), and 46.92 mAP (MultiTHUMOS CLIP), with 26.1-27.9M parameters, 61-63% fewer than PAT.

</details>


### [79] [Spoofing the Latent State: Imperceptible Goal-Image Attacks Derail Planning in LeWorldModel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00427)
*Kunal Bhosikar,Nicole Meng,Charu Sharma,Yingjie Lao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析JEPA类视觉世界模型(以LeWM为例)潜在规划接口的测试时鲁棒性：其潜空间度量由任务相关物体状态主导，且该几何结构在测试时高度脆弱——仅ε=4/255的ℓ∞白盒扰动即可在73–95%场景中将方块"伪造"到任意攻击者指定位姿而不改变智能体状态编码，使Push-T规划成功率从87%降至32%，并在四个基准上一致存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA式联合嵌入预测架构（如LeWM）已作为通用视觉世界模型用于控制，通过SIGReg防止坍缩并以欧氏距离为目标嵌入进行规划。但该"以潜距离衡量任务进展"的规划接口在测试时是否可靠、其学到的度量几何是否可被利用，尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 1) 对Push-T上学习到的潜空间度量做几何分析：量化方块单像素位移与智能体位姿位移对潜距离的贡献（3倍关系、方块位姿占场景对距离的99%），并检验该位姿信息是否分布于弯曲高维流形而非固定线性子空间（故无法事后线性编辑编码器）；2) 测试时鲁棒性攻击：对单帧输入施加白盒ℓ∞扰动（ε=4/255、8/255），分别扰动目标图像与实时观测，并与两倍幅度的均匀随机噪声对照；3) 在Push-T、Two-Room、Reacher、OGBench-Cube四个LeWM标准基准上验证攻击的可迁移性与潜距离桥接比例。

Result: 潜距离中方块状态高度主导：单像素方块位移引起的嵌入变化为等效智能体位移的3倍，仅交换方块位姿即占场景对潜距离的99%；但该信息分布于弯曲高维流形，无法简单线性编辑。攻击方面，ε=4/255的单帧ℓ∞扰动可在73–95%场景中使编码器将方块注册为攻击者选择的任意位姿，且不扰乱智能体状态编码；扰动目标图像或实时观测使Push-T规划成功率由87%降至32%，而两倍幅度均匀随机噪声影响可忽略；该脆弱性在四个基准上均存在，ε=8/255时在各环境桥接82–97%的平方潜距离，规划退化程度与任务对所伪造实体的依赖度成正比。

Conclusion: JEPA/LeWM的潜距离规划接口存在结构性测试时脆弱性：度量几何由可欺骗的物体状态主导且呈弯曲流形，微小对抗扰动即可在不影响智能体编码的前提下劫持目标匹配，导致规划失效。这表明基于潜空间距离的世界模型规划需要显式的鲁棒性与度量几何防护。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) have recently emerged as general-purpose visual world models for control. A prominent example is LeWorldModel (LeWM), which trains end-to-end from pixels using an isotropic Gaussian anti-collapse regularizer (SIGReg) and plans by minimizing the squared Euclidean distance between predicted and goal embeddings. In this work, we investigate the test-time robustness of this latent planning interface. Analyzing the learned metric on Push-T, we first find that task-relevant object state heavily dominates latent distance: shifting the block by a single pixel displaces the embedding $3\times$ further than an equivalent agent shift, and swapping the block pose alone accounts for $99\%$ of the distance between scene pairs. However, because this pose information is distributed across a curved, high-dimensional manifold rather than a fixed linear subspace, the encoder cannot be trivially edited post-hoc to isolate or redirect it. We next demonstrate that this geometry is highly vulnerable at test time. A white-box $\ell_\infty$ perturbation of just $ε{=}4/255$ applied to a single input frame causes the encoder to register the block at an arbitrary, attacker-chosen pose in $73$--$95\%$ of scenes without disturbing the encoded agent state. Perturbing either the goal image or the live camera observation drops Push-T planning success from $87\%$ to $32\%$, whereas uniform random noise at twice the magnitude has negligible impact. This vulnerability persists across all four standard LeWM benchmarks (Push-T, Two-Room, Reacher, and OGBench-Cube). At $ε{=}8/255$, the attack bridges $82$--$97\%$ of the squared latent distance to the target embedding in every environment, inducing planning degradation proportional to each task's reliance on the spoofed entity.

</details>


### [80] [Real-VAS: a Real-World Video Amodal Segmentation Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00430)
*Finlay G. C. Hudson,William A. P. Smith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Real-VAS：基于真实图像视频合成的大规模零样本评估数据集，用程序化生成管线获得精确的amodal真值，覆盖动态遮挡与三类容器交互场景，为真实视频amodal分割提供兼具真实性与合成级精度的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: amodal视频目标分割缺乏兼具真实世界复杂性与精确真值标注的数据集；人工估计标注不精确，3D重建成本高昂。

Method: 从单一未遮挡源派生目标掩码、遮挡边界与合成图像，程序化构造一致的amodal真值，无需人工估计或3D重建；构建两类场景：由两段运动目标片段合成的动态遮挡场景，以及容器类场景（表面承载、铰接承载、移动承载），后者依据容器跟踪变换模拟内部目标运动以生成精确真值。

Result: 构建了大规模零样本评估数据集，涵盖多样化、高难度的真实视频场景，同时具备合成数据级别的标注精度，可用于评估amodal分割模型。

Conclusion: Real-VAS以真实图像合成方式弥合真实性与标注精度之间的鸿沟，为真实视频amodal分割提供挑战性基准；数据集与生成代码将公开。

Abstract: Amodal video object segmentation is fundamentally limited by the absence of datasets that combine real-world complexity with precise ground-truth annotations. To address this, we present Real Video Amodal Segmentation (Real-VAS), a new large-scale, zero-shot evaluation dataset built from composited real-image video. We introduce a novel data generation pipeline that enables the creation of programmatically consistent amodal ground truth for real-world footage, in which the object mask, occlusion boundary, and composited image are all derived from a single unoccluded source, without relying on human estimation or expensive 3D reconstruction. Our dataset is structured into two challenging scenarios: dynamic Occlusion, created by compositing two clips of moving objects, and a unique Container category featuring complex, physically constrained interactions. These container scenarios--On Surface Containment, Articulated Containment, and Mobile Containment--allow us to generate precise ground truth by simulating an object's motion based on its container's tracked transformation. As a result, Real-VAS provides a diverse and challenging benchmark for evaluating amodal segmentation models on realistic video with the precision of synthetic data. The dataset and our generation code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [81] [PRISM-Net: Dynamic Routing Network with VLM-Guided Disentangled Attribute Distillation for Explainable Fine-Grained Product Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00458)
*Chung-Ming Lo,Che-Wei Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRISM-Net，一种相位路由的可解释语义混合专家网络，将整体视觉特征解耦为线条与颜色专家通路，无需人工属性标注即可实现细粒度商品检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电商多媒体系统的细粒度商品检索需要同时处理宏观类别语义与微观视觉属性（结构轮廓"线条"、色彩"颜色"），但CNN与ViT等常规骨干网络存在特征纠缠与捷径学习问题，且获取人工属性标注成本高昂。

Method: PRISM-Net为可解释多专家架构，由条件元路由器（meta-router）调控。阶段一：引入外部VLM引导的Sigmoid语义蒸馏机制，配合梯度截断的路由门控，利用SigLIP的零样本文本提示监督专门的结构专家与色彩专家。阶段二：通过学习的元路由门控将解耦的专家嵌入与宏观基础特征自适应融合，以校准类别边界。

Result: 在275,454张电商图像（18类）上做5折交叉验证，并在55,091查询对220,363图像库的全规模检索基准上测试SwinV2、ConvNeXt-V2与DINOv3。SwinV2上，全折mAP@10由80.98%提升至83.90%（+2.92%），线条mAP@10提升+5.74%，颜色mAP@10提升+9.12%。元路由门控的无监督分布分析显示，ViT与CNN骨干均涌现性地将最高达6.8倍的结构线条权重分配给刚性家电与五金，并将>96%的色彩权重分配给服装。

Conclusion: PRISM-Net通过VLM蒸馏实现无标注的线条/颜色特征解耦，并通过元路由融合显著提升细粒度商品检索性能，且门控分布具备可解释性，在多种骨干网络上均有效。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual product retrieval in large-scale e-commerce multimedia systems demands discriminative representations capable of simultaneously resolving macro-level category semantics and micro-level visual attributes such as structural contour (line) and chromatic appearance (color). However, conventional backbones including convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and vision Transformers (ViTs) inherently suffer from feature entanglement and shortcut learning. To learn the micro-level visual attributes without costly manual attribute annotations, we propose phase-routed interpretable semantic mixture-of-experts network (PRISM-Net), an explainable multi-expert architecture that disentangles holistic visual features into disentangled line and color expert pathways governed by a conditional meta-router. In phase 1, we introduce an external VLM-Guided sigmoid semantic distillation mechanism with gradient-detached routing gates, utilizing zero-shot text prompts from SigLIP to supervise specialized structural and chromatic experts. In phase 2, the disentangled expert embeddings are adaptively fused with macro base features via a learned meta-router gate to calibrate category boundaries. Extensive 5-fold cross-validation across 275,454 e-commerce images (18 categories) and full-scale retrieval benchmarks (55,091 queries against a 220,363-image gallery) across SwinV2, ConvNeXt-V2, and DINOv3 demonstrate substantial gains. On SwinV2, PRISM-Net boosts full-fold product mAP@10 from 80.98% to 83.90% (+2.92%), line mAP@10 by +5.74%, and color mAP@10 by +9.12%. Furthermore, unsupervised distribution analysis of the meta-router gate reveals that both ViT and CNN backbones emergently allocate up to 6.8x higher structural line weights to rigid appliances and hardware while dedicating >96% chromatic weight to clothing.

</details>


### [82] [Spatial Uncertainty Analytics for Budget-Limited Inspection of Hyperspectral Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00478)
*Ritesh Sharma,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用分类器集成的不确定性空间结构，指导有限验证预算下无人机/地面核查站点的选址：以视野感知的贪心覆盖法选择不确定性质量最高的足迹，可覆盖最多57%的分类错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大范围高光谱调查所得土地覆盖图在类别边界与模糊区域最不可靠，误差呈空间结构化；而低空无人机或地面人员只能核查极少数站点，因此"去哪里核查"成为关键问题。

Method: 用光谱分类器集成的不一致度估计逐像素不确定性曲面；以贪心、视野（传感器足迹）感知的方式对不确定性质量进行覆盖式选址，并与不确定性无关采样、非重叠高不确定足迹选择、高斯过程选择等基线比较。

Result: 在七个基准场景中，最高不确定十分位像素的错误密度为整体的2.6–7.9倍；15个站点下贪心覆盖可覆盖至多57%的错误，为同区域不确定性无关采样的1.7–18倍；非重叠高不确定足迹选择效果相当；空间分块评估与光谱-空间分类器下优势仍存（但不一致度排序错误能力下降）；需将曲面限制在已标注土地覆盖上，否则全图不一致度峰值落在无法评分的未标注地面；最优策略依赖场景与阈值，高斯过程选择在全部场景中覆盖的不确定性均低于覆盖贪心；流程可在普通CPU上运行。

Conclusion: 误差的空间结构可转化为高效的验证采样先验；收益来源于以不确定性质量对整个足迹打分，而非足迹互不重叠；实际部署需限定在可评分的已标注地类范围，且策略选择应视场景与阈值而定。

Abstract: Land-cover maps classified from wide-area hyperspectral surveys are least reliable along class boundaries and in ambiguous regions, so their errors are spatially structured. We study how this structure can direct a limited verification budget: when a low-altitude UAV or a ground crew can visit only a handful of sites, where it should go depends on how classifier uncertainty is arranged across the scene and how a fixed sensor footprint tiles that arrangement. We estimate a per-pixel uncertainty surface from the disagreement of an ensemble of spectral classifiers and select sites by greedy, field-of-view-aware coverage of the uncertainty mass. On seven benchmark scenes, the highest-uncertainty decile of pixels is 2.6 to 7.9 times richer in errors than the scene as a whole, and the coverage greedy covers up to 57% of the classifier's errors with 15 sites, 1.7 to 18 times as many as uncertainty-agnostic sampling of the same labelled region. A non-overlapping selection of the footprints richest in uncertainty does equally well, so the gain comes from scoring whole footprints by uncertainty mass. The advantage persists under spatially blocked evaluation and with a spectral-spatial classifier, although disagreement then ranks errors less well. It requires restricting the surface to labelled land cover: over the whole scene, disagreement peaks on unlabelled ground, where inspections cannot be scored. The best policy for covering connected high-uncertainty regions depends on the scene and on the threshold that defines them, and Gaussian-process selection covers less uncertainty than the coverage greedy on every scene. The pipeline runs on commodity CPUs.

</details>


### [83] [A Straight Line Is Not a Camera Pose: Auditing Short-Clip Pose Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00482)
*Xinyi Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATE（绝对轨迹误差）作为SLAM与三维重建的相机运动精度标准指标存在两处结构性缺陷，作者据此提出姿态锚定ATE（pose-anchored ATE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Sintel协议上，一条不使用任何图像的匀速直线预测，其平均ATE竟低于11个近期模型中10个官方代码复现的结果，说明ATE无法可靠区分“真实几何精度”与“退化预测”，需要诊断其失效机理并给出修正指标。

Method: 从两方面剖析ATE：(1) 指出其对齐将任意匀速预测映射到真实路径的最佳拟合直线，故直线得分等于路径回归残差，并引入残差信噪比（SNR）准则预测“向匀速拟合靠拢是否降低ATE”；(2) 指出仅位置的Umeyama对齐忽略相机朝向，构造将轨迹相对朝向旋转30°的对照实验，测量重投影误差变化。进而提出pose-anchored ATE：将对齐旋转拟合到预测朝向，仅改动一个函数，单clip耗时0.17 ms（原ATE为0.11 ms）。

Result: SNR准则在1056个模型-clip对中预测正确率95%，792个留出对98%，1034个ScanNet对97%；可区分Sintel（中位SNR 0.86）与TUM、ScanNet、全长Bonn及Wild-SLAM（2.7–27，后者“拉直”会抬高所有模型ATE）。旋转30°对ATE无影响，但在Sintel、TUM、ScanNet主设置下恶化所有模型对静态点的重投影；Sintel上ATE第二优的直线在几何均值重投影上最差。pose-anchored ATE在Sintel上将全部无图像参考排在所有模型之下，旋转/拉直下与重投影误差相关性Spearman 0.61（ATE为0.32），基本保持模型排序，并在旋转与参考测试上与10帧平移RPE及DTE持平。

Conclusion: ATE的低值并不等价于更好的几何，因其对齐对匀速退化与朝向不敏感；pose-anchored ATE以极小开销修正对齐旋转，显著提升对几何退化的判别力与鲁棒性。代码、预测与结果将开源。

Abstract: The absolute trajectory error (ATE) is the standard measure of camera-motion accuracy, from SLAM to feed-forward 3D reconstruction. On the common Sintel protocol, a constant-velocity straight line using no images has a lower mean ATE than 10 of 11 recent models re-run with official code. Two properties of ATE's alignment explain this. First, it maps any constant-velocity prediction onto the best-fitting line through the true path, so the line scores the path's regression residual. A residual signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) criterion predicts when moving a prediction towards its constant-velocity fit lowers ATE. It gives the outcome for 95% of 1056 model-clip pairs, 98% of 792 held-out pairs and 97% of 1034 ScanNet pairs, and it separates Sintel (median SNR 0.86) from TUM, ScanNet and full-length Bonn and Wild-SLAM (2.7 to 27), where straightening raises every model's mean ATE. Second, position-only alignment ignores where cameras look: rotating a trajectory by 30 degrees against its orientations leaves ATE unchanged, yet worsens the reprojection of static points for every model on Sintel, TUM and ScanNet in the main setting, and the line, second by mean ATE on Sintel, places them worst by geometric mean: a lower ATE need not mean better geometry. Fitting the alignment's rotation to the predicted orientations gives pose-anchored ATE, a one-function change in ATE's units costing 0.17 ms per clip (ATE: 0.11 ms). It ranks every image-free reference below every model on Sintel, tracks the reprojection error under rotation and straightening, where ATE does not (Spearman 0.61 vs. 0.32), largely preserves the models' order, and ties 10-frame translational relative pose error (RPE) and the discernible trajectory error (DTE) on our rotation and reference tests. Code, predictions and results will be released.

</details>


### [84] [Selecting or Solving? What Best-of-N Verification Delivers in Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00507)
*Martin Q. Ma,Yuxiao Qu,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Louis-Philippe Morency,Paden Tomasello,Juan Pino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出"best-of-N选择"管线常用单一headline数字（相对多数投票的超出量）汇报，会混淆两个效果；作者提出Selection Increment (SI)指标将二者分离，并在五个长视频基准上分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频复杂问答本质是grounding问题：答案依赖数秒证据，VLM在定位证据上远低于人类。标准增加测试时计算的方式是best-of-N选择（采样多条CoT再由第二个模型重看视频挑选）。此类管线只用单一headline数字（相对majority vote的超出量）报告，该数字混淆了两个部署后果相反的效果：(1)验证器在候选中做选择带来的增益；(2)验证器若能自己解题本可提供的增益。

Method: 定义Selection Increment (SI)=选择准确率−同一验证器在"不给候选"时的准确率，即隔离出"选择"本身的贡献；同时测量相对self-consistency（多数投票）的margin。在五个长视频基准上评测多种验证器。

Result: SI对最弱验证器最大（自身解题差→候选留出的帮助空间大）；但"击败多数投票"呈相反画像：投票接近上限处没有验证器能超越，低于上限时只有最强验证器能超越。Video-Holmes上两效果在同一候选池上分离：弱验证器从候选中+19.3点却仍低于投票7.1点；前沿验证器增益最小却击败投票。最弱验证器在EgoSchema上出现按选项在列表中位置而非视频内容选择的失效模式。

Conclusion: 若把SI当作"优劣评分"（merit score），会把未能击败多数投票的验证器排到榜首；若当作"审计"（audit），则能说明headline数字究竟交付的是两种效果中的哪一种。作者主张分离而非混同这两个效果。

Abstract: Answering a complex question about a long video is a grounding problem: the answer turns on a few seconds of evidence, and vision-language models remain well below human accuracy at finding them. The standard way to spend extra test-time compute on this kind of video reasoning is best-of-N selection: sample several chain-of-thought answers from a fixed model, then let a second model re-watch the video and pick one. Such pipelines are reported by a single headline number, their margin over a majority vote. That number conflates two effects with opposite deployment consequences: the benefit the second model adds by selecting among candidates, and the benefit it would have supplied by solving the question itself. We separate them, measuring the Selection Increment (SI) -- selection accuracy minus the same verifier's accuracy with the candidates withheld -- alongside the margin over self-consistency, across five long-video benchmarks. The Selection Increment is largest for the weakest verifiers: a verifier that solves poorly on its own leaves the candidates the most room to help. Beating the majority vote follows the opposite profile: where the vote already sits near its ceiling no verifier we test improves on it; below that ceiling only the strongest do. On Video-Holmes, the two come apart on a single pool of candidates -- a weak verifier gains +19.3 points from the candidates yet lands 7.1 points below the vote, while the frontier verifier gains the least and beats the vote. The weakest verifier also shows a failure mode: on EgoSchema it picks by where an option sits in the list rather than by what the video shows. Read as a merit score, SI puts a verifier that fails to beat a majority vote at the top of its ranking; read as an audit, it says which of the two effects a headline number delivers. Code is available at https://github.com/facebookresearch/best-n-selection-video-reasoning.

</details>


### [85] [SASFormer: Stage-Aware Selective Attention Transformer for All-in-One Blind Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00523)
*Xiangpeng Tian,Mingyang Li,Fajing Liu,Xiaofeng Wang,Xiaohai He,Chao Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SASFormer，一种阶段感知选择性注意力Transformer，编码器用混合通道-空间注意力、解码器用自适应选择性注意力，实现全一盲图像复原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一复原方法（混合注意力、提示学习、路由机制）多聚焦退化自适应性提升，却在各网络阶段采用相似的特征建模策略，忽略了编码与解码在不同退化下对表征需求的差异。

Method: 遵循阶段感知设计：编码器采用成熟的混合通道-空间注意力构建内容保持且退化感知的鲁棒表征；解码器采用自适应选择性注意力。具体设计ASCSA（自适应查询-通道选择与注意力掩码）和ASSSA（多窗口尺寸空间注意力自适应融合），并以紧凑隐式提示调制选择过程，无需显式退化标签。

Result: 在3、5、11种退化设置上的大量实验表明，SASFormer在较好参数效率下取得强复原性能；逐阶段表征分析与消融实验验证了阶段感知设计与解码端自适应选择的有效性。

Conclusion: 区分编码/解码阶段表征需求是统一盲图像复原的有效设计原则，解码端自适应选择性注意力可在不增加显式标签依赖的前提下提升复原质量与参数效率。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration aims to recover high-quality images from multiple degradation types using a single unified model. Recent methods improve unified restoration with hybrid attention, prompt learning, or routing mechanisms, but generally focus on enhancing degradation adaptivity while employing similar feature modeling strategies across network stages. Consequently, the distinct representation requirements of encoding and decoding under diverse degradations remain insufficiently explored. In this paper, we propose SASFormer, a Stage-Aware Selective Attention Transformer for all-in-one blind image restoration. SASFormer follows a stage-aware design principle: the encoder uses established hybrid channel-spatial attention to build robust content-preserving and degradation-aware representations, while the decoder employs adaptive selective attention for restoration-oriented reconstruction. To address the stage-specific requirements of decoder reconstruction, we develop Adaptive Selective Channel Self-Attention (ASCSA) and Adaptive Selective Spatial Self-Attention (ASSSA), enabling the decoder to dynamically identify restoration-relevant channel responses and spatial contexts. Specifically, ASCSA performs adaptive query-channel selection and attention masking, while ASSSA adaptively integrates spatial attention across multiple window sizes. Compact implicit prompts further modulate the selection process without requiring explicit degradation labels. Extensive experiments on 3-, 5-, and 11-degradation settings demonstrate that SASFormer achieves strong restoration performance with favorable parameter efficiency. Moreover, stage-wise representation analysis and ablation studies provide empirical support for the proposed stage-aware design and the effectiveness of decoder-side adaptive selection.

</details>


### [86] [3D Neural Rendering for Automated Ultrasonic Laser Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00527)
*Thomas Pierson,Matthew Mitchell,Kira Perna,Anand Iyer,Eric Flynn,Adam Wachtor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将6-DOF机械臂与复值NeRF（complex-nerfacto）结合，从激光多普勒测振（LDV）扫描重建三维零件稳态表面波场，在留出视角上ZNCC达0.60（RGB基线0.28）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDV扫描可用于零件出厂前的缺陷与裂纹检测，但三维复杂零件表面波场的完整重建困难；且传统基于图像的位姿估计与实值（RGB）渲染管线会丢失相位信息、引入量化误差。

Method: xArm6六自由度机械臂内置压电换能器，将受激试件以一系列已知位姿呈现给固定LDV，得到带位姿的复值二维扫描；位姿由机械臂运动学解析给出而非图像位姿估计。据此训练complex-nerfacto：在NeRF框架中把三通道RGB输出头替换为二通道输出，直接表征波场的实部与虚部速度分量，保留复测量而不量化。

Result: 在焊接铝圆柱与焊接T形支架上以ZNCC评估：复值管线在留出视角平均ZNCC为0.60，同数据渲染图像训练的RGB nerfacto基线为0.28；重建波场可支持复杂构件的缺陷检测与表征。

Conclusion: 复值神经渲染+机器人自动扫描可实现稳态表面波场重建并用于缺陷检测；剩余主要障碍是仅凭稀疏LDV扫描进行几何重建，这限制了实际部署。

Abstract: Laser doppler vibrometer (LDV) scans can be used to detect defects and cracks in manufactured parts before they are deployed. This work presents a method for reconstructing the steady-state surface wavefield of a three-dimensional part by combining automated robotic manipulation with neural rendering. A six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) robotic manipulator (UFactory xArm6) with an embedded piezoelectric transducer presented an acoustically excited test article to a stationary LDV at a sequence of known orientations, yielding a set of complex-valued 2D scans with poses derived analytically from the arm kinematics rather than from image-based pose estimation. These scans were used to train a novel complex-nerfacto model, a modified Neural Radiance Field (NeRF) pipeline that replaces the standard three-channel RGB output head with a two-channel output. Complex-nerfacto represents the real and imaginary velocity components of a wavefield, preserving the complex measurement without quantization. Reconstructions of a welded aluminum cylinder and a welded T-bracket were evaluated against measured scans using Z-normalized cross-correlation (ZNCC). The complex-valued pipeline achieved a mean ZNCC of 0.60 at held-out viewing angles, compared with 0.28 for a baseline RGB nerfacto model trained on rendered images of the same data. The resulting surface wavefield reconstructions supported defect detection and characterization on complex components. We identify geometry reconstruction from sparse LDV scans as the remaining obstacle to practical deployment.

</details>


### [87] [RATR: Training-Free Material Rebinding via Role Assignment and Temporal Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00551)
*Jiehui Tang,Yunlong Yu,Zhao Wang,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无训练方法 RATR，通过为条件图像赋予互补角色（目标条件仅提供结构、参考条件仅提供材质）解决材质重绑定中的角色纠缠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材质重绑定方法难以兼顾材质保真度与结构保持，根源在于目标条件与参考条件中结构与外观线索相互耦合，造成"角色纠缠"。

Method: 基于扩散图像编辑器的无训练框架：对目标条件引入 albedo 解耦的表面释放机制，抑制原表面外观、保留几何结构；对参考条件设计材质证据隔离机制，突出材质线索、削弱物体特有结构干扰，并在去噪过程中协调二者的影响。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果（如指标数值或对比实验数据）。

Conclusion: 通过条件角色解耦与去噪阶段协调，RATR 有望在无需训练的前提下缓解材质保真与结构保持的权衡，实现更优的材质重绑定。

Abstract: Material rebinding aims to transfer the material appearance from a reference image while preserving the target object's geometry and identity. Existing methods typically struggle to balance material fidelity with structural preservation due to the coupled influence of structural and appearance cues in their visual conditions. We identify role entanglement in the target and reference conditions as a contributing factor the target condition carries both geometry and its original appearance while the reference condition contains material cues alongside object-specific structure. To address this issue, we propose RATR, a training-free material rebinding method built on a diffusion-based image editor that assigns complementary roles to the two conditions and coordinates their influence during denoising. To make the target condition primarily convey structure, we introduce an albedo-resolved surface release mechanism, which suppresses the target's original surface appearance while retaining geometric cues, yielding a structure-oriented condition for material transfer. On the reference side, the designed material evidence isolation mechanism emphasizes material cues while reducing interference from object-specific structure, yielding a complementary material-oriented condition.

</details>


### [88] [Spatial-Spectral Interactive POCS Network for Large-Gap Seismic Data Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00556)
*Baisong Jiang,Chunxia Zhang,Anxiang Di,Xiaoli Wei,Xiangyu Zhao,Deng Xiong,Chuangji Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出空间-谱交互的POCS展开网络SSI-POCS，融合Restormer局部先验与快速傅里叶卷积全局上下文，实现大缺口地震道插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大地震道缺失会破坏反射同相轴并阻碍谱恢复；现有深度展开插值网络主要依赖局部空间先验，难以聚合重建大范围缺失区所需的全局上下文，问题高度病态。

Method: 将经典POCS迭代求解器展开为网络，通过学习变换、软阈值与精确数据回插实现物理一致性与双域深度先验结合；先验中空间Restormer分支捕获局部波形结构，FFC分支提取全局上下文；双域融合模块以双向通道注意力和门控调制交换互补特征；采用SSIM监督并结合焦点频率损失（FFL）与变换一致性约束。

Result: 在合成盐丘与北海实际数据上，50%随机与连续道去除实验中，SSI-POCS显著提升大缺口处同相轴连续性并抑制伪影，相较最佳对比方法SNR提升3.285 dB，参数量减少75.66%。

Conclusion: 双域（空间-f-k）交互的展开式POCS框架与结构化监督可有效应对大缺口地震插值，在性能与参数效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Large gaps in seismic acquisition disrupt reflection events and hinder spectral recovery. Existing seismic interpolation deep unfolding networks primarily rely on localized spatial priors, limiting their ability to aggregate the global context necessary for reconstructing these extensive missing regions. We propose a spatial-spectral interactive projection-onto-convex-sets network (SSI-POCS) to tackle this severely ill-posed problem. By unfolding the classical iterative solver, our network integrates physical consistency with dual-domain deep priors via learned transforms, soft thresholding, and exact data reinsertion. Within the learned prior, a spatial Restormer branch captures local waveform structures, while a fast Fourier convolution (FFC) branch extracts global context, effectively suppressing incoherent components and enhancing spectral energy concentration in the $f-k$ domain. A dual-domain fusion module exchanges these complementary features via bidirectional channel attention and gated modulation, yielding substantial interpolation improvements. To overcome the severe loss of lateral continuity caused by large gaps, we introduce structural similarity (SSIM) supervision, complemented by focal frequency loss (FFL) and transform consistency constraints to suppress incoherent high-frequency components and regularize training. Experiments on a synthetic salt dome and a North Sea field dataset under 50% random and consecutive trace removal demonstrate the effectiveness of SSI-POCS. It significantly enhances event continuity across large gaps and mitigates artifacts, achieving a 3.285~dB SNR improvement over the best competing method while reducing parameters by 75.66%.

</details>


### [89] [Structured Channel Network Pruning with Bi-Sparse Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00566)
*Jingjing Liu,Ziyang Wang,Xianchao Xiu,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双稀疏矩阵分解(BSMF)的结构化通道剪枝方法，用共享基上的输出通道系数分解卷积权重，并以行向ℓ2,p与列向ℓ2,q(p,q∈[0,1))正则化刻画共享冗余与个体通道贡献，ADMM求解后重构滤波器并剪除冗余通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化通道剪枝多基于单个滤波器幅值计算通道重要性分数，难以区分"共享冗余"与"个体通道贡献"，导致重要性度量失真、剪枝不够精准。

Method: 将卷积权重矩阵建模为共享基上的输出通道系数，同时施加行向ℓ2,p范数与列向ℓ2,q范数正则（p,q∈[0,1)，属文献未探索的非凸稀疏区间）；基于ADMM设计高效更新方案，各子问题均有闭式解；随后由学到的因子重构卷积滤波器，丢弃冗余通道获得紧凑CNN。

Result: 在CIFAR-10与ImageNet-1K上的大量实验表明，BSMF在保持较高重构精度的同时实现显著网络压缩。

Conclusion: 通过双稀疏分解将通道重要性建模为基共享结构下的系数稀疏性，可在非凸ℓ2,p/ℓ2,q(p,q<1)正则下获得闭式可解的高效优化，是一种兼顾压缩率与精度的结构化通道剪枝方案。

Abstract: Structured channel pruning for convolutional neural networks (CNNs) plays an important role in pattern recognition and deep learning. Nevertheless, existing methods usually calculate channel importance scores based on the magnitudes of individual filters, making it difficult to distinguish shared redundancy from individual channel contributions. To tackle this limitation, we propose a novel structured channel pruning method termed bi-sparse matrix factorization (BSMF). Specifically, BSMF characterizes the convolutional weight matrix as output-channel coefficients over a shared basis with row-wise $\ell_{2,p}$-norm and column-wise $\ell_{2,q}$-norm regularization terms, respectively. Note that $p,q\in[0,1)$, which, to our best knowledge, has not been explored in the literature. To solve the resulting non-convex optimization problem, we develop an efficient update scheme built upon the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), in which all subproblems admit closed-form solutions. After that, a compact CNN is obtained by reconstructing the convolutional filters from the learned factors and discarding the redundant channels. Extensive experiments on the CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-1K datasets demonstrate that our proposed BSMF achieves significant network compression while maintaining high reconstruction accuracy. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/BSMF

</details>


### [90] [Words Before Pixels: Selective Modality Routing for Vision-Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00608)
*Laura Yao,Haochen Zhang,Jinhao Duan,Sijia Liu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ModRoute：一种梯度引导的逐样本模态路由策略，将高视觉压力样本转为纯文本形式、其余保留多模态，从而在VLM遗忘中兼顾行为抑制与视觉grounding保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM遗忘研究多聚焦于目标函数设计，默认图像-文本原始形式即为合适的遗忘输入，忽视了"每个遗忘样本应以何种模态表征"这一数据中心问题；作者通过模态分解实验检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 构建VLM基准的模态分解版本（仅图像、仅文本、多模态），在同一批样本上进行受控的模态混合对比实验；据此提出ModRoute，一种基于梯度的逐样本模态路由策略，选择性把高视觉压力样本转换为纯文本形式，其余样本保持多模态。

Result: 不安全指令遵循行为通过文本表征比仅用图像更易被抑制；但纯文本遗忘在目标行为依赖视觉证据时会削弱视觉grounding。在VLGuard与LLaVA-1.5-7B上，γ=0.5时ModRoute取得最高综合遗忘-效用分数0.847，较最佳随机切换提升2.5%，比纯多模态或纯文本遗忘高出≥46%。

Conclusion: 有效的VLM遗忘不仅取决于优化目标，还取决于遗忘数据的构成与逐样本模态路由；数据中心的模态选择是与目标设计同等重要的维度。

Abstract: Vision-language model (VLM) unlearning is often treated as an objective-design problem: given multimodal forget data, the goal is to optimize a loss that removes undesirable behavior while preserving retained capabilities. We argue that this view overlooks an equally important data-centric question: how should each forget example be represented before optimization? Existing methods typically keep image-conditioned failures in their original image-text form, implicitly assuming that pixels are the right route for unlearning whenever pixels appear in the input. We challenge this assumption by constructing modality-decomposed versions of VLM benchmarks, enabling controlled comparisons among image-only, text-only, and multimodal representations of the same examples. Our modality-mixing experiments show that unsafe instruction-following behavior is more effectively suppressed through textual representations than through images alone, while purely text-only unlearning can still weaken visual grounding when the target behavior depends on visual evidence. Motivated by this trade-off, we introduce ModRoute, a gradient-guided per-sample modality routing strategy that selectively converts high visual-pressure examples to text-only form while keeping the remaining examples multimodal. Notably, on VLGuard with LLaVA-1.5-7B, ModRoute has the strongest composite unlearning-utility score of 0.847 at $γ=0.5$, a 2.5% improvement over the best random-switching score and $\geq$46% better than pure multimodal or text unlearning. Overall, our results show that effective VLM unlearning depends not only on the objective, but also on the composition and per-sample modality routing of the unlearning data.

</details>


### [91] [Regularized Wasserstein Distance for Discrete Generative Modeling and Clustering Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00653)
*Senmao Ye,Fei Liu,Dawei Zhang,Yuqian Zheng,Jun Zhang,Zhonglong zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将聚类分析建模为离散生成建模：在有限候选空间上约束生成器，生成样本自然成为数据分布的代表性原型；以正则化Wasserstein距离替代DDN的l2损失，并用随机近邻选择替代硬最近邻，形成离散生成聚类(DGC)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将聚类重新表述为离散生成建模，使生成器的输出（来自有限候选空间的样本）天然成为最能代表数据分布的原型。动机核心是解决DDN因硬最近邻选择导致的训练不稳定，并改善几何一致性。

Method: 1) 将聚类形式化为离散生成建模，生成器限制在有限候选空间，用离散分布网络(DDN)建模该离散候选空间；2) 用真实图像与生成图像经验分布之间的正则化Wasserstein距离替换DDN的图像间l2损失，以获得平滑优化目标并鼓励几何一致性；3) 用随机近邻选择替代DDN的最近邻(硬)选择，避免多步前向中的密度偏移问题。所得框架称为离散生成聚类(DGC)。

Result: DGC具有四项优势：(1) 学到的聚类中心可直接解释，因其为逼真的合成样本；(2) 推断代价随簇数K呈对数增长，即O(log K)，可扩展至极大K；(3) 正则化Wasserstein距离提供平滑良态的优化目标，促进稳定收敛，而DDN的l2损失易收敛至较差的局部最优；(4) 以随机近邻选择替代最近邻选择，规避多步前向中的密度偏移。多个基准上的大量实验验证了DGC的有效性与上述性质，代码已公开于GitHub。

Conclusion: 聚类可有效转化为有限候选空间上的离散生成建模问题；正则化Wasserstein目标与随机近邻选择共同解决了DDN的不稳定性与密度偏移，使DGC在可解释性、可扩展性、优化稳定性上兼具优势，实验与开源代码支持其有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we formulate clustering analysis as discrete generative modeling. Specifically, when the generator is restricted to a finite candidate space, the generated samples naturally emerge as the most representative prototypes of the underlying data distribution. To model such a discrete candidate space, we employ a Discrete Distribution Network (DDN). However, DDN suffers from unstable training due to its hard nearest-neighbor selection. To stabilize training and encourage geometric consistency, we replace DDN's l2 loss between images with regularized Wasserstein distance between the empirical distributions of real and generated images. The resulting framework, termed Discrete Generative Clustering (DGC), offers four notable advantages: 1) the learned cluster centers are directly interpretable, as they are realistic synthetic samples; 2) the inference cost scales logarithmically with the number of clusters, i.e., O(log K), making the method scalable to extremely large K; 3) the regularized Wasserstein distance provides a smooth and well-behaved optimization objective that facilitates stable convergence, whereas DDN's l2 loss tends to converge to poor local optima; and 4) DGC replaces DDN's nearest-neighbor selection with random-neighbor selection, which avoids the density shift problem during multi-step forward. Extensive experiments on several benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and desirable properties of DGC. The code is publicly available at GitHub.

</details>


### [92] [SE-Mem: Semantic-Episodic Memory for Scene-Consistent Generative View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00658)
*Changsong Lei,Yihan Li,Yu-Hui Wen,Huankun Sheng,Ming Li,Yeying Fan,Minnan Luo,Yong-Jin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SE-Mem，受人类语义记忆与情景记忆启发，将历史观测组织为带语义重要性分数的结构化3D表示，并用两阶段记忆检索选取信息量最大的帧作为上下文，从而在新视角视频合成中兼顾全局场景一致性与细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机条件交互式视频生成器在用户自定义轨迹下难以保持全局场景一致性；现有方法仅依据相机位姿选择参考帧，忽略了内容是否语义相关甚至是否可见。

Method: 借鉴人类语义记忆（跨经验的泛化知识）与情景记忆（特定过去事件与上下文相关信息）的互补作用，构建SE-Mem框架：以结构化3D表示组织历史观测，并为各元素附加语义重要性分数；设计两阶段记忆检索策略，同时考虑语义显著性与几何可见性，筛选最具信息量的帧提供上下文引导。

Result: 大量评估表明，该方法提升了视觉质量，并在多个基准上取得有竞争力的几何一致性。

Conclusion: 融合语义与情景记忆的检索式记忆机制可有效改善新视角合成的语义连贯性与细粒度视觉细节，是缓解交互式视频生成全局一致性问题的可行方向。

Abstract: Generative view synthesis aims to synthesize novel-view videos from reference observations along user-defined camera trajectories. Maintaining global scene consistency throughout this process remains challenging for camera-conditioned interactive video generators. Existing approaches typically select reference frames based solely on camera poses, overlooking whether the content remains semantically relevant or even visible. To address these limitations, we draw inspiration from semantic and episodic memory in human cognition, in which semantic memory captures generalized knowledge across experiences, whereas episodic memory preserves information tied to specific past events and contexts. Motivated by the complementary roles of semantic and episodic memory, we introduce SE-Mem, a Semantic-Episodic Memory framework that organizes historical observations in a structured 3D representation and augments its elements with semantic importance scores. A two-stage memory retrieval strategy is designed to identify the most informative frames to provide contextual guidance. Taking into account both semantic significance and geometric visibility, SE-Mem preserves semantic coherence and fine-grained visual details during novel view synthesis. Extensive evaluations show that our approach improves visual quality and achieves competitive geometric consistency across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [93] [Smooth bounding box for object detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00676)
*Chao Zhou,Shenghua He,Fengchuang Xing,Han Yang,Qiangguo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出平滑边界框(SBB)策略，通过全局缩放校正(GSC)与区间缩放校正(ISC)统计性修正检测数据集中的边界框标注误差，并引入归一化残差波动(NRF)指标评估平滑效果，在不增加推理开销的前提下提升多种检测模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测模型高度依赖标注质量，而大规模数据集中普遍存在边界框定位噪声；现有标签噪声处理方法多针对图像分类，难以迁移到更复杂的边界框误差问题，相关研究仍不充分。

Method: 核心洞察是标注误差更多源自标注者因素而非图像内容，因而可采用统计校正：1) GSC对全局施加统一缩放因子以估计并修正全局平均误差；2) ISC按目标尺寸分区间分别估计校正因子，实现更精细校准，并设计了针对多参数优化的定制微调策略；同时提出NRF指标定量衡量平滑效应与训练稳定性。

Result: 在多个基准与多种模型架构上实验表明，GSC与ISC均显著优于强基线（含最新YOLOv11n），持续提升检测精度，且不增加推理开销。

Conclusion: 基于统计的边界框缩放校正是一种高效、即插即用的标签噪声缓解方案，可跨架构泛化；未来可扩展到更复杂的框形变换与多源噪声场景。

Abstract: Deep learning-based object detection models are highly dependent on the quality of annotated training data. However, large-scale datasets often contain inevitable label noise, with bounding box localization errors being a prevalent and impactful form in object detection. While label noise handling techniques are well-studied for image classification, their adaptation to the more complex problem of bounding box errors remains underexplored. To address this, we propose a novel Smooth Bounding Box (SBB) strategy to mitigate the adverse effects of bounding box annotation inaccuracies. Our key insight is that annotation errors are often linked to annotator factors rather than specific image content, allowing for a statistical correction approach. Based on object size distribution within datasets, we implement the SBB strategy through two methods: 1) Global Scaling Correction (GSC), which applies a uniform scaling factor to estimate and correct a global average error; and 2) Interval Scaling Correction (ISC), which categorizes objects by size into intervals and estimates a dedicated correction factor for each, enabling more precise, fine-grained calibration. For efficient optimization of multiple parameters in ISC, we design a tailored model fine-tuning strategy. Furthermore, we introduce a Normalized Residual Fluctuation (NRF) metric to quantitatively assess the smoothing effect and training stability. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently enhances detection accuracy across various model architectures. Both GSC and ISC achieve significant performance improvements over strong baselines, including the latest YOLOv11n, without adding inference overhead. The codes are available at https://github.com/Acui001/SBB.git.

</details>


### [94] [ROD-Dataset: A Pedestrian-Perspective Benchmark of Urban Obstacle Classes for YOLO-Based Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00681)
*Abtin Zandi,Ariyan Azami,Bardia Sabagh Kermani,Parsa Abbasian,Roza Ganjipour,Sarvin Nami,Hamed Farbeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出ROD数据集：24,000+图像、40,000+标注障碍、25类，融合29个公共数据集并新增德黑兰与多伦多两地视线高度图像，附带6个轻量YOLO检测器基准，召回率均超0.86。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市人行道上两类行人面临可避免风险：视障者在无预警情况下撞上障碍，分心行人对危险感知滞后甚至完全无感。视觉预警系统的保护能力受训练数据限制；现有公开基准多来自车载、航拍或固定机架，通用检测器忽略致绊的街道设施（台阶、井盖、长椅等），行人相关数据集又局限于单一地区且类别体系不兼容，缺乏合适训练集。

Method: 构建ROD（Real-time Obstacle Detection）数据集：将29个公开数据集统一到同一分类体系，并补充来自德黑兰和多伦多两座城市的视线高度（eye-level）图像；全部标签经人工校验，每批采集数据仅在紧凑检测器证明其可学习该类别后才纳入。在ROD上基准测试6个轻量YOLO检测器（nano与tiny变体）。

Result: 6个轻量YOLO检测器召回率均超0.86：YOLO12n召回最高（0.889），YOLO26n精确率最高（0.925），YOLOv9t以最少参数取得最佳mAP@0.50（0.882）。召回率与mAP的排序不一致。

Conclusion: 按mAP选模型并不能最小化漏检障碍，因此报告全部25类的逐类召回率。ROD为行人侧实时障碍检测提供了跨区域、类别统一的可靠训练与评测基础。

Abstract: Two groups of pedestrians face avoidable risk on the city sidewalk: people living with visual impairment, who reach an obstacle before anything warns them, and people walking with divided attention, who register a hazard late or not at all. A vision system can warn them only about obstacles it has learned to recognize, so its training data limits the protection it offers. No suitable training set exists: public benchmarks are recorded from vehicles, aerial platforms, or fixed rigs, general-purpose detectors omit the street furniture that causes trips, and pedestrian-focused collections are confined to single regions with incompatible class schemes. This paper introduces the Real-time Obstacle Detection (ROD) dataset: more than 24,000 images and 40,000 annotated obstacles across 25 classes, from vehicles and people to the stairs, manholes, traffic cones, and benches that general-purpose detectors overlook. Because street furniture differs between cities, ROD unifies twenty-nine public collections onto one taxonomy and adds eye-level imagery from two contrasting cities on two continents, Tehran and Toronto. Every label is human-verified, and each capture campaign is admitted only after a compact detector shows it can learn the class. Six lightweight YOLO detectors (nano and tiny variants) benchmarked on ROD all exceed 0.86 recall; YOLO12n attains the highest recall (0.889), YOLO26n the highest precision (0.925), and YOLOv9t the best mAP@0.50 (0.882) with the fewest parameters. Because the recall and mAP rankings disagree, selecting a model by mAP does not minimize missed obstacles, so per-class recall is reported for all 25 classes.

</details>


### [95] [Content-Preserving Principal Feature Blending for Training-Free Style Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00683)
*Le-Hoang Bui,Hoang-Nguyen Cao,Trung-Nghia Le,Minh-Triet Tran,Dinh-Khoi Vo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的内容保持主特征混合方法 CoPFB，在视觉自回归模型中去除风格残差中与内容对齐的分量以缓解内容泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考式风格个性化需复现风格参考的外观，同时保留内容提示指定的主体。但在视觉自回归模型中，占主导地位的参考特征会携带物体与空间信息，造成内容泄漏。

Method: CoPFB 无需训练、无可学习参数：从干净生成分支估计低秩内容基，并剔除风格残差中与内容对齐的分量；在前两个自回归尺度做秩一混合，随后三个尺度进行较弱的内容投影细化。

Result: 在 1,900 组 Parti 内容提示与 FineStyle 参考图组合上，相比 PFB+SAC，文本对齐由 0.293 提升至 0.297，调和分数由 0.391 提升至 0.393，但风格相似度由 0.590 降至 0.577。

Conclusion: 以内容基投影抑制内容泄漏可小幅改善文本对齐与综合平衡，但以牺牲风格相似度为代价，整体增益有限。

Abstract: Reference-based style personalization should reproduce the appearance of a style reference while preserving the subject specified by a content prompt. In visual autoregressive models, dominant reference features can also carry object and spatial information, causing content leakage. We propose Content-Preserving Principal Feature Blending (CoPFB), a training-free method that estimates a low-rank content basis from a clean generation branch and removes the content-aligned component of the style residual. CoPFB applies rank-one blending at the first two autoregressive scales, followed by weaker content-projected refinement at the next three scales. On 1,900 combinations of Parti content prompts and FineStyle reference images, CoPFB improves text alignment from 0.293 to 0.297 and the harmonic score from 0.391 to 0.393 over PFB+SAC, while style similarity decreases from 0.590 to 0.577. CoPFB requires no fine-tuning or learnable parameters. Project page: https://vdkhoi20.github.io/CoPFB/.

</details>


### [96] [Density-Aware Calibration for Resource-Efficient Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00684)
*Tien-Dat Dam,Huu-Thuan Huynh,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoreCalib：一种免训练、与量化器无关的校准集构建框架，通过临床问题分层与视觉模态聚类，在固定128对预算下提升医学VLM训练后量化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型计算与内存开销大，难以部署在资源受限的临床硬件上；PTQ虽无需重训练即可压缩，但其效果高度依赖校准数据，现有校准集（如COCO）与目标医学VQA分布不匹配。

Method: 按临床问题类别对数据分层；用冻结的DINOv2特征结合UMAP降维与HDBSCAN聚类识别代表性视觉模态；将选定图像扩展为类别均衡的问答对，构建紧凑且保持目标分布的校准集。该方法免训练且与量化器无关。

Result: 在Kvasir-VQA-x1上以固定128对预算，配合MASQuant、GPTQ、QIG三种量化器评估：CoreCalib在三者上均取得最佳METEOR，在MASQuant与GPTQ上取得最佳ROUGE-L；W4A8 MASQuant下METEOR由COCO校准的0.5672提升至0.6234，约恢复相对BF16退化量的41%。

Conclusion: CoreCalib可作为面向资源高效医学VLM部署的数据中心式组件，验证了校准数据分布对齐对PTQ性能的关键作用。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) show promise for clinical image understanding, but their computational and memory requirements hinder deployment on resource-constrained clinical hardware. Post-training quantization (PTQ) offers a practical compression strategy without retraining, yet its effectiveness depends strongly on the calibration data. We propose CoreCalib, a training-free and quantizer-agnostic framework for constructing compact calibration sets that preserve the target medical VQA distribution. CoreCalib stratifies data by clinical question class, identifies representative visual modes using frozen DINOv2 features with UMAP and HDBSCAN, and expands selected images into class-balanced question-answer pairs. On Kvasir-VQA-x1, we evaluate CoreCalib with MASQuant, GPTQ, and QIG using a fixed 128-pair budget. CoreCalib achieves the best METEOR with all three quantizers and the best ROUGE-L with MASQuant and GPTQ. With W4A8 MASQuant, it improves METEOR from 0.5672 with COCO calibration to 0.6234, recovering approximately 41% of the degradation relative to BF16. These results support CoreCalib as a data-centric component for resource-efficient medical VLM deployment. Project page: https://magnusdtd.github.io/CoreCalib/ .

</details>


### [97] [LoD-Loc v4: Towards Generalized Aerial Visual Localization via Panoptic LoD 3D Map Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00704)
*Shuaibang Peng,Chengming Zhang,Juelin Zhu,Xia Li,Xiaoya Cheng,Zeyu Cui,Shen Yan,Maojun Zhang,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出LoD-Loc v4，通过融合OSM、DEM与LoD资产构建统一的Panoptic LoD 3D地图，将航空视觉定位重构为全景配准任务，并构建AirPan数据集（85k图像）支撑全景感知，从而在无建筑场景下保持可靠定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LoD 3D地图的航空视觉定位方法几乎完全依赖建筑物要素，在农村或欠发达地区建筑稀疏或缺失时会导致灾难性定位失败，限制了方法的全球适用性。

Method: 1）融合OpenStreetMap、数字高程模型与LoD资产，构建统一的Panoptic LoD 3D地图，将定位问题重构为全景配准（panoptic registration）任务；2）将查询图像解析为离散的建筑"thing"实例与连续的道路、植被、水体等"stuff"区域，与该地图进行整体对齐以鲁棒优化相机位姿；3）设计自动化流水线构建AirPan——迄今最大的航空全景分割数据集，含85k图像及像素级精确标注，使全景分割模型具备显著零样本泛化能力。

Result: 大量实验表明LoD-Loc v4显著优于SOTA基线；最突出的是在无建筑场景中仍能维持可靠定位，彻底克服了以往以建筑为中心范式的灾难性失败。

Conclusion: 将定位要素从单一建筑扩展到全景（thing+stuff）配准，可有效解决稀疏建筑区域定位失效问题，结合大规模高质量数据集与零样本全景感知，实现了更通用、鲁棒的航空视觉定位。

Abstract: Aerial visual localization over Level-of-Detail (LoD) 3D Maps has recently gained significant traction due to its global scalability, storage efficiency, and privacy-preserving nature. However, existing methods rely almost exclusively on building elements, which leads to catastrophic localization failures in rural or undeveloped areas where buildings are sparse or non-existent. To address this, we construct a unified Panoptic LoD 3D Map by fusing OpenStreetMap (OSM), Digital Elevation Models (DEM), and LoD assets, reformulating the problem as a panoptic registration task. By parsing the query image into discrete building `thing' instances and continuous `stuff' regions comprising road, vegetation, and water, we establish a holistic alignment with this map to robustly optimize the camera pose. To enable reliable panoptic perception, we develop a new automated pipeline that constructs AirPan - the largest aerial panoptic segmentation dataset to date, comprising 85k images with pixel-accurate annotations. This empowers our panoptic segmentation model to exhibit remarkable zero-shot generalization capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LoD-Loc v4 consistently outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines by a significant margin. Most notably, it successfully maintains reliable localization in building-less scenarios, completely overcoming the catastrophic failures of prior building-centric paradigms.

</details>


### [98] [Adversarial robustness does not entail human-aligned categorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00715)
*Itay Reznik,Raz Lapid,Moshe Sipper,Tal Golan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对抗鲁棒性并不保证人类对齐的分类行为：四个鲁棒 ImageNet 分类器在争议性刺激上对个体被试选择的预测接近随机，模型-人类一致性显著低于人类-人类一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练已能改善部分人工视觉与生物视觉的对应关系，但鲁棒分类器是否获得与人类一致的分类行为尚不清楚，需检验鲁棒性与人类对齐是否为可分离属性。

Method: 利用预训练GAN优化潜变量，生成1,008个能诱导两两鲁棒分类器高置信分歧的"争议性刺激"；比较ResNet-101、ViT-B、ConvNeXt-L、Swin-L四个鲁棒ImageNet分类器与60名被试的二选一类别判断。先在各模型自身被优化的刺激上评估，再在针对其他模型对优化的刺激上评估，并扩展到17个未参与刺激生成的RobustBench模型。

Result: 在诱导模型间分歧的刺激上，模型预测个体单试次选择的准确率接近随机（48.6–51.3%），而其他被试多数判断的预测准确率达85.0–86.8%。纳入模型预测强度与被试自报置信度的分析显示，两个卷积模型存在弱正向的模型-人类对应。跨模型刺激上，四个鲁棒分类器均高于随机但低于人类多数判断；17个额外模型上平均模型-被试一致性为67.3%，而同一图像上平均被试-被试一致性为81.3%。

Conclusion: 存在模型特定的对齐失败，且模型-人类不匹配在鲁棒分类器中普遍存在并显著超过人类观察者间的分歧；对抗鲁棒性本身不能保证人类对齐的分类，鲁棒性与人类对齐应作为视觉识别系统的两个独立属性分别评估。

Abstract: Adversarial training can improve some forms of correspondence between artificial and biological vision, but whether robust classifiers acquire human-aligned classification behavior remains unclear. We generated 1,008 controversial stimuli by optimizing latent inputs to a pretrained GAN to elicit high-confidence disagreements between pairs of robust ImageNet classifiers. We compared four adversarially robust ImageNet classifiers - ResNet-101, ViT-B, ConvNeXt-L, and Swin-L - with two-alternative category judgments from 60 participants. We first evaluated each classifier on stimuli optimized to elicit disagreement with another model. Across classifiers, accuracy in predicting individual participants' single-trial category choices was near chance (48.6-51.3%), whereas majority judgments from other participants predicted responses with 85.0-86.8% accuracy. An analysis incorporating model prediction strength and participants' reported confidence found weak positive model-human correspondence for the two convolutional models. On stimuli optimized for disagreement between other model pairs, all four robust classifiers predicted human responses above chance but less accurately than predictions based on other participants' majority judgments; the same pattern held for 17 additional RobustBench models not used in stimulus generation. Across these 17 models, mean model-participant agreement was 67.3%, versus 81.3% mean participant-participant agreement on the same images. These results identify model-specific failures of human alignment and a model-human mismatch that persists across robust classifiers and substantially exceeds disagreement among human observers. Our findings show that adversarial robustness alone does not guarantee human-aligned categorization and suggest that robustness and human alignment should be evaluated as distinct properties of visual recognition systems.

</details>


### [99] [SAVIER: Source-Anchored Visual Inspection and Evidence-Guided Repair for Vietnamese Cultural Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00722)
*Gia-Huy Thai,Duc-Tien Khong,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAVIER 是一个免训练、单次修复的框架，在保持用户场景需求的同时修正生成图像中越南文化实体的描绘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成图像可能场景合理却缺失标识该文化实体身份的关键线索，而仅靠提示词层面的优化从不检查生成图像本身，难以纠正该类偏差。

Method: 从 Culture-TRIP 扩展提示词出发；Curator 分离与来源相关的文化线索和提示词保留需求；prompt-blind Observer 描述首张图像；Refiner 将差异转为至多三条正向指令，随后在相同种子与固定参考照片库下进行单次重生成。

Result: 在 ViCuP 的 50 条双语提示上，用 SDXL 与 FLUX.1-dev 对照冻结的人工核验视觉契约：契约满足率较 Culture-TRIP 分别由 55.6 提至 69.6、由 59.1 提至 67.3，并在 FLUX.1-dev 上提升 CAIRE 文化相关性。仅参考图消融显示契约增益主要来自参考照片；agent 角色在 FLUX.1-dev 上带来国家级相关性提升，且在官方 VQAScore 下不降低对原提示词的遵循度。

Conclusion: 图像级差异反馈加固定参考库的单次重生成，可有效提升文化实体描绘准确性并保持场景保真；参考照片贡献主要增益，agent 角色补充国家级文化相关性。

Abstract: We present SAVIER, a training-free, one-pass framework that repairs the depiction of a named Vietnamese cultural entity in a generated image while preserving the scene details requested by the user. The problem is hard because a plausible scene can miss the cues that identify the entity, and prompt-level refinement never inspects the image it produces. SAVIER starts from a Culture-TRIP expanded prompt; a Curator separates source-linked cultural cues from prompt-preservation requirements, a prompt-blind Observer describes the first image, and a Refiner turns the discrepancies into at most three positive instructions before one regeneration with the same seed and a fixed bank of reference photographs. On the 50 bilingual prompts of ViCuP, evaluated with SDXL and FLUX.1-dev against a frozen, human-verified visual contract, SAVIER raises contract fulfillment from 55.6 to 69.6 and from 59.1 to 67.3 over Culture-TRIP and raises CAIRE cultural relevance on FLUX.1-dev. A reference-only ablation that removes the agent repair shows that SAVIER's photographs account for most of the contract gain; the agent roles add country-level relevance on FLUX.1-dev without lowering adherence to the original prompt under the official VQAScore. Project page: https://oxyzgiahuy.github.io/savier/

</details>


### [100] [SceneBound: How Much Should a Streaming 3D Reconstruction Model Remember?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00756)
*Ziming Wang,Chuyue Gong,Chutian Wang,Jingmin Zhu,Hongliang Cao,Yuheng He,Xiuyu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无训练规则 SceneBound：仅当预测相机位姿与所有已存关键帧距离超过固定半径时才写入帧，关键帧数量受覆盖数常数倍约束，在多模型多记忆类型下取得最低轨迹误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式3D重建模型需将历史帧写入循环状态、测试时快速权重或KV缓存，写入的帧会改变后续相机坐标系并占用显存；既有无训练门控依赖当前帧信号决定写入，但其平均精度本质上由写入预算决定——均匀间隔无信号写入即可匹配九种已发表门控，且预算限定状态漂移，因此需要按几何覆盖而非信号来决定写什么。

Method: SceneBound：无训练规则，当预测相机位姿与每个已存关键帧的距离至少为固定半径时才写入该帧；关键帧数为访问位姿覆盖数的常数倍（覆盖数=使每个相机附近均有关键帧所需的最少视图数）；大场景中将位置回访的首帧重解码揭示漂移后封闭子图；四个已发布模型、三种记忆类型共用同一半径。

Result: 在CUT3R的循环状态与StreamVGGT的KV缓存中，单遍及九遍遍历场景均取得所评估已发表无训练规则中最低平均轨迹误差，关键帧仅增长3%至6%。

Conclusion: 写入预算而非信号主导门控精度，SceneBound以几何覆盖准则实现常数因子有界的内存增长与状态漂移界限，且免调参（单一半径）可跨模型与记忆类型迁移。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction models keep past video frames in a recurrent state, fast weights from test-time training, or a key-value (KV) cache. Each written frame shifts the coordinate frame of later cameras or occupies GPU memory. Training-free rules decide what to write from a signal of the current frame. How much they write accounts for their mean accuracy: spending the update budget of a gate at uniformly spaced frames, with no signal, matches nine published gates, and the budget bounds the drift of the state wherever the writes fall. We propose SceneBound, a training-free rule that writes a frame only when its predicted camera pose lies at least a fixed radius from every stored keyframe. Its keyframe count stays within a constant factor of the covering number of the visited poses, the fewest views that keep a keyframe near every camera, so the count grows with the places visited and stays flat when the camera returns. In large scenes, SceneBound closes a submap once decoding its first frame again reveals drift. SceneBound uses one radius across four released models and three memory types. In the recurrent state of CUT3R and the KV cache of StreamVGGT, it achieves the lowest mean trajectory error among the published training-free rules we evaluate, in one pass and over nine passes of a scene, where its keyframes grow by 3% to 6%.

</details>


### [101] [Old Is Not False: Valid-Time Memory for Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00758)
*Ziming Wang,Chuyue Gong,Hongliang Cao,Jingmin Zhu,Chutian Wang,Yuheng He,Xiuyu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReDate 将时序数据库的"有效时间"概念引入交互式视频世界模型：按智能体动作日志为每个检索出的 token 标注有效时间戳，使冻结的生成器在动作撤销后仍能正确读取当前状态，无需训练即可提升 FAR 与 HY-WorldPlay 的状态正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型依赖记忆库中的历史帧（带拍摄时间戳）生成新帧，且普遍把最新时间戳视为真值。该假设仅在状态不可回退时成立；智能体撤销动作后，区域的最新视图已过时、当前视图反而更旧。删除旧视图的记忆会丢失当前状态，保留旧视图的记忆则会把"过去"喂给生成器。作者由此引入时间数据库中的 valid time（内容生效时间）与存储时间的区分。

Method: ReDate 保留所有帧，并依据智能体动作日志为每个检索出的 token 打上其有效时间（valid time），而非拍摄/存储时间；冻结的生成器据此把被回退的区域读作"刚到达新状态"，因此无需任何训练。理论部分给出三条结论：(1) 信任最新时间戳正确的充要条件是每个当前视图的日期都晚于所有过时视图；(2) 若回退后没有新拍摄的视图，则仅动作日志能区分"已回退区域"与"最新区域"；(3) 当被移动 token 权重较小时，可依据自身预测生成的生成器从"重定日期"中获得的收益大于"删除"。另提出一个校准的状态度量，用于在撤销后检验任意记忆机制。

Result: 在一次撤销（undo）之后，ReDate 将 FAR 步骤中呈现当前状态的比例从 0.36 提升至 0.62，将 HY-WorldPlay 的对应比例从 0.003 提升至 0.19。

Conclusion: 时间戳的"存储时间"语义不适用于可逆的交互世界；以动作日志推导的有效时间重新标注记忆 token，是一种免训练、可直接作用于冻结生成器的记忆修复方案，显著缓解撤销带来的状态陈旧问题，并提供可复用的状态度量基准。

Abstract: Interactive video world models draw each frame from a memory bank of earlier frames stamped with their capture times. With frames, weights and noise fixed, reversing the timestamps of two views flips the state that generators of two families draw: they take the newest timestamp as the truth. That reading is right as long as states never return. After an agent undoes an action, the newest view of a region is outdated and the only view of the present is older; memories for changing worlds delete that older view, and memories that keep it hand the generator the past. Temporal databases separate when a record is stored from when its content holds, its valid time. ReDate keeps every frame and dates each retrieved token by its valid time, read from the agent's action log. The frozen generator then reads a reverted region as one that has just reached a new state, so ReDate needs no training. We prove that trusting the newest timestamp is right exactly when every current view is dated after every outdated one, that without a view taken after the return only the action log tells a reverted region from an up-to-date one, and that a generator able to draw from its own prediction gains more from re-dating than from deletion when the moved tokens keep little weight. After an undo, ReDate raises the share of FAR's steps showing the current state from 0.36 to 0.62 and HY-WorldPlay's from 0.003 to 0.19. We also propose a calibrated state metric to test any memory after undos.

</details>


### [102] [DADKC: Distribution-Aware Adaptive Differentiated Knowledge Consolidation for Cloth-Hybrid Lifelong Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00768)
*Yan Zhang,Minghao Long,Meiqi Wan,Yan Yang,Xin Yuan,Wei Wang,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DADKC框架，通过分布感知的自适应差异化知识整合，在无样本回放的CH-LReID设定下平衡衣物相关/无关知识并抑制遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际长期部署中，模型需同时应对跨摄像头/场景的域偏移与同一身份的不规则换装，形成Cloth-Hybrid Lifelong Person Re-identification (CH-LReID)任务；现有LReID的回放或蒸馏方法在无样本训练下难以充分建模阶段性域分布与跨阶段特征连续性。
