Method: 在Differentiated Knowledge Consolidation (DKC)范式上引入自适应分布建模，设计三个轻量模块：(1) Anchor Pool Adapter (APA) 检索可学习锚点线索以精化身份特征；(2) Phase Path Mixer (PPM) 进行阶段感知的专家路由以整合域特定分布路径；(3) Stage Bridge Alignment (SBA) 约束相邻阶段间base、anchor与path表示的一致性。

Result: 在CH-LReID与LReID基准上，相比DKC基线，Order-1提升4.1%/4.5% mAP/R1，Order-2提升3.6%/3.4% mAP/R1；加权遗忘率由2.4%/1.7%降至1.8%/1.4%，且不存储历史图像与逐图像特征。

Conclusion: 分布感知的自适应知识整合可在无样本回放条件下有效缓解CH-LReID中的灾难性遗忘，实现阶段分布建模与跨阶段特征连续性的统一，具备实际长期部署价值。

Abstract: Lifelong Person Re-identification (LReID) aims to match the same person using sequentially collected data. In practical long-term deployments, however, the model must handle both domain shifts across cameras and scenes, and irregular clothing changes of the same identity. This gives rise to the Cloth-Hybrid Lifelong Person Re-identification (CH-LReID) task, which demands balancing cloth-relevant and cloth-irrelevant knowledge in streaming data. While existing LReID methods have explored replay-based or distillation-based anti-forgetting strategies, they typically fail to sufficiently model stage-aware domain distributions and cross-stage feature continuity under exemplar-free training. To address this problem, we propose a Distribution-Aware Adaptive Differentiated Knowledge Consolidation (DADKC) framework. The core idea is to enhance the existing Differentiated Knowledge Consolidation (DKC) paradigm with adaptive distribution modeling. To this end, three lightweight modules are designed: (1) an Anchor Pool Adapter (APA) to retrieve learnable anchor cues for refining identity features; (2) a Phase Path Mixer (PPM) to perform stage-aware expert routing for integrating domain-specific distribution paths; and (3) a Stage Bridge Alignment (SBA) module to regularize the continuity of base, anchor, and path representations between adjacent stages. Extensive experiments on the CH-LReID and LReID benchmarks show that DADKC gains 4.1%/4.5% mAP/R1 on Order-1 and 3.6%/3.4% mAP/R1 on Order-2 over the DKC baseline, and lowers weighted forgetting from 2.4%/1.7% to 1.8%/1.4%, while storing neither historical images nor per-image features. The code is available at https://github.com/fire667/DADKC.

</details>


### [103] [DramaVQA: Probing Detailed Understanding of Ultra-Long TV Dramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00769)
*Xinyue Huo,Pengfei Chen,Jiannan Ge,Kaiwen Duan,Jiacheng Shao,Yunning Cao,Lingxi Xie,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DramaVQA，一个基于长篇电视剧的超长视频理解基准，含约2万条QA对，规模比Video-MME、LVBench大一个数量级，并具有LLM辅助+人工校验的标注流程与多级时间结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解基准多聚焦短视频或小时级视频，缺乏对持续数十小时的超长视频理解的评测，形成评测空白。

Method: 基于长篇电视剧构建基准，约20K QA对；采用可扩展的LLM辅助标注流水线，由多模态大模型生成复杂问题再经严格人工核查；设计多级时间结构，问题覆盖不同视频时长，最难问题需对整部剧进行整体理解。

Result: 规模较Video-MME、LVBench等既有长视频数据集高出一个数量级；广泛评测显示当前SOTA多模态大模型在超长视频上仍面临重大挑战。

Conclusion: DramaVQA填补了超长视频理解评测的空白，结果表明需发展具备长期记忆管理能力的智能体式AI算法。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in long-form video understanding, current benchmarks mostly focus on short clips or hour-scale videos. This leaves a major gap in evaluating ultra-long video understanding, where videos often last tens of hours. To bridge this gap, we introduce DramaVQA, a large-scale benchmark built upon long-form TV dramas. Containing approximately 20K question-answering (QA) pairs, the scale of DramaVQA exceeds previous long-video datasets, such as Video-MME and LVBench, by an order of magnitude. Our benchmark has two main features that make it stand out from existing datasets. First, we establish a scalable, LLM-assisted annotation pipeline that uses multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to generate complex questions, followed by strict human checking to ensure high data quality. Second, DramaVQA introduces a multi-level time structure; queries are designed across different video lengths, with the hardest questions requiring overall understanding across the entire drama. Extensive evaluations show that state-of-the-art MLLMs face major challenges when tested on these very long videos, highlighting the need for agentic AI algorithms capable of managing long-term memory. The project is available at https://github.com/xinyuehuo/DramaVQA

</details>


### [104] [Now You See Them, Now You Dont FusionGhost Gait for Occlusion-Robust Gait Recognition with ArabGait dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00780)
*Seba Al Mokdad,Tamer Shanableh,Reham Mohamed Aburas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ArabGait海湾服饰多视角步态数据集，揭示海湾服饰下骨骼提取下肢姿态退化问题，并提出轻量融合时空网络FusionGhost-Gait。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于骨骼的步态识别通常比像素方法更抗外观变化，但该优势依赖稳定的姿态提取。海湾服饰（Kandoora/Abaya）造成严重遮挡，使主要失效模式由外观变化转为下肢姿态退化，现有研究缺乏对该场景的系统刻画。

Method: 构建ArabGait数据集（45名受试者、室内多视角、4种行走条件×3个视角）；提出FusionGhost-Gait，将空间图块与时间建模集成于同一残差单元，去除冗余通道投影，并以紧凑的时序Ghost卷积核作为轻量正则，抑制姿态噪声轨迹同时保持高效时间下采样。

Result: 海湾服饰使下肢平均置信度由0.811降至0.683，下肢失效比例由7.98%升至17.82%，踝部受影响最大。跨服饰评估（Protocol A/B）中Rank-1最高67.91%，参数量108,970–133,674、0.035–0.052 GFLOPs，较基线参数减少65%以上、FLOPs降低75%。

Conclusion: 所提模型在海湾服饰挑战场景下具备良好泛化性，且轻量可部署，适用于监控与门禁生物识别系统。

Abstract: Skeleton-based gait recognition is generally more robust to appearance changes than pixel-based methods, but this robustness depends on stable pose extraction. Clothing variation in gait recognition includes changes in garments, silhouette, and carried items; under Gulf clothing (Kandoora/Abaya), the dominant failure mode shifts from appearance change to lower-limb pose degradation caused by severe occlusion. To study this regime, we introduce ArabGait, a 45-subject indoor multi-view dataset with four walking conditions across three viewpoints, and show that Gulf clothing lowers mean lower-limb confidence from 0.811 to 0.683 and raises lower-limb failure rate from 7.98% to 17.82%, with ankles most affected. We propose FusionGhost-Gait, a fused spatiotemporal architecture that integrates spatial graph blocks and temporal modeling in one residual unit, removes redundant channel projections, and uses compact temporal Ghost kernels as a lightweight regularization mechanism for pose-noisy trajectories while preserving efficient temporal downsampling. On ArabGait cross-clothing evaluation, including Protocol A and Protocol B, FusionGhost-Gait reaches up to 67.91% Rank-1 while remaining compact (108,970- 133,674 parameters and 0.035-0.052 billion floating-point operations (GFLOPs)), achieving more than 65% fewer parameters and 75% lower FLOPs than existing baselines. These results indicate that the proposed model generalizes effectively to challenging Gulf-clothing conditions while remaining lightweight and deployable for surveillance and access-control biometric systems.

</details>


### [105] [ConstraintMatch: Compositional Image-Text Matching through Constrained Regional Evidence Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00784)
*Yuhong Zhou,Yubo Gao,Xiangbo Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的推理方法 ConstraintMatch，将实体级区域一致性转化为显式匹配代价，用于组合式图像—文本检索，在 ARO 与 SugarCrepe++ 属性替换任务上显著优于全局匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部图像—文本匹配虽能为单个概念找到强证据，但倾向于偏好属性—实体绑定错误的描述；需要显式约束实体级区域一致性来修正该偏差。

Method: 在冻结的条件—区域相似度矩阵上，对比"各类别/属性独立选裁剪"与"同一实体共享区域选择"两种结果的差距，量化放松共享区域约束所得分数优势；用同图空间参考在校准中固定类别证据、联合平移实体的属性图；采用逐实体截断防止残差相互抵消，并将缩放均值代价从全局相似度中扣除；仅用池化嵌入，空间校准无需额外编码器调用，参数按骨干网络固定。

Result: 在 Qwen~2B 与 CORE~2B 平均下，相比全局匹配，ARO Attribute 全集提升 7.70 个百分点，留出 SugarCrepe++ 属性替换提升 5.63 个百分点；相比适配同一 13-crop 预算的 SGI，分别提升 4.85 与 3.51 个百分点；控制实验区分了共享区域匹配与候选分数校准的效应。

Conclusion: 实体级证据选择是组合式多模态检索中可行的评分原则。

Abstract: Local image--text matching can find strong evidence for individual concepts yet favor captions with incorrect attribute--entity bindings. We introduce ConstraintMatch, a training-free inference method that turns entity-level regional consistency into an explicit matching cost. On the same frozen condition--region similarity matrix, it compares independent crop selection for each category and attribute with a shared crop choice within each entity. Their gap quantifies the score advantage obtained by relaxing the shared-region constraint. Spatial references from the same image calibrate this gap by keeping category evidence fixed while jointly shifting an entity's attribute maps. Per-entity clipping prevents cancellation across residuals, and the scaled mean cost is subtracted from global similarity. ConstraintMatch uses pooled embeddings, and spatial calibration requires no additional encoder calls. With parameters fixed per backbone across tasks, ConstraintMatch improves over global matching by 7.70 percentage points on full ARO Attribute and 5.63 points on held-out SugarCrepe++ attribute swaps, averaged across Qwen~2B and CORE~2B. Against SGI adapted to the same 13-crop budget, the respective gains are 4.85 and 3.51 points. Controlled analyses distinguish the effects of shared-region matching and candidate score calibration. The results establish entity-level evidence selection as a practical scoring principle for compositional multimodal retrieval.

</details>


### [106] [PhoneValue-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark and Datasets for Consumer Smartphone Recommerce Valuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00791)
*Zihan Deng,Jun Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhoneValue-Bench（双真值、分级计分卡评测套件）与PhoneValue-Corpus（>2万订单、数十万带字幕图像弱标注训练集），并给出ValueAgent分阶段感知-融合-估价流程及可执行评分器；在Bench-Full上得84.1/100（外观字段填充97.0、工单字段填充54.1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级二手手机回收规模化后，买家需在设备入库前依据卖家上传的多视角照片与诊断截图给出有约束力的回购报价。远程估值须融合噪声较大的外观证据与截图/工单中的身份、电池、锁、渠道风险，任一模态出错都会改变成色等级与付款。现有资源多面向通用VLM能力、屏幕阅读或工厂缺陷检测，忽视与人工工单对齐的换新字段、外观/工单双真值及可执行估值计分卡。

Method: 缺口源于两点挑战：(1)缺乏带审计双标注的大规模真实换新数据，以及与训练标签分离的紧凑排序基准；(2)需在运营扣减规则下对无序多视角外观证据与诊断截图联合推理。为此构建PhoneValue-Bench（双真值评测套件＋分级门控计分卡）与PhoneValue-Corpus（匹配的弱标注训练集），并提出ValueAgent——与产线质检对齐的分阶段“感知-融合-估值”参考流水线，附公开可执行评分器与提交格式。

Result: ValueAgent在Bench-Full上实测84.1/100，外观字段填充率97.0、工单字段填充率54.1，验证了该双真值基准在真实场景下的可用性。

Conclusion: 工作建立了面向多模态二手回收估值的实用双真值基准，并揭示工单类结构化字段填充仍是主要短板（54.1），为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: As consumer smartphone recommerce scales, buyers must quote binding repurchase prices from seller-uploaded multi-view photos and diagnostic screenshots before devices reach the warehouse. Accurate remote valuation requires fusing noisy cosmetic evidence with identity, battery, lock, and channel risk from screenshots and staff forms, since an error in any modality changes both grade and payout. However, existing multimodal resources mainly target general VLM competence, screen reading, or factory defect detection, and largely overlook staff-aligned trade-in fields, dual appearance/staff ground truth, and executable valuation scorecards. This gap stems from two challenges: (1) the lack of large-scale real trade-in data with audited dual annotations and a compact ranking benchmark kept separate from training labels, and (2) the need to jointly reason over unordered multi-view cosmetics and diagnostic screens under operational deduction rules. To address these challenges, we propose PhoneValue-Bench, a dual-GT evaluation suite with tier-gated scorecards, together with PhoneValue-Corpus, a matched weakly labeled training set of over twenty thousand orders and hundreds of thousands of captioned images. We further present ValueAgent, a staged perception-fusion-valuation reference pipeline aligned with production inspection, plus an executable public scorer and submission schema. Experiments show that ValueAgent reaches a measured 84.1/100 on Bench-Full, with 97.0 appearance fill and 54.1 staff-field fill, establishing a practical dual-GT benchmark for multimodal recommerce valuation.

</details>


### [107] [Training-free Conditional Object Composition for Multi-object Image Generation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00804)
*Hsuan-Fu Wang,Hsuan-Tien Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的条件对象组合框架，将N个对象的生成分解为先生成N-1个对象、再以已生成内容为条件组合剩余对象，缓解联合生成中的对象间干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多对象文本到图像生成中，组合式方法分别生成对象难以刻画对象间交互，联合生成方法则易产生对象间干扰，现有方法难以可靠生成提示中所有对象。

Method: 免训练框架：两阶段分解，先联合生成N-1个对象子集，再条件化组合剩余对象；采用基于inpainting的公式化与布局引导以保留已有区域并保证空间一致放置；提出基于交叉注意力图的注意力打分机制选择单独生成的对象，并探索现成目标检测器。

Result: 在MS COCO多对象生成上取得与现有方法相当或更优的性能，尤其随对象数量增加优势更明显；消融验证了各设计选择的必要性。

Conclusion: 向组合式方法“退一步”的分阶段条件组合策略，可在免训练条件下有效平衡对象间交互建模与干扰抑制。

Abstract: Multi-object image generation in text-to-image diffusion models remains challenging, as existing approaches struggle to reliably generate all objects specified in a prompt. Compositional approaches that generate objects independently often fail to capture the interactions among them, whereas joint generation methods tend to suffer from inter-object interference. In this work, we propose a training-free framework, termed conditional object composition, that relaxes joint generation methods by taking one step back toward compositional approaches. Our framework decomposes the generation of $N$ objects into two stages: it first generates a subset of $N-1$ objects and then composes the remaining object conditioned on the generated content. To enable effective composition, we adopt an inpainting-based formulation with layout guidance to preserve existing regions and encourage spatially coherent placement. To address the challenge of selecting which objects to generate separately, we introduce an attention-based scoring mechanism based on cross-attention maps and further explore the use of off-the-shelf object detectors. We evaluate our method on multi-object image generation using the widely adopted MS COCO dataset. Experimental results show that our approach achieves competitive or improved performance compared to existing methods, particularly as the number of objects increases. Further studies highlight the importance of the proposed design choices. The source code is available at https://github.com/ShampooWang/condComp_imageGen/tree/main

</details>


### [108] [UniCaMo: Unified Camera and Motion Control for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00810)
*Long Vu,Tan Ngo,Animesh Karnewar,Amir Habibian,Binh-Son Hua,Hung Bui,Minh Hoai Nguyen,Phong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniCaMo统一框架，通过3D运动一致的输入噪声构造，同时实现文/图生视频中相机与物体运动的联合控制，无需改架构或额外控制模块，仅需轻量LoRA微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频生成方法多分别处理相机运动与物体运动，且往往依赖额外控制模块或重建未知场景几何，导致控制复杂、开销大。

Method: 输入参考图像、文本提示、稀疏3D物体轨迹与3D相机轨迹，构建直接编码目标运动的运动一致噪声：参考图像噪声传播（reference-derived noise propagation）+ 对新生区域使用球面高斯噪声表示（spherical Gaussian noise representation）作为运动先验，避免重建不可见几何；不改架构、不加控制模块，仅用少量数据做LoRA微调。

Result: 在标准可控视频生成基准上达到SOTA的运动可控性与高视觉质量，推理开销极小。

Conclusion: 以"噪声即控制"的简单有效范式统一相机与物体运动控制，兼具通用性、低训练成本与低推理开销。

Abstract: We present \textbf{UniCaMo}, an unified framework for joint control of camera and object motion in image-and-text-to-video diffusion models. Given a reference image, text prompt, sparse 3D object trajectories, and a 3D camera trajectory, UniCaMo constructs motion-consistent input noise that directly encodes the desired motion. Our 3D-grounded noise construction combines reference-derived noise propagation with a spherical Gaussian noise representation for newly revealed regions, providing a simple and effective motion prior without requiring reconstruction of unseen scene geometry. UniCaMo requires no architectural modifications or dedicated control modules and can be adapted using lightweight LoRA fine-tuning with low training data volume. Experiments on standard controllable video generation benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art motion controllability and high visual quality with minimal inference overhead.

</details>


### [109] [ODC: Orthogonal Drift Correction for Improved Text-to-Image Semantic Alignment at Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00819)
*Kasra Kamali,Basim Azam,Tuan Duong Trinh,Jean Honorio,Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出正交漂移校正（ODC），一种无需重训练的推理时引导方法，通过两阶段流程修正文本嵌入以缓解文生图语义漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图模型在复杂/细微提示下常出现语义漂移（属性绑定错误、物体缺失等），已有方法多为局部修补，缺少直接在用户提示文本嵌入层面进行整体性修正的方案。

Method: 两阶段推理时方法：第一阶段在共享视觉-语言嵌入空间比较初始生成图像与提示，得到漂移向量，提取其相对提示方向的正交分量，并借助基于词表的替代机制映射回文本；第二阶段将初始文本嵌入与该重嵌入的漂移表示一并输入自适应降秩概念去除模块，生成精炼文本条件用于二次生成。

Result: 在COALBench-300与PartiPrompts上CLIP分数较基线最高提升5.4%；在T2I-CompBench复杂子集上平均3-in-1分数较对比方法提升4.5%。

Conclusion: ODC作为即插即用模块，无需重训练或额外用户输入，能有效提升文生图模型的提示-图像对齐能力与可靠性。

Abstract: Text-to-image models have achieved remarkable success in generating high-quality images from textual descriptions. However, they often struggle with "semantic drift," where the generated output fails to precisely align with complex or nuanced text prompts. While recent approaches have attempted to address semantic errors regarding attribute binding or object presence, there remains a gap for a more holistic method that addresses these issues by directly refining the text embeddings of the initial user prompt. In this work, we introduce Orthogonal Drift Correction (ODC), an inference-time guidance method designed to mitigate semantic drift without requiring model retraining or additional user inputs. ODC guides image generation through a two-stage process. In the first stage, it identifies the semantic drift by evaluating the initially generated image against the user prompt in a shared vision-language embedding space. It then isolates the component of this drift vector that is orthogonal to the prompt's direction and translates it back into text via a vocabulary-based surrogate mechanism. In the second stage, it produces refined text conditioning for a second generation pass by feeding both the initial text embedding and the re-embedded drift representation into an adaptive rank-reduced concept removal module. Our experiments demonstrate that ODC consistently improves prompt-image alignment, increasing CLIP scores by up to 5.4% over the baseline on the COALBench-300 and PartiPrompts benchmarks. Furthermore, on the T2I-CompBench complex subset, ODC improves the average 3-in-1 score by 4.5% over the competing methods. As a plug-and-play module, ODC offers a practical and efficient method for improving the reliability of text-to-image models. Code is available at https://github.com/kasrakamali/ODC.

</details>


### [110] [Beyond Temporal Overlap: Consumer-Aware Evaluation of Sign-Language Segmentation across Three Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00823)
*Guohou Zhang,Kalin Stefanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文质疑手语分割仅用重叠度(IoU)评价的合理性，以“冻结识别器”为下游消费者，考察重叠度量与识别度量是否偏好同一分割器。实验覆盖3种架构×3随机种子×3语料的27个输出，并做受控边界编辑，结论是重叠度平均上可预测识别性能但不总能决定模型选择，建议从时间一致性、参考恢复、片段识别、序列错误四轴评价分割器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语分割评价指标将预测片段与人工标注边界做重叠比较，并不直接衡量片段是否保留识别器所需的视觉证据，也不惩罚片段数量过多，与真实下游用途（冻结识别器为每段分配一个手语标签）脱节。

Method: 以冻结识别器作为下游消费者；评估3种分割架构、3个随机种子、3个语料共27个输出；通过受控边界编辑将“去除手语证据”与“加入周边上下文”的效应分离，并比较重叠指标、片段识别性能与序列错误(WER)的模型选择一致性。

Result: 重叠度平均上能预测识别性能，但未必决定模型选择：在相同IoU下，不同识别器可能偏好更短或更长的片段。真实输出呈现两种运行区间：DGS上重叠度、识别性能与序列错误选择同一模型；BSL上基于识别的选择恢复并识别更多参考手语，但WER升高；Dicta-Sign呈反向权衡，WER选择使词内WER降低31.17个百分点，却漏掉更多可识别手语。

Conclusion: 不应仅用重叠度评价手语分割器，而应从四个维度评价：时间一致性(temporal agreement)、参考恢复(reference recovery)、片段识别(clip recognition)与序列错误(sequence error)。

Abstract: Sign-language segmentation divides continuous signing into clips. In this study, the downstream consumer is a frozen recognizer that assigns one sign label to each predicted clip. Existing metrics instead compare predicted clips with manually annotated boundaries. They do not directly measure whether a clip preserves the visual evidence needed by the recognizer or whether the segmenter produces too many clips. We therefore ask whether overlap metrics and this consumer prefer the same segmenter. We evaluate 27 outputs from three segmentation architectures, three random seeds, and three corpora. Controlled boundary edits separate the effect of removing sign evidence from that of adding surrounding context. Overlap predicts recognition on average, but it does not always determine model choice. At the same intersection-over-union, different recognizers can prefer the shorter or the longer clip. The real outputs reveal two operating regimes. On DGS, overlap, recognition, and sequence error select the same model. On BSL, recognition-based selection recovers and recognizes more reference signs but increases word error rate (WER). Dicta-Sign shows the reverse trade-off: WER selection reduces in-vocabulary WER by 31.17 points but misses more recognizable signs. Segmenters should therefore be evaluated on four axes: temporal agreement, reference recovery, clip recognition, and sequence error, rather than on overlap alone.

</details>


### [111] [Answer Correction with Verifiable Rewards: A Gemma-Family Study of Multi-View Driving QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00828)
*T N Rickesh,Pranav Vinodh,Sowmya Kamath S*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在Gemma系列上基于WaymoQA归档评估，区分后训练增益是"答案选择改变"还是"答案抽取改善"：匹配实验条件下GRPO使准确率从73.78%升至75.43%（+1.65pp），增益几乎全部来自可抽取但原答案错误的样本被改选，且常规感知提升掩盖了安全关键风险预测的退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相机驾驶问答常被当作视觉语言模型的离散测试，但后训练后的性能提升可能来自"改选了不同答案"，也可能来自"答案抽取能力增强"，二者在聚合准确率中被混淆。作者希望在Gemma模型族内厘清这一区别，并检验无加权结果奖励是否会在类别层面造成安全相关的不对称代价。

Method: 使用归档的、与任务关联的WaymoQA评估，在5,519道测试题上做零样本诊断，覆盖effective-small、MoE、unified与dense等多类checkpoint，并把服务/推理配置差异明确披露而非解读为架构效应。核心证据为同一评测框架下的Gemma-4-12B base/GRPO配对比较，采用场景聚类bootstrap估计置信区间；同时将"正确性"与"可抽取状态"交叉制表，区分正确但不合规的输出与真正的预测错误。另设归档的Gemma-31B thinking对比。

Result: 匹配框架下准确率由73.78%提升至75.43%，增益1.65个百分点，场景聚类bootstrap区间[+0.84, +2.41]。215例改善、124例退化；其中213例改善源于基线本可抽取但答案错误，仅2例挽救了此前不可抽取的响应。类别计数显示安全不对称：常规感知类别+5.93pp，而安全关键的危害预测下降1.82pp。Gemma-31B thinking对比未检出平均提升，但其不确定性不足以确立等价性。作者将奖励饱和描述为采样训练动态而非架构上限。

Conclusion: 结果为所记录的调参配置提供了适度的"答案选择"增益及可复现的诊断性记账方法；作者不将纠正后的标签归因于特定grounding机制，也不声称优于匹配的监督微调。无加权结果奖励可能以稀有边缘案例为代价换取聚合准确率，提示需在类别层面加权评估安全关键能力。

Abstract: Multi-camera driving question answering provides a discrete test of vision-language models, but post-training gains can reflect changed selected answers or improved answer extraction. We investigate this distinction within the Gemma model family using archived, task-linked WaymoQA evaluations. A zero-shot diagnostic on 5,519 test questions spans effective-small, mixture-of-experts, unified, and dense checkpoints, with serving differences disclosed rather than interpreted as architecture effects. The strongest controlled evidence is a matched-harness Gemma-4-12B base/GRPO pair: accuracy rises from 73.78% to 75.43%, a gain of 1.65 percentage points with a scene-cluster bootstrap interval of [+0.84, +2.41]. There are 215 improvements and 124 regressions; 213 improvements start from an extractable but incorrect base answer, and only two recover previously unextractable responses. Crossing correctness with extraction state shows why correct noncompliant outputs cannot be counted as prediction errors. Category counts reveal a critical safety asymmetry: routine perception gains (+5.93 pp) mask a regression in safety-critical hazard prediction (-1.82 pp), demonstrating that unweighted outcome rewards can trade off rare edge cases for aggregate accuracy. Reward saturation describes sampled training dynamics rather than an architectural ceiling. A separate archived Gemma-31B thinking comparison yields no detected average improvement, with uncertainty that does not establish equivalence. These results support a modest answer-selection gain for the recorded tuning configuration and a reproducible diagnostic accounting, without attributing corrected labels to specific grounding mechanisms or claiming superiority over matched supervised tuning.

</details>


### [112] [Fewer Tokens, More Answers: Verifiable-Reward Tuning of an Ornith-Style VLM for Multi-View Driving QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00833)
*T N Rickesh,Pranav Vinodh,Sowmya Kamath S*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在256 token预算下，GRPO将可提取答案率从78.25%提升至90.25%、准确率从55.50%提升至63.75%，但收益主要来自避免无答案的截断输出；512 token预算下增益仅+0.75pp。全量开发集上平均生成token由108.6降至47.1，准确率增益的区间包含零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型模型衍生版本必须在部署预算内把中间推理转化为可用的最终答案。作者以Ornith检查点适配多摄像头驾驶问答（可验证奖励）为场景，研究"答案交付"这一转换环节在有限token预算下的表现，并强调Ornith自身自脚手架谱系与Bonsai等新衍生模型提供了问题动机。

Method: 在400题开发筛选集上，以256 token上限对配对存档输出进行比较，使用GRPO+可验证奖励训练；对错误→正确转变逐条归因；在512 token下做检查点可验证的重构实验；并单独分析"答案提示续写"后处理；最后在2,511题全量开发集上评估含恢复机制的整体流程。

Result: 256 token：答案提取率78.25%→90.25%，准确率55.50%→63.75%；39个错误→正确转变中33个源于基座原本不可提取的响应，基座的87条不可提取响应全部触及token上限。512 token下首轮准确率增益仅+0.75pp（对比256 token的+8.25pp）。答案提示续写对基座帮助更大，缩小了适配器差距。全量集上平均生成token从108.6降至47.1，配对准确率区间包含零增益。

Conclusion: 作者将结论定位为"有限预算下的答案交付适配"，而非推理能力提升：增益主要由可提取性与预算约束驱动。研究存在明确的选择偏差与来源限制，仅适用于Ornith，不能推及共享架构机制或跨模型迁移。

Abstract: Reasoning-enabled model derivatives must turn intermediate deliberation into a usable answer within a deployment budget. We study that transition in an Ornith checkpoint adapted to multi-camera driving question answering with verifiable rewards. Paired archived outputs show that, on a selected 400-question development screen with a 256-token cap, GRPO increases answer extraction from 78.25% to 90.25% and accuracy from 55.50% to 63.75%. Of 39 wrong-to-correct transitions, 33 begin with an unextractable base response. The base's 87 unextractable responses all reach the token cap. Checkpoint-verified reconstruction on those same questions gives a much smaller first-pass accuracy gain at 512 tokens: +0.75 percentage points, versus +8.25 at 256. A later answer-cued continuation is analyzed separately; it helps the base more and narrows the adapter difference. On the full 2,511-question development set, this recovery-enabled pipeline reduces mean generated tokens from 108.6 to 47.1, while the paired accuracy interval includes zero gain. We frame these findings as adaptation of answer delivery under finite budgets, with explicit selection and provenance limits. Ornith's self-scaffolding lineage and newer reasoning-enabled derivatives such as Bonsai motivate the problem, but our measured results concern Ornith alone and do not establish a shared architectural mechanism or cross-model transfer.

</details>


### [113] [Running a 20B Video Diffusion Transformer on Consumer Hardware: A Deployment and Measurement Report for MiniMax H3 on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00843)
*Luke Shi,Steven Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在Apple Mac mini(M5 Pro, 64 GiB统一内存)上端到端部署并实测20.38B参数视频DiT模型MiniMax H3的Q8量化版本，实现完全本地、无云端依赖的文生视频流水线，并报告构建时间、峰值内存与各分辨率渲染延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证大规模视频扩散Transformer能否在消费级Apple Silicon硬件上完全本地运行，提供可复现的部署流程与实测数据（属系统/实证类报告，不声称模型创新）。

Method: 使用官方bf16→Q8（8-bit，group size 64）量化路径在本地执行；采用MLX框架；记录完整部署流程，包括两个关键阻塞问题及其修复方案；在四个分辨率档位（Draft 384x640 至 Native 768x1344）下测量构建时间、MLX峰值内存与单片段渲染时延；并将系统作为无人值守生产流水线运行。

Result: 15.08秒 576x1024片段渲染耗时4838.49秒，MLX峰值内存27.467 GiB；无人值守流水线在26.49小时内完成17个片段，零渲染失败；17个生产片段的盲评人类评审（隐藏提示词）在结构完整性、时序一致性、可见伪影三项上平均得分分别为3.12、3.18、2.94（满分5）。

Conclusion: 消费级Apple Silicon设备可在完全本地、无云依赖条件下部署运行200亿参数级视频DiT并稳定投入生产，但渲染效率仍较低（约5.3分钟输出1秒视频）；生成质量处于中等水平，伪影控制为主要短板。

Abstract: We report the end-to-end deployment and measurement of MiniMax H3, a 20.38-billion-parameter video diffusion transformer (DiT) - the count measured from the safetensors headers of the Q8 checkpoint as deployed locally (Section 3.2) - running entirely on consumer Apple Silicon hardware: an Apple Mac mini with M5 Pro chip and 64 GiB of unified memory. Using the official bf16-to-Q8 (8-bit, group size 64) quantization path executed locally on the machine, we produced a working text-to-video pipeline with no cloud dependency. We document the full deployment procedure, including two non-trivial blockers and their fixes, and report measured figures for build time, peak memory, and per-clip render latency across four resolution tiers (Draft 384x640 through Native 768x1344). A 15.08-second 576x1024 clip rendered in 4838.49 seconds with a measured MLX peak of 27.467 GiB. We further operated the system as an unattended production pipeline, completing 17 clips in 26.49 hours with zero render failures. A blind human review of the 17 production clips (generation prompts withheld) averaged 3.12, 3.18, and 2.94 out of 5 for structural integrity, temporal consistency, and visible artifacts. All figures in this report are our own measurements except where explicitly labeled as third-party data. This is a systems/empirical report; we make no claim of model innovation.

</details>


### [114] [Toward Counterfactual Learning for Selective Re-Examination in Agentic Visual Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00848)
*Ao Chen,Shengchao Chen,Yan Luximon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出用于高分辨率图像视觉搜索的“复查决策”基准 ReSee 与决策级强化学习方法 CARE，通过反事实优势估计判断是否值得再观察，提升搜索准确率与决策选择性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像视觉搜索中，VLM 不仅要决定看哪里，还需判断在给出答案前是否值得再观察一次。现有提示、示例与结果导向的 RL 只能间接鼓励复查，无法在具体搜索状态下直接监督“复查是否有益”，导致模型缺乏对复查时机的选择性判断。

Method: 构建 ReSee 基准：3,227 条视觉搜索轨迹、8,827 个显式“提交或复查”决策，并用反事实裁定区分应继续观察与应直接提交的状态。在此基础上提出 CARE（Counterfactual Advantage for Re-Examination）：在同一搜索状态下，对 commit 与 re-examine 两种判决的成对续写进行比较，用代价感知的回报差估计额外观察的反事实价值，并仅将信用分配给对应的判决 token，实现局部化信用分配。

Result: 实验显示现有 VLMs 的复查选择性有限；CARE 在两个骨干模型上相较 GRPO 显著提升准确率与决策选择性，并在六个未见 OOD 基准上取得更高准确率与更少视觉请求。

Conclusion: 将“是否复查”显式建模为决策级问题并提供反事实优势监督，是提升 VLM 视觉搜索效率与可靠性的有效途径，且具备良好的分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: Visual search over high-resolution images requires a vision-language model (VLM) not only to determine where to inspect, but also whether another observation is warranted before committing to an answer. Existing prompting, demonstrations, and outcome-based reinforcement learning can encourage re-examination, yet do not directly supervise whether it is beneficial at a particular search state. We introduce ReSee, a benchmark of re-examination decisions comprising 3,227 visual-search trajectories and 8,827 explicit commit-or-re-examine decisions, with counterfactual adjudication that distinguishes states where additional inspection is warranted from those where commitment is preferable. Building on this formulation, we propose CARE (Counterfactual Advantage for Re-Examination), a decision-level reinforcement learning method that compares paired continuations under commit and re-examine verdicts from the same search state. Their cost-aware return difference estimates the counterfactual value of an additional observation and is assigned only to the corresponding verdict tokens, providing localized credit for whether search should continue. Experiments expose limited re-examination selectivity in existing VLMs and show that CARE significantly improves accuracy and decision selectivity over GRPO across two backbones, with higher accuracy and fewer visual requests on six unseen OOD benchmarks.

</details>


### [115] [Wave Forcing: Towards Speed-of-Light Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00859)
*Yijie Jin,Yuxuan Liu,Jiajun Xu,Ye Huang,Chuzhi Wang,Jiayang Li,Ruiyang Chen,Chenling Hong,Xu Wang,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wave Forcing (WF) 通过生成、训练与执行协同设计，实现连续长视频实时生成，达成 1F1C（填满流水线后每轮前向输出一个 chunk），4-step 在 8×H200 上端到端 134.8 FPS、单卡吞吐 7.07× 加速，Wave Parallelism 相比 Ulysses/Ring 最高 2.53× 吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 chunk-wise 自回归方法存在效率与质量双重瓶颈：即使单步去噪，串行去噪与历史缓存刷新仍需两次前向；更长 rollout 会导致质量退化与镜头漂移（camera drift）。因此需要兼顾生成效率与长视频一致性的实时连续生成方案。

Method: 方法分三层协同：(1) 生成层面采用 chunk-wise 因果注意力，可在较早（更干净）的 chunk 尚未完成去噪时即访问其特征，并将错峰（staggered）的去噪与缓存刷新阶段分布到完整模型副本上，实现 1F1C；(2) 训练层面设计三阶段流程，依次引入混合噪声去噪、运动监督、以及基于自生成历史的因果适配；(3) 执行层面提出 Wave Runtime（WaveRT），含 Wave Parallelism（WP）实现早期 K/V 传递与通信-计算重叠，配合优化 kernel 与流水线化 VAE 解码。

Result: 在 Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 上，5-step WF 整体质量与 Rolling Forcing 相当，且在短视频与长视频上 Dynamic Degree 更高。效率方面：4-step WF 在 8×H200 上达 147.6 DiT FPS、134.8 端到端 FPS，为单卡端到端吞吐的 7.07×；在仅 DiT、对齐 SageAttention+W8A8 后端、去噪步数与 GPU 数的测试中，WP 在 1.3B–14B 六个模型规模上吞吐最高为 Ulysses 与 Ring 中较优基线的 2.53×。

Conclusion: WF 表明将连续生成的生成机制、训练策略与运行时系统联合设计，可在保持质量的同时显著提升长视频动态性与实时吞吐；WP 在多种模型规模上优于现有上下文并行方案，具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Real-time long-video generation requires continuous output with consistent appearance and natural motion. Existing chunk-wise autoregressive methods face challenges in both generation efficiency and long-video quality: serialized denoising and history-cache refresh require two forward passes even with one-step denoising, and longer rollouts can degrade quality and cause camera drift. We propose Wave Forcing (WF), which co-designs generation, training, and execution for continuous video output with multi-step denoising. WF uses chunk-wise causal attention to access earlier, cleaner chunks before their denoising is complete, and distributes staggered denoising and cache-refresh stages across full-model replicas to achieve 1F1C: one output chunk per concurrent forward round after pipeline fill. To adapt the model to this continuous generation process, we develop a three-stage training procedure that successively introduces mixed-noise denoising, motion supervision, and causal adaptation using self-generated history. To execute this process efficiently, Wave Runtime (WaveRT) uses Wave Parallelism (WP) for early K/V delivery and communication-computation overlap, together with optimized kernels and pipelined VAE decoding. Experiments on Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B show that 5-step WF maintains overall quality comparable to Rolling Forcing while achieving higher Dynamic Degree on both short and long videos. Our system efficiency experiments show that 4-step WF achieves 147.6 DiT FPS and 134.8 end-to-end FPS on eight H200 GPUs, yielding 7.07x the single-GPU end-to-end throughput. In DiT-only tests with matched SageAttention+W8A8 backends, denoising steps, and GPU counts, WP delivers up to 2.53x the throughput of the faster of the Ulysses and Ring context-parallel baselines across six model scales (1.3B-14B).

</details>


### [116] [Learning to Group and Order: Cross-Instance Self-Supervised RL for Vision-Centric MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00892)
*Songming Yang,Hong-Tao Yu,Xiu-Shen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GO-SSL，一种基于跨实例拼图的自监督强化学习框架，用于以视觉为中心的多模态大模型后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督视觉前置任务作为可验证奖励时，主要利用实例内部结构，对跨实例判别和细粒度比较提供的监督有限。

Method: 给定两个视觉实例，混合其局部元素，训练模型按来源实例分组，并恢复每组内部的顺序，将实例级比较与空间/时间/几何结构恢复相耦合；通过多样配对、打乱以及"由随机到困难"的课程学习，将同一无标注数据转化为更丰富的可验证监督。主要基于跨图像拼图实例化，并扩展至3D深度与视频时序排序。

Result: 在多个基准上稳定优于基座MLLM及既有自监督后训练基线，在细粒度感知与空间理解上有明显提升；跨模型规模与族的评估支持其泛化性。

Conclusion: 跨实例比较可为MLLM提供有效的自监督RL信号，提升可迁移的视觉理解能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has become an important paradigm for improving multimodal large language models (MLLMs), especially with automatically verifiable rewards. Self-supervised visual pretext tasks provide such rewards without human annotations. However, existing methods mainly exploit within-instance structure, offering limited supervision for cross-instance discrimination and fine-grained comparison. We introduce Group-and-Order Self-Supervised Reinforcement Learning (GO-SSL), a cross-instance jigsaw framework for vision-centric MLLM post-training. Given two visual instances, GO-SSL mixes their local elements and trains the model to group them by instance of origin while recovering the order within each group, coupling instance-level comparison with spatial, temporal, or geometric structure recovery. This paired formulation turns the same unlabeled data into richer verifiable supervision through diverse pairings, shuffles, and a random-to-hard curriculum. We instantiate GO-SSL mainly on cross-image jigsaw tasks and further extend it to 3D depth and video temporal ordering. Across diverse benchmarks, GO-SSL consistently improves over the base MLLM and prior self-supervised post-training baselines, with clear gains in fine-grained perception and spatial understanding. Further evaluations across model scales and families support its generality. These results suggest that cross-instance comparison provides a useful self-supervised RL signal for improving transferable visual understanding in MLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/SEU-VIPGroup/GO-SSL.

</details>


### [117] [FilmTogether: Cinematic Video Generation from Low-Cost Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00899)
*Shiying Zhang,Xiaobin Hu,Qianchi Zhang,Jiaqi Zhao,Xiaoda Yang,Bo Yin,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出CineCraft-120K大规模对齐的高低成本视频配对数据集及双记忆特征适配器，用于低成本视频到电影级视频的外观升级编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备普及使视频录制大众化，但随手拍摄的低成本视频与高成本电影制作之间存在明显美学鸿沟。任务目标是在不改变原始素材的布局、身份与时间运动的前提下，升级照明、服装、材质等异构制作要素。现有工作受两大瓶颈制约：1）缺乏专为复杂低到高外观转变设计的高质量配对训练数据；2）忽视了现有视频编辑模型对多种低成本构件的感知与增强能力。

Method: 数据层面构建CineCraft-120K：对齐的高低成本视频对，配以pair-aware编辑提示，含高分辨率高成本"电影工艺"资源库，按5大领域、23个细粒度子类分类，并提供元素级映射与丰富的文本提示。架构层面提出简单有效的双记忆特征适配器（dual-memory feature adapter）：文本记忆扩展低到高外观变化的语义容量，提示记忆在上下文注入前提供逐层的高成本元素视觉特征校正。

Result: 大量实验与消融研究表明，CineCraft-120K在低到高成本视频编辑上具有优越性，双记忆适配器同样有效。

Conclusion: 通过配对数据集与双记忆适配器的结合，可有效缓解低成本与电影级视频间的外观美学差距，实现保持结构与运动一致性的高质量视频外观升级编辑。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of mobile devices has democratized video recording, but a stark aesthetic divide persists between casual, low-cost captures and high-cost cinematic productions. Bridging this gap requires upgrading heterogeneous production elements, such as lighting, specialized costumes, and materials, without altering the core layout, identity, and temporal motion of the original footage. However, this task is heavily bottlenecked by two important challenges: 1) Lacking a high-quality paired training data specifically designed to handle complex low-to-high appearance shifts. 2) Ignoring the capacity of current video editing models to perceive and enhance the multiple low-cost compositions. To address these issues, we present CineCraft-120K, a large-scale dataset of aligned low- and high-cost video pairs annotated with pair-aware editing prompts. CineCraft-120K features a diverse, high-resolution and high-cost Cinematic Crafting bank spanning decades of media production, meticulously taxonomized into 5 broad domains and 23 fine-grained sub-categories. It also provides element-level mapping between low- and high-cost counterparts, offering pair-aware rich textual prompts that precisely detail the required aesthetic transformations while anchoring structural layout. Architecturally, we propose a simple yet effective dual-memory feature adapter, where a text-memory expands semantic capacity for low2high appearance changes, and a hint memory supplies layer-wise visual feature corrections of high-cost element before context injection. Extensive experiments and ablation studies demonstrate the superiority of CineCraft-120K for low-to-high-cost video editing, alongside the effectiveness of our dual-memory adapter.

</details>


### [118] [ZOPGA-KD: Zeroth Order PGA Guided Data Free Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00908)
*Tuhin Subhra Mukherjee,Tiya Rose Pulikunnel,Vaidhav Adit,Devanand Thanissery,S. H. Shabbeer Basha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖教师输出logits的无数据知识蒸馏方法：从平滑随机噪声出发，用零阶梯度估计配合投影梯度上升(PGA)生成合成数据，再训练学生模型，性能与需访问教师参数的方​​法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始训练数据常因隐私问题不可得，而现有无数据KD方法或依赖白盒教师参数、或依赖计算代价高昂的生成模型与生成式训练，黑盒方法同样计算开销大，因此需要一个仅用教师logits、不需架构/参数/梯度、且计算高效的无数据蒸馏方案。

Method: 以平滑随机噪声为初始点，通过投影梯度上升(PGA)生成合成样本；所需梯度不由教师反传获得，而采用零阶梯度估计（仅通过查询教师输出logits近似梯度）；随后用该合成样本集训练学生网络。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上评估，蒸馏性能与可访问教师模型参数的方法相当。

Conclusion: 无数据知识蒸馏可仅凭教师输出logits实现，无需教师架构、参数或梯度信息，也避免了昂贵的生成器训练，在保证可比性能的同时降低了访问假设与计算负担。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) enables the transfer of learned representations from a large teacher network to a small student network. In foundational KD, the student network uses the original training data of the teacher model to accomplish this task. However, due to privacy concerns, the original training data is often not available. To overcome this reliance on original training data, several data-free knowledge distillation methods have been developed. Some of these methods operate in the white-box setting, and a few methods generate synthetic data by training a generator network. White-box methods use the teacher model's parameters to obtain a synthetic dataset. To avoid relying on teacher parameters, some methods use computationally expensive generative approaches to obtain such a dataset. There also exist certain black-box methods that generate the synthetic data without accessing teacher parameters. These methods are also computationally intensive. We propose a data-free knowledge distillation method that utilizes just the teacher's output logits and does not require access to its architecture, parameters, or gradients. We generate synthetic data via Projected Gradient Ascent (PGA) starting from smooth random noise signals. Required gradients are obtained through zeroth-order gradient estimation rather than backpropagating through the teacher's parameters. The student is then trained on these synthetically generated samples. We evaluate the proposed method on several benchmark datasets and show that the distillation performance is comparable with approaches that have access to the teacher model parameters.

</details>


### [119] [UI-UG 2: Advancing Mobile UI Understanding and Generation with Fine-Grained Supervision and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00926)
*Hao Yang,Shuoyang Liu,Xiaoyu Lin,Zhou Fang,Maji Huang,Zhaosong Huang,Ruichao Mao,Teng Guo,Hai Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UI-UG 2 是一个 4B 参数的多模态大模型，通过前端对齐的 80 类两级 UI 分类体系、多源理解语料与 5000+ 需求-截图-JSON 三元组，以及基于 Playwright 在线渲染的 GRPO 强化学习，统一提升移动 UI 理解与生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动 UI 理解与生成同时需要细粒度组件感知与可靠的结构化合成；前作 UI-UG 已提出统一方向，但任务覆盖、分类体系对齐性及生成优化方式（离线 DPO）仍有改进空间，且理解与生成之间是否存在表征层面的相互促进尚待验证。

Method: (1) 构建来自 RICO、VINS、Android in the Wild、Android Control 的多源理解语料，标注为与前端组件语义对齐的 80 类两级 UI 分类体系；(2) 配套 5000+ requirement-screenshot-JSON 三元组，通过领域特定语言（DSL）进行渐进式结构化生成；(3) 理解侧引入分解奖励：类别预测、OCR 保真度、颜色识别、稠密定位、GUI 定位；(4) 生成侧用端到端在线 GRPO 替代离线 DPO，采样 DSL 输出经 Playwright 服务端管线渲染，并以 LLM-as-a-Judge 与对比偏好排序评估。

Result: 在自建 80 类基准上，UI-UG 2 在被评估模型中取得最强的指代（referring）与稠密定位结果；在 4B 紧凑规模下取得最佳综合生成分数；并在固定训练预算下观察到稳定的理解→生成迁移：加入细粒度 UI 感知监督可提升生成质量。

Conclusion: 组件级语义为理解与生成提供了共享表征基础，理解与生成可在统一框架下相互增强；在线渲染感知的强化学习与前端对齐分类体系是紧凑规模下实现高质量 UI 理解与生成的有效途径。

Abstract: Mobile UI understanding and generation require both fine-grained component perception and reliable structured synthesis. Inspired by the unified direction introduced by UI-UG, we present UI-UG 2, a compact 4B-parameter multimodal large language model (MLLM) that advances mobile UI Understanding and Generation with broader task coverage, a front-end-aligned taxonomy, and online render-aware reinforcement learning. UI-UG 2 constructs a multi-source understanding corpus from RICO, VINS, Android in the Wild, and Android Control, annotated with an 80-category two-level UI taxonomy aligned with front-end component semantics, and pairs it with over 5,000 requirement-screenshot-JSON tuples for progressive structured UI generation through a domain-specific language (DSL). For understanding, UI-UG 2 introduces decomposed rewards for category prediction, OCR fidelity, color recognition, dense grounding, and GUI grounding. Unlike the offline Direct Preference Optimization (DPO)-style generation optimization in UI-UG, UI-UG 2 optimizes generation through end-to-end online Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO): sampled DSL outputs are rendered with a Playwright-based server-side pipeline and evaluated using LLM-as-a-Judge and contrastive preference ranking. Experiments show that UI-UG 2 achieves the strongest referring and dense-grounding results among the evaluated models on our 80-category benchmark, and achieves the best composite generation score among compared models at a compact scale (4B). More importantly, we observe a consistent understanding-to-generation transfer: adding fine-grained UI perception supervision improves generation quality under a fixed training budget, suggesting that component-level semantics provide a shared representational foundation for both tasks.

</details>


### [120] [What Helps Denoising Can Hurt Memory: Rethinking History in Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00937)
*Yubin Jing,Ziyang Zhang,Meng Chen,Yuyao Li,Haofan Wang,Yichen Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出自回归视频生成中"提交"(committing)阶段的历史上下文与去噪阶段存在冲突,提出免训练规则 Commit Forcing 将提交组织成链式结构,在 6 个预训练生成器上显著提升长视频的一致性与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成器逐块生成长视频,依赖历史上下文维持时序一致性。每个干净块经提交过程编码为 KV 供后续块读取,而同一块在不同历史下编码结果不同。作者希望将历史上下文不仅视为去噪条件,也视为提交阶段的输入,探究其对后续生成的影响。

Method: 先在固定上下文大小和时间位置下分析历史上下文对一致性的影响,发现较旧历史在提交阶段被读取时反而有害;据此提出 Commit Forcing——一种免训练规则,规定提交时每个块仅读取紧邻前一块存储的 KV,使更早的历史只能沿链条间接传递。该规则应用于 6 个预训练生成器。

Result: 在 60 秒 VBench 上,6 个生成器的 Quality 与 Total 均提升,最高分别提升 1.27 和 1.10 分;长程一致性收益可扩展至 240 秒视频。在 120 秒 LongLive 视频的盲测对比中(不计平局),主体与场景一致性胜率 83.5%,整体偏好胜率 82.9%。

Conclusion: 历史上下文在去噪与提交两阶段作用不同,过旧上下文在提交阶段会损害后续生成;Commit Forcing 通过链式提交有效隔离该负面影响,无需训练即可普遍提升多个生成器的长视频质量与一致性。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generators produce long videos block by block, using historical context to maintain temporal consistency. After denoising, a committing pass encodes each clean block into keys and values (KV) that later blocks read, and the same clean block can be encoded into different KV depending on the history it reads. We therefore study historical context not only as a condition for denoising but also as an input to committing. Older historical context that supports denoising can harm subsequent generation when read during committing. At fixed context size and temporal positions, older historical context lowers mean clip-to-clip Subject Consistency. Across 5 generator configurations supporting longer contexts, consistency rises, plateaus, and then declines as committing context expands. Motivated by these findings, we propose Commit Forcing, a training-free rule that organizes committing into a chain. During committing, each block reads only the preceding block's stored KV, so older historical context reaches new entries only through the chain. We apply this rule to 6 pretrained generators. On 60-second VBench, Commit Forcing improves Quality and Total on all 6 generators, by up to 1.27 Quality points and 1.10 Total points. Long-range consistency gains extend to 240-second videos. In a blinded comparison on 120-second LongLive videos, Commit Forcing achieves win rates of 83.5% for subject and scene consistency and 82.9% for overall preference against the base generator, excluding ties.

</details>


### [121] [PSF-Informed Reconstruction: An Enhanced 2D Directional Filtered Backprojection Method for Cone-Beam Rotary Computed Laminography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00953)
*Chengxiang Wang,Wei Yu,Baodong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于点扩散函数（PSF）的二维方向滤波反投影（DFBP）方法，用于锥束旋转计算层析（CL）重建，以克服传统CL-FBP/CL-FDK沿探测器行一维滤波导致的严重叠影、低对比度与边缘模糊问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥束旋转计算层析适合成像高长径比物体，但CL-FBP与CL-FDK依赖沿探测器行方向的一维行滤波，重建结果存在明显的叠影伪影、对比度下降和边缘模糊，重建质量欠佳，需改进。

Method: 1) 建立重建像素与探测器上对应投影点之间的关系，推导投影过程的精确表达式；2) 在反投影中引入两个权重因子以抵消常规反投影的非线性，得到简化的PSF表达式；3) 在一定假设下对Fourier空间中的坐标关系做线性化近似，将三维图像空间滤波转移到二维投影空间，导出形式简单而有效的二维方向滤波器（融合二维von Hann窗与二维sinc窗）。

Result: 实验结果表明，与CL-FBP和CL-FDK相比，所提方法重建图像对比度更高、边界更锐利、叠影伪影更少，并能更准确地恢复气泡缺陷、电子元件等内部结构。

Conclusion: 基于PSF的二维方向滤波反投影可在避免三维滤波计算负担的前提下显著提升CL重建质量，是CL重建中替代传统一维行滤波方案的有效途径。

Abstract: Cone-beam rotary computed laminography (CL) presents a viable alternative to cone-beam CT for imaging objects with high aspect ratios. Conventional reconstruction techniques, such as CL-FBP and CL-FDK, often yield suboptimal results charac?terized by pronounced superimposition artifacts, reduced contrast,and blurred edges, primarily due to their reliance on 1D row-wise filtering along detector rows. To address these limitations and improve reconstruction quality, we proposed a novel 2D directional filtered backprojection (DFBP) method based on the point spread function (PSF). First, an exact expression for the projection process is derived by establishing the relationship between a reconstructed pixel and its corresponding projection point on the detector. Second, a revised backprojection process incorporating two weighting factors is introduced to counteract the nonlinearity inherent in conventional backprojection, leading to a simplified PSF formulation. Third, to circumvent the computational burden of 3D image space filtering,an approximate method by linearizing the coordinate relationship in Fourier space under some assumptions is developed that transfers the 3D filtering operation to the 2D projection space. This approach facilitates the derivation of a formally simple yet effective 2D direc?tional filter, which integrates a 2D von Hann window and a 2D sinc window. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method produces reconstructions with superior contrast, sharper boundaries,and fewer superimposition artifacts compared to CL-FBP and CL?FDK. Moreover, it enables more accurate recovery of internal structures, such as bubble defects and electronic components.

</details>


### [122] [4K Image Super-Resolution on Mobile NPUs: Mobile AI 2026 Challenge Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00963)
*Andrey Ignatov,Georgy Perevozchikov,Radu Timofte,Fubin Ren,Shihao Wang,Liwen Zhang,Zhe Xu,Zhipei Lei,Minjian Zhang,Kailing Tang,Dingyong Gou,Yanlin Wu,Cong Li,Xiaohui Cui,Kihwan Yoon,Ganzorig Gankhuyag,Heo MyeongCheol,Yonghyun Park,Juho Jung,Sungjei Kim,Phuong-Nam Nguyen-Pham,Nam-Tien Le,Tinh-Anh Nguyen-Nhu,Matin Fazel,Abdelhak Bentaleb,Xinrui Li,Xusheng He,Jianlong Wu,Mohsin Ikram,Dmitry Ignatov,Hao Chen,Long Sun,Jiangxin Dong,Jinshan Pan,Jinhui Tang,Siyue Teng,Yuxuan Jiang,Fan Zhang,David Bull,Zhe Xiao,Jun Xiao,Zhi-Song Liu,Jiayi Pei,Songtao Wu,Hong Shang,Kuanhong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是Mobile AI挑战赛报告，聚焦移动设备端高效图像超分辨率，要求将Full HD图像3倍上采样至4K并在Qualcomm与MediaTek NPU上实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备需对多种图像数据上采样，亟需既高性能又能部署于主流移动/IoT芯片NPU的深度学习模型。

Method: 基于DIV2K数据集训练3倍放大模型，设两条赛道：赛道一为全量化INT8模型，在Qualcomm Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5 NPU上评测；赛道二为浮点模型，在MediaTek Dimensity 9500 NPU上评测。

Result: 优胜方案可在5毫秒以内重建4K图像，同时PSNR达到30 dB。

Conclusion: 论文系统描述了挑战赛中开发的各类模型，展示了NPU端超分在速度与质量上的可行平衡。

Abstract: Image super-resolution is widely used on mobile devices for upscaling various types of image data. This creates a need for efficient deep learning-based models that are not only performant but can also be deployed on neural processing units (NPUs) present in the majority of mobile and IoT chipsets. In this Mobile AI challenge, we address this problem and propose the participants to design fast solutions that can upsample images from Full HD to 4K resolution while running on popular mobile NPUs from Qualcomm and MediaTek. For this, the participants were provided with the DIV2K dataset and trained models to do an efficient 3X image upscaling. The competition had two tracks: in the first track, the models had to be fully quantized (INT8), and the runtime of all models was evaluated on the Qualcomm Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5 NPU. In the second track, the participants were developing floating-point solutions that were evaluated on the MediaTek Dimensity 9500 NPU. Top solutions proposed in this challenge are able to reconstruct 4K images under 5 milliseconds while reaching a PSNR score of 30 dB. A comprehensive description of the models developed in the challenge is provided in this paper.

</details>


### [123] [Efficient Image Denoising on Smartphone GPUs: Mobile AI 2026 Challenge Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00974)
*Andrey Ignatov,Georgy Perevozchikov,Radu Timofte,HaoDong Wu,Desheng Cai,Nikolay Falaleev,Faraz Kayani,Sarmad Kayani,Asad Ahmed,Dmitry Ignatov,Kihwan Yoon,Ganzorig Gankhuyag,Yoonsuk Kang,The Van Le,Jin Young Lee,Sunzhe Yang,Qiuhai Yan,Kang Chen,Tingting Wang,Faming Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告Mobile AI挑战赛中的移动端全高清图像去噪任务：面向Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5（Adreno）与Dimensity 9500（Mali）GPU，参赛者需用真实采集的噪声-干净图像对训练高效模型；优胜方案在100 ms内完成FHD去噪并达到PSNR>37 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动摄影中的图像去噪是基础问题，但现有方案多在合成噪声图像上训练，且计算量大，难以在移动设备上处理全分辨率图像。因此需要既有性能又可在受限移动硬件上部署的高效深度学习模型。

Method: 以Mobile AI挑战赛形式组织：向参赛者提供大规模真实场景采集的噪声-干净图像对数据集，要求设计可在Qualcomm与Arm主流智能手机GPU上对Full HD分辨率照片实时去噪的快速方案；全部模型在Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5（Adreno GPU）和Dimensity 9500（Mali GPU）上进行运行时评测。

Result: 优胜方案在两个平台上均能在100毫秒以内完成高质量FHD图像去噪，PSNR超过37 dB。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛表明，轻量级深度学习模型可在主流移动GPU上实现实时、高质量的真实场景图像去噪；论文对参赛模型进行了全面描述与总结。

Abstract: Image denoising is a fundamental problem in mobile photography. Despite a large number of solutions proposed for this task, the majority of them are trained on synthetically produced noisy images, and are additionally too computationally heavy for processing full-resolution images on mobile devices. This creates a need for efficient deep learning-based models that are not only performant but can also be deployed on constrained mobile hardware. In this Mobile AI challenge, we address this problem and propose the participants to design fast solutions that can denoise Full HD resolution photos while running on popular smartphone GPUs from Qualcomm and Arm. For this, the participants were provided with a large-scale dataset consisting of noisy-clean image pairs captured in the wild. The runtime of all models was evaluated on the Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5 and Dimensity 9500 chipsets with the Adreno and Mali GPUs, respectively. Top solutions proposed in this challenge are able to perform high-quality image denoising under 100 milliseconds on both platforms while reaching a PSNR score of over 37 dB. A comprehensive description of the models developed in the challenge is provided in this paper.

</details>


### [124] [Robust Pixel-wise Digital Image Correlation under Large Motions and Illumination Variations via a Global-to-Local Hybrid Neural Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01018)
*Xianglong Guo,Jianyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种"全局到局部"混合神经DIC框架：第一阶段估计主导刚体变换实现鲁棒初始化，第二阶段用KAN+MLP表示残差位移并以重叠有效、抗光照的结构损失优化，实现像素级鲁棒测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动DIC推理快但泛化差；经典优化DIC与图像约束型神经网络DIC无需标注位移，但收敛性和精度易受初始化、大刚体运动、细微非均匀变形及灰度扰动影响。

Method: 两阶段混合框架。阶段1：粗旋转搜索+相位相关+掩膜细化，估计主导面内刚体变换，在大平移和大旋转下提供可靠初值。阶段2：以KAN与MLP混合网络表示残差位移场，并采用有效重叠、光照鲁棒的结构损失优化；局部灰度模型显式建模乘性与加性光强变化。

Result: 仿真与真实实验表明，在噪声、非均匀变形、大旋转、光照变化及成像质量退化条件下，均能准确、稳定地恢复位移。

Conclusion: 全局到局部的混合神经DIC可在无标注位移数据条件下兼顾鲁棒初始化与精细变形表征，提升复杂成像条件与极端运动下的测量精度与稳定性。

Abstract: Digital image correlation (DIC) is a widely used non-contact technique for full-field displacement measurement. Data-driven deep-learning DIC offers rapid inference but may generalize poorly to unseen speckle patterns, deformation modes, noise levels, and illumination conditions. Classical optimization-based DIC and recent image-constrained neural formulations avoid labeled displacement data, yet their convergence and accuracy remain vulnerable to initialization, large rigid-body motion, fine non-uniform deformation, and gray-value disturbances. This paper presents a global-to-local hybrid neural DIC framework for robust pixelwise measurement. Stage 1 estimates a dominant in-plane rigid transformation through coarse rotation search, phase correlation, and masked refinement, providing a reliable initialization under large translation and rotation. Stage 2 represents the residual displacement with a hybrid Kolmogorov-Arnold network (KAN) and multilayer perceptron (MLP), and optimizes it using a valid-overlap, illumination-robust structural loss. The local gray-value model explicitly accommodates multiplicative and additive intensity changes. Simulated and real experiments demonstrate accurate and stable displacement recovery under noise, non-uniform deformation, large rotation, illumination variation, and degraded imaging conditions.

</details>


### [125] [Grounding Across Views: Cross-View Referring Reasoning for Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01074)
*Hao Gan,Liuqian Wang,Shuang Li,Yufei Gao,Wentao Chao,Chong Fu,Chengming Liu,Lei Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出跨视角视觉定位CVVG新任务，即由指代表达与视觉示例联合引导的实例级定位，并给出视图感知框架GAV，在MoP-UAV上取得62.55% Acc@0.5的新SOTA，同时发布CVOGL-Text基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角目标地理定位（CVOGL）多用点击点或边界框指定查询，未充分利用自然语言蕴含的语义属性与关系；且指代表达常依赖查询视角，难以直接在卫星图像中落地。

Method: 形式化CVVG任务；提出GAV框架，核心为视图感知指代推理：从标注的查询图像生成目标中心描述、校验目标类别一致性、并加入无方向角色线索；结合示例提示、跨视角对齐与场景级判别，将语义证据落到卫星实例上。

Result: MoP-UAV上Acc@0.5达62.55%，为新的SOTA；新建CVOGL-Text基准，GAV在街景与无人机视角划分上分别比点击点基线高7.09与6.79个Acc@0.25点。

Conclusion: 语言与视觉示例联合引导的视图感知定位可显著提升跨视角地理定位性能，并对更大视角差异更鲁棒；代码与标注将公开。

Abstract: Cross-view object geo-localization (CVOGL) aims to localize a target in a satellite image from an observation captured under another viewpoint. Existing methods typically specify the query with a click point or bounding box, marking the target region but underusing the semantic attributes and relations conveyed by natural language. Moreover, referring expressions are often viewpoint-dependent, making query-view descriptions difficult to ground directly in the satellite image. In this paper, we formulate cross-view visual grounding (CVVG), extending conventional point- or box-prompted CVOGL to instance grounding jointly guided by a referring expression and a visual exemplar. We present GAV (Grounding Across Views), a view-aware framework for selectively transferring visual and linguistic evidence across views. Its core is view-aware referring reasoning: target-centered descriptions are derived from the marked query image, checked for target-category consistency, and augmented with a direction-free role cue. Exemplar prompting, cross-view alignment, and scene-level discrimination ground this semantic evidence in a satellite instance. GAV achieves a new state of the art on MoP-UAV with 62.55% Acc@0.5. We also introduce CVOGL-Text, a benchmark for text-guided grounding from street- and drone-view queries to satellite images. GAV outperforms the click-point baseline by 7.09 and 6.79 Acc@0.25 points on the street- and drone-view splits, respectively, demonstrating robustness to larger viewpoint gaps. The code and annotations will be publicly released.

</details>


### [126] [CuPH: Parallel Cubical Persistent Homology via Level-Synchronous Component Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01078)
*Guangyu Meng,Pengfei Gu,Erin Wolf Chambers,Danny Z. Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CuPH：直接在像素上运行的GPU并行立方持续同调算法，将并行深度从Θ(n)降至O(L log n)，比GPU基线快18.6–99.6倍，并可加速拓扑感知分类与分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准立方持续同调需按过滤顺序对立方单元（顶点、边、面）串行约简，对n像素图像具有Θ(n)的并行深度，严重限制图像内部GPU并行性，阻碍其用于拓扑感知深度学习。

Method: 提出CuPH：以像素而非立方单元为操作对象；按过滤值对像素分桶；采用层级同步扫描激活像素；用无锁并查集跟踪连通分量；将整个层级扫描实现为单个CUDA内核以消除跨层内核启动开销。并证明整数输入下结果与标准立方PH一致，浮点输入下具稳定性界。

Result: 并行深度由Θ(n)降为O(L log n)（L为强度级数）；较现有GPU基线CMP快18.6–99.6倍；作为可直接替换的PH后端，使拓扑感知分类加速4.2–16.7倍、分割加速8.3–44倍且保持精度；可在高分辨率图像上计算全图PH损失，分割Dice提升2.2–4.6点，训练加速25–31倍。

Conclusion: CuPH通过像素级并行与无锁并查集实现高效可扩展的立方持续同调计算，兼具理论等价性与显著实证加速，可作为现有拓扑感知深度学习流程的即插即用后端。

Abstract: Persistent homology (PH) is a mathematically principled multi-scale topological descriptor of shape and connectivity in data. It is widely applied in image classification, segmentation, and shape analysis across medical and aerial imaging, providing structural information that pixel-wise deep learning may overlook. Standard cubical PH decomposes an image into cubical cells (vertices, edges, and squares) and reduces them sequentially in filtration order. This sequential reduction has $Θ(n)$ parallel depth for an $n$-pixel image, limiting within-image GPU parallelism and constraining its use in topology-aware deep learning. We introduce CuPH, a GPU-based parallel algorithm for computing cubical PH on 2D images that operates directly on pixels rather than cubical cells. CuPH buckets pixels by filtration values, activates them with a level-synchronous sweep, and tracks connected components with a lock-free union-find method. We prove that CuPH produces persistence diagrams identical to standard cubical PH on integer inputs, with a stability bound for floating-point inputs. CuPH reduces parallel depth from $Θ(n)$ to $O(L\log n)$ for an image with $L$ intensity levels. We implement the level sweep as a single CUDA kernel, eliminating cross-level kernel-launch overhead. Empirically, CuPH is $18.6$--$99.6\times$ faster than the existing GPU baseline (CMP). As a drop-in PH backend, CuPH accelerates topology-aware classification ($4.2$--$16.7\times$) and segmentation ($8.3$--$44\times$) while preserving accuracy. It also enables computing full-image PH losses on high-resolution images, improving segmentation Dice by $2.2$--$4.6$ points over patch-local PH baselines and accelerating training by $25$--$31\times$.

</details>


### [127] [QVR3D: Query-Guided Visual Reasoning for Training-Free 3D Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01084)
*Jung-Hao Kao,Jing-En Huang,Jia-Wei Liao,Tzuhsuan Huang,Yu-Lun Liu,Cheng-Fu Chou,Jun-Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架QVR3D，通过查询引导的最大覆盖视图选择与接地概率估计，实现RGB-D数据上的零样本3D视觉接地，在ScanRefer与Nr3D上达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练3D视觉接地方法依赖显式场景变换或场景级帧选择，易将有限视觉容量浪费在与查询无关的区域；作者认为有效接地应优先选取能为查询提供证据的观测，而非均匀表征整个场景。

Method: 基于现成基础模型，在RGB-D观测上构建查询引导的视觉推理框架：1) Query-Guided Maximum Coverage (QGMC)，选择互补的、与查询相关的视图；2) Grounding Probability Estimation (GPE)，融合实例置信度、属性一致性与关系一致性进行概率估计。框架无需任务特定适配，同时支持单目标与多目标接地。

Result: 在ScanRefer和Nr3D上取得零样本SOTA性能；QGMC在多个3D视觉-语言任务中提升了帧效率。

Conclusion: 查询引导的证据优先视图选择优于均匀场景表征，可在免训练条件下高效实现高质量3D视觉接地，且视图选择策略具跨任务通用性。

Abstract: 3D visual grounding localizes objects in 3D scenes from natural language descriptions, requiring reasoning over semantics, spatial relationships, and multi-view observations. Training-free approaches avoid costly annotations but often rely on explicit scene transformation or scene-level frame selection, which may waste limited visual capacity on query-irrelevant regions. We observe that effective grounding should prioritize observations that provide evidence for the query rather than uniformly represent the entire scene. Based on this insight, we introduce QVR3D, a query-guided visual reasoning framework that operates on RGB-D observations using off-the-shelf foundation models. QVR3D combines Query-Guided Maximum Coverage (QGMC) for selecting complementary query-relevant views with Grounding Probability Estimation (GPE) that integrates instance confidence, attribute consistency, and relationship consistency. The framework supports both single- and multi-object grounding without task-specific adaptation. Experiments on ScanRefer and Nr3D achieve state-of-the-art zero-shot performance, while QGMC also improves frame efficiency across multiple 3D vision-language tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/jwliao1209/QVR3D.

</details>


### [128] [StreamDuo: A Live Commentator and a Research Agent for Unseen Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01120)
*Zixian Gao,Penghao Luo,Xiangcheng Cao,Lixin Xiu,Ziqi Cai,Zhen Li,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 StreamDuo：可训练解说器 + 冻结的、可读游戏 wiki 的研究代理组合，仅用单一游戏训练即可实现跨游戏流式解说，显著提升实体召回并降低源游戏泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频大模型解说的训练与测试均在同一游戏上进行，缺乏跨游戏泛化评估。实验发现当前模型跨游戏时只迁移了"解说时机"，而未迁移"游戏世界知识"（实体识别与命名能力缺失）。

Method: 解耦为两个模块：可训练解说器 + 冻结的研究代理（检索当前所玩游戏 wiki）。解说器遇到无法命名的内容时，将当前帧发送给研究代理，代理在约 2 秒内返回少量 wiki 条目，直播流不中断。仅训练解说器，且在单一游戏上用两阶段训练：(1) 信息增益标签，教会模型只在答案有助于复现人类解说词时提问；(2) 基于策略的自蒸馏，由能看到理想答案的自身副本指导，学会使用含噪答案。

Result: 在《博德之门 3》训练、10 个未见游戏测试：实体召回从 Proact-VL（同数据训练）的 0.082 提升至 0.116；源游戏泄漏率从 9.9% 降至 1.0%；在流式模型中取得最佳时机控制，以及人工评判下最高的生动度与整体质量。

Conclusion: 将"何时发问"与"如何使用外部知识"作为可学习能力，配合冻结检索代理，可在单游戏训练下实现有效的跨游戏流式解说；时序迁移与知识迁移需分别处理。

Abstract: Streaming video LLMs can now commentate live gameplay, but they are trained and tested on the same games. We study cross-game streaming commentary, in which a model trained on one game commentates games it has never seen, and find that current commentators transfer their timing but not their knowledge of the game world. We propose StreamDuo, which pairs a trainable commentator with a frozen research agent that reads the wiki of the game being played. When the commentator sees something it cannot name, it sends the current frames, and the research agent returns a few wiki entries two seconds later while the stream continues. We train only the commentator, on a single game, in two stages. Information-gain labels teach it to ask only where an answer would help it say what the human caster said, and on-policy self-distillation from a copy of itself that sees the ideal answer teaches it to use noisy answers. Trained on Baldur's Gate 3 and tested on ten unseen games, StreamDuo raises entity recall from 0.082 to 0.116 over Proact-VL trained on the same data, cuts source-game leakage from 9.9% to 1.0%, and achieves the best timing and the highest judged liveliness and overall quality among streaming models.

</details>


### [129] [What Does a Safety Label Measure? Auditing Situational-Safety Benchmarks for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01125)
*Wayner Barrios,Kenneth Barrios*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Benchmark Card（七个可测字段）与Spot-Stop分解，揭示情景安全基准的标签实际混合了场景、请求与基准自身文件的信息，并区分排序能力与标注能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情景安全基准通过SAFE/UNSAFE标签评分MLLM并指导部署，但标签分数究竟度量了什么并不清楚——分数可能来自场景本身、请求内容，或基准自身文件（如图像尺寸、条目划分）。

Method: 提出Benchmark Card（七项实测字段）对六个基准进行刻画；引入Spot-Stop分解，在安全/不安全场景对上比较排序与标注，将标签交换零假设之上的差距拆分为阈值部分、安全与不安全分数重叠、仅在配对内成立的顺序三部分；并检验单阈值修正与无标签重中心化。

Result: 仅MSSBench chat通过图像文件泄露标签，单凭图像尺寸达82.3%准确率，追平或超过七个商用模型中的六个；MOSSBench中条目划分决定标签，HoliSafe中仅凭请求的配对排序与场景相当；修正后的chat划分上尺寸规则降至随机水平。十四个开源MLLM中多数排序优于标注，超出AUROC预期；chat上共享阈值修复三分之一到一半的超额，平衡批次上无标签重中心化可完全恢复；在VSCBench上对同情形无标签场景重中心化使13/14模型排序提升。

Conclusion: 基准标签分数并非纯粹反映模型的场景安全判断，需用Benchmark Card审计数据泄露与设计偏差，并用Spot-Stop分解分离排序与标注能力；简单的阈值与重中心化修正即可显著改善，说明部分"不安全"表现源于分数校准而非判别力。

Abstract: Situational-safety benchmarks label each scene SAFE or UNSAFE and score a multimodal large language model (MLLM) against that label, usually by judging its free-form answer, and these scores guide deployment. We ask what such a label measures. A label score can come from the scene, the request or the benchmark's own files. We propose the Benchmark Card, seven measured fields that tell them apart, and fill it for six benchmarks. Only MSSBench chat leaks the label through its image files: image size alone reaches 82.3% accuracy, matching or beating six of seven commercial models. Elsewhere the signal outside the scene comes from design. In MOSSBench the item partition fixes the label, and on HoliSafe the request alone ranks pairs about as well as the scene. On our corrected chat split, the size rule falls to chance. We then introduce the Spot-Stop decomposition, which compares ranking with labelling on pairs of a safe and an unsafe scene. It splits the gap above a label-swap null into three parts: a threshold part, overlap between safe and unsafe scores, and order that holds only within a pair. Most of fourteen open MLLMs rank better than they label, beyond what their AUROC predicts. On chat, one shared threshold repairs a third to a half of the excess, and label-free recentering recovers it on balanced batches. Recentering on unlabelled scenes of the same situation improves ranking on VSCBench for 13 of 14 models.

</details>


### [130] [GESTALT: Fast, Generalizable Histopathology Image Synthesis from Gene Expression with Pretrained Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01141)
*Felix Ho,Alexandros Graikos,Dimitris Samaras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GESTALT将基因表达到图像的合成重构为预测病理基础模型的图像嵌入，借助预训练视觉知识避免从头训练，在多细胞与单细胞设定下均超越Xenium基准SoTA，并支持原生1024×1024合成与多种泛化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学关注"细胞位置+基因表达"，而其逆问题——特定转录组状态对应的形态学表现——研究不足，但价值很大：可可视化分子状态的形态后果，并将空间检测的分子判定对照真实组织结构进行解释。现有SoTA方法GeneFlow在Xenium基因表达到图像的基准上需针对每个新目标数据集从头训练，成本高昂。

Method: 将基因表达到图像合成重构为预测病理基础模型的图像嵌入：轻量级基因token transformer从多细胞基因表达预测该嵌入，再由冻结或LoRA微调的扩散解码器（PixCell）渲染为图像；视觉知识被复用而非重新学习。此外结合空间表达的统计模型，实现细胞类型、密度与组成可控的配对合成。

Result: 在多细胞与单细胞两种设定下，于全部三个Xenium数据集上感知指标均超越SoTA；原生支持1024×1024分辨率合成；在三种泛化设定中评估：跨基因面板与数据集的零样本迁移、留出患者与疾病状态、跨研究与跨组织来源迁移；并首次实现细胞级转录组场与组织图像的配对合成且具显式控制。

Conclusion: 复用预训练病理基础模型的视觉表征可显著降低基因表达到图像合成的训练成本并提升图像质量与泛化能力，为分子状态与组织形态的关联可视化及可控数据合成提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics measures gene expression while preserving where each cell sits in the tissue. The inverse, what a given transcriptomic state looks like, is less explored but equally useful: it lets researchers visualize the morphological consequence of a molecular state and interpret a spatial assay's molecular calls against real tissue structure. GeneFlow, the current state of the art on the Xenium gene expression-to-image benchmark, trains a model from scratch, incurring a high cost on every new target dataset it is applied to. We instead propose GESTALT, which reformulates gene expression-to-image synthesis as predicting a pathology foundation model's image embedding. A lightweight gene-token transformer predicts this embedding from multi-cell gene expression, and a frozen or LoRA-adapted diffusion decoder (PixCell) renders it as an image. Because this visual knowledge is reused rather than relearned, GESTALT trains in a fraction of the time. In both multi-cell and single-cell settings, our method outperforms the SoTA on perceptual metrics across all three Xenium datasets. Beyond improved image quality, GESTALT natively synthesizes at 1024 x 1024, and is evaluated in three generalization settings: zero-shot transfer across gene panels and datasets, held-out patients and disease states, and transfer across studies and tissue sources. Coupled with a statistical model of spatial expression, GESTALT also provides, to our knowledge, the first paired synthesis of cell-resolved transcriptomic fields and tissue images with explicit control over cell type, density, and composition. Code is available at https://github.com/trongnhandev1011/Gestalt.

</details>


### [131] [SHield: Watermarking 4D Gaussian Splatting via Spherical Harmonic Coefficient Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01162)
*Kunal Bhosikar,Prajas Wadekar,Venkata Sai Pranav Bachina,Ankit Gangwal,Charu Sharma,Nicole Meng,Yingjie Lao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SHield 是首个面向 4D Gaussian Splatting（4DGS）参数文件的非盲、免解码器水印方法，可将全分辨率 RGB 图像原生嵌入 SH 系数，并以闭式算术恢复，渲染惩罚≤0.13 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4DGS 训练昂贵但以参数文件形式易被复制分发；现有水印依赖对神经解码器微调、从渲染视频中提取比特串，流程重且受动态场景时间变化影响。若威胁模型针对被再分发的文件本身，验证可大幅简化。

Method: 利用 4DGS 规范 SH 系数的时间与视角不变性，将零均值载荷分块嵌入密钥选定高斯子集的 SH 系数，辅以短暂锚定优化保持视觉保真；验证时以规范高斯中心做几何重关联以抵抗文件篡改，并闭式恢复载荷。

Result: 30 个场景（D-NeRF、HyperNeRF、DyNeRF）、150 个模型上载荷近完美恢复（Pearson r≥0.991），平均渲染惩罚≤0.13 dB；留出评估中 400 次真实试验全检测、1600 次错误子集零假设试验零误报；图像载荷可抵抗 INT8 量化与 30% 不透明度剪枝，SH 1 阶截断下比特载荷仍可解码。

Conclusion: 面向文件级威胁模型，免解码器的参数空间水印在 4DGS 上兼具高保真、高可靠取证与可界定的鲁棒性，是比渲染视频解码更轻量的资产保护范式。

Abstract: 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) models are computationally expensive to train but distributed as easily copied parameter files. Existing watermarks protect these assets by fine-tuning the scene against a neural decoder to extract bit strings from rendered video, a heavy process vulnerable to the temporal dynamics of dynamic scenes. However, if the threat model targets the redistributed file itself, verification can be vastly simplified. Because the canonical Spherical-Harmonic (SH) coefficients of 4DGS are time- and view-invariant, parameter-space perturbations can be recovered via deterministic arithmetic. We propose **SHield**, the first non-blind, decoder-free watermarking approach that natively embeds full-resolution RGB images into 4DGS files. SHield tiles a zero-mean payload across the SH coefficients of a key-selected Gaussian subset, applying a brief anchored optimization to preserve visual fidelity. To verify ownership, it geometrically re-associates the key using canonical Gaussian centres to survive file tampering and recovers the payload in closed form. Across 30 scenes (D-NeRF, HyperNeRF, DyNeRF), SHield guarantees near-perfect payload recovery (Pearson $r \geq 0.991$) on all 150 tested models with a mean rendering penalty of $\leq 0.13$~dB. Held-out evaluation yields perfect detection on 400 true trials and zero false positives across $1{,}600$ wrong-subset null trials. We also strictly bound the method's robustness: SHield survives INT8 quantization and $30\%$ opacity pruning for the image payload, and on aggressive SH-degree-1 truncation bit payloads are still decodable.

</details>


### [132] [Modeling Fat-shift for Improved Head Segmentation from MRI Scans using a Probabilistic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01222)
*Merle Diedrichsen,Koen Van Leemput,Douglas N. Greve,Oula Puonti,Axel Thielscher*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FLOW——一种基于变分推断的概率模型，在T1w MRI全头分割中显式建模脂肪化学位移伪影，从而更准确地重建颅骨与头皮等颅外解剖结构，并降低生物物理仿真偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体化计算头模型依赖T1w MRI的准确全头分割。但T1w对颅外组织（致密骨几乎无信号）对比度差，且存在化学位移伪影：脂肪组织沿频率编码方向（常为上下方向）下移，使颅骨显得比实际更薄。现有分割方法未对此建模，导致颅骨与头皮标注不准，进而在生物物理仿真中引入偏差。

Method: 利用扫描参数可确定脂肪位移的方向与幅度，据此显式建模该物理位移。作者提出FLOW（Fat-shift-informed Label Optimization of the Whole head）方法，采用变分推断的概率模型，在分割头与脑结构的同时将脂肪位移纳入标签优化，实现伪影校正后的解剖重建。

Result: 与不考虑脂肪位移的分割方法相比，FLOW重建的颅外解剖更准确，同时其余结构仍保持高分割精度；基于FLOW校正分割的电场仿真，其偏差相比未建模位移的方法显著降低。

Conclusion: 将MR物理伪影的先验知识显式融入分割模型，可有效校正脂肪化学位移、提升全头分割精度，并减少下游生物物理仿真（如电场仿真）的系统偏差。

Abstract: Personalized computational head models for biophysical simulations rely on accurate whole-head segmentations from T1-weighted (T1w) magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) scans. While T1w scans provide excellent contrast for intracranial anatomy, extracerebral tissues have partly almost no signal (compact bone) and also suffer from the so-called chemical shift artifact, which affects the fatty tissues in MRI and causes them to spatially shift in the frequency encoding direction of the scan. This artifact occurs primarily in two structures of the head: the spongy bone, a fatty compartment inside the compact bone, and the subcutaneous fat, located directly underneath the skin. The frequency-encoding direction is often set in the superior-inferior direction, causing the fatty tissues to shift down and, as a result, making the skull appear thinner than it actually is. Existing segmentation methods do not model this artifact, consequently leading to inaccurate labeling of the skull and scalp, which introduces a bias in biophysical simulations. The direction and magnitude of the fat-shift can be determined from the scan parameters, thus making it possible to explicitly model the shift and to recover the correct anatomy. This study introduces a probabilistic model that uses variational inference to segment head and brain structures from a T1w scan while accounting for the fat-shift artifact. The proposed Fat-shift-informed Label Optimization of the Whole head (FLOW) method reconstructs extracerebral anatomy more accurately than a fat-shift-agnostic segmentation approach while maintaining high segmentation accuracy in all other structures. FLOW-based fat-shift corrected segmentations significantly reduce bias in electric field simulations compared to a segmentation method that does not model the shift.

</details>


### [133] [Reproducing INP-Former: Reconstructing Intrinsic Normal Prototypes for Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01230)
*Yunfei Liang,Juan Pablo Osorio Rivera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 复现INP-Former多类工业异常检测方法，验证其在MVTec AD/VisA上的主结论，并扩展至MVTec AD 2，发现部分原始结论（如默认6个原型）在更难数据上不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证INP-Former核心主张的可复现性，并检验其在更具挑战性的MVTec AD 2上的泛化能力与默认设置的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用作者开源代码复现MVTec AD与VisA性能；扩展评估到MVTec AD 2；进行五种子消融分析INP机制与两个辅助损失贡献；以双损失禁用作配对比较；考察原型数量、分辨率、骨干规模、训练长度、LoRA适配等权衡，并按缺陷尺寸、光照条件和更严格AU-PRO阈值分析。

Result: MVTec AD/VisA性能可复现；MVTec AD 2默认设置图像AUROC仅63.4（MVTec AD为99.7）；消融显示性能主要来自INP机制，辅助损失影响小且方向依指标而异；默认6个原型迁移性差，MVTec AD 2上单原型AU-PRO优于6原型。

Conclusion: INP机制是主要性能来源且具可复现性，但原基准的部分结论（尤其默认原型数）无法迁移到更难数据，需针对硬数据重新调参。

Abstract: We reproduce the central claims of INP-Former, a reconstruction-based method for multi-class industrial anomaly detection. INP-Former extracts Intrinsic Normal Prototypes (INPs) from each test image and uses them to guide a decoder that reconstructs normal features, without a stored memory bank. Using the authors' released code, we reproduce the reported performance on MVTec AD and VisA and extend the evaluation to MVTec AD 2, where image AUROC with the default settings is 63.4, compared with 99.7 on MVTec AD. On MVTec AD, a five-seed ablation shows that most of the performance comes from the INP mechanism, while the two auxiliary losses add small changes whose direction depends on the metric. A paired comparison with both losses disabled confirms the benefit of INP on MVTec AD 2. The default of six prototypes transfers less well: the plateau beyond four prototypes reproduces on MVTec AD and VisA, but on MVTec AD 2 a single prototype outperforms six in AU-PRO on the public split and on both hidden test splits. We also measure practical trade-offs in resolution, backbone size, training length, and LoRA adaptation, and examine performance by defect size, lighting condition, and a stricter AU-PRO limit. Several conclusions from the original benchmarks, including the default prototype count, do not carry over to harder data. Code: https://github.com/garasnote/Repro-INPFormer

</details>


### [134] [Context-Shielded Backdoor Attack against Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01252)
*Zhifang Zhang,Qiang Huang,Chenpeng Wang,Weitong Chen,Olaf Maennel,Wei Emma Zhang,Lei Feng,Lei Zhang,Miao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"上下文剥离"现象：全长度干净视觉token会压制嵌入的触发器，而推理期视觉token压缩会把上下文剥离掉从而激活后门；据此提出仅投影器层的后门StripWake，在9种未见压缩器上保持0.4%全token ASR而压缩后ASR达94.0%–99.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM后门在触发图像出现时即激活，常规后门在全token推理下ASR接近100%，在部署前审计中立刻暴露被污染的checkpoint；而此前的"部署触发型"攻击仅针对量化或剪枝权重。因此缺乏一种在完整推理审计下潜伏、且能在未见压缩器上泛化的后门。

Method: 先揭示并利用"上下文剥离"机制：干净视觉token构成的上下文会抑制触发器，token剪枝去除上下文后激活后门。基于此设计仅作用于projector的后门StripWake：将干净监督的全token视图与目标监督的随机压缩视图配对训练，并将早期视觉注意力对齐到冻结的良性教师模型以保持干净性能。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5-7B上，跨9种未见压缩器：全token ASR仅0.4%（保持潜伏），在5种主要压缩器上压缩ASR达94.0%–99.7%，且CIDEr无损失。

Conclusion: 视觉token压缩可作为后门的隐式触发器通道，形成"审计潜伏、部署激活"的风险；该攻击对未见压缩器具有强迁移性且不牺牲干净效用，提示预部署审计需联合考虑推理期压缩策略。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) routinely use inference-time visual-token compression to prune hundreds of patch tokens down to a compact budget. Existing LVLM backdoors fire whenever a triggered image appears, whereas prior deployment-triggered attacks activate only when model weights are quantized or pruned. However, conventional LVLM backdoors exhibit nearly 100% attack success rate (ASR) under full-token inference, immediately exposing compromised checkpoints during pre-deployment auditing. We reveal context stripping: surrounding clean visual tokens suppress an embedded trigger at full length, whereas token pruning strips this context away and activates the backdoor. Motivated by this finding, we propose StripWake, a projector-only backdoor that remains dormant under full-token auditing yet activates across unseen compressors. Specifically, StripWake pairs clean-supervised full-token views with target-supervised randomized compressed views, and aligns early visual attention with a frozen benign teacher to preserve clean utility. On LLaVA-1.5-7B across nine unseen compressors, StripWake stays dormant at 0.4% full-token ASR yet achieves 94.0%-99.7% compressed ASR across five primary compressors without CIDEr loss.

</details>


### [135] [StreamClarify: Proactive Clarification for Grounded Streaming Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01263)
*Zhuchen Shao,Bo Chen,Klara Nahrstedt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamClarify 是一个免训练框架，面向流式视频问答，先判断不确定性再决定直接作答还是主动澄清，并用分层记忆支撑重答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频问答需要在严格延迟与有限活跃上下文预算下实时推理。现有流式VLM在查询含歧义、前提错误、或关键视觉证据已移出活跃上下文时，常给出自信但无依据的答案。主动澄清可缓解，但同样受延迟与内存约束。

Method: 方法为免训练框架：（1）预作答不确定性检查，以二值预作答判断的熵作为轻量不确定性分数，决定直接回答或请求澄清；（2）分层记忆，时间金字塔KV缓存维持有界活跃视觉上下文，CPU端语义检索档案提供历史帧访问以支持重答；（3）构建 StreamClarify-QA 诊断基准，含1,837条QA对（来自StreamBench与OVO-Bench），覆盖标准、指代歧义、不可回答三类场景。

Result: 在三个VLM主干上，Macro准确率较同主干基线最高提升8.2分；在相近触发率下，基于熵的选择在识别初始错误答案上的精确率比随机选择高18分以上；在oracle用户引导下，澄清使Macro准确率最高提升3.5分；在Ambiguous QA上进行的受控oracle辅助Qwen3-VL模拟（生成式提问+仅指代回复）中，熵门控条件提升8.3分。
