Conclusion: 以熵为不确定性信号的门控澄清机制与分层有界记忆相结合，可在不训练、不额外增加延迟与内存开销的前提下显著提升流式视频问答的准确性与答案可靠性，尤其改善歧义与不可回答场景。

Abstract: Streaming video question answering (QA) requires real-time reasoning over continuous video streams under strict latency and active-context budgets. Current streaming vision-language models (VLMs) often give confident yet ungrounded answers when queries are ambiguous or have false premises, or when relevant visual evidence is no longer in the active context. Proactive clarification can help, but must satisfy the same latency and memory constraints. We present StreamClarify, a training-free framework for grounded streaming QA that combines a pre-answer uncertainty check with hierarchical memory. The check uses the entropy of a binary pre-answer judgment as a lightweight uncertainty score to decide whether to answer directly or request clarification. To support re-answering, a temporal-pyramid KV cache maintains a bounded active visual context, while a CPU-side semantic retrieval archive provides access to historical frames. We further construct StreamClarify-QA, a diagnostic benchmark of 1,837 QA pairs from StreamBench and OVO-Bench, spanning standard, referential ambiguity, and unanswerable scenarios. Across three VLM backbones, StreamClarify improves Macro accuracy by up to 8.2 points over backbone-matched baselines. At comparable trigger rates, entropy-based selection achieves more than 18 points higher precision than random selection in identifying initially incorrect answers. Under oracle user guidance, clarification improves Macro accuracy by up to 3.5 points. We also report a controlled, oracle-assisted Qwen3-VL simulation on Ambiguous QA with generated questions and referent-only user replies, where the entropy-gated condition improves accuracy by 8.3 points.

</details>


### [136] [DriftingPlanner: Drifting Generative Models for One-Step Trajectory Planning in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01283)
*Ziming Liu,Yizhan Qu,Leichen Wang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriftingPlanner：仅用 drifting loss（无坐标回归项）训练的端到端轨迹规划器，推理时用反对称噪声对平均消除"壳层"偏移，单步推理达 SOTA 93.75 PDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端规划器存在两难：坐标回归快但把多种可行机动混合成一条可能均不属于任何机动的轨迹；扩散/流匹配能建模轨迹分布但需多步推理。drifting 模型把迭代搬到训练阶段（一步生成器学习吸引—排斥场），但原本面向类条件图像生成，每类有大量正样本；而在规划中每个场景自成条件。跨场景汇聚会把某场景生成轨迹吸引到无关场景真值上，而逐场景计算场又只剩单一正样本（专家轨迹），相互排斥会把生成轨迹压在以专家轨迹为中心的"壳"上，导致任何单条生成轨迹都偏离真值。

Method: 逐场景计算 drifting 场；去除坐标回归项，仅用 drifting loss 训练。推理时通过调整壳层影响实现去壳：在一次批处理中对每个噪声向量与其相反向量同时解码，成对（antithetic）平均抵消掉壳层中心位移的线性分量。

Result: NAVSIM navtest 上达到 93.75 PDMS 的 SOTA；单步推理下 drifting 解码器与需十步推理的最佳扩散/流匹配设置仅差 0.13 PDMS。

Conclusion: 逐场景 drifting 场 + 推理期反对称配对平均，可在无回归项、单步推理条件下实现高精度轨迹规划，兼顾生成式分布建模能力与回归式推理效率。

Abstract: End-to-end planners decode the ego trajectory either by coordinate regression, which is fast but blends plausible maneuvers into one trajectory that may follow none of them, or by diffusion and flow matching, which model the trajectory distribution but need several inference steps. Drifting models move this iteration into training: a one-step generator learns to follow an attraction--repulsion field between generated and ground-truth samples. Drifting was developed for class-conditional image generation, where every class supplies many positive samples. In planning, every scene is its own condition. Pooling scenes would attract the generated trajectories of one scene to the ground truth of unrelated scenes, whereas computing the field per scene leaves a single positive, the expert trajectory. Mutual repulsion then holds the generated trajectories on a shell around it, so any single generated trajectory deviates from the ground truth. We present DriftingPlanner, a trajectory planner trained with the drifting loss alone, without any coordinate regression term. It computes the field per scene and removes the shell at inference: it decodes every noise vector together with its negation in one batched pass, and averaging each antithetic pair cancels the linear part of the displacement from the shell center. On NAVSIM navtest, DriftingPlanner reaches a state-of-the-art 93.75 PDMS, and with single-step inference its drifting decoder comes within 0.13 PDMS of the best diffusion or flow matching setting, which needs ten inference steps.

</details>


### [137] [[Re] Pure Noise to the Rescue of Insufficient Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01295)
*Seungjae Ryan Lee,Seungmin Brian Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是对 Zada, Benou, Irani (2021) 关于"纯噪声过采样(OPeN)+专用BN"方法的复现与再检验：四条主要论断均可复现，但额外实验显示其增益可能与更大网络或更复杂数据增强的增益更为正交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原论文提出用纯噪声图像对少数类过采样并结合专门的批归一化层，声称能改善不平衡图像分类的泛化；其论断较强且方法反直觉，因此有必要独立复现并检验其稳健性与增益来源。

Method: 依据原文描述用 PyTorch 重建训练流程，并整合作者的纯噪声图像采样代码片段与"噪声/自然图像分别批归一化"的实现，逐条复现四项主张（少数类精度提升、专用BN优于基线、对数据增强水平的稳健性、在平衡数据上同样有效），并补充对照实验考察增益与网络容量、数据增强复杂度之间的关系。

Result: 四项主要主张均得到复现：(i) 纯噪声过采样通过提升少数类精度改善泛化；(ii) 所提BN优于基线；(iii) 该改进在不同数据增强设置下保持稳健；(iv) 纯噪声图像在平衡训练数据上也能提升分类性能。额外实验表明，OPeN 带来的性能提升可能与更大网络或更复杂数据增强带来的提升更呈正交关系。

Conclusion: 原论文结论具有可复现性，复现实验支持其方法有效性；但增益来源的解释需要修正——OPeN 的效果不宜简单归因于与容量/增强的同一机制，二者可能互补，提示应将其视为与模型容量、数据增强互补的正则化手段。

Abstract: We examine the main claims of Zada, Benou, and Irani (2021), which states that in an image classification task with imbalanced training data, (i) using pure noise to augment minority-class images encourages generalization by improving minority-class accuracy. This method is paired with (ii) a new batch normalization layer that normalizes noise images using affine parameters learned from natural images, which improves the model's performance. Moreover, (iii) this improvement is robust to varying levels of data augmentation. Finally, the authors propose that (iv) adding pure noise images can improve classification even on balanced training data. We implemented the training pipeline from the description of the paper using PyTorch and integrated authors' code snippets for sampling pure noise images and batch normalizing noise and natural images separately. We reproduced the main claims that (i) oversampling with pure noise improves generalization by improving the minority-class accuracy, (ii) the proposed batch normalization (BN) method outperforms baselines, (iii) and this improvement is robust across data augmentations. Our results also support that (iv) adding pure noise images can improve classification on balanced training data. However, additional experiments suggest that the performance improvement from OPeN may be more orthogonal to the improvement caused by a bigger network or more complex data augmentation.

</details>


### [138] [IMPACT: Image-Conditioned Motion with Physics-Aware Collision and Trajectory Guidance for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01372)
*Shiwei Yang,Yunlong Yu,Zhao Wang,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IMPACT——一种以物理状态驱动的图像到视频生成框架，通过可学习标定的物理状态生成器与稀疏-稠密互补物理引导，缓解生成视频中物理不一致及"预测-生成"失配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉逼真的视频生成并不保证运动物理一致：生成物体可能出现不合理的碰撞响应或无能量耗散的反弹；即便是物理有效的运动预测，在合成阶段也未必被忠实实现，存在预测与生成之间的偏差。

Method: 核心是可学习标定的Physics State Generator，将连续自由飞行动力学与离散碰撞响应统一为一致的物理状态序列，关联撞击时刻、撞后速度、反弹衰减与最终静止；再从同一物理rollout中导出稀疏轨迹与稠密区域级运动先验作为生成约束——稀疏轨迹约束全局位移，稠密先验引导扩散采样以促进物体区域运动一致、抑制背景变化，共享物理基础协调全局与局部引导。

Result: 在覆盖不同材料组合与视觉环境的100个受控球-平面碰撞场景上，相较代表性通用、物理驱动及运动可控基线，物理合理性均有提升。

Conclusion: 将显式动力学预测与稀疏-稠密物理引导耦合，可有效把物理状态演化转化为生成约束，减少预测-生成失配并提升碰撞场景视频的物理真实性。

Abstract: Visually realistic video generation does not guarantee physically consistent motion. Generated objects may exhibit implausible collision responses or rebounds without energy dissipation, while even physically valid motion predictions may not be faithfully realized during synthesis. We propose \textbf{IMPACT}, a physics-state-driven image-to-video framework that addresses these challenges by coupling explicit dynamics prediction with complementary sparse--dense physical guidance. A learnably calibrated Physics State Generator unifies continuous free-flight dynamics with discrete collision responses, linking impact timing, post-impact velocity, rebound attenuation, and eventual settling within a coherent physical-state sequence. To translate this evolution into effective generative constraints, IMPACT derives sparse trajectories and dense region-level motion priors from the same physical rollout. Sparse trajectories constrain global object displacement, while dense priors guide diffusion sampling to encourage coherent object-region motion and suppress unwanted background changes. Their shared physical basis coordinates global and local motion guidance to reduce prediction--generation mismatch. Experiments on 100 controlled sphere--plane collision scenes spanning different material combinations and visual environments demonstrate improved physical plausibility over representative general-purpose, physics-grounded, and motion-controllable baselines. The project repository is available at https://weiweiwiewei.github.io/impact

</details>


### [139] [Vocabulary Aligned Explanations for Computer Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01391)
*Erfan Mirhaji,Nikhil Krishnaswamy,Jill Zarestky,Lisa Mason,Nathaniel Blanchard,Sarath Sreedharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLUENT 是一个事后解释框架，用领域专家撰写的逻辑规则（结合必需、可选与缺失概念）、视觉证据及不确定性估计来解释图像分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅优化视觉模型的准确率，并不能保证模型学习并使用人类可理解、领域特定的概念与规则，因此需要以应用领域术语来生成可解释的分类解释。

Method: 提出 FLUENT 事后框架：对专家编写的规则（联合必需、替代、缺失概念）进行排序；将预测标签与概念状态同经验估计的似然及概念先验结合以处理预测误差；对每条规则在其涉及概念的所有满足赋值上计算支持度并归一化为相对置信度；最终通过遮挡式高亮展示查询图像中的正向概念，并在检索到的参考图像中定位负向概念的示例。

Result: 框架输出经排序的规则解释及显式规则置信度，并给出正向概念的遮挡高亮与负向概念的局部参考示例，帮助不熟悉该领域的终端用户理解概念的实际外观。

Conclusion: FLUENT 将领域知识与模型准确率相结合，以应用领域词汇、可视化概念高亮和明确的规则置信度来解释图像分类。

Abstract: Optimizing vision models for accuracy does not force the model to learn and utilize human-understandable and domain-specific concepts and rules. We propose explaining image classifications through domain-specific concepts, visual evidence, and expert knowledge expressed as logical rules. We introduce FLUENT (Framework for Localized, Uncertainty-Aware Explanations in Normative Terminology), a post-hoc framework that ranks expert-authored rules combining jointly required, alternative, and absent concepts. To account for prediction errors, FLUENT combines predicted labels and concept states with empirically estimated likelihoods and concept priors. It scores each rule by calculating support over satisfying assignments to the concepts mentioned in that rule, then normalizes these scores to express relative confidence among candidate explanations. The selected rule is illustrated through occlusion-based highlights for positive concept mentions in the query image and localized examples in retrieved reference images for negative mentions. These illustrations are intended to show end users unfamiliar with the domain what the concepts named in an explanation look like. FLUENT combines domain knowledge and model accuracy, and delivers visual concept highlights and explicit rule confidence to explain an image in the vocabulary of an application domain.

</details>


### [140] [SpecUltra: A Unified Foundation Model for Heterogeneous Spectral Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01396)
*Weili Kong,Baisen Liu,Xiaojun Bi,Jiaming Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpecUltra：将异构光谱影像视为连续光谱的离散采样，用3D卷积tokenization+波长感知3D RoPE构建统一坐标系的谱-空基础模型，配MultiHyper-200K预训练与Spectral Kernel Transformation跨越HSI/MSI粒度差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSI与MSI在波段数、波长与光谱分辨率上差异巨大，难以统一建模与跨传感器迁移；从成像物理看，二者不过是同一连续光谱在不同采样过程下的离散测量，作者据此寻求以"连续光谱采样"为统一视角的基础模型。

Method: 1) 3D卷积tokenization + 波长感知3D RoPE，编码相对波长与空间关系，在共享坐标系中实现细粒度谱-空建模；2) 构建大规模多传感器HSI数据集MultiHyper-200K；3) 采用辅助token掩码图像建模(MIM)预训练：level token指示辐射产品类型，register tokens吸收传感器与采集相关变异；4) Spectral Kernel Transformation (SKT)：由HSI预训练的细粒度卷积核切片构造MSI嵌入核，在参数空间迁移知识。

Result: 在HSI与MSI的分类与分割基准上，跨不同传感器与下游任务取得SOTA或具竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 以连续光谱离散采样为统一建模范式可行，波长感知结构与核级参数迁移能有效桥接HSI/MSI的谱粒度鸿沟，实现异构光谱影像的单一基础模型。

Abstract: Spectral imagery, including hyperspectral image (HSI) and multispectral image (MSI), exhibits substantial heterogeneity in band numbers, wavelengths, spectral resolutions, hindering unified modeling and cross-sensor transfer. From the perspective of imaging physics, these heterogeneous observations can be regarded as discrete measurements of an underlying continuous spectrum acquired through different sampling processes. Motivated by this perspective, we propose SpecUltra, a unified foundation model for heterogeneous spectral images. Specifically, SpecUltra employs 3D convolutional tokenization and wavelength-aware 3D RoPE to encode relative wavelength and spatial relationships, enabling fine-grained spatial-spectral modeling within a shared coordinate system. We further construct MultiHyper-200K, a large-scale multi-sensor HSI dataset, and pretrain SpecUltra using auxiliary-token-assisted mask image modeling (MIM), where a level token indicates the radiometric product type and register tokens absorb sensor- and acquisition-dependent variations. To bridge the spectral-granularity gap between HSI and MSI, we introduce Spectral Kernel Transformation (SKT), which transfers pretrained knowledge in parameter space by constructing MSI embedding kernels from fine-grained HSI-pretrained kernel slices. This design preserves pretrained spectral priors while maintaining compatibility with the pretrained backbone. Experiments on HSI and MSI classification and segmentation benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art or competitive performance across disparate sensors and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [141] [RadPose: ISAC-based RF 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01400)
*Geo Jie Zhou,Kunzhe Song,Ruxin Lin,Huacheng Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RadPose 提出一种基于单静态全双工 ISAC 的学习框架，使毫米波通信设备可直接从标准 OFDM 通信信号的 CIR 中提取三维人体姿态点云并完成 3D 人体姿态估计，无需专用雷达硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机与 LiDAR 在逆光/遮挡条件下的人体姿态估计仍具挑战；毫米波雷达虽对光照与遮挡鲁棒，但依赖专用硬件与授权频谱，可扩展性受限。作者希望复用现有毫米波通信设备实现感知功能。

Method: 利用单静态全双工操作，从标准 OFDM 通信信号中提取信道冲激响应（CIR），生成三维无线电点云而无需额外传感硬件；针对 RF 观测稀疏、噪声大的问题，设计跨帧 Transformer 姿态推理网络，包含语义引导的空间注意力与目标驱动的时序注意力以进行鲁棒时空聚合；并引入基于图卷积的关节感知逆运动学细化模块，约束骨架拓扑、骨长一致性与解剖合理性。

Result: 大量实验表明 RadPose 与现有基于雷达的先进方法相比达到有竞争力的精度，并在未见场景与遮挡场景中保持鲁棒性能；数据集与源码已公开。

Conclusion: 验证了利用商用毫米波通信信号实现 3D 人体姿态估计的可行性，为免专用硬件的 ISAC 感知提供了一种可扩展方案。

Abstract: Human pose estimation under adverse lighting and occlusion conditions remains challenging for camera- and LiDAR-based systems. Although mmWave radars provide robustness to illumination and occlusion, they rely on dedicated hardware and licensed spectrum, limiting scalability. In this paper, we present RadPose, a learning-based monostatic integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) framework that enables mmWave communication devices to perform 3D human pose estimation. Unlike specialized radars, RadPose leverages monostatic full-duplex operation to extract channel impulse responses (CIRs) directly from standard OFDM communication signals, generating 3D radio point clouds without additional sensing hardware. To address the sparsity and noise of RF observations, RadPose employs a cross-frame transformer-based pose inference network with semantic-guided spatial attention and target-driven temporal attention for robust spatio-temporal aggregation. It further adopts a joint-aware inverse kinematics refinement module based on graph convolution to enforce skeletal topology, bone-length consistency, and anatomical plausibility. Extensive experiments demonstrate RadPose achieves competitive accuracy compared to state-of-the-art radar-based approaches while maintaining robust performance in unseen and occluded scenarios. The dataset and source code are available at https://github.com/Geo-Chou/RadPose.

</details>


### [142] [ContactCut: A Partition-Aware Contact Selection Framework for False-Merge Correction in Connectomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01457)
*Byunghyun Ko,Won-Ki Jeong,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将局部错误合并修正建模为“3D原生分区感知的contact选择”问题，提出CC-Auto（学习式单contact选择）与CC-Search（有界成对contact搜索+学习式排序），在MICrONS与FlyWire上显著提升failure-aware 3D分区mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接组学中局部false-merge修正需要把已识别的错误转化为可执行的3D原生分区（native 3D partition），但中间修正预测或带种子的图求解器本身都无法决定应使用哪些原生source-sink约束来界定该分区，因此需要显式解决约束选择问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为3D-native partition-aware contact selection：依据候选contact所诱导的完整分区进行选择。方案含两部分：CC-Auto采用学习的单contact选择；CC-Search进行有界成对contact搜索、生成紧凑候选方案并施加学习式panel ranking。

Result: 在MICrONS与FlyWire的历史双向false-merge修正上，CC-Auto的FA-3D Part. mIoU较最强图方法基线分别提升5.9与3.4点，较最佳学习基线分别提升12.5与10.0点；MICrONS训练的ranker迁移至FlyWire性能衰减可忽略；CC-Search扩大了oracle可达性并优先给出近oracle的候选。

Conclusion: 基于完整分区诱导效果来选择source-sink约束，是连接组学false-merge修正的有效建模范式；学习式排序具备跨数据集可迁移性，搜索机制可提升候选覆盖。

Abstract: Localized false-merge correction in connectomics requires converting an identified error into an executable native 3D partition. Still, neither intermediate correction predictions nor seeded graph solvers alone determine which native source-sink constraints should define that partition. We formulate this problem as a 3D-native partition-aware contact selection problem: selecting candidate contacts based on the complete partitions they induce. Our solution comprises two components: ContactCut (CC)-Auto, which uses learned single-contact selection, and ContactCut (CC)-Search, which enables bounded paired-contact search, generates compact alternatives, and applies learned panel ranking. On historical two-way false-merge corrections from MICrONS and FlyWire, CC-Auto improves failure-aware 3D partition (FA-3D Part.) mIoU by 5.9 and 3.4 points over the strongest graph-based controls and by 12.5 and 10.0 points over the best learned baselines. A MICrONS-trained ranker transfers to FlyWire with negligible degradation. CC-Search expands oracle reachability and prioritizes near-oracle alternatives. The code is available at https://github.com/bentake/ContactCut.

</details>


### [143] [Resolve, Clear, or Defer: Training-Free Active Prototype Triage for Ecological Detection Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01466)
*Anirudh Potlapally,Brijesh Nanda Brunda Yogananda,Jianyang Gu,Maanav Jajodia,Luke Meyers,Wei-Lun Chao,Hari Subramoni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的原型分流（active prototype triage）方法，把人工复核过的检测结果当作可复用证据，用于确认前景类别、剔除背景误报或转交专家复核，从而在高召回动物检测器之后降低复核负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态监测依赖相机陷阱与航拍调查，产生海量图像。高召回动物检测器虽能大规模定位候选动物，但输出包含大量误报、模糊样本及难以识别到物种的样本，仍需专家逐一复核，成本极高。因此核心问题从"找到动物"转向"决定复核哪些样本、如何复用复核结果、以及何时证据不足以自动决策"。

Method: 免训练的主动原型分流框架：以已复核检测作为可复用证据原型，对未复核检测执行三路决策——(1) 解析有支持的前景类，(2) 清除可能的背景误报，(3) 对证据不足样本延迟并转人工。利用不同预训练表征分工：BioCLIP2 提供前景证据、DINOv3 提供背景证据。与置信度阈值化多类 k-NN 及不确定性采样逻辑回归对比，并研究原型选择对证据传播可靠性的影响。

Result: 在相机陷阱与航拍数据集上，前景识别与误报剔除分别受益于不同的预训练表征；双表征组合降低了错误清除前景的比例，同时提升自动前景回收率。对证据不足样本进行延迟处理，避免了强制决策带来的更高错误率。与两类基线相比，原型分流在提升前景回收的同时保持相当或更低的复核负担。

Conclusion: 采集/主动学习不仅是类别发现：即使类别已被表征，原型的选择仍决定证据向其余检测传播的可靠性。当动物定位达到高召回后，关键挑战在于样本选择、证据复用与不确定时的延迟决策。

Abstract: Ecological monitoring increasingly relies on camera traps and aerial surveys that generate large image collections. High-recall animal detectors can localize candidate animals at scale. However, they also produce many detections that still require expert review, including false positives, ambiguous cases, and animals difficult to identify at the species level. Since exhaustive review requires substantial effort, we propose training-free active prototype triage, where reviewed detections become reusable evidence to (1) Resolve supported foreground classes, (2) Clear likely background false positives, or (3) Defer insufficiently supported cases for expert review. Across camera-trap and aerial datasets, foreground recognition and false-positive rejection benefit from different pretrained representations: BioCLIP2 provides foreground evidence while DINOv3 provides background evidence, reducing erroneous foreground clearing while improving automatic foreground recovery. Deferring unsupported cases avoids the higher error rates caused by forced decisions. Compared with confidence-thresholded multiclass k-NN and uncertainty-sampling logistic regression, prototype triage improves foreground recovery while maintaining competitive or lower review burden. Acquisition goes beyond class discovery: even after classes are represented, prototype choice determines how reliably evidence propagates through the remaining detections. Our results suggest that once animal localization reaches high recall, the challenge shifts from finding animals to deciding which examples to review, how to reuse them, and when evidence does not support an automatic decision.

</details>


### [144] [See, Think, Use: Diagnosing Perception and Reasoning Failures in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01467)
*Jiacheng Ding,Yanliang Qi,Cong Guo,Peihua Mai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 See, Think, Use 协议，将视觉语言模型的每个答案归因到感知、推理或对感知的利用三个阶段，以指导开发定位短板并分配测试时计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只评分最终答案，正确答案无论来自真实看图、猜测还是记忆先验都获得同等分数，无法揭示模型在感知、推理还是"利用感知"环节出错，也难以指导改进与计算分配。

Method: 协议无需模型权重，仅对每道题额外提问两次：定位探针要求在实例周围给出边框以度量"看"（seeing）；文本替换探针要求仅凭文字描述作答以度量"思考"（thinking）；"利用"（use）定义为反事实图像对中答案跟随模型自身感知的比率，无需答案标签，因为两图中问题与先验相同。

Result: 在两个模型家族、六个数据集上，有效旋钮依任务族而异；答案通常跟随模型感知，但在与常见先验相矛盾的图像上失效，先要求模型定位可在其边框正确时修复该失败；按阶段度量分配计算接近最佳均匀策略且成本更低，并在旋钮能提升短板阶段时优于同等计算量的随机分配。

Conclusion: 该协议提供了可解释、免权重的细粒度诊断，能定位失败阶段、指导测试时计算分配，并将开源代码、提示与逐题输出。

Abstract: We present See, Think, Use, a protocol that traces each answer of a vision-language model to perception, reasoning or the use of perception. The diagnosis shows a developer which stage to improve and directs test-time compute to the stage that fails. Benchmarks score the final answer, and an answer that rests on the image earns the same credit as a guess or a memorized prior. The protocol asks the model two extra questions per item and needs no access to its weights. A localization probe asks for a box around every instance and measures seeing, and a text-replacement probe answers from a description and measures thinking. Use is the rate at which the answer follows the model's own perception across counterfactual image pairs. The rate needs no answer labels, because the question and its prior are the same on both images. On two model families and six datasets the protocol shows that the knob that helps follows the task family. Answers follow the model's perception except on images that contradict a common prior, and asking the model to localize first repairs this failure when its boxes are right. Allocating compute by the measured stages comes close to the best uniform policy at lower cost and beats a random allocation of the same compute wherever a knob raises the short stage. We will release the code, the prompts and the per-question outputs.

</details>


### [145] [Label-Free Localization and Test-Time Suppression of CLIP's Typographic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01513)
*Bohan Liu,Wenqian Ye,Guangzhi Xiong,Zhenghao He,Sanchit Sinha,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种按注意力头分解的稀疏字典学习（SDL）方法，将CLIP视觉编码器中视觉与文本信息纠缠的机制解释并解耦，通过无标签指标定位携带排版信息的注意力头，并在测试时进行干预，显著提升排版攻击下的鲁棒性且几乎不损失通用感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP视觉编码器在对齐目标下既能识别物体又能读取文字，导致词汇与语义形状编码纠缠，易受排版攻击（typographic attack）影响；但其黑盒特性使该纠缠的机制性解释缺失，现有方法多为监督式或免训练但未从机制层面解耦。

Method: 提出按注意力头分解的稀疏字典学习架构（head-decomposed SDL），设计上把每个注意力头分解为可解释的头特异性隐变量；基于该归纳式解耦，改造Clarity Score并提出归一化Text Attribution Score（均为无标签），用于识别携带排版信息的注意力头；再做机制干预：测试时将已识别词汇头对patch token的注意力重新加权；进一步提出测试时自适应注意力头路由。

Result: 机制干预能有效恢复排版攻击下的物体分类，在5种CLIP变体、4个排版基准上最高提升46.9%，通用图像感知精度损失最多约1%，优于已有监督式与免训练方法；自适应头路由在同等鲁棒性下进一步降低感知性能影响，突破现有鲁棒性—感知权衡的Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 通过头级稀疏字典分解可实现CLIP视觉—文本编码纠缠的机制性解耦与因果验证，测试时注意力重加权与自适应路由是有效且低代价的鲁棒性提升手段，且无需标签与训练。

Abstract: CLIP-based Vision-Language Models have become cornerstones of modern multimodal systems. The cross-modal contrastive objective equips the CLIP vision encoder to both see objects and read text. These two abilities induce an entanglement between the encoding of lexical and semantic shapes, creating a distinctive vulnerability to typographic attacks, in which rendered text overrides an image's semantic content with its literal meaning. The black-box nature of these encoders has prevented a mechanistic interpretation of such entanglement. We introduce a head-decomposed Sparse Dictionary Learning (SDL) architecture that, by design, decomposes individual attention heads into interpretable, head-specific latents. Based on this inductive disentanglement, we re-purpose Clarity Score and propose normalized Text Attribution Score, which are both label-free, to identify attention heads that carry typographic information. As a causal validation of successful circuit separation, we perform a mechanistic intervention that reweighs attention away from the patch tokens in the identified lexical heads at test-time. Experiments show that the intervention effectively recovers object classification under typographic attack and, across five CLIP variants and four typographic benchmarks, achieves up to 46.9% improvement while incurring up to 1% accuracy loss in general image perception, outperforming prior supervised and training-free methods. Expanding on the mechanistic intervention, we propose a test-time adaptive attention head routing to further reduce the impact on performance at matched robustness, pushing beyond the Pareto frontier of current robustness-perception tradeoff.

</details>


### [146] [VLMs Read Aloud: The Gap Between Recognizing and Using Visual Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01517)
*Zhiyun Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出现有视觉-文本压缩的评估基准（"能还原多少文字"）是错的：VLM能逐字转录图像文字却用不上，提出"读用差距"（read-use gap）概念；该差距由"把证据写成词"而非分辨率或模型规模决定，据此提出ALOUD，按用途分配视觉分辨率与语言token，在5x/10x压缩下将冻结Qwen3-VL-8B准确率翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文视觉-文本压缩（把文本渲染成图像）普遍以"VLM能从图中还原多少文字"为设计目标与评价指标。但作者观察到：VLM可以逐字转录图像中的文字，却仍无法据此作答；而把其自身的转录文本再喂回去，效果几乎等同原始文本。说明"识别"与"使用"是两个可分离的能力，现有围绕识别率优化的设计方向存在根本偏差，需重新定义压缩的瓶颈。

Method: (1) 提出并量化"读用差距"：对比OCR还原输入与自身转录输入下的下游问答表现；(2) 分辨率消融：Qwen3-VL-8B识别在15像素/字符饱和，而"使用"需24像素/字符才追上；(3) 规模消融：2B→32B提升识别但不缩小差距；(4) 机制分析：thinking型VLM在思维链中复述证据，其"未见问题"的自我转录可恢复大部分thinking增益；(5) 预注册留出测试，检验差距对8个VLM"自述优于像素"增益的排序预测力；(6) 提出ALOUD：将高视觉分辨率用于"定位+识别证据"，把语言token用于"使用证据"，全程不保留源文本。

Result: 预注册留出实验中，读用差距对8个VLM从自述文本相对像素输入所得增益的排序预测Spearman ρ=0.86，而识别率不具备该预测力。ALOUD在长文档5x与10x压缩下使冻结Qwen3-VL-8B准确率翻倍：5x时27.7%→54.8%，接近原文58.5%，且上下文比压缩图像本身更小；成本低于直接读原文，在同等预算下全面领先所有不保留原文的方法，并优于回填原文的AGAR与VERA；5x时亦优于经训练且保留原文的LensVLM-9B。

Conclusion: 视觉-文本压缩的瓶颈不在于模型能还原多少文字，而在于能使用多少文字；识别与使用对分辨率有不同需求，且该差距无法由模型规模扩大自动弥合，但可通过"把证据转写为语言token"予以闭合。据此，按用途分配视觉分辨率与语言token的ALOUD在无源文本条件下显著优于现有方法，确立了以"可用性"而非"可还原性"为目标的压缩设计范式。

Abstract: Visual-text compression renders long contexts as images and is engineered around how much of the text a vision-language model (VLM) can recover. Yet recovered text is not usable text. VLMs can transcribe the text in an image word for word and still fail to answer from it; given back their own transcript, they answer nearly as well as from the original text. We call this difference the read-use gap. Recognizing and using visual text have different resolution requirements: Qwen3-VL-8B's recognition saturates at 15-pixel characters while use catches up only at 24 pixels, and scaling from 2B to 32B raises recognition without narrowing the gap. Putting the evidence into words closes it: thinking VLMs recite the evidence in their thinking, and their own transcript, written without seeing the question, recovers most of what thinking adds. In a preregistered held-out test, the gap ranks how much eight VLMs gain from their own words over pixels (Spearman $ρ$ = 0.86), which recognition rates do not. We propose ALOUD, which spends visual resolution on finding and recognizing evidence and language tokens on using it. Without keeping the source text, it doubles the accuracy of a frozen Qwen3-VL-8B on long documents compressed 5x and 10x: at 5x, from 27.7% to 54.8%, near the original text's 58.5%, with a context smaller than the compressed pages themselves. For less than the cost of reading the original text, it leads at equal budget every method that does without it, at every level, and beats AGAR and VERA, which paste the original text back; at 5x it also beats LensVLM-9B, which is trained and keeps the original text. Visual-text compression is limited not by how much text a model can recover, but by how much it can use.

</details>


### [147] [AR-RRG: Anatomy-Guided Retrieval-Driven Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01529)
*Xiang Zhang,Shijie Song,Wanqing Zhao,Wei Zhou,Rui Cao,Dekui Wang,Qiyao Hu,Xianlin Peng,Hangzai Luo,Sheng Zhong,Jianping Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解剖引导的检索驱动放射报告生成方法 AR-RRG，含病灶检测网络 ASLDNet 与检索驱动生成模块 RRRG，在三个数据集上超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型仅捕捉全局视觉线索，缺乏解剖区域与诊断文本的细粒度对齐；多数检索增强方法直接检索完整历史报告，引入冗余噪声与严重图文错配，导致临床幻觉与病灶漏检。

Method: AR-RRG 包含两模块：ASLDNet 通过解剖感知空间验证（ASV）与类别自适应动态阈值（CDT）自训练，实现精准病灶定位；RRRG 为生成分支，采用双粒度检索（DR）抑制临床幻觉、邻域共识（NC）机制补全漏检病灶；并行连续视觉感知（PCVP）流将X光片经固定医学编码与重采样转为视觉token；融合多尺度文本与视觉特征后，用LoRA高效微调大语言模型。

Result: 在 MS-CXR、MIMIC-CXR、IU X-Ray 上，于主流评价指标上持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 解剖先验引导的细粒度检索与生成相耦合，可有效提升报告生成的临床一致性与视觉可溯源性，代码将公开。

Abstract: Radiology report generation (RRG) streamlines clinical workflows and alleviates the burden of radiologists' documentation. Standard vision-language models capture global visual cues and lack fine-grained alignment between anatomical regions and diagnostic text. Most retrieval-augmented methods retrieve complete historical reports, introducing redundant noise and severe vision-text mismatches that trigger clinically risky hallucinations and missed lesions. In this paper, we introduce AR-RRG, an anatomy-guided retrieval-driven method for the generation of radiology reports. AR-RRG consists of two core modules: the anatomy-aware self-training lesion detection network (ASLDNet) and the retrieval-driven radiology report generation (RRRG). ASLDNet leverages anatomy-aware spatial verification (ASV) and class-adaptive dynamic thresholding (CDT) via self-training, delivering accurate lesion localization to support subsequent generation. As the generative branch of AR-RRG, RRRG eliminates clinical hallucinations through dual-granularity retrieval (DR) and complements missing lesions with a neighborhood consensus (NC) mechanism. A parallel continuous visual perception (PCVP) stream converts radiographs into visual tokens through fixed medical encoding and resampling. Multi-scale text and visual features are fused, followed by LoRA-efficient large language model tuning to generate clinically consistent, visually grounded radiology reports. Extensive evaluations on MS-CXR, MIMIC-CXR, and IU X-Ray demonstrate that AR-RRG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across mainstream metrics. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [148] [AbacusGS: Gaussian Splatting without Per-Splat Sorting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01534)
*Jiacheng Ding,Yanliang Qi,Cong Guo,Peihua Mai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AbacusGS 将 3DGS 场景的混合顺序写入场景布局：把 splat 按八叉树装箱（每箱≤128），每箱沿写入时选定的轴有序存储，每帧只需对可见箱排序并用一次点积决定箱内正/逆序读取，从而在 CPU 端大幅减少排序开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数 Web/移动端 3DGS 查看器使用硬件光栅化绘制 splat、在 CPU 上按深度排序，排序占据了大部分帧时间，成为主要性能瓶颈。

Method: 1) 将 splat 分组为八叉树箱（每箱最多 128 个）；2) 写入时每箱沿选定轴存储为有序序列，将混合顺序固化到场景布局；3) 渲染时仅对可见箱排序，每箱一次点积判断正/逆序读取；4) 通过短时 order-aware 微调将现有 3DGS 模型转换，保持 3DGS 的 over 算子不变；5) 另提出 17 字节/splat 的紧凑格式与复用同一顺序的 CUDA tile 渲染器。

Result: 在 13 个标准场景上，相比同等微调的 3DGS 模型平均仅损失 0.13 dB PSNR；在 CPU 端排序的 OpenGL 查看器中，相比逐 splat 排序实现 4.5 倍帧率提升。

Conclusion: 通过把排序顺序内嵌到场景存储结构中并以箱级排序替代逐 splat 排序，AbacusGS 在几乎不损画质的前提下显著降低 CPU 排序开销，并兼容紧凑存储格式与 CUDA tile 渲染器；代码将开源。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting sorts every splat by depth in every frame. Viewers that draw splats with the hardware rasterizer and sort them on the CPU, as most web and mobile viewers do, spend most of the frame time in this sort. We present AbacusGS, which stores the blending order of a 3DGS scene in the scene layout. AbacusGS groups the splats into octree boxes of at most 128 splats and stores each box in order along an axis chosen at write time. In each frame, AbacusGS sorts only the visible boxes, and one dot product per box decides whether the box is read forward or backward. A short order-aware fine-tuning converts an existing 3DGS model, and the over operator of 3DGS stays unchanged. On 13 standard scenes, AbacusGS loses 0.13 dB PSNR on average against an equally fine-tuned 3DGS model. In an OpenGL viewer that orders splats on the CPU, AbacusGS renders frames 4.5 times faster than per-splat sorting. We also present a compact format of 17 bytes per splat and a CUDA tile renderer that use the same order. The code will be released.

</details>


### [149] [CUFormer: Modality-Aware CT-Ultrasound Point Cloud Registration for Orthopedic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01536)
*Lixing Tan,Qing Xia,Yuting Guo,Shuai Li,Aimin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CUFormer，一种模态感知的CT-超声骨表面点云配准框架，通过身份嵌入与模态专属适配器建模成像可见性，并对粗/精对应加权，在部分覆盖与近对称骨上显著降低配准误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中超声骨表面点云与术前CT模型配准可实现无辐射骨科导航，但成像物理差异导致表面采样模式不同，跨模态特征匹配困难；骨强反射超声并产生声影，US仅捕获探头侧骨表面，CT点大多无对应，部分覆盖使近对称骨上的对应关系高度歧义。

Method: CUFormer：1) 模态感知Transformer（MAT），以身份嵌入编码输入模态，并在共享几何匹配器内通过模态专属适配器更新特征；2) 显式建模成像可见性，用预测的参考侧可见性对粗配准与精配准阶段的对应关系进行加权。

Result: 在UltraBones100k、UltraBonesHip两个CT-US数据集及CT-RGB-D数据集SpineDepth上均取得最低平均配准误差。UltraBones100k上平均旋转误差4.53°、平移误差3.19 mm、RMSE 2.54 mm，RMSE较该指标下最优基线降低65.8%。

Conclusion: CUFormer能有效将部分骨表面与术前CT对齐，并在多解剖部位、跨模态（CT-US、CT-RGB-D）场景下验证了泛化性与有效性。

Abstract: Registering intraoperative Ultrasound (US) bone-surface point clouds to preoperative Computed Tomography (CT) models can support radiation-free orthopedic navigation. Differences in imaging physics produce distinct surface sampling patterns, complicating cross-modal feature matching. Bone strongly reflects ultrasound and casts an acoustic shadow, so US captures only the probe-facing bone surface and leaves most CT points unmatched. This partial coverage makes correspondences ambiguous on near-symmetric bones. We propose CUFormer, a modality-aware registration framework. We introduce a Modality-Aware Transformer (MAT) that encodes input modality through identity embeddings and updates features through modality-specific adapters within a shared geometric matcher. We model imaging visibility and use predicted reference-side visibility to weight coarse and fine correspondences. CUFormer achieves the lowest mean registration errors among evaluated methods on two CT-US datasets, UltraBones100k and UltraBonesHip, and the CT-RGB-D dataset SpineDepth. On UltraBones100k, it reaches mean rotation error 4.53$^\circ$, translation error 3.19\,mm, and Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE) 2.54\,mm, reducing RMSE by 65.8\% relative to the best-performing baseline by this metric. These results demonstrate CUFormer's effectiveness in aligning partial bone surfaces with preoperative CT across anatomical sites.

</details>


### [150] [MoPort: A View-Aware 2D Interface for Motion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01541)
*Lixing Tan,Qing Xia,Yuting Guo,Shuai Li,Aimin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoPort 是一个视图感知的 2D 接口，使运动语言模型（MoLM）可直接以单目视频检测到的 2D 关节序列作为输入，并通过 Paired View Alignment 与 Camera-Modulated Encoding 缓解维度鸿沟与视角歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoLM 以离散 3D 运动 token 训练，但单目部署时只能获得 2D 关节序列：一是存在 2D→3D 的维度鸿沟，二是 2D 观测随相机视角变化、投影丢失深度导致跨视角歧义。

Method: 提出视图感知 2D 接口 MoPort。Paired View Alignment（PVA）：对同一运动的两个视角给定共享的 3D teacher 目标，并直接约束两视角 latent 的一致性，从而将不同视角的 2D 观测映射到一致语义表示。Camera-Modulated Encoding（CME）：从 2D 序列估计相机外参，并用其调制编码，为 2D latent 注入视角几何信息。

Result: HumanML3D R@1 达 0.5674，KIT-ML 达 0.3286，优于全部对比基线；HumanML3D 上 R@1 由分布内视角 0.5763 仅降至分布外视角 0.5585；在三个下游 MoLM 上验证了可移植性；真实 RGB 视频数据集 Fit3D 上准确率 80.36%。

Conclusion: 以轻量适配接口而非重训练的方式，MoPort 有效弥合 2D 观测与 3D 运动语言模型之间的维度与视角差距，具备跨模型可移植性与真实场景可用性。

Abstract: Motion language models (MoLMs) encode 3D human motion as discrete tokens for language-based understanding. Deploying them on monocular video raises two problems. Pose detection yields 2D joint sequences, leaving a dimensional gap from the 3D motion used in training. Moreover, the 2D observation changes with the camera viewpoint, while the depth lost in projection makes the 2D pose ambiguous across viewpoints. We propose MoPort, a view-aware 2D interface that enables MoLMs to take 2D observations as input. We introduce Paired View Alignment (PVA), which gives two views of the same motion a shared 3D teacher target and directly constrains the consistency of their latents, so that 2D observations from different viewpoints are mapped to a consistent semantic representation. Camera-Modulated Encoding (CME) estimates the camera extrinsics from the 2D sequence and uses them to modulate encoding, providing the 2D latents with geometric information about the viewpoint. MoPort achieves R@1 of 0.5674 on HumanML3D and 0.3286 on KIT-ML, outperforming all evaluated baselines. On HumanML3D, its R@1 decreases only from 0.5763 on in-distribution viewpoints to 0.5585 on out-of-distribution viewpoints. We validate portability on three downstream MoLMs. Furthermore, on the real-world RGB video dataset Fit3D, MoPort achieves 80.36\% accuracy. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [151] [GSExplorer: Interactive World Generation with a Persistent 3D Gaussian World State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01543)
*Sicheng Yu,Liu Liu,Xinjie Wang,Xinjiang Wang,Taojun Ding,Wenhao Hu,Beizhen Zhao,Puxin Yan,Zhizhong Su,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GSExplorer，以持久化3D高斯世界状态为核心的生成-重建框架，实现长程可交互世界生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界生成需同时具备精确局部控制与长 rollout 的场景一致性；现有方法虽有相机/几何条件与记忆机制，但长程探索中目标视图引导不完整、重复场景更新导致误差累积。

Method: 以当前3DGS场景为条件驱动视频生成器提供多模态渲染条件，并通过独立Relative Ray Encoding分支注入相机几何；生成视频反馈至SLAM式重建后端，含不一致性过滤与跨chunk bundle adjustment；另设计复现3DGS外推退化的数据构建流程，产出293K训练对。

Result: 在单步与三步WorldScore评测中总体最优，相机控制与一致性均强，单步外推Camera Control得分99.07；481帧离开-返回rollout展现良好重访一致性。

Conclusion: 维护可靠3D世界状态对持续探索具有重要价值，该框架有效缓解长程不一致累积问题。

Abstract: Interactive world generation requires both accurate local control and stable scene consistency over long rollouts. Existing methods improve local generation through camera or geometry conditioning and extend historical context with memory mechanisms. However, long-horizon exploration remains challenging as target-view guidance becomes incomplete and inconsistencies accumulate across repeated scene updates. We present \textbf{GSExplorer}, a generation--reconstruction framework built around a persistent 3D Gaussian world state. At each step, the current 3DGS scene provides multimodal rendering conditions for a condition-driven video generator, while camera geometry is injected through a separate Relative Ray Encoding branch. Generated videos are then fed back to a SLAM-based reconstruction backend, where inconsistency filtering prevents conflicting content from corrupting the scene and cross-chunk bundle adjustment improves spatial alignment across generation steps. We further design a data-construction pipeline that reproduces realistic 3DGS degradation during scene extrapolation, yielding 293K training pairs of degraded multimodal renderings and real target videos. GSExplorer achieves the best overall performance among the compared methods on both single-step and three-step WorldScore evaluation, with strong camera control and consistency across both settings, including a Camera Control score of 99.07 in single-step extrapolation. On 481-frame leave-and-return rollouts, it also shows strong revisit consistency, demonstrating the value of maintaining a reliable 3D world state for continued exploration.

</details>


### [152] [Band2Net: Frequency-to-Network Transport Distillation for fMRI-Privileged, EEG-Only Session-Condition Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01548)
*Nishi Kanta Paul,Md Shihabul Islam Shovo,Dwarikanath Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Band2Net处理"fMRI为特权信息"的EEG解码问题：将EEG编码为5个频段特异性连接图、fMRI编码为7个规范网络token，通过平衡熵传输求解两组token间的匹配，为每个EEG频段分配独立的fMRI目标，实现仅用EEG的会话条件解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI空间分辨率高但昂贵且需静止扫描，EEG便携但定位差。作者希望把训练期可得、部署期不可得的fMRI作为"特权信息"来增强EEG表征，且二者来自同一批被试、条件匹配但非同步采集。

Method: EEG编码为五个频率特异性连接图；冻结教师模型将fMRI编码为七个典型网络token；在两组token间求解平衡熵最优传输问题，从而为每个EEG频段指定各自的fMRI目标，进行频段特异性蒸馏。

Result: 在PsiConnect裸盖菇素数据集的按被试分组交叉验证中，仅用EEG的会话条件解码达到0.801 ROC-AUC，比仅EEG的学生模型高5.0个AUC点（95% CI 1.8–8.2）。

Conclusion: 主结论成立；但相对单一共享分配方案、KL松弛方案与无标签网络描述子的优势尚不明确。次要比较中频段特异性传输目标优于logit、全局特征、对比学习与池化目标蒸馏，但Holm校正后不显著，证据强度有限。

Abstract: Functional MRI resolves large-scale brain networks but is expensive and stationary, whereas EEG is portable but poorly localised. We treat fMRI as privileged information, available during training and absent at deployment, with EEG and fMRI acquired from the same participants under matched conditions but not simultaneously. Band2Net encodes EEG into five frequency-specific connectivity graphs and fMRI into seven canonical network tokens from a frozen teacher, and solves a balanced entropic transport problem between the two token sets that gives each EEG band its own fMRI target. In subject-grouped cross-validation on the PsiConnect psilocybin data set, Band2Net reaches 0.801 ROC-AUC for EEG-only session-condition decoding, 5.0 AUC points (95% CI 1.8-8.2) above an EEG-only student. Although its margins over a single shared plan, a KL-relaxed plan and label-free network descriptors remain unresolved, secondary comparisons favour band-specific transported targets over logit, global-feature, contrastive and pooled-target distillation, though not after Holm correction.

</details>


### [153] [TopoDPO: Dense Preference Alignment for Structure-Sensitive Aerial Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01551)
*Huaiyuan Peng,Jisheng Dang,Wencan Zhang,Jingwen Zhao,Hong Peng,Wenjun Ma,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出拓扑感知的稠密偏好对齐框架TopoDPO，解决多模态分割中掩码级稠密偏好信号与图像级单一权重的粒度不匹配问题，在航拍指代分割的结构压力集上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态分割的偏好优化存在粒度不匹配：稠密掩码包含空间异质的偏好信号，而图像级DPO目标对所有空间margin施加同一个非线性权重，难以对局部结构错误（如连通分量数目错误）进行针对性纠正，尤其不利于对拓扑结构敏感的高分辨率航拍指代分割任务。

Method: 1) Topo-Prior：基于连通分量差异与macro-corner召回筛选信息量高的偏好对；2) 稠密目标：在空间聚合前对每个局部margin分别施加偏好惩罚，并以冻结的监督模型作为参考进行参考相对加权；3) 构建Topo-RS-Hard结构压力测试集（4590个实例）；4) 设置匹配监督对照与完整因子分析，分离拓扑感知配对挖掘、局部化纠正与稠密参考相对权重三者各自贡献；5) 采用成对图像簇bootstrap区间进行显著性检验。

Result: Topo-RS-Hard上IoU由图像级DPO的50.91%提升至51.24%，Betti-0误差由1.34降至0.90；相对图像级DPO的成对图像簇bootstrap区间在全部五项报告指标上均不含零，提升显著。在全量39,244条验证记录的无过滤分层分析中，预对齐连通分量计数误差为中等或严重的样本上结构增益最明显。

Conclusion: 稠密、拓扑感知、参考相对的偏好优化能够有效弥补图像级DPO在结构敏感分割上的粒度错配，其增益可归因于配对挖掘、局部化纠正与稠密加权的独立贡献，并在误差较严重的结构样本上收益最大。

Abstract: Preference optimization for multimodal segmentation faces a granularity mismatch: dense masks contain spatially heterogeneous preference signals, whereas image-level objectives assign one shared nonlinear weight to all spatial margins. We introduce TopoDPO, a topology-aware dense preference alignment framework for structure-sensitive aerial referring segmentation. Topo-Prior mines informative preference pairs using connected-component discrepancy and macro-corner recall, while the dense objective applies the preference penalty to each local margin before spatial aggregation against a frozen supervised reference. On Topo-RS-Hard, a 4590-instance structural stress set, TopoDPO improves intersection over union (IoU) from 50.91% to 51.24% over image-level direct preference optimization (DPO) and reduces Betti-0 error from 1.34 to 0.90; paired image-cluster bootstrap intervals against image-level DPO exclude zero for all five reported metrics. Matched supervised controls and a complete factorial analysis separate the effects of topology-aware pair mining, localized correction, and dense reference-relative preference weighting. An unfiltered stratification over all 39,244 validation records further shows the clearest structural gains on samples with moderate or severe pre-alignment component-count errors. The code is publicly available at: https://github.com/phy709/TopoDPO.

</details>


### [154] [Text-Assisted Event Stream Visual Place Recognition via Multi-Scale Spatiotemporal Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01558)
*Lan Chen,Xingxing Xiong,Xiao Wang,Ziyan Zhang,Zikang Yan,Yuanhao Zheng,Bo Jiang,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MSTOT-EPR，一种多尺度时空最优传输方法，用于事件相机视觉位置识别（VPR），通过跨模态最优传输约束与瓶颈融合实现事件流与文本的语义对齐，并利用多尺度空间与帧间时间最优传输挖掘高质量时空特征，在NYC-Event-VPR和EPRBench上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高动态范围、高时间分辨率和低能耗优势，但事件流存在结构空间信息稀疏的问题。已有工作引入自然语言描述以缓解该问题，但仍未有效解决两个核心挑战：事件流的高效编码，以及事件流与文本的语义对齐。

Method: 在给定事件流与场景文本后，先用视觉编码器与文本编码器分别提取特征表示；引入最优传输约束实现跨模态对齐；设计跨模态瓶颈融合模块进一步融合双模态特征；并构建多尺度空间最优传输模块与帧间时间最优传输模块，挖掘高质量的时空事件特征用于场景识别。

Result: 在NYC-Event-VPR和EPRBench数据集上的大量实验充分验证了所提框架在事件相机VPR任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: MSTOT-EPR通过多尺度时空最优传输与跨模态瓶颈融合，有效应对事件流编码与事件-文本语义对齐两大挑战，为事件相机视觉位置识别提供了有效方案；源代码将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: Benefiting from the high dynamic range, high temporal resolution, and low energy consumption of event cameras, low-altitude economic research leveraging event cameras has become a popular topic. Several existing attempts introduce natural-language descriptions into visual place recognition (VPR) to mitigate the sparsity of structural spatial information inherent to event cameras. Nevertheless, these methods still fail to fully address two core challenges: effectively encoding event streams and semantically aligning event streams with text. In this paper, we propose a multi-scale spatial-temporal optimal transport approach, MSTOT-EPR, to address these challenges. Specifically, given event streams and scene text information, we first adopt a visual encoder and a text encoder to extract respective feature representations. We then apply optimal-transport constraints to achieve cross-modal alignment. We then design a cross-modal bottleneck fusion module to further fuse bimodal features. Furthermore, we develop multi-scale spatial optimal transport and inter-frame temporal optimal transport modules to mine high-quality spatial-temporal event features for scene recognition. Extensive experiments on the NYC-Event-VPR and EPRBench datasets fully validate the effectiveness of our proposed framework for event-based VPR. The source code of this paper will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [155] [Spend the Bits Where the Tiny Objects Are: Rate-Utility Region Coding for Aerial Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01573)
*Yanliang Qi,Jiacheng Ding,Cong Guo,Peihua Mai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出率-效用区域编码(RUO)：按“期望检测效用是否抵偿实测字节代价”决定区域的丢弃/低质量/高质量发送，无需训练模型，共享单一乘子；在VisDrone上比调优阈值ROI省42.4%比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带宽受限的航拍相机需先压缩图像再由远端检测器寻找大量微小目标。基于阈值的ROI编码只发送高置信区域，而微小目标视觉证据有限、得分偏低，易被丢弃，导致漏检。

Method: RUO以率-效用准则替代固定阈值：对每个区域比较期望检测效用与其实测字节代价，从而选择丢弃或低/高质量发送，三个决策由跨图像共享的单一乘子控制；不训练任何模型，复用标准图像编解码器，解码仅需数毫秒。

Result: 在全部1,610张VisDrone-DET test-dev图像上，于二者共有微小目标AP区间内平均，RUO比调优阈值ROI少用42.4%比特；对微小目标所需比特少于AVIF、HEVC及三种已发表学习型编解码器；收发端使用不同检测器时优势仍存在。但全体尺寸AP下RUO比调优ROI多25.4%比特，因其低码率时优先丢弃大区域。

Conclusion: RUO在微小目标检测任务上显著优于阈值式ROI编码与通用/学习型编解码器，且具检测器无关的鲁棒性；其代价是对大目标不友好，适用场景应限于以微小目标为靶的压缩-检测链路。

Abstract: Bandwidth-limited aerial cameras must compress their images before a remote detector can find the many tiny objects in them. Threshold-based region-of-interest (ROI) coding sends only confidently detected regions, and because tiny objects provide limited visual evidence and often score low, it can discard the regions that contain them. We propose rate-utility region coding (RUO), which sends a region when its expected detection utility justifies its measured byte cost, choosing to drop it or to send it at low or high quality with one multiplier shared across images. RUO trains no model, uses a standard image codec and decodes in a few milliseconds. On all 1,610 VisDrone-DET test-dev images, RUO uses 42.4% fewer bits than a tuned threshold-based ROI coder, averaged over their shared tiny-object average precision (AP) range. RUO also needs fewer bits for tiny objects than the AV1 Image File Format (AVIF), High Efficiency Video Coding (HEVC) and three published learned codecs, and its advantage over threshold-based ROI coding persists when the sender and receiver use different detectors. For AP over all object sizes RUO needs 25.4% more bits than the tuned ROI coder, because its allocation drops large regions first at low rates, so it suits tasks that target tiny objects. We plan to release the code.

</details>


### [156] [Recoverability-Aware Evaluation of Diffusion Video Inpainting and Object Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01578)
*Yanliang Qi,Jiacheng Ding,Cong Guo,Peihua Mai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"可恢复性分层评估"（recoverability-stratified evaluation）将视频物体移除的补全像素按"是否在他帧可见、距离多远"分层，从而分离"复制"与"生成"两种能力；并据此提出免训练的"证据引导采样"（EGS），将干净视频跟踪到的颜色融入扩散模型预测。实验表明主流扩散移除器在DAVIS上未充分利用可观测内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频修复评测在整块补全区域（whole hole）上打分，而该区域内部分像素在其他帧可见、部分从未可见，整体分数混淆了"复制已观测内容"与"生成新内容"两类能力，无法揭示移除器究竟擅长哪一种，也无法判断扩散模型是否浪费了可用证据。

Method: 1）可恢复性分层评估：在干净视频上运行点跟踪器，为每个补全像素标注其可观测性及观测时间距离，形成分层；以"复制参考"为基准，按层报告保真度与时间稳定性。2）证据引导采样（EGS）：无需训练，将跟踪得到的颜色按证据权重混合进视频扩散移除器的干净潜变量（clean-latent）预测中。

Result: 在DAVIS上，三种扩散移除器相对光流传播的感知优势在内容于数帧外可见的区域缩小（相对E2FGVI-HQ的LPIPS双重差分+0.046至+0.056）；真实配对拍摄数据中两种方法呈相同趋势。其DAVIS hole warping error为ProPainter的2–4倍；沿干净视频轨迹复制可将可恢复区域的LPIPS降低1/4至2/5。EGS在留出DAVIS上、颜色来自干净轨迹时可弥合至复制参考差距的43%与56%；但若颜色追踪自mask输入，预注册验证显示MiniMax-Remover无增益、ROSE上反而变差。

Conclusion: 扩散式视频移除器在DAVIS上未能有效利用已观测内容，可恢复区域的复制能力显著弱于其生成能力；免训练的观测证据复用（EGS）在证据来源为干净视频时有效，但依赖对被遮挡输入的鲁棒跟踪，目前尚不能稳定成立。

Abstract: Video inpainting for object removal is scored over the whole hole, although the background behind the object is visible in other frames of the shot for some hole pixels and in no frame for others. A whole-hole score therefore mixes copying with generation and cannot show which of the two a remover does well. To separate the two, we propose recoverability-stratified evaluation, in which a point tracker run on the clean video labels each hole pixel by whether and how far away it is observed, so that fidelity and temporal stability can be reported per stratum against a copy reference. To reuse the observed content, we also propose evidence-guided sampling (EGS), which blends tracked colors into the clean-latent prediction of a video diffusion remover without training. On DAVIS, the perceptual advantage of three diffusion removers over flow propagation shrinks where the content is visible a few frames away (LPIPS difference-in-differences +0.046 to +0.056 against E2FGVI-HQ), and real paired captures show the same pattern for two of them. Their DAVIS hole warping error is two to four times that of ProPainter, and copying along clean-video tracks lowers their recoverable LPIPS by a quarter to two fifths. EGS closes 43% and 56% of the gap to this copy reference on held-out DAVIS when its colors come from clean-video tracks. With colors tracked on the masked input, a pre-registered confirmation finds no gain on MiniMax-Remover and a loss on ROSE. Diffusion removers thus leave observed content unused on DAVIS, and training-free reuse does not yet hold with tracked evidence.

</details>


### [157] [Accurate automated photo-identification of Near Eastern and European fire salamanders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01601)
*Maayan Yesharim,Hava Goldstein,Gabi Kolodny,Assaf Ben-Levy,Tamar Kis-Papo,Dor Gavish,Rotem Vidan,Nirit Lavie-Alon,Olga Rybak,Yoav Ram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需物种特异性训练的零样本局部特征匹配流水线（SuperPoint+LightGlue），用于两栖动物照片个体识别，在火蝾螈数据集上实现高精度再识别与数据库审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 照片识别是非侵入式捕获-重捕监测的基础，但参考库扩大后人工识别缓慢且易错；同时现有方法通常依赖物种特异性训练数据，难以跨物种迁移与规模化。

Method: 针对近东火蝾螈，构建基于零样本局部特征匹配（SuperPoint 关键点提取、LightGlue 匹配）的流水线：先分离黄色斑点图案，以高置信关键点匹配数量作为图像相似度（并校正多斑点个体产生虚假匹配的混杂），再通过两层聚类在全库中分配身份。数据为以色列北部海法与 Tel Dan 的 2,440 张野外照片。在留出个体上评估排序与聚类性能，并在欧洲火蝾螈两个数据集上零改动测试泛化性，与五个成熟软件及人工目检比较。

Result: 流水线辅助专家复核纠正了误识别，使身份数量减少近四分之一。留出个体上，正确个体排名第一的查询占 95.2%，身份恢复 ARI 为 0.958。零改动迁移至欧洲火蝾螈两个数据集，top-1 准确率达 95.1% 与 91.7%，在其中之一识别出 93.7% 的重捕，而五个成熟程序为 38%–77%，人工目检为 93.9%。

Conclusion: 零样本局部特征匹配为两栖动物监测提供了无需训练数据、可跨近缘物种迁移、可扩展至上千个体数据库的再识别工具，并可在捕获-重捕分析前审计既有数据库。

Abstract: Photo-identification underpins non-invasive capture-recapture monitoring of amphibians, but manual identification becomes slow and error-prone as reference libraries grow. Focusing on the Near Eastern fire salamander (Salamandra infraimmaculata), we developed a pipeline based on zero-shot local-feature matching (SuperPoint and LightGlue) without species-specific training and evaluated it on $2{,}440$ field photographs from Haifa and Tel Dan in northern Israel. We isolated the yellow spot pattern, scored image similarity by the number of high-confidence keypoint matches, which reduced a confound whereby many-spotted individuals accumulate spurious matches, and assigned identities across the database by two-tier clustering. Pipeline-assisted expert review corrected misidentifications, reducing the number of identities by almost a quarter. On held-out individuals, the pipeline ranked the correct individual first for $95.2\%$ of queries and recovered identities with an ARI of $0.958$. Without modification, it reached $95.1\%$ and $91.7\%$ top-1 accuracy on two datasets of the European fire salamander (Salamandra salamandra) and recognised $93.7\%$ of recaptures in one of them, compared with $38$-$77\%$ for five established programs and $93.9\%$ for visual inspection. Zero-shot local-feature matching thus offers amphibian monitoring a re-identification tool that needs no training data, transfers between related species, scales to databases of over a thousand individuals, and can audit existing databases before capture-recapture analysis.

</details>


### [158] [MedNet and U$^2$-ViT: A Nested Vision Transformer Diffusion Framework for Diverse Medical Images Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01605)
*Lakshmikar R. Polamreddy,Jialu Li,Youshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出大规模多模态医学文生图数据集MedNet（61种疾病、160类，涵盖人类与动物）及嵌套U-Net结构ViT的扩散生成架构U²-ViT，取得最优FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像领域缺乏多样、高质量数据：现有数据集存在类别不平衡、疾病覆盖有限、成像条件变化不足等问题，制约了鲁棒且可泛化的AI诊断与治疗规划模型的发展。

Method: 构建MedNet数据集，图像取自可信公开仓库，保证多样性、质量与伦理合规；提出U²-ViT，将Vision Transformer以嵌套U-Net结构组织的扩散式文生图生成架构，用于生成多样且高保真的医学图像。

Result: 大量实验表明，相较当前最先进模型，U²-ViT取得最佳的FID分数。

Conclusion: MedNet与U²-ViT为缓解医学图像数据稀缺、提升生成质量与多样性提供了可行方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: The scarcity of diverse, high-quality medical images remains a significant bottleneck in advancing AI-driven medical diagnostics and treatment planning. Traditional datasets often suffer from class imbalances, limited disease coverage, and insufficient variations in imaging conditions - challenges that hinder the development of robust, generalizable models. To address this, we introduce MedNet, a large-scale, multi-modal text-to-image dataset spanning 61 disease categories with 160 classes, covering both humans and animals. We built this with images from trusted public repositories, ensuring data diversity, quality, and ethical compliance. Additionally, we propose U$^2$-ViT, a novel diffusion-based text-to-image generation architecture arranging Vision Transformers (ViTs) in a nested U-Net structure to enhance the generation of diverse, and high-fidelity medical images. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that our model achieves the best Fr'echet Inception Distance (FID) score compared to state-of-the-art models. The source code is available at https://github.com/LakshmikarPolamreddy/MedNet-U2-ViT.

</details>


### [159] [NatureFusion: Rethinking Infrared and Visible Image Fusion as Natural Complementary Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01607)
*Jianfeng Ding,Linfeng Tang,Hao Zhang,Han Xu,Xunpeng Yi,Yibing Zhang,Meiqi Gong,Zhongyuan Wang,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NatureFusion将红外与可见光图像融合重新定义为“自然互补增强”，利用生成式基础模型作教师提供监督，通过轻量融合适配器以条件流匹配微调预训练生成模型，并引入检测器监督的任务适配器增强红外的互补贡献，生成视觉自然且利于下游任务的融合图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合方法以“源保真”为目标，将物理属性不同的两种模态直接叠加，导致红外显著目标表现为贴附在场景上的热辐射亮斑，遮蔽目标原本颜色与纹理，融合图像显得不自然，且偏离可见光预训练视觉模型所学到的数据分布。

Method: (1) 将融合任务重构为自然互补增强，利用跨模态互补线索从低质可见光与红外图像对推断清晰、自然的可见光风格图像；(2) 针对缺乏真值，采用生成式基础模型作为教师直接生成视觉自然的融合图像用于监督；(3) 为在降低计算开销的同时充分蒸馏教师生成能力，通过轻量融合适配器以条件流匹配微调预训练生成模型；(4) 冻结融合适配器，引入检测器监督的任务适配器以增强红外模态的互补贡献。

Result: 与7种多模态融合方法和7种单模态增强方法的广泛对比表明，NatureFusion生成的融合图像视觉自然、感知质量最佳；在可见光预训练的检测与分割模型上，其融合图像的精度显著高于所有对比方法。

Conclusion: 以自然互补增强替代源保真作为融合目标，可在保证视觉自然性的同时提升下游检测与分割性能，验证了生成式基础模型蒸馏加轻量适配器这一范式的有效性。

Abstract: In the absence of ground truth, most existing infrared and visible image fusion methods pursue fidelity to the source images and directly superimpose two physically distinct modalities. As a result, salient infrared targets appear as bright patches of thermal radiation pasted onto the scene. These patches obscure the original color and texture of the targets, so the fused images look unnatural to human observers and deviate from the data distribution learned by vision models pretrained on visible images. Therefore, we redefine infrared and visible image fusion as natural complementary enhancement rather than source fidelity, and propose NatureFusion, which exploits cross-modal complementary cues to infer a clear and natural visible-style image from low-quality visible and infrared image pairs. Specifically, to address the lack of ground truth, we employ a generative foundation model as the teacher to directly generate visually natural fused images for supervision. Furthermore, to fully distill the teacher's generative capability while reducing computational cost, we fine-tune a pretrained generative model with conditional flow matching through a light-weight fusion adapter. Last but not least, to further improve downstream performance without sacrificing natural appearance, we freeze the fusion adapter and introduce a detector-supervised task adapter that strengthens the complementary contribution of infrared. Extensive comparisons with seven multi-modal fusion and seven single-modality enhancement methods demonstrate that NatureFusion generates visually natural fused images with the best perceptual quality. Detection and segmentation models pretrained on visible images achieve substantially higher accuracy on our fused images than on those of any competing method.

</details>


### [160] [Special and General Computational Aesthetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01626)
*Ganqing Luo,Chuan Li,Liaoruxing Zhang,Xin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文主张将计算美学划分为两个层次：狭义计算美学（SCA，聚焦审美测量与审美过程建模）与广义计算美学（GCA，额外纳入受计算审美模型引导的"审美生成"），并据此厘清其与AIGC、AI艺术、计算创造力等邻近概念的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算美学横跨计算机科学、人工智能与美学等学科，随AI发展已与计算艺术、AI艺术、AI美学、AIGC、计算创造力、情感计算、神经美学等形成包含、交叉或支撑关系。若不区分研究范围、目标与方法，该领域易被窄化为图像美学质量评估，或被扩张为AI介入艺术的统称，造成概念边界模糊。作者因此需要明确其内部结构与外延边界。

Method: 概念分析与框架构建：一方面剖析计算美学的内部结构及与邻近概念的关系，提出两级范围划分——狭义计算美学（SCA）以审美测量与审美过程建模为核心，前者对应"审美感知"（计算地识别与表征审美属性、预测人类审美评价），后者对应"审美理解"（计算地描述、检验与解释人类审美感知与判断的形成）；广义计算美学（GCA）在核心之上扩展出"审美生成"，即由计算审美模型引导的艺术生成。另一方面强调部分计算美学任务可采用非AI方法，并将使用AI算法与模型的相关研究界定为"AI美学"；同时指出"审美生成"与AIGC、AI艺术、计算创造力存在交叉，但仅当计算审美模型实质性地约束、引导或优化作品生成时才归属GCA。此外借鉴丰鹏关于美、审美经验与艺术可计算性的论述作为理论参照。

Result: 论文给出了一套层次化的界定方案：SCA包含"审美感知"与"审美理解"两个子域，GCA在此基础上加入"审美生成"；"AI美学"被界定为在该范围内使用AI算法与模型的研究，可覆盖狭义核心与广义扩展；"审美生成"与AIGC、AI艺术、计算创造力的交集以"计算审美模型是否实质性参与生成约束"为判据。该框架为计算美学与相关概念之间提供了可操作的边界。

Conclusion: 计算美学不宜被简化为图像美学质量评估，也不应泛化为AI介入艺术的伞形术语；采用SCA/GCA两级范围、并以AI方法为标准区分"AI美学"、以模型实质性约束为标准界定"审美生成"，可有效澄清计算美学的学科内涵与外部关系，并为后续研究提供统一的概念基础。

Abstract: Computational aesthetics is a field at the intersection of computer science, artificial intelligence, aesthetics, and other disciplines. As AI has developed, the field has formed relations of inclusion, intersection, or support with computational art, AI art, AI aesthetics, AI-generated content (AIGC), computational creativity, affective computing, and neuroaesthetics. Without distinguishing scope, objectives, and methods, it may be narrowed to image aesthetic quality assessment or expanded into an umbrella term for AI's involvement in art.
  We analyze its internal structure and its relations with neighboring concepts, and propose a two-level scope: special computational aesthetics (SCA) and general computational aesthetics (GCA). SCA centers on aesthetic measurement and aesthetic process modeling. The former primarily corresponds to "Aesthetic Perception": computationally identifying and representing aesthetic attributes and predicting human aesthetic evaluations; the latter primarily to "Aesthetic Understanding": computationally describing, testing, and explaining how human aesthetic perception and judgment are formed. GCA extends this core to include "Aesthetic Generation," namely artistic generation guided by computational aesthetic models. Some computational aesthetic tasks can also use non-AI methods. Research in this scope using AI algorithms and models we define as AI aesthetics, which may involve the special core and the general extension. "Aesthetic Generation" intersects with AIGC, AI art, and computational creativity, but belongs to the general extension defined here only when computational aesthetic models substantively constrain, guide, or optimize artwork generation. We also draw on Feng Peng's discussion of the computability of beauty, aesthetic experience, and art as a theoretical reference for clarifying boundaries between computational aesthetics and related concepts.

</details>


### [161] [EgoMo3R: Joint Egocentric Motion and Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01627)
*Chaitanya Patel,Yusuke Kato,Rin Takahira,Kazuki Kozuka,Juan Carlos Niebles,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoMo3R 提出首个从日常单目第一视角视频中联合重建三维人体运动与场景几何的一阶优化框架，摆脱对惯性传感器轨迹的依赖，在 EgoExo4D 与 Nymeria 上优于专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角视频中的整体人体运动理解与环境几何重建本质耦合，但现有方法将它们分开处理：运动重建方法通常假设已有来自专用惯性传感器的度量、重力对齐相机轨迹，而从单目第一视角视频恢复此类轨迹因头部快速运动与运动模糊而极具挑战。

Method: 构建联合优化框架，融合低层视觉几何基元（单目深度、光流、点轨迹）与基于扩散的人体运动先验，采用一阶优化；并提出交替优化方案形成协同反馈闭环：场景重建为运动先验提供物理锚定与轨迹条件，运动先验反过来正则化相机轨迹并将场景锚定到度量、重力对齐的世界坐标系。

Result: 在 EgoExo4D 与 Nymeria 的高动态序列上评测，EgoMo3R 在三维场景重建与第一视角运动估计两项任务上均超过专用基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模运动先验与场景几何的交替优化，可在无外部惯性传感器的条件下实现度量、重力对齐的第一视角人体运动与场景重构，验证了任务耦合建模的有效性。

Abstract: Understanding holistic 3D human motion and the surrounding environment from egocentric video is fundamental for applications in AR/VR and robotics. Although these tasks are inherently coupled, existing methods treat them separately. In particular, egocentric motion reconstruction methods typically assume access to metric, gravity-aligned camera trajectories from specialized inertial sensors. Recovering such trajectories from monocular egocentric video remains a significant challenge due to rapid head movements and motion blur. We present EgoMo3R, a joint optimization framework that simultaneously reconstructs 3D human motion and scene geometry from casual egocentric video. It leverages the robustness of low-level visuo-geometric primitives, including monocular depth, optical flow, and point tracks, integrated with a diffusion-based human motion prior via first-order optimization. We propose an alternating optimization scheme that establishes a synergistic feedback loop: scene reconstruction provides physical grounding and trajectory conditioning for the motion prior, while the motion prior regularizes the camera trajectory and anchors the scene in a metric, gravity-aligned world frame. We evaluate EgoMo3R on the highly dynamic sequences of the EgoExo4D and Nymeria datasets and show that it outperforms specialized baselines in both 3D scene reconstruction and egocentric motion estimation.

</details>


### [162] [Zero-shot evaluation of open vision-language models as System 1 decision models on radiology tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01631)
*Bradley J Erickson,Sara Salehi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究比较两种多模态System 1决策模型（Clef 27B、d1 3B）与传统LLM（Qwen3.8-27B）在七项放射学任务上的表现，以AUROC为主要终点，且均不做微调。结论：System 1模型能以高精度、低算力完成重要放射学任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM在放射学任务上计算成本高，需评估System 1式决策模型在准确性、速度与能耗上能否替代或接近LLM的常规使用方式。

Method: 对比Clef、Qwen3.8-27B与d1三模型，在7项任务上测试（3项文本解读、4项图像征象检出）：CT-RATE胸部CT报告与3D体积、PadChest胸片报告、AccessGUDID器械MR安全性、RANZCR CLiP导管/管线位置、MR-ART运动伪影严重度、BraTS 2021序列识别；以AUROC为主要终点，无微调。

Result: 全部请求无失败；报告文本征象提取近乎完美（AUROC>0.95）。Clef在CT-RATE体积上优于Qwen主干、在RANZCR上较低，两者在BraTS、GUDID、PadChest无差异，CT-RATE报告仅差0.002；BraTS上Clef正确识别90/100例FLAIR而Qwen从未选择。d1在7项任务中6项低于Clef。所有模型在运动伪影严重度任务上表现均差。多问题/输入任务中Clef较Qwen更快；d1每请求能耗比Clef低3–9倍。

Conclusion: System 1模型可在放射学关键任务上实现高精度并降低算力成本；LLM被限制为单一答案token时，其成本与性能接近System 1模型，除非需回答多个问题；3B System 1模型在报告文本上接近27B模型，但图像任务上不及；运动伪影严重度对所有模型仍是难点。

Abstract: Purpose: We compared two multimodal System 1 decision models, Clef 27B and d1 3B, with a traditional LLM (Qwen 3.8-27B) on seven radiology tasks covering text, radiographs, CT and MRI. Methods: Three models were compared: Clef, Qwen3.8-27B and d1. Three tasks were interpretation of text and 4 were detection of finding in images. The tasks were: 1. finding presence of finding in CT-RATE chest CT reports; 2. Presence of finding in PadChest chest radiograph reports; 3. the same 18 findings on CT-RATE chest CT but 3D volumes; 4. MR safety status of devices from AccessGUDID device records; 5. line and tube position on RANZCR CLiP chest radiographs; 6. motion-artifact severity on MR-ART T1-weighted brain MRI and 7. sequence identification on BraTS 2021 brain MRI. The primary endpoint was AUROC. There was no fine-tuning of the models on the tasks. Results: All models completed all tests without a failed request. All retrieved findings from reports almost perfectly (AUROC >0.95). Clef ranked higher than its Qwen backbone on CT-RATE volumes and RANZCR lower on MR-ART. The two did not differ on BraTS, GUDID or PadChest, and differed by 0.002 on CT-RATE reports. On BraTS, Clef correctly identified 90 of 100 FLAIR volumes while Qwen never chose it. d1 ranked lower than Clef on six of seven tasks. No model performed well on motion severity. Clef was faster per request than Qwen on tasks with many questions per input. d1 used 3 to 9 times less energy per request than Clef. Conclusions: System 1 models can perform important tasks in radiology with high accuracy, at lower compute cost than LLMs used in the usual way. When an LLM is constrained to a single answer token, its cost and performance is similar to System 1 models unless multiple questions are asked. A 3B System 1 model was close to the 27B models on report text but not on images. No model performed well on motion severity.

</details>


### [163] [Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Urdu Nastaliq Handwriting Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01635)
*Haseeb Yaqoob,Fatima Azfar,Emaan Arif Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在UNHD乌尔都语Nastaliq手写数据集上零样本基准测试六种系统（Tesseract、印刷体专用识别器UTRNet-Large、两个开源VLM、两个商用VLM），并开展OCR后处理纠错研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nastaliq草书无固定基线与清晰字符边界，手写识别困难；同时需厘清VLM的"阅读能力"与"纠错能力"各自的贡献。

Method: Part A：六系统在2465张单行图像上零样本评测，四个VLM使用统一固定提示（可配置者温度设为0，Claude Sonnet 5除外）。Part B：VLM结合源图像与含噪文本对另一OCR系统输出进行纠错，并与各模型自身识别能力分离评估。

Result: 两个商用VLM错误率低于印刷体识别器与开源VLM；归纳出三种失败模式，其中商用模型会对极难辨认图像明确拒答而非猜测。纠错收益对Tesseract输出仅0.7%–7.4%；仅两个商用纠错器处理UTRNet文本时达17.9%与21.9%。

Conclusion: 零样本条件下商用VLM表现领先，但未与手写专用识别器比较，故刻画的是零样本行为而非SOTA；纠错收益取决于所给文本的可读性，较弱纠错器甚至会使较清晰文本变差。

Abstract: Handwritten Urdu is hard to recognize because the cursive Nastaliq script has no fixed baseline and no clear character boundaries. We benchmark six systems on the Urdu Nastaliq Handwritten Dataset (UNHD): Tesseract (default configuration), a script-specific recognizer trained on printed Urdu (UTRNet-Large), two open-source vision-language models (VLMs), and two commercial VLMs. All six systems are evaluated without fine-tuning on 2,465 single-line images (Part A), and the four VLMs receive the same fixed prompt (decoding uses temperature 0 where configurable, except for Claude Sonnet 5, whose decoding cannot be configured). Both commercial VLMs reach lower error than the printed-Urdu recognizer and the open-source VLMs. We do not compare against handwriting-trained recognizers, which would likely do better in-domain; we characterize zero-shot behavior, not state of the art. We also describe three distinct failure modes among the ranked systems. In one of them, a commercial model explicitly declines to transcribe a few very hard-to-read images instead of guessing. In a second study (Part B), VLMs correct the output of a separate OCR system using both the source image and the noisy text. Once separated from each model's own reading ability, the benefit is small on Tesseract's output (0.7% to 7.4%) and larger (17.9% and 21.9%) only for the two commercial correctors given UTRNet's text. It appears to depend on how legible the supplied text already is, and a weaker corrector can make a more legible text worse.

</details>


### [164] [UA-Depth: Uncertainty-Aware Depth Restoration for Material-Agnostic Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01645)
*Rui Cao,Jia-Hui Pan,Yin Zhang,Qiwei Meng,Jiapeng Li,Gang Wang,Runsong Zhu,Wei Chen,Chi-Wing Fu,Jiangliu Wang,Yun-Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UA-Depth：不确定性感知的深度修复框架，迭代联合优化深度与像素级不确定性，并用不确定性门控特征抑制不可靠线索；借助冻结深度基础模型初始化与两阶段MAD加权对数深度尺度归一化提升零样本泛化，在八数据集基准上取得最佳平均排名并改善下游6D位姿估计与抓取合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用RGB-D相机在透明、反光、半透明等非朗伯物体上失效，产生空洞、结构化噪声与深度偏置；现有修复方法仅输出单一深度图，对有噪与可靠区域一视同仁，忽略区域不确定性，且零样本泛化差。

Method: 提出UA-Depth框架：1）迭代协同精化深度与像素级不确定性，并采用不确定性门控特征抑制不可靠线索以获得稳定更新；2）以冻结的深度基础模型作为先验，初始化缺失或不可靠深度；3）采用两阶段MAD加权的对数深度尺度归一化，降低相对深度到度量深度对齐中的离群偏置；4）构建覆盖多场景、多材质、多传感器的八数据集零样本基准。

Result: 在八数据集零样本基准上，UA-Depth在代表性基线中取得最佳平均排名，在透明与反光物体占主导的数据集上提升尤为显著；利用预测的不确定性，在类别级6D位姿估计与6D抓取合成下游任务上均取得改进。

Conclusion: 联合建模深度与像素级不确定性可有效应对非朗伯材质的深度退化，并借助基础模型先验与稳健尺度对齐实现强零样本性能；不确定性本身可作为下游位姿估计与抓取的有效信号。代码、模型与数据将于发表后公开。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation requires accurate metric depth, yet commodity RGB-D cameras often fail on non-Lambertian objects such as transparent, reflective, and translucent materials, producing holes, structured noise, and biased depth. Most restoration methods produce only a single depth map, treating noisy and reliable regions uniformly for downstream tasks while ignoring regional uncertainty. We present UA-Depth, an uncertainty-aware depth restoration framework that iteratively co-refines depth and pixel-wise uncertainty, and uses uncertainty-gated features to suppress unreliable cues for stable updates. To strengthen zero-shot behavior, UA-Depth leverages a frozen depth foundation model as a prior to initialize missing or unreliable depth, and applies two-stage MAD-weighted log-depth scale normalization to reduce outlier bias in relative-to-metric alignment. We introduce an eight-dataset zero-shot benchmark across diverse scenes, materials, and sensors. Across these benchmarks, UA-Depth achieves the best mean rank among representative baselines, with particularly strong gains on datasets dominated by transparent and reflective objects. Beyond depth metrics, we demonstrate downstream improvements in category-level 6D pose estimation and 6D grasp synthesis by exploiting the predicted uncertainty. \emph{Code, models, and data for training and evaluation will be released upon publication.}

</details>


### [165] [dKV Forcing: Separating Generation and Memory Representations through a Differentiable KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01648)
*Zhiqi Li,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 dKV Forcing，通过学习"写入侧"专用记忆表示（可微 KV），使因果视频生成器从未来读取中学习记忆写入，并以单次遍历实现精确记忆梯度，显著提升长视频生成质量并降低训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果视频生成器中，某段的"生成用 KV"与"写入缓存供后续段读取的 KV"存在实质差异，后续段对二者注意力行为不同；若用生成 KV 替换写入 KV 会导致续写质量下降。因此需要专门学习记忆写入表示，而"从未来使用中学习写入"要求梯度穿过缓存，但常用可变缓存在训练中无法提供该梯度。

Method: 1) dKV Forcing：在共享主干上为写入方引入低秩残差，将"记忆写入"与"生成"解耦，并依据其被未来段读取的方式训练；2) 用持久化可微页（persistent differentiable pages）配合局部重算（local rematerialization）替代训练期可变缓存；3) 在单次时间遍历中计算与两遍重建目标完全相同的精确梯度。

Result: 相对 detached-write 训练，单次遍历即获得完整记忆梯度，生成器更新开销仅 7%，常驻内存减少 28%，训练周期缩短 20.8%（相比两遍重建）。在相同目标与相同遍历下对比 SGF：60 s 视频 VBench-Long 美学质量提升 6.6 分，240 s 提升 8.5 分，且主体与背景一致性最高；对残差做 rollout dropout 可保留大部分增益，同时恢复无写入梯度训练时的运动表现。

Conclusion: 记忆写入应与生成表示分离，并可通过可微缓存以低成本（单次遍历、低额外开销）从未来读取中端到端学习；该机制在长时程视频生成的美学质量与一致性上带来显著增益，且对残差做丢弃可在增益与运动动态之间取得平衡。

Abstract: A causal video generator produces two key--value (KV) states for every segment: the KV it uses while denoising that segment, and the KV it writes into the cache for later segments to read. In a pretrained generator the two already differ substantially, later segments attend to them differently, and replacing the written KV with the generation KV degrades the continuation. We propose dKV Forcing (dKV denotes differentiable KV) to learn a dedicated memory representation from its future use: a writer-specific low-rank residual on the shared backbone separates memory writing from generation and is trained through the future reads of what it writes. Learning a write from its future use needs gradients through the cache, which the mutable cache in common use cannot provide. dKV Forcing replaces the training-time cache by persistent differentiable pages with local rematerialization and, in one temporal traversal, computes exactly the gradient that a two-pass reconstruction of the same objective computes. One traversal computes the full memory gradient at $7\%$ generator-update overhead over detached-write training, with $28\%$ less resident memory and $20.8\%$ shorter training cycles than the two-pass reconstruction. Over Self Gradient Forcing (SGF), the same objective trained with the same traversal, writer adaptation raises VBench-Long aesthetic quality by $6.6$ points at 60\,s and $8.5$ points at 240\,s with the highest subject and background consistency, and rollout dropout of the residual keeps most of this gain while restoring the motion of training without write gradients.

</details>


### [166] [GatedVLA with Camera-Based Emergency Braking: AlpaSim E2E Closed Loop Challenge Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01663)
*Kaneyoshi Hiratsuka,Naoki Takada,Makoto Kawano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出GatedVLA端到端自动驾驶策略：以驾驶预训练的Qwen-Drive-1.0-4B视觉语言模型为骨干，接层间流匹配动作专家，输出2.5秒时域的加速度与曲率控制，并在专家交叉注意力上施加软视觉掩码；加入规则式AEB后闭环均分由0.479提升至0.599，有责碰撞场景数由34.0降至11.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向PAI-AV赛道，在保持开环误差不劣化的前提下提升左转等关键场景表现，并降低闭环部署中的碰撞风险。

Method: 驾驶预训练VLM（Qwen-Drive-1.0-4B）+ 层间流匹配动作专家，输出2.5 s加速度/曲率序列；对专家交叉注意力施加软视觉掩码；部署阶段叠加规则式AEB模块。评估：开环误差对比同构基线，闭环150场景×2次rollout，配对bootstrap置信区间与符号检验。

Result: 视觉掩码使左转误差下降、整体开环误差相当；闭环均分0.479→0.517（配对差+0.038，95% CI [-0.018,+0.093]，胜负37/26，p=0.21，不显著）；叠加AEB后均分达0.599（+0.082，CI [+0.023,+0.143]），有责碰撞场景均值34.0→11.0。

Conclusion: 软视觉掩码方向有效但统计证据不足；性能主要增益来自部署期规则式AEB，说明学习策略与安全规则模块的协同是当前实用路径，掩码机制仍需更大样本验证。

Abstract: This report presents GatedVLA, our entry for the Physical AI AV (PAI-AV) track. Our learned vision-language-action (VLA) policy pairs the driving-pretrained Qwen-Drive-1.0-4B vision-language model with a layer-wise flow-matching action expert that outputs a 2.5 s horizon of acceleration and curvature controls, augmented with a soft visual mask applied to the expert's cross-attention. Compared to an identically trained baseline, the masked policy achieves comparable overall open-loop error while reducing error on left turns. In local closed-loop evaluation across the same 150 scenes (two rollouts per scene), the visual mask increases the mean scene score from 0.479 to 0.517 (paired difference +0.038, 95% bootstrap CI [-0.018, +0.093], wins/losses 37/26, sign test p = 0.21). Incorporating a rule-based automatic emergency braking (AEB) module at deployment further increases the mean score to 0.599 (+0.082, CI [+0.023, +0.143]) and reduces the mean number of scenes with at-fault collisions from 34.0 to 11.0.

</details>


### [167] [MMCoT$^3$: Empowering 3D Scene Understanding with 3D-Text Interleaved Multi-Modal Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01676)
*Yongqi Shan,Yifan Wang,Yunzhi Zhuge,Lijun Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MMCoT³——首个面向3D场景理解的3D-文本交错思维链框架，结合歧义感知证据聚合与PointCoT指令微调数据集，在六个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D大语言模型存在两大局限：(i)在复杂3D场景中缺乏grounded的多步推理能力；(ii)以文本为中心的推理范式使文本推理与视觉证据之间缺乏有效交互，导致中间推理难以校验、相似实例难以消歧、易产生幻觉。

Method: 1) 提出3D-文本交错CoT框架，将文本推理步骤与任务相关的3D证据显式交错，使模型在推理全程可回溯场景观测；2) 针对文本提及对应多个候选物体的多实例歧义，设计歧义感知证据聚合机制，用查询引导的交叉注意力融合候选物体token，并将证据与歧义线索共同注入推理过程；3) 构建PointCoT大规模3D专用CoT指令微调数据集（约176.2K条，覆盖captioning、问答、对话与规划任务）。

Result: 在六个基准上实验，性能一致提升并取得SOTA，验证了歧义感知的3D-文本交错推理对3D场景理解的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式交错文本推理与3D视觉证据、并显式建模物体提及的多实例歧义，可有效提升3D大语言模型的多步推理可靠性、消歧能力与抗幻觉能力。

Abstract: Understanding and reasoning over 3D scenes from point clouds is essential for a broad range of AI applications. Recent 3D large language models have shown promising results, yet they still face two fundamental limitations: (i) insufficient capability for grounded multi-step reasoning in complex 3D scenes, and (ii) a text-centric inference paradigm that lacks effective interaction between textual reasoning and visual evidence. As a result, these models often fail to verify intermediate inferences, disambiguate similar object instances, and avoid hallucinations. To address these limitations, we propose MMCoT$^3$, the first 3D-Text Interleaved Chain-of-Thought (CoT) framework for 3D scene understanding. The core idea is to explicitly interleave textual reasoning with task-relevant 3D evidence, enabling the model to consult scene observations throughout the reasoning process. In addition, since a textual mention in 3D scenes may correspond to multiple candidate objects, naive object-mention alignment can inject incorrect evidence and mislead subsequent reasoning. To mitigate this multi-instance ambiguity, we design an ambiguity-aware evidence aggregation mechanism that uses query-guided cross-attention to fuse candidate object tokens and inject both evidence and ambiguity cues into the reasoning process. To better equip the model with multi-step reasoning capability, we further develop PointCoT, a large-scale 3D-specific CoT instruction tuning dataset with approximately 176.2K instances spanning captioning, question answering, dialogue, and planning tasks. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks show that our method consistently improves performance and achieves state-of-the-art results, validating the effectiveness of our proposed ambiguity-aware 3D-text interleaved reasoning for 3D scene understanding.

</details>


### [168] [Leveraging 2D Foundation Priors for Generalized Category Discovery under Test-Time Adaptation in 3D LiDAR Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01682)
*Hanqi Chen,Chaoqi Chen,Tian Ji,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架解决3D LiDAR语义分割中的GCD-TTA问题：第一阶段基于几何密度自适应聚合局部特征并在全局类原型一致性约束下生成可靠伪标签；第二阶段将类别发现建模为最优传输分配，并融合2D视觉基础模型语义特征以缓解特征坍缩。在Synth4D→SemanticKITTI等3个迁移基准、10个划分中9个取得最佳mIoU，最高提升12.21个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶要求3D LiDAR语义分割能适应变化环境并识别新类别，因此测试时自适应下的广义类别发现（GCD-TTA）十分关键。现有工作将GCD与TTA分开处理；而3D点云语义信息有限，域偏移下二者结合易导致特征坍缩。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 依据几何密度变化自适应聚合局部3D特征，在全局类级原型一致性约束下获得可靠伪标签；(2) 将类别发现转化为最优传输分配过程，实现新类别的全局一致分组；同时融合2D视觉基础模型的语义特征与3D点云特征，丰富语义信息并提升特征空间可分性。

Result: 在Synth4D→SemanticKITTI、SynLiDAR→SemanticKITTI、Synth4D→nuScenes上验证有效性；统一评测协议下10个基准划分中9个取得最佳总体mIoU，相较最强复现基线最高提升12.21个mIoU点。

Conclusion: 统一处理GCD与TTA、结合密度自适应特征聚合、原型一致性伪标签、最优传输聚类与2D-3D特征融合，可有效缓解域偏移下的特征坍缩，显著提升3D LiDAR语义分割的广义类别发现与测试时自适应性能。

Abstract: In autonomous driving, 3D LiDAR semantic segmentation is expected to adapt to evolving environments and recognize previously unseen categories, making generalized category discovery under test-time adaptation (GCD-TTA) an important problem.
