However, existing works treat GCD and TTA separately, while the limited semantic information in 3D point clouds makes GCD-TTA under domain shift more prone to feature collapse when combining GCD with TTA. To address this issue, we propose a two-stage framework for GCD-TTA in 3D LiDAR semantic segmentation. Specifically, in the first stage, we adaptively aggregate local 3D features according to geometric density variations and obtain reliable pseudo labels under a global class-wise prototype consistency constraint. In the second stage, we cast category discovery as an optimal transport assignment process for globally consistent grouping of novel categories. Meanwhile, semantic features from 2D vision foundation models are fused with 3D point cloud features to enrich 3D semantic information and promote a more separable feature space. Extensive experiments on Synth4D to SemanticKITTI, SynLiDAR to SemanticKITTI, and Synth4D to nuScenes demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework. Under a unified evaluation protocol, our method achieves the best overall mIoU on 9 out of 10 benchmark splits and improves the overall mIoU by up to 12.21 points over the strongest re-implemented baseline.

</details>


### [169] [GaitFM: A Cross-Modal Foundation Model for Wearable Gait Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01684)
*Yize Cai,Zhiqing Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaitFM 是一个基于 IMU 传感器的跨模态步态评估基础模型，通过从大型视觉模型迁移知识并在大规模无标注 IMU 数据上预训练，提升了步态评估的鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 IMU 的深度学习步态评估模型多在规模小、同质化的数据集上针对特定临床任务开发，受个体差异、佩戴位置与设备配置影响，鲁棒性与真实场景泛化性受限；同时传感器与医学领域存在标注数据稀缺问题。

Method: 构建跨模态基础模型 GaitFM：一方面从大型视觉模型迁移知识，另一方面在约 4,000 名参与者、123 万个步态窗口构成的大规模精选、跨临床与日常场景、多传感器模态与多身体部位的未标注 IMU 数据上进行自监督预训练。

Result: 在 18 项步态相关任务上表现优异，包括 3 项步态参数估计、14 项疾病与步态异常检测、1 项临床结局预测；且能泛化到不同传感器配置与身体部位，涵盖临床常用与日常可穿戴设备的设置。

Conclusion: 验证了迁移视觉知识可有效提升基于传感器的步态评估性能，为解决传感器与医疗领域的数据稀缺问题提供了新路径。

Abstract: Gait assessment supports the diagnosis, monitoring, and prognosis of diverse movement-related conditions, such as neurological and musculoskeletal disorders. Recent advances in deep learning using everyday wearable inertial measurement units (IMUs) offer an accessible and low-cost approach to automated gait assessment. However, IMU measurements vary substantially across individuals, body locations, and device configurations, while existing deep learning models are typically developed using small, homogeneous datasets for specific clinical tasks, limiting their robustness and generalizability in diverse real-world contexts. Here we present GaitFM, a cross-modal foundation model for gait assessment using IMU sensors. GaitFM improves the generalizability of gait assessment by transferring knowledge from large vision models and pretraining on unlabeled IMU sensor data. We pretrain GaitFM on a large curated dataset comprising about 4,000 participants and 1.23 million gait windows across diverse clinical and daily conditions, sensor modalities, and body locations. GaitFM demonstrates strong performance across 18 gait-related tasks, including 3 gait parameter estimation tasks, 14 disease and gait abnormality detection tasks, and 1 clinical outcome prediction task. Furthermore, GaitFM generalizes across different sensor configurations and body locations, including those commonly used in clinical settings and everyday wearable devices. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of transferring visual knowledge in improving sensor-based gait assessment performance, offering a new promising pathway for addressing data scarcity in the sensor and medical domains.

</details>


### [170] [SEALECT: Scene-Conditioned Pose Priors for Plausible Maritime Vessel Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01688)
*Faisal Mohammad,Mohamed Younis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对海事图像中的合成数据增强任务，提出场景条件化目标检索框架SEALECT，将神经相似度、场景条件姿态先验与观测姿态一致性统一到单一得分中，以提升船舶插入的几何合理性，同时保持检索召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事图像存在强几何约束（船体姿态、尺度、场景几何），仅依赖外观相似度的检索会选出物理上不合理的船舶用于插入，导致合成增强样本失真。现有前景搜索类基线未显式建模场景条件下的姿态与尺度先验。

Method: 提出SEALECT检索框架，用统一打分融合三部分：(1)神经网络外观相似度；(2)场景条件姿态先验；(3)观测姿态一致性。构建海事场景-姿态库，并以无参数核方法对姿态与尺度先验建模，使先验随场景连续条件化，无需学习参数。另设一个免训练（training-free）先验模块以低召回代价进一步约束几何合理性。

Result: 在7个海事基准上，训练后的场景条件检索器召回率较最强外观编码器SigLIP 2提升+0.14；免训练先验以可忽略的召回代价将几何约束违反数减少一半以上，并优于GALA-style、DiscoFOS等学习式前景搜索基线。端到端合成（BootPlace放置+DiffHarmony协调）的盲测中，SEALECT在6种方法中排名第一，优于随机选择与GALA-style，与CLIP、DiscoFOS、DINO无显著差异。

Conclusion: 显式引入场景条件的姿态/尺度先验可显著提升合成增强中目标插入的物理合理性，且不牺牲召回；但端到端盲测中与强外观基线仅持平，说明下游合成质量还受协调与放置模块影响，场景先验的收益尚未完全转化为显著感知优势。

Abstract: We address scene-conditioned object retrieval for compositional augmentation in maritime imagery, where strong geometric constraints make appearance-only retrieval produce physically implausible vessel insertions. We propose SEALECT, a retrieval framework that jointly models visual compatibility, vessel pose, scale, and scene geometry through a unified score combining neural similarity, scene-conditioned pose priors, and observed-pose consistency. We construct a maritime scene-pose library and model the pose and scale priors nonparametrically, as a kernel that conditions each prior continuously on the scene with no learned parameters. Across seven maritime benchmarks, our trained scene-conditioned retriever exceeds the strongest appearance encoder (SigLIP 2) in recall (+0.14), and the training-free prior raises geometric plausibility further at negligible recall cost, more than halving the constraint violations of appearance retrieval and outperforming learned foreground-search baselines (GALA-style, DiscoFOS). In a blind study of end-to-end composites (BootPlace placement, DiffHarmony harmonization), SEALECT ranks first among six methods, ahead of random and GALA-style selection and statistically tied with CLIP, DiscoFOS, and DINO.

</details>


### [171] [Restoring Semantic Fidelity for Training-Free High-Resolution Image Generation in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01696)
*Junjie Li,Zengbin Wang,Gaoxiang Li,Muyi Sun,Yixin Zhang,Man Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架解决DiT文本到图像模型超训练分辨率生成时的语义退化问题，通过文本-视觉尺度解耦注意力（TVSDA）与结构-细节引导（SDG）提升结构一致性和细节保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT-based文生图模型直接用于超出训练分辨率时出现严重语义退化（有意义的语义内容消失）；现有免训练高分辨率方法只能缓解表面问题，语义退化本身未解决，导致细粒度细节劣化；且常用结构引导方法缺乏对中间轨迹中结构与细节的建模。

Method: 免训练高分辨率生成框架，包含两个模块：(1) 文本-视觉尺度解耦注意力（TVSDA）：保持文本token的全局感受野，同时使图像token感受野与训练分辨率对齐，从而恢复高分辨率下的语义生成能力；(2) 结构-细节引导（SDG）：在尺度维度和时间维度上显式建模结构信息与精细细节，克服传统低分辨率引导的局限。

Result: 大量实验表明，相比现有基于DiT的免训练高分辨率生成方法，本方法在结构一致性和细节保真度上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示并针对性地缓解了DiT高分辨率生成的语义退化根源，验证了尺度解耦注意力与多维结构细节引导的有效性，为免训练高分辨率文生图提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformer (DiT)-based text-to-image models have recently achieved remarkable success in image synthesis. However, when directly applied beyond their training resolution, these models often suffer from severe semantic degradation, where meaningful semantic content disappears. Although existing training-free high-resolution generation methods can mitigate this issue, the inherent semantic degradation at high resolutions remains largely unresolved, leading to degraded fine-grained details. Furthermore, widely used structural guidance methods lack modeling of the structure and details in the intermediate trajectory, which degrades the quality of the generated results. In this paper, we propose a training-free framework for high-resolution image generation to address these challenges. First, we propose a text-visual scale-decoupled attention (TVSDA) module, which restores the model's ability to generate semantically meaningful content at high resolutions by preserving the global receptive field of text tokens while aligning the receptive field of image tokens with the training resolution. Second, we propose a structure-detail guidance (SDG) strategy that explicitly models structural information and fine details through scale-wise and temporal-wise guidance, overcoming the limitations of conventional low-resolution guidance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly improves both structural coherence and detail fidelity over existing training-free DiT-based high-resolution generation approaches. The code is available at https://github.com/ljjcoder/Restoring-Semantic-Fidelity.

</details>


### [172] [Fréchet Oral Distance: A Coordinate-Free Metric and Benchmark for Speech-Driven Oral Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01705)
*Jaehong Myung,Seungho Eum,Hyunjung Chung,Unsang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Fréchet Oral Distance（FOD）：用口腔运动编码器（OME）提取的嵌入与TIMIT-Vis参考分布计算Fréchet距离，作为语音驱动面部/口部动画生成质量的客观评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音驱动面部与口部动画已用于教育、言语治疗和健康护理等领域，但生成结果质量的客观评价方法仍然有限。现有评估依赖昂贵设备或主观打分，缺乏可复现的基准。

Method: 构建Oral Motion Encoder（OME）将口部运动映射为嵌入表示，并以TIMIT-Vis数据集构建参考分布，在嵌入空间中计算Fréchet距离得到FOD指标；随后与坐标级指标Lip Vertex Error（LVE）及用户偏好进行对比实验。

Result: FOD给出的模型排序与LVE接近，且变化趋势与用户偏好一致。

Conclusion: 该框架无需昂贵设备或主观评价即可提供客观、可复现的基准测试，并可扩展到多种2D/3D模型的比较。

Abstract: Recently, speech-driven facial and oral animation technologies have been used in various fields such as education, speech therapy, and healthcare. But methods to objectively evaluate the quality of generated results are still limited. Accordingly, we propose the Fréchet Oral Distance (FOD), which uses embeddings from an Oral Motion Encoder (OME) and a TIMIT-Vis reference distribution. In experiments, FOD reproduced model rankings similar to the coordinate-based metric, Lip Vertex Error (LVE), and showed trends consistent with user preference. Our framework provides objective and reproducible benchmarking without relying on expensive equipment or subjective evaluation, and it can be extended to compare a variety of 2D/3D models.

</details>


### [173] [Deep Learning for Cervical Cytology Screening: Methods, Algorithms, Challenges and Prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01730)
*Jiang Yangbo,Zheng Nenggan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理深度学习用于宫颈细胞学筛查的文献，按数据规模、功能任务、监督方式三轴组织，区分全切片置信度聚合与细胞级检测+Bethesda分类两条临床路线，比较数据集与性能并指出指标不可比与数据泄漏问题，最终归纳为五大挑战领域并给出证据到议程映射与部署决策矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宫颈癌仍是女性主要癌症死因，逾90%死亡发生在中低收入国家，当地细胞学筛查受限于细胞病理医师短缺。深度学习使自动化筛查趋于可行，但文献分散于细胞、图像块、全切片等尺度以及不同任务形式与监督范式，难以横向比较，阻碍临床转化，故需系统综述。

Method: 叙述/系统综述方法：沿数据规模、功能任务、监督方式三条轴线组织文献；区分两类临床路线——全切片图像置信度聚合，以及细胞级检测结合Bethesda分类；考察16个公开或公开文档化的细胞学数据集及CAISHI组织病理数据集；定量比较性能并揭示指标不可比性与数据泄漏；评估商业系统、监管状态、HPV时代筛查路径与低资源部署条件。

Result: Herlev与SIPaKMeD上的单细胞准确率已趋于饱和，但这反映基准饱和而非临床就绪；前瞻性与随机化证据正在出现但仍有限；商业系统与监管进展被纳入评估；整体证据被整合为五个挑战领域：数据与基准、架构与标签高效学习、可信AI、临床转化与卫生经济学、评测与可复现性。

Conclusion: 领域进展取决于多中心基准、经外部验证的标签高效方法、良好校准且公平的预测，以及务实的临床试验，而非在已饱和的单细胞基准上继续追求精度提升；作者提供证据到议程映射与部署决策矩阵以指导转化。

Abstract: Cervical cancer remains a leading cause of cancer death among women, with more than 90\% of deaths occurring in low- and middle-income countries, where cytology screening is constrained by a shortage of trained cytopathologists. Deep learning has made automated cytology screening increasingly feasible, but the literature is fragmented across cell, patch and whole-slide-image scales, task formulations and supervision regimes, hindering comparison and clinical translation. This Review synthesizes the deep learning literature for cervical cytology screening, organizing the field along three axes---data scale, functional task and supervision---and distinguishing two clinical roadmaps: whole-slide-image confidence aggregation and cell-level detection with Bethesda classification. We examine 16 publicly available or publicly documented cytology datasets alongside the CAISHI histopathology dataset, compare quantitative performance while exposing incomparable metrics and data leakage, and assess commercial systems, regulatory status, HPV-era screening pathways and low-resource deployment. Single-cell accuracy on Herlev and SIPaKMeD has plateaued, but this reflects benchmark saturation rather than clinical readiness; prospective and randomized evidence is emerging but remains limited. We consolidate the evidence into five challenge areas---data and benchmarks, architecture and label-efficient learning, trustworthy AI, clinical translation and health economics, and evaluation and reproducibility---and provide an evidence-to-agenda map and a deployment decision matrix. Progress now depends on multi-center benchmarks, externally validated label-efficient methods, calibrated and fair predictions, and pragmatic clinical trials rather than further gains on saturated single-cell benchmarks.

</details>


### [174] [RSI-Env: An Open-Ended Environment for Recursive Self-Improvement of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01736)
*Wanying Qu,Zhiyuan Fan,Jiyao Liu,Jiang Zhou,Jianghan Shen,Yanwei Fu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出持续式环境 RSI-Env，实现模型与 harness 的协同递归自我改进，并在三种设定、七个任务上验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归自我改进（RSI）工作通常限制优化目标、可编辑组件或评估流程，导致开放式探索受限、跨代连续性不足。

Method: 构建持续运行环境 RSI-Env，支持自主训练、harness 修改与研究状态留存；由独立 Host Controller 管理服务、评测与版本控制。每个被接受的模型-harness 版本（Self）接管下一轮改进。设定三种目标具体度与基准反馈可得性不同的场景：Open-General、Capability-Directed、Benchmark-Guided；以 Qwen3.8-27B 加最小 harness 为起点，在七个任务上与两个固定外部 Teacher 对比。

Result: 无基准反馈时，Open-General Self 将 GPQA Diamond 从 65.2% 提升至 73.2%，AIME 2026 从 53.3% 提升至 56.7%；三次 Capability-Directed Self 均获得与目标一致的增益；Knowledge Self 在多次系统更新中持续提升，SimpleQA 由 82.0%→85.0%→88.0%。基准引导与 Teacher 的收益因任务而异。

Conclusion: RSI-Env 在所研究轮次内支持开放式探索与持续性递归改进，但跨能力的增益保持仍存在明显挑战。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) envisions agent systems that autonomously improve their own models and harnesses, with each updated system continuing to improve itself. Existing approaches typically restrict optimization objectives, editable components, or evaluation procedures, limiting open-ended exploration and continuity across generations. We introduce RSI-Env, a persistent environment for coordinated model-harness improvement, supporting autonomous training, harness modification, and retention of research state. An independent Host Controller manages services, evaluation, and versioning. Under Self, each accepted model-harness version takes over the next improvement round. We define three settings that vary goal specificity and access to benchmark feedback: Open-General, Capability-Directed, and Benchmark-Guided. Across these three settings, we compare Self with two fixed external Teachers on seven tasks, starting from Qwen3.8-27B with a minimal harness. Without benchmark feedback, Open-General Self raises GPQA Diamond accuracy from 65.2% to 73.2% and AIME 2026 accuracy from 53.3% to 56.7%. All three Capability-Directed Self runs achieve target-aligned gains. Knowledge Self demonstrates continued improvement across system updates, with SimpleQA accuracy rising from 82.0% to 85.0% and then 88.0%. Benchmark guidance and Teacher benefits vary by task. These results demonstrate RSI-Env's support for open-ended exploration and sustained recursive improvement within the studied runs, while highlighting challenges in preserving gains across capabilities.

</details>


### [175] [Teleoperation System for Service Robots using Virtual Reality Headset and Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01759)
*Eduardo Fernandes,Tiago Ribeiro,Antonio Ribeiro,Carolina Lopes,Gil Lopes,Fernando Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合人体姿态估计与VR的服务机器人遥操作系统：基于Mediapipe做2D检测并结合RealSense D455的RGB-D数据实现3D人体姿态估计，映射到机器人运动学以自然控制机器人，并开发VR界面提供沉浸式视角；在CHARMIE机器人上通过抓取/操作实验验证，腕部偏航等关节角精度仍是局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人需在复杂动态环境中部署，传统遥操作方式直观性与操作员态势感知不足；希望借助人体姿态估计与VR实现自然、沉浸的远程控制。

Method: 以Mediapipe框架进行2D人体姿态检测，融合Intel RealSense D455的RGB-D数据扩展为3D姿态估计；提出实时关节角计算算法，将人体姿态映射到机器人运动学；构建VR沉浸式界面；在CHARMIE服务机器人上开展真实场景物体操作实验验证。

Result: 真实实验表明系统在涉及物体操作的任务中有效，验证了VR遥操作的可行性；但腕部偏航等部分关节角难以准确捕捉，人体与机器人运动映射精度仍待提升。

Conclusion: RGB-D感知结合Mediapipe实时关节角算法提升了人机运动映射精度，并通过VR实现沉浸式控制，为人机交互尤其是服务机器人遥操作提供新思路；未来将引入IMU改善旋转跟踪并采用更先进控制算法。

Abstract: This paper presents a teleoperation system for service robots that integrates human pose estimation with virtual reality (VR) to enable intuitive remote control. By using the Mediapipe framework for 2D pose detection and extending it with RGB-D data from the Intel RealSense D455 camera, a comprehensive 3D human pose estimation is achieved. This data is then mapped to the robot's kinematics, allowing the operator to control the robot's movements naturally. A VR interface was developed to provide an immersive perspective, enhancing the operator's situational awareness. Real-world experiments were conducted using the CHARMIE service robot to validate the system's effectiveness in tasks involving object manipulation. While the current system successfully demonstrates the potential of VR-based teleoperation, challenges remain in accurately capturing certain joint angles (e.g., wrist yaw) and ensuring precise mapping between human and robot movements. Future work includes the integration of inertial measurement units (IMUs) for improved rotational tracking and the implementation of more advanced control algorithms. Overall, this research contributes to the development of more intuitive and effective teleoperation systems for service robots, facilitating their deployment in complex and dynamic environments. This work further introduces a novel approach to teleoperation by combining RGB-D sensing with an innovative algorithm for real-time joint angle computation using Mediapipe, enhancing the precision of human-robot motion mapping and enabling immersive control through a VR interface. These contributions represent significant advancements in the field of human-robot interaction, particularly for service robot applications.

</details>


### [176] [FinishStrong: Learning to End Well after Imperfect Start in Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01765)
*Yuda Zou,Boxiang Zhou,Xin Zhou,Yibo Chen,Dejia Song,Xu Tang,Yao Hu,Yongchao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FinishStrong两阶段后训练框架，使自回归视频时序定位模型在起点设置不佳时仍能输出条件最优的结束点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频时序定位存在"端点性能不对称"：结束点预测显著差于起点。已确定的错误起点是不可更改的物理约束，会不可逆地封顶可达IoU（沉没损失）；模型应转而最小化剩余的条件遗憾，但现有后训练方法无法教会这一点——teacher-forced SFT 有严格前缀暴露偏差，标准GRPO的整体轨迹评估会在差起点之后惩罚条件最优的结束点。

Method: 两阶段后训练：1）Online-start SFT，让结束点预测暴露于模型自身生成的上下文（缓解前缀暴露偏差）；2）Conditional-tree GRPO，重构采样拓扑，将结束点奖励仅置于同一起点分支内，数学上隔离条件结束质量与继承的起点惩罚。

Result: FinishStrong-9B 在 TimeLens-Bench 上取得 61.7% 整体IoU的SOTA，超过 Gemini-3.8-Flash、Qwen3.8-Max 等旗舰闭源模型；在被迫从不完美起点续接时，后续结束点预测误差降低36.5%。

Conclusion: 将"差起点"视为不可逆约束、并在此基础上优化条件化结束质量，是可显式教学的对齐目标；修正训练时的暴露方式与奖励归因拓扑即可显著提升端点鲁棒性。代码将开源。

Abstract: A poor start lowers the ceiling, but does not negate the value of finishing well. In autoregressive video temporal grounding, models systematically exhibit an endpoint performance asymmetry, predicting ends significantly worse than starts. A committed flawed start acts as an immutable physical constraint, irreversibly capping the attainable overlap as a sunk loss. While models should minimize the remaining conditional regret, standard post-training paradigms fail to teach this adaptation. Specifically, teacher-forced SFT suffers from strict prefix exposure bias, and holistic trajectory evaluation in standard GRPO inadvertently penalizes conditionally optimal ends if they follow a poor start. To address this, we introduce FinishStrong, a novel two-stage post-training framework explicitly designed to teach optimal continuations. First, Online-start SFT exposes the end prediction to the model's own generated contexts. Second, Conditional-tree GRPO restructures the sampling topology, centering end rewards exclusively within the same-start branch to mathematically isolate the conditional end quality from the inherited start penalty. FinishStrong-9B achieves a state-of-the-art 61.7\% overall IoU on TimeLens-Bench, remarkably outperforming flagship closed-source models like Gemini-3.8-Flash and Qwen3.8-Max. Crucially, when forced to continue from imperfect starts, it reduces the subsequent end prediction error by 36.5\%, proving its exceptional ability to end well after flawed starts. Code will be open.

</details>


### [177] [OCBI: Order-Sensitive Cross-Branch Interaction for Image Manipulation Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01783)
*Zhouli Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OCBI，通过建模RGB引导的跨分支注意力与取证自注意力两种组合顺序的差异（对易子/交换子）实现图像篡改定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像篡改定位需结合语义结构与取证痕迹；分支内空间混合与RGB引导的跨分支混合以相反顺序组合时会产生不同消息，该顺序不一致性未被现有Co-Transformers利用。

Method: 在Co-Transformers骨架上扩展：局部跨分支融合模块利用RGB引导跨注意力与取证自注意力的交换子（commutator），为特征传递添加可学习的带符号修正；随后像素级自适应多尺度聚合融合空间上下文再进行预测；沿用Co-Transformers骨干与监督方式。

Result: 与重训Co-Transformers（仅CASIA任务训练、50 epoch、末轮评估）对比：五个干净测试集上宏PixelF1为0.5773，较基线高0.43个百分点；在三个数据集的JPEG压缩、高斯模糊、缩放共21种条件组合下，平均PixelF1较基线高0.95个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式建模两种混合顺序的差异可为篡改定位带来稳定增益，且在干净与退化条件下均优于基线，但提升幅度较小。

Abstract: Image manipulation localization combines semantic structure with forensic traces to identify modified pixels. Within-branch spatial mixing and RGB-guided cross-branch mixing can produce different messages when composed in opposite orders. We propose OCBI, an extension of Co-Transformers that models the discrepancy between these two composition orders for localization. A local cross-branch fusion module uses the commutator of RGB-guided cross-attention and forensic self-attention to add a learnable signed correction to feature transfer. Pixelwise adaptive multiscale aggregation then combines spatial contexts before prediction. OCBI uses the Co-Transformers backbone and supervision. We compare OCBI with retrained Co-Transformers using CASIA-only task training, a 50-epoch budget, and final-epoch evaluation. Across five clean test datasets, OCBI achieves a macro PixelF1 of 0.5773, exceeding the baseline by 0.43 percentage points. Under JPEG compression, Gaussian blur, and resizing on three datasets, its average PixelF1 over 21 dataset-transformation conditions exceeds the baseline by 0.95 percentage points.

</details>


### [178] [Detection Instead of Inversion: T1-T2 Microstructure Quantification With Detection Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01790)
*Sebastian Endt,Fatih Özkan,Marion I. Menzel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将T1-T2相关MRI的谱重建视为目标检测任务，用DETR从64个TI/TE信号直接预测由T1、T2和信号占比定义的微结构分区，在仿真数据上mAP@10达76.8%，优于先前模型(53.8%)与正则化NNLS(66.1%)，且重建速度快数个量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上逐体素重建完整T1-T2谱需执行病态且耗时的逆拉普拉斯变换；作者希望在不对微结构强加生物模型的前提下，更高效地量化组织微结构分区。

Method: 将T1-T2谱重建重新表述为目标检测问题：输入64个TI/TE信号，由Detection Transformer (DETR) 直接输出一组以T1、T2和信号占比为参数的分区，绕过显式逆拉普拉斯变换。

Result: 仿真数据上mAP@10为76.8%，优于先前模型设计（53.8%）与正则化NNLS（66.1%）；在分区数量、分区信号占比和SNR的几乎所有设置下均优于NNLS，且重建速度比NNLS快数个量级。

Conclusion: DETR式目标检测框架可作为T1-T2谱重建的高精度、高效率替代方案；结果可由公开代码 github.com/SebastianEndtTHI/t1t2-detr 完全复现。

Abstract: T1-T2 correlation MRI has strong potential to quantify microstructural compartments without imposing a biological model. Traditionally, full T1-T2 spectra per voxel are reconstructed with an ill-conditioned and time-consuming Inverse Laplace Transform. We propose to view this challenge as an object detection task: A Detection Transformer (DETR) takes 64 TI/TE signals and directly predicts a set of compartments specified by T1, T2, and signal fraction. We achieve a mean average precision mAP@10 of 76.8% on simulated data, outperforming both our previous model design (53.8%) and regularized NNLS (66.1%). We show that our DETR approach outperforms NNLS in almost every setting across compartment count, compartmental signal fraction, and SNR. Reconstruction is orders of magnitude faster than NNLS. The results are fully reproducible from our public code: github.com/SebastianEndtTHI/t1t2-detr

</details>


### [179] [RIFT: Residual-Informed Flow Transfer for One-Step Generative Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01791)
*Qizhe Wang,Jiaye Fu,Feng Ye,Haocheng Tang,Zetian Song,Chuanmin Jia,Siwei Ma,Jiaqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RIFT：一种残差感知的流迁移一步式生成视频压缩框架，通过残差几何诱导器将预训练流模型的预测转化为内容自适应的修复更新，解决编解码退化与流匹配扰动轨迹之间的方向失配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频压缩借助预训练时空先验修复编码丢失的细节，但编解码退化与流匹配模型学到的扰动轨迹之间存在显著差距。作者识别出一种"压缩相关的方向失配"：仅依据退化强度校准时间步无法解决该问题——压缩隐变量在不同码率点间高度对齐，而其重建残差在幅度和方向上变化显著。

Method: 提出RIFT框架，核心为残差几何诱导器（RGI）：利用解码器可见的编解码线索，将预训练流预测转换为内容自适应的修复更新；RGI融合流时间步条件、可学习的全频带残差强度估计，以及内容条件的方向与增益调制，使更新的方向与幅度对齐压缩相关的残差几何；整体为一步式生成。

Result: 在三个公开视频压缩基准上，RIFT优于同类生成式视频压缩方法，代码已公开。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模压缩残差的几何特性并对齐流模型的更新方向与幅度，可在单步生成下有效弥合编解码退化与流先验之间的失配，提升生成式视频压缩的修复质量。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation have enabled generative video compression to reconstruct details lost during coding using pretrained spatiotemporal priors. However, a substantial gap remains between codec-induced degradation and the perturbation trajectories learned by flow-matching models. In this paper, we identify a compression-dependent directional mismatch that degradation-strength-based timestep calibration alone cannot resolve. Compressed latents remain strongly aligned across codec operating points, while their reconstruction residuals vary substantially in magnitude and direction. To bridge this gap, we propose RIFT, a Residual-Informed Flow Transfer framework for one-step generative video compression. At its core, RIFT introduces a Residual Geometry Inducer (RGI) that converts the pretrained flow prediction into a content-adaptive restoration update using decoder-visible codec cues. RGI integrates flow-timestep conditioning with a learned full-band residual-strength estimate and content-conditioned direction and gain modulation, aligning the orientation and magnitude of the update with the compression-dependent residual geometry. Extensive experiments on three public video compression benchmarks show that RIFT outperforms competing generative video compression methods. The code is publicly available on RIFT.

</details>


### [180] [When Does Latent Prediction Error Support Physical Surprise Claims in Video World Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01815)
*Anton Hristov,Aleksandar Slavov,Vasil Vasilev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评价框架，用于区分冻结表征中"包含什么"、表征如何组织外观与物理差异、以及什么因素会改变模型自身的预测误差；结果表明，模型可以表征物理信息并对错误动作作出响应，但其原生预测误差并不能可靠地度量"物理惊奇"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型的预测误差常被直接当作"物理惊奇"（physical surprise）的度量，但误差也可能源自外观、静态内容、运动或一般性的预测难度。作者希望厘清表征内容与预测误差含义之间的混淆，检验预测误差是否真能反映物理不合理性。

Method: 构建三层评测框架：(1) 分析冻结表征所含信息；(2) 分析表征几何——外观变化相对视频间差异的幅度、方向跨场景一致性、与物理变化方向的重叠随层深的变化；(3) 分析改变模型自身预测误差的因素。在 IntPhys2 上比较潜空间预测（V-JEPA2）、掩码像素重建与简单视觉统计三类表征；在 Physion++ 上测试单帧重复与全局颜色变化的效应；用 Kubric 仿真渲染框架检验物理不可能事件的误差。

Result: IntPhys2 上外观信息易于解码，而 V-JEPA2 对事件"可能/不可能"仅暴露有限信息；随层深增加，外观变化幅度相对视频间差异变小、方向跨场景更一致、与物理变化的重叠减少。动作条件模型中，错误动作提高预测误差，但外观变化也引起显著响应。Physion++ 中重复单帧可比原始片段产生更高误差，全局变色反而降低误差。Kubric 中不可能事件的误差可低于可能事件；固定误差阈值会把大量可能事件误判为不可能。

Conclusion: 预测误差并非物理惊奇的可信代理：模型能在表征中编码物理信息并对错误动作敏感，但其原生预测误差同时受外观与低层统计因素干扰，且缺乏单调性。评估物理理解需将表征探测与误差行为分开考察，避免以单一固定阈值判定物理合理性。

Abstract: Prediction errors from video world models are often treated as physical surprise, but they can also reflect appearance, static content, motion, or ordinary prediction difficulty. We present an evaluation framework that separates what frozen representations contain, how they organize appearance and physical differences, and what changes a model's own prediction error. On IntPhys2, a benchmark of physically possible and impossible events, we compare representations from latent prediction, masked pixel reconstruction, and simple visual statistics. Appearance remains easy to decode, while V-JEPA2 exposes modest information about whether events are possible or impossible. Looking at the latent geometry, appearance changes become smaller compared with differences between videos, their directions line up more consistently across scenes, and their overlap with physical changes decreases in deeper layers. Across action-conditioned models, incorrect actions raise prediction error, while appearance changes also produce substantial responses. In Physion++ video clips, repeating a single frame can produce higher error than the original clip, while changing color throughout the clip can lower it. Using Kubric, a simulation and rendering framework, we find that impossible events can produce lower error than possible events. A fixed error threshold also incorrectly flags many possible events as impossible. A model can therefore represent physical information and respond to incorrect actions without its native prediction error reliably measuring physical surprise.

</details>


### [181] [RCFormer: Reliability-aware Context Transformer for Occlusion-Robust Facial Landmark Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01822)
*Ashish Kumar,Prashant Kumar,Mallika Garg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可靠性感知上下文Transformer（RCFormer），通过可见性条件化、可见性引导聚合与空间残差校正三个组件强化遮挡等复杂条件下的潜在图像块恢复，并以恢复热图为结构线索提升人脸关键点检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规成像下人脸关键点检测已较成熟，但遮挡、弱光照与大姿态变化会使局部面部证据不完整或具误导性。上下文引导的潜在图像块恢复是有前景的方案，但恢复的上下文本身可能不可靠：对输入人脸适配不足、受损坏邻块影响、融合时未保持局部空间一致性。

Method: RCFormer包含三部分：VCC（可见上下文条件化）用可见性加权的全局人脸摘要自适应调整上下文表示；VGCA（可见性引导上下文聚合）在注意力中降低不可靠源图像块的权重；SRC（空间残差校正）利用邻域块信息精炼融合后的潜在特征。恢复出的热图作为可靠性感知结构线索输入现有关键点检测网络。

Result: 实验表明RCFormer改善了热图恢复质量，并在WFLW、COFW和300W数据集上取得较强的人脸关键点定位结果。

Conclusion: 以可靠性为显式设计原则的上下文Transformer可有效缓解复杂条件下的局部证据缺失问题，且其恢复热图可作为通用结构先验增强既有关键点检测网络。

Abstract: Facial landmark detection has advanced substantially under regular imaging conditions, yet occlusion, poor illumination, and large pose changes remain major sources of error because local facial evidence can become incomplete or misleading. Context-guided latent patch recovery offers a promising way to address this issue by inferring unreliable patch representations from surrounding facial context before heatmap prediction. However, the recovered context can itself be unreliable when it is weakly adapted to the input face, influenced by corrupted neighboring patches, or blended without preserving local spatial coherence. We propose RCFormer, a Reliability-aware Context Transformer for occlusion-robust facial landmark detection. RCFormer strengthens latent patch recovery with three reliability-oriented components: visible-context conditioning (VCC), which adapts the context representation using a visibility-weighted global facial summary; visibility-guided context aggregation (VGCA), which reduces the influence of unreliable source patches during attention; and spatial residual correction (SRC), which refines blended latent features using neighboring patch information. The recovered heatmaps are then used as reliability-aware structural cues for existing landmark detection networks. Experiments show that RCFormer improves heatmap recovery and achieves strong landmark localization results on the WFLW, COFW, and 300W datasets.

</details>


### [182] [LUMEN: Geometry-Guided Diffusion Model for Spatially-Varying HDR Illumination Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01823)
*Hongze Chen,Fangyu Lin,Runze Zhang,Lingting Zhu,Weikai Chen,Zeyu Hu,Yingda Yin,Keyang Luo,Xin Wang,Zehong Lin,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LUMEN是一个几何条件化的扩散框架，可从单目或多视角图像在任意空间位置合成高动态范围（HDR）环境贴图，实现空间变化的场景光照估计，相比已有方法显著提升视觉保真度与空间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像中的光照本质上是空间变化的（受几何、遮挡与光源影响），而现有方法多依赖局部环境贴图或探针表示并结合回归/参数化模型，导致光照过度平滑、真实感有限，因此作者从生成式视角重新审视空间变化光照估计问题。

Method: 构建几何条件化扩散框架：先从输入图像重建粗略3D场景，在任意位置渲染部分环境贴图以编码可见性与空间结构；将这些信号与多视角图像特征融合后输入扩散Transformer生成完整HDR环境贴图。同时构建含700个室内场景的合成数据集，提供多视角RGB、深度及多采样位置的HDR光照密集空间监督。

Result: 大量实验表明，LUMEN在视觉保真度和空间一致性上显著优于已有方法。

Conclusion: 以生成式扩散代替回归/参数化建模，并结合几何条件与密集空间监督，可有效解决空间变化HDR光照估计中的过度平滑问题，实现更真实、空间一致的光照分布。

Abstract: Estimating scene illumination from images is challenging as lighting is inherently spatially varying, depending on geometry, occlusion, and light sources. Existing methods incorporate geometric cues by deriving local environment maps or probe-based representations, but largely rely on regression or parametric models, resulting in over-smoothed lighting and limited realism. We revisit spatially-varying illumination estimation from a generative perspective and introduce LUMEN, a geometry-conditioned diffusion framework for synthesizing high-dynamic-range (HDR) environment maps at arbitrary spatial locations from single or multi-view images. Specifically, we reconstruct a coarse 3D scene from input images and render partial environment maps at arbitrary locations to encode visibility and spatial structure. These signals are fused with multi-view image features and fed into a diffusion transformer to generate full HDR environment maps. To train the model, we build a synthetic dataset of 700 indoor scenes with dense spatial supervision, including multi-view RGB images, depth, and HDR illumination at multiple sampled locations. This dataset enables the model to learn spatially consistent lighting distributions across diverse scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LUMEN significantly improves visual fidelity and spatial coherence compared to prior approaches.

</details>


### [183] [Z-Cache: Accelerating Diffusion Transformers via Self-Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01846)
*Zegang Cheng,Zhikai Wang,Jiacheng Liu,Xiaobing Tu,Chang Zou,Junjie Chen,Yue Ma,Zhiyuan Ma,Jinkui Ren,Likai Zou,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Z-Cache 是一种基于"自反思"的扩散 Transformer 特征缓存方法：先预测未来缓存步特征并执行一次完整计算，再回到缓存步利用前后两次计算结果重新预测以修正特征，从而在高加速下保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散 Transformer 是视觉生成最强模型但计算开销巨大；现有特征缓存（复用前序步骤特征）虽能显著加速，却造成生成质量下降。作者观察到特征缓存的误差在每次完整计算后往往急剧减小，据此希望借助完整计算来纠正缓存特征。

Method: Z-Cache 分三步：1）先预测未来缓存步的特征；2）执行一次完整计算；3）返回这些缓存步，基于前一次与当前完整计算的结果重新预判/更新特征，实现对缓存误差的"自我反思"式修正。

Result: 实验表明，采用 Z-Cache 的扩散 Transformer 在保持与原始模型相当的生成质量的同时获得更快推理速度，例如在 FLUX-dev 文本到图像生成上实现 6.22 倍加速。

Conclusion: 利用完整计算带来的误差收敛特性对缓存特征进行预测-校正的迭代式反思，可在几乎不损失生成质量的前提下大幅加速扩散 Transformer 推理；代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers have become the most powerful models for visual generation, but still suffer from massive computation costs. To solve this problem, feature caching has been proposed to cache the features of diffusion models in the previous computation steps and then reuse them in the following caching steps, which brings significant acceleration but also degradation in generation quality. To address this problem, this paper proposes Z-cache as a feature caching method that can maintain high-quality generation through self-reflection. Concretely, we observe that the error from feature caching tends to be sharply reduced after each full computation. Based on this observation, Z-Cache is designed to first predict the features in the future caching steps and then perform a full computation. After that, Z-Cache returns to the caching steps and re-predicts them based on the previous and the current computation steps, which brings correction in features. Experiments demonstrate that with Z-Cache, diffusion transformers achieve comparable generation quality to the original model but with faster inference speed, for instance, 6.22X acceleration on FLUX-dev for text-to-image generation. Our code is released on GitHub.

</details>


### [184] [Biological Congruence: Evaluating Microscopy Generation Across Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01850)
*Duway Nicolas Lesmes-Leon,Shumpei Takezaki,Fabian Schmeisser,Ryoma Bise,Gillian Lovell,Bianca Migliori,Andreas Dengel,Sheraz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"生物一致性（biological congruence）"评估原则，用联合生成显微图像与实例掩码的文本条件扩散框架，从条件、外观、结构三种表征及群体层面检验生成数据中生物信息是否自洽并与真实生物背景一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型评估主要刻画视觉保真度与分布覆盖度，无法反映合成数据所承载的生物学信息；需一个衡量生物相关信息在跨表征间是否连贯、是否与经验生物背景一致的评估视角。

Method: 构建文本条件扩散框架，联合生成显微图像与实例掩码，使同一生成生物状态同时体现在条件文本、视觉外观与实例结构上；开展提示—掩码、提示—图像、图像—掩码四类配对分析及群体层面分析，并进行逐步引入不支持表型组合的压力测试。

Result: 表现强烈依赖具体特征：密度与汇合度被稳定保持；形态学出现反复不一致；最近邻距离显示强图像—掩码一致性可与较弱的条件符合度和群体保真度共存。压力测试中，目标可控性提升的同时，非目标表型发生重组，整体生成状态向更被真实数据支持的构型移动。

Conclusion: 生成质量、可控性、跨表征一致性与群体保真度刻画的是互补属性；生物一致性可作为评估科学生成模型的关系性视角。

Abstract: Generative models increasingly produce realistic microscopy images, yet conventional evaluation primarily characterizes visual fidelity and distributional coverage rather than the biological information represented by synthetic data. We introduce biological congruence as an evaluation principle describing whether biologically relevant information forms a coherent description across representations and remains consistent with its empirical biological context. To study this principle, we develop a text-conditioned diffusion framework that jointly generates microscopy images and instance masks, exposing the same generated biological state through conditioning, visual appearance, and instance structure. Across prompt--mask, prompt--image, image--mask, and population-level analyses, we find strongly feature-dependent behavior: density and confluency are consistently preserved, morphology exhibits recurrent disagreement, while nearest-neighbor distance reveals that strong image--mask agreement can coexist with weaker conditioning and population fidelity. Stress testing increasingly unsupported phenotype combinations further shows that target controllability can increase while untargeted phenotypes reorganize and the complete generated state moves toward configurations better supported by real data. These findings demonstrate that generative quality, controllability, cross-representation agreement, and population fidelity capture complementary properties, motivating biological congruence as a relational perspective for evaluating scientific generative models.

</details>


### [185] [Learning-to-Memorize: Dynamic Context Management for Long-Horizon Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01858)
*Haowei Zhu,Qijie Wang,Jia Li,Xing Wang,Tianyu Zhao,Jiexi Wang,Xurui Peng,Xinglong Wu,Junhai Yong,Bin Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可学习上下文记忆管理框架L2M，按预测重要性动态保留、淘汰与演化历史帧，缓解自回归长视频生成中的误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成在短视频上效果良好，但长时程生成受误差累积困扰。现有静态上下文管理策略（滑动窗口缓存丢弃早期帧、固定sink帧永久保留初始内容）要么丢失关键长程上下文，要么将生成锚定在过时信息上，导致保真度下降、运动停滞与多样性降低。

Method: L2M包含三部分：(1) 重要性预测路由器，估计每个历史帧的相关性；(2) 退化感知训练，提升对噪声或损坏上下文的鲁棒性；(3) 动态记忆初始化与演化机制，用EMA重要性分数更新长期记忆，使高质量帧替代陈旧锚点并随场景演化自适应。以教师-学生蒸馏目标加稀疏正则训练，在固定KV-cache预算下高效利用上下文。

Result: 大量实验表明，L2M在长时程视频生成质量与时序一致性上优于现有基线。

Conclusion: L2M为长自回归视频生成的上下文记忆管理提供了一种可学习策略，在固定缓存预算下保留最具信息量的上下文。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation has achieved impressive results on short clips, yet generating long videos remains challenging due to error accumulation over extended horizons, where each predicted frame depends on potentially imperfect previous frames. Existing approaches typically rely on static context management strategies, such as sliding-window caches that discard early frames or fixed sink frames that permanently preserve initial content. These designs either lose critical long-range context or anchor generation to outdated information, which can lead to degraded fidelity, motion stagnation, and reduced diversity. In this paper, we propose Learning to Memorize (L2M), a learned context memory management framework that dynamically retains, evicts, and evolves historical frames according to predicted importance. The framework consists of an importance prediction router that estimates the relevance of each historical frame, degradation-aware training that improves robustness to noisy or corrupted context, and a dynamic memory initialization and evolution mechanism that updates long-term memory using exponential moving average (EMA) importance scores. This allows high-quality frames to replace stale anchors and enables memory to adapt naturally to scene evolution. Trained with a teacher-student distillation objective and sparsity regularization, L2M makes efficient use of a fixed KV-cache budget while preserving the most informative contextual information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that L2M improves long-horizon video generation quality and temporal consistency over existing baselines, providing a learned strategy for context memory management in long autoregressive video generation.

</details>


### [186] [Advances in Agentic Computer Graphics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01860)
*Hengyu Liu,Jihyeon Je,Ruiqi Wang,Mingxuan Wu,Haven Feng,Qixing Huang,Georgios Pavlakos,Hao Zhang,Jiajun Wu,Maneesh Agrawala,Leonidas Guibas,R. Kenny Jones,Shangzhe Wu,Guandao Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一份综述性报告，针对"智能体图形学"（agentic graphics）这一新兴方向，提出统一的分类法（taxonomy），并构建一个可由智能体驱动的大规模文献分析系统，用以梳理研究趋势并讨论开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机图形学以代码为基础，LLM 智能体已能生成并迭代优化图形代码，催生了以智能体系统为核心的新范式。该领域研究快速扩张，人工文献综述难以跟上，且不同任务间术语不统一，阻碍了系统化理解。

Method: 两项贡献：其一，提出一个统一的分类法，用于跨不同图形学任务刻画智能体系统；其二，开发一个智能体文献系统，辅助人类研究者大规模分析已有及新发表的论文。

Result: 基于上述工作，分析并归纳出新兴研究趋势，识别出四类开放挑战：跨图形任务的智能体与工具协同、人—智能体协作的接口与表示设计、长时程规划与精化的维持、交互式图形系统的可靠评估。

Conclusion: 该报告为智能体图形学提供了系统化的概念框架与自动化文献分析工具，明确了领域趋势与关键开放问题，为后续研究提供参考基础。

Abstract: Computer graphics is built on code. Large language model (LLM) agents can now generate graphics code and iteratively refine it through various feedback. This capability opens the door to a potentially new paradigm of computer graphics, with graphics algorithms increasingly built around agentic systems to automate progressively complex tasks. Over the past years, research on agentic graphics has expanded rapidly across a diverse range of tasks. This rapid growth makes it increasingly difficult for manual literature reviews to keep pace with new developments. At the same time, similar ideas are often described using different terminology across these tasks, further complicating efforts to develop a systematic understanding of the field. In this report, we address these challenges through two contributions. First, we introduce a unified taxonomy for characterizing agentic systems across diverse computer graphics tasks. Second, we develop an agentic literature system that can assist human researchers in analyzing existing and newly published papers at scale. Building on these contributions, we analyze emerging research trends and discuss open challenges in coordinating agents and tools across graphics tasks, designing interfaces and representations for human-agent collaboration, sustaining long-horizon planning and refinement, and reliably evaluating interactive graphics systems.

</details>


### [187] [Prior-Guided Physics-Informed Deep Learning for Fast 4D MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01888)
*Mojtaba Safari,Parag J. Parikh,Shansong Wang,Mingzhe Hu,Philip Connell,Stanley L. Liauw,Ralph R. Weichselbaum,Zhen Tian,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PGPI4MR——一种先验引导、物理信息约束的深度学习4D MRI重建方法，利用其他呼吸相位信息或同一扫描前30秒的setup扫描作为先验，在不增加扫描时间的前提下，以秒级延迟准确重建4D MRI和亚秒级单帧3D图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D MRI在MR引导放疗的运动管理与治疗计划中很重要，但传统压缩感知重建速度慢、精度受限于稀疏采样；而额外采集先验信息通常需要更多扫描时间。作者希望在无额外扫描时间的条件下提升4D与亚秒级MRI的重建精度与实时性。

Method: PGPI4MR为展开式（unrolled）网络，利用setup扫描图像及其他呼吸相位信息作为先验，从60/30/15 s数据重建16个呼吸相位，并从0.29–0.49 s数据重建单帧3D体；网络仅在志愿者数据上训练一次、不对新志愿者微调。评估于实测k空间数据：自由呼吸MR-linac（1.5T）、腹部（3T）及袖带压迫大腿（1.5T）三套数据集，所有方法（含对照模型）使用相同样本并采用相同评分。

Result: 体内实验中，PGPI4MR在所有扫描时长下留出误差最低；16相位在2块GPU上于扫描结束后中位7.5–13 s完成。16相位联合重建使60 s误差较逐相位独立重建降低9.1%。亚秒MRI在0.49 s处误差比最优对照（朝同一先验的经典重建）低15.5%；在较快GPU上每个0.29 s窗口可在0.5 s内完成采集与重建。未重训时在3T腹部数据上匹配最优方法，大腿数据未匹配；在大腿数据重训后留出误差较最优对照降低6.6%。

Conclusion: 先验信息使PGPI4MR在压缩感知及已发表的4D网络之上实现更准确的4D与亚秒MRI重建，无需额外扫描时间且延迟仅为秒级，有望支撑MR引导放疗中的4D MRI治疗计划与3D运动监测。

Abstract: Background and purpose: We propose PGPI4MR (prior-guided, physics-informed 4D MRI reconstruction), a deep learning method that leverages information from other respiratory phases or a setup scan, the first 30 s of the same scan, to improve 4D MRI reconstruction without extra scan time. Materials and methods: PGPI4MR is an unrolled network that reconstructs 16 respiratory phases from 60, 30, or 15 s of data, and single 3D volumes, each from 0.29-0.49 s of data, using a setup-scan image. The networks were trained once, on other volunteers, and were not retrained for new volunteers. We evaluated PGPI4MR on measured k-space from three datasets: free-breathing MR-linac (1.5T) and abdominal (3T) scans, and thigh scans with cuff compression (1.5T). All methods, including comparative models, received identical samples and were scored similarly. Results: In vivo, PGPI4MR had the lowest held-out error for 4D MRI at every scan time, and its 16 phases were ready a median of 7.5-13 s after the scan on the 2 GPUs tested. Coupling the 16 respiratory phases reduced the error at 60 s by 9.1% compared with reconstructing each phase alone. For sub-second MRI, the error at 0.49 s was 15.5% lower than that of the best comparison method, classical reconstruction toward the same prior, and on the faster GPU, every 0.29 s window was acquired and reconstructed within 0.5 s. Without retraining, PGPI4MR matched the best methods on the 3T abdominal scans but not on the thigh scans; after retraining on thigh data, it reduced the held-out error by 6.6% relative to the best comparison method. Conclusion: Prior information allowed PGPI4MR to reconstruct 4D and sub-second MRI more accurately than compressed sensing and a published 4D network, within seconds and without extra scan time. This could support 4D MRI for treatment planning and 3D motion monitoring during MR-guided radiotherapy.

</details>


### [188] [Systematic Evaluation of Robustness in Fine-tuned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01900)
*Angela John,Hamayoon Behmanush*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统评估微调预训练模型在多种数据集与攻击类型下的鲁棒性，并给出面向图像分类的选型建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助研究者在图像分类任务中选择预训练模型时优先考虑抗攻击鲁棒性，并兼顾算力、内存与干净数据精度等实际约束。

Method: 对预训练模型进行权重微调，在 MNIST、CIFAR10、CIFAR100、SVHN 等公开数据集上，分别评估未受攻击与多种攻击情形下的表现，并对比 ResNet50、DenseNet、MobileNet 的鲁棒性。

Result: 微调模型的鲁棒性随数据分布与样本规模变化而显著不同；ResNet50、DenseNet、MobileNet 在不同数据集上的防御表现存在差异。

Conclusion: 预训练模型的鲁棒性依赖具体任务条件，需按任务需求定制化选择模型；研究为实际图像分类应用中的模型选型提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Our study systematically evaluates the robustness of fine-tuned pre-trained models across different datasets and attack types, aiming to provide insights for researchers in selecting pre-trained models for image classification tasks while prioritizing robustness against potential attacks. Our work explores the influence of fine-tuning pre-trained models with different datasets and attack types on their robustness and overview of the appropriate pre-trained model for image classification based on factors such as computation resources, memory usage, and accuracy on clean data. We fine-tune the models with weights. Evaluate the models on data before any attack and after different attack cases. The study utilizes publicly available image datasets such as MNIST, CIFAR10, CIFAR100, and SVHN to assess the robustness of pre-trained models. The results indicate that the robustness of fine-tuned models varies across different data distributions and sample sizes, emphasizing the need for tailored selection based on specific task requirements. The findings also highlight the varying performance of ResNet50, DenseNet, and MobileNet in defending against attacks on different datasets. Overall, this research provides valuable insights into the robustness of fine-tuned pre-trained models and their suitability for real-world applications in image classification.

</details>


### [189] [Training-Free Motion-Corrected 3D Brain MRI Reconstruction via Event-Guided References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01902)
*Wei Jiang,Hongli Chen,Tianyi Ding,Feng Liu,Nan Ye,Hongfu Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EventMC：一种免训练的帧间运动校正方法，通过检测运动事件并利用稳定区间重建的参考图像迭代估计运动，PSNR最高提升4.0 dB，速度最高提升7.5倍且显存减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头部运动会在3D脑MRI中产生伪影并干扰结构测量。基于模型的校正交替进行图像与运动估计，但重建参考图像中的伪影会反过来强化运动估计误差；学习型先验虽能减少伪影，却需预训练并增加测试时计算开销。

Method: 提出EventMC（事件引导的运动校正），面向交错笛卡尔采集的免训练方法：迭代检测运动事件，并使用由稳定区间重建的参考图像估计运动；随后由低空间频率到高空间频率逐步细化图像与运动估计。

Result: 在相同重建设置的公平仿真对比中，EventMC的运动估计较学习型方法MotionTTT的平均PSNR最高提升4.0 dB；单次运行速度最高快7.5倍，GPU显存占用不到一半。在体运动损坏扫描上，EventMC在无任何训练的情况下取得与MotionTTT相近的平均图像质量。

Conclusion: EventMC以事件检测+稳定区间参考+由低频到高频的迭代细化，在无需预训练的条件下实现了高效、低显存且高质量的3D脑MRI运动校正。

Abstract: Head motion causes artefacts in 3D brain MRI and can bias structural measurements. Model-based correction alternates between image and motion estimation, but artefacts in the reconstructed reference can reinforce motion errors. Learned priors can reduce these artefacts but require pretraining and may add test-time computation. We propose EventMC (event-guided motion correction), a training-free method for interleaved Cartesian acquisitions. It iteratively detects motion events and estimates motion using reference images reconstructed from stable intervals. It then refines the image and motion from low to high spatial frequencies. In a fair comparison in simulation (same reconstruction), EventMC's motion estimates improve mean PSNR by up to 4.0 dB over those of the learning-based MotionTTT. A single run of EventMC is up to 7.5 times faster than MotionTTT with less than half the GPU memory. On in vivo motion-corrupted scans, EventMC achieves similar mean image quality to MotionTTT without any training. Code is available at https://github.com/bayesbreeze/EventMC.

</details>


### [190] [Online Active Learning Architecture for Safety-Critical Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01907)
*Shuiwen Shen,Jinhua Zhang,Yan Wang,Huanqiang Zeng,Yueyun Xu,Song Gao,Haifeng Xiang,Yifu Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Control AI——一种受生物智能"预测-评估-修正"循环启发的在线主动学习（OAL）架构，融合深度网络的分层表征与自适应控制的实时误差反馈，通过交叉重构避免平凡恒等映射，并给出一系列缩短误差传递路径的变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离线训练范式在开放世界环境中存在局限，制约了信息中心型人工智能向安全关键应用迁移；需要具备在线自适应能力的学习架构。

Method: 构建三模块结构：预测模型将感知输入映射到潜在流形，可选潜在模型刻画该空间中的状态转移，重构模型将潜在状态映射回可观测信号；以预测与测量之间的残差作为内部学习信号。设计空间与时间交叉重构架构以防 OAL 退化为恒等映射，并提出耦合型、对偶型、局部化型变体以缩短误差传递路径；局部化预测编码式 OAL 通过 guidance-backpropagation 与 target-backpropagation 架构实现，另有混合与缩放变体支持高维部署。

Result: 论文以架构论证为主，说明从概念性反馈闭环到实用大规模配置的演进路径，并给出支持高维部署的变体设计；摘要未报告定量实验结果。

Conclusion: OAL 的关键在于预测-评估-修正闭环与交叉重构机制的架构设计，局部化误差传递可提升可扩展性，为在线主动学习的大规模落地提供设计原则。

Abstract: The transition of information-centric artificial intelligence to safety-critical applications is impeded by the limitations of conventional offline-training paradigms in open-world environments. This paper presents Control AI, an architecture for Online Active Learning (OAL) inspired by the continuous Prediction-Evaluation-Correction process of biological intelligence. The framework combines the hierarchical representation capacity of deep neural networks with the real-time error-feedback adaptation of adaptive control. A prediction model maps sensory input into a latent manifold, an optional latent model represents transitions in that space, and a reconstruction model maps the latent state back to observable signals. Residuals between predictions and measurements provide the internal learning signal. Spatial and temporal cross-reconstruction architectures prevent OAL from collapsing to a trivial identity mapping, while coupled, dual, and localized variants shorten error-transmission paths. Localized predictive coding style OAL is implemented through guidance-backpropagation and target-backpropagation architectures, and hybrid and scaled variants support high-dimensional deployment. The paper focuses on the architectural rationale and the progression from a conceptual feedback loop to practical large-scale configurations.

</details>


### [191] [SurgQ-VLM: Question-Conditioned Dual-Stream Compression and Answer-Type-Aware Reinforcement for Surgical Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01913)
*Dhruv Makwana,Ananya Mantravadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SurgQ-VLM：两阶段手术视频问答模型，第一阶段用问题条件化的双流（慢/快）压缩器为LoRA微调的Qwen2.5-VL-7B压缩视觉token（49倍），第二阶段用答案类型感知奖励的GRPO后训练，在21,840题封闭测试集上达0.779关键词准确率、BLEU-4较零样本提升10倍，GRPO增益2.5pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术视频推理（阶段识别、器械动作、事件时序）是术中记录、流程分析与技能评估的基础，但现有手术VQA多基于单帧与封闭标签，无法表达时长、顺序或计数；视频模型对所有片段采用与问题无关的统一压缩，丢弃回答所需的时序证据；且答案结构异构（二分类、分类、多标签、计数、句子-时序五种），缺乏统一训练信号。

Method: 两阶段方法。阶段1：为LoRA适配的Qwen2.5-VL-7B训练问题条件化双流（slow/fast）压缩器，通过交叉注意力使两路流均以问题为条件，每个片段的视觉token从原始ViT patch压缩49倍（相对backbone合并后token为11倍）。阶段2：以GRPO后训练，采用答案类型感知奖励，对每类问题使用匹配其答案结构的指标（标签集用micro-F1、计数用归一化误差、时序窗口用内容-时序混合指标）。

Result: 在Cholec80与CholecT50上密封的21,840题测试划分中，达到0.779关键词准确率，BLEU-4较零样本提升10倍；GRPO带来2.5pp K-Acc提升（其中2.0pp归因于答案类型感知奖励），增益集中于结构困难的题目类型，且答案格式保持不变（ROUGE-L）。

Conclusion: 问题条件化的极致token压缩与答案类型感知的强化学习奖励相结合，可有效支持跨多种答案结构的手术视频问答，并在保持答案格式的同时提升结构困难题型的准确率。

Abstract: Reasoning about surgical video -- which phase is underway, which instruments act, and when events occur -- underpins operative documentation, workflow analysis, and skill assessment. Yet surgical visual question answering is still largely built on single frames and closed labels that cannot express a duration, an ordering, or a count, and the video models that lift the frame limit typically compress every clip the same way regardless of the question, discarding the temporal evidence the answer needs. Worse, the answers are structurally heterogeneous, spanning five incompatible structures -- binary, classification, multi-label, counting, and sentence-temporal -- that no single training signal scores uniformly well. We propose SurgQ-VLM, a two-stage model for surgical video question answering. Stage 1 trains a question-conditioned dual-stream (slow/fast) compressor for a LoRA-adapted Qwen2.5-VL-7B: cross-attention conditions both streams on the question, and each clip's visual tokens shrink 49x from the raw ViT patches (11x vs. post-merge backbone tokens). Stage 2 post-trains with GRPO under an answer-type-aware reward that scores each question on the metric matching its answer structure (micro-F1 for label sets, normalised error for counts, a content-timing blend for temporal windows). On a sealed 21,840-question test split over Cholec80 and CholecT50, SurgQ-VLM reaches 0.779 keyword accuracy and a 10x BLEU-4 gain over zero-shot; GRPO adds 2.5 pp K-Acc (2.0 pp attributable to the answer-type-aware reward), concentrated in the structurally hard types, with answer format unchanged (ROUGE-L). Code is available at https://github.com/DhruvMakwana/SurgQ-VLM.

</details>


### [192] [Vision-language classification of Torsades de Pointes risk from pseudo-coloured ECG images: do T-wave morphology and colour context help or hinder?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01921)
*Dario Labrador Alonso,Alaa Alahmadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在 BiomedCLIP 上将 ECG 渲染为彩色增强图像进行 TdP 风险分类，图像着色贡献显著提升（MCC +0.126），而提示中命名颜色无影响；提示集影响为双向但幅度极小，杠杆在于信号如何绘制而非如何描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文提示（contextual prompting）常被视为提升视觉模型性能的低成本途径，但现有证据多来自"提示中命名的属性同时存在于图像中"的基准测试，两种通道相互混淆，无法判断收益来源。作者希望在一个语义复杂的生物医学信号分类任务中，干净地分离"图像内容"与"提示文本"两条通道的贡献。

Method: 基于前期将 ECG 信号按 QT-nomogram 与伪彩色技术渲染为彩色增强图像以标记 Torsades de Pointes 风险的工作，使用领域适配的视觉-语言模型 BiomedCLIP。设计 4 种上下文策略 × 4 种 T 波形态标签集的交叉实验，在 9,789 张图像上采用五折分层交叉验证，分别评估零样本推理与类别平衡线性探针（linear probe）。

Result: 零样本推理不可用。经训练后，彩色增强图像在全部指标上优于无彩色图像：平衡准确率 0.903 vs 0.825，敏感度 0.914 vs 0.827，特异度 0.892 vs 0.823，ROC-AUC 0.955 vs 0.888，MCC 0.437 vs 0.311。交叉"图像中着色"与"提示中命名颜色"可解耦两通道：图像贡献恒为 +0.126 MCC（+0.078 平衡准确率、+0.067 ROC-AUC），与提示是否提及颜色无关，而提示命名颜色在任何指标下均无改变。上下文呈双向效应：最佳提示集在有颜色时增益 0.008 MCC、无颜色时 0.016；最差提示集分别损失 0.017 与 0.013。

Conclusion: 对于被渲染为图像的信号，性能杠杆在于信号如何被绘制（渲染方式），而非如何被文字描述（提示措辞）；且当端点为区间测量时，命名 T 波形态的提示所描述的是错误的目标量。

Abstract: Contextual prompting is widely presented as a cheap route to better performance from vision models, but the evidence comes largely from benchmarks in which an attribute named in a prompt is also present in the image, confounding the two channels. We investigate the significance of context for a semantically complex biomedical signal classification task using a domain-adapted vision-language model, BiomedCLIP. Building on our previous work that renders electrocardiogram (ECG) signals as colour-enhanced images to flag Torsades de Pointes risk using the QT-nomogram and pseudo-colouring technique, we cross four context strategies with four T-wave morphology label sets, evaluated zero-shot and with a class-balanced linear probe, under five-fold stratified cross-validation on 9,789 images. Zero-shot inference is unusable. Once trained, the colour-enhanced images outperform the colourless ones on every metric: balanced accuracy 0.903 versus 0.825, sensitivity 0.914 versus 0.827, specificity 0.892 versus 0.823, ROC-AUC 0.955 versus 0.888 and Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) 0.437 versus 0.311. Crossing colour-in-image with colour-in-prompt separates the confounded channels: the image contributes +0.126 MCC (+0.078 balanced accuracy, +0.067 ROC-AUC) whether or not the prompt names colour, whereas naming it changes nothing under any metric. Context is two-sided: the best prompt set gains 0.008 MCC where colour is present and 0.016 where it is not, while the worst loses 0.017 and 0.013. For a signal rendered as an image, the leverage lies in how the signal is drawn rather than in how it is described - and where the endpoint is an interval measurement, prompts naming T-wave morphology describe the wrong quantity.

</details>


### [193] [Spade-SLAM: Sparse Correspondence Anchors for Dense Self-Supervised Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01937)
*Julian Philipp Schmitt,Weirong Chen,Ganlin Zhang,Felix Wimbauer,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spade-SLAM：一种自监督稠密视觉SLAM框架，通过稀疏到稠密的互补训练策略，在零样本设定下大幅缩小与监督方法的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密视觉SLAM中性能领先的方法依赖显式位姿与深度真值标签进行全监督预训练，标注成本高；而自监督方法与之存在长期性能鸿沟。作者希望证明无需真值标签也能实现高精度跟踪，从而提供标签高效且有效的替代方案。

Method: 提出"稀疏到稠密"互补训练范式：以基于warping的光度损失提供稠密训练信号，同时引入由图像特征匹配导出的稀疏对应损失，用于稳定优化并抑制歧义区域的漂移。

Result: 大量实验表明，Spade-SLAM相较现有自监督基线在鲁棒性与精度上显著提升，性能接近使用显式位姿与深度监督训练的SLAM系统。

Conclusion: 自监督稠密SLAM可在零样本条件下逼近监督方法，验证了稀疏对应与稠密光度信号结合的标签高效训练路线的有效性；代码将开源。

Abstract: We introduce Spade-SLAM, a self-supervised dense visual SLAM framework that substantially narrows the long-standing performance gap to supervised methods in a zero-shot setting. By demonstrating that high-fidelity tracking can be achieved without explicit ground-truth pose and depth labels, our approach offers a highly effective and label-efficient alternative to fully supervised pretraining. Our core contribution is a complementary sparse-to-dense training regime: While a warping-based photometric loss provides a dense training signal, we stabilize the optimization and suppress drift in ambiguous regions using a sparse correspondence loss derived from image feature matches. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Spade-SLAM significantly improves robustness and accuracy compared to existing self-supervised baselines, closely rivaling SLAM systems trained with explicit pose and depth supervision. The code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/JulianPSchmitt/Spade-SLAM.

</details>


### [194] [VLSplat: Vision-Language Guided Object-Centric 3D Gaussian Splatting via Scene Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01939)
*Yuntae Jeon,Younho Jeon,Sujin Jin,Sungho Jo,Seunghee Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLSplat 提出用视觉-语言模型提供的对象级语义先验与对象组场景图，增强 3D Gaussian Splatting 中从 2D 掩码到 3D 的语义提升，改善对象级语义一致性与结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 2D→3D 语义提升方法主要依赖 2D 分割监督，缺乏对象级先验，导致学到的表示由局部掩码证据驱动，而非整体对象结构。

Method: 在基于掩码的提升基础上，引入 VLM 衍生的对象级语义先验，并借助对象组场景图对高斯表示进行细化；细化由两个语言引导模块完成：组内细化（intra-group refinement）抑制对象组内部语义噪声，组间获取（inter-group acquisition）从相邻组引入结构相关候选以扩展对象支撑区域。

Result: 在室内场景数据集上的大量实验表明，VLSplat 提升了对象级类别的语义一致性与结构完整性。

Conclusion: 对象级视觉-语言先验与场景图引导的组内/组间细化能有效弥补纯掩码监督的不足，为 3DGS 的对象中心语义提升提供可行方案；代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Learning semantic representations for 3D Gaussian Splatting has recently emerged as a promising direction for 3D scene understanding. However, existing 2D-to-3D semantic lifting approaches rely primarily on 2D segmentation supervision and lack object-level priors, causing the learned representation to be driven by local mask evidence rather than holistic object structure. In this paper, we present VLSplat, a vision-language guided approach for object-centric semantic lifting in 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our method augments mask-based lifting with object-level semantic priors derived from vision-language models and refines Gaussian representations using an object-group scene graph. The refinement is performed through two language-guided modules: intra-group refinement suppresses semantic noise within object groups, while inter-group acquisition expands object support regions by incorporating structurally relevant candidates from neighboring groups. Extensive experiments on indoor scene datasets demonstrate that VLSplat improves the semantic consistency and structural completeness of object-level categories. Our code and models are available at https://vlsplat.github.io/.

</details>


### [195] [Event-Based Optical Flow Constraint via Time-Sliced Plane Fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01960)
*Masaki Koike,Yusuke Kameda,Shunichi Sato,Takayuki Hamamoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出沿时间轴扩展平面近似的基于事件相机光流估计方法，通过时空子区间划分与两步平面近似建模时间运动变化，结合空间补偿与时间正则化提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于事件的平面近似光流估计隐含恒定速度假设，会将时间上的速度变化平均化，导致对真实运动变化的建模能力不足。

Method: 将平面近似沿时间轴扩展，构建可嵌入现有变分光流框架的事件光流约束；把时空体划分为若干子区间，施加两步平面近似以一致地跟踪目标边缘；并引入空间补偿机制补偿边缘位移、时间正则项稳定时序流估计。

Result: 实验表明，建模时间运动变化相比传统方法显著提升了光流估计精度。

Conclusion: 沿时间轴扩展平面近似可有效克服恒定速度假设的限制，在变分事件光流框架中实现对时间运动变化的建模，提升估计准确性。

Abstract: Event-based optical flow estimation using spatiotemporal planar approximation inherently assumes constant velocity and tends to average out temporal velocity variations. In this paper, we propose a flow estimation method that extends this planar approximation along the temporal axis. The resulting event-based optical flow constraint allows existing variational optical flow formulations to model temporal motion variation. Specifically, we partition the space-time volume into sub-intervals and apply a two-step planar approximation to track object edges consistently. Moreover, a spatial compensation mechanism and a temporal regularization term are incorporated to compensate for edge displacements and temporally stabilize the flow estimation, respectively. The experimental results show that modeling temporal motion variation improves flow estimation accuracy over conventional methods.

</details>


### [196] [Object-Only Is Not Context-Free: A Counterfactual Diagnostic for Context Removal in Object Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01962)
*Boaz Meivar,Shai Avidan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Keyhole Aperture Probe (KAP) 反事实诊断，发现"仅目标"的池化描述子仍强烈依赖背景上下文，且该依赖可通过轻量适配器降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标中心检索流程通常先用掩码屏蔽背景再池化骨干特征，但"仅目标描述子"是否真正脱离场景上下文鲜有检验，缺乏可量化的诊断手段。

Method: KAP 复用 COCO、ADE20K 全景分割的真值掩码：固定池化的目标区域，仅改变其外部像素（中性填充或其他真实场景），以单一数值刻画骨干的上下文依赖；在十个现代骨干上评估，并提出轻量适配器促使描述子在上下文移除时保持稳定，同时设置匹配的对比式微调对照。

Result: 池化目标区域已整合了周边信息；替换上下文会改变描述子，空白填充下检索失败时描述子更接近纯背景图像而非目标本身；十个骨干上仅目标描述子仅保留全图精度的约 1/4 至 2/3，卷积骨干同样存在该效应；适配器降低了依赖性并提升 INSTRE 全图实例检索，迁移至 ILIAS 精度提升或保持，对比式微调对照未能复现该增益。

Conclusion: "仅目标"并不等于"无上下文"；KAP 作为与骨干无关的简单评测协议，可在部署前度量并降低该依赖。

Abstract: Object-centric retrieval pipelines often mask the background before pooling backbone features, aiming for a descriptor that reflects the object rather than its scene. Whether this truly yields an object-only descriptor, however, is rarely tested. We introduce the Keyhole Aperture Probe (KAP), a counterfactual diagnostic that repurposes the ground-truth masks of existing panoptic segmentation datasets (COCO, ADE20K) for retrieval: it holds the pooled object region fixed and varies only the pixels outside it, summarizing a backbone's context dependence in a single number. We find that the pooled object region has already integrated its surround. Replacing the context, with a neutral fill or even a different real scene, changes the descriptor, and when retrieval fails under a blank fill, the descriptor matches plain-background images rather than its own object. Object-only is not context-free. Across ten modern backbones, object-only descriptors retain only about a quarter to two-thirds of their full-image accuracy, and the effect is not unique to Transformer self-attention: a convolutional backbone shows it too. The dependence is also reducible: a lightweight adapter that encourages the descriptor to stay stable as context is removed lowers it and improves full-image instance retrieval on INSTRE, transferring to ILIAS with accuracy improved or preserved. A matched contrastive-fine-tuning control does not reproduce those gains. We release KAP as a simple, backbone-agnostic evaluation protocol to measure and reduce this dependence before deployment.

</details>


### [197] [Knowledge-Guided Floodwater Level Reasoning for Cloud-Edge Collaborative Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01970)
*Zhengshu Zhou,Shaoyu Wang,Ziyi Geng,Lu Tao,Qian Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HydroReasoner，将积水水位检测重构为显式视觉推理任务，基于Qwen2.5-VL与GRPO训练，用知识图谱路径奖励监督推理过程，结合4-bit量化与置信度云边路由实现部署，并构建HydroLevel数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市积水威胁智能交通系统安全与效率；现有视觉方法缺乏显式推理，性能与可解释性差，且难以泛化到不同视角的复杂真实道路场景。

Method: 1）把任务重述为显式视觉推理问题；2）以Qwen2.5-VL为基座，采用Group Relative Policy Optimization训练；3）构建积水领域知识图谱HydroKG，设计KG路径奖励，通过确定性图遍历对推理过程做细粒度监督；4）边缘侧4-bit量化；5）提出基于置信度的云边路由机制；6）构建3,000张图像的HydroLevel基准数据集。

Result: 量化后GPU显存降57%、推理时延降43%，域外精度仅降0.2个百分点；域内与域外检测精度分别较传统视觉基线高4.0与13.6个百分点，域外性能仅下降4.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式推理+知识图谱路径奖励可同时提升精度、可解释性与跨场景泛化；量化与置信度云边路由在保证安全性的同时降低云侧算力负担，方案适用于云边协同的智能交通积水检测部署。

Abstract: Frequent urban floodwater poses a serious threat to the safety and efficiency of intelligent transportation systems. Existing vision-based methods for floodwater level detection face two main limitations. First, the lack of explicit reasoning leads to poor detection performance and little interpretability. Second, these methods struggle to generalize to the complex and varied real-world road scenes seen from different viewpoints. To address this, we reformulate the task as an explicit visual reasoning problem and propose HydroReasoner, a floodwater level reasoning framework designed for cloud-edge collaboration. HydroReasoner builds on Qwen2.5-VL and is trained with Group Relative Policy Optimization. To address the scarcity of reasoning process annotations, we build a floodwater domain knowledge graph HydroKG and design a novel KG path reward. Through deterministic graph traversal over HydroKG, this reward supervises the model's reasoning process at a fine granularity, which improves both reasoning and generalization. For edge deployment, we apply 4-bit quantization to the model, cutting GPU memory usage by 57% and inference latency by 43% while out-of-domain accuracy drops by only 0.2 percentage points. We also propose a confidence-based cloud-edge routing mechanism, which improves system safety and eases the computational load on the cloud. In addition, we build HydroLevel, a floodwater benchmark dataset of 3,000 images, for training and evaluating the model. Experimental results show that HydroReasoner achieves detection accuracy 4.0 and 13.6 percentage points higher than conventional vision baselines in the in-domain and out-of-domain evaluations respectively, with performance dropping by only 4.4 percentage points out of domain.

</details>


### [198] [MUPA${}^2$S: Multimodal Unified Perception with Asymmetric Attention for Stress Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01974)
*Stefanos Gkikas,Thomas Kassiotis,Yu Fang,Christian Arzate Cruz,Miao Cheng,Zongyi Jiang,Zacharias Kamarianakis,Houshyar Asadi,Randy Gomez,Raul Fernandez Rojas,Giorgos Giannakakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MUPA²S多模态压力评估框架，将面部视频与ECG映射到统一token表示，用分段局部交叉注意力与全局自注意力处理，在58人数据集上融合精度69.58%，参数约为特征级融合的一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压力是长期身心健康下降的主要诱因，自动压力评估有助于持续监测并减少人工偏差；但生理与行为信号异质性强，制约自动化系统开发。

Method: 构建统一感知架构：两模态映射为无模态专用组件的共享token表示，经非对称注意力（分段内局部交叉注意力＋跨分段状态的全局自注意力）处理；在新建58被试压力数据集上采用分层被试级协议评估，并与仅视频、仅ECG、特征级融合基线对比。

Result: 面部视频测试精度69.44%；仅ECG在所提表示下接近随机水平；统一视频-ECG融合达69.58%，参数约为特征级融合的一半，额外计算开销可忽略；特征级融合未超过仅视频基线。

Conclusion: 统一输入级整合是参数高效地联合处理两模态的机制，优于特征级融合；ECG在该表示下贡献有限。

Abstract: Stress is a leading contributor to long-term physical and mental health decline. The automatic assessment of stress is a promising approach for continuous monitoring and mitigating human biases. However, the development of automated systems remains challenging due to the heterogeneity of physiological and behavioral signals. This paper presents MUPA${}^2$S, a multimodal framework for automatic stress assessment that combines facial video and ECG signals within a unified perception architecture. Both modalities are mapped into a shared token representation without modality-specific components, and processed through asymmetric attention comprising segment-local cross-attention and global self-attention across segment states. Experiments on a newly collected 58-subject stress dataset, evaluated under a stratified subject-level protocol, show that facial video achieves 69.44% test accuracy, while ECG-only performance remains close to chance under the proposed representation. Unified Video-ECG fusion achieves a test accuracy of 69.58% at approximately half the number of parameters of feature-level fusion, with negligible additional computational cost. Feature-level fusion does not exceed the video-only baseline, while unified input-level integration provides a parameter-efficient mechanism for jointly processing the two modalities.

</details>


### [199] [Multimodal BVP and Respiration for Automated Pain Recognition with Hankel-FCN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01978)
*Valentina Becchetti,Muhammad Umar Khan,Stefanos Gkikas,Alessandro Giuseppi,Raul Fernandez Rojas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于BVP与RESP多模态生理信号的深度学习疼痛评估框架，用Hankel矩阵嵌入显式建模时间动态，FCN提取模态特征后晚期融合实现三分类，准确率67.09%、F1为66.41%，无需手工特征工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疼痛具有主观性与动态性，自动疼痛识别对临床护理与人机共情交互至关重要；现有方法多依赖手工特征工程，且未充分刻画生理信号的时序动态特性。

Method: 在AI4Pain数据集上，选取血容量脉冲（BVP）与呼吸（RESP）两种互补自主神经响应信号；将其转换为Hankel矩阵嵌入的结构化轨迹表示以学习动力学模式；各模态分别由全卷积网络（FCN）处理，采用晚期融合策略完成无痛/低痛/高痛三分类。

Result: 准确率67.09%、F1分数66.41%，与现有方法相比具有竞争力；多模态融合显著优于单模态，Hankel嵌入呈现进一步提升趋势。

Conclusion: Hankel嵌入+多模态FCN晚期融合可在无手工特征工程条件下实现有效疼痛分级，但泛化至更广泛人群与临床场景仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Automated pain recognition is a fundamental challenge for the advancement of clinical care and empathetic human-computer interaction, due to the subjective and dynamic nature of pain. In this study, we propose a deep learning framework for objective pain assessment based on multimodal physiological signals from the AI4Pain dataset. The approach focuses on Blood Volume Pulse (BVP) and Respiration (RESP), capturing complementary aspects of autonomic responses to nociceptive stimulation. To explicitly model their temporal dynamics, the signals are transformed into structured trajectory representations using Hankel matrix embeddings, enabling the learning of dynamical patterns beyond conventional time-series representations. These embeddings are processed by modality-specific Fully Convolutional Networks, and their outputs are integrated through a late fusion strategy for three-class classification (No Pain, Low Pain, High Pain). Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves an accuracy of 67.09% and an F1-score of 66.41%, providing competitive performance compared to existing approaches, while relying on no handcrafted feature engineering. Multimodal fusion significantly outperformed either modality alone, and the Hankel embedding showed a trend towards further improvement. Further validation is required to establish generalisability to broader populations and clinical settings.

</details>


### [200] [SurgStream: A Workflow-Anchored Proactive Streaming Framework and Benchmark for Real-Time Surgical Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01982)
*Ruipeng Ma,Qing Xu,Jie Liang,Zhen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SurgStream，一个以工作流为锚点的主动式流式手术视频理解框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术视频理解对术中辅助（工作流监测、器械准备、过程记录）至关重要，实时辅助要求模型同时感知当前场景、记忆既往手术事件并在临床关键时机前响应；现有方法难以同时保留过程证据并决定何时响应。

Method: 1) Workflow-Anchored Memory（WAM）：记录结构化的器械-动作-目标观察，并折叠为阶段级摘要，形成紧凑可查询的手术记忆；2) Deadline-aware Transition Learning（DTL）：在临床时间窗内监督响应状态，实现截止时刻前的及时响应；3) 构建 SurgStream-Bench 基准。

Result: SurgStream-Bench 包含 11.7K 流式交互，覆盖实时感知、回溯记忆查询、前向主动问答、主动告警与字幕记忆等任务；实验表明该方法优于现有SOTA，尤其在记忆与前向主动问答方面提升明显。

Conclusion: 工作流锚定记忆与截止感知的转移学习可有效支撑流式手术视频中的记忆保留与主动响应，代码将开源。

Abstract: Surgical video understanding is essential for intraoperative assistance such as workflow monitoring, instrument preparation and procedural documentation. Delivering such assistance in real time requires a model to jointly perceive the ongoing scene, remember past surgical events, and respond before clinically relevant moments pass. Existing approaches provide limited support for jointly retaining procedural evidence and deciding when to respond in streaming surgical video. In this work, we propose SurgStream, a workflow-anchored proactive streaming framework for real-time surgical video understanding. We first introduce Workflow-Anchored Memory (WAM) to record structured instrument-action-target observations and fold them into phase-level summaries, providing compact and queryable surgical memory. Deadline-aware Transition Learning (DTL) is then devised to supervise response states within clinical windows, enabling timely responses before procedural deadlines. We further introduce SurgStream-Bench, comprising 11.7K streaming interactions across various tasks covering real-time perception, backward memory queries, forward proactive QA, proactive alerts and caption memory. Experiments on SurgStream-Bench show gains over state-of-the-art methods, especially in memory and forward proactive QA. The source code will be available.

</details>


### [201] [Synthetic Realities: Evaluating Human Ability to Distinguish AIGenerated Videos from Real Footage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01990)
*Danyal Sarfraz,Raja Mubashar Karim,KwanMyung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过87名参与者的二选一强制判断任务，比较了文本生成视频、图生视频动画和视频外扩三种AI视频生成方式在历史素材、影视内容与自然环境三类主题下的可检测性，发现平均正确率为60%，且检测准确率显著依赖生成技术与主题及其交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已能通过文本生成视频、图生视频和视频外扩等多种途径合成高保真视频，但普通未受训观众如何感知并区分这些AI输出与真实影像的系统性对比评估仍然匮乏，因此需要量化不同生成技术与内容主题对检测难度的影响。

Method: 构建由真实与AI生成视频各18段组成的平衡刺激集，AI片段在三种生成方式间均匀分配（使用Google Veo 3、Lightricks LTX Video、Wan VACE），并在ComfyUI框架内统一制作与标准化以保证质量与时长一致；87名参与者在二选一强制判断任务中对每段视频判定"真实"或"AI生成"，并采用逻辑回归分析技术、主题及其交互效应。

Result: 参与者平均正确识别率为60%。按生成方式，图生视频识别准确率最高（79%），其次为真实素材（64%）、视频外扩（49%）、文本生成视频（43%）；按主题，影视与历史场景的检测率高于环境类片段，环境片段常被误判为AI。逻辑回归显示生成技术与主题的主效应及二者交互作用均显著（p < 0.001）。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了观众倾向于假定视频为合成来源的偏差，并表明AI视频的感知真实感更多由内容语境而非模型类型决定，凸显了媒介素养教育及情境感知评估工具在应对日益合成化视觉媒介中的重要性。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI have enabled multiple pathways for high-fidelity video synthesis: text-to-video, image-to-video animation, and video outpainting. Empirical side-by-side evaluations of how untrained viewers perceive and distinguish these outputs from real footage remain scarce. In this study, we systematically compare human detection accuracy across these three AI generation techniques within three thematic contexts: historical footage, film and media content, and natural environments. We constructed a balanced stimulus set comprising equal amounts of real and AI-generated videos (18 each). The AI clips were evenly distributed across the three generation methods using Google's Veo 3, Lightricks LTX Video, and Wan VACE. All videos were produced and standardized within the ComfyUI framework to ensure consistent quality and duration. 87 participants judged every clip in a binary forced-choice task ("real" vs. "AI-generated"). Participants correctly identified videos 60% of the time on average. Image-to-video clips were recognized most accurately (79%), followed by real footage (64%), outpainting (49%), and text-to-video (43%). Accuracy also varied by theme: film and historical scenes yielded higher detection rates than environmental clips, which were frequently mistaken for AI. Logistic regression confirmed significant effects of both technique and theme as well as their interaction (p < 0.001), indicating that detection success depends jointly on how the content was generated and what it depicts. Findings reveal a consistent bias toward assuming synthetic origins and highlight that perceptual realism in AI video is shaped more by context than by model type, underscoring the importance of media-literacy approaches and context-aware evaluation tools for navigating increasingly synthetic visual media.

</details>


### [202] [NAS-PO: Native Attention-Strategy Policy Optimization for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02008)
*Ke An,Shizhe Xiang,Yue Liu,Liang Chen,Pei Fu,Shaojie Zhang,Xiaoxing Hu,Yupei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NAS-PO框架，用模型原生注意力作为奖励锚定的token级路由信号来塑造VLM的视觉感知，在7个OOD基准上超越主流RL微调VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR对所有权重相同的outcome credit，忽视token的感知相关性；现有感知感知方法依赖图像扰动/掩码等间接代理，需额外扰动分支，且损坏敏感性未必能识别真正用于推理的视觉证据。

Method: 以原生注意力为内在信号，构建Native Attention-Strategy Policy Optimization：包括visual mass policy（量化视觉分配）与conditional visual policy（刻画视觉token上的空间路由）；NAS-AS依据visual-mass policy对视觉参与型rollout重新分配带符号的策略梯度credit；AAC损失在所有响应token上对齐visual-mass策略、在视觉活跃token上对齐conditional visual策略。

Result: 在7个域外推理与感知基准上显著优于领先的RL调优VLM，8B与32B规模一致有效；Visual-Routing Sensitivity分析显示优化后的注意力更集中于视觉承载关键区域。

Conclusion: 将注意力从事后解释转为奖励锚定的token对齐路由信号，可有效增强VLM视觉grounded推理，无需扰动分支，且具备跨规模可扩展性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become an effective post-training paradigm for vision-language models (VLMs). Standard RLVR, however, assigns identical outcome-derived credit to all generated tokens regardless of their perceptual relevance. Recent perception-aware methods estimate visual reliance through image perturbation or masking. These indirect proxies require perturbation branches, while corruption sensitivity does not necessarily identify the visual evidence used for reasoning. We instead shape model perception through native attention, an intrinsic model signal. Rather than treating attention as a post-hoc explanation, we formulate it as a reward-anchored, token-aligned routing signal that encourages attention patterns supporting visually grounded reasoning. To this end, we propose NAS-PO, a Native Attention-Strategy Policy Optimization framework. NAS-PO analyzes native attention with a visual mass policy that quantifies visual allocation and a conditional visual policy that captures spatial routing over visual tokens, making the optimization well-posed. Based on this, Native Attention-Strategy Advantage Shaping (NAS-AS) draws on the visual-mass policy to redistribute signed policy-gradient credit for visually engaged rollouts. The Adaptive Attention-Coupled (AAC) loss then aligns the visual-mass policy on all response tokens and the conditional visual policy on visually active ones, consolidating the visual-routing patterns that drive successful trajectories. Experiments demonstrate that NAS-PO substantially surpasses leading RL-tuned VLMs across 7 out-of-domain reasoning and perception benchmarks, with its effectiveness consistently validated across 8B and 32B. Visual-Routing Sensitivity (VRS) analysis further indicates that the optimized attention prioritizes visually load-bearing regions.

</details>


### [203] [Reconstruction as Spatial Evaluation: Diagnosing Cross-View Spatial Intelligence in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02026)
*Junfei Wu,Qiuyue Wang,Xuhong Huang,Qihang Peng,Mingsheng Li,Jian Guan,Qiang Liu,Shu Wu,Liang Wang,Shuai Bai,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RECONSPATIAL 基准，以多视角 3D 场景重建为核心任务，评估大视觉语言模型（LVLM）的空间智能，揭示"相对结构准确但度量精度不足"的系统性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间智能评测存在裂缝：静态基准只测孤立的空间判断（相对方向、距离），而交互式具身评测又把感知与探索、规划、控制、模拟器执行混在一起，无法单独、规范化地度量"把局部视觉观测整合为几何一致世界表征"这一核心能力。
