Method: 构建 RECONSPATIAL 基准：覆盖室内、桌面、室外三类环境，输入真实多视角图像与目标物体查询，要求 LVLM 在统一的、以相机为锚的度量坐标系中联合预测带朝向的 3D 包围盒。评测分两轴：Structure（相对布局）与 Metric（物理尺度与相机锚定定位）。设计 9 个原子空间 QA 任务，分为 Geometric Perception 与 Spatial Relations 两组；并以离线导航（路径规划）扩展评测。另做诊断分析（原子能力与重建质量的关联）、3D 地图注入实验（分离场景构建与路径规划），以及空间微调实验。

Result: 评测 18 个前沿 LVLM，普遍存在相对结构与度量重建之间的稳定落差，即使 GPT-6-Astra 也仅得 Structure 81.4%、Metric 58.7%。诊断分析表明关系一致性并不保证度量准确；3D 地图注入实验显示几何误差如何限制导航成功率；空间微调在空间与通用视觉语言基准上均有广泛迁移。

Conclusion: 联合多视角 3D 重建是弥合"静态空间判断"与"具身交互"之间评测空白的有力探针；当前 LVLM 的空间智能瓶颈主要在度量层面而非关系层面，且可通过空间微调实现跨基准迁移。

Abstract: The spatial intelligence of large vision-language models (LVLMs) requires the ability to combine local visual observations into a geometrically consistent representation of the physical world. Many static benchmarks assess isolated spatial judgments, such as relative direction or distance, while interactive embodied evaluations combine perception with exploration, planning, control, and simulator-specific execution. To bridge this gap, we introduce RECONSPATIAL, a benchmark centered on multi-view 3D scene reconstruction across indoor, tabletop, and outdoor environments. Given real multi-view images and target-object queries, LVLMs are required to jointly predict oriented 3D bounding boxes in a shared, camera-anchored metric coordinate frame. Reconstructions are evaluated along two complementary axes: Structure for relative layout and Metric for physical scale and camera-anchored placement. Nine atomic spatial QA tasks, grouped into Geometric Perception and Spatial Relations, probe spatial capabilities involved in joint reconstruction. Offline navigation extends evaluation to path planning. Evaluation of 18 frontier LVLMs reveals a consistent gap between relative scene structure and metric reconstruction; even GPT-6-Astra scores 81.4% on Structure but only 58.7% on Metric. Tracing this gap, diagnostic analyses link atomic spatial capabilities to reconstruction quality and show that relational consistency alone does not ensure metric accuracy. Experiments with 3D map injection further separate scene construction from path planning, revealing how these geometric errors limit navigation success. Finally, spatial fine-tuning yields broad transfer across both spatial and general vision-language benchmarks.

</details>


### [204] [4D Triangle Splatting Reconstruction of Dynamic Endoscopic Scenes from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02034)
*Laura Salort-Benejam,Stamatia Giannarou,Antonio Agudo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SurgicalTS，一种自监督三角面片溅射（triangle-splatting）框架，从静态单目相机视频重建动态内窥镜场景的4D表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目内窥镜视频的变形场景4D重建受非刚性组织形变、光照变化、遮挡与未知相机轨迹影响而困难；可靠的动态重建可提升可视化、诊断精度与微创手术术中导航。

Method: 以规范化三角面片集合与形变网络联合优化表示场景；引入基于四元数的形变建模、改进的颜色参数化、时空先验，以及重新定义的初始化策略以提升遮挡下的完整性。

Result: 在多种手术的在体（in-vivo）内窥镜视频上，成功建模软组织形变与器械-组织交互，持续优于现有最优方法；在保持有竞争力的几何与光度精度的同时缩短训练时间。

Conclusion: 四元数形变建模结合物理一致性，可实现对内窥镜场景稳健且高效的4D重建。

Abstract: Robust 4D reconstruction of deformable endoscopic scenes from monocular videos remains challenging due to non-rigid tissue deformation, illumination changes, occlusions, and unknown camera trajectories. Reliable dynamic reconstruction of endoscopies could significantly enhance visualization, diagnostic accuracy and intraoperative guidance of minimally invasive procedures. We propose SurgicalTS, a self-supervised triangle-splatting framework for dynamic endoscopic scene reconstruction from static monocular cameras. The scene is represented by a canonical set of triangles jointly optimized with a deformation network. We introduce a quaternion-based deformation model, an improved color parametrization, spatio-temporal priors, and a redefined initialization strategy that improves completeness under occlusions. Extensive evaluation on challenging in-vivo endoscopy videos from diverse procedures shows that SurgicalTS successfully models soft-tissue deformations and tool-tissue interactions over time, while consistently outperforming state-of-the-art methods. Our method also reduces training times while maintaining competitive geometric and photometric accuracy results, highlighting the effectiveness of quaternion-based deformation modelling with physical consistency for robust and efficient 4D reconstruction of endoscopic scenes.

</details>


### [205] [Unified Loss-Aware Density Control for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02044)
*Tae-Young Kim,Jiwoo Chung,Jae-Pil Heo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将3D高斯泼溅的自适应密度控制重构为局部损失下降决策，用Taylor近似的逐高斯代理量预测分裂、克隆、透明度衰减三种操作的损失变化，实现统一的损失感知框架，在标准基准上以相当或更少的高斯数量取得更优渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3DGS密度控制依赖启发式视图空间梯度、尺度阈值和全局透明度重置，未显式评估每次分裂、克隆或透明度更新对损失的降低效果，导致基元被分配到已充分重建区域、遗漏欠重建结构，并因全局透明度重置破坏训练稳定性。

Method: 将自适应密度控制建模为一组局部损失下降决策，推导高效的Taylor逐高斯基元代理量以预测三种操作引起的目标变化：(1) 把二阶分裂扩展到归一化联合位置-尺度空间，使子节点同时适配位置与各向异性尺度；(2) 将重复克隆表述为代理最小化问题，仅选择复制贡献可预测改善重建的高斯，并给子节点赋予有原则的透明度；(3) 以选择性软透明度衰减替代全局重置。

Result: 在标准3DGS基准上，该方法产生更有针对性的基元分配，与基线相比在相当或更小的高斯预算下获得更好的渲染质量。

Conclusion: 显式的损失感知代理量为密度控制提供了统一且更合理的决策依据，可在控制基元预算的同时提升重建保真度与训练稳定性。

Abstract: The efficiency and fidelity of 3D Gaussian Splatting critically depend on adaptive density control, which grows and suppresses Gaussian primitives throughout optimization. However, the standard pipeline still relies on heuristic view-space gradients, scale thresholds, and global opacity resets, rather than explicitly assessing the loss-reducing effect of each split, clone, or opacity update. This mismatch can allocate primitives to already-sufficient regions, miss under-reconstructed structures, and destabilize training through blanket opacity changes. In this work, we recast adaptive density control as a collection of local loss-reduction decisions. We derive efficient Taylor-based per-Gaussian surrogates that predict the objective change caused by three primitive-level operations: splitting, cloning, and opacity decay. This yields a unified loss-aware framework with three components. First, we extend second-order splitting to a normalized joint position-scale space, enabling children to adapt not only their locations but also their anisotropic extents. Second, we formulate duplicate cloning as a surrogate minimization problem, selecting only Gaussians whose duplicated contribution is predicted to improve reconstruction and assigning each child a principled opacity. Third, we replace global opacity reset with selective soft opacity decay that weakens harmful primitives while preserving useful ones. Across standard 3DGS benchmarks, our method produces more targeted primitive allocation and achieves improved rendering quality with comparable or smaller Gaussian budgets compared to baselines.

</details>


### [206] [UniGS-SLAM: Monocular Gaussian SLAM with Enhanced Geometry and Open-Vocabulary Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02051)
*Xingyuan Ding,Sicheng Yu,Kaiyong Zhao,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniGS-SLAM 是一个仅用 RGB 输入的开放词汇语义 SLAM 框架，将语义与高斯重建解耦并用稀疏体素地图承载语义，同时保留双向交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯（Gaussian）的语义 SLAM 将语义标签/特征绑定在不断演化的高斯基元上，语义融合难以维护且重建开销大；混合高斯—体素表示虽语义支撑更稳定，但多依赖 RGB-D 输入或已知位姿，且几何更新后不回修历史语义证据。

Method: ① 利用基础模型先验增强单目深度与位姿估计；② 将语义观测融合进带"观测溯源（observation provenance）"的独立稀疏体素地图，使几何演化时可回修历史语义贡献；③ 融合语义反向引导高斯滤波与局部表面细化，形成双向交互。

Result: 在 Replica、ScanNet、KITTI-360 上，跟踪、渲染与几何重建性能在代表性 RGB-only 方法中领先；在 Replica 与 ScanNet 上具备较强的开放集语义建图能力；相比最快的对比语义 SLAM 基线实现 2.37 倍重建加速。

Conclusion: 语义与高斯重建的解耦加稀疏体素溯源融合，可在无 RGB-D、无已知位姿条件下兼顾开放词汇语义建图、重建质量与效率。

Abstract: Monocular semantic SLAM aims to reconstruct scene geometry and build semantic maps from RGB observations. Existing Gaussian-based methods often attach semantic labels or features to evolving Gaussian primitives, making semantic fusion difficult to maintain and increasing reconstruction overhead. Hybrid Gaussian--voxel representations provide more stable semantic support, but often rely on RGB-D input or known poses and do not revise historical semantic evidence after geometry updates. We present UniGS-SLAM, an RGB-only framework that decouples open-vocabulary semantics from Gaussian reconstruction while preserving bidirectional interaction. Foundation priors enhance monocular depth and pose estimation, while semantic observations are fused into an independent sparse voxel map with observation provenance, enabling historical contributions to be revised as reconstruction evolves. The fused semantics further guide Gaussian filtering and local surface refinement. Experiments on Replica, ScanNet, and KITTI-360 show leading tracking, rendering, and geometric reconstruction performance among representative RGB-only methods, together with strong open-set semantic mapping on Replica and ScanNet. We also achieve a 2.37-fold reconstruction speedup over the fastest compared semantic SLAM baseline. Project page: https://3dagentworld.github.io/UniGS-SLAM/.

</details>


### [207] [Learning to Look: A Foundation for Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02053)
*Wenshuai Zhao,Yihao Wang,Benjamin Robson,Juho Kannala,Arno Solin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 LOOK 任务，考察 VLM 在理解图像语义之前是否"知道"自己看到了哪里，即定位其实际可见的视觉输入区域；该任务以自监督方式构建，实验表明现有 VLM 无法完成可见区域定位，而针对 LOOK 微调可增强模型的视觉输入感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 存在视觉幻觉（如描述图中不存在的物体）。作者提出更根本的问题：模型在理解语义之前，是否具备对自身视觉输入范围的感知意识。

Method: 将问题形式化为 LOOK 任务：识别视觉输入中哪些部分对模型是可见的。该任务通过自监督方式构建，无需人工标注可见区域。

Result: 现有 VLM 均无法准确定位可见区域，说明其缺乏对视觉输入的感知。

Conclusion: 在 LOOK 任务上微调能够提升模型对视觉输入的感知能力，表明该任务可作为增强 VLM 视觉感知的有效手段。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) suffer from visual hallucination, such as saying an object that is not present in an image. We ask a more fundamental question: whether a VLM is even aware of where it is looking before understanding the semantic meaning in the image. We formulate this question as a LOOK task: identifying which parts of its visual input were available to the model. We construct the task in a self-supervised way without manually labeling the available region to the model. Our experiments show that existing VLMs fail to localize the visible region. We further show that fine-tuning VLMs on the proposed LOOK task improves a model's awareness of visual input.

</details>


### [208] [Scene-Adaptive Readout and Decoding in Diffusion Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02076)
*Seungjun Yu,Jiho Choi,Inha Kang,Hojun Choi,Seungwook Kim,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出场景自适应读出与解码框架SARD，利用"深度-解码解耦"现象，用场景条件读出MoE在单次主干前向中选择并加权晚层轨迹预测，仅在需要时进行额外原生去噪，实现精度提升与最高6.0倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式驾驶VLA在每个去噪阶段都重复执行主干网络，推理开销大；而所有场景是否需要相同解码计算并不清楚，存在计算冗余与场景需求异质性之间的矛盾。

Method: 分析轨迹读出在网络深度与解码进度上的表现，发现晚层表征在首次前向后即可支持有效读出、早层表征更受益于持续解码，且偏好读出层与解码深度随场景变化。据此设计两组件：场景条件读出MoE，对单次主干扫描后各晚层的层特定轨迹预测进行自适应加权组合；自适应解码策略，仅当场景需要时施加额外原生精化。

Result: 在两个扩散驾驶VLA上、于Waymo E2E与nuScenes基准评测：验证集RFS最高提升0.56，测试性能亦提升；Waymo E2E上端到端加速最高6.0倍；nuScenes上轨迹误差持续下降。

Conclusion: 场景级的读出层与解码深度分配可在避免不必要解码的同时提升规划质量，表明按场景自适应分配计算是扩散VLA高效推理的有效途径。

Abstract: Diffusion driving vision-language-action models repeatedly execute the backbone at every denoising stage, incurring substantial inference cost, while it remains unclear whether all scenes require the same decoding computation. To distinguish when a trajectory becomes readable from how much further refinement it requires, we analyze trajectory readout across network depth and decoding progress. We identify a depth-decoding dissociation in which late-layer representations already support useful trajectory readout after the first forward sweep, whereas early-layer representations benefit more from continued decoding. We further find that both the preferred readout layer and decoding depth vary across scenes. Based on these observations, we propose a scene-adaptive readout and decoding framework. A scene-conditioned readout MoE adaptively weights and combines trajectory predictions from layer-specific late-layer representations after a single backbone sweep, while an adaptive decoding policy applies additional native refinement only when required by the scene. We evaluate our framework on two diffusion driving VLAs across the Waymo E2E and nuScenes benchmarks. Our method improves validation RFS by up to 0.56 while also improving test performance, and achieves an end-to-end speedup of up to $6.0\times$ on Waymo E2E, while consistently reducing trajectory error on nuScenes. These results demonstrate that scene-level readout and decoding allocation improves planning quality while avoiding unnecessary decoding. Our code is available at https://github.com/sjyu001/SARD

</details>


### [209] [EarthJev: Calibrated, Non-Generative Answers to Natural-Language Questions about Sentinel-2 Land Cover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02096)
*Domenico Benfenati,Antonio Maria Rinaldi,Cristian Tommasino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EarthJev：0.8B 参数、无需生成任何 token 的多光谱遥感答题模型，19.9 ms 答一题，36,028 题上达 86.0% 准确率并具良好校准概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感视觉语言模型通过逐 token 生成文本回答问题，每题都要一个解码循环，答案需再解析回选项，且无可用的概率输出，推理成本高、格式不可靠、缺乏不确定性度量。

Method: 将 Qwen3.5-0.8B 视觉编码器输入层拓宽，由 RGB 卷积核初始化以接入 Sentinel-2 全部 12 个波段；按 OneJev 开放配方，在单一位置上读取选项字母的 logits 作为答案，用交叉熵加 Brier 分数训练。与同骨干生成式微调模型及三个 LoRA 微调的多模态语言模型在 70,890 个问题（BigEarthNet v2.0 的 5%）上同卡同任务计时对比，并以固定答案词表的 VQA 模型作参照。

Result: 单题 19.9 ms，比多模态语言模型快 4.3–8.7 倍，比自身骨干生成式快 6.8 倍；整图全部问题批量回答 42.8 ms，快 3.7–10.1 倍。测试集 36,028 题上三种子平均 86.0%，低于两个 2B 模型 0.6 与 1.2 个点、低于 EarthDial-4B 3.5 个点；答案天然合法，ECE 为 0.006。九条非 RGB 波段以均值替代损失 4.5 个点，说明模型确实利用了多光谱信息；消融中训练整个视觉编码器可达 88.4% 且成本不变。

Conclusion: 以非生成式选项 logits 读取取代文本解码，可在显著降低延迟的同时保持接近更大模型的准确率，并获得可直接使用的校准概率；多光谱输入有效，且全编码器微调可进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models for remote sensing answer questions about satellite images by generating text: every answer costs a decoding loop, has to be parsed back to an option, and comes without a usable probability. We present EarthJev, a 0.8B-parameter model that answers typed questions about multispectral Sentinel-2 patches without generating any token. All 12 bands enter the vision encoder of Qwen3.5-0.8B through a widened input layer initialised from the RGB kernels, and, following the open OneJev recipe, the answer is read from the logits of the option letters at a single position and trained with cross-entropy plus Brier score. Under a pre-specified analysis plan, we compare it with the same backbone fine-tuned to generate the answer and with three multimodal language models fine-tuned with LoRA, all trained on the same 70,890 questions from 5% of BigEarthNet v2.0 and timed in one job on the same GPU; VQA models that classify over a fixed answer vocabulary are reported as a reference for the cost of answering only the questions seen in training. EarthJev answers a question in 19.9 ms, 4.3 to 8.7 times faster than the multimodal language models and 6.8 times faster than its own backbone used generatively; when all the questions about an image are asked together, it answers them in 42.8 ms, 3.7 to 10.1 times faster than the batched generative models. On 36,028 test questions it reaches 86.0% accuracy (three seeds), 0.6 and 1.2 points below the two 2B multimodal models and 3.5 points below the remote-sensing EarthDial-4B, with answers that are valid by construction and calibrated probabilities (ECE 0.006). Replacing the nine non-RGB bands with their mean costs 4.5 points, so the model uses the multispectral input, and in an ablation training the whole vision encoder raises accuracy to 88.4% at the same cost. Code and model: https://github.com/CosmoIknosLab/EarthJev

</details>


### [210] [CNN-Based Retinal OCT Classification: Research Gap Analysis and a From-Scratch Baseline, ED126-A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02114)
*Shkelqim Sherifi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出从零训练的紧凑四类CNN基线ED126-A（约124万参数），在Kermany OCT数据集上取得95.03%准确率、macro-F1为0.9205，并系统刻画其类条件错误模式，作为可控评测的参考基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT图像中诊断相关的形态学模式存在显著的类间相似性，自动分类仍有难度；同时基准数据集上的高准确率缺乏可比性，评测协议异质，且对随机初始化训练的紧凑CNN基线缺乏充分刻画，导致跨研究比较受限。

Method: 两项工作：一是对基于CNN的视网膜OCT分类进行聚焦综述，梳理可控基准评测的方法学要点；二是构建ED126-A——在Kermany标注OCT数据集上按明确记录的实验协议从零训练的四类CNN，共1,241,380个参数（可训练1,239,460个），并从训练动态、类级指标、混淆矩阵、置信度、t-SNE表示空间可视化、Grad-CAM、分层特征图以及一对多ROC/PR等维度进行评估。

Result: 图像级留出测试集上准确率95.03%，macro-F1 0.9205，weighted-F1 0.9521。主要类条件短板为Drusen：召回0.9605但精确率仅0.7493，F1为0.8419；混淆矩阵显示Normal→Drusen与CNV→Drusen是主要错误通道。

Conclusion: ED126-A可作为有记录的从零训练参考基线，并为后续迁移学习、加深网络、注意力机制、多尺度特征融合与置信度校准的可控评估提供经验基础。

Abstract: Automated classification of retinal pathologies from Optical Coherence Tomography (OCT) images remains challenging because diagnostically relevant morphological patterns may exhibit substantial inter-class similarity. Although high classification accuracies have been reported on benchmark OCT datasets, cross-study comparison is limited by heterogeneous evaluation protocols and by the limited characterization of compact convolutional neural network (CNN) baselines trained from random initialization. This study addresses this gap through two contributions. First, it provides a focused review of CNN-based retinal OCT classification and identifies methodological considerations relevant to controlled benchmarking. Second, it presents ED126-A, a purpose-built four-class CNN baseline trained from scratch under a documented experimental protocol on the Kermany labeled OCT dataset. The model contains 1,241,380 total parameters, including 1,239,460 trainable parameters, and is evaluated using training-dynamics analysis, class-wise performance metrics, confusion-matrix analysis, confidence analysis, t-SNE representation-space visualization, Grad-CAM, hierarchical feature-map analysis, and one-vs-rest ROC/Precision-Recall analysis. On the image-level held-out test set, ED126-A achieved 95.03% accuracy, with a macro-F1 of 0.9205 and a weighted-F1 of 0.9521. The principal class-conditional limitation occurred for Drusen, which attained a recall of 0.9605 but a precision of 0.7493 and an F1-score of 0.8419. The confusion matrix identified Normal to Drusen and CNV to Drusen as major error channels. These results support the use of ED126-A as a documented from-scratch reference baseline and provide an empirical basis for controlled evaluation of transfer learning, increased depth, attention, multi-scale feature fusion, and calibration in subsequent studies.

</details>


### [211] [Testing concept-level audits of melanoma classifiers with diffusion counterfactuals and matched positive controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02119)
*Md. Shohanoor Rahman,Md. Hasibul Islam,Md Kishor Morol,Moinul Hossain,Dip Nandi,Liew Tze Hui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型、以7点清单概念为条件的反事实审计方法，并用已知目标的对照与零分类器检验黑色素瘤分类器的概念解释可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑色素瘤分类器的概念解释很少用具有已知目标的对照进行检验，缺乏对解释有效性的严格验证。

Method: 构建条件扩散模型（以7个7点清单概念为条件）做反事实审计；采用颜色校准的起始噪声；设置四分位正对照、与生成器伪标签匹配的16个对照、第二评估者标注的12个对照、置换标签的18个零分类器，并计算标准化效应边际；在15个ISIC 2020与6个HAM10000分类器上做色素扫掠；并用对照评估探索性分解。

Result: 颜色校准使颜色离群值由78.5–84.8%降至6.6–13.3%；7个旋钮中4个目标主导但同时改变其他概念；四分位正对照存在混杂，审计未通过预设规则；在三个概念上恢复了16个对照全部目标及12/12第二评估者标注目标，概念激活向量仅恢复16个中的11个；18个置换标签分类器也出现显著扫掠响应与目标排序，但标准化效应边际可将其与对照区分；色素扫掠在15/15 ISIC 2020分类器上提高黑色素瘤logit（平均+3.12）、6/6 HAM10000（+4.01），约为零响应尺度的4倍，回归效应未迁移；分解表明旋钮串扰部分掩盖了血管响应。

Conclusion: 匹配对照与零分类器是概念审计的实用检查手段，可揭示混杂与串扰问题。

Abstract: Concept-based explanations of melanoma classifiers are rarely tested against controls with known targets. We built a counterfactual audit from a diffusion model conditioned on seven 7-point checklist concepts and tested it with such controls. Colour-calibrated start noise reduced colour outliers from 78.5-84.8\% to 6.6-13.3\%. Four of seven knobs were target-dominant but also changed other concepts. Quartile-based positive controls were confounded, and the audit failed its pre-specified rule. It recovered the targets of all 16 controls matched on the generator's pseudo-labels and of 12 of 12 controls labelled by a second evaluator, for three concepts; concept activation vectors recovered fewer (11 of 16). In 18 permuted-label classifiers, significant sweep responses and target rankings also arose, whereas standardised effect margins separated the controls from these null classifiers. The pigmentation sweep raised the melanoma logit in 15 of 15 ISIC 2020 classifiers (+3.12 on average) and 6 of 6 HAM10000 classifiers (+4.01), about four times the null response in scale-free units; the regression effect did not transfer. An exploratory decomposition, evaluated against the controls, suggested that knob cross-talk partly hides vascular responses. Matched controls and null classifiers are practical checks of concept audits.

</details>


### [212] [Refining Roadside 4D Radar Vehicle Classification Using Point-Cloud Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02147)
*Mohammed Alsakabi,Andreia Figueiredo,António Almeida,John Dolan,Miguel Luís,Pedro Rito,Ozan Tonguz,Susana Sargento*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文构建了4D雷达与双目深度相机同步采集的路侧多传感器平台并采集真实数据集，发现模态间类别分布显著不一致，尤其雷达对乘用车与摩托车混淆严重（两类分别占数据集49.01%与48.60%）。为此提出基于雷达点云簇二维位置标准差（经时间平滑）的Neyman-Pearson二分类器，将分类误差从52.1%降至42.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路侧摄像头与传统传感器在探测距离、恶劣天气鲁棒性和深度精度上存在局限；4D雷达可同时解析距离、方位、俯仰与径向速度且适应恶劣条件，但其点云稀疏、噪声大，导致可靠分类困难。作者希望提升雷达单模态分类可靠性，并为后续相机-雷达融合奠定基础。

Method: 搭建4D雷达与双目深度相机同步采集的路侧多传感器平台，采集真实世界数据集；开展跨模态类别分布差异分析，定位雷达在乘用车/摩托车上的混淆问题；对雷达点云簇的二维位置标准差做时间平滑，并据此构建Neyman-Pearson二分类器，方法轻量、可边缘部署。

Result: 分析显示模态间类别分布差异显著，乘用车与摩托车在数据集中分别占49.01%和48.60%，雷达对其混淆强烈；所提Neyman-Pearson二分类器将分类误差由52.1%降低至42.7%。

Conclusion: 基于点云簇位置统计特性（时间平滑的二维位置标准差）的轻量判别方法可有效缓解4D雷达点云稀疏噪声导致的分类混淆，提升雷达单模态分类可靠性，并为未来相机-雷达融合提供基础。

Abstract: Roadside sensing infrastructure is essential for Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS), but cameras and legacy sensors are limited in range, weather robustness, and depth accuracy. 4D radar complements these modalities by resolving range, azimuth, elevation, and radial velocity under adverse conditions, though its sparse, noisy point clouds make reliable classification difficult. We present a roadside multi-sensor platform combining a 4D radar and stereo depth camera under synchronized acquisition, used to collect a real-world dataset. Our analysis reveals a substantial class-distribution discrepancy between modalities, most notably a strong radar confusion between passenger cars and motorcycles (49.01% and\ 48.60% of the dataset). To address this, we propose a Neyman-Pearson-based binary classifier on the temporally-smoothed 2D positional standard deviation of radar point cloud clusters, reducing classification error from 52.1% to 42.7%. This lightweight and edge-deployable approach improves radar-only classification reliability and lays the foundation for future camera-radar fusion.

</details>


### [213] [A conditional generative model for T2-weighted prostate MRI with downstream evaluation in clinically significant cancer classificatio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02197)
*Xavier Rafael-Palou,Alejandro Vergara-Richart,Ignacio Iborra Roncales,Carmen Prieto-de-la-Lastra,Andreu Antolin-Redondo,Juan Morote,Ana Jimenez-Pastor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种带病变/分区条件引导的条件生成模型合成T2W前列腺MRI，显著提升图像保真度并在模拟数据稀缺场景下提升csPCa分类性能，但跨机构增益不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前列腺MRI预测模型受限于训练数据稀缺且代表性不足，合成T2W图像有望扩充含临床显著前列腺癌（csPCa）及移行区（TZ）病变的训练数据。

Method: 回顾性单中心研究；在MAISI-v2基础上扩展，加入csPCa类别、分区及病变掩膜条件引导，并采用相对优势加权损失在1,303例患者（1,654个病变）上微调。通过增量消融评估保真度（FID），并在五种模拟训练数据场景、每种18种合成数据配置下，用基于T2W的csPCa分类器评估下游效用。

Result: 内部FID由预训练MAISI-v2的13.28降至2.04±0.06（PI-CAI：3.83±0.51）。合成数据增强在全部五种场景中显著提升AUC（+0.027至+0.088；校正p=0.016–0.043）；但跨机构PI-CAI增强增益不显著（+0.013；p=0.397）。

Conclusion: 该模型提升了T2W MRI合成保真度，合成图像在模拟数据受限条件下改善csPCa分类；但该增益未能显著跨机构迁移。

Abstract: Scarce and unrepresentative training data limit the performance of prostate MRI prediction models. Synthetic T2-weighted (T2W) MRI may augment training data containing clinically significant prostate cancer (csPCa) and transition zone (TZ) lesions. In this retrospective, single-center, ethics-committee-approved study, we developed a conditional generative model for T2W prostate MRI extending MAISI-v2 with csPCa class, zonal, and lesion-mask-guided conditioning, incorporating a relative advantage weighted loss fine-tuned on 1,303 patients (1,654 lesions). Fidelity was assessed by incremental ablation, and downstream utility was evaluated with T2W-based csPCa classifiers across five simulated training-data scenarios, each testing 18 synthetic-data configurations. The proposed model reduced Fréchet inception distance (FID) from 13.28 (pretrained MAISI-v2) to $2.04 \pm 0.06$ internally (PI-CAI: $3.83 \pm 0.51$). Synthetic augmentation significantly increased AUC in all five scenarios (+0.027 to +0.088; adjusted $p = 0.016$--$0.043$), though cross-institutional augmentation of PI-CAI yielded a non-significant gain (+0.013; $p = 0.397$). Overall, our model improved T2W MRI fidelity, and synthetic images improved csPCa classification under simulated data constraints, although benefit did not transfer significantly across institutions.

</details>


### [214] [Depth Anyviewpoint? Evaluating Monocular Depth Over Viewpoint Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02203)
*Theo Rode,Rui Zhang,Ryan Aubrey,Olivia Li,Jessica Huang,Liv Chu,Healani Dowd,Calden Wloka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于移动相机的视频序列的评估协议，用于检验单目深度模型在视角变化下的空间结构一致性与度量标定能力，并揭示现有基础模型在长期时序一致性上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目深度模型预测的是仿射不变深度（相对深度），虽在单图像基准上表现优异，但其跨视角空间结构一致性尚未被充分研究；而视频流等现实应用依赖场景的三维一致性与度量参考锚定，因此需要针对性评估手段。

Method: 构建新的评估协议：使用相机位置移动的真实视频序列，检验深度预测在视角变化下的一致性；并利用structure from motion（SfM）将深度模型输出锚定到度量深度值。

Result: 在多样化的真实视频序列上验证该协议，表明单目深度基础模型在长时段时间一致性方面存在明显开放挑战，同时发现将SfM作为度量锚定工具存在直接套用的困难。

Conclusion: 广泛适用的单目深度基础模型仍需在跨视角、长时序三维一致性上改进；SfM作为度量标定手段并非可直接采用，需谨慎处理。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation aims to predict depth maps from single RGB images (forgoing the need for stereo camera pairs) and has seen impressive leaps in performance in recent work due to the introduction of affine-invariant losses and the use of foundation model features. These monocular depth models predict affine-invariant depth, which describes the relative positions of objects in the scene instead of absolute depth. While these models demonstrate strong performance on single image benchmarks and qualitatively consistent predictions in video, their effectiveness at preserving consistent spatial structure has been less thoroughly explored. Many potential real-world applications, such as handling video streams as input, rely on coherent three dimensional consistency in scenes. Further, anchoring relative depth predictions to a metric reference for a given scene is a key challenge for applying monocular depth models to real-world situations. We develop a novel evaluation protocol for monocular depth models using video sequences with moving camera positions to test the consistency of prediction across variation in viewpoint. We demonstrate this methodology over diverse, real-world video sequences where we are able to anchor depth model output to metric depth values using structure from motion. Our work demonstrates open challenges in the development of broadly applicable monocular depth foundation models, particularly with their ability to provide temporal consistency over long time frames. We also identify challenges in the straightforward adoption of structure-from-motion as a metric grounding tool for monocular depth estimation models.

</details>


### [215] [Learning from high-resolution 2D images to reconstruct 3D microstructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02204)
*Wenbo Sui,Lars P. Mikkelsen,Jens W. Andreasen,Daniel Lichau,Harold Phelippeau,Philip J. Withers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PCRD（特权跨模态关系蒸馏）框架，利用训练时的精细2D电镜图像改进仅凭低分辨micro-CT输入的样品特异性3D重建，并在碳纤维复合材料上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高空间分辨率的体积成像昂贵、耗时甚至破坏样品，难以规模化开展3D微结构表征；而精细2D成像与低分辨体积测量通常都易获得，因此希望把2D中的精细信息在训练阶段"蒸馏"迁移到低分辨体数据的推理模型中。

Method: 提出特权跨模态关系蒸馏框架：以SEM为特权模态（仅训练时可用），micro-CT为推理输入；不直接对齐异构特征向量，而是在教师/学生表征中围绕对应空间锚点构造相似性分布并进行匹配，从而迁移空间组织关系。应用对象为拉挤碳纤维增强聚合物复合材料。

Result: 2D横截面重建中mAP较直接用micro-CT训练提升1.66个百分点；与高分辨同步辐射X射线CT的独立3D验证在纤维体积分数、直径与局部间距上高度一致；PCRD重建可匹配89.97%的参考纤维中心线长度，而低分辨体插值仅为75.08%。

Conclusion: 通过关系型跨模态蒸馏，在推理时仅需低成本低分辨体积数据即可显著提升样品特异性3D微结构重建质量，为规模化3D表征提供可行途径。

Abstract: High-resolution three-dimensional (3D) microstructure characterization remains difficult to undertake at scale because volumetric imaging at the required spatial resolution is expensive, time-consuming or destructive. In many settings, however, fine-resolution two-dimensional (2D) imaging and lower-resolution volumetric measurements are both readily available. We introduce a Privileged Cross-modal Relational Distillation (PCRD) framework that leverages fine-resolution 2D observations during training to improve specimen-specific 3D reconstruction from lower-resolution volumetric imaging alone. We demonstrate PCRD on pultruded carbon fiber-reinforced polymer composites, using scanning electron microscopy as the privileged modality and micro-CT as the inference input. The framework transfers spatial organization by matching similarity distributions defined around corresponding spatial anchors in the teacher and student representations, avoiding direct alignment of heterogeneous feature vectors. In 2D cross-sectional reconstruction, PCRD improves mAP by 1.66 percentage points over direct micro-CT training. Independent 3D validation against high-resolution synchrotron X-ray CT data shows close agreement in fiber volume fraction, diameter and local spacing. The PCRD reconstruction matches 89.97% of the reference fiber centerline length, compared with 75.08% for the interpolated low-resolution volume.

</details>


### [216] [Forecasting Earth Surface Change in Embedding Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02211)
*Jingtao Li,Sadiq Jaffer,Anil Madhavapeddy,Pedro Sousa,Anthony Ortiz,Kyle Gao,Zhengpeng Feng,Yanfei Zhong,Kevin White,Srinivasan Keshav*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在嵌入空间而非光谱空间进行卫星时间序列预测的方法E²Forecast，在Sentinel-1/2基准上将余弦距离减半并显著降低过度自信，同时无需标签即可用于异常变化检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于卫星时间序列的研究多在像素级光谱值上做预测，但云、阴影和传感器噪声会污染观测，导致光谱预测器把大量容量浪费在低层特征或噪声上而非真实变化。随着地球基础模型的出现，作者认为在嵌入空间预测是可行且更有效的替代方案。

Method: 设计一个token空间，用于处理来自不同传感器和基础模型的不规则历史观测，并支持在任意查询日期进行预测；该空间的token输入到E²Forecast——一个双分支Transformer：一个高斯分支直接预测均值点预测及各维度标准差，另一个整流流（rectified-flow）分支生成预测集合。

Result: 在Sentinel-1和Sentinel-2基准上，预测嵌入而非光谱值使余弦距离减半（0.20→0.10）并大幅降低过度自信（z-score标准差2.63→1.22）；通过比较新观测与预测，无需标签或重训练即可检测异常变化，在全球基准上F₁从41.58提升至48.70；在九个遥感基础模型上均验证了一致优势。

Conclusion: 在嵌入空间预测优于光谱空间预测，该方法除变化检测外，还可有效复用多样化的预训练任务头，推动领域发展；代码已公开。

Abstract: Repeated satellite imagery permits understanding of how our planet changes over time, letting us predict its future and plan mitigating actions. Most existing studies that rely on a time series of satellite observations forecast the spectral values at each pixel for a future target date. However, clouds, shadows, and sensor noise often corrupt these observations, so that spectral forecasters waste much of their capacity on low-level features or noise rather than real change. With the advent of Earth foundation models, we argue that forecasting in embedding space is a feasible and more effective alternative. To this end, we design a token space that handles irregular histories from different sensors and foundation models and supports forecasts at any query date. Tokens from this space are inputs to our proposed forecaster, E$^2$Forecast, which is a transformer with two branches: a Gaussian branch that directly predicts a mean point forecast and per-dimension standard deviations, and a rectified-flow branch that generates forecast ensembles. On Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 benchmarks, forecasting embeddings instead of spectral values halves the cosine distance (from 0.20 to 0.10) and greatly reduces overconfidence, lowering the z-score standard deviation from 2.63 to 1.22. By comparing new observations with its forecasts, E$^2$Forecast can also detect anomalous changes without labels or retraining, raising F$_1$ from 41.58 to 48.70 on a global benchmark. This consistent superiority is validated on nine remote sensing foundation models. Beyond change detection, we believe our approach advances the field by permitting effective reuse of diverse pretrained task heads. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/ucam-eo/e2forecast.

</details>


### [217] [SVD-Prune: Spectral Vision Token Pruning under Extreme Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02284)
*Yvon Apedo,Martyna Poreba,Michal Szczepanski,Samia Bouchafa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SVD-Prune：将视觉 token 剪枝重新表述为保留视觉表示的**主导低秩谱结构**，基于 SVD 与统计杠杆分数（leverage scores）进行全局信息驱动的 token 选择，是一种免训练、架构无关的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 与 VLA 模型需处理数百个视觉 token，视觉表示成为主要计算瓶颈；现有剪枝思路多着眼于"逐个识别重要 token"，缺乏对整体表征结构的考量。

Method: 以 SVD 分解视觉特征，结合统计杠杆分数实现全局感知的 token 选择；框架与架构无关，可部署于编码器侧、解码器侧及混合配置等多种多模态流水线阶段。

Result: 在 VLM 与 VLA 基准上展现极端压缩下的强鲁棒性：视觉 token 预算最高压缩 99.3%；在 16-token 预算下，编码器侧剪枝降低推理 FLOPs 82.4%，含剪枝开销的端到端加速达 4.18×。

Conclusion: 视觉 token 剪枝应视为保持表征的主导低秩谱结构问题，而非独立 token 重要性判别；SVD-Prune 以免训练方式在极端压缩下兼顾鲁棒性与显著效率收益。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models process hundreds of visual tokens, making visual representations a major computational bottleneck. We argue that visual token pruning should be formulated not as identifying individually important tokens, but as preserving the dominant low-rank spectral structure of the visual representation. Based on this formulation, we introduce SVD-Prune, a training-free framework that leverages Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) and statistical leverage scores for globally informed token selection. SVD-Prune is architecture-agnostic and can be applied at different stages of the multimodal pipeline, including encoder-side, decoder-side, and hybrid configurations Extensive experiments across VLM and VLA benchmarks demonstrate strong robustness under extreme compression. SVD-Prune reduces the visual token. SVD-Prune cuts the visual token budget by up to 99.3%, while, at a 16-token budget, encoder-side pruning lowers inference FLOPs by 82.4% and achieves a 4.18x end-to-end speedup, including pruning overhead.

</details>


### [218] [Peripheral prototypical contrastive loss for supervised medical image segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02305)
*Takuro Shimaya,Ryosaku Shino,Masahiro Saiko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Peripheral Prototypical Contrastive (PPC) loss，利用边界附近局部区域的原型特征进行原型对比学习，在噪声标注下稳定提升医学图像分割的边界识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学图像中目标边界模糊（ill-defined object boundaries）导致分割困难；现有边界增强损失函数对边界歧义引起的标注不稳定较为敏感，鲁棒性不足。

Method: PPC 损失：从目标边界附近的局部区域提取原型特征（prototype features），聚焦现有方法难以处理的歧义区域，通过原型对比学习强化边界识别；并提出一种自适应确定原型计算局部区域的策略，以提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，PPC 损失在噪声标签条件下仍能持续提升分割性能；自适应局部区域策略进一步增强鲁棒性并优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PPC 损失有助于实现可扩展且可靠的医学图像分割，并可支持在不完美标注下构建基础模型（foundation models）。

Abstract: Robust segmentation of biomedical images remains challenging primarily due to ill-defined object boundaries. Although several loss functions have recently been proposed to enhance object boundary recognition, concerns remain about the susceptibility to unstable annotations resulting from boundary ambiguities. Here, we introduce the Peripheral Prototypical Contrastive (PPC) loss, which leverages prototypical contrastive learning to improve boundary recognition and enable stable training even under noisy annotation conditions. The PPC loss utilizes prototype features obtained from local regions near object boundaries, focusing specifically on ambiguous areas that have been challenging for existing approaches. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets demonstrated that the PPC loss consistently improve segmentation performance even under conditions with noisy labels. A newly proposed strategy, which adaptively determines the local region for prototype calculation, enhanced robustness and outperformed existing methods. Our findings indicate the potential of the PPC loss in facilitating scalable and reliable medical image segmentation, including the development of foundation models with imperfect annotations.

</details>


### [219] [Lie Routing Vision Language Models for View-Robust 3D Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02306)
*Jingjun Yi,Chen Hu,Qi Bi,Hao Zheng,Huimin Huang,Yongkang,Wei Ji,Yawen Huang,Xian Wu,Yefeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 LieVLM，一个基于李群（SO(3)）路由的视角鲁棒 3D 理解框架，针对多视角下 MLLM 的“视角诱导语义漂移”问题，用局部旋转变换调整视觉特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在多视角 RGB 3D 场景理解中已取得进展，但同一目标与查询在不同相机视角下会产生不一致的响应，即“视角诱导语义漂移”（viewpoint-induced semantic drift）。作者希望建模视角变化中的旋转成分，使特征表示随视角自适应。

Method: 提出 Lie routing：由 VGGT 提取的几何 token 预测局部旋转参数与通道级路由系数；在学到的三维子空间内，对投影后的视觉特征施加局部 SO(3) 变换，变换由闭式 Padé–Cayley 映射实现；将相对于恒等映射的修正量投影回原空间，并以门控方式融合进原始视觉与几何特征，从而在保留预训练语义与直接空间上下文的同时学习视角相关的特征调整。

Result: 在 3D 描述生成、定位、检测与空间推理任务上，跨不同训练规模与骨干网络均稳定优于加性融合基线；受控消融实验分析了几何条件与变换结构的作用；视角分析显示预测路由状态随视角系统性变化，目标响应更局部化，跨视角任务性能更强。

Conclusion: 以李群旋转路由替代简单加性融合，可有效缓解视角诱导的语义漂移，为视角鲁棒的 3D 多模态理解提供了具几何可解释性的特征调制范式。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made substantial progress in 3D scene understanding from multi-view RGB observations. Yet a fixed object and query can elicit inconsistent target-related responses across camera viewpoints, a phenomenon we characterize as viewpoint-induced semantic drift. We introduce LieVLM, a Lie routing framework for view-robust 3D understanding. Motivated by the rotational component of viewpoint change, Lie routing adapts visual representations by applying local $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ transformations to projected features within learned three-dimensional subspaces. Geometry tokens extracted by VGGT predict local rotation parameters and channel-wise routing coefficients. A closed-form Padé--Cayley map transforms projected visual features, whose identity-relative corrections are projected back and gated into the original visual and geometry features. This design retains the pretrained semantic and direct spatial context while learning view-dependent feature adjustments. Experiments on 3D captioning, grounding, detection, and spatial reasoning show consistent improvements over additive fusion across training scales and backbones. Controlled ablations examine the roles of geometric conditioning and transformation structure. Viewpoint analyses further reveal systematic variation in the predicted routing states alongside more localized target responses and stronger task performance across viewpoint changes.

</details>


### [220] [The DynaCell Evaluation Suite for Dynamic 3D Virtual Staining of Live Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02324)
*Alexandr A. Kalinin,Dihan Zheng,Taylla Milena Theodoro,Ivan Ivanov,Eduardo Hirata-Miyasaki,See-Chi Lee,Aofei Liu,Sricharan Reddy Varra,Talon Chandler,Soorya Pradeep,Chad Liu,Manuel D. Leonetti,Carolina Arias,Bo Huang,Shalin B. Mehta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向活细胞3D延时数据的虚拟染色评测套件，包含配对数据、基线模型与三级指标，揭示回归与生成模型在结构与表型保真度上的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活细胞成像中荧光标记与反复照明会损害细胞健康，虚拟染色可从无标记图像预测荧光图像；但现有虚拟染色模型在体层（3D）延时数据上的性能缺乏系统表征。

Method: 构建评测套件：配对采集的带/不带荧光标记的3D延时图像、一组基线模型，以及三个层级的指标（像素级保真度、细胞器分割性能、单细胞表型相似度）；并在3种细胞类型、2种显微镜、4种细胞器、3种扰动状态下进行度量与模型行为分析。

Result: 回归基线在目标细胞器空间定位上表现更好；生成式基线在刻画跨细胞表型分布上更优，说明结构与表型保真度之间存在权衡，单指标评测会掩盖该权衡。

Conclusion: 虚拟染色模型需针对定位、分割、追踪、表型分析等不同下游任务差异化开发；作者以DynaCell框架发布数据集、基线权重与评测代码。

Abstract: Understanding how cells respond to perturbations over time is central to cell biology and drug discovery. Live-cell imaging captures the dynamics of perturbation-induced phenotypes in individual cells. While fluorescence imaging can visualize specific molecular phenotypes, labeling and repeated illumination often affect cell health. Virtual staining offers an alternative by predicting these fluorescence images from images acquired without fluorescent labels. Although many virtual staining models have been developed, their performance on volumetric time-lapse data remains poorly characterized. We address this gap with an evaluation suite for 3D time-lapse virtual staining of live cells. The suite combines paired 3D time-lapse images acquired with and without fluorescent labels, a set of baseline models, and three metric tiers measuring pixel-level fidelity, organelle segmentation performance, and single-cell phenotype similarity. Using this evaluation suite, we study metric and model behavior across 3 cell types, 2 microscopes, 4 organelles, and 3 perturbation states. We find that regression baselines better predict the spatial localization of target organelles, whereas the generative baseline better captures the distribution of phenotypes across cells. These differences expose trade-offs between structural and phenotypic fidelity that are obscured by single-metric evaluation and suggest how virtual staining models can be developed for diverse downstream tasks such as localization, segmentation, tracking, and phenotypic profiling. We release the curated datasets, baseline checkpoints, and evaluation code as part of DynaCell, a broader framework for image-based profiling of cell-state dynamics.

</details>


### [221] [Findings-aware Language-Image Pretraining for CT Volume Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02327)
*Takuro Shimaya,Junya Inoh,Masahiro Saiko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FiLIP，一种基于临床知识的监督式CT-语言表征学习框架：用大语言模型从报告中抽取异常所见短语，并用生物医学文本嵌入模型构造图像编码器的显式目标特征，在零样本多发异常分类与报告生成任务上优于弱监督（CLIP式）方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT视觉-语言基础模型多采用CLIP式对比学习，原则上把不同病例的图文对视为负样本并相互推远；但在医学影像中，不同病例间常存在临床相似性，将其强制作为负对并不合理，弱监督信号不足。

Method: 以语言基础模型提供的临床知识替代CLIP的弱监督：用大语言模型从放射报告中提取异常所见短语（abnormal finding phrases），再用生物医学文本嵌入模型将这些短语编码为语义表示，作为图像编码器应输出的显式目标特征，进行监督式表征学习。

Result: 在多标签零样本异常分类任务中，FiLIP在域内与外部域评测均优于弱监督方法；在下游放射报告生成任务上微调时同样有效。

Conclusion: 基于临床语义的监督式图文表征学习可避免不当负样本假设，为CT视觉-语言基础模型提供更合理、更有效的预训练范式。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) provides diverse patient information and various clinical tasks, for which numerous deep learning models have been investigated. Recent years have seen the development of vision-language foundation models that can comprehensively extract clinical features, driven by representation learning that combines CT volumes with radiology reports. Existing approaches typically adopt Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP)-based representation learning, where pairs from different cases are, in principle, treated as negative pairs such that the volume and report embeddings are pushed apart. In the biomedical imaging, however, treating different cases as negative pairs is not always appropriate, particularly when clinical similarity exists. In this study, we propose a supervised representation learning framework grounded in clinical knowledge from language foundation models, rather than the weak supervision used in CLIP. Specifically, we utilized a large language model to extract abnormal finding phrases from reports. We then explicitly constructed the target features that the image encoder should output, as the semantic representation of the extracted findings using a biomedical text embedding model. Our Findings-aware Language-Image Pre-training (FiLIP) outperformed weakly supervised approaches in multi-label zero-shot finding classification in both in-domain and external-domain evaluations, and also proved effective when fine-tuned for the downstream task of radiology report generation.

</details>


### [222] [AgiWorld: An Agentic Video World Model for Building Persistent Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02341)
*Shaoheng Fang,Yiqun Mei,Hanwen Jiang,Xin Sun,Haitian Zheng,Hao Tan,Kalyan Sunkavalli,Georgios Pavlakos,Qixing Huang,Yiwei Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgiWorld 是一个"智能体式"视频世界模型，依据单张图像与自由文本指令自主规划并逐块生成可持久一致的世界，通过智能体与生成模型围绕共享空间记忆的闭环实现分钟级长程探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型依赖用户提供底层相机运动与文本提示，用户实现高层意图成本高；且随探索推进，生成世界难以保持持久一致性（persistence）。

Method: 提出两层协同框架：(1) AgiWorld-Agent 负责高层规划，构建世界状态并规划旅程、指导探索；(2) AgiWorld-WM 负责低层实现，逐块生成视频并将其与几何一并存入空间记忆，智能体据此更新世界状态。两者以共享空间记忆及"度量相机轨迹+结构化提示"接口耦合形成闭环。针对长程 rollout 中的遮挡与累积几何误差，提出遮挡感知历史选择（按表面可见性检索历史视角）与基于可靠性的记忆维护（偏好可靠几何与更早观测）。

Result: AgiWorld-WM 在世界重访与探索基准上取得总体 SOTA；定性结果与消融实验显示，AgiWorld 能在分钟级旅程中依据自由指令构建持久世界。

Conclusion: 持久性并非单靠视频模型获得，而源于视频模型与智能体形成的闭环；高层意图规划与低层生成的协同是长程一致世界建模的关键。

Abstract: Recent interactive video world models generate visually compelling videos from a single image, following user-provided camera motion and text prompts. However, such low-level control demands substantial user effort to realize a high-level intent, and the generated world struggles to remain persistent as the exploration proceeds. In this work, we present AgiWorld, an agentic video world model that explores and builds a persistent world from a single image and a free-form instruction. For high-level planning, our agent AgiWorld-Agent designs the world and the journey through it in a world state and directs the exploration. For low-level realization, our video world model AgiWorld-WM generates the video chunk by chunk and stores it with its geometry in a spatial memory, from which the agent updates its world state. This closed loop keeps the world faithful to the user's intent and keeps the agent's world state consistent with the generated content throughout the exploration. We co-design the two levels around the shared spatial memory and an interface of metric camera trajectories and structured prompts. In AgiWorld-WM, to handle occlusion and accumulated geometric errors in the spatial memory over long rollouts, we propose occlusion-aware history selection, which retrieves past views by surface visibility, and reliability-based memory maintenance, which prefers reliable geometry and earlier observations. AgiWorld-WM achieves state-of-the-art overall performance on world revisiting and exploration benchmarks, and qualitative results and ablations show that AgiWorld builds persistent worlds from free-form instructions over minute-long journeys. Persistence thus arises not from the video model alone but from its closed loop with the agent.

</details>


### [223] [Exploiting Textual Semantics for Robust Cross-View Object Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02356)
*Bing Fan,Yunhe Feng,Yan Huang,Heng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TeSCo通过视觉语言模型生成目标的文本描述，并将文本语义与视觉线索融合，实现跨视角目标对应，在Ego-Exo4D和HANDAL-X上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角目标对应（CVOC）方法主要依赖视觉线索，在视角变化大、外观差异显著的复杂场景中因目标信息不足而性能受限。

Method: 1) 用视觉语言模型从查询视角生成目标的文本表达并提取文本特征；2) 提出文本条件跨模态融合（TCF）模块，通过文本引导调制将文本特征注入视觉特征，得到鲁棒的多模态目标表示；3) 提出迭代文本特征精炼（ITFR）模块，在TCF之前利用搜索视角信息渐进调整文本特征，实现搜索视角感知的语义融合。

Result: 在Ego-Exo4D和HANDAL-X上取得最先进结果，显著优于其他模型，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 目标的文本描述（类别、纹理、颜色等属性）具有视角与外观不变性，与视觉线索互补，可显著提升复杂场景下跨视角目标对应的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cross-view object correspondence (CVOC) aims to establish associations of the same target between query and search videos captured from different viewpoints. Existing methods often rely on visual cues for CVOC. Despite recent progress, these methods struggle in complicated scenarios with substantial viewpoint changes and appearance variations, due to lacking sufficient target information. Addressing this, we introduce TeSCo, a framework that exploits rich Textual Semantics of an object, in addition to its visual cues, for cross-view object Correspondence. Our key insight is, textual description of a target, by capturing diverse attributes such as category, texture, and color, provides viewpoint- and appearance-invariant semantics, which are complementary to visual cues and can hence enhance correspondence robustness in complicated scenes. Inspired by this, TeSCo first generates a textual expression of the target from the given query view using a vision-language model, and then extracts textual feature to enhance visual features for localization in the search view. To realize this, we introduce a text-conditioned cross-modal fusion (TCF) module that incorporates the textual feature into visual features with text-guided modulation, producing more robust multimodal target representation for object correspondence. Since not all textual cues are equally compatible with the target in current search-view frame, we propose an iterative textual feature refinement (ITFR) module that progressively adjusts textual feature using search-view information before the TCF module, enabling search-view-aware textual semantics to be fused into visual features and leading to better performance. In extensive experiments on Ego-Exo4D and HANDAL-X, TeSCo demonstrates state-of-the-art results and largely outperforms other models, validating its efficacy. Our code is released at https://github.com/VAIL-Fan/TeSCo.

</details>


### [224] [GEAR: Generative Editing for Scene-Conditioned Outfit Retrieval for VR Travel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02363)
*Gia-Huy Dinh,Thinh-Phuc Nguyen,Thanh-Hai Nguyen,Lam-Huy Nguyen-Ngoc,Hoang-Bach Nguyen-Phan,Nam-Quan Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GEAR框架，通过生成式中间视觉查询（VLM描述→指令扩散渲染到标准角色→裁剪服装区域检索）实现场景条件下的3D虚拟环境服装资产检索，并在Unity原型与基准评测中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟环境中服装选择需与周围场景语境协调，但3D环境视角与商品目录服装资产之间存在视觉域差距（视角、风格、渲染域不一致），直接视觉相似度难以有效匹配；因此需研究以用户第一人称视角为检索信号的场景条件服装检索问题。

Method: GEAR（Generative Editing for Asset Retrieval）：1）用视觉语言模型从捕获场景中描述合适服装概念；2）用指令引导的扩散模型将该概念渲染到标准化参考角色上；3）裁剪服装区域，用嵌入相似度检索已有2D服装资产；4）构建Unity交互原型，支持实时场景捕获、资产检索与引擎内3D化身试穿。

Result: 在受控数据集上以自动Swiss-system成对比较评测，对比直接视觉相似度、美学评分和VLM重排：GEAR取得有竞争力的首位性能（mean Elo@5=1012.75，top-10重叠0.180），成对一致率0.539；20个虚拟旅行场景用户研究中，GEAR在视觉吸引力（34.5%）与语境适配性（34.5%）上均获最高总体偏好。

Conclusion: 以生成式中间查询弥合跨域差距，优于直接兼容性预测；但绝对检索精度（top-10重叠0.180、一致率0.539）仍有限，用户偏好优势相对分散，扩展性与真实资产覆盖仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: In virtual environments, choosing appropriate outfits requires contextual harmony with surrounding scenes, yet bridging the visual gap between 3D ambient views and catalog garment assets remains challenging. This paper investigates scene-conditioned outfit retrieval for interactive 3D environments, where a user's egocentric view serves as the retrieval signal. We present GEAR (Generative Editing for Asset Retrieval), a framework that bridges the cross-domain gap by synthesizing intermediate visual queries rather than predicting direct compatibility. GEAR uses a vision-language model to describe suitable clothing from a captured scene, employs an instruction-guided diffusion model to render the concept onto a standardized reference character, and utilizes the cropped clothing region to retrieve existing 2D outfit assets via embedding similarity. An interactive Unity prototype demonstrates real-time scene capture, asset retrieval, and in-engine 3D avatar fitting. We benchmark GEAR against direct visual similarity, aesthetic scoring, and VLM-based reranking on a controlled dataset using automated Swiss-system pairwise judgments, where GEAR achieves competitive top-ranked performance (mean Elo@5 of 1012.75 and top-10 overlap of 0.180) alongside 0.539 pairwise agreement. Furthermore, a user study across 20 virtual travel scenes demonstrates that GEAR achieves the highest overall participant preference in both visual appeal (34.5%) and contextual suitability (34.5%).

</details>


### [225] [IMUdrape: Generating Garment-Mounted Virtual IMU Data from Text through Physics-Based Cloth Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02393)
*Zikang Leng,Thomas Plötz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IMUdrape 通过基于物理的布料仿真从文本生成服装安装式虚拟 IMU 数据，缓解服装传感器标注数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惯性传感器正被嵌入衣物（从智能运动服到数百个 IMU 的服装）。缝入衣物的传感器不再与身体刚性运动：宽松布料会滑动、扭转、摆动，因此服装安装式传感器记录的是布料与身体的叠加运动，且这一额外运动因服装而异。此类传感器的模型需要与部署服装配套采集的训练数据，但标注数据稀缺。基于视频或文本生成的虚拟 IMU 数据已缓解身体贴附传感器的数据稀缺，但现有生成器将虚拟传感器刚性附着于身体，无法建模衣物的额外运动。

Method: 提出 IMUdrape：从文本经物理布料仿真生成服装安装式虚拟 IMU 数据。流程包括：(1) 据活动描述合成运动；(2) 从照片重建目标服装的缝纫纸样（sewing pattern）；(3) 将虚拟 IMU 置于部署时的真实传感器位置；(4) 用无穿透接触求解器（intersection-free contact solver）仿真服装，使每次仿真同时产出配对的服装信号与身体信号。

Result: 在四个含服装安装式传感器的真实数据集上，IMUdrape 在活动识别与全身姿态估计上均优于现有虚拟数据方法。单一真实受试者条件下，其数据使识别 macro-F1 相比仅用真实数据提升 7%–46%，相比同一运动的身体贴附虚拟数据提升 2%–11%。当在已录运动上仿真服装时，无任何真实训练数据的姿态误差相对生成运动下降 30%–33%，且服装数据比身体数据精度高 12%。

Conclusion: IMUdrape 通过将文本驱动的物理布料仿真引入虚拟 IMU 生成，显式建模服装相对身体的附加运动，从而减少服装安装式传感所需的真实数据量。

Abstract: Inertial sensors are moving into clothing, from sensorized sportswear to garments instrumented with hundreds of IMUs. Sewn into clothing, a sensor no longer moves rigidly with the body: loose fabric slides, twists and swings, so a garment-mounted sensor records the motion of the fabric as well as that of the body, and this extra motion differs from one garment to the next. Models for such sensors need training data recorded with the deployed garment, yet labeled data are scarce. Virtual IMU data generated from videos or text have eased this scarcity for body-attached sensors, but existing generators attach their virtual sensors rigidly to the body. We present IMUdrape, which generates garment-mounted virtual IMU data from text through physics-based cloth simulation. IMUdrape synthesizes motion from activity descriptions, reconstructs the target garment as a sewing pattern from photographs, places virtual IMUs at the deployment's real sensor positions, and simulates the garment with an intersection-free contact solver, so that every simulation yields paired garment and body signals. On four real datasets with clothing-mounted sensors, IMUdrape improves on existing virtual-data approaches for both activity recognition and full-body pose estimation. With one real subject, its data improve recognition macro-F1 by 7% to 46% over real data alone and by 2% to 11% over body-mounted virtual data from the same motion. When the garment is simulated on recorded motion, the pose error without any real training data falls by 30% to 33% relative to generated motion, and garment data remain 12% more accurate than body data. IMUdrape thus reduces the amount of real data that garment-mounted sensing requires.

</details>


### [226] [TERA: Task-Evidence Restoration for Underwater Object Detection under Bandwidth-Constrained Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02419)
*Heejin Kim,Joon-Seok Song,Eunil Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出AquaTX基准（含真实水下数据集与seen/held-out编解码器）与TERA框架（元数据盲、检测器冻结、任务引导像素复原+双状态特征校正），用于传输退化下的水下目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下目标检测器多在传输前图像上评测，忽略了带宽受限下采样与压缩带来的额外退化，导致评估与实际部署脱节。

Method: 构建AquaTX受控基准；提出TERA接收端框架，元数据盲（不依赖编解码器参数），将任务引导的像素级复原与双状态特征校正结合，且保持检测器冻结。

Result: seen复合退化下，mAP50:95由37.7提升至50.0（DUO-TX）、由31.5提升至41.3（RUOD-TX）；保持干净输入性能；在held-out AVIF编解码器下与检测器微调相当。

Conclusion: 结果支持传输感知评测范式及任务引导复原对水下目标检测的有效性。

Abstract: Underwater object detectors are typically evaluated on pre-transmission images, overlooking additional degradation from bandwidth-constrained downscaling and compression. We introduce AquaTX, a controlled benchmark built from real underwater detection datasets with seen and held-out codecs. We also propose TERA, a metadata-blind receiver-side framework combining task-guided pixel restoration and dual-state feature correction while keeping the detector frozen. Under seen compound degradation, TERA improves mean average precision (mAP50:95, averaged over IoU thresholds from 0.50 to 0.95) from 37.7 to 50.0 on DUO-TX and from 31.5 to 41.3 on RUOD-TX relative to the frozen detector without restoration. TERA also preserves clean-input performance and remains competitive with detector fine-tuning under the held-out AVIF codec. These findings support transmission-aware evaluation and task-guided restoration for underwater object detection. Code is available at https://github.com/hijinkim/TERA.

</details>


### [227] [Blueprint2CAD: Benchmarking Foundation Models on Engineering Drawing-to-CAD Render Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02433)
*Alexander Valverde,Danny Xie-Li,Zhichao Wang,Marcos V. Conde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Blueprint2CAD 基准，用于"工程图—渲染图对应"任务（判断正交投影线图与另一视角的着色渲染是否属于同一零件）；其数据层 BlueCAD-30K 基于 Zero-to-CAD 的 29,925 个模型构建。实验表明冻结编码器在已知视角时 Recall@1 可达 65%、未知视角时仅 43%，四种编码器差异不超过 6 个百分点，说明瓶颈在于视角而非骨干网络；六个零样本视觉语言模型验证准确率仅 0.52–0.67，存在 Yes/No 偏置，LoRA 微调略有提升但加剧偏差且未解决视角问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程图通过正交投影描述机械零件，识别图纸所绘零件是零件检索、设计复用与数字化的基础，但目前没有公开基准衡量视觉模型能否完成这一任务。因此作者构建首个面向"图纸—渲染图一致判定"的公开基准，以系统评估该能力并定位瓶颈。

Method: 构建 Blueprint2CAD 基准：任务为给定一张正交线图与一张（通常来自不同视角的）着色渲染图，判断二者是否为同一零件。数据层 BlueCAD-30K 对 29,925 个 Zero-to-CAD 模型应用开源制图流水线，生成视角对齐的渲染、六个视角下的干净图纸与标注尺寸图纸，以及表面类型元数据。评测包括：冻结编码器在 10,000 模型库中的检索实验（对比已知/未知视角），以及六个零样本视觉语言模型的验证实验，并测试思维链提示与 LoRA 微调的影响。

Result: 冻结编码器在视角已知时 Recall@1 最高达 65%，视角未知时降至 43%；参与测试的四种编码器结果相差不超过 6 个百分点。六个零样本视觉语言模型验证准确率仅 0.52–0.67，并存在模型特有的 Yes/No 回答偏置；思维链可改变但无法消除该偏置；在域内样本对上快速 LoRA 微调可将验证准确率提升数个百分点，但会强化偏置，且视角导致的失败依然存在。

Conclusion: 当前视觉模型在图纸—零件对应任务上仍有明显不足，主要瓶颈是视角差异而非骨干网络性能；视觉语言模型存在顽固的回答偏置，简单微调无法从根本上解决。Blueprint2CAD 为该问题提供了可量化的公开评测平台，并指出视角不变性与偏置校正是有待突破的方向。

Abstract: Engineering drawings specify mechanical parts through orthographic projections. Recognizing which part a drawing depicts underlies part search, design reuse, and digitization, yet no public benchmark measures whether vision models can do it. We introduce Blueprint2CAD, a benchmark for drawing-to-render correspondence: given an orthographic line drawing and a shaded render, usually from another viewpoint, decide whether they show the same part. Its data layer, BlueCAD-30K, applies an open drafting pipeline to 29,925 Zero-to-CAD models, adding view-aligned renders, clean and dimensioned drawings from six viewpoints, and surface-type metadata. Frozen encoders retrieve the matching part from a 10,000-model gallery with up to 65% Recall@1 when viewpoints are known but 43% when they are not, and the four encoders we test lie within six points of each other: viewpoint, not backbone, is the bottleneck. Six zero-shot vision-language models reach only 0.52-0.67 verification accuracy, with model-specific Yes/No biases that chain-of-thought shifts but does not remove; a quick LoRA fine-tuning on in-domain pairs improves verification accuracy by a few points but sharpens these biases and leaves the viewpoint failure intact.

</details>


### [228] [Evaluating Synthetic Skin Lesion Images for Classifier Training: A Safety-Aware Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02437)
*Amindu Kumarasinghe,Vinh Bui,Reza Ghanbarzadeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文用16种质量指标的“锦标赛”检验皮肤癌合成图像筛选中常用的单一FID准则，发现无单一指标对所有类别都有效，并提出按类别加权各指标、剔除过近真实图的“安全感知”评估算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤癌分类器缺乏标注图像，尤其罕见病变，AI生成图像可填补缺口；但生成图像质量参差，需筛选保留哪些图像。现有做法通常依赖单一质量分数（多为FID），却未经分类器需求的检验，也无法判断生成器是否记忆训练照片。

Method: 构建16种质量指标的锦标赛：每个指标单独从275,472张EDM2扩散模型生成图像池中选出训练子集，用各子集训练的分类器按类别为每个指标打分；再据逐类测量结果设计安全感知评估算法——按各指标在各类别中的实测价值加权，并剔除与真实训练图像过近的样本。

Result: 无单一指标适用于所有类别：基于Inception特征的FID对某一病变类型分类最优，但对另一类型相对随机采样无显著优势，且其偏好的生成设置恰是训练图像最接近的设置。在ISIC-2018测试集上，该算法对Swin-B的Macro-F2较FID基线提升+0.0136至+0.0164（四个安全阈值，Bonferroni校正p<0.02），较随机采样约+0.03，但对ResNet-50无效；收紧阈值可提升记忆安全性且效用无明显变化，但所有精选集安全性仍不及随机采样。

Conclusion: 结果显示在主分类器上取得适度但统计显著的提升，并提供了一套实用的、安全感知的合成数据评估与使用流程。

Abstract: Skin cancer classifiers need large sets of annotated images, which are scarce, especially for rare lesion types. AI-generated images can fill the gap, but a generator produces images of widely varying quality, so selecting which images to retain is important. That choice usually rests on a single quality score, most often the Fréchet Inception Distance (FID), which has not been tested against what a classifier needs and cannot tell whether the generator memorizes its training photographs. We test that practice with a tournament of 16 quality metrics. Each metric alone selects a training subset from a pool of 275,472 images generated by a diffusion model (EDM2), and a classifier trained on each subset scores the metric per class. The tournament shows that no single metric serves every class: FID computed on Inception features gives the best classifier for one lesion type and no measurable advantage over random sampling for another, and the generation setting it prefers is also the one closest to the training images. We turn these per-class measurements into a safety-aware evaluation algorithm that weights each metric by its measured value in each class and removes images too close to a real training image. On the held-out ISIC-2018 test set, the algorithm improves Macro-$F_2$, a recall-weighted measure of classification performance, over the FID baseline for a Swin-B classifier by +0.0136 to +0.0164 across four safety thresholds (Bonferroni-corrected $p < 0.02$) and over random sampling by about +0.03, though not for ResNet-50. Tightening the threshold improves a memorization-safety score with no measurable change in utility, but every curated set remains less safe than random sampling. The results show a modest but statistically significant gain on the primary classifier, and offer a practical, safety-aware procedure for evaluating and using synthetic data.

</details>


### [229] [Gnomon4D: Learning Where and When to Focus for Geometric Motion-Grounded World-Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02474)
*Lingwei Dang,Yan Zhou,Juntong Li,Zhenhao Zhang,Yiteng Chen,Xiaoqiang Liu,Liang An,Hongwen Zhang,Wei Min,Guoxin Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Yebin Liu,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gnomon4D 是一个世界-动作模型（WAM），联合生成未来视频、机器人动作以及与视频对齐的 4D 几何运动场 GeoDelta，从而把“本体运动—物体响应”的时序关系直接暴露给动作专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 的 RGB 未来预测将物理状态变化与外观纠缠，逐帧深度/点图缺乏跨帧时序对应；已有 4D 方法要么把运动隐式留在视觉特征中，要么需借助独立接口把预测轨迹转成动作，导致本体运动与物体响应之间的关系无法被动作模块直接利用。

Method: 提出 GeoDelta：与视频对齐的 4D 几何运动场，编码本体点与物体点在跨帧图像平面上的位移及深度变化，二者共享同一空间；配合 Interaction-Salient Attention（InSAttn）依据上述运动关系将空间读出聚焦到任务相关交互区域，以及 Transition-Anchored Aggregation（TransAgg）在相对运动突变（如抓取、释放）附近聚合证据。GeoDelta 与本体特定控制解耦，可作为人—机器人共享的准动作，支持在 EgoManip-5000H 上的无动作预训练，随后在 Robot-30H 上做机器人中训练。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0、VLABench、RoboDojo 上取得领先；在分布内与分布外设置下分别以 7.0 和 16.0 个百分点超过最强真实机器人基线。

Conclusion: 以显式 4D 几何运动场作为共享中间表征，可有效桥接视频生成先验与动作输出，并借助跨本体准动作实现大规模无动作预训练，显著提升泛化能力。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) use video-generation priors to jointly predict future observations and robot actions. Yet RGB futures entangle physical state changes with appearance, while frame-wise depth and point maps lack temporal correspondence. Existing 4D approaches either keep motion implicit in visual features or translate predicted traces into actions through a separate interface. We present Gnomon4D, a WAM that jointly generates future video, robot actions, and GeoDelta, a video-aligned 4D geometric motion field encoding the cross-frame image-plane displacement and depth change of embodiment and object points. By placing embodiment motion and object responses in a shared space, GeoDelta exposes their motion relations to the action expert at inference. Interaction-Salient Attention (InSAttn) uses these relations to focus spatial readout on task-relevant interaction regions, while Transition-Anchored Aggregation (TransAgg) aggregates evidence around changes in their relative motion, such as grasp and release. GeoDelta describes surface motion independently of embodiment-specific controls, providing a shared human--robot quasi-action for action-free pretraining on EgoManip-5000H followed by robot mid-training on Robot-30H. Gnomon4D leads on RoboTwin~2.0, VLABench, and RoboDojo, and outperforms the strongest real-robot baseline by 7.0 and 16.0 percentage points under in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, respectively.

</details>


### [230] [Fast-Slow Evolutionary Occupancy Prediction via Controlled Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02499)
*Runhe Yang,Zhiyuan Zhou,Yuxiang Yan,Xuetong Yang,Yikai Pan,Jiacheng Tang,Zhuolin He,Jianhua Han,Hang Xu,Jian Pu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoOcc：将快慢双系统占用预测建模为连续时间受控动力系统，在不改动上游架构的前提下融合二者优势，实现更优的精度—时延平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D语义占用预测要求高精度与低时延。现有方法呈现不同精度—时延特性：性能导向模型预测更强但慢，部署友好模型响应快但精度低，二者互补性未被联合利用。

Method: 提出快慢演化占用预测框架EvoOcc，把演化占用预测建模为连续时间受控动力系统，以适应可变的慢系统延迟和不规则的快系统到达；并将演化分解为双重状态演化：自车状态演化补偿确定性坐标偏移，场景状态演化建模真实的几何与语义变化。

Result: 在三组不同的快慢系统对及不规则预测到达条件下，EvoOcc相较快系统基线持续提升，同时时延远低于慢系统，表现出良好的精度—时延平衡与实际推理下的稳定行为。

Conclusion: 通过快慢系统协同的连续时间演化建模，可在保持低时延的同时提升占用预测精度，且不依赖上游架构修改，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: 3D semantic occupancy prediction provides dense geometric and semantic scene representations for autonomous driving, where both high accuracy and low response latency are crucial for safe downstream forecasting and planning. Existing methods usually exhibit different accuracy-latency characteristics. Performance-oriented models provide stronger geometric and semantic predictions, while deployment-friendly models provide faster responses. This motivates EvoOcc, a fast-slow evolutionary occupancy prediction framework that jointly exploits their complementary strengths to improve the accuracy-latency balance without changing the upstream architectures. EvoOcc models evolutionary occupancy prediction as a continuous time controlled dynamical system, enabling flexible adaptation to variable slow system delays and irregular fast system arrivals. The framework further decomposes the evolution process into dual state evolution where ego state evolution compensates for the deterministic coordinate shifts and scene state evolution models the genuine geometric and semantic scene changes. Extensive experiments on three different fast-slow system pairs and irregular prediction arrivals show that EvoOcc consistently improves over fast system baselines while maintaining much lower latency than the slow system, indicating a favorable accuracy-latency balance and stable behavior under practical inference situations.

</details>


### [231] [QFacePlacement: From Arbitrary Tensorization to Semantic Spatial Correspondence in Modern Face Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02505)
*Krishna Bhatia,Kavita Choudhary,Pruthwik Mishra,Praveen Kumar Chandaliya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过两个受控实验界面研究张量网络模型在人脸验证中的"放置"问题，发现量子启发式因式分解仅在界面提供任务对齐轴与可压缩结构时才可靠，并强调AUC需与低FAR指标联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子启发式计算机视觉常将高维特征重塑为张量并施加张量网络模型，但现代人脸嵌入针对余弦相似度优化，单纯张量化并不天然具备张量列/MPO所需的低秩结构，这种结合可能具有误导性。作者因此系统考察模型应放置在何处才有效。

Method: 设计两个受控接口实验：(1)跨模型嵌入对齐，需512×512算子将AdaFace映射到ArcFace空间，对比稠密Ridge、Procrustes、TT-SVD与MPO压缩（含基变换与低秩残差，64K参数预算）；(2)全局池化前，由局部AdaFace描述子构造7×7×7×7探针-图库对应张量，使用几何感知对称张量列（bond dimension=2，122参数）对比稠密线性匹配器（2403参数）。

Result: 对齐任务：64K预算下平均方向AUC为稠密Ridge 0.9297、秩64 SVD 0.8952、最佳低秩MPO混合0.8685、原始MPO约0.649。对应张量任务：张量列模型以122参数在LFW上达AUC 0.9802，但低FAR表现差——CFP-FP上FAR=0.001时TAR为0.0102（全局余弦0.1734），CPLFW上0.7805（对比0.8737）。

Conclusion: 量子启发式因式分解的可信度取决于所选接口是否提供任务对齐的轴与可压缩结构；压缩在聚合AUC上的优势未必转化为低误接受率下的运行性能，因此必须将AUC与低FAR操作指标并置评估。

Abstract: Quantum-inspired computer vision often reshapes high-dimensional features into tensors and applies tensor-network models. In face verification, this can be misleading: modern embeddings are optimized for cosine similarity, and tensorization alone does not establish the low-rank structure needed for tensor trains or matrix-product operators (MPOs). We study model placement through two controlled interfaces. First, cross-model embedding alignment requires a 512 x 512 operator to map AdaFace embeddings into ArcFace space. Dense Ridge and Procrustes mappings remain strong, while deterministic TT-SVD and MPO compression underperform even with basis changes and low-rank residuals. At a 64K-parameter storage budget, mean directional AUC is 0.9297 for dense Ridge, 0.8952 for rank-64 SVD, 0.8685 for the best low-rank-MPO hybrid, and approximately 0.649 for raw MPO. Second, before global pooling, we construct a 7 x 7 x 7 x 7 probe-gallery correspondence tensor from local AdaFace descriptors. A geometry-aware symmetric tensor train with bond dimension 2 uses 122 trainable parameters, compared with 2,403 for a dense linear matcher, while attaining an LFW AUC of 0.9802. Yet compression does not translate into better low-false-acceptance performance: on CFP-FP, TAR at FAR = 0.001 is 0.0102 versus 0.1734 for global cosine matching, and on CPLFW it is 0.7805 versus 0.8737. These results suggest that quantum-inspired factorization is most credible when the chosen interface provides task-aligned axes and compressible structure. They also show why aggregate AUC must be assessed alongside operational low-FAR metrics.

</details>


### [232] [CellPlex: Benchmarking Single-Cell Spatial Proteomics Prediction from H&E](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02506)
*Zijun Gao,Yijiang Chen,Xiangde Luo,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CellPlex是一个细胞级基准，用七大队列（717样本、4480万细胞、149蛋白）在同一组细胞上统一评测从H&E推断单细胞空间蛋白组学的方法，发现适配的病理基础模型均最准，但跨队列泛化不足半数精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间蛋白组学成本高、样本量小，而H&E切片几乎每个患者都有；虚拟染色模型各自在自有队列、panel和像素级指标上评测，模型迁移性差，导致无法判断应基于何种方法、H&E究竟能揭示哪些蛋白。

Method: 构建CellPlex基准：七个同片H&E与多重免疫荧光队列（结直肠癌、乳腺癌、淋巴结转移、骨髓、胶质瘤），经统一流程处理为逐细胞目标；用同一细胞级协议适配病理基础模型（PFMs），在匹配预算下微调专用模型，并在相同细胞上统一评分。

Result: 适配的PFM在每个队列上均为最准模型，即使专用模型微调后仍不及；但PFM在未训练队列上仅保留不到一半精度；可预测的蛋白取决于组织类型，而非该蛋白的测量细胞数。

Conclusion: 通过固定评测细胞、目标与数据划分，CellPlex为从常规H&E推断昂贵分子测量设定统一标准，界定了H&E替代空间蛋白组学的上限；将公开数据、划分、评测代码与基线作为未来PFM与虚拟染色方法的测试平台。

Abstract: Spatial proteomics measures tens of proteins in every cell of a tissue section, but its cost confines it to small research cohorts, whereas hematoxylin and eosin (H\&E) slides exist for nearly every patient. Inferring single-cell spatial proteomics from H\&E would bring its analyses to routine pathology, and a growing number of virtual staining models pursue this goal. Each is evaluated on its own cohort, panel and pixel-level metric, and released models transfer poorly to new cohorts, leaving open which approach to build on and which proteins H\&E can reveal. We introduce CellPlex, a benchmark that poses the task at the level of the cell: seven cohorts with same-section H\&E and multiplexed immunofluorescence, from colorectal and breast cancer to lymph-node metastases, bone marrow and glioma, with 717 samples, 44.8 million cells and 149 proteins, processed by one pipeline into per-cell targets. Pathology foundation models (PFMs) are adapted with one cell-level protocol, specialist models are fine-tuned under a matched budget, and all are scored on the same cells. Adapted PFMs are the most accurate models on every cohort, ahead of the specialists even after fine-tuning, but keep less than half of their accuracy on cohorts they were not trained on. Which proteins can be predicted depends on the tissue, not on how many cells were measured for them. By fixing the cells, targets and splits on which predictions are judged, CellPlex sets a common standard for inferring costly molecular measurements from routine H\&E and shows how far H\&E can stand in for spatial proteomics. We will release the data, splits, evaluation code and baselines as a testbed for developing and comparing future pathology foundation models and virtual staining methods.

</details>


### [233] [Latent Spectral Prompting for Multimodal Hyperspectral Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02512)
*Wangshu Cai,Jun Zhou,Lei Wang,Mohammad Aminul Islam,Xu Han,Kuldip Paliwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SProTrack，通过潜在光谱提示（latent spectral prompting）将预训练 RGB 跟踪器的时间知识迁移至高光谱域，实现时序建模与光谱解耦，在 HOT2020/HOT2023 上取得最佳总体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱视频在伪装、低照度等挑战场景下具备丰富的光谱签名，但高光谱标注稀缺且 RGB-高光谱域间差异大，导致难以直接复用现代时序预训练的 RGB 跟踪器。

Method: 多模态框架，核心是将时序建模与光谱适配解耦：时序动态继承自 RGB 基础模型，高光谱信息以自监督方式从无标注数据学习。区别于丢弃大部分光谱信息的波段选择法与依赖局部搜索裁剪的提示方法，SProTrack 用自编码器从完整高光谱帧学习全局、场景级的光谱表示，该潜在表示用于引导光谱提示提取，同时预训练 RGB 跟踪器在 RGB 兼容输入上保持强时序建模能力；光谱-空间结构被编码为紧凑潜在提示。

Result: 在 HOT2020 与 HOT2023 数据集上取得最佳总体性能，并在各光谱域均表现稳健。

Conclusion: 将光谱-空间结构编码为紧凑潜在提示，可在无需大量重训练与稠密监督的情况下弥合 RGB-高光谱模态鸿沟，验证了时序-光谱解耦范式的有效性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral videos provide rich spectral signatures for robust tracking under challenging conditions such as camouflage and low illumination. However, limited hyperspectral annotations and the large RGB-hyperspectral domain gap hinder the direct use of modern temporally pretrained RGB trackers. We propose SProTrack, a multimodal framework that transfers temporal knowledge from pretrained RGB trackers to the hyperspectral domain through latent spectral prompting. The key idea is to decouple temporal modeling from spectral adaptation, allowing temporal dynamics to be inherited from RGB foundation models while hyperspectral information is learned from unlabeled data in a self-supervised manner. Unlike band-selection methods that discard most spectral information or prompt-based approaches that rely on local-search crops, SProTrack learns a global, scene-level spectral representation from the full hyperspectral frame using an autoencoder. This latent representation is used to guide spectral prompt extraction while a pretrained RGB tracker preserves strong temporal modeling on RGB-compatible inputs. By encoding spectral-spatial structure into compact latent prompts, SProTrack bridges the RGB-hyperspectral modality gap without extensive retraining or dense supervision. Experiments on HOT2020 and HOT2023 demonstrate that SProTrack achieves the best overall performance, with strong results across all spectral domains.

</details>


### [234] [MedHEB: Benchmarking Medical Embeddings Across Heterogeneous Clinical Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02519)
*Yingshu Li,Shaoyang Zhou,Zhanyu Wang,Yunyi Liu,Xinyu Liang,Xi Zhang,Lingqiao Liu,Lei Wang,Luping Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MedHEB统一医学检索基准（54个数据集、107个检索任务），从证据形式、配对类型、相关性粒度三轴评测嵌入模型，并发布 MedEmb 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床证据本质上是关系性的（需结合既往影像、跨模态检查、报告与局部病灶），而现有嵌入评测多聚焦孤立匹配，无法判断模型能否在模态、外观、粒度变化下检索到相关证据。

Method: 构建MedHEB：107个检索任务、54个数据集，含分布内与留出源评测；三维度划分——证据形式（2D图像/3D体数据/文本）、配对类型（图-图、图-文、文-文、跨模态视觉匹配）、相关性粒度（全局、问题条件化、区域级）；单一排序协议下变换临床相关证据定义；另训练 MLLM 基座参考嵌入模型 MedEmb（在MedHEB ID划分上训练）。

Result: 代表性模型在检索族间表现不均衡：在常规图-文、文-文检索上强的模型，在跨模态桥接与区域级任务上并不可靠；当前嵌入空间能捕捉部分临床关系，但尚未实现异质证据间的均衡检索。

Conclusion: 现有医学嵌入模型缺乏跨异质证据的均衡检索能力；MedHEB 与 MedEmb 作为可复现基线与开源资源（含预处理脚本、评测代码、访问清单）支撑临床有意义医学嵌入研究。

Abstract: Clinical evidence is inherently relational: medical images are interpreted with prior studies, cross-modal examinations, reports, and localized findings, and retrieving relevant evidence is therefore central to clinical interpretation. Recent foundation models increasingly encode these heterogeneous sources into shared embedding spaces, yet existing embedding evaluation often focuses on isolated matches, leaving unclear whether current models retrieve relevant evidence across changes in modality, appearance, and granularity. We introduce MedHEB, a unified benchmark with 107 retrieval tasks from 54 datasets, including both in-distribution and held-out-source evaluation, across three axes: evidence form (2D images, 3D volumes, text), pairing type (image--image, image--text, text--text, and modality-bridging visual matching), and relevance granularity (global, question-conditioned, region-level). Under a single ranking protocol, MedHEB varies clinically relevant evidence definitions to test whether embedding proximity remains meaningful across heterogeneous evidence, including modality-bridging visual retrieval and region-level text-to-region retrieval. Across representative models, performance is uneven across retrieval families: models strong on conventional image--text or text--text retrieval are less reliable on modality-bridging and region-level tasks. Current embedding spaces capture some clinical relationships well but do not yet provide balanced retrieval across heterogeneous evidence. We further provide MedEmb, an MLLM-based reference embedding model trained on the MedHEB ID split, as a reproducible baseline for unified 2D--3D--text medical retrieval. We release MedHEB with preprocessing scripts, evaluation code, access manifests, and MedEmb to support research on clinically meaningful medical embeddings.

</details>


### [235] [INTERACT: Learning Interaction-Aware World Dynamics from Driving Videos for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02526)
*Zhan Chen,Songyan Zhang,Wenhui Huang,Zirui Li,Zhidong Wang,Qihang Huang,Shiyu Fang,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出INTERACT三阶段框架，从无标注驾驶视频学习槽位式物体中心世界模型并蒸馏至轨迹规划器，实现交互感知、动作条件化的轨迹学习，在NAVSIM上取得SOTA并在碰撞安全指标上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶不仅需识别当前场景，还需预测周围交通参与者在自车运动下的演化。但现有端到端模型将场景编码为稠密视觉或BEV特征，物体级交互仅隐式存在，缺乏显式的实体级交互与自车动作耦合建模。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 从无标注驾驶视频学习循环槽位（recurrent slots），将稠密视觉特征聚合为具有时序结构的场景实体表示，无需物体标注；(2) 由槽位上下文与视频帧间转移推断隐式自车动作（latent ego actions），并以之作为条件预测未来槽位，刻画自车运动与周围实体的协同演化；(3) 将槽位、隐式动作与未来槽位预测蒸馏进轨迹规划器，用于引导轨迹生成与选择。

Result: 在NAVSIM与nuScenes上验证有效性；在NAVSIM上达到SOTA，尤其在与安全密切相关的碰撞类指标上取得提升。

Conclusion: 以无监督方式获得的物体中心槽位世界模型可为规划提供显式交互先验，动作条件化未来预测能有效提升轨迹规划的安全性与整体性能。

Abstract: Safe autonomous driving requires more than recognizing the current scene: the ego vehicle must anticipate how surrounding traffic participants will evolve under its own motion. However, existing end-to-end driving models typically encode scenes into dense visual or BEV features, leaving object-level interactions implicit. We propose (INTERACT INTERaction-aware Action-Conditioned Trajectory learning), a three-stage framework that learns a slot-based object-centric world model from unlabeled driving videos and transfers it to trajectory planning. INTERACT first learns recurrent slots that group dense visual features into temporally structured scene-entity representations without object annotations. It then infers latent ego actions from slot context and video transitions, and uses them to condition future slot prediction, capturing how ego motion and surrounding entities co-evolve. Finally, the learned slots, latent actions, and future slot predictions are distilled into a trajectory planner to guide trajectory generation and selection. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM and nuScenes validate the effectiveness of INTERACT. In particular, it achieves state-of-the-art performance on NAVSIM, with improvements in safety-critical collision metrics.

</details>


### [236] [PixelBench: Can LLMs Understand and Generate Images Pixel by Pixel?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02540)
*Haoquan Zhang,Feiyang Liu,Juanxi Tian,Wanhua Li,Haodong Duan,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PixelBench 基准，考察 LLM 不借助图像分词器/解码器，直接以"调色板+像素索引网格"文本序列生成像素图像（T2P/I2P）并理解像素的能力，并提出 Pixel Reconstruction Tuning (PRT) 作为有效辅助监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 生成视觉资产依赖 SVG、CAD 等高层符号原语，无法直接输出显式栅格像素值。作者追问：LLM 能否仅用自身词表直接生成像素图像，而无需学习式图像分词器或解码器。

Method: 构建 PixelBench，涵盖多分辨率的文生像素（T2P）与图生像素（I2P）任务，并配套像素理解评测；模型先生成 RGB 调色板，再输出指定每像素调色板颜色的索引网格。开展分辨率缩放研究与理解—生成相关性分析；提出 PRT，在视觉指令微调中混入"caption–palette–grid"轨迹，从预训练检查点后训练 Molmo2-4B。

Result: T2P 中强模型可直接从文本生成可辨识图像；I2P 在低分辨率下优于调色板匹配降采样与专用像素化算法；像素理解与生成性能强相关；生成性能随分辨率升高先升后降；PRT 后在 16 个视觉中心基准上均获提升。

Conclusion: LLM 具备通过原生词表进行像素级生成与理解的潜力，显式像素重建可作为有用的辅助监督信号，为无分词器的视觉生成提供新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate visual assets through text-based formats such as SVG and CAD, but these symbolic representations describe images using high-level primitives rather than explicit raster values. We ask whether LLMs can instead generate pixel images directly through their existing vocabulary, without a learned image tokenizer or decoder. We introduce PixelBench, a benchmark for text-to-pixel (T2P) and image-to-pixel (I2P) generation across multiple resolutions, together with a complementary pixel-understanding evaluation. Models generate an RGB color palette followed by a grid of indices specifying the palette color of each output pixel. In T2P, the strongest models produce recognizable images directly from text. In I2P, the strongest models outperform palette-matched downsampling and specialized pixelization algorithms at low resolutions. Pixel-understanding performance correlates strongly with generation performance. A resolution-scaling study shows that pixel-generation performance often improves initially before declining at high resolutions.
