Finally, we introduce Pixel Reconstruction Tuning (PRT), which augments visual instruction tuning with caption-palette-grid trajectories. We post-train Molmo2-4B from its pretraining checkpoint by mixing general instruction data with our curated PRT data, improving performance across 16 visual-centric benchmarks, indicating that explicit pixel reconstruction can provide useful auxiliary supervision.

</details>


### [237] [Flat Features, Detailed Images? Simple Detail Control for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02551)
*Qingtao Yu,Zhen Wang,Jiyang Zheng,Minghao Sun,Hongdong Li,Dylan Campbell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练方法，通过按标量连续调整token能量分布熵来控制扩散Transformer生成图像的细节层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示工程虽能让文生图模型生成更细节丰富的图像，但缺乏可靠、连续且解耦的控制能力。

Method: 分析六种扩散Transformer在有无细节增强提示下产生的特征token，发现提示会提升每个token能量分布的熵而基本保持其排序不变；据此将每个token的通道平方幅值向更高熵分布偏移，并配合时间感知调度器将干预集中于中间采样步，实现免训练、仅数行代码、推理开销可忽略的连续细节控制。

Result: 在多种扩散Transformer上的大量实验表明，该方法可连续控制细节指标，同时基本保持图像质量与语义结构。

Conclusion: "熵提升即细节增加"这一反直觉发现揭示了细节控制的机制，并支撑了一种简单有效的连续细节调节方案。

Abstract: Prompt engineering is commonly used to encourage text-to-image models to generate more detailed images. However, it does not provide reliable, continuous, or disentangled control. We analyze feature tokens produced with and without detail-enhancing prompts across six diffusion transformers and observe a common pattern: these prompts tend to increase the entropy of each token's energy distribution while largely preserving its ordering. That is, flattening the distribution increases the level of detail. Motivated by this counter-intuitive finding, we introduce a simple training-free approach that continuously adjusts the level of detail according to a scalar. Specifically, it shifts the squared channel magnitudes of each token toward a higher entropy distribution with a time-aware scheduler to concentrate the intervention at intermediate sampling steps. Across extensive experiments on different types of diffusion transformers, we show that our approach provides continuous control over detail metrics while largely preserving image quality and semantic structure. It requires only a few lines of code and adds negligible inference time overhead.

</details>


### [238] [SeMo3D: Unifying 3D Generation, Editing, and Completion via Selective Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02568)
*Xiaokun Sun,Jian Yang,Zhenyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Selective Modeling 范式并构建 SeMo3D，将 3D 生成、编辑、补全统一为"在保持其余部分不变的前提下合成指定区域"的单一定向区域建模任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 生成/编辑/补全通常都按"重新生成完整资产"处理，即使只需修改局部，导致三类任务彼此割裂、本应不变的内容出现不一致，且依赖任务专属的配对数据。

Method: 关键观察：三任务本质都是合成指定区域而保留其余内容——生成对应全区域、编辑对应目标区域、补全对应缺失区域。训练上用 region-selective denoising：在标准 3D 资产上随机选取不同大小的区域，仅对其加噪并计算损失，从而联合学习三类任务、无需配对数据。架构上提出 Omni3DiT，多条件 3D 扩散 Transformer，区分待生成区域与待保留区域，融合图像、文本与点云引导实现细粒度控制。

Result: 在三个基准上，SeMo3D 在生成、编辑、补全三类任务中均超越 SOTA；生成具备多模态可控性，编辑与补全具有强一致性与高准确度；还可作为 agent 的统一建模工具，通过自然语言交互完成高效 3D 创作。

Conclusion: SeMo3D 以统一的选择性建模范式打破任务隔离，天然保证未修改内容一致性，并摆脱对任务专属配对数据的依赖，验证了统一 3D 建模的可行性与实用价值。

Abstract: 3D generation, editing, and completion are typically treated as regenerating the complete 3D asset under task-specific constraints, even when only a local region needs modification. This leads to task isolation, inconsistency in content that should remain unchanged, and dependence on task-specific paired data. We address these issues with a key observation: the three tasks all synthesize a designated region while keeping the rest intact---the full region for generation, the target region for editing, and the missing region for completion. We term this paradigm Selective Modeling and build SeMo3D upon it; it naturally unifies the three tasks, preserves unchanged content by construction, and enables training without paired editing or completion data, since any 3D asset can be partitioned into a selected region and its context. For training, we propose region-selective denoising, which randomly selects regions of varying sizes on standard 3D assets and applies noise and loss only to them, jointly learning all three tasks. For architecture, we propose Omni3DiT, a multi-condition 3D diffusion transformer that distinguishes regions to create from those to retain and fuses image, text, and point guidance for fine-grained control. Experiments across three benchmarks show SeMo3D surpasses state-of-the-art methods on all tasks, delivering multimodal controllability in generation and strong consistency and accuracy in editing and completion. SeMo3D further serves as a unified modeling tool for agents, enabling efficient 3D creation through natural-language interaction. Project page: https://xiaokunsun.github.io/SeMo3D.github.io/.

</details>


### [239] [SMILE: A Structured Multi-Agent Clinical Reasoning for Liver Ultrasound Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02581)
*Haris Ghafoor,Minjee Seo,Seonaeng cho,Keungmo Yang,Jaejun Lee,Kyungho Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SMILE——结构化多智能体临床推理框架，用三个互补专家智能体解读肝脏超声图像，经判官VLM共识过滤整合输出，在公开与内部数据集上显著优于现有VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动生成肝脏超声报告可提升肝病诊断，但现有生成式视觉语言模型多依赖单一推理路径，直接将超声外观映射为文本描述。除固有的视觉-语言模态鸿沟外，该设计在肝脏超声上受限：疾病判读依赖细微视觉证据、疾病模式异质、配对监督有限。

Method: SMILE采用多智能体结构化临床推理：①图像解读智能体负责形态学描述；②临床分期推理智能体；③视觉问答（VQA）智能体处理针对性查询。三者响应经判官VLM以共识过滤提示词聚合与审查，生成统一、临床连贯的输出。

Result: 在公开肝脏超声数据集与内部临床数据集上，SMILE持续优于SOTA视觉语言模型：ROUGE-1最高提升19.0%，临床F1提升18.8%；VQA F1在公开与内部数据集分别达0.82与0.65。

Conclusion: SMILE在多智能体协作与共识整合下有效缓解单一推理路径的局限，展示其在数据稀缺、监督有限临床场景中的潜力；模型、数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated liver ultrasound report generation improves the diagnosis of liver diseases by producing clinically structured descriptions of anatomical and disease-related findings. However, current generative vision-language models (VLMs) often rely on a single reasoning pathway that maps ultrasound appearance directly to textual descriptions. In addition to the inherent vision-language modality gap, this design is limited for liver ultrasound because disease interpretation is constrained by subtle visual evidence, heterogeneous disease patterns, and limited paired supervision. To address these limitations, we propose SMILE, a Structured Multi-agent clInicaL rEasoning framework that uses multiple complementary specialist agents to interpret ultrasound images and consolidates their outputs through a judge-guided consensus mechanism. Our approach employs three specialized agents: an image interpretation agent for morphological descriptions, a clinical-stage reasoning agent, and a visual question answering (VQA) agent for targeted queries. These agent responses are then aggregated and scrutinized by a judge VLM using a consensus filtering prompt to produce a unified, clinically coherent output. Across a public liver ultrasound dataset and an internal clinical dataset, SMILE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art VLMs, achieving up to 19.0\% improvement in ROUGE-1 and 18.8\% improvement in clinical F1-score, while reaching VQA F1-scores of 0.82 and 0.65 on public and internal datasets, respectively. These results demonstrate the potential of SMILE for data-scarce and limited-supervision clinical settings. Our models, datasets, and code are available \href{https://github.com/CMME-Lab/SMILE}{here}.

</details>


### [240] [AVID: A 5T fMRI Dataset for Benchmarking Auditory-induced Visual Mental Imagery Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02593)
*Shiqi Shen,Shurui Li,Xilin Zhang,Yuanning Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 AVID——首个面向听觉诱导视觉心理意象（VMI）解码的专用训练规模 5.0T fMRI 数据集（约67小时、9名被试），包含自然场景级与属性可控两级划分，并形式化了一套将源图像一致性与语义对齐解耦的标准化评测协议；基线实验显示现有重建流程在该数据集上仍受限，但可保留可识别的场景级语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VMI解码研究受限于缺乏专用、训练规模的数据集：主流范式依赖视觉提示或短时记忆图像，引入视觉与记忆混淆，难以将内生意象构建相关神经活动与先前/同时视觉输入驱动的信号分离；同时已有数据集对视觉属性的描述性控制不足，难以系统评估细粒度视觉细节。因此需要减少感觉污染、具备属性控制的专用VMI资源与评测基准。

Method: 构建AVID数据集：采用听觉（普通话描述）诱导VMI，采集5.0T fMRI，约67小时密集记录、9名被试；数据划分为两个互补子集——使用自然场景描述的scene-level complex split，以及显式指定视觉属性的controlled simple split。同时形式化标准化评测协议，将源图像一致性与语义对齐两类指标解耦。

Result: 基线的定性与定量结果均表明，现有重建流程在AVID上表现仍然有限；表现最好的重建结果能保留来自语音描述的可识别场景级语义，但细粒度属性重建效果不足。

Conclusion: AVID通过训练规模的专用神经记录与标准化评测资源，为意象特异性模型开发提供数据基础，并提供显著降低感觉污染的VMI解码基准。

Abstract: Visual mental imagery (VMI) decoding uses brain activity to reconstruct internally generated visual scenes, providing a potential pathway for externalizing internal experiences and developing neuroprosthetic communication interfaces. However, progress is limited by the lack of dedicated, training-scale VMI datasets. Current VMI paradigms often rely on visual prompts or short-term memory images, introducing visual and mnemonic confounds that make it difficult to disentangle neural activity associated with endogenous image construction from signals driven by prior or concurrent visual input. Furthermore, existing VMI datasets offer limited descriptive control over individual visual attributes, making it difficult to systematically assess fine grained visual details. To address these gaps, we introduce AVID, the first dedicated training-scale 5.0 Tesla functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) resource for auditory-induced VMI decoding. AVID comprises approximately 67 hours of dense recordings from nine participants. The dataset is structured into two complementary splits: a scene-level complex split using naturalistic Mandarin descriptions and a controlled simple split with explicitly specified visual attributes. We further formalize a standardized evaluation protocol that decouples source-image consistency from semantic alignment, allowing for a more nuanced assessment of imagery reconstruction. Baseline qualitative and quantitative results show that current reconstruction pipelines remain limited on AVID, although the most successful reconstructions preserve recognizable scene level semantics from spoken descriptions. By combining dedicated training-scale neural recordings and standardized evaluation resources, AVID provides both a data foundation for imagery-specific model development and a benchmark for evaluating VMI decoding with substantially reduced sensory contamination.

</details>


### [241] [BGD-SF PolySegNet: boundary-guided dynamic selective fusion network for robust polyp segmentation in colonoscopy images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02603)
*Nishi Kanta Paul,Shamia Maherin,Morsheda Akter,Moinul Hossain,Md Kishor Morol*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出边界引导的分割网络 BGD-SF PolySegNet，将边界信息作为架构级组件贯穿多尺度上下文聚合、跳跃特征融合与掩码细化三阶段，在边界指标上显著优于 CNN 基线，35 FPS 实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜息肉分割对结直肠癌早期筛查至关重要，现有深度模型在 Dice 等区域指标上表现良好，但边界分割仍困难。多数方法仅将边界作为训练期附加损失信号，网络无法显式进行边界引导的特征选择或掩码细化。

Method: 编码器-解码器架构，骨干为预训练 EfficientNet-B4，含三个模块：边界引导动态级联多尺度上下文聚合（BGD-CMSCA）、边界引导选择性注意力融合（BG-SAGF）、多级边界引导头（MBGH）；在 Kvasir-SEG、BKAI-IGH NeoPolyp-Small 与 PolypGen 共 3,535 张标注图像上训练，在四个外部基准上评测。

Result: 内部测试集 Dice 0.887、IoU 0.839、边界 F1 0.531、HD95 30.32、ASSD 18.10，边界 F1/HD95/ASSD 分别较最强 CNN 基线提升 15.7%、27.5%、34.6%；CVC-ClinicDB、CVC-ColonDB、ETIS-Larib、CVC-300 外部 Dice 为 0.891/0.776/0.863/0.885；35 FPS、22.51M 参数；Wilcoxon 符号秩检验显示相对 7 个消融变体中 6 个边界 F1 提升显著（p<0.001，r=0.215–0.434）。

Conclusion: 将边界引导作为主动的架构组件而非仅损失约束，可切实提升息肉边界刻画能力；但提升幅度在弱效应量区间，且 ETIS-Larib 上跨域 Dice 明显偏低，泛化性与临床部署仍待进一步验证。

Abstract: Effective polyp segmentation in colonoscopy images is crucial for early detection of colorectal cancer, a leading cause of cancer-related death worldwide. While current deep learning models perform well on traditional Dice benchmarks, border segmentation remains difficult. Most methods use boundary information only as an additional loss signal during training, which prevents the network from explicitly learning border-guided feature selection or mask refinement. This paper introduces BGD-SF PolySegNet, a boundary-guided encoder-decoder architecture that controls three phases of the segmentation pipeline: dynamic multi-scale context aggregation, selective skip-feature fusion, and final mask refinement. The architecture comprises a pretrained EfficientNet-B4 backbone and three proposed modules: Boundary-Guided Dynamic Cascaded Multi-Scale Context Aggregation (BGD-CMSCA), Boundary-Guided Selective Attention Fusion (BG-SAGF), and a Multi-Level Boundary Guidance Head (MBGH). The model is trained on 3,535 annotated images from Kvasir-SEG, BKAI-IGH NeoPolyp-Small, and PolypGen, and evaluated on four held-out benchmark datasets. On the internal test set, BGD-SF achieves a Dice of 0.887, IoU of 0.839, mask-derived Boundary F1 of 0.531, HD95 of 30.32, and ASSD of 18.10, improving Boundary F1, HD95, and ASSD by 15.7%, 27.5%, and 34.6% over the strongest CNN baselines. External validation on CVC-ClinicDB, CVC-ColonDB, ETIS-Larib, and CVC-300 yielded Dice scores of 0.891, 0.776, 0.863, and 0.885, respectively. The model runs at 35 FPS with 22.51M parameters. Wilcoxon signed-rank tests confirm statistically significant mask-derived Boundary F1 improvements over six of seven ablation variants (p < 0.001; small-to-moderate effect sizes, r = 0.215-0.434), demonstrating that integrating boundary guidance as an active architectural component measurably advances polyp boundary delineation.

</details>


### [242] [Latent Diffusion-GAN: Adversarial Learning in the Autoencoded Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02625)
*U-Chae Jun,Jaeeun Ko,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LD-GAN框架，将预训练VAE复用为基于能量的判别器，把对抗学习无缝集成进潜在扩散模型流程，无需额外参数即可提升高频细节保真度与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于似然（如去噪分数匹配）的扩散训练会使高频细节（细纹理、锐利边缘）局部过平滑，限制感知保真度与结构细节；而GAN对抗训练虽增强锐度，但需额外判别器网络，增加计算成本并使训练不稳定。

Method: LD-GAN不改动扩散模型原始流程，将潜在扩散模型中的预训练VAE用作基于能量的判别器（EBM判别器），实现无额外参数的对抗训练并保留大规模数据学到的结构化潜在先验；同时引入结构一致性能量，对齐编码器与解码器特征表示，以提升感知质量及与预训练潜在空间的兼容性。

Result: 大量实验表明，在多种生成任务上，LD-GAN相较SOTA基线显著提升样本保真度、感知锐度与多样性，并保持高效的训练动态。

Conclusion: 通过复用VAE作为能量判别器并施加结构一致性约束，可在不增加参数与破坏原流程的前提下，以对抗学习弥补扩散似然训练的过平滑问题，兼顾感知质量与训练稳定性。

Abstract: Diffusion models are powerful generative frameworks for producing high-quality images by denoising latent variables from random noise. However, training with likelihood-based objectives, such as denoising score matching, can lead to locally over-smoothed high-frequency details, including fine textures and sharp edges, thereby limiting perceptual fidelity and structural detail. Adversarial training with GANs enhances sharpness but typically requires additional discriminator networks, increasing computational costs and destabilizing training. To this end, we propose Latent Diffusion Generative Adversarial Networks (LD-GAN), a novel framework that seamlessly integrates adversarial learning into diffusion models without modifying their original pipeline. LD-GAN leverages the pretrained variational autoencoder (VAE) in latent diffusion models as an energy-based discriminator, enabling adversarial training without extra parameters and preserving the structured latent priors learned from large datasets. We also introduce a structural consistency energy that aligns encoder and decoder feature representations, thereby enhancing perceptual quality and compatibility with the pretrained latent space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LD-GAN significantly improves sample fidelity, perceptual sharpness, and diversity over state-of-the-art baseline methods across various generation tasks while ensuring efficient training dynamics.

</details>


### [243] [UniCross3D: Unified Cross-View and Cross-Domain Diffusion for Consistent Single-Image 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02631)
*U-Chae Jun,Jaeeun Ko,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniCross3D 统一跨视角与跨域扩散框架，通过跨视角潜变量正则与跨域互信息目标解决单图三维重建中的视角几何一致性与跨域语义对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张 RGB 图像重建细节几何与真实外观因遮挡、光照变化及纹理-几何纠缠而存在本质歧义。现有扩散生成模型虽提升了新视角合成质量，但存在两大缺陷：缺乏跨视角几何一致性，以及跨域语义对齐不足。

Method: 提出 UniCross3D，包含两项核心设计：(1) 跨视角潜变量正则（cross-view latent regularization），通过惩罚潜变量方差强制合成视角间的几何一致性；(2) 基于图像成像物理的跨域互信息目标（cross-domain mutual information objective），显式对齐合成的颜色图与法线图。

Result: 大量实验表明，UniCross3D 在视角一致性与语义对齐上显著优于现有 SOTA 方法，并在复杂纹理与歧义视角等挑战性场景下获得更高保真度的重建结果。

Conclusion: 将跨视角一致性与跨域语义对齐统一纳入扩散框架，可有效缓解单图三维重建的固有歧义，验证了物理先验驱动的正则化在一致且物理合理的三维生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Reconstructing detailed geometry and realistic appearance from a single RGB image is essential yet fundamentally challenging due to inherent ambiguities such as occlusion, lighting variations, and texture-geometry entanglement. While recent diffusion-based generative models have significantly improved novel view synthesis, existing approaches suffer from two critical limitations: lack of cross-view geometric consistency and insufficient cross-domain semantic alignment. To address these issues, we introduce UniCross3D, a unified cross-view and cross-domain diffusion framework designed explicitly for consistent and physically coherent 3D generation. UniCross3D features two novel contributions: (1) a cross-view latent regularization that enforces cross-view geometric consistency across synthesized viewpoints by penalizing latent variance, and (2) a cross-domain mutual information objective grounded in the physics of image formation, explicitly aligning synthesized color and normal maps. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UniCross3D achieves significantly improved view consistency and semantic alignment over state-of-the-art methods and yields higher-fidelity reconstructions, particularly under challenging textures and ambiguous viewpoints.

</details>


### [244] [ReinSeg: Retrieval-Guided Bounded Multimodal Refinement for 3-D Road Anomaly Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02648)
*Haifa Zhang,Yijing Wang,Zhiqiang Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReinSeg：以检索引导的有界多模态残差精化，将环视图像上下文注入LiDAR点特征，在不改变冻结LIDO打分接口的前提下提升3-D道路异常点的排序与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3-D道路异常分割的核心难点在于：稀疏观测的危险目标需排在大量正常路面结构之前，同时保持逐点度量定位。LiDAR提供精确3-D支撑，环视图像提供互补语义以区分异常与易混淆的正常结构，但需避免破坏已有异常打分接口。

Method: 提出检索引导的有界多模态精化框架。以占据的LiDAR鸟瞰栅格为单位，从共享记忆中的8路环视图像检索场景特定上下文（检索模块不输入标定变量）；通过相对范数约束的残差将该上下文回注LiDAR点特征，从而保留冻结的LIDO异常打分接口，且仅用分布内语义标签优化精化模块。

Result: STU基准：验证集AP由27.54%升至35.67%，95%异常召回下FPR由34.85%降至10.61%，高召回筛选集由67.54M点缩至20.55M点；相对REL，AP高24.99个百分点、FPR95低0.99个百分点。接口匹配与组件隔离实验显示检索到的场景图像与相对范数约束均为必要；相机缺失、损坏或失配时仍优于纯LiDAR，速度21.8 FPS。

Conclusion: 对场景特定视觉证据进行有界检索，是通往可靠3-D道路危险感知的有效路径。

Abstract: 3-D road anomaly segmentation is essential for risk-aware perception in autonomous driving. Its central challenge is to rank sparsely observed hazards ahead of abundant normal road structures while preserving point-wise metric localization. LiDAR provides accurate 3-D point support, whereas surround-view imagery offers complementary semantic context for separating unexpected hazards from challenging normal structures. We propose ReinSeg, a retrieval-guided bounded multimodal refinement framework for point-wise road anomaly segmentation. Occupied LiDAR bird's-eye-view cells retrieve scene-specific context from a shared memory of eight surround-view images without supplying calibration variables to the retrieval module. A relative-norm-bounded residual returns this context to the LiDAR point features, so the frozen LIDO anomaly-scoring interface is preserved, and the refinement is optimized with in-distribution semantic labels only. On the Spotting the Unexpected (STU) benchmark, ReinSeg improves validation average precision from 27.54\% to 35.67\% and reduces the false-positive rate at 95\% anomaly recall from 34.85\% to 10.61\%, shrinking the high-recall screening set from 67.54 to 20.55 million points. Relative to REL, AP is 24.99 percentage points higher and FPR95 is 0.99 points lower. Matched interfaces and component isolation show that retrieved scene imagery and the relative-norm bound are both essential, and ReinSeg stays above LiDAR-only performance under missing, corrupted, or misaligned camera inputs while running at 21.8 frames per second. These results establish bounded retrieval of scene-specific visual evidence as an effective route to reliable 3-D road-hazard perception.

</details>


### [245] [Computer Vision Applications for Art History: Reflections and paradigms for future research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02649)
*Amalia Foka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文从计算机科学视角系统梳理艺术史期刊论文，识别艺术史家实际使用的图像特征，并将其转译为计算机视觉可理解的术语，进而提出连接计算机视觉与艺术史核心研究问题的范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 艺术史家对计算方法的持续争论，部分源于现有计算研究未触及当代艺术史研究的核心问题；两个研究群体缺乏紧密合作，使得将艺术史方法论转译为良定义的计算机视觉问题极为困难。

Method: 设计一套方法论，从计算机科学视角分析艺术史期刊中的文章，提取艺术史家在研究中使用的图像特征，并用直接、可操作的术语向计算机视觉研究群体进行描述与映射。

Result: 归纳出艺术史研究中所使用的一类图像特征，并给出面向计算机视觉的术语化描述，形成两学科间的特征—任务对应关系。

Conclusion: 提出若干新范式，作为探索"计算机视觉应用如何切中当代艺术史研究核心"的起点，强调跨学科紧密协作的重要性。

Abstract: One of the contributing factors to the continuing debate among art historians over the use of computational methods in art history research is that they do not consider the core of today's art history research questions. The lack of close collaboration between the two involved research communities makes the definition of contemporary art-historical methods as well-defined computer vision problems extremely difficult. For that purpose, it is devised as a methodology to study articles in art history journals from a computer science perspective. The objective is to identify which image features art historians utilise within their research and describe them in immediate and meaningful terms to the computer vision research community. Finally, some paradigms that could serve as a new starting point for exploring how computer vision applications for art history can address the core of today's art history research are given.

</details>


### [246] [Probe-Guided Gradient Balancing for Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02651)
*Vu Vo,Haytham M. Fayek,Thuy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出探针引导梯度增强(PGGB)，用各模态独立质量分数之差(利用率差距)作为不平衡信号，自适应放大弱模态梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合训练的多模态网络中，学习快的模态主导共享融合目标并压制流向慢模态的梯度，导致弱模态利用不足。现有"抑制强者"方法有效但直接"增强弱者"常失败，因为基于联合损失的监测信号仍被强模态主导。

Method: 在每个模态的stop-gradient特征上接轻量线性分类器，独立估计各模态表征质量；用这些分数之间的差值(utilization gap)作为不平衡信号，通过有界且平滑的调制自适应缩放弱模态梯度；不改变损失函数与融合结构，可与抑制类方法组合。

Result: 在覆盖4个领域、2–4个模态的八个基准上，PGGB在高不平衡多模态数据集上优于SOTA，在低/可忽略不平衡下仍具竞争力；理论上给出有界缩放、自衰减性质，以及在平滑性与有界方差假设下标准SGD的下降界。

Conclusion: 以表征质量探针取代联合损失作为监测信号，可在不抑制强模态的前提下有效缓解模态不平衡，兼具方法可组合性与理论保证。

Abstract: Jointly trained multimodal networks frequently underutilize weaker modalities, as faster-learning modalities dominate the shared fusion objective and suppress gradients reaching slower ones. Most existing approaches throttle dominant modalities, whereas directly boosting weaker modalities often fails because monitoring signals derived from the joint loss remain dominated by stronger modalities. We propose Probe-Guided Gradient Boosting (PGGB), a gradient modulation approach that boosts weaker modalities while preserving the strength of dominant ones. PGGB attaches lightweight linear classifiers to stop-gradient features from each modality to estimate representation quality independently. The gap between these scores, termed the utilization gap, provides an imbalance signal used to adaptively scale weaker-modality gradients through bounded and smoothed modulation. PGGB is composable with throttling methods, as it leaves the loss function and fusion architecture unchanged. Experiments across eight benchmarks spanning four domains and 2-4 modalities demonstrate that PGGB outperforms state-of-the-art methods on highly imbalanced multimodal datasets while remaining competitive under low or negligible imbalance. We further establish bounded scaling, self-attenuation, and a descent bound for standard SGD under smoothness and bounded-variance assumptions. Code is available at https://github.com/VuVoCodes/PGGB.

</details>


### [247] [VerdictAD: Few-Shot Anomaly Detection via Decision-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02654)
*Shangquan Sun,Wenjun Luo,Yuanzhi Wang,Ruiqi Li,Ruoyu Chen,Jiachen Liu,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 VerdictAD，通过类型化决策模型对查询图像及局部区域进行视觉决策，以互补证据精炼现有异常检测器，作为插件在 one-shot/few-shot 场景下提升多个检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本异常检测依赖查询图像与正常参考之间的特征差异度量，但视觉差异并不总等于真实缺陷：正常外观变化也会产生高异常分，而结构性/逻辑性异常可能缺乏显著局部特征差异，导致误检与漏检。

Method: VerdictAD 为免训练、即插即用框架，不替换原检测器。采用类型化决策模型，将查询图像及其局部区域与少量正常参考对比，直接输出结构化决策概率（无需文本生成），提供互补证据。构建两个模块：Global Verdict Refinement（GVR）利用图像级决策精炼整体异常分数；Local Verdict Refinement（LVR）聚合区域决策精炼异常图；并与基于 DINOv2 的简单检测器结合。

Result: 结合 DINOv2 检测器时，在 one-shot 与 few-shot 异常检测中取得有竞争力性能；作为插件在 MVTec AD、VisA、MVTec LOCO AD 上一致提升现有检测器，one-shot 与 few-shot 设置下均表现强劲。

Conclusion: 以视觉决策作为互补证据可有效克服单纯特征差异度量的局限，VerdictAD 以即插即用方式通用地增强现有少样本异常检测器，代码与实验日志已公开。

Abstract: Detecting defects in real-world industrial inspection often requires learning from only a few normal images per category. Recent few-shot anomaly detection methods have achieved strong performance by measuring feature differences between query images and normal references. However, visual differences do not always indicate genuine defects, as normal appearance variations can also produce high anomaly scores. Conversely, structural or logical anomalies may not produce strong local feature differences, making them difficult to detect. To address this limitation, we introduce VerdictAD, a training-free framework that refines existing anomaly detectors through visual decisions. Rather than replacing existing detectors, VerdictAD adopts a typed decision model to assess the query image and its local regions against a few normal references. By directly producing structured decision probabilities without text generation, the model provides complementary evidence for refining anomaly responses at different spatial scales. Building on this capability, we introduce two modules: Global Verdict Refinement (GVR) uses image-level decisions to refine the overall anomaly score, while Local Verdict Refinement (LVR) aggregates regional decisions to refine the anomaly map. Combined with a simple DINOv2-based detector, VerdictAD achieves competitive performance in one-shot and few-shot anomaly detection. More importantly, as a plug-and-play framework, it consistently improves existing anomaly detectors across MVTec AD, VisA, and MVTec LOCO AD, achieving strong results across one-shot and few-shot settings. Our code and experimental logs are publicly available at https://github.com/sgstq0811/VerdictAD-Jev-AD.

</details>


### [248] [OctreeSplat: Hierarchical Octree-Guided Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02658)
*Yibin Zhao,Yihan Pan,Wenli Yang,Jun Nan,Jianjun Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OctreeSplat：一种层次八叉树引导的前馈3D高斯泼溅方法，按局部复杂度自适应分配高斯基元，结合随机实体体渲染与可选测试时优化，在更少基元下实现竞争的视图合成、深度合成与表面重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈方法避免了逐场景优化，但固定图像/空间分辨率限制了基元按局部复杂度分配的能力。

Method: 从冻结的视觉基础模型（VFM）提取多视图特征与几何先验，构建层次八叉树并预测加密分数（densification scores），实现空间变化密度的高斯基元分配；采用随机实体体渲染（stochastic solids volume rendering）改进深度渲染与几何监督；在具备相机参数时引入可选测试时优化（TTO）模块。

Result: 仅训练7600万额外参数、35 GPU小时；在Replica、TUM、Tanks and Temples、DTU上RGB视图合成、深度合成与表面重建均具竞争力，并在保持高质量的同时减少高斯基元数量。

Conclusion: 层次八叉树引导的自适应基元分配可在前馈框架下以更低参数与算力成本、更少高斯基元获得高质量NVS与重建效果。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has shown considerable potential for novel view synthesis (NVS) and 3D reconstruction. Feed-forward methods avoid costly per-scene optimization, but fixed image or spatial resolutions limit their ability to allocate primitives according to local complexity. We present OctreeSplat, a hierarchical octree-guided feed-forward Gaussian splatting. OctreeSplat extracts multi-view features and geometric priors from a frozen visual foundation model (VFM), constructs a hierarchical octree, and predicts densification scores. The resulting representation allocates Gaussian primitives at spatially varying densities according to local complexity. Stochastic solids volume rendering improves depth rendering, strengthens geometric supervision, and supports surface reconstruction. When camera parameters are available, an optional test-time optimization (TTO) module improves agreement between predicted and ground-truth parameters. Training updates only 76 million additional parameters and requires 35 GPU-hours. Experiments on Replica, TUM, Tanks and Temples, and DTU demonstrate competitive RGB view synthesis, depth synthesis, and surface reconstruction. OctreeSplat reduces the number of Gaussian primitives while maintaining high reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [249] [PriCBMs: Multi-granular Concept Bottleneck Vision-Language Models for Visual Privacy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02661)
*Shenglei Pei,Haotian Sun,Pengfei Li,Bingxu Lu,Wenlong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出隐私概念瓶颈模型(PriCBMs)，以隐私相关概念作为中间语义表示，先做视觉-概念匹配识别类别相关隐私线索，再预测隐私类别，提升多标签视觉隐私识别的性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体的广泛图像共享使视觉隐私识别需求上升。多标签视觉隐私识别存在两大挑战：(1) 隐私类别是高层语义属性，与视觉物体并非一一对应——不同物体可能对应同一隐私类别，同一类物体也可能包含不同隐私敏感信息，直接预测方法缺乏对类别相关隐私线索的显式引导；(2) 隐私线索常分散于图像各区域，整图特征难以充分表达。

Method: 采用概念瓶颈结构：引入隐私相关概念作为中间语义表示，通过视觉-概念匹配识别类别相关隐私线索，再基于概念响应预测隐私类别。设计基于目标类别相关性、跨类别可区分性和语义多样性的概念选择策略以提升概念质量；并引入全局-区域建模模块，在保留全局上下文的同时捕获局部隐私证据。

Result: 在 VISPR 与 FineViP 数据集上，PriCBMs 在全部四项多标签指标上优于所对比方法；消融实验与概念质量分析验证了各设计的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 以概念为中心的中间表示可有效对齐高层隐私语义与底层视觉线索，兼顾识别性能与可解释性，为多标签视觉隐私识别提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Widespread image sharing on social media has increased the need for visual privacy recognition to identify inadvertently exposed privacy information. However, multi-label visual privacy recognition presents two challenges. First, privacy categories represent high-level semantic attributes and do not have a one-to-one correspondence with visual objects. Different visual objects may involve the same privacy category, while the same type of object may contain different types of privacy-sensitive information. In this setting, direct prediction methods generally lack explicit guidance for identifying category-relevant privacy cues, leaving this process implicit in the learned image-to-label mapping and potentially limiting classification performance. Second, privacy cues are often distributed across image regions and may not be adequately represented by whole-image features. To address these challenges, we propose Privacy Concept Bottleneck Models (PriCBMs). By introducing privacy-related concepts as intermediate semantic representations, PriCBMs first identify category-relevant privacy cues through visual-concept matching and then predict privacy categories based on concept responses. This process makes privacy predictions more interpretable. To improve the quality of these concepts, we design a concept selection strategy based on target-category relevance, cross-category discriminability, and semantic diversity. Meanwhile, we introduce a global-region modeling module to capture localized privacy evidence while retaining global context. Experiments on VISPR and FineViP show that PriCBMs outperform evaluated competitors across all four multi-label metrics. Ablation studies and concept-quality analyses support the effectiveness of the proposed design. Code is available at https://github.com/sun2293674491-dotcom/PriCBMs.

</details>


### [250] [TME-ContextNet: Class-Conditioned Spatial Supervision and Grouped Decoding for Joint Nuclei Instance Segmentation and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02671)
*Rukang Ma,Bingxue Wang,Liwen Zou,Yingying Cao,Zhenghua Cai,Yu Li,Dong Wang,Huiyu Zhou,Luying Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TME-ContextNet多任务框架，利用欧氏距离变换将核标注扩展为类别条件密集空间监督，并将解码组织为几何相关与语义任务组，实现紧凑的核实例分割与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合核实例分割与分类需要精确边界及邻域信息以区分形态相似的细胞类型；现有方法通常依赖额外组织区域标注或参数冗余较大。

Method: （1）类别条件空间监督：用欧氏距离变换将已有核标签转成密集目标，无需额外组织区域标注，并给出最近区域解释、分析其对小扰动标注的稳定性；（2）局部—全局上下文模块：空洞卷积与自注意力结合；（3）分组任务解码器：按几何相关与语义任务分组以减少冗余计算；（4）空间头仅用于训练监督，推理时移除。

Result: PanMix、CoNSeP、PanNuke上Dice分别为80.23%、65.32%、79.13%，mPQ+分别为67.62%、35.50%、43.74%（PanNuke为三折测试均值）；参数量36.40M，对比HoVer-Net的54.74M。目标与解码器消融验证了距离监督与分组解码的组合有效性，并揭示其依赖任务配置。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种紧凑、上下文感知的公式用于计算病理中的联合核分析；空间监督与分组解码的收益与具体任务配置相关。

Abstract: Joint nuclei instance segmentation and classification requires precise boundaries and neighborhood information that helps distinguish morphologically similar cell types. We present TME-ContextNet, a multi-task framework that learns class-conditioned spatial context from existing nuclear annotations and organizes decoding into geometry-related and semantic task groups. Class-conditioned spatial supervision extends nuclear labels into a dense target using Euclidean distance transforms, requiring no additional tissue-region annotation. We derive its nearest-region interpretation and characterize its stability to small annotation perturbations. A local--global context module combines dilated convolution and self-attention, while a grouped task decoder reduces redundant computation. The spatial head supplies training supervision and is omitted during inference. Experiments on PanMix, CoNSeP, and PanNuke yield Dice scores of 80.23%, 65.32%, and 79.13%, and mean multi-class panoptic quality (mPQ+) scores of 67.62%, 35.50%, and 43.74%, respectively. PanNuke results are averaged across three test folds. Target and decoder controls support the combination of distance-based supervision and grouped decoding while identifying their dependence on task configuration. The framework uses 36.40 million parameters, compared with 54.74 million for HoVer-Net. These findings support a compact, context-aware formulation for joint nuclei analysis in computational pathology.

</details>


### [251] [Geometry-Constrained Dynamic Hypergraph Convolutional Network with Contrastive Score Refinement for Skeleton-Based Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02685)
*Minh Ta Tran Nhat,Yuning Ye,Yuseok Ban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DyGCN——几何约束的动态超图卷积网络，通过动态构建动作特定的超边并利用对比学习精炼兼容性分数，在NTU RGB+D、NTU RGB+D 120和Northwestern-UCLA上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GCN仅建模成对关节关系，难以捕捉人体运动中的高阶交互；现有超图方法依赖基于解剖先验的静态结构，缺乏捕捉动态、动作特定关节相关性的灵活性。

Method: 核心为DyGCT块，含两个模块：(1) GeoHC模块，利用几何约束的二阶兼容性动态构造动作特定超边，突破静态超边结构限制；(2) CSR模块，在超图构建过程中通过基于原型的对比学习显式精炼成对兼容性分数，获得更具判别性的超边，且仅在训练时生效，推理零额外开销。

Result: 在NTU RGB+D、NTU RGB+D 120和Northwestern-UCLA三个基准上验证有效性，均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 动态、动作特定的超边构建结合对比分数精炼可有效建模高阶关节交互，优于静态超图与成对GCN方法；代码已开源。

Abstract: Skeleton-based action recognition is a pivotal task in computer vision; however, conventional Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) are often constrained by pairwise joint modeling, which fails to capture the intricate high-order interactions inherent in human movement. While recent hypergraph-based methods attempt to address this limitation, they typically rely on static structures derived from anatomical priors, lacking the flexibility to capture dynamic, action-specific joint correlations. To this end, we propose DyGCN, a Geometry-Constrained Dynamic Hypergraph Convolutional Network for skeleton-based action recognition. At the core of DyGCN is the Dynamic Hypergraph Construction Network with Contrastive Score Refinement (DyGCT) block, which comprises two novel components: the Geometry-Constrained Dynamic Hypergraph Construction (GeoHC) module and the Contrastive Score Refinement (CSR) module. Specifically, GeoHC dynamically constructs action-specific hyperedges by leveraging geometry-constrained second-order compatibility, transcending the limitations of static hyperedge structures. Furthermore, CSR explicitly refines the pairwise compatibility scores during hypergraph construction through prototype-based contrastive learning, fostering more discriminative action-specific hyperedges, with zero inference-time overhead as CSR operates only during training. Extensive experiments on NTU RGB+D, NTU RGB+D 120, and Northwestern-UCLA validate the effectiveness of DyGCN, achieving state-of-the-art performance on widely used benchmarks. The source code is available at https://github.com/marvineta0405/DyGCN.

</details>


### [252] [Discovering Semantic Granularity in Images through Clustering of Pretrained Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02686)
*Ying Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需语义监督的表征驱动方法：拼接预训练视觉模型的特征图并用SVD投影得到像素级主表征图（PRM），再通过聚类发现多粒度语义区域；同时引入语义连续率（SCR）及其信噪比（SNR）来评估表征的多粒度可分性，并在COCO-2017与VOC-2012验证集上用HIoU验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义粒度（视觉表征将图像内容组织为有意义实体的层级）是图像理解的基础，但预训练视觉表征是否天然编码多粒度语义结构仍缺乏清晰认识。作者希望在不依赖语义监督的前提下，验证并挖掘预训练表征中隐含的多粒度语义结构。

Method: 1) 从预训练视觉模型选取并拼接特征图；2) 采用基于奇异值分解（SVD）的投影生成像素级主表征图（PRM），以显式化表征中隐含的语义结构；3) 对PRM进行语义聚类以获得不同候选粒度的图像区域；4) 基于轮廓系数原则提出语义连续率（SCR），量化不同候选聚类数下簇内/簇间像素表征的一致性；5) 分析SCR剖面的信噪比（SNR），用于判定表征在多粒度下发现语义区域的适宜性。

Result: 在初始聚类数区间内SNR更高的PRM，往往能在多个粒度上产生更具语义意义的区域。在COCO-2017与VOC-2012验证集上以匈牙利匹配交并比（HIoU）评估，所提方法相较对比方法取得有竞争力的分割性能。

Conclusion: 无需语义监督，仅通过对预训练表征的PRM聚类即可发现有用的语义粒度，表明预训练视觉表征中确实蕴含可被显式化的多粒度语义结构；SCR/SNR可作为选择合适表征与粒度的诊断指标。

Abstract: Semantic granularity, which refers to the levels at which visual representations organize image content into meaningful entities, is fundamental to image understanding. However, whether pretrained visual representations inherently encode semantic structures at multiple granularities remains poorly understood. This paper presents a representation-driven approach for discovering semantically meaningful image regions at different granularities through clustering of pretrained visual representations without semantic supervision. Specifically, we concatenate selected feature maps extracted from a pretrained visual model and apply a singular value decomposition (SVD)-based projection to derive pixel-wise principal representation maps (PRMs). The resulting PRMs capture implicit semantic structures embedded in pretrained visual representations, making them accessible to subsequent semantic clustering. We then introduce the semantic continuity rate (SCR), based on the silhouette principle, to quantify the coherence of pixel-wise representations within and between clusters at different candidate granularities. Furthermore, we analyze the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of SCR profiles to assess representation suitability for discovering semantic regions at multiple granularities. Our results indicate that PRMs exhibiting higher SNR values over an initial range of cluster numbers tend to yield more semantically meaningful regions across multiple granularities. We evaluate the proposed approach on the COCO-2017 and VOC-2012 validation datasets using Hungarian-matched intersection over union (HIoU). The results demonstrate competitive segmentation performance among the compared methods, supporting the feasibility of discovering useful semantic granularities through clustering PRMs without semantic supervision.

</details>


### [253] [3L-D: A Low-Light Level Dataset from a Sensor-Agnostic, Alignment-Preserving Acquisition Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02695)
*Léo Rueff,Maurgène Ghezal,Valentin Gorse,Astrid Dufaure,Esteban Monsalvo,François Atteia,Ludovic Escoubas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理定标的低光图像/视频数据集3L-Dataset，基于模块化、传感器无关的采集装置，按Midavaine夜天亮度等级（Level 1≈1000 mlx至Level 5≈0.1 mlx）可控复现可见光至近红外谱段的真实低光子场景，并保证亚像素级空间对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督式低光增强模型受数据集制约：主流数据依赖不可控环境光采集、相机参数调整（曝光/ISO）或中性密度滤光片，无法还原真实夜空近红外光谱成分与真实低光子噪声分布；同时，无畸变的动态配对序列采集仍属难题，且易引入传感器特定偏置。

Method: 构建模块化、传感器无关的标定采集平台，采用高精度可编程机械驱动实现同步六元组（1张参考图+5个标准夜天等级图）的亚像素空间对齐，无需后处理图像配准；覆盖乡村、森林、城市、沙漠等缩比真实场景，谱段从可见光延伸至近红外；并在各夜天等级上评测多种代表性深度学习基线。

Result: 在不同夜天等级下评测多个代表性深度学习基线，揭示在严重光子匮乏条件下重建保真度、感知质量与推理时延之间的关键权衡关系。

Conclusion: 3L-Dataset及配套采集框架可适配不同相机、光学系统、传感器技术与分辨率，支持数据集持续扩展，为低光图像/视频增强研究提供物理可标定、可复现的基准。

Abstract: Supervised low-light image and video enhancement has achieved remarkable progress, yet current models remain fundamentally constrained by the limitations of existing datasets. Prevailing methods typically rely on uncontrolled ambient captures, camera parameter manipulation (e.g., exposure or ISO adjustments), or neutral density filters, failing to replicate authentic night-sky near-infrared spectral content and genuine low-photon noise distributions. Furthermore, capturing dynamic paired sequences without introducing warping artifacts or sensor-specific biases remains an open challenge. In this paper, we introduce 3L-Dataset, a physically calibrated low-light image and video dataset generated using a modular, sensor-agnostic acquisition framework. Our test bench faithfully reproduces the standardized Midavaine night level scale from Level 1 (full moon, approximately 1000 mlx) down to Level 5 (overcast moonless night, approximately 0.1 mlx), spanning the visible to near-infrared spectrum across diverse realistic environments, including countryside, forest, urban, and desert scale models. Using high-precision programmable mechanical actuation, our acquisition framework guarantees sub-pixel spatial alignment across synchronized sextuplets, each composed of one reference image and five corresponding images acquired at the five standardized night levels, without post-processing image warping. We evaluate multiple representative deep learning baselines across varying night levels, revealing critical trade-offs between reconstruction fidelity, perceptual quality, and inference latency under severe photon starvation. The modular acquisition framework can be adapted to different cameras, optical systems, sensor technologies, and resolutions, enabling the dataset to be continuously expanded.

</details>


### [254] [Attention Residual U-Net for Enhanced Cross-Dataset Polyp Localization in Endoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02726)
*Romil Imtiaz,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于注意力残差U-Net的息肉检测框架，通过抑制背景响应学习病灶聚焦的空间表征，再在推理阶段转换为边界框，在多设备公开内镜数据集上AUC最高提升22.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内镜病灶精确定位是关键任务，YOLO等通用目标检测方法虽为当前最优，但在真实临床条件下因设备差异、肠道内容物、数据集偏倚及小目标/低对比度病灶而鲁棒性下降。

Method: 不同于从全局特征直接回归边界框，该框架学习病灶聚焦的空间表征并在推理时转换为框预测；相比将全部编码器激活直接传播至解码路径的常规编解码框架，其在特征重建前选择性抑制无关背景响应。具体集成残差特征传播、注意力引导的编码器滤波与多尺度上下文学习。

Result: 在多个不同设备采集的公开内镜数据集上评估，与相关SOTA方法相比验证了有效性，AUC最高提升22.0%。

Conclusion: 选择性抑制背景的注意力残差U-Net可提升异质成像条件下的内镜病灶定位鲁棒性，为息肉自动检测提供了有效替代方案。

Abstract: Precise localization of pathologies in endoscopy is a critical task in medical imaging, with deep learning (DL) frameworks now serving as the primary methodology for automated lesion detection. Today, generic object detection methods, such as YOLO, provide state-of-the-art performance in this context. However, their robustness often decreases under real-world clinical conditions due to variations across endoscopic devices, intestinal content, dataset bias, and the presence of small or low-contrast lesions. Motivated by these challenges, we propose a novel polyp detection framework based on a Attention Residual U-Net offering enhanced polyp localization. Instead of directly regressing bounding boxes from global feature representations, the proposed method learns lesion-focused spatial representations and converts them into bounding-box predictions during inference. Unlike conventional encoder-decoder localization frameworks that directly propagate all encoder activations into the decoding pathway, the proposed method selectively suppresses irrelevant background responses before feature reconstruction. This encourages the network to focus on lesion-relevant spatial evidence under heterogeneous imaging conditions. To achieve this, the framework integrates residual feature propagation, attention-guided encoder filtering, and multi-scale contextual learning. The proposed framework was evaluated on multiple public endoscopic datasets acquired using different devices. The comparative experimental results validate its effectiveness over the relevant state-of-the-art methods, with an improvement up to 22.0% in terms of the area under the receiving operating characteristic (AUC).

</details>


### [255] [MoviEdit: Long-form Multi-Shot Video Editing Made Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02737)
*Suhyeon Lee,Zhengang Li,Soo Ye Kim,Peihao Zhu,Jong Chul Ye,Chun-Hao Paul Huang,Duygu Ceylan,Yan Kang,Hyeonho Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoviEdit：面向长时长多镜头视频编辑的端到端框架，通过自动数据生成管线（87K 配对数据）、级联"双向+自回归"架构及专用基准 MoviEdit-Bench，实现跨镜头一致的规模化长视频编辑，性能超越开源模型并比肩工业前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时长多镜头视频编辑要求在场景、视角、主体外观突变时保持编辑意图一致，且需处理远超单镜头编辑器的视觉上下文；现有方法在数据（缺跨镜头监督）、建模（长上下文一致性）与评测（缺专用基准）三方面均受限。

Method: 1）数据：构建自动生成管线，涵盖同步多视角素材、真实多镜头视频与生成式多镜头内容，无需人工标注即产出 87K 配对编辑视频；2）架构：级联式双向-自回归设计——轻量双向阶段在时空压缩表征上建立全局一致的编辑草稿，随后自回归地传播并精化至完整视频；3）评测：提出 MoviEdit-Bench 专用基准。

Result: 大量实验表明，在文本引导与图像引导编辑任务上，MoviEdit 显著优于现有开源模型，并与工业前沿模型性能相当。

Conclusion: MoviEdit 以数据-建模-评测三位一体的方案有效解决长时长多镜头视频编辑的可扩展性与一致性问题，并公开基准以推动该方向系统化研究。

Abstract: Long-form multi-shot video editing requires an intended edit to remain coherent across abrupt changes in scene, viewpoint, and subject appearance, while processing substantially longer visual context than conventional single-shot editors. We introduce MoviEdit, an end-to-end framework for scalable long-form multi-shot video editing that jointly addresses the challenges of data, modeling, and evaluation. To provide cross-shot supervision data at scale, we develop automatic data-generation pipelines spanning synchronized multi-view footage, real-world multi-shot videos, and generated multi-shot content, yielding 87K paired editing videos without manual annotation. Architecturally, MoviEdit employs a cascaded bidirectional-autoregressive design: a lightweight bidirectional stage first establishes a globally coherent editing draft over a spatiotemporally compressed representation, which is then autoregressively propagated and refined over the full video. We further introduce MoviEdit-Bench, a dedicated benchmark for systematic evaluation of long-form multi-shot video editing. Extensive experiments show that MoviEdit substantially outperforms existing open-source models and performs on par with industry frontier models across text-guided and image-guided editing. Visit our project page for complete results and to access the benchmark: https://multi-shot-edit.github.io/

</details>


### [256] [Subspace Representations for Synthetic-to-Real Transfer in 3-D Surface Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02744)
*Abdulla Nasir Chowdhury,Naish Ibrahim,Gulpi Qorik Oktagalu Pratamasunu,Kazuhiro Fukui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究用三类经典子空间方法（最近子空间分类器、单类模板子空间、广义差分GDS）作为工具，考察子空间表示在"仅合成缺陷训练、仅有良品扫描"条件下的合成到真实迁移中贡献了什么：发现各决策规则的窗口AUC相近、图像级检测仅为随机水平、域内阈值全部失效；子空间边界的位移可由其外泄量界定，且在Real3D-AD上零训练达到点AUROC 0.957与类别平均物体AUROC 78–81。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3-D表面检测中，部署前只能获得合成缺陷与真实良品扫描，合成到真实的域偏移使阈值与决策规则失效。作者希望厘清"子空间表示"本身对迁移的贡献，而非仅仅提出新的检测器，并寻找可从无标注良品数据中估计的偏移可控性指标。

Method: 以最近子空间分类器、单类模板子空间、广义差分子空间（GDS）三种经典构造作为实验工具；在MVTec 3D-AD的1,197次扫描上（无目标标注）比较不同决策规则的排序性能并检验阈值保持性；在冻结特征上分析最近子空间规则的margin变化，用类子空间外的特征偏移分量（"泄漏"leakage）从无标注良品扫描估计其上界；在Real3D-AD的597个物体上，用配准模板的单类子空间在冻结特征上逐位置评估。

Result: 各决策规则在真实缺陷上的窗口AUC相近；图像级检测处于随机水平；没有规则能保持其域内阈值。理论上给出最近子空间margin的位移至多等于特征偏移中位于类子空间之外的部分，且该外泄量可从无标注良品扫描测得并界定未见过扫描上的实际位移。Real3D-AD上在预注册扫描上达到点AUROC 0.957，类别平均物体AUROC 78–81，且未在该基准上训练。同时显示参考与配准比子空间本身更重要。

Conclusion: 子空间在域偏移下的收益与其所能张成的偏移部分成正比，可由无标注良品扫描估计的外泄量进行刻画；在基于参考的检测范式中，参考模板及其配准的质量比子空间选择更关键。因此阈值在跨域时不可移植，应转向基于排序/距离的评估与参考质量把控。

Abstract: This paper studies what subspace representations contribute to synthetic-to-real transfer in 3-D surface inspection, where models learn from synthetic defects and the only real data before deployment are scans of good parts. We use three classical constructions as instruments: the nearest-subspace classifier, one-class template subspaces and the generalized difference subspace (GDS). On 1,197 MVTec 3D-AD scans without target annotation we find that every decision rule ranks real defects at a similar window AUC, that image-level detection stays at chance, and that no rule keeps its in-domain threshold. We show that the margin of a nearest-subspace rule moves by at most the part of the feature shift outside its class subspaces, and that this leakage, measured from unlabeled good scans, bounds the displacement observed on unseen scans. On 597 Real3D-AD objects, a one-class subspace of registered templates evaluated at every location on frozen features reaches point AUROC 0.957 on pre-registered scans and a category-mean object AUROC of 78 to 81 with no training on the benchmark. Our results suggest that a subspace helps under shift in proportion to the shift it spans, and that in reference-based inspection the reference and its registration matter more than the subspace.

</details>


### [257] [Pair-Relative Partial Equivariance for Rotation-Robust Keypoint Descriptor Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02751)
*Jongmin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"成对相对部分等变性"度量与PaSteer松弛式描述子转向框架，在冻结DeDoDe描述子上显著提升大角度平面旋转下的匹配鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于正立图像训练的keypoint描述子在平面内大旋转下性能下降。现有steerer方法对描述子空间施加单一的精确SO(2)表示，这一全局群约束对keypoint描述子过于刚性，因为其角度响应未必遵循单一群表示。

Method: 提出pair-relative partial equivariance：在图像对相对旋转的经验分布下度量等变性；提出PaSteer，允许转向映射偏离精确表示流形，同时保留轻量、无需骨干网络的steering设计。给出两种实例：保持恒等的残差转向器（PaSteer-soft）与自由形式的傅里叶转向器（PaSteer-free）。并证明：对固定非等变描述子，逐角度最优线性steerer一般不满足群复合律。

Result: 在Roto-360、MegaDepth-1500、AIMS与IMC-PT上，PaSteer在冻结DeDoDe描述子上一致优于精确steerer的旋转鲁棒匹配，尤其在大相对旋转下提升明显，同时正立性能退化极小。

Conclusion: 精确SO(2)表示在冻结描述子场景中可能是次优的；以经验旋转分布定义的松弛部分等变性是可有效替代方案，PaSteer在保持轻量与低正立退化的前提下提升旋转鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learned keypoint descriptors trained on upright imagery often fail under large in-plane rotation. Steerers address this by applying a single exact SO(2) representation to the descriptor space, but this global group constraint can be too rigid for keypoint descriptors, whose angular responses need not follow a single group representation. We propose pair-relative partial equivariance, which measures equivariance under the empirical distribution of relative rotations in image pairs, and PaSteer, a relaxed descriptor-steering framework that allows the steering map to leave the exact representation manifold while retaining the lightweight, backbone-free design of steering. PaSteer instantiates this relaxation with two variants: an identity-preserving residual steerer (PaSteer-soft) and a free-form Fourier steerer (PaSteer-free). We show that the per-angle optimal linear steerer for a fixed non-equivariant descriptor generally does not obey the group composition law, explaining why exact representations can be suboptimal in the frozen-descriptor regime. On Roto-360, MegaDepth-1500, AIMS, and IMC-PT, PaSteer consistently improves rotation-robust matching over an exact steerer on frozen DeDoDe descriptors, especially under large relative rotations, with minimal upright degradation.

</details>


### [258] [DINOFAS: Bonafide-Centric Representation Learning for Face Anti-Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02756)
*Shakeel Muhammad Ibrahim,Muhammad Sohail Ibrahim,Muhammad Shahid Jabbar,Shujaat Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DINOFAS将人脸反欺骗(FAS)重构为以真脸为中心的表示学习，基于DINOv3结合LoRA微调、注意力风格-内容解耦与双分支架构，在跨域与合成到真实场景下取得领先的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FAS模型易学到与攻击类型和数据集相关的特异性线索，在未见采集条件与未知呈现攻击下泛化性能下降。作者希望摆脱"攻击检测"范式，转而学习与域无关的真脸（bonafide）本质表示。

Method: 提出DINOFAS框架：(1) 采用LoRA对DINOv3进行参数高效适配；(2) 设计注意力风格-内容解耦(ASCD)模块，削弱域相关外观变化；(3) 构建双分支结构，一支做基于原型的活体学习，另一支做攻击语义监督；(4) 联合优化真脸中心原型目标、作用于detached特征的攻击语义分类损失及正交正则项。

Result: 在OCIM留一域协议（三源域训练、留出未见域测试）下平均HTER为9.09%；仅在SynthASpoof合成数据训练并测试四个未见真实数据集时，取得最低平均HTER 21.41%与最高平均AUC 86.14%；在OULU-NPU与SiW-Mv2上亦表现出对采集条件、设备和未知呈现攻击的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 以真脸为中心的表示学习结合大规模预训练视觉基础模型，可有效缓解FAS的跨域与跨攻击泛化难题；合成到真实的迁移评估进一步验证了该范式的实用价值。

Abstract: Face anti-spoofing (FAS) models often learn attack- and dataset-specific cues that degrade performance under unseen acquisition conditions and presentation attacks. We present DINOFAS, a DINOv3-based framework that formulates FAS as bonafide-centric representation learning. The proposed framework combines parameter-efficient DINOv3 adaptation using low-rank adaptation (LoRA), an attention style-content disentanglement (ASCD) module for reducing domain-specific appearance variations, and a dual-branch architecture that separates prototype-based liveness learning from attack-semantic supervision. The model is jointly optimized using a bonafide-centric prototype objective, an attack-semantic classification loss applied to detached features, and orthogonality regularization. DINOFAS achieves an average HTER of 9.09\% under the standard OCIM leave-one-domain-out protocol, where training is performed on three source datasets and evaluation is conducted on the held-out unseen domain. We further evaluate synthetic-to-real generalization by training exclusively on SynthASpoof and testing on four unseen real-world datasets, where DINOFAS achieves the lowest average HTER of 21.41\% and the highest average AUC of 86.14\% among the compared methods. Evaluations on OULU-NPU and SiW-Mv2 additionally demonstrate robustness to variations in acquisition conditions, devices, and unseen presentation attacks.

</details>


### [259] [Trace4D: Grounded Spatiotemporal Reasoning via 4D State Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02759)
*Wooseok Jang,Seungryong Kim,Yan Lu,Lei Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Trace4D，训练视觉语言模型预测时序4D状态序列并推理其跨时刻变化，以4D-grounded思维链作为中间证据，实现无需外部几何模型或标注的视频问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在视角变化下难以理解物体及其空间关系在3D空间中随时间演化；现有方法虽引入几何信息，但对时序有序的4D状态演化的显式监督、以及将其作为中间证据使用仍很有限。

Method: 构建Trace4D-Train（约60万条4D感知样本、约7万条有据QA样本，源自in-the-wild视频）；设计4D-grounded思维链，显式表示4D状态、分析时序视觉上下文并将二者作为答案证据；采用联合监督微调后接geometry-aware GRPO训练；推理时直接从视频预测4D状态与推理，无需参考标注或外部几何模型。

Result: 在Trace4D-Bench与VLM4D上，QA准确率优于基座VLM；消融显示对4D状态变化与时序视觉观测的推理带来增益。

Conclusion: 显式的4D状态序列预测与时序变化推理可作为有效的中间证据，提升VLM的时空理解能力，并可摆脱对外部几何模型与标注的依赖。

Abstract: Understanding how objects and their spatial relationships evolve in 3D space over time remains challenging for vision-language models (VLMs), particularly under changing viewpoints. Recent approaches incorporate geometric information into reasoning, but explicit supervision for temporally ordered 4D state evolution and its use as intermediate evidence remains limited. We introduce \textbf{Trace4D}, which trains VLMs to predict 4D state sequences and reason over their inter-timestep changes alongside temporal visual observations. Its 4D-grounded chain-of-thought (CoT) explicitly represents 4D states, analyzes temporal visual context, and connects both as evidence for the answer. We construct Trace4D-Train from in-the-wild videos with 600K 4D perception examples and approximately 70K grounded QA examples, and train with joint supervised fine-tuning followed by geometry-aware GRPO. At inference, Trace4D predicts 4D states and reasoning directly from video without reference annotations or an external geometric model. Experiments on Trace4D-Bench and VLM4D show improved QA accuracy over the base VLMs and benefits from reasoning over 4D state changes and temporal visual observations.

</details>


### [260] [GaussFlaw: Fine-Grained Perceptual Diagnosis for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02767)
*Shaocheng Shen,Zihan Zheng,Kejin Li,Lu Liu,Xiaoyun Zhang,Qiang Hu,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个3DGS细粒度伪影感知诊断数据集GaussFlaw-80K，并构建几何引导多任务模型GeoCritic用于伪影定位与类型分类，其诊断结果可指导高斯致密化、属性校正与离群点剪枝以提升渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽能实时渲染并实现逼真新视图合成，但其伪影空间局部化、退化模式多样，全局质量指标与图像级基准无法指示伪影位置与类型，缺乏细粒度诊断能力。

Method: 构建模型辅助+人在回路的流水线生成GaussFlaw-80K：14,289张渲染图像中80,049个伪影区域，每区域具备像素级掩码与八类缺陷标签（几何、混合、光度退化）。据此提出GeoCritic，融合RGB外观与深度衍生线索的几何引导多任务模型，联合定位伪影与分类缺陷类型；并将诊断用于高斯致密化、属性校正、离群点剪枝三种下游操作。

Result: GeoCritic达到AUC-J 0.9507与Acc@1 80.08%；诊断引导的细化在多种数据集与3DGS骨干上均提升全部全局与裁剪级指标。

Conclusion: 将3DGS伪影诊断转化为显式可测任务，细粒度诊断不仅可评估，还能有效反哺渲染优化，体现诊断—修复闭环价值。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables photorealistic novel- view synthesis with real-time rendering, yet fine-grained di- agnosis of its rendering artifacts remains challenging. These artifacts are typically spatially localized and exhibit diverse degradation patterns, making them difficult to character- ize using global quality metrics and image-level assessment benchmarks, which provide no explicit indication of where they occur or what types of artifacts they represent. To ad- dress this limitation, we introduce GaussFlaw-80K, the first large-scale dataset for fine-grained perceptual diagnosis in 3DGS. Constructed through a model-assisted human-in-the- loop pipeline, GaussFlaw-80K contains 80,049 artifact re- gions across 14,289 rendered images, each annotated with a pixel-level mask and one of eight flaw labels spanning ge- ometric, blending, and photometric degradations. These an- notations turn 3DGS artifact diagnosis into an explicit and measurable task. Building on this dataset, we propose Ge- oCritic, a geometry-guided multi-task model that integrates RGB appearance with depth-derived cues to jointly localize artifacts and classify their flaw types. Beyond evaluation, we further demonstrate the downstream benefits of fine-grained diagnosis through three complementary targeted operations: Gaussian densification, attribute correction, and outlier prun- ing. Experiments show that GeoCritic achieves an AUC-J of 0.9507 and an Acc@1 of 80.08%, while diagnosis-guided re- finement improves all reported global and crop-level metrics across diverse datasets and 3DGS backbones.

</details>


### [261] [Unlocking the Potential of Vision Foundation Models for Multi-Task Dense Prediction via Mixture of Task Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02769)
*Yunzhi Zhuge,Xinzhuo Yu,Sitong Gong,Lu Zhang,Pingping Zhang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 D2MoE，在冻结的 DINOv3 骨干中通过"任务通用 + 任务自适应"两级适配（TG-LoRA + 末端 TA-MoE + 语义先验注入器）解决多任务稠密预测中的负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型的预训练语义不变性与稠密预测目标（频域偏置、平滑偏置）存在冲突；联合微调或单路径适配器把竞争梯度压缩到单一更新通道，导致负迁移与跨任务一致性不稳定。

Method: 将适配分解为两级：任务通用对齐（保留先验）与任务感知特化（解决后期冲突）。具体实现 D2MoE：(1) 全网注意力投影中插入 TG-LoRA 提供稳定共享适配通道；(2) 仅在每个 stage 最后一个注意力块注入 TA-MoE，单次前向中以稀疏、任务条件化方式扰动 token mixing；(3) 语义先验注入器将多层级任务特征与冻结基础模型先验耦合，保持语义一致性与边界/结构保真。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上的实验表明：该结构化分解在较小可训练参数量下取得稳健的多任务性能提升。

Conclusion: 验证了"先验保持、以基础模型为中心"的多任务稠密感知的可扩展范式，即结构化因子化优于单通道联合适配。

Abstract: Multi-task dense prediction seeks a single, coherent representation that supports simultaneously discriminative semantic recognition and regularized geometric/structural inference. For vision foundation models, pretrained semantic invariances conflict with the frequency and smoothness biases of dense objectives; joint fine-tuning and single-route adapters therefore compress competing gradients into one update channel, causing negative transfer and unstable cross-task agreement. We frame this as two-level adaptation: task-generic alignment that preserves priors, and task-aware specialization that resolves late-stage conflicts. Based on this principle, we propose D2MoE , a Mixture of Task Adapters that explicitly factorizes these roles within a frozen DINOv3 backbone. D2MoE introduces Task-Generic Low-Rank Adaptation (TG-LoRA) in attention projections throughout the network to provide a stable, shared adaptation channel, and a Task-Adaptive Mixture of Experts (TA-MoE) injected only at the final attention block of each stage to enact sparse, task-conditioned perturbations of token mixing in a single forward pass. A Semantic Prior Injector further couples multi-level task features with frozen foundation priors to reinforce semantic coherence while preserving boundary and structural fidelity. Experiments across diverse benchmarks substantiate that this structured factorization yields robust multi-task improvements with a small trainable footprint, establishing a principled and scalable route to prior-preserving, foundation-centric multi-task dense perception.

</details>


### [262] [Risk-Constrained Evaluation of Financial Document Recognition: A Formal Framework for Evidence, Critical Errors, and Selective Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02775)
*Subodh Lonkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文将财务文档识别形式化为“风险约束的证据恢复问题”，提出多层质量模型、基于置信上界的非补偿性验收门控与选择性自动化的风险-覆盖约束优化，并给出标注、数据划分与统计估计规范；属纯理论工作，不含实证基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有财务文档识别普遍用字符/词错误率（CER/WER）评价，但这些聚合指标无法反映错误在财务工作流中的实际后果，尤其是关键字段错误带来的风险；且“零观测错误”常被误当作“零风险”。

Method: 1）定义带类型、可证据链接的财务事实；2）构建覆盖文本、版面、表格、字段、语义关系、校准、鲁棒性与运行性能的多层质量模型；3）引入基于关键字段错误率置信上界（UCB）的非补偿性验收门控；4）将选择性自动化刻画为带审核容量约束的风险-覆盖约束优化问题；5）规定标注要求、防泄漏基准划分、统计估计量与可复现性卡片；6）以形式化命题论证聚合分数无法认证关键字段可靠性、零观测错误不蕴含零风险；7）用合成示例说明判定规则。

Result: 给出两个形式化命题：（a）聚合指标无法认证关键字段的可靠性；（b）零观测错误不意味着零风险。合成示例演示了所提出的验收/决策规则如何运作。论文明确声明无实证基准或模型性能结论。

Conclusion: 财务文档识别的评价应从聚合错误率转向以证据恢复与风险约束为中心的框架，采用非补偿性门控与风险-覆盖权衡来支撑可信的选择性自动化；后续需实证验证该框架的有效性。

Abstract: Financial document recognition is commonly evaluated using character and word error rates, which do not adequately represent the consequences of errors in financial workflows. This paper formalizes financial document recognition as a risk-constrained evidence-recovery problem. It defines typed, evidence-linked financial facts and a multi-layer quality model spanning text, layout, tables, fields, semantic relations, calibration, robustness, and operational performance. It introduces non-compensatory acceptance gates based on upper confidence bounds for critical-field errors, and formulates selective automation as a constrained risk-coverage problem with review-capacity limits. The paper also specifies annotation requirements, leakage-resistant benchmark splits, statistical estimands, and a reproducibility card. Formal propositions establish why aggregate scores cannot certify critical-field reliability and why zero observed errors do not imply zero risk. A synthetic example illustrates the decision rule. The work is theoretical; no empirical benchmark or model performance claim is presented.

</details>


### [263] [Lookahead Prediction-Guided Adaptive Visual Refocusing for Interleaved Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02780)
*Mengzhao Wang,Yanli Ji,Peng Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练测试时框架LPAVR，利用MTP生成的未来token前缀作为前瞻信号动态重聚焦视觉证据，缓解"时序证据近视"，同时提升视觉推理准确率与MTP投机解码接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错式多模态思维链依赖在推理过程中动态引入视觉证据，但现有方法多从历史上下文中选取证据。当推理焦点转移时，历史引导的证据仍集中于先前区域，无法覆盖下一步所需的视觉信息，作者将此错配称为"时序证据近视"（Temporal Evidence Myopia）。

Method: 提出LPAVR（Lookahead Prediction-guided Adaptive Visual Refocusing），免训练、仅在测试时生效。将多Token预测（MTP）由解码加速机制改造为前瞻信号：MTP解码时草稿头生成多个未来token，目标模型并行校验并保留与其解码轨迹一致的最长前缀；该经目标模型验证的前缀蕴含近期未来语义，据此构建前瞻引导的视觉证据，并评估历史引导证据是否覆盖所指区域——覆盖足够则保留历史证据，否则切换为前瞻证据。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准上，LPAVR同时提升了推理准确率与MTP投机token的接受率，实现视觉推理能力与投机解码效率的协同增强。

Conclusion: 以目标模型验证的未来token前缀作为前瞻信号进行自适应视觉重聚焦，可使视觉上下文与即将到来的推理需求对齐，并提升草稿预测与目标解码的一致性，是一种无需额外训练的通用测试时改进方案。

Abstract: Interleaved multimodal chain-of-thought reasoning improves the visual reasoning capabilities of multimodal large language models by dynamically incorporating visual evidence during reasoning. However, existing methods primarily select evidence from historical context. When the reasoning focus shifts, history-guided evidence may remain focused on prior regions and fail to cover the visual information required by the next step. We refer to this lag between visual evidence and subsequent reasoning demands as Temporal Evidence Myopia. To address this issue, we propose Lookahead Prediction-guided Adaptive Visual Refocusing (LPAVR), a training-free test-time framework. LPAVR transforms Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) from a decoding acceleration mechanism into a lookahead signal for anticipating upcoming reasoning intent. During MTP decoding, the draft head generates multiple future tokens, while the target model verifies them in parallel and retains the longest prefix consistent with its decoding trajectory. This target-verified prefix contains near-future semantics, which LPAVR uses to construct lookahead-guided visual evidence and assess whether history-guided evidence covers the indicated regions. If the coverage is sufficient, the history-guided evidence is retained; otherwise, LPAVR switches to the lookahead-guided evidence. This better aligns the visual context with upcoming reasoning and improves consistency between draft predictions and target-model decoding. Experiments on multiple multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that LPAVR improves both reasoning accuracy and the acceptance rates of MTP speculative tokens, jointly enhancing visual reasoning and speculative decoding efficiency.

</details>


### [264] [History Forcing: Distilling History-Dependent Continuations for Long-Horizon Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02782)
*Hangyi Kuang,Chengzhi Wang,Yefei He,Weijie Wang,Yuke Zhao,Zhuokun Chen,Zisu Huang,Yifan Yang,Xiaoxuan He,Feng Chen,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 History Forcing，一种统一的 on-policy 蒸馏框架，通过历史上下文残差修正蒸馏目标，显著提升少步自回归视频生成的长程一致性与世界建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步自回归视频扩散虽高效，但在长rollout中难以保持一致性，尤其当主体或场景离开视野后再次出现时。现有蒸馏方法尚未充分利用更丰富的历史信息来约束学生模型当前的预测。

Method: 构建统一on-policy蒸馏框架：利用教师模型在"有/无信息性历史上下文"两种预测之差的残差来解耦历史监督；历史上下文可来自漂移积累较少的早期生成分块，或来自保持场景可见的固定视角教师rollout；将该残差加权后加到用于蒸馏的score/velocity预测上，形成修正目标，对学生进行蒸馏对齐。

Result: 在1.3B规模模型中达SOTA：15秒生成VBench质量分85.07，60秒生成VBench-Long综合分79.82；视野外世界建模中PlayWorld的Evolution Quality由2.35提升至2.70，MemoBench的Object Consistency由58.43提升至62.18。

Conclusion: 通过残差解耦并注入长程历史约束，History Forcing使少步自回归生成器在长视频一致性与镜头返回时的状态连贯性上取得明显改进，验证了历史监督对蒸馏框架的关键价值。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive video diffusion enables efficient generation, yet maintaining consistency over long rollouts remains challenging, especially when subjects or scenes reappear after going out of sight. Despite progress in fidelity and rollout robustness, existing distillation methods do not fully exploit richer historical information to constrain the student's current prediction. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{History Forcing}, a unified on-policy distillation framework that strengthens historical supervision for few-step autoregressive video generators. The framework uses the residual between teacher predictions with and without informative historical context to decouple this supervision. Such a historical context can be obtained using earlier generated chunks with less accumulated drift or a fixed-view teacher rollout that keeps the scene visible while the student looks away. The residual is then weighted and added to the score or velocity prediction used for distillation, forming a corrected target. By aligning the student with this corrected target through distillation, History Forcing incorporates long-range historical constraints into autoregressive generation. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance among 1.3B models, with a VBench quality score of 85.07 for 15s video generation and a VBench-Long aggregate score of 79.82 for 60s generation. For out-of-sight world modeling, evaluations further demonstrate more plausible scene evolution and more consistent states when the camera returns, with Evolution Quality improving from 2.35 to 2.70 on PlayWorld and Object Consistency from 58.43 to 62.18 on MemoBench.

</details>


### [265] [Local-Global Joint Modeling for Point Cloud Super-Resolution Guided by Multi-Scale Spatial Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02786)
*Wang Yang,Wenyi Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于多尺度空间先验与局部-全局联合建模的range图像点云超分方法，含空间门控融合、多粒度局部-全局协同感知模块与2D中值-残差去噪，在KITTI-360与nuScenes上IoU达0.4703与0.3593。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有range图像点云超分存在三问题：(1) range图像垂直方向非均匀，远处稀疏、近处稠密，部分跨尺度融合未考虑不同垂直位置特征可靠性差异，导致无效值与噪声传入解码器；(2) 单一卷积或序列建模难以兼顾局部高频几何细节与全局长程依赖，重建过度平滑；(3) 前后景边界处深度过渡平滑，反投影回3D时产生尖峰伪影。

Method: 由三个模块构成：空间门控融合模块（spatially gated fusion）按垂直位置可靠性加权跨尺度特征；多粒度局部-全局协同感知模块（multi-granularity local-global cooperative perception）联合建模局部细节与全局依赖；2D中值-残差去噪（2D median-residual denoising）抑制反投影尖峰伪影。

Result: 在KITTI-360与nuScenes上实现有效重建，IoU分别为0.4703与0.3593，验证了方法用于range图像超分的可行性。

Conclusion: 多尺度空间先验与局部-全局联合建模可有效缓解垂直非均匀融合、细节过度平滑与反投影尖峰问题，实现较好室外稀疏点云重建效果。

Abstract: Range-image-based point cloud super-resolution provides an effective way to reconstruct sparse outdoor point clouds by projecting low-resolution LiDAR scans into a regular 2D representation. However, current methods still face three challenges. First, range images exhibit pronounced vertical nonuniformity, with sparse observations in distant regions and denser observations in nearby regions. Some cross-scale fusion mechanisms do not account for the varying reliability of features at different vertical positions, allowing invalid values and noise from distant sparse regions to propagate into the decoder. Second, a single convolutional or sequence-modeling architecture often struggles to preserve both local high-frequency geometric details and global long-range dependencies, resulting in over-smoothed reconstructions. Third, smooth transition depths near foreground-background boundaries can produce spike artifacts when the 2D range image is back-projected into 3D. To address these issues, we propose a point cloud super-resolution method based on multi-scale spatial priors and joint local-global modeling. The design comprises a spatially gated fusion module, a multi-granularity local-global cooperative perception module, and 2D median-residual denoising. Experiments on KITTI-360 and nuScenes demonstrate effective reconstruction, with IoU scores of 0.4703 and 0.3593, respectively, supporting the feasibility of the proposed method for range-image super-resolution.

</details>


### [266] [OmniOPD: Stable On-Policy Distillation for Joint Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02803)
*Zihao Fan,Xin Lu,Jie Huang,Hexin Zhang,Xueyang Fu,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniOPD：面向音视频联合生成的模态级on-policy蒸馏框架，通过特权视觉信息、分组多噪声采样与时步重要性加权，缓解跨模态token不均衡导致的音频监督稀疏与蒸馏不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可提升音视频联合生成，但对异构的模态专属音频、视频奖励联合优化会引入跨模态干扰；on-policy蒸馏（OPD）可将专门化的音频与视频RL教师能力迁移到单一学生模型，然而音频与视频token数量严重不均衡，使音频监督稀疏、蒸馏不稳定。作者进一步观察到简单提高音频损失权重无效，因为音频学习高度依赖视觉上下文。

Method: 提出OmniOPD模态级OPD框架，包含三部分：(1) 采用非对称噪声调度构造特权视觉信息，使音频教师能从更干净的视觉上下文中生成更具信息量的目标；(2) 引入分组多噪声采样，扩大音频监督覆盖范围并稳定训练；(3) 引入基于教师—学生转移散度的时步重要性加权，保留可靠监督、降低不可靠信号权重。

Result: 在JavisBench与VBench上的评测表明，OmniOPD在保持音频质量的同时显著提升视频生成效果，实现有效的联合蒸馏。

Conclusion: 跨模态token不均衡是音视频OPD的核心瓶颈，单纯加权无效；利用更丰富的视觉上下文为音频提供特权信息，配合采样扩展与可靠性加权，可稳定实现均衡的音视频联合蒸馏。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) elevates joint audio-video generation, but optimizing heterogeneous, modality-specific audio and video rewards often introduces cross-modal interference. On-policy distillation (OPD) alleviates this issue by transferring the capabilities of specialized audio and video RL teachers into a single student model. However, a significant imbalance in cross-modal token counts leaves audio with sparse supervision, causing unstable distillation. We observe that naively increasing the audio loss weight fails to solve this problem since audio learning relies heavily on visual context, indicating richer visual cues are crucial to offset sparse audio signals. To address this, we propose OmniOPD, a modality-wise on-policy distillation framework designed for balanced and stable audio-video generation. First, we construct privileged visual information using asymmetric noise scheduling, allowing the audio teacher to generate more informative targets from cleaner video context. Second, we introduce grouped multi-noise sampling to expand audio supervision coverage and stabilize training. Third, we introduce timestep-wise importance weighting based on teacher-student transition divergence, preserving reliable supervision while down-weighting unreliable learning signals, to stabilize fluctuations in audio learning. Evaluated on JavisBench and VBench demonstrate OmniOPD preserves audio quality while significantly enhancing video generation, achieving effective joint distillation.

</details>


### [267] [Disen-Forcing: Disentangling Semantic Anchoring from Motion for Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02822)
*Zhengyang Yu,Akio Hayakawa,Masato Ishii,Qingtao Yu,Xuesong Li,Takashi Shibuya,Yuki Mitsufuji,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文重新审视 AR-VDM 中的 frame sink 机制，发现长程一致性由稀疏的少数注意力头锚定，多数头负责局部时间动态；据此提出 Disen-Forcing，通过解耦上下文路由与运动解耦正则缓解过度条件化，在长时程视频生成中同时提升长程一致性与运动动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型推理时程超过训练时程后会因累积曝光偏差而迅速退化。近期方法启发式地借鉴 LLM 的 attention sink，将初始帧作为持久上下文（frame sink）来稳定长 rollout，但其作用机理未经检验，且存在潜在缺陷。

Method: 首先对 frame sink 的作用机理进行实证分析：长程一致性并非由 LLM 式 attention sink 实现，而是由稀疏子集的注意力头锚定，其余多数头主要捕捉局部时间动态。由此指出现有 frame sink 策略的"过度条件化"病态：对每个头统一暴露固定的初始帧上下文，在蒸馏中易使运动坍缩至教师的低动态模式。提出 Disen-Forcing，一种蒸馏阶段的即插即用方法，包含解耦上下文路由（disentangled context routing）与运动解耦正则（motion-disentangling regularization），以解耦并强化头的专业化分工。

Result: 在长时程视频生成基准上，Disen-Forcing 同时提升了基础 AR-VDM 的长程一致性与运动动态性；而基于 sink 的方法无法平衡这一权衡。

Conclusion: 初始帧对 AR-VDM 长程一致性的稳定作用源于稀疏注意力头的专业化，而非 LLM 式 attention sink；统一施加 frame sink 会造成过度条件化与运动退化。Disen-Forcing 以解耦路由加运动解耦正则的蒸馏插件形式，解开了长程一致性与运动动态之间的权衡。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models (AR-VDMs) enable real-time, long-horizon video generation but degrade rapidly once the inference horizon outruns the training horizon, due to compounding exposure bias. To stabilize long rollouts, recent methods heuristically adopt frame sink, keeping initial frames as a persistent model context, inspired by attention sinks in LLMs. We revisit this design and find that the mechanism by which initial frames stabilize long-range consistency in AR-VDMs is not an LLM-style attention sink; instead, long-range consistency is anchored through a sparse subset of attention heads, while the majority of heads predominantly capture local temporal dynamics. This observation reveals an over-conditioning pathology in existing frame-sink strategies: by universally exposing every head to the pinned initial-frame context, the model becomes prone to collapsing motion toward the low-dynamic modes of the teacher during model distillation. We propose Disen-Forcing, a distillation-time plug-in for AR-VDMs that disentangles and reinforces this head specialization through disentangled context routing and motion-disentangling regularization. On long-horizon video generation benchmarks, Disen-Forcing simultaneously improves long-range consistency and motion dynamics of base AR-VDMs, a trade-off that sink-based methods fail to balance.

</details>


### [268] [Aerial Content-Aware Sample-Adaptive Classifier for Urban Photogrammetric Point Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02844)
*Chong Liu,Yonghua Lu,Zhanglin Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ACASAC，在特征级融合之外引入决策级自适应，为每个样本动态构造分类器以提升城市点云语义分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市摄影测量点云通常以局部3D块处理，上下文有限，难以区分几何相似的类别；同一数据的正射投影（DOM/RGB-D）虽能提供大范围上下文，却压缩了垂直几何信息。现有投影-点方法多在特征级融合两种表示，并沿用对所有样本共享的固定分类器，未利用样本间差异。

Method: 提出Aerial Content-Aware Sample-Adaptive Classifier（ACASAC）：以点对齐的航拍区域连接完整航拍裁剪与局部3D块；在投影内部建模场景到局部的变化，并建模对齐的2D与3D支撑之间的表示差异；将二者关系迁移用于细化局部点的类别表示；由细化表示、场景级航拍上下文和固定的融合头先验共同构成当前样本的分类器。

Result: 在SensatUrban与UrbanBIS上，相较固定双表示分类头分别提升2.1和2.7 mIoU；在四种测试的编码器组合上提升2.1–2.3 mIoU。

Conclusion: 决策级样本自适应可与特征级融合互补，且提升在数据集与编码器之间具有一致性，表明将固定分类器替换为样本条件化分类器是投影-点云分割的有效改进方向。

Abstract: Urban photogrammetric point clouds are commonly processed as local 3D patches, limiting the context available for distinguishing geometrically similar categories. Orthographic projection of the same data yields a co-registered digital orthophoto map (DOM)/RGB-D representation that supports efficient large-context modeling but compresses vertical geometry. Existing projection--point methods mainly fuse these two representations at the feature level while retaining a classifier shared across all samples. We present the Aerial Content-Aware Sample-Adaptive Classifier (ACASAC), which complements feature fusion with decision-level adaptation. A point-aligned aerial region bridges the full aerial crop and local 3D patch. ACASAC models scene-to-local variation within the projection and the representation discrepancy between aligned 2D and 3D supports, then transfers both relations to refine the local point class representation. The refined representation, scene-level aerial context, and fixed fused-head prior jointly form a classifier for the current sample. ACASAC improves the fixed dual-representation head by 2.1 and 2.7 mIoU points on SensatUrban and UrbanBIS, respectively, and by 2.1--2.3 points across four tested encoder combinations.

</details>


### [269] [Scaling Action-Free Video Latent Prediction for Transferable Representations in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02853)
*Xingtai Gui,Xubin Zhou,Yucheng Zhou,Dongqian Guo,Jiahao Gong,Feiyang Tan,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ScaleDrive：仅用大规模互联网无动作标注的行车视频做视频潜表示预测预训练，冻结编码器后接入端到端规划策略，验证了无动作视频预训练规模化可显著提升冻结表示的时空与几何信息，并跨策略架构与闭环基准稳定提升规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶策略依赖能同时刻画空间场景结构与时间动态的视觉表示。视频潜预测已成为学习时空视觉表示的有效范式，但"用无动作行车视频规模化该目标能否产生有益于端到端规划的表示"尚不明确；同时需要把表示学习与下游策略训练解耦，以单独评估从互联网视频中获得的表示可迁移性。

Method: 将无动作视频潜表示预测作为可扩展预训练目标，提出面向驾驶的ScaleDrive框架，仅在无动作标注、非基准专属的大规模互联网开放世界行车视频上预训练；下游端到端规划训练时保持预训练视频编码器冻结，以隔离表示本身的迁移能力；并通过ego-motion与深度探针（probe）定量分析冻结表示中的时间与几何信息；进而系统评估该表示在多种端到端驾驶策略与闭环基准/协议间的迁移。

Result: 随无动作视频预训练规模增大，ego-motion与深度探针显示冻结表示中编码的时间信息与几何信息逐步增强；在多个端到端驾驶策略与闭环基准上，ScaleDrive均带来一致的下游性能提升，表明其表示可跨策略架构、评估场景与协议泛化。

Conclusion: 规模化无动作视频潜表示预测能够产生对端到端规划真正有用且可迁移的时空视觉表示，是一条无需动作标注、可扩展的自动驾驶预训练路径；冻结编码器即可获得稳定收益，说明收益来源于表示质量本身而非联合微调。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving policies require effective visual representations that capture both spatial scene structure and temporal dynamics. Video latent prediction has emerged as an effective paradigm for learning spatiotemporal visual representations, yet it remains unclear whether scaling this objective with action-free driving videos can yield representations that benefit end-to-end planning. We study action-free video latent prediction as a scalable pretraining objective and introduce ScaleDrive, a driving-oriented framework pretrained exclusively on large-scale internet open-world driving videos, without action annotations or benchmark-specific data. We keep the pretrained video encoder frozen during downstream end-to-end planning training to isolate the transferability of representations learned from internet driving videos. Ego-motion and depth probes show that scaling action-free video pretraining progressively strengthens the temporal and geometric information encoded in the frozen representation. We systematically study the visual representation transfer across different end-to-end driving policies and closed-loop benchmarks, where ScaleDrive consistently improves downstream performance and demonstrates that scaling action-free video latent prediction yields representations that generalize across both planning policy architectures and evaluation scenarios and protocols.

</details>


### [270] [Task Adaptation Through Guidance in Frozen Feature Upsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02854)
*Pingan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过"读出零干预"辨析上采样前特征自适应增益来源，定位空间引导为有效适应目标，并在VOC/Cityscapes上取得稳定mIoU提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上采样前的特征自适应能改善稠密预测，但增益究竟来自特征数值变化、空间聚合变化，还是两者兼有，尚不明确；需要解耦这一歧义以指导冻结上采样系统的适配设计。

Method: 在冻结的JAFAR上采样器前后分别放置匹配的残差适配器，共享同一父模型进行对照；构造"读出零干预"（readout-null）：保持冻结线性分类器的粗粒度logits不变，仅允许空间注意力权重自适应；并在已训练模型上做引导/值交换实验，配合固定引导的重训练对照；在PASCAL VOC与Cityscapes上做三随机种子实验，并覆盖JAFAR与AnyUp两种上采样器。

Result: 上采样前自适应相对上采样后自适应，mIoU分别提升0.807（VOC）与1.413（Cityscapes）个百分点；在两数据集与两种上采样器上，读出零干预相对对应冻结线性基线平均增益1.379–1.987个百分点；重训练对照进一步支持自适应空间引导的有效性。

Conclusion: 稠密预测可在粗分类器输出不变的情况下得到改善，说明增益主要来自空间引导（聚合）的自适应；空间引导是适配冻结特征上采样系统的有效作用靶点。

Abstract: Adapting features before upsampling can improve dense prediction, but the gains may arise from changes in feature values, spatial aggregation, or both. We investigate this ambiguity by comparing matched residual adapters before and after a frozen JAFAR upsampler under a shared parent. We construct a readout-null intervention that preserves a frozen linear classifier's coarse logits while allowing spatial attention weights to adapt.
