Guidance/value exchanges on trained models and retrained fixed-guidance controls test the utility of this adaptation route. In three-seed experiments on PASCAL Visual Object Classes (VOC) and Cityscapes, adaptation before upsampling improves mean intersection over union (mIoU) by 0.807 and 1.413 percentage points, respectively, over adaptation after upsampling. Across both datasets and the JAFAR and AnyUp upsamplers, readout-null adaptation produces mean gains of 1.379-1.987 points over the corresponding frozen linear baselines. Retrained controls further support the benefit of adaptive spatial guidance. These findings explain how dense predictions can improve despite unchanged coarse classifier outputs and identify spatial guidance as a useful target when adapting frozen feature upsampling systems.

</details>


### [271] [Solv3R: 3D Looped Transformer as a Multi-view Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02857)
*Jeong-gi Kwak,Andrea Tagliasacchi,Sho Kagami,Yuki Ono,Kwang Moo Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Solv3R 是一个循环式（looped）Transformer，学习从当前状态继续求解多视图几何，其更新算子与迭代次数无关，单一模型即可统一支持离线重建、增量重建、重定位与流式重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 SfM 是迭代式优化，可自然地增量扩展地图、随观测增多改进几何；而多数几何基础模型要么单次前向输出全部几何，要么以 RNN 式循环状态顺序处理帧，导致"继续扩展与精化"变得困难。作者希望让学习型求解器具备经典 SfM 那种可迭代、可继续求解的特性。

Method: 提出 Solv3R——一个 looped Transformer，迭代地从当前几何状态继续求解多视图几何。关键在于更新算子与迭代步数无关（iteration-count independent），使已精化视图与新引入视图无论"解到何种程度"都能被联合更新。训练目标同时包含：在持续迭代下保持准确重建不被破坏，以及纠正不准确的初始状态。已求解视图的表示可缓存一次，并在后续迭代中作为几何上下文复用，从而支持紧凑高效的 KV cache。作者还分析了求解器的迭代动力学。

Result: 与 SOTA 对比的动力学分析表明，单一学习型求解器无需任务特定的训练或模块，即可支持离线重建、增量重建、重定位和流式重建四类任务。

Conclusion: 将经典 SfM 的"持续迭代精化"思想带入学习型几何基础模型是可行且有效的：状态无关的更新算子 + 可复用 KV 缓存，使一个统一求解器覆盖多种重建设定。代码与模型将公开于 https://ubc-vision.github.io/solv3r/。

Abstract: Classical structure-from-motion reconstruction proceeds iteratively, refining camera poses and scene geometry from a current estimate. This allows maps to easily expand without recomputing already computed views, or geometry estimates to improve as more observations are provided. Most geometric foundation models, however, predict entire geometry in a single forward pass or update a recurrent state as frames arrive sequentially, making this expansion and refinement non-trivial. We introduce Solv3R, a looped transformer that learns to continue solving multi-view geometry from its current state. Importantly, its update operator is independent of the iteration count, allowing previously refined and newly introduced views to be jointly updated regardless of how ``solved'' they are. We train the operator to preserve accurate reconstructions under continued iteration and correct inaccurate states, enabling refinement of fixed image collections and integration of new observations. Representations of solved views can also be cached once and reused as geometric context across iterations, enabling compact and efficient key-value caching. We analyze the solver dynamics and compare it with the state-of-the-art, demonstrating that a single learned solver supports offline reconstruction, incremental reconstruction, relocalization, and streaming without task-specific training or modules. The code and models will be made publicly available at https://ubc-vision.github.io/solv3r/.

</details>


### [272] [Performance evaluation of super-resolution techniques for characterization and phenotyping of commodity crops using multispectral imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02862)
*Abhiram Karukayil,João F. C. Mota,Mateo Vásquez-Guevara,Tito Arevalo-Ramirez,Fernando Auat Cheein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估将RGB训练的八种单图像超分方法直接应用于红绿近红外(RGN)作物影像的可行性，发现在保持光谱保真与植被指数可靠性的前提下，Transformer类方法最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光谱影像光谱信息丰富但空间分辨率受限，易遮蔽精细作物结构并影响植被指数(VI)估计；需验证为RGB设计的SISR方法能否迁移至RGN多光谱场景。

Method: 不微调，直接将8种预训练方法（双三次/最近邻插值、Real-ESRGAN、HAT、HiT-SR、SRFormer、ResShift、StableSR）应用于RGN影像，做4×与16×重建，覆盖整图与人工分割叶片区域；用MAE、PSNR、SSIM、SAM、ERGAS评估重建质量，并用MAE、PSNR、SSIM评估8种植被指数。

Result: Transformer类方法在两种评估设置下均显著优于GAN与扩散类方法(p<0.001)，其中HAT、HiT-SR、SRFormer构成最高性能统计层级。

Conclusion: RGB训练的SISR可提升RGN作物影像空间细节，同时保持光谱一致性与VI可靠性，可服务于高分辨率作物表型分析。

Abstract: Multispectral imagery provides spectral information beyond the visible range for vegetation monitoring, but it typically suffers from limited spatial resolution, which can obscure fine crop structures and affect vegetation-index (VI) estimates.} This study evaluates whether single-image super-resolution (SISR) methods developed for red, green, and blue (RGB) imagery can enhance the spatial resolution of red, green, and near-infrared (RGN) crop images while preserving spectral fidelity. Eight pretrained methods are applied directly to RGN images without fine-tuning: bicubic and nearest-neighbor interpolation, Real-ESRGAN, HAT, HiT-SR, SRFormer, ResShift, and StableSR. Reconstruction at $4\times$ and $16\times$ is evaluated for whole images and manually segmented leaf regions. Reconstruction quality is assessed using mean absolute error (MAE), peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity index (SSIM), spectral angle mapper (SAM), and erreur relative globale adimensionnelle de synthèse (ERGAS). Eight VIs, computed from reference and super-resolved images, are evaluated using MAE, PSNR, and SSIM. Transformer-based methods \modification{significantly} outperform GAN- and diffusion-based approaches in both evaluation settings \modification{($p < 0.001$)}, with \modification{HAT, HiT-SR, and SRFormer forming the highest-performing statistical tier}. These findings indicate that RGB-trained SISR can enhance spatial detail in RGN crop imagery while maintaining spectral consistency and VI reliability for high-resolution crop phenotyping.

</details>


### [273] [ProtoFusion: Protocol-Guided Prompt Refinement for Generative Infrared and Visible Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02877)
*Pengwei Liang,Junjun Jiang,Junhui Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoFusion 将红外与可见光图像融合重构为"协议约束的生成"任务，用结构化协议显式规定应保留/抑制的证据与融合属性，并通过闭环的提示精炼与结构化信念构建实现更优融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合方法依赖固定融合算子、手工设计目标函数或人工指定的一次性提示，缺乏对"哪些证据应保留或抑制、融合结果应满足何种性质"的显式、可学习表达，难以灵活适配场景并处理源图像间的残余不一致。

Method: 1) 以结构化协议表征融合意图，协议由环境状态卡、接地原则与风格画像三部分组成；2) 提出协议引导的提示精炼，构建"生成—评估—修正"闭环学习框架，在多模态证据下迭代优化协议，训练完成后协议在推理阶段固定并直接引导生成；3) 引入结构化信念构建，将模态可靠性、场景证据与跨模态冲突组织为结构化表征，用于生成后的保守修正以缓解残余源不一致。

Result: 在标准基准上的大量实验表明，ProtoFusion 提升了定量指标，在美学评估上取得较好结果，并生成视觉上连贯一致的融合图像。

Conclusion: 将融合建模为协议约束的生成并提供闭环协议学习，是一种可行且有效的范式；固定化协议配合结构化信念修正，可在指标、主观美学与视觉一致性上同时获益。

Abstract: Infrared and visible image fusion integrates complementary information from both modalities to provide a more comprehensive representation of the scene. We propose ProtoFusion, a protocol-guided generative framework for infrared and visible image fusion. Instead of relying on fixed fusion operators, hand-crafted objectives, or manually specified one-shot prompts, ProtoFusion formulates fusion as protocol-constrained generation. It represents fusion intent with a structured protocol, consisting of environment state cards, grounding principles, and a style profile, which explicitly specifies which evidence should be preserved or suppressed and which properties the fused result should satisfy. To optimize this protocol, we introduce protocol-guided prompt refinement, a closed-loop learning framework that iteratively performs generation, evaluation, and revision under multimodal evidence. Once learned, the optimized protocol is fixed at inference time and directly guides fusion generation. To address residual source inconsistencies, we further incorporate structured belief construction, which organizes modality reliability, scene evidence, and cross-modal conflicts into a structured representation for conservative correction after generation. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks show that ProtoFusion improves quantitative performance, achieves favorable results in aesthetic evaluation, and produces visually coherent fusion outputs. Project Page: {https://erfect2020.github.io/ProtoFusion/}.

</details>


### [274] [PhotoGuider: Composition-Aware Cropping and 360$^{\circ}$ View Framing with Explainable Photographic Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02897)
*Yuan-Kang Lee,Kuan-Lin Chen,Chia-Che Chang,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhotoGuider：面向360°全景的"先测量后取景"方法，用统一的构图感知特征与紧凑学习评分器同时发现观看方向并完成取景，并提供相机参数与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像裁剪默认照片本身已具备值得保留的构图，只需决定裁在哪里；而360°全景中观看方向尚未确定，必须先在场景中"发现"值得框取的视角，再谈构图。已有方法缺少方向选择、原生重渲染参数输出与测量驱动的解释。

Method: 提出measure-then-frame流程：用基础视觉模型与任务专用模型抽取构图感知特征，结构化为Subject、Composition、Aesthetics、Integrity四类"摄影token"；在这些共享特征上训练紧凑评分器，同时完成方向选择与精细取景；微调MLLM解释器，基于图像与token测量结果生成构图相关解释，并输出相机参数以支持原生重渲染。

Result: 在两个标准2D裁剪基准上取得对真值裁剪的最高平均mIoU；盲评排序实验中，120名参与者对Poly Haven HDRI库100个全景场景的三种裁剪方法排序，本方法取景以53.2%位列第一，两个强基线分别为27.3%和19.5%。

Conclusion: 首个将360°全景中显式构图驱动的方向选择、精细取景、原生重渲染相机参数输出与测量条件化解释相结合的方法，验证了"测量-再取景"范式在2D裁剪与全景取景上的有效性。

Abstract: Traditional image cropping assumes the photograph already contains the composition worth keeping, and asks only where to crop. A full 360$^{\circ}$ scene offers no such guarantee: the viewing direction in a scene has to be discovered before anything can be framed. We present PhotoGuider, a measure-then-frame method that answers both questions with compact learned scorers over one shared set of composition-aware features. The designed features are extracted using foundation and task-specific vision models and structured into four photographic tokens: Subject, Composition, Aesthetics, and Integrity. Our MLLM explainer is fine-tuned to generate composition-related explanations conditioned on images and measured token assessments. To our knowledge, PhotoGuider is the first method to combine explicit composition-based direction selection in 360$^{\circ}$ panoramas with refined framing, camera-parameter output for native re-rendering, and measurement-conditioned explanations. On two standard 2D cropping benchmarks, PhotoGuider achieves the highest average mIoU performance against ground-truth crops. In a blind ranking study, 120 participants ranked three cropping methods over 100 panoramic scenes from the public Poly Haven HDRI library, placing our framings first in 53.2% of cases, against 27.3% and 19.5% for two strong alternatives. Project page: https://ntuneillee.github.io/research/photoguider/

</details>


### [275] [TailorRig: Controllable and Structured Auxiliary Skeleton Generation for Garment-Aware Rigging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02902)
*Yajing Chen,Xin Chen,Saidan Lu,Yeqi Yuan,Weikai Chen,Changyu Hu,Cheng Zeng,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TailorRig 将服装感知的人形绑定重构为结构化、可控的全套骨骼生成问题，通过用户指定身体骨骼模板与艺术家语义标签驱动自回归生成器合成辅助骨链，并采用链到骨的分层蒙皮权重分配，在准确性、形变稳定性和可控性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动绑定方法主要从几何恢复骨骼，难以支持对辅助关节的艺术家控制；密集服装骨缺乏显式语义或运动链结构，导致训练不稳定与权重串扰（weight bleeding）。论文旨在把目标从"自动恢复绑定"转向"艺术家驱动的绑定创作"，使身体模板兼容性与辅助关节成为显式设计选择。

Method: 提出统一框架，暴露两条显式控制轴：(1) 用户指定的身体骨骼模板，保证身体绑定兼容性；(2) 艺术家提供的语义标签，驱动自回归生成器合成指定类别的辅助骨链。为在密集骨架下稳健分配蒙皮权重，引入分层"链→骨"公式：先将顶点关联到某运动链，再在链内细化骨权重，从而抑制空间相邻但语义无关结构间的权重串扰。

Result: 在多样角色集上评估，并构建 Human-Mix —— 超过 38,000 个人形模型的大规模数据集，专为服装感知绑定整理。实验表明相较现有方法，在骨骼准确度、形变稳定性、可控性与实用易用性上均有提升。

Conclusion: 将人形服装绑定表述为结构化、可控制的全套骨骼生成问题可行且有效：显式艺术家的控制轴与分层链到骨蒙皮显著改善密集服装骨架下的绑定质量，为角色资产生产流程提供更实用的方案。

Abstract: Humanoid rigging for modern characters with complex garments remains challenging due to the need for dense auxiliary skeletons and precise skinning control. Existing automatic methods primarily recover rigs from geometry, providing limited artist control over auxiliary articulation and often lacking explicit semantic or kinematic-chain structure for dense garment bones, leading to instability and weight bleeding. We present TailorRig, a framework that reformulates garment-aware humanoid rigging as a structured, controllable full-rig generation problem. This formulation shifts the goal from automatic rig recovery to artist-driven rig authoring, where both body-template compatibility and auxiliary articulation are explicit design choices. TailorRig exposes two explicit axes of artist control within a unified framework: a user-specified body skeleton template that preserves body-rig compatibility, and artist-provided semantic labels that drive an autoregressive generator to synthesize requested category-specific auxiliary bone chains. To robustly assign skinning weights under dense skeletal configurations, we further introduce a hierarchical chain-to-bone formulation: vertices are first associated to a kinematic chain, and bone weights are then refined within that chain, reducing weight bleeding between spatially adjacent but semantically unrelated structures. We evaluate TailorRig on a diverse set of characters and introduce Human-Mix, a large-scale dataset of over 38,000 humanoid models curated for garment-aware rigging. Experiments show that TailorRig improves skeleton accuracy, deformation stability, controllability, and practical usability over existing methods.

</details>


### [276] [Unlocking the Potential of Contrastive Regularization for Diffusion Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02921)
*Yi Tan,Beier Zhu,Sirui Chen,Yunzhe Lin,Yuan Zhou,Cheng Chen,Xingyu Zhu,Tianrun Chen,Yanbin Hao,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探究 InfoNCE 式对比学习作为扩散 Transformer 正则化器的有效性，发现朴素 InfoNCE 收益甚微，原因是高噪声水平下近正交噪声分量构成"捷径"；提出以干净样本表示距离锚定对比离散度的 CRAN 正则化，在 ImageNet 上稳定提升训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示正则化已被证明能改善扩散 Transformer 训练，但最经典的 InfoNCE 式对比学习在此场景下仍缺乏系统研究。作者由此提出核心问题：标准对比学习能否作为扩散 Transformer 的有效正则化器？实验显示朴素 InfoNCE 仅带来边际收益，需诊断其失效机制并加以修正。

Method: 作者分析发现目标易被捷径主导：扩散输入中的近正交噪声分量可在拉大负样本对距离的同时削弱正样本对齐，在高噪声水平尤为明显。为此提出 Clean Representation-ANchored Contrastive（CRAN）正则化，用干净样本的表示距离锚定对比离散度，将干净样本的关系语义结构迁移到含噪扩散表示上，从而抑制噪声捷径。

Result: 在 ImageNet 上的大量实验表明，CRAN 在不同模型规模与训练预算下均能持续改善扩散 Transformer 训练，优于内部特征对齐与离散正则化基线，并与外部 DINO 引导的 REPA 相当。

Conclusion: 结论是：只要适当约束噪声驱动的过度离散，基于 InfoNCE 的对比学习可重新成为扩散训练的有效正则化器，从而恢复其在此场景中的价值。

Abstract: Representation regularization has emerged as an effective way to improve diffusion transformer training. Yet one of the most canonical regularizers--InfoNCE-style contrastive learning--remains largely underexplored in this setting. We naturally ask a question: can standard contrastive learning serve as an effective regularizer for diffusion transformers? Surprisingly, we find that naive InfoNCE regularization brings only marginal gains. Our analysis reveals that the objective is easily dominated by a shortcut: near-orthogonal noise components in diffusion inputs provide a bypass for enlarging negative-pair distances while weakening positive-pair alignment, especially at high noise levels. To unlock the benefit of contrastive regularization, we propose Clean Representation-ANchored Contrastive (CRAN) regularization. Specifically, CRAN anchors contrastive dispersion with clean-sample representation distances, transferring clean relational semantic structure to noisy diffusion representations to suppress the noise shortcut. Extensive experiments on ImageNet show that CRAN consistently improves diffusion transformer training across model scales and training budgets, outperforming internal feature-alignment and dispersive regularization baselines while matching external DINO-guided REPA. These results re-establish InfoNCE-based contrastive learning as an effective regularizer for diffusion training, once its noise-driven over-dispersion is properly constrained.

</details>


### [277] [Comprehensive Evaluation of Monocular and Multi-view 3D Human Pose Estimation Models for Fitness Exercise Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02929)
*Néstor Ortega,Agustín Salgado,Javier Sánchez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在Fit3D健身数据集上统一评测了两种单目提升方法（TCPFormer、PoseMamba-L）与一种多视图方法（MVGFormer），并分析RTMPose/YOLO-Pose两种2D检测器质量及三种时序滤波器对精度、时序一致性与鲁棒性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习3D人体姿态估计在标准基准上精度已很高，但其在真实运动健身场景下的表现研究不足；同时单目与多视图方法的权衡、以及后处理对时序一致性的增益，尚缺乏在健身类数据集上的系统评估。

Method: 在同步多视角、带精确真值标注的Fit3D数据集上建立统一评测协议；单目方法分别输入真值关键点与RTMPose、YOLO-Pose生成的2D检测结果，以考察输入数据影响；并评估三种时序滤波器在抑制抖动与保持运动动态之间的效果。

Result: 给出了各方法在精度、时序一致性和对不完美2D检测鲁棒性方面的详细对比；揭示了2D检测器质量对提升类方法的显著影响，对比了Transformer与状态空间（Mamba）时序架构的表现，并讨论了单目与多视图系统在健身动作分析中的实际权衡。

Conclusion: 在面向运动分析的姿态估计流程中，2D检测质量、时序建模与后处理必须被联合考量，而非孤立优化。

Abstract: Three-dimensional human pose estimation has become a fundamental component of computer vision systems for sports analytics, rehabilitation, and motion understanding. Although recent deep learning approaches have achieved remarkable accuracy on standard benchmarks, their performance under realistic sports scenarios remains insufficiently studied. Furthermore, the trade-offs between recent monocular and multi-view approaches, as well as the benefits of post-processing techniques for improving temporal consistency, have not been systematically evaluated on fitness-oriented datasets. This paper evaluates recent state-of-the-art 3D human pose estimation methods on Fit3D, a dataset of synchronized multi-view recordings of a wide variety of fitness exercises with accurate ground-truth annotations. We study two recent monocular lifting approaches, TCPFormer and PoseMamba-L, and a multi-view model, MVGFormer, under a unified evaluation protocol. To investigate the influence of the input data, monocular methods are evaluated using ground-truth keypoints and detections generated by recent 2D pose estimators, RTMPose and YOLO-Pose. Additionally, we analyze the effectiveness of three temporal filters for reducing prediction jitter while preserving motion dynamics. Experimental results provide a detailed comparison of accuracy, temporal consistency, and robustness to imperfect 2D detections across the evaluated methods. Our analysis highlights the impact of detector quality on lifting-based approaches, compares transformer- and state-space-based temporal architectures, and discusses the practical trade-offs between monocular and multi-view systems for fitness motion analysis. Our results show that 2D detection quality, temporal modeling, and post-processing must be considered jointly in pose estimation pipelines for exercise analysis.

</details>


### [278] [Training-Time Domain Transfer for Generator-Free Diagnostics on Novel Modalities in Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02931)
*Julia A. Mielcarz,Andrea Camilloni,Nita Mulliqi,Jessica A. Frank,Anders Blilie,Emilius A. M. Janssen,Martin Eklund,Kimmo Kartasalo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"训练时域迁移"策略：用存档常规样本合成目标域训练数据，使模型可直接在真实新模态输入上诊断，免去推理时生成。在前列腺未染色病理上验证，其与原H&E基准的一致性优于推理时虚拟染色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新成像模态有诊断潜力，但训练数据稀缺制约AI开发。推理时域迁移（虚拟染色）虽可复用已有诊断模型，却带来幻觉风险、计算开销与监管复杂性，因此借sim-to-real思路改为训练时迁移。

Method: 以未染色前列腺组织病理为概念验证：从存档常规样本生成合成目标域训练数据，训练模型直接诊断真实输入；与推理时虚拟染色及常规H&E癌症检测进行对比，用Cohen's κ与Gwet's AC1评估一致性。

Result: 原始组织：κ=0.59 vs 0.37，AC1=0.73 vs 0.29；去石蜡组织：κ=0.66 vs 0.61，AC1=0.73 vs 0.64，训练时迁移一致性均更高。

Conclusion: 训练时域迁移在新兴诊断模态上具应用潜力，可在无推理时生成的前提下复用既有诊断能力。

Abstract: Novel imaging modalities promise improved diagnostics, but AI development is constrained by scarce training data. Inference-time domain transfer can address this by mapping novel modalities to established ones, enabling reuse of existing diagnostic models. However, this introduces hallucination risks, computational overhead, and regulatory complexity. Inspired by sim-to-real transfer, we propose training-time domain transfer, generating synthetic target-domain training data from archived routine samples to enable direct diagnosis on real inputs without inference-time generation. Using unstained prostate histopathology as a proof-of-concept, we compare this approach with inference-time virtual staining and conventional H&E-based cancer detection. Training-time transfer achieved higher concordance with the conventional benchmark than virtual staining for both raw (Cohen's $κ$=0.59 vs. 0.37; Gwet's AC1=0.73 vs. 0.29) and deparaffinized tissue ($κ$=0.66 vs. 0.61; AC1=0.73 vs. 0.64), supporting its potential for emerging diagnostic modalities.

</details>


### [279] [RouteREPA: Routed Representation Alignment for Just Image Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02941)
*Yi Tan,Beier Zhu,Sirui Chen,Cheng Chen,Xingyu Zhu,Yuan Zhou,Zhenshan Tan,Tianrun Chen,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对像素空间扩散Transformer（JiT）中REPA表征对齐与像素重建存在冲突的问题，提出RouteREPA，通过轻量级表征依赖路由器自适应分流面向对齐与生成的表征，并在多个网络深度上应用路由，在ImageNet-1K 256×256上稳定优于标准REPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REPA广泛用于加速潜空间DiT训练，但迁移到像素空间模型（如JiT）时效果受限。作者发现判别性对齐与像素生成之间存在显著表征冲突：JiT学到的表征与DINOv2的对齐程度远低于潜空间对应模型；对JiT施加REPA会持续降低其中间表征的可重建性，而Latent JiT无此现象。作者将此归因于JiT直接重建像素，要求表征在不同网络深度上分层编码判别语义与重建相关视觉信息。

Method: 提出Routed Representation Alignment（RouteREPA）：使用轻量级、依赖表征的路由器（routers）自适应地将任务导向的表征分别路由到对齐分支与生成分支；并进一步在多个网络深度上应用路由，以适配不同深度对表征的差异化需求。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K 256×256上的大量实验表明，RouteREPA在不同模型规模与训练预算下均持续提升JiT，优于标准REPA。在JiT-H/16上，仅使用PixelREPA三分之一的训练轮次（且无需无分类器引导）即超越PixelREPA。

Conclusion: 像素空间DiT中表征对齐与像素重建存在内在冲突，需按深度分层、按任务分流地处理表征；RouteREPA通过表征依赖路由与多深度路由有效缓解该冲突，在效率与生成质量上均优于REPA及PixelREPA。

Abstract: Representation alignment (REPA) is widely used to accelerate the training of latent-space diffusion transformers (DiTs). However, applying it to pixel-space models such as Just Image Transformers (JiT) is challenging. Our analysis reveals a pronounced representation conflict between discriminative alignment and pixel-space generation. Compared with its latent counterpart, JiT learns representations that are substantially less aligned with DINOv2. Applying REPA to JiT consistently reduces the reconstructability of its intermediate representations, whereas this effect is not observed in Latent JiT. We attribute this conflict to JiT directly reconstructing pixels, which requires its representations to hierarchically encode discriminative semantics and reconstruction-relevant visual information across network depths. To address these challenges, we propose Routed Representation Alignment (RouteREPA), which uses lightweight, representation-dependent routers to adaptively route task-oriented representations for alignment and generation. We further apply routing across multiple network depths to accommodate their depth-dependent representation requirements. Extensive experiments on ImageNet-1K at 256x256 resolution show that RouteREPA consistently improves JiT across model scales and training budgets, outperforming standard REPA. On JiT-H/16, RouteREPA further surpasses PixelREPA using only one-third of its training epochs without classifier-free guidance.

</details>


### [280] [Balancing Representations by Identifying and Generating Under-represented Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02952)
*Filipe Laitenberger,Hirokatsu Kataoka,Yuki Asano,Cees Snoek,Mohammadreza Salehi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BRIDGE：一种无标签反馈回路，用编码器kNN距离识别表征稀疏的长尾区间，再由扩散模型生成忠实局部变体并继续预训练，从而在不改变预训练目标的前提下修复长尾自监督表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习的覆盖范围继承自源数据。当无标签语料呈长尾分布时，表征在频繁模式附近密集、在覆盖薄弱处稀疏。现有长尾SSL方法仅对语料中已有图像重新加权，不引入新样本，无法扩展覆盖。

Method: BRIDGE围绕源数据构建无标签反馈回路：当前编码器以平均kNN距离为每张源图像打分；预算受限的采集规则在分数分布中选择“支持稀疏且生成器仍能产生忠实变体”的区间（排除极端尾部）；冻结的图像到图像扩散模型为选中锚点生成局部变体，扩充源集后继续预训练。作者将采集形式化为可恢复性加权的局部支持分配，并推导有限预算下偏好稀疏带、以及固定目标下每轮修复后重新分配最优的条件。

Result: 在五种长尾、网络爬取与合成源数据情形下，BRIDGE取得七任务线性探测与kNN平均最高分；配对实验将增益扩展至对比学习与自蒸馏目标下的ViT-S、低标签微调及语义分割；掩码重建下线性探测增益较小。生成修复效果可匹配恢复被扣留真实图像的oracle；冻结编码器下，被插入图像使选中锚点邻域收缩幅度约为匹配的未选中图像的两倍。

Conclusion: BRIDGE无需标签、不改变预训练目标，可附加于非精选网络数据预训练，由编码器决定生成哪些图像，以低成本方式弥补长尾覆盖不足。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning inherits the coverage of its source data. When an unlabeled corpus is long-tailed, the representation becomes dense around frequent modes and thin where coverage is weak. Objectives for long-tailed SSL reweight the images already in the corpus and add no new ones. We introduce BRIDGE (Balancing Representations by Identifying and Generating Under-represented Data), a label-free feedback loop around the source set. The current encoder scores every source image by its mean kNN distance, and a budgeted acquisition rule selects images from a band of that score distribution where support is thin and the generator still produces faithful variants. A frozen image-to-image diffusion model then adds local variants of the selected anchors, and pretraining continues on the enlarged source set. We formulate acquisition as recoverability-weighted allocation of local support and derive when a finite budget should favor a sparse band that excludes the extreme tail and when reallocation after each repair is optimal under a fixed objective. Across five long-tailed, web-scraped and synthetic source regimes, BRIDGE has the highest seven-task linear-probe and kNN average of the compared methods, and paired experiments extend the gain to ViT-S under contrastive and self-distillation objectives, to low-label fine-tuning and to semantic segmentation. The linear-probe gain is smaller under masked reconstruction. Generated repair matches an oracle that restores the withheld real images, and under a frozen encoder the inserted images shrink the neighborhoods of the selected anchors about twice as much as those of matched unselected images. BRIDGE needs no labels and leaves the pretraining objective unchanged, so it can be added to pretraining on uncurated web data, where the encoder decides which images to generate.

</details>


### [281] [One Image Is Enough: Self-Generated Supervision for Personalizing Human Video Diffusion at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02964)
*Sadman Rakib Pinon,Adamo Orsini,Yanis Falaki,Linlian Jiang,Jacob Kingi,Yang Wang,Xinxin Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需真实多姿态视频的自监督个性化方法：用域微调的姿态条件生成器合成关键姿态伪真值帧，配合推理期仅参考采样筛选身份一致帧，再用静态片段训练逐主体低秩适配器，从而在姿态驱动的人体视频扩散中更好地保持身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 姿态驱动的人体视频扩散在目标姿态偏离参考图时难以保持身份，面部与服装失真。常规个性化依赖监督信号，但单张图只能固定单一姿态下的身份，实际中又无法假定拥有多姿态的主体视频，因此监督缺失。

Method: 1）域微调的姿态条件生成器在选定关键姿态处生成伪真值帧；2）推理期仅参考（reference-only）采样器将候选帧向主体引导，并保留身份一致性最高者；3）仅凭参考图配置逐主体低秩适配器（LoRA），在由这些合成帧组成的静态片段上训练，无需主体视频、真值或标签。

Result: 在 TikTok 与 UBC Fashion 上，适配后的模型在所有指标上均优于基座模型，逆转了身份与运动之间的权衡，并在未使用任何真实帧的情况下匹配或超过基于五帧真实帧的适配效果。分析表明合成帧虽保真度较低，但监督更有效：真实帧携带的运动信息会侵蚀时间先验，而静态片段只传递外观。

Conclusion: 合成监督可替代缺失的多姿态真实数据，实现单图驱动的身份保持个性化；静态外观片段比含运动信息的真实帧更适合作为个性化监督信号。

Abstract: Pose-driven human video diffusion animates a person from a single reference image and a target pose sequence, yet it struggles to preserve identity, rendering the face and garments unfaithfully as the pose departs from it. Personalization is the standard remedy, but its supervision is missing: one image fixes identity at a single pose, while a multi-pose subject video cannot be assumed in practice. Instead, we synthesize the missing supervision. A domain-tuned, pose-conditioned generator produces pseudo-ground-truth frames at selected key poses. An inference-time reference-only sampler steers these candidates toward the subject and keeps the most identity-consistent. A per-subject low-rank adapter then trains on static clips of them, configured from the reference alone, with no subject video, ground truth, or labels. Although lower in fidelity, these synthetic frames supervise more effectively than real ones. Real frames as captured carry motion that erodes the temporal prior, while a static clip conveys appearance alone. On TikTok and UBC Fashion, the adapted model improves the base on every metric, inverts the identity-versus-motion trade-off, and matches or surpasses adaptation on five real frames despite using none. Code and weights are available at https://github.com/SadmanPinon/one-image-is-enough.

</details>


### [282] [ILLRAW: Illumination-Aware RAW Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02995)
*Xinzhe Wang,Xiao Liang,Xiaoli Zhang,Kang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ILLRAW，一种光照感知的端到端 RAW 目标检测框架，绕过 RAW-to-sRGB 重建，直接在输入阶段用轻量特征适配器学习面向检测的 RAW 特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAW 检测器通常引入可学习 ISP 模块把 RAW 转成类 sRGB 格式以适配现成检测器，但这一范式将表示学习与下游检测任务解耦；同时迫使网络继承以人为中心的视觉先验，丢弃了 RAW 的线性传感器响应、高比特位深、少处理场景线索等内在优势。

Method: ILLRAW 不重建视觉上合理的 sRGB 图像，而是在现有检测器输入阶段插入轻量特征适配器直接学习面向检测的 RAW 特征：估计光照掩码，并引入通道敏感嵌入（channel-sensitive embeddings）建模 Bayer 通道响应，再通过光照注意力融合模块（illumination attention fusion module）融合上述特征。

Result: 在多样的 AODRaw 基准上取得明显提升，在 LOD 与 PascalRAW 上表现具有竞争力，尤其在暗曝光条件下结果突出。

Conclusion: 端到端、检测导向的 RAW 特征学习优于 RAW-to-sRGB 解耦范式，可保留 RAW 内在信息并提升低光场景下的检测性能。

Abstract: Existing RAW-based detectors typically introduce learnable ISP modules to convert RAW inputs into sRGB-like formats to fit off-the-shelf detectors. However, this RAW-to-sRGB paradigm decouples representation learning from the downstream detection task. Crucially, it forces the network to inherit human-centric visual priors while discarding intrinsic RAW advantages, such as linear sensor responses and high-bit-depth, minimally processed scene cues. In this paper, we propose \textbf{ILLRAW}, an \textbf{ILL}umination-aware end-to-end \textbf{RAW} object detection framework. Instead of reconstructing visually plausible sRGB images, ILLRAW directly learns detection-oriented RAW features through a lightweight feature adapter inserted at the input stage of existing detectors. ILLRAW estimates an illumination mask and introduces channel-sensitive embeddings to model Bayer-channel responses. These features are fused by an illumination attention fusion module. Experiments show clear improvements on the diverse AODRaw benchmark and competitive performance on LOD and PascalRAW, with particularly strong results under dark exposure.

</details>


### [283] [Q-CamTuning: A Multimodal Large Language Model for Fine-Grained Quality Reasoning in Livestreaming Camera Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03007)
*Yanchao Bi,Pengfei Chen,Yulong Yan,Qian Zhang,Biao Wang,Leida Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对直播相机自动调参场景，构建细粒度图像对偏好与质量推理标注数据集FGLive-Comments，并提出渐进式"感知—证据—指引"框架的多模态大模型Q-CamTuning，在质量打分与细粒度图像对排序上优于现有IQA方法，并可给出相机参数调整建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直播应用普及使自动相机调参成为保障普通用户高QoE的必要手段，但现有IQA方法在相机调参中面临两大挑战：一是难以区分图像间细微质量差异（调参场景中常见），二是缺乏对细粒度差异的解释能力以及为调参提供建议的能力。

Method: 1) 构建FGLive-Comments指令微调数据集，包含细粒度图像对偏好标注及基于全局与局部视觉证据的质量推理标注，支持相机参数的选择性调整建议；2) 基于该数据集提出多模态大模型Q-CamTuning，采用渐进式感知—证据—指引（progressive perception-evidence-guidance）框架：学习质量感知视觉表征、结合全局—局部证据增强细粒度推理、并将质量问题映射到相机参数调整方向。

Result: 大量实验表明，Q-CamTuning在质量评分和细粒度图像对排序两方面均优于现有IQA模型；用户研究进一步验证了其推荐相机调参建议的有效性。

Conclusion: 将多模态大语言模型与细粒度质量推理标注相结合，可有效应对相机调参中的细微质量判别与可解释调参建议需求，为直播场景的自动相机调优提供了新范式。

Abstract: With the popularization of livestreaming applications, automatic camera tuning has become indispensable for ordinary users to enjoy high Quality of Experience (QoE). However, the existing Image Quality Assessment (IQA) methods face two challenges in camera tuning. First, they struggle to identify subtle quality difference between images, which is common in camera tuning. Second, the ability to explain fine-grained differences and to provide suggestions for camera tuning are even more lacking. To address these challenges, we first construct FGLive-Comments, an instruction tuning dataset with rich annotations of both preferences for fine-grained image pairs and quality reasoning from global and local visual evidence, which supports selective adjustment recommendations of camera parameters. Based on FGLive-Comments, we develop Q-CamTuning, a multimodal large language model for livestreaming camera tuning. Its progressive perception-evidence-guidance framework learns quality-aware visual representations, enhances fine-grained reasoning with global-local evidence, and maps quality issues to camera-parameter adjustment directions. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that Q-CamTuning outperforms existing IQA models in both quality scoring and fine-grained image pair ranking. User study also validates its ability to recommend camera tuning suggestions.

</details>


### [284] [Efficient Hybrid Distillation: Synergizing Score and Adversarial Objectives for One-Step Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03017)
*Fei Peng,Junqiang Wu,Haoxian Tan,Jun Zhou,Jie Hu,Xiaoming Wei,Jie Guo,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EASD框架，通过"区域自适应采样"将Score Distillation与Adversarial Distillation分别约束在各自有效噪声区间内协同工作，用于扩散/流匹配模型的一步加速蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合蒸馏范式优于单一方法，但现有工作仅简单叠加损失目标，缺乏对"如何有效协同"的机制性分析与设计准则。

Method: 分析两类目标的可行域：Score Distillation在高噪声区有效（捕获全局分布）但低噪声区缺乏细粒度监督；Adversarial Distillation在低噪声区有效但高噪声区不稳定且判别器易失效。据此设计regime-adaptive sampling策略，按噪声区间将采样与目标分配至各自有效区间，并推广至DiT-based Flow Matching模型。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256上，基于SiT-XL/2+REPA实现一步生成FID 1.39。

Conclusion: 揭示了两种蒸馏目标在噪声区间上的互补性，为混合蒸馏的最大化利用提供了具体设计准则，并验证了其在流匹配架构上的可迁移性。

Abstract: Numerous distillation methods have been developed to accelerate diffusion models. Recent research indicates that hybrid strategies---blending different paradigms---yield superior results compared to single-method approaches. However, current works predominantly rely on a simple combination of objectives, leaving the question of how to effectively synergize them largely underexplored. In this paper, we specifically investigate the synergy between Score Distillation and Adversarial Distillation. We reveal that their operating zones are distinct and complementary: Score Distillation is effective in high-noise regimes for capturing the global distribution yet lacks fine-grained supervision in low-noise regions. Conversely, Adversarial Distillation is effective in low-noise regions but is prone to instability and suffers from discriminator failure when applied to high-noise intervals. Leveraging this insight, we propose Efficient Adversarial Score Distillation (EASD), a framework that synergizes these objectives via a regime-adaptive sampling strategy, directing each objective to focus on its effective interval. We further apply this paradigm to DiT-based Flow Matching models. Empirically, our method achieves a promising one-step FID of 1.39 on ImageNet 256$\times$256 using the SiT-XL/2+REPA model. This work provides a concrete guideline for maximizing the potential of hybrid distillation. Our code and model are available at https://github.com/pf0607/EASD .

</details>


### [285] [VizDAGuide: Misleading Chart Question Answering in Compact Vision Language Models via DAG-Structured Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03023)
*Haiyang Zhang,Sicheng Song,Zixin Chen,Jie Gu,Huiya Ou,Chenhui Li,Changbo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VizDAGuide，一种DAG结构的加权监督训练方法，用于误导性图表问答，在开放与多选两种格式上均优于匹配的线性CoT-SFT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 误导性图表会使视觉语言模型得出与底层数据相冲突的结论；现有工作集中于推理时策略，训练阶段的监督方式仍缺乏探索。

Method: 将中间推理状态及其依赖关系表示为有向无环图，按各状态角色分配监督权重，并把图序列化为统一训练目标，从而以单次前向推理同时支持开放式与多选问答。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL与InternVL3.5上、两个基准的实验中，两种答案格式均优于内容与权重均匹配的线性CoT-SFT基线；过程级评估显示在grounding与操作执行上取得提升；消融表明依赖结构与加权监督组合使用时效果最佳。

Conclusion: 结果支持采用DAG结构化的加权监督来应对误导性图表问答任务。

Abstract: Misleading charts can lead vision-language models to conclusions that conflict with underlying data. Existing work focuses on inference-time strategies, while training-time supervision remains less explored. We present VizDAGuide, a DAG-structured supervision method for misleading-chart question answering. The method represents intermediate reasoning states and their dependencies as a directed acyclic graph, assigns supervision weights according to their roles, and serializes the graph into a unified training target for open-ended and multiple-choice QA with single-pass inference. Experiments on two benchmarks across Qwen3-VL and InternVL3.5 models show improvements over a content- and weight-matched Linear CoT-SFT baseline on both answer formats. Process-level evaluation shows gains in grounding and operation execution, while ablations indicate that dependency structure and weighted supervision are most effective when combined. These results support DAG-structured weighted supervision for misleading-chart question answering.

</details>


### [286] [VLM-as-a-Judge for Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03027)
*Bessie Dominguez-Dager,Miguel Cazorla,Vincent Lepetit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将冻结的通用视觉语言模型（VLM）作为零样本"裁判"对专家模型的短候选列表重排，在CHIRLA长期换衣ReID基准的四个场景上大幅提升rank-1，并通过"专家rank-1分歧"门控削减约40%的VLM调用与总算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 换衣行人重识别（CC-ReID）现有方法依赖不同的身份线索，领先于某一基准的方法在其它基准上失效，尚无普遍占优的方案，说明单一专家模型的判别依据不稳健。

Method: 以两个冻结的专家Re-ID模型生成短候选列表（可合并两者短名单），用未微调的通用VLM作为判别器对候选进行重排；目标数据上无任何组件微调。为降低算力，借用集成学习的"分歧"准则，仅当两专家rank-1预测不一致时才调用VLM裁判（无阈值门控）。

Result: 在CHIRLA四个场景上，重排均提升rank-1，平均较最佳专家高12.9个点、较两者最佳朴素集成高9.4个点；合并两专家短名单的效果不低于仅重排强专家，且成本更低；门控去除40%的VLM调用与相近比例总算力，rank-1无显著下降，代价为mAP损失1.8–3.8个点。

Conclusion: 在专用专家模型陷入瓶颈的基准上，冻结的通用VLM是最强的身份判别者；VLM裁判仍是主要算力开销，但通过分歧门控可实现可观的效率-精度折衷。

Abstract: Cloth-changing person re-identification (CC-ReID) is still very challenging, and no method clearly dominates: methods that lead on one benchmark collapse on the others, because they rest on different identity cues. We show that a general-purpose vision-language model (VLM), used zero-shot as a judge for reranking a short candidate list, beats the expert retrieval models it reranks by a wide and consistent margin. On the four scenarios of CHIRLA, a long-term CC-ReID benchmark that no publicly released Re-ID model is trained on, reranking the candidates of two frozen experts raises rank-1 on every scenario, by 12.9 points on average over the best expert and 9.4 over the best naive ensemble of the two, with no component fine-tuned on the target data. Merging both experts' shortlists matches or beats reranking the stronger expert's shortlist alone, at lower cost. Introducing a VLM does, however, come at a cost: almost all of the pipeline's FLOPs are spent on it. We therefore borrow a criterion from ensembling and invoke the VLM judge only where the two experts disagree at rank 1. This threshold-free gate removes 40% of the calls, and a comparable share of total compute, with no significant drop in rank-1, trading 1.8 to 3.8 points of mAP. Judging remains the dominant cost, but on a benchmark where dedicated experts stall, we find a frozen general-purpose model to be the strongest judge of identity. The code is available at https://github.com/bdager/JudgeReID.

</details>


### [287] [GPU-Native Real-Time Textured Mesh Reconstruction with Seam-Aware Multi-View Radiometric Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03033)
*Hiroyuki Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GPU原生的体积视频系统，在GPU上全流程完成多RGB-D传感器的实时带纹理三角网格重建，避免中间数据回传主机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSDF系统虽加速了融合与网格提取，但扩展到逐帧带纹理建网时需引入CPU网格处理、CPU端纹理工作或多阶段CPU-GPU传输，形成性能瓶颈。

Method: 输入暂存后，将TSDF融合、Marching Cubes、连通分量噪声去除、基于视角的纹理映射、修补及辐射接缝校正全部驻留GPU；用固定大小数组表示网格拓扑，实现GPU上常数时间邻接访问并使网格级操作数据并行；因每面仅用单相机纹理，估计每相机低频增益场并在GPU上烘焙进纹理图集以消除曝光接缝。

Result: 在CMU Panoptic数据集上，各测试体素分辨率与传感器数量下均快于基线，相对CUDA加速的nvblox与CPU版Open3D峰值加速超过一个数量级，重渲染感知质量接近离线带纹理建网基线。

Conclusion: 全GPU流水线可消除阶段间主机回传开销，实现实时高质量带纹理体积视频重建。

Abstract: We present a GPU-native volumetric-video system for real-time reconstruction of graphics-compatible textured triangle meshes from multiple RGB-D sensors. Existing TSDF systems accelerate fusion and mesh extraction, but extending them to per-frame textured meshing adds CPU mesh processing, CPU-side texturing work, or extra CPU-GPU transfers between stages. After input staging, our method keeps the reconstruction and texturing stages (TSDF fusion, Marching Cubes, connected-component noise removal, view-based texture mapping, inpainting, and radiometric seam correction) resident on the GPU; no intermediate mesh or image data returns to the host between these stages. A fixed-size-array representation of mesh topology gives constant-time neighbor access on the GPU and makes the downstream mesh-level operations data-parallel. Because we texture each face from a single camera, exposure seams that vary smoothly across each image remain; we estimate a per-camera low-frequency gain field and bake it into the atlas on the GPU. On the CMU Panoptic dataset, our method is faster than the baselines at every tested voxel resolution and number of sensors, achieves peak speedups of more than an order of magnitude over CUDA-accelerated nvblox and CPU-based Open3D, and approaches an offline textured-meshing baseline in perceptual re-rendering quality.

</details>


### [288] [Bayesian Sequential Optimal Experimental Design for Single-Pixel Imaging using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03042)
*Marcos Antonio Obando,Tianyuan Wang,Tanja Tarvainen,Tristan van Leeuwen,Felix Lucka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将单像素成像中的自适应压缩感知测量设计表述为贝叶斯序贯最优实验设计(sOED)问题，并用强化学习求解：智能体以期望信息增益为奖励学习策略，直接把当前后验信念映射为下一个二值照明图案，避免在测量过程中直接优化复杂信息论目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应压缩感知需序贯选取最具信息量的测量，对现代成像系统至关重要；但单像素成像的设计空间由全部二值照明图案构成，规模巨大，用传统方法直接优化贝叶斯sOED目标通常不可行。

Method: 把SPI的sOED重构为强化学习问题，奖励设为期望信息增益（贝叶斯OED中最完整的不确定性削减度量），通过反复试验学习策略函数，从而在推理时高效地由当前后验直接输出下一最优二值测量图案，而无需在线求解信息论优化。

Result: 在简单但具代表性的SPI成像场景下，评估了两种策略参数化下所学策略的可扩展性；考察了仅有近似后验信息或测量受噪声干扰时的性能退化情况；并针对生物医学应用提出若干额外设计约束策略。数值实验表明该框架保持稳健，可扩展到更复杂的大规模SPI场景。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯sOED-RL框架为SPI自适应测量设计提供了一种高效、稳健且可扩展的途径，能够应对近似后验、噪声与设计约束等实际条件。

Abstract: Adaptive compressed sensing (CS) aims to select the most informative measurements sequentially, which is crucial for many modern imaging systems. This task can be formulated as a Bayesian sequential optimal experimental design (sOED) problem, where each new acquisition is chosen to maximally reduce uncertainty about the unknown image given prior information and subsequent data provided by acquisitions. However, in single-pixel imaging (SPI), the large design space consists of all possible binary illumination patterns. Directly optimizing the Bayesian sOED objective over this space using conventional methods is generally challenging.
  In this work, we reformulate sOED for SPI as a reinforcement learning (RL) problem. In RL, an agent learns a policy through repeated experimental trials to maximize a reward, here given as the expected information gain, the most complete measure of uncertainty reduction in Bayesian OED. This avoids the need to directly optimize complex information-theoretic objectives during a measurement to obtain the next design. Instead, the result is a learned policy function that directly maps the current posterior belief about the image to the next optimal binary measurement pattern, in a computationally efficient way.
  Using a simple yet representative SPI imaging scenario, this work evaluates the scalability of the learned policy under two different policy parameterizations. Furthermore, we investigate how performance deteriorates when only approximate posterior information is available or the measurements are impaired by noise, and propose several strategies to introduce additional design constraints motivated by biomedical applications. The numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed Bayesian sOED-RL framework remains robust and can be extended to more complex, large-scale SPI scenarios.

</details>


### [289] [VideoEditor-MoE: Scaling Complex Video Editing with Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03043)
*Xiao Cao,Fuchen Long,Yansong Qu,Wen Xiao,Xin Zhang,Xinlong Sun,Xi Chen,Zhiyong Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个开源视频编辑混合专家（MoE）框架 VideoEditor-MoE，除稀疏 MLP-MoE 外引入 MoE-Attention（路由 key-value 专家），从 5B 模型初始化扩展到 14B/56B、每 token 约激活 8B 参数，在复杂指令视频编辑上优于 Explicit-MoE 与 MLP-MoE 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂视频编辑需在理解复杂指令的同时联合满足多个编辑请求；依靠稠密模型规模化会显著增加计算成本，因此需要稀疏化以在扩展容量的同时控制开销。

Method: 1) 构建 MoE 视频编辑框架：在稀疏 MLP-MoE 层之外提出 MoE-Attention，通过路由的 key-value 专家扩展注意力容量，同时保留视频 token 间的全局交互；2) 从 5B 视频编辑模型初始化，扩展至 14B 与 56B 两个变体，每 token 激活约 8B 参数；3) 设置 Explicit-MoE（显式专家引导）与 MLP-MoE（仅 MLP 专家）两个互补基线进行对照；4) 开展专家激活、路由分布与空间特征分析；5) 用解耦权重衰减稳定专家扩展。

Result: 14B/56B 变体在复杂指令视频编辑上优于现有方法及两个基线的收益得到验证；分析表明所有专家均保持激活（无专家坍缩）；学到的路由对样本内容与扩散噪声水平的响应强于人工定义的编辑类别；空间分析显示对物体级语义特征敏感；解耦权重衰减可稳定专家扩展。

Conclusion: MoE 稀疏化（含注意力层专家化）是提升视频编辑容量与指令复杂度的有效路径，可在近似恒定的激活参数下获得规模收益；路由行为由内容与噪声而非人类语义类别主导，为后续专家设计与训练稳定性（解耦权重衰减）提供依据。

Abstract: Complex video editing requires understanding complex instructions while jointly fulfilling multiple editing requests. Meeting these demands through dense model scaling substantially increases computational cost. We introduce VideoEditor-MoE, the first open-source mixture-of-experts framework for video editing. Beyond sparse MLP-MoE layers, MoE-Attention expands attention capacity through routed key--value experts while preserving global interactions among video tokens. Initialized from a 5B video editing model, VideoEditor-MoE scales to 14B and 56B parameters, with approximately 8B parameters activated per token in both variants. Comparisons with existing methods and two complementary baselines, Explicit-MoE and MLP-MoE, assess its benefits over explicit expert guidance and MLP-only expert architectures. Further analyses demonstrate that all experts remain active and reveal that learned routing responds more strongly to sample content and diffusion noise levels than to human-defined editing categories. Spatial analysis further indicates sensitivity to object-level semantic features. We also identify decoupled weight decay as a strategy for stabilizing expert scaling.

</details>


### [290] [MuCHMDPoser: Multiview RGB-Camera Full-Body Pose Tracking for Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03062)
*Antoine Maiorca,George Fletcher,Thierry Ravet,Thierry Dutoit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 为缓解仅靠头显与手柄等稀疏传感器时VR全身姿态欠约束、下肢误差大的问题，作者在追踪框架中引入外部多视角RGB相机，将RGB人体姿态估计与稀疏信号融合，显著降低下肢追踪误差，并提出无需物理标记的一次性HMD-相机标定方法，最终在RTX3080上实现60 FPS端到端动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级VR仅依赖HMD与手持控制器提供的3个上半身关节，全身（尤其下肢）姿态高度欠约束，导致显著追踪误差；需在低成本的前提下提升全身自化身动画精度。

Method: 扩展追踪配置：在HMD+手柄稀疏信号之外加入外部多视角RGB相机，将基于RGB的姿态估计结果融入整体框架进行联合推理；同时评估一种无需物理标记的一次性HMD-相机标定流程；并给出完整系统的工程实现。

Result: 融合RGB姿态估计可显著降低下肢追踪误差，且在RGB估计存在常见伪影的情况下仍保持鲁棒性；标定无需物理标记即可完成；系统在Nvidia RTX3080 GPU上实现60 FPS端到端动画，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 多视角RGB相机作为外部感知补充，可有效补充稀疏VR传感器的信息缺失，是提升全身自化身追踪精度与实用性的可行方案，具备实时性与易部署性（免标记标定、开源实现）。

Abstract: Accurate full-body tracking in Virtual Reality (VR) is heavily underconstrained when relying solely on consumer-grade sparse sensors, such as Head-Mounted Displays (HMDs) and handheld controllers. Indeed, the self-avatar full-body pose must be inferred based on the 3 available upper-body joints tracked by these devices. This leads to significant tracking error, especially in lower-body pose. To overcome this issue, we propose to extend the tracking setup with external multiview RGB cameras. We show that integrating RGB-based pose estimation this framework is able to significantly decrease lower-body tracking errors, even under common artifacts in RGB-based estimates. Additionally, we evaluate a only-once HMD-camera calibration procedure that does not require any physical markers. We finally propose an implementation of the overall framework, achieving an end-to-end animation at 60 FPS on a Nvidia RTX3080 GPU. Codes are available at https://github.com/antmaio/MuCHMDPoser.

</details>


### [291] [VL-Dedup: Graded Multimodal Entity Resolution for Image-Text Data Deduplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03080)
*Zhenxi Liu,Guanze Chen,Dehui Yu,Xingyu Wang,Bingxu Hou,Chengwei Li,Tao Ma,Ke Wang,Gongsheng Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将图像-文本去重重新表述为分级多模态实体解析（graded multimodal ER），同时判定视觉同一性、字幕处理与不匹配，并在配对、簇、保留集三个层级评估；提出VL-Dedup方法与VL-DedupBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像-文本语料中存在同一图像的多份副本、同一图像的不同描述、被复用于不同图像的字幕、以及图文不匹配等情形，各需不同的治理动作。而已有的去重与多模态实体解析仅判断记录是否相同，且仅在标注配对上评估，配对分数无法反映哪些记录最终进入同一簇、哪些有用字幕被删除。

Method: 提出VL-Dedup：将同一视觉实体的记录聚成簇并限制簇规模；在簇内用视觉语言模型（VLM）与CLIPScore决定保留或标记哪些字幕。构建VL-DedupBench，含5万条COCO与SOP记录、66,788个标注配对。

Result: 在VL-DedupBench上，即使某分类器在标注对上不产生任何假边，仍会将数百万条来自不同实体的记录对合并；将VLM调用用于簇内配对而非分数不确定的配对，可在同一模型下获得更好的字幕决策；即便几乎所有簇都正确，每簇保留一条记录的做法在许多删除中丢失事实，而VL-Dedup的删除丢失信息显著更少。

Conclusion: 配对级指标不足以衡量去重质量，需在簇级与保留集级评估；簇内调用VLM的字幕治理策略优于基于不确定性的配对选择，且能有效减少信息损失。

Abstract: Image-text corpora contain copies of one image, captions that describe one image in different ways, captions reused for different images, and captions that do not match their images. Each case calls for a different curation action. Deduplication and multimodal entity resolution (ER) only decide whether records are the same, and they are evaluated on labeled pairs. Yet pair scores do not tell which records end up in the same cluster or which useful captions get deleted. We therefore formulate image-text deduplication as graded multimodal ER, which decides visual identity, caption handling, and mismatches for each pair, and we evaluate it at three levels: pairs, clusters, and the retained set. Our method, VL-Dedup, groups records of the same visual entity into clusters and caps the size of each cluster. Within each cluster, a vision-language model (VLM) and CLIPScore decide which captions to keep and which to flag. We also build VL-DedupBench, with 50,000 COCO and SOP records and 66,788 labeled pairs. On it, a classifier that selects no false edge among the labeled pairs still joins millions of record pairs from different entities. Spending VLM calls on the pairs inside clusters, rather than on pairs with uncertain scores, gives better caption decisions from the same model. Even when almost all clusters are correct, keeping one record per cluster loses facts in many deletions, while the deletions of VL-Dedup lose much less information.

</details>


### [292] [MIRA: Reinforcing Multimodal Reasoning via Deceptive Contextual Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03087)
*Zhihan Yin,Jianxin Liang,Yifeng Yao,Nonghai Zhang,and Bingyue Peng,Chunguang Qie,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MIRA训练框架，通过注入筛选后的欺骗性视觉上下文构造"hard traps"并结合组内反思触发机制，提升多模态推理对错误视觉上下文的鲁棒性与逻辑推理能力，无需人工标注，域内基准平均提升7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR多模态推理框架存在"感知-推理鸿沟"：模型过度依赖看似相关的视觉token，微小的感知扰动会在推理链中传播并累积为幻觉，缺乏对错误视觉上下文的鲁棒性。

Method: MIRA训练框架：1）在训练中注入经过筛选的欺骗性视觉上下文，构造高难度"hard traps"以压力测试模型对误导信息的鲁棒性；2）引入组内（group-wise）反思触发机制，使模型自主检测并纠正视觉线索与逻辑约束间的不一致，并将该行为内化为内在策略；3）不依赖人工标注。

Result: 在多个推理与感知基准上，域内基准平均提升7%，以视觉为中心的基准上也取得一致性提升；消融实验与分析进一步解释了MIRA有效的内在原因。

Conclusion: 主动的错误恢复能力对鲁棒的多模态推理至关重要，MIRA通过hard traps与反思触发机制有效弥合感知-推理鸿沟。

Abstract: While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has significantly advanced multimodal reasoning, existing frameworks suffer from a critical "perception-reasoning gap." Due to an over-reliance on seemingly relevant visual tokens, even minor perceptual perturbations can propagate through the reasoning process, leading to compounding hallucinations. To address this issue, we propose MIRA, a training framework that improves robustness to erroneous visual contexts while enhancing logical reasoning ability. By injecting filtered, deceptive visual contexts during training, we construct challenging "hard traps" that stress-test the model's robustness to misleading information. Furthermore, we introduce a group-wise reflection-triggering mechanism that enables the model to autonomously detect and rectify inconsistencies between visual cues and logical constraints, which is ultimately internalized as an intrinsic policy behavior. Importantly, MIRA does not rely on human annotations. Experimental results across multiple reasoning and perception benchmarks show that MIRA yields an average 7% improvement on in-domain benchmarks, with consistent improvements on vision-centric benchmarks. These findings highlight that active error recovery is essential for robust multimodal reasoning. Comprehensive ablation studies and analyses further provide insights into why MIRA is effective.

</details>


### [293] [DR Pixel: Page-Native Deep Research with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03097)
*Tiansheng Hu,Yichuan Wang,James V. Talwar,Nicholas Tomlin,Chen Zhao,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个开源端到端多模态深度研究智能体 DR Pixel，以渲染的页面图像作为唯一检索与推理单元，并引入反事实信用重加权训练方法，证明"原生页面"式深度研究优于传统 OCR 抽取文本管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学论文的表格、图、公式蕴含丰富信息，但现有深度研究（DR）智能体通常将论文简化为 OCR 抽取的纯文本，丢失版式与视觉信息。作者由此提问：VLM 能否实现 page-native deep research，即直接在渲染页面图像上检索与推理；并指出用教师轨迹做智能体监督微调时，轨迹噪声大（关键推理动作与冗余/失败尝试混杂），需要细粒度信用分配。

Method: 1) 构建 DR Pixel：首个开源端到端多模态 DR 智能体，以页面图像为唯一检索与推理单元，语料规模 1.06M 页；2) 构建 DRPixelBench 基准，要求对论文中的文本、图、表、公式进行推理；3) 提出 counterfactual credit reweighting（反事实信用重加权），按各推理/动作步骤对最终答案的反事实贡献进行加权，用于智能体 SFT。

Result: 免训练即可生效：page-native DR 在 GPT-5.4、Gemini-3.5-Flash、Qwen3.6-27B 等模型上均优于 OCR 基线，且工具调用更少。训练后：DRPixelBench 检索召回提升约 15 点、最终准确率提升 10 点以上，并在 4 个现有基准上超过未训练基线；分布外 ViDoRe-V3 上距"直接给定金标页面"的 oracle 仅差 1.8 点。

Conclusion: 传统的"先 OCR 再检索"管线不仅非必要，而且次优；直接对视觉文档页面进行检索与推理，可让复杂深度研究更有效、更高效。

Abstract: Complex documents such as scientific papers contain rich information in tables, figures, and equations; however, existing deep research (DR) agents typically reduce papers to OCR-extracted text. We instead ask whether vision-language models (VLMs) can enable page-native deep research: directly retrieving and reasoning over rendered document page images. We introduce DR Pixel, the first open-source end-to-end multimodal DR agent that uses rendered page images as its sole retrieval and reasoning unit, operating over a corpus of 1.06M pages. To evaluate this setting, we construct DRPixelBench, a benchmark on scientific papers requiring reasoning over text, figures, tables, and equations. Surprisingly, page-native DR already works well without training, outperforming OCR-based baselines across state-of-the-art models like GPT-5.4, Gemini-3.5-Flash, and Qwen3.6-27B, while requiring fewer tool calls. We next turn to DR training. A key challenge is that teacher trajectories for agentic supervised finetuning are noisy, mixing critical reasoning and action steps with redundant or failed attempts. To address this, we introduce counterfactual credit reweighting, a fine-grained credit assignment method that weights individual reasoning and action steps by their counterfactual contribution to the final answer. Our training improves retrieval recall by about 15 points and final accuracy by over 10 points on DRPixelBench, and likewise outperforms the untrained baseline on four existing benchmarks. On out-of-distribution ViDoRe-V3, DR Pixel comes within 1.8 points of an oracle that is given the gold pages directly. Our results suggest that the conventional OCR-then-retrieve pipeline is not only unnecessary but suboptimal: directly retrieving and reasoning over visual document pages makes complex DR both more effective and more efficient.

</details>


### [294] [Ray-SCR: Generalizing Scene Coordinate Regression Across Camera Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03098)
*Jaemin Cho,Oleksii Nasypanyi,Francois Rameau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ray-SCR 通过在 SCR 中以标定视线（viewing ray）为条件调制局部特征，并在光线空间建立监督，使单一场景表示可跨针孔、鱼眼与全向相机实现高精度定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景坐标回归（SCR）虽紧凑、精确、快速，但对不同相机模型（全向、强去畸变影像等）泛化差。原因是视场变化、镜头畸变或去畸变重采样会使同一场景内容在不同像素位置呈现不同外观，从而破坏基于像素坐标的特征-坐标映射，降低定位精度。

Method: 提出 Ray-SCR，对现有 SCR 架构的轻量扩展：(1) 以标定视线对局部视觉特征进行条件调制；(2) 用浅层局部 Transformer 对相邻的视线条件特征进行细化；(3) 在光线空间直接构造监督，并以稀疏 SfM 点作为度量锚点，串联跨视角、跨相机模型的观测。三部分共同支持从全景建图序列构建单一场景表示。

Result: 在多种 SCR 架构及针孔、鱼眼、全向相机数据集上评估，光线条件化显著提升跨相机泛化能力，在跨相机场景下取得 SOTA 定位性能。

Conclusion: 将相机几何以视线形式显式注入特征与监督，是缓解 SCR 跨相机模型泛化瓶颈的有效且轻量的途径，使单场景表示可服务异构相机查询。

Abstract: Among visual localization strategies, scene coordinate regression (SCR) has emerged as an attractive approach for its compactness, accuracy, and speed. Despite these advantages, SCR models generalize poorly across substantially different camera models, such as omnidirectional or strongly rectified imagery. This limitation arises because, changes in field of view, lens distortion, or rectification-induced resampling can make the same scene content appear differently depending on its pixel position, degrading localization accuracy. To cope with this limitation, we introduce Ray-SCR, a lightweight extension to existing SCR architectures that conditions local visual features on their calibrated viewing rays and refines neighboring ray-conditioned features with a shallow local Transformer. We further formulate supervision directly in ray space and use sparse SfM points as metric anchors that link observations across views and camera models. Taken together, these components enable a single scene representation built from a panoramic mapping sequence to accurately localize query images captured with very different cameras, including pinhole, fisheye, and omnidirectional cameras.
  We evaluate Ray-SCR across multiple SCR architectures and on datasets with pinhole, fisheye, and omnidirectional cameras. The proposed ray conditioning substantially improves generalization across cameras and leads to state-of-the-art localization performance in these challenging cross-camera settings. The project page is available at https://jaeminch0.github.io/ray-scr-generalizing-scene-coordinate-regression-across-camera-models/.

</details>


### [295] [Multimodal Prompt Learning with Dual-Granularity Contrastive Alignment for Preoperative Prediction of Lung Adenocarcinoma Invasiveness Using 3D CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03109)
*Qing Huang,Xiaotong Tan,Lintong Yao,Wufeng Xue,Ruobing Huang,Dong Ni,Hao Wu,Tian Xiao,Jun Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双粒度图文对齐的多模态提示学习框架，用于3D CT肺腺癌浸润性分类，仅需CT推理即达AUC 0.9499。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术前准确预测肺腺癌浸润性对手术规划与个体化治疗至关重要，但浸润性腺癌（IAD）与浸润前病变（Pre-IAD）的CT表现重叠，鉴别困难。

Method: 采用3D ResNet-CBAM编码器提取全局与空间视觉特征；冻结RadBERT主干，引入分别面向报告级与关键词级的可学习提示进行文本编码，并用真实临床报告衍生的语义表示初始化提示。全局对比对齐将全容积CT表示与基于临床标注影像学属性生成的报告对齐，提供整体语义监督；基于多示例学习的局部对齐模块将空间CT特征与血管相关影像学关键词关联，捕捉与浸润相关的局部影像学特征。联合优化分类与对齐目标，推理仅需CT图像。

Result: 纳入1,593例病理确诊结节；测试集准确率87.92%，F1为87.78%，AUC为0.9499，显著优于相同视觉主干的纯图像Res-CBAM基线（AUC 0.9297，DeLong检验p=0.0078）。消融验证全局与局部对齐的互补贡献及报告提示初始化的增益；可视化显示激活位于结节及邻近血管结构。

Conclusion: 双粒度影像学语义监督可提升基于CT的浸润性预测性能，同时保持仅用CT的推理流程。

Abstract: Accurate preoperative prediction of lung adenocarcinoma invasiveness is important for surgical planning and individualized treatment. However, overlapping CT appearances make it challenging to distinguish invasive adenocarcinoma (IAD) from Pre-IAD lesions. We propose a multimodal prompt-learning framework with dual-granularity image-text alignment for 3D CT-based IAD classification. A 3D ResNet-CBAM encoder extracts global and spatial visual features. A frozen RadBERT backbone is adapted with separate learnable prompts for report-level and keyword-level text encoding. These prompts are initialized using semantic representations derived from real-world clinical reports. Global contrastive alignment connects whole-volume CT representations with reports generated from clinician-annotated radiological attributes to provide overall semantic supervision. A multiple instance learning-based local alignment module associates spatial CT features with vascular-focused radiological keywords, to capture local imaging characteristics potentially associated with tumor invasion. The framework jointly optimizes classification and alignment objectives, while inference requires only CT images. Experiments included 1,593 pathologically confirmed nodules. On the test set, the proposed method achieved an accuracy of 87.92%, F1-score of 87.78%, and AUC of 0.9499. Its AUC significantly exceeded that of the image-only Res-CBAM baseline with the same visual backbone (0.9297; DeLong test, p = 0.0078). Ablation analyses supported the complementary contributions of global and local alignment and the benefit of report-based prompt initialization. Qualitative visualizations showed activation within nodules and adjacent vascular structures. These findings suggest that dual-granularity radiological supervision can enhance CT-based invasiveness prediction while preserving a CT-only inference workflow.

</details>


### [296] [3D Gaussian Splatting within Self-Learned Neural View-Dependent Color Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03114)
*Yixiao Yang,Yu-Shen Liu,Zhizhong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出用神经网络参数化的连续视角相关颜色场约束3DGS，改善深度与几何建模质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散3D高斯产生较差深度、几何细节少且漂浮物多，阻碍平滑辐射场建模；颜色属性独立优化导致几何建模缺乏引导。

Method: 在连续视角相关颜色场（神经网络参数化）中学习辐射场；利用神经网络低频偏置引导几何建模去解释高频颜色变化；施加Lipschitz连续性约束，使每个高斯的颜色变化与其位置变化按比例匹配；引入自学习策略，无需外部知识或先验。

Result: 在常用基准上的数值与视觉比较显示所提思路有效，3DGS的几何恢复保真度更优。

Conclusion: 连续神经颜色场结合Lipschitz约束与自学习策略可显著提升3DGS几何重建质量与泛化性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become a vital tool in novel view synthesis. 3D Gaussian functions with attributes like color, opacity, and scale are stacked together to explicitly model a radiance field. However, discrete 3D Gaussians usually produce poor depth with few geometry details but lots of floaters, which hinders the effective modeling of smooth radiance fields. To address this issue, we propose to learn a radiance field within a continuous view-dependent color field parameterized by neural networks. With continuous constraints in color, the bias on low-frequency signals of neural networks can highly encourage the geometry modeling to contribute to high-frequency color variations on images, getting rid of solely relying on the color attribute itself. Moreover, we impose a constraint to improve the Lipschitz continuity of the neural color field, making large changes of each Gaussian's color also match with large changes of its position in terms of a ratio. To this end, we additionally introduce novel self-learning strategies to improve the learning of the neural view-dependent color field without any external knowledge or priors, aiming for better generalization on unseen scenes. Our numerical and visual comparisons on widely used benchmarks justify our idea and show better ability of high fidelity geometry recovery in 3DGS. Project page: https://github.com/yangyixi21/Neural-Color-Fields-3DGS.

</details>


### [297] [KAUSTics-Net: Diffusion-Based Through-Water UAV Restoration with a Semi-Synthetic Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03120)
*Dario Scilla,Omar A. Lopez,Kasper Johansen,Brian O. Nieuwenhuis,Matthew F. McCabe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出半合成浅水图像数据集KAUSTics及对应的像素空间条件扩散复原框架KAUSTics-Net，并在新数据集与独立数据集上验证其复原性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV浅水成像受折射、焦散及波长相关衰减等水体致畸变影响；同时配对“退化-参考”影像稀缺，限制了监督式复原方法的发展。

Method: 构建KAUSTics半合成数据集：32幅高分辨率UAV影像切出1,280个参考patch，经Blender水面渲染与经验性颜色、模糊增强生成12,800个退化观测。提出KAUSTics-Net，采用像素空间条件扩散框架，集成多尺度条件编码器、FiLM调制、交叉注意力与复合重建目标。

Result: 在KAUSTics上三个训练种子均值：PSNR 31.715±0.439 dB、SSIM 0.849±0.002、LPIPS 0.145±0.007；对比8种复原方法并做架构消融与跨数据集实验。在独立Sea-Undistort数据集上，采样器感知微调在复合水面畸变下达到19.469±0.332 dB PSNR、0.554±0.005 SSIM，但存在感知质量权衡。真实UAV影像上进一步考察了特征保持与分割行为。

Conclusion: 半合成训练结合条件扩散在浅水图像复原上具潜力，且需同时评估重建保真度与下游遥感可用性。

Abstract: Accurate mapping of shallow-water environments using unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) imagery is challenged by water-induced distortions, including refraction, caustics, and wavelength-dependent attenuation. The scarcity of paired distorted and reference imagery further limits the development of supervised restoration methods. To address this challenge, we introduce KAUSTics, a semi-synthetic dataset combining 1,280 reference patches derived from 32 high-resolution UAV images with 12,800 degraded observations generated through Blender-based water-surface rendering and empirical color and blur augmentations. We further propose KAUSTics-Net, a pixel-space conditional diffusion framework integrating a multi-scale conditional encoder, feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM), cross-attention, and a composite reconstruction objective to restore shallow-water imagery. We evaluated the framework against eight image-restoration methods, complemented by architectural ablations and cross-dataset experiments. On KAUSTics, KAUSTics-Net achieved a mean peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) of 31.715 +/- 0.439 dB, structural similarity index (SSIM) of 0.849 +/- 0.002, and learned perceptual image patch similarity (LPIPS) of 0.145 +/- 0.007 across three training seeds. On the independent Sea-Undistort dataset, sampler-aware fine-tuning improved restoration fidelity, reaching 19.469 +/- 0.332 dB PSNR and 0.554 +/- 0.005 SSIM under combined water-surface distortions, although perceptual trade-offs remained. Additional experiments on real UAV imagery examined feature preservation and segmentation behavior. These findings demonstrate the potential of semi-synthetic training and conditional diffusion for shallow-water image restoration while highlighting the importance of evaluating both reconstruction fidelity and downstream remote-sensing utility.

</details>


### [298] [SurgPace: Complexity-Aware Progressive Distillation for Surgical Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03125)
*Zhicheng He,Xiaohan Xing,Hongqiu Wang,Kun Yuan,Guankun Wang,Yanheng Li,Haoxuan Che,Nassir Navab,Hongliang Ren,Long Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SurgPace，一种复杂度感知的连续课程蒸馏框架，用于训练紧凑的手术视觉-语言模型；通过样本复杂度估计与分层表示对齐，稳定教师-学生迁移并动态调整监督重心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在机器人手术中的问答、场景理解与指代辅助潜力强，但计算开销大制约临床部署。直接知识蒸馏不适合手术数据：样本在难度、视觉模糊性与所需临床上下文上差异大，均匀迁移不稳定；且训练中监督角色会变化，早期需更强教师指导完成多模态对齐，后期更依赖任务监督。

Method: 1) 样本复杂度估计：融合预测置信度与 Lorentz 几何一致性得分；2) 连续由易到难课程：用融合分数引导连续的课程训练进程；3) 分层表示对齐：在特征、logit、概率三个层面进行师生对齐；4) 目标级调度：训练焦点由教师模仿逐步转向任务监督，实现样本级课程与目标级调度的解耦。

Result: 在 Surg-396K（由四个公开手术数据集构建、覆盖五种对话范式的基准）上评估，SurgPace 一致优于强 MLLM 蒸馏基线，并在受控基准下实现更快解码、更低内存占用。

Conclusion: SurgPace 通过复杂度感知课程与分层对齐/监督调度的分离设计，缓解手术数据异质性导致的蒸馏不稳定，有效构建紧凑、内存高效的手术 MLLM，具备临床部署可行性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential in robotic surgery for question answering, scene understanding, and grounded assistance, but their high computational cost limits real clinical deployment. Knowledge distillation provides a practical way to build compact surgical MLLMs, yet direct distillation is not well suited to surgical data because surgical samples vary greatly in difficulty, visual ambiguity, and required clinical context, making uniform teacher-student transfer unstable. In addition, the role of supervision changes during training: early stages require stronger teacher guidance for multimodal alignment, while later stages rely more on task supervision for effective optimization. To address these challenges, we propose SurgPace, a complexity-aware continuous curriculum framework for progressive distillation of surgical vision-language models. SurgPace estimates sample complexity using predictive confidence and Lorentz geometric consistency, and uses the fused score to guide a continuous easy-to-hard training process. We further introduce hierarchical representation alignment, which aligns the student with the teacher at the feature, logit, and probability levels, while gradually shifting the training focus from teacher imitation to task supervision. This design separates sample-level curriculum from objective-level scheduling, leading to more stable and effective distillation. We evaluate SurgPace on Surg-396K, a benchmark built from four public surgical datasets and covering five dialogue paradigms. SurgPace consistently outperforms strong MLLM distillation baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness for building compact, memory-efficient surgical MLLMs with faster decoding under controlled benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/hezhicheng2002/SurgPace.

</details>


### [299] [Behavioral Affinity Transduction for Zero-Shot Vision-Language Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03132)
*Junxi Huang,Wenshuo Chen,Xiucheng Wang,Meitong Shen,Shaofeng Liang,Yuehan Kou,Yutao Yue,Nan Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Behavioral Affinity Transduction (BAT)，利用无标签查询批次中图像的类别响应画像构建稀疏图进行转导，为冻结VLM的多种读出方式提供邻居证据，平均精度普遍提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本视觉语言模型独立对每张图像打分，忽略了同一无标签批次中图像间的相互佐证信息；而单纯视觉相似性又可能因姿态、背景而连接不同类别，因此需要一种更可靠的邻居选择依据。

Method: 以每张图像在类别词表上的打分类别响应画像（而非原始视觉特征）度量相似性，据此构建稀疏图实现转导，并在预测时引入邻居证据；对EM-Dirichlet还用于候选类别筛选与分配初始化，可即插即用地接入冻结VLM的直接与迭代读出。

Result: 在多个数据集上，BAT提升了所有测试读出方式的平均精度；主实验（RN50）中，soft与hard EM-Dirichlet分别提升4.22和2.72个百分点。

Conclusion: 类别响应画像是一种比视觉相似性更稳健的邻居构造信号，小规模无标签批次上的转导可有效增强零样本VLM分类，且方法通用、无需训练。

Abstract: An image that is hard to classify on its own may have a helpful neighbor in the same unlabeled batch. Zero-shot vision-language models (VLMs) score images independently and leave this evidence unused. We study small unlabeled query batches with no labeled examples. Visual similarity can reflect pose or background, so it may connect images from different classes. Each image's scores over the class vocabulary offer another way to choose neighbors. We introduce Behavioral Affinity Transduction (BAT), which builds a sparse graph from these class-response profiles. The graph supplies neighbor evidence during prediction. For EM-Dirichlet, it also screens candidate classes and initializes assignments. BAT plugs into direct and iterative readouts of a frozen VLM. Across diverse datasets, BAT improves the mean accuracy of every tested readout. In the main RN50 comparison, soft and hard EM-Dirichlet gain $4.22$ and $2.72$ percentage points.

</details>


### [300] [Znaki: A Russian Fingerspelling Recognition Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03136)
*Petr Surovtsev,Pavel Korotaev,Alexander Nagaev,Alexander Kapitanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Znaki是首个公开的俄罗斯手语(RSL)指语数据集，含37,252段视频(35.4小时)、1,593个唯一序列，由68名母语者众包采集并专家校验，并给出两个指语识别基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指语(逐字母拼写专有名词、术语及未登录词)是任何手语的组成部分；RSL有逾15万母语使用者，但相比美国、英国、德国手语，尚无公开的RSL指语数据集，阻碍了相关识别研究。

Method: 构建众包采集流程：从16个领域类别采样专有名词，经两阶段签名者资格筛选与专家校验，由68名RSL母语者(37名聋人、29名重听者、2名认证译员)录制；覆盖全部33个西里尔字母手形，字母频率分布与俄罗斯国家语料库一致。基线取自ASL指语识别：基于Squeezeformer的编码器-解码器，以及Fingerspelling-PoseNet。

Result: Squeezeformer编码器-解码器取得字符级准确率(1-CER)0.932、序列准确率0.596；Fingerspelling-PoseNet分别为0.886和0.545。

Conclusion: Znaki填补了RSL指语公开数据的空白，具备规模、母语者多样性与语言学代表性(手形与字母频率)，并提供了可复现的识别基线，为后续RSL指语识别研究提供参考基准。

Abstract: Fingerspelling - the sequential signing of letters that compose proper names, technical terms, and out-of-vocabulary words - is an integral component of any sign language. Despite Russian Sign Language (RSL) being used by more than 150,000 native signers, no public fingerspelling dataset for RSL exists, in contrast to American, British, German, and several other sign languages. We introduce Znaki the first open RSL fingerspelling dataset: 37,252 video recordings (35.4 hours) of 1,593 unique sequences - proper names sampled from 16 domain categories - recorded by 68 native RSL signers (37 Deaf, 29 hard-of-hearing, and 2 certified interpreters) through a crowdsourcing pipeline with a two-stage signer qualification procedure and expert validation. The dataset covers all 33 Cyrillic handshapes, and its letter-frequency distribution matches the Russian National Corpus. As reference points for future research, we establish two baselines adapted from American Sign Language fingerspelling recognition: a Squeezeformer-based encoder-decoder, reaching a character-level accuracy ($1-\mathrm{CER}$) of 0.932 and a sequence accuracy of 0.596, and Fingerspelling-PoseNet, reaching 0.886 and 0.545, respectively.

</details>


### [301] [When Optimization Meets Human Intent: Human-Centered Diffusion Model Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03140)
*Renye Yan,Jikang Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述从"人类意图如何进入并影响扩散模型优化"的视角，系统梳理了意图的表达、目标转化、作用于模型/生成、以及输出评估四个环节，指出以固定指标近似多维人类偏好的局限，并讨论个性化图像生成中的对齐权衡与自动指标和真实用户判断之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型正从通用工具走向通过微调与引导适配用户的个性化系统，但其优化主要针对美学等可度量目标，很少检验是否与人类意图一致。人类需求是多维、情境化且动态的，现有方法用固定指标近似，既只能部分捕捉需求，又可能在优化中放大与真实意图的偏差，导致更高分数未必带来更符合人类期望的结果。因此需要理解人类需求如何进入并影响优化过程。

Method: 采用以人为中心的系统性综述方法，沿"意图表达—转化为可优化目标—应用于模型行为或生成—经由输出评估"的链条组织分析，并对个性化图像生成中影响人类意图对齐的权衡进行讨论，比较自动化指标与真实用户判断之间的差距。

Result: 重构了扩散模型优化的研究框架，给出人类意图在优化各环节的传递路径与失配来源的分析，识别出个性化图像生成中的对齐权衡，并揭示自动评估指标与用户实际判断之间存在系统性缺口。

Conclusion: 以人为本的综述视角为扩散优化提供了系统化参考，强调仅追求可度量指标不足以实现与人类意图的对齐，面向人类偏好的个性化生成系统需在目标建模与评估环节更完整地纳入多维、情境化和动态的人类需求。

Abstract: Diffusion models are evolving from general tools into personalized systems that adapt to user needs through fine-tuning and guidance. However, diffusion optimization mainly targets measurable objectives such as aesthetics, rarely examining whether they align with human intent. Human needs are multidimensional, contextual, and dynamic, whereas existing methods approximate them with fixed metrics. Because these metrics capture human needs only partially and may amplify deviations from true intent during optimization, higher scores need not yield results better matching human expectations. To understand how human needs enter and influence optimization, we systematically analyze how human intent is expressed, translated into optimizable objectives, applied to model behavior or generation, and evaluated through outputs. We further discuss trade-offs affecting human-intent alignment in personalized image generation and gaps between automated metrics and real user judgments. This human-centered synthesis reorganizes diffusion optimization and provides a systematic reference for personalized generation systems designed for human preferences.

</details>


### [302] [Visual-to-Executable Procedural Reconstruction of Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03143)
*Zichong Zhu,Zhihao Liu,Andrei Sharf,Zhanglin Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究从野外单张图像重建可执行的程序化建筑模型：通过“全局结构布局树”与“组件参数化”两阶段结构化预测，再确定性编译为CGA程序，实现可编辑、可复用的3D建筑资产生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化建模虽紧凑、可编辑、可复用，但依赖人工编写文法规则；单张图像仅提供部分且视角相关的证据，直接生成底层程序又易脆弱。

Method: 将任务分解为两级结构化预测：(1) Global Structure Layout Tree，用微调布局模型配合渲染引导的视觉—符号反射，恢复立面尺度的split-and-repeat组织；(2) Component Parameterization模式，将代表性立面瓦片映射为结构化程序化属性以合成局部构件；两者确定性编译为可执行CGA程序，并将立面程序挂接到简单体量模型以完成建筑级实例化。

Result: 在规则立面和具有挑战性的野外图像上均有效，保持高结构一致性、程序可执行性与细粒度建筑细节；生成的程序支持可控、可复用的3D建筑资产。

Conclusion: 以“结构化中间表示+确定性编译”替代直接低层程序生成，是图像驱动程序化建筑重建的可行且稳健的范式。

Abstract: Procedural modeling offers compact, editable, and reusable representations for architectural content, but building such models still depends heavily on manually authored grammars. We study image-based procedural building reconstruction from in-the-wild images, with the goal of recovering an executable procedural description that can be instantiated as an editable 3D building asset. Because a single image provides only partial and view-dependent evidence, we focus on recovering the dominant visible facade(s) and attach the recovered facade program to a simple mass model for building-level instantiation. Our method decomposes this task into two structured prediction stages. First, a Global Structure Layout Tree recovers facade-scale split-and-repeat organization through a fine-tuned layout model with render-guided visual-symbolic reflection. Second, a Component Parameterization schema maps representative facade tiles to structured procedural attributes for local architectural component synthesis. These two representations are then deterministically compiled into executable Computer Generated Architecture (CGA) programs, avoiding the brittleness of direct low-level program generation. Experiments show that the proposed framework handles both regular facades and challenging in-the-wild images, while maintaining high structural consistency, procedural executability, and fine-grained architectural detail. The recovered programs further enable controllable and reusable 3D building asset generation.

</details>


### [303] [MoSoLoco: Monocular 3D Human Localization through Visual Latent Features and Social Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03189)
*Yasamin Borhani,Javad Khoramdel,Yang Gao,Po-Chien Luan,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoSoLoco，一种逐帧单目3D人体定位器，复用姿态检测器内部视觉特征与2D骨架，无需额外RGB/深度编码器，并可用稀疏同帧多人上下文精化位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导航与运动预测需要从单目相机估计人的度量3D位置；基于姿态的轻量定位器适配算力受限硬件，但通常丢弃姿态估计器计算的更丰富视觉表征。

Method: 按帧工作的单目3D人体定位器：融合姿态检测器内部视觉特征与检测到的2D骨架，不引入额外RGB或深度编码器；并设计稀疏社会交互模块，引入同一图像中邻近检测以精化个体位置估计。

Result: 在JRDB与NuScenes上，复用姿态检测器表征提升定位精度，JRDB最优误差相比最强关键点基线降低约23%；稀疏交互模块带来进一步精化；受控轨迹预测实验显示下游收益；完整流程可在Jetson AGX Xavier上高效推理。

Conclusion: 复用姿态检测器内部特征可低成本提升单目3D人体定位，稀疏多人上下文提供额外增益，且整体满足边缘设备实时性需求。

Abstract: Estimating the metric 3D position of people from a monocular camera is important for robot navigation and motion prediction. Lightweight pose-based localizers are attractive for robotic hardware with limited computational resources because they operate on compact 2D keypoints. However, they typically discard the richer visual representations computed by the pose estimator. We introduce MoSoLoco, a per-frame monocular 3D human localizer that combines the pose detector's internal visual features with the detected 2D skeleton, without requiring an additional RGB or depth encoder. MoSoLoco can further incorporate sparse same-frame multi-person context to refine individual position estimates. Experiments on JRDB and NuScenes show that reusing pose-detector representations improves localization, reducing the best JRDB error by approximately 23 percent relative to the strongest evaluated keypoint-based baseline. A sparse social-interaction module incorporates nearby detections from the same image for further refinement. Controlled trajectory-forecasting experiments show downstream benefits, while the complete pipeline remains suitable for efficient Jetson AGX Xavier inference.

</details>


### [304] [AnchorComplete: Recovering Complete Scene Meshes from Partial Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03228)
*Weirong Chen,Ismail El-Yamany,Ganlin Zhang,Chuanxia Zheng,Andrea Vedaldi,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnchorComplete 从部分点云重建完整场景网格，在保留观测几何的同时支持不同规模场景；基于 TRELLIS.2 引入部分点条件化与度量尺度由粗到细生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图像的三维重建方法主要恢复可见表面，得到的点云存在大空洞，未观测几何无法补全。

Method: 在 TRELLIS.2 基础上结合两项设计：(1) 部分点条件化，将全局场景 token 与局部空间证据结合，使生成锚定于输入；(2) 度量尺度由粗到细生成，在固定尺寸窗口内逐步细化几何并保持全局结构一致性。

Result: 在 Replica 与 SCRREAM 数据集上，完整场景重建效果持续优于强基线，且在稀疏视角设置下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 该框架可从不完整点云生成完整且保持观测一致性的场景网格，并具备跨场景尺度的适应性与稀疏视角鲁棒性。

Abstract: Most image-based 3D reconstructors primarily recover visible surfaces, producing partial point clouds with large holes and leaving unobserved geometry unresolved. To address this limitation, we introduce AnchorComplete, a framework that reconstructs complete scene meshes from partial point clouds while preserving observed geometry and supporting scenes of varying scales. Building on TRELLIS.2, our method combines partial-point conditioning, which integrates global scene tokens with local spatial evidence to anchor generation to the input, and metric-scale coarse-to-fine generation, which progressively refines scene geometry within fixed-size windows while maintaining global structural consistency. Experiments on Replica and SCRREAM demonstrate that AnchorComplete consistently outperforms strong baselines in complete-scene reconstruction, particularly under sparse-view settings.

</details>


### [305] [CL3DOD: Understanding the Stability-Plasticity Dilemma in Outdoor Class-Incremental 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03229)
*Muhammad Ahmed Ullah Khan,Khalil Ahmed,Didier Stricker,Muhammad Zeshan Afzal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次跨基准（KITTI、nuScenes）统一分析室外类增量3D目标检测，覆盖两任务与多阶段设定，并提出CL3DOD框架以更好平衡稳定性-可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室外3D目标检测的类增量学习（CIL）研究不足，现有工作仅在单一基准上评估（SDCoT++用KITTI、CL2OD用nuScenes且增量设定不同），难以判断持续学习行为能否跨自动驾驶基准泛化，因此需要跨基准统一分析。

Method: 提出CL3DOD框架：（1）用上一阶段检测器的预测恢复旧类缺失监督；（2）通过query-wise知识蒸馏保留旧类响应；（3）采用EMA-based时间一致性正则化。并在KITTI和nuScenes上以两任务和多阶段设定开展受控对比实验。

Result: 两个基准上均呈现一致的稳定性-可塑性困境；CL3DOD较受控增量基线取得更优的稳定性-可塑性平衡，在报告的基准比较中优于已发表方法，且该优势在多增量阶段中持续存在，在保持旧知识的同时有效获取新类。

Conclusion: 室外类增量3D检测存在跨基准一致的稳定性-可塑性困境，所提CL3DOD能有效缓解该问题；作者将公开代码、评估设置与增量任务划分。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning (CIL) for outdoor 3D object detection remains largely underexplored.
