Existing outdoor studies are limited to single-benchmark evaluations, with SDCoT++ considering KITTI and CL2OD considering nuScenes under different incremental settings. This makes it difficult to assess whether observed continual-learning behavior generalizes across autonomous-driving benchmarks. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first unified cross-benchmark analysis of outdoor class-incremental 3D object detection across multiple autonomous-driving benchmarks, covering both two-task and multi-stage settings. We investigate: (1) how severely existing 3D detectors forget previously learned classes, (2) how incremental training exposes the stability-plasticity dilemma between knowledge retention and new-class acquisition, and (3) which forms of supervision are most effective for balancing these objectives. We introduce CL3DOD, a class-incremental 3D detection framework that restores missing supervision for previous classes using predictions from the previous-stage detector, preserves old-class responses through query-wise knowledge distillation, and employs EMA-based temporal consistency regularization. Experiments on KITTI and nuScenes reveal a consistent stability-plasticity dilemma across both benchmarks. CL3DOD generally achieves a more favorable stability-plasticity balance than controlled incremental baselines and improves upon published prior methods in the reported benchmark comparisons. This advantage persists across multiple incremental stages, demonstrating strong retention of previously learned knowledge while maintaining effective new-class acquisition. Our code, evaluation setup, and incremental task splits will be publicly released.

</details>


### [306] [Calibrating Multimodal Test-Time Adaptation under Reliability and Availability Modality Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03232)
*Jiangmeng Li,Jinglin Xu,Yi Li,Yumeng Liu,Jiahuan Zhou,Fanjiang Xu,Hui Xiong,Gang Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自适应概率高斯校准（AdaPGC），将类别条件高斯建模扩展到模态特定与融合表征，用于多模态测试时适应下的概率校准与缺失融合表征恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态测试时适应比单模态更复杂：多表征视角共存且相互作用；且需应对模态可靠性漂移（可用但信息质量下降）与模态可用性漂移（模态完全缺失）。单模态中有效的类别条件高斯建模需更精细的多模态扩展。

Method: 在模态特定表征与多模态融合表征上分别建立并持续更新类别条件高斯模型；用其概率预测校准源模型预测并引导基于可靠性的修正；并利用二者的联合分布恢复不可用的融合表征。

Result: 在多种模态损坏与缺失条件下的大量实验表明，AdaPGC在模态可靠性与可用性漂移下均有效提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 类别条件高斯建模可经AdaPGC自然扩展到多模态TTA，实现校准、可靠性修正与融合表征恢复的统一框架；源码将开源。

Abstract: Multimodal test-time adaptation (TTA) enables pretrained multimodal models to accommodate distribution shifts during inference using only unlabeled test data, without recourse to the original training data. Compared with unimodal adaptation, multimodal adaptation is further complicated by the coexistence and interaction of multiple representation perspectives. This complexity is particularly pronounced under modality reliability shifts, which preserve modality availability but degrade information quality, and modality availability shifts, which render the corresponding modality entirely inaccessible. Category-conditional Gaussian modeling offers a natural starting point for handling such distributional variations, having proven effective in unimodal test-time adaptation for characterizing representation distributions, yet its extension to multimodal settings requires a more elaborate formulation. To this end, we propose Adaptive Probabilistic Gaussian Calibration (AdaPGC), which extends category-conditional Gaussian modeling across modality-specific and multimodal fusion representations for probabilistic calibration under multimodal distribution shifts. AdaPGC continuously updates category-conditional Gaussian models over modality-specific and fusion representations, using the resulting probabilistic predictions to calibrate source model predictions and guide reliability based rectification, while exploiting their joint distributions to recover unavailable fusion representations. Extensive experiments under diverse modality corruption and missingness conditions demonstrate that AdaPGC effectively improves robustness under both modality reliability and availability shifts. Our source code will be released at https://github.com/XuJinglinn/AdaPGC-plus.

</details>


### [307] [HyperFlow: Class-Incremental Semantic Change Detection via Hyperbolic Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03245)
*Manon Béchaz,Léo Buecher,Emanuele Dalsasso,Ciprian Tomoiaga,Patrick Jayet,Marcin Detyniecki,Laetitia Chapel,Lucas Drumetz,Nicolas Courty,Devis Tuia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HyperFlow利用双曲空间中Busemann原型建模语义流异常，将变化检测与类增量语义分割耦合，实现类增量语义变化检测，并发布HiChange数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感语义变化检测方法假定变化类别集合固定，难以适应地理区域或时间推移中变化关注类型的不同；本文研究类增量语义变化检测，即模型逐步学习检测新类型语义变化。

Method: 利用土地覆盖语义的层级结构（新类别为既有类别的细粒度细分），在双曲空间中表示语义类别；提出HyperFlow，基于Busemann原型，将变化建模为由分割诱导的双曲嵌入空间中所学语义流上的异常；将变化检测与类增量语义分割耦合。同时构建HiChange大规模高分辨率遥感数据集，含细粒度层级标签。

Result: 实验表明HyperFlow在各增量步骤上均取得强变化检测与语义分割性能；逐步细化语义类别（而非一次性学习全部类别）可提升变化检测效果。

Conclusion: 层级语义在双曲空间中的表示与Busemann原型语义流异常建模，能有效支撑类增量语义变化检测；增量式细化类别带来性能增益，体现增量学习对语义变化检测的价值，HiChange为相关研究提供基准数据。

Abstract: Semantic change detection in remote sensing aims to identify and characterize land-cover transitions observed in pairs of satellite or aerial images. Existing methods assume a fixed set of change categories, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios where changes of interest may vary, for example across geographies or over time. In this work, we tackle the problem of class-incremental semantic change detection, where a model progressively learns to detect new types of semantic change. To do so, we exploit the hierarchical structure of land-cover semantics, where newly introduced categories correspond to fine-grained refinements of existing ones, and represent said semantic categories in hyperbolic space, which is particularly well suited to represent such hierarchical relationships. To capture semantic changes, we introduce HyperFlow, a method relying on Busemann prototypes, modeling change as an anomaly in a learned semantic flow within the hyperbolic embedding space induced by segmentation. HyperFlow couples change detection to class-incremental semantic segmentation, enabling class-incremental semantic change detection. We also present HiChange, a new large-scale, high resolution remote sensing dataset with detailed, fine-grained hierarchical labels. Experimental results demonstrate that HyperFlow achieves both strong change detection and semantic segmentation performance across incremental steps. In particular, we show that progressively refining semantic categories, rather than considering them all directly, yields improved change detection, highlighting the benefits of incremental learning for semantic change detection.

</details>


### [308] [R$^2$-Omni: Reducing Structural and Precision Redundancy in Omni-LLMs for Long-Form Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03247)
*Bolong Feng,Pengfei Zhou,Zhenglin Wan,Wei Wang,Wangbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 R²-Omni，从结构冗余与精度冗余两个层面压缩 Omni-LLM 的音视频 token 与 KV cache，在长文本生成任务上实现平均 5.0× 显存降低、保留 97.98% 生成质量并获 1.48× 端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Omni-LLM 需处理大量音视频 token，KV cache 显存与计算开销巨大。激进的 token 剪枝在只需输出单个选项的多选题上表现尚可，但在长文本、开放式生成时质量显著下降。作者将其归因于 query 引导的选取机制：它足以保留答题所需的证据，却丢弃了完整生成所需的细节。因此需要减少 token 间的重叠信息（结构冗余）并保持内容多样性；同时保留下来的表示仍存在缓存值数值精度过高的精度冗余。

Method: 免训练框架，包含两个模块：(1) Structure-Aware Token Consolidation (STC)：利用视频的时空相似性与音频的局部连续性来合并冗余表示；(2) Semantic-Sensitivity Guided Precision Allocation (SSPA)：在量化 key 的条件下，按注意力输出误差的估计下降量为保留下来的表示分配 value 精度，并以 query 注意力与音视频对齐作为语义先验。

Result: 在长文本生成基准上，平均显存占用降低 5.0×，保留基线生成质量的 97.98%，端到端加速 1.48×。

Conclusion: 联合降低结构冗余与精度冗余优于单一维度的压缩，可在长文本开放生成场景下以显著更低的显存和时延维持接近基线的质量；该框架无需训练，具有较好的即插即用性。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) typically process large numbers of multimodal tokens from audio-visual inputs, incurring substantial computational costs and a large memory footprint for the key-value (KV) cache. Aggressive token pruning can alleviate this problem while maintaining competitive performance on multiple-choice tasks, which require only a single option as output. However, we observe substantial quality degradation when generating long-form, open-ended responses. We attribute this gap to query-guided selection, which can retain sufficient evidence for answer selection while discarding details needed for comprehensive generation. This motivates reducing overlapping information across tokens, termed structural redundancy, while preserving diverse content. However, the retained representations may still contain precision redundancy due to unnecessarily high numerical precision in their cached values. To address these two levels of redundancy in token representations, we introduce a training-free framework named R$^2$-Omni. First, Structure-Aware Token Consolidation (STC) consolidates redundant representations using spatiotemporal similarity in video and local continuity in audio. Then, Semantic-Sensitivity Guided Precision Allocation (SSPA) allocates value precision to the retained representations according to the estimated reduction in attention-output error under quantized keys, with query attention and audio-visual alignment providing a semantic prior. Extensive experiments on long-form generation benchmarks demonstrate an average 5.0$\times$ reduction in the memory footprint while preserving 97.98% of baseline generation quality and achieving a 1.48$\times$ end-to-end speedup, highlighting the benefits of jointly reducing structural and precision redundancy.

</details>


### [309] [ReVA-HPE: Relative 6DoF Head Pose Estimation with Synthetic Pose Supervision and Personalized Virtual Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03283)
*Vasiliki Vasileiou,Panagiotis P. Filntisis,Petros Maragos,Kostas Daniilidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReVA-HPE：通过单张无标注注册图重建个性化avatar并渲染已知姿态的虚拟anchor库，实现无需anchor真实姿态的6DoF相对头部姿态估计，且仅用合成数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相对头部姿态估计虽以“anchor-query相对几何变化”替代绝对回归，但推理时仍要求已知anchor图像姿态，实用性受限；同时先前工作多局限于旋转而非完整6DoF。

Method: 将相对头部姿态估计扩展至完整6DoF：额外预测深度归一化的相对平移，并与anchor组合恢复相机坐标系下的度量query姿态。为免除对anchor真值姿态的依赖，用单张无标注注册图重建个性化avatar，渲染一组已知姿态的虚拟anchor库；该库通过多anchor共识提供鲁棒旋转假设，真实注册图则通过landmark PnP在平移分支充当真实anchor。另提出高效变体：在注册阶段一次性摊销库的代价，此后每个query只需一次real-to-real相对预测。估计器仅用合成人脸渲染训练，不使用真实姿态监督、伪标签、域适应或评测集微调。

Result: 在真实6DoF基准上，ReVA-HPE在BIWI上取得SOTA旋转精度，并在给定近似正脸注册图的FaMoS上同样达到SOTA；尽管仅依赖合成姿态监督，度量平移仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 虚拟anchor库+真实anchor PnP的组合可消除对anchor真值姿态的需求，实现无真实姿态监���的6DoF相对头部姿态估计；高效变体以有限精度损失换取推理成本大幅降低，验证了合成数据训练在该任务上的可行性。

Abstract: Monocular head pose estimation is usually formulated as absolute regression from a single face crop, yet many applications observe the same person over time and can supply an explicit anchor image. Recently, relative head pose estimation was proposed, which casts the problem as estimation of the relative geometric change between an anchor and a query image. However, existing methods still require the anchor image pose to be known at inference, significantly limiting their practical use. We first extend relative head pose estimation to full 6DoF by additionally predicting a depth-normalized relative translation, which composes with the anchor to recover metric query pose in the camera frame. To eliminate the need for a ground-truth anchor pose, we reconstruct a personalized avatar from a single unlabeled enrollment image and render a bank of virtual anchors with known poses. The bank provides robust rotation hypotheses through multi-anchor consensus, while the real enrollment image serves as the real anchor in the translation branch via landmark PnP. We also introduce an efficient variant that amortizes the bank cost once at enrollment, after which each query requires a single real-to-real relative prediction, with limited accuracy sacrifice. The relative pose estimator is trained exclusively on synthetic face renderings, without real pose supervision, pseudo-labels, domain adaptation, or fine-tuning on the evaluation sets. On real 6DoF benchmarks, ReVA-HPE achieves state-of-the-art rotation accuracy on BIWI and, given a near-frontal enrollment image, on FaMoS, while remaining competitive in metric translation despite relying solely on synthetic pose supervision. Project page and code: https://vasilikivas.github.io/ReVA-HPE/

</details>


### [310] [Mesh-Agnostic Refinement Models for Latent Space Encoding of 3D Human Body Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03285)
*Chaima Zouaouid,Jonathan Cerqueirab,Sylvain Arguillere,Martin Bauer,Nicolas Charon,Mohamed Daoudi,Emmanuel Hartman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无对应关系的参数化人体模型重建精化框架，含Latent-VaRefine、Vertex-VaRefine与ElaOpt三种互补方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMPL等参数化人体模型虽提供紧凑的形状与姿态表示，但重建结果仍受潜变量估计误差与解码器表达能力限制，需在无顶点对应条件下利用初始解码曲面与目标网格间的几何差异进行精化。

Method: 框架包含三种互补途径：(1) Latent-VaRefine 利用变分流形(varifold)距离梯度修正潜码，保留原参数化表示；(2) Vertex-VaRefine 以DiffusionNet为骨干处理多尺度varifold梯度特征，在标准姿态下预测顶点位移，形成近似姿态等变的精化流水线；(3) ElaOpt 在弹性正则与法向约束下直接优化表面形变。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量结果，仅说明三种方法在不依赖顶点对应关系的前提下实现了对初始解码曲面的几何精化。

Conclusion: 通过varifold几何差异驱动，可在潜空间、顶点空间与形变优化三个层面统一精化参数化人体重建，为超越解码器表达上限与潜码误差提供可行方案。

Abstract: Parametric human body models such as the Skinned Multi-Person Linear (SMPL) model provide compact representations of human shape and pose, but their reconstructions remain affected by both errors in estimated latent parameters and limitations in the decoder's expressive capacity. We propose a framework for refining these reconstructions by exploiting the geometric discrepancy between an initial decoded surface and a target mesh, without requiring vertex correspondences between them. The framework comprises three complementary approaches. Latent-VaRefine uses varifold-distance gradients to correct the estimated latent code while preserving the original parametric representation. Vertex-VaRefine processes multiscale varifold-gradient features with a DiffusionNet backbone to predict vertex displacements in a canonical pose, yielding an approximately pose-equivariant refinement pipeline. ElaOpt directly optimizes surface deformations under elastic regularization and normal-direction constraints.

</details>


### [311] [ArabGeoText: Tool-Augmented Vision-Language Models for Arabic Geospatial Text Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03339)
*Yakoub Bazi,Sarah Alsaleh,Mohamad M. Al Rahhal,Mohamed A. Mekhtiche,Mansour Zuair*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出阿拉伯语地理空间文本检测基准ARABGEOTEXT，并构建工具增强的视觉语言框架（YOLO26定位+LoRA微调的Qwen3.5-4B识别），在OSM与遥感叠加双视图、同城与跨城划分上均优于直接微调的端到端方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间文本（路名、区名、地标）具有小尺度、密集排列、任意朝向的特点；阿拉伯语文本识别在文档与自然场景已有大量研究，但阿拉伯语地理空间文本检测（定位+转录）研究严重不足，且缺乏配套基准与系统性评测协议。

Method: 构建ARABGEOTEXT基准：覆盖利雅得与吉达970个地理位置，每种视图类型17,875个人工标注文本实例；每个地点将OSM渲染图与其叠加于对应遥感影像的合成图配对，保持标签内容与像素几何一致，并提供地理分区以支持同城与城市不相交（city-disjoint）的检测、识别与端到端评测。方法上先测试Qwen3.5-4B直接spotting，发现难以覆盖tile内全部标签，遂提出工具增强VLM框架：YOLO26负责定位，裁剪后做矫正，再由LoRA适配的Qwen3.5-4B进行识别。

Result: 直接Qwen3.5-4B spotting在单个tile内难以恢复全部标签；所提框架在两种图像视图与两个测试城市上均优于直接LoRA适配的Qwen3.5-4B；在定位固定的条件下，reader适配相对零样本预训练reader带来识别性能进一步提升。

Conclusion: 显式解耦定位与识别的工具增强范式比端到端VLM更适配密集、小尺度、多朝向的阿拉伯语地理空间文本场景，且reader的领域适配具有独立增益；数据集与代码将开源。

Abstract: Geospatial text spotting involves locating and transcribing geographic labels, such as road names, district names, and landmarks, in raster maps. These labels are often small, densely arranged, and arbitrarily oriented. Although Arabic text recognition has been studied extensively in documents and natural scenes, Arabic geospatial text spotting remains underexplored. We introduce ARABGEOTEXT, an Arabic-centric benchmark covering 970 geographic locations in Riyadh and Jeddah, with 17,875 manually annotated text instances per view type. For each location, we pair an OpenStreetMap (OSM) rendering with a composite created by overlaying that rendering on the corresponding remote sensing (RS) imagery. The paired views preserve label content and pixel geometry, allowing evaluation across different backgrounds. Geographic partitions support in-city and city-disjoint evaluation of detection, recognition, and end-to-end spotting. Experiments with direct Qwen3.5-4B spotting show difficulties in recovering all labels within a tile. We therefore propose a tool-augmented vision-language framework that combines YOLO26 localization and crop rectification with Qwen3.5-4B recognition adapted through low-rank adaptation (LoRA). The framework outperforms direct LoRA-adapted Qwen3.5-4B spotting across both image views and test cities. With localization held fixed, reader adaptation further improves recognition over the pretrained zero-shot reader. The dataset and code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/yakoubbazi/ArabGeoText.

</details>


### [312] [Every Pixel Is an Option: A System One Model for Medical Ultrasound Biometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03360)
*Moein Heidari,Junbo Rao,Yudan Chen,Yasamin Medghalchi,River Jiang,Wenjin Chen,David J. Foran,Jasmine Grewal,Ilker Hacihaliloglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Kev-Bio：用临床测量问题引导的视觉语言模型做超声关键点检测，借用语言模型的决策表示生成每个卡尺端点的查询，与共享稠密超声特征匹配得到空间概率图，从而导出关键点坐标与临床测量值，在九视图FU/GU Biometry 2026数据集上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声生物测量依赖手动放置卡尺，存在操作者间变异；常规方法为不同任务设计专用预测头，缺乏通用性；视觉语言模型虽可通过文本指令实现灵活统一，但直接回归坐标无法显式建模空间似然分布，难以表达定位歧义。

Method: 提出Kev-Bio视觉语言模型，以临床测量问题为指令引导超声关键点检测；受System One决策模型启发，利用语言模型的决策表示生成每个卡尺端点的查询，将其与共享的稠密超声特征匹配以产生空间概率图，再由概率图导出关键点坐标与临床测量；模型以关键点定位损失与临床测量损失端到端联合训练。

Result: 在九视图FU/GU Biometry 2026数据集上，平均径向误差（MRE）为22.50像素，临床测量平均绝对误差（MAE）为15.38，整体性能优于对比基线。

Conclusion: 结果验证了Kev-Bio在不同解剖视图下超声关键点检测与临床测量的有效性；代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: **Purpose:** Ultrasound biometry is widely used across various clinical applications, including prenatal and cardiac examinations, to obtain quantitative measurements by placing calipers at anatomical landmarks. However, these measurements remain subject to interoperator variability as they rely largely on manual caliper placement. Conventional methods often use specialized prediction heads for different ultrasound tasks, whereas vision-language models offer flexibility through text instructions. However, direct coordinate prediction does not explicitly model spatial likelihood distributions, limiting the representation of localization ambiguity.
  **Methods:** We propose Kev-Bio, a vision-language model that uses clinical measurement questions to guide ultrasound landmark detection. Inspired by System One decision models, Kev-Bio uses decision representations of the language model to generate queries for each caliper endpoint. These queries are matched against shared dense ultrasound features to produce spatial probability maps, from which landmark coordinates and clinical measurements are derived. The model is trained end-to-end using both landmark localization and clinical measurement losses.
  **Results:** On the nine-view FU/GU Biometry 2026 dataset, Kev-Bio achieves a mean radial error (MRE) of 22.50 pixels and a clinical measurement mean absolute error (MAE) of 15.38, outperforming the compared baselines in overall performance.
  **Conclusion:** These results demonstrate the effectiveness of Kev-Bio for ultrasound landmark detection and clinical measurement across different anatomical views. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/moeinheidari7829/Kev-Bio.

</details>


### [313] [Fuse To Align: Multimodal Representation Learning for Retrieval via Hypergraph Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03370)
*Anindya Nag,Ambuj Mehrish,Sebastiano Vascon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出训练时超图融合框架（wHyVol、wRHG），在样本内与语义相关样本间连接观测模态以增强检索表示，推理阶段无额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态检索中样本可能缺失、噪声大或信息量不均，仅靠单样本视角难以学到有效检索表示；现有基于volume的目标只在样本内对齐模态，未显式实现跨样本信息交换。

Method: 构建训练期超图，将样本内与语义相关样本间的观测模态相连；HyVol为特例，wHyVol学习视角贡献权重与对称语义亲和度，wRHG进一步通过关系传播建模类型化交互与参与者角色；缺失模态直接省略而非插补，训练后丢弃超图。

Result: 在11个零样本视频-文本与音频-文本检索基准上验证；17个视频基准-模态设置双向共34次比较中，wRHG全部优于GRAM，33/34优于HyperGRAM；T2V R@1均值由63.4升至68.2（GRAM）与68.4（HyperGRAM），ActivityNet上V2T最大提升13.3个百分点；缺失模态下7个基准各缺失率均保持正增益，即使90%片段缺一模态。

Conclusion: 跨样本超图融合可有效利用语义相关样本的互补证据，提升多模态检索鲁棒性，且不增加推理成本。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval must integrate complementary evidence from views that may be missing, noisy, or unequally informative. When a sample provides limited reliable evidence, learning from its views alone can hinder the acquisition of useful retrieval representations. Semantically related training samples may provide complementary observations, yet existing volume-based objectives jointly align modalities within each sample without explicitly enabling information exchange across samples. We therefore propose a training-time hypergraph fusion framework that connects observed modalities both within samples and across semantically related samples, allowing representation learning to draw on evidence beyond an individual sample while leaving the retrieval pipeline unchanged at inference. The framework includes HyVol as a special case and introduces two configurations: wHyVol learns view contribution weights and symmetric semantic affinities, whereas wRHG additionally models typed interactions and participant roles through relational propagation. Missing modalities are omitted rather than imputed, and the hypergraph is discarded after training, incurring no additional inference cost. Experiments on eleven zero-shot video-text and audio-text retrieval benchmarks demonstrate broad improvements with both GRAM and HyperGRAM. Across 17 video benchmark-modality settings evaluated in both retrieval directions, wRHG outperforms GRAM in all 34 comparisons and HyperGRAM in 33 of 34. Mean text-to-video R@1 increases from 63.4 to 68.2 with GRAM and from 64.8 to 68.4 with HyperGRAM, while the largest video-to-text gain reaches 13.3 percentage points on ActivityNet. Under missing-modality evaluation, both weighted configurations retain positive text-to-video gains over GRAM across seven benchmarks at every evaluated missingness rate, even when 90% of clips lack one modality.

</details>


### [314] [EO-Jev: Decision Model for Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03371)
*Mohamed Atef Abouelhadid,Diaa Addeen Abuhani,Muhammad Akhtar Munir,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过23个公开EO基准的30个设置诊断决策模型d3-flash，并用低秩微调将其适配为EO通用模型EO-Jev。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测（EO）工作流需要在已知候选中选答案、估计置信度并处理海量影像；生成式视觉语言模型的自回归解码计算开销大且置信度不可靠，而决策模型单次前向即输出候选答案概率，但其在EO任务上的精度、校准与迁移性尚不清楚。

Method: 对8.4B参数的决策模型d3-flash在23个公开EO基准的30个设置上开展诊断性研究；随后在公开EO训练集上用低秩微调（LoRA）适配d3-flash，得到EO-Jev。

Result: 组合推理迁移良好，24个设置的校准优于Qwen3.5-9B骨干；计数、变化量化、损毁分级、缺失目标识别仍具挑战。EO-Jev在30个设置中26个显著提升，28个中20个达到或超过专家模型，12个留出设置平均提升8.0分，28个中21个校准误差低于专家，通用决策性能仅下降1.8分。

Conclusion: 将通用决策模型适配为有竞争力的EO通用模型可行，但定量感知、选项顺序一致性和分布偏移下的校准仍是持续挑战。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) workflows require selecting among known outcomes, estimating confidence, and processing large volumes of imagery. Generative vision-language models can address these tasks, but autoregressive decoding is computationally costly and their confidence estimates are unreliable. Decision models offer an alternative, returning probabilities over candidate answers in a single forward pass. However, their accuracy, calibration, and transfer across EO tasks remain insufficiently understood. We investigate these properties through a diagnostic study of d3-flash (8.4B parameters) across 30 settings from 23 public EO benchmarks. We find that compositional reasoning transfers well and calibration improves over its Qwen3.5-9B backbone on 24 settings, while counting, change quantification, damage grading, and absent-object recognition remain challenging. To address these limitations, we adapt d3-flash through low-rank fine-tuning on public EO training splits, producing EO-Jev. EO-Jev improves significantly over d3-flash on 26 of 30 settings and matches or exceeds specialist references on 20 of 28. It also achieves an average gain of 8.0 points across 12 held-out settings and lower expected calibration error than specialists on 21 of 28 settings. These improvements come at a modest cost of 1.8 points in general decision performance. Our results demonstrate the potential of adapting general decision models into competitive EO generalists while highlighting persistent challenges in quantitative perception, option-order consistency, and calibration under distribution shift.

</details>


### [315] [ASCoR: Asymmetric Semantic-Spatial Learning for Remote Sensing Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03382)
*Zitong Wang,Shuchang Lyu,Meng Li,Qiang Wang,Guangliang Cheng,Zhenwei Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ASCoR——基于SAM3的非对称语义-空间框架，仅用源域监督实现遥感语义分割的源域域泛化，在9个跨域迁移任务上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源域域泛化（DG）遥感语义分割需在无目标域数据下获得可靠预测。视觉基础模型虽具可迁移知识，但类别识别仍对源域特定视觉线索敏感，而任务特定适配又可能削弱预训练可迁移性。

Method: 以SAM3为骨干构建非对称双分支：(1) 文本条件语义分支，用类名提示提供显式类别引导；(2) Probe-Once动态自适应LoRA，将固定适配预算分配到各编码器块以抑制源域特化；(3) 任务特定稠密分支，使用独立冻结的SAM3图像编码器学习多尺度空间表示；(4) 轻量可靠性路由器，基于局部预测可靠性以有界空间校正融合两分支。全部组件仅用源域监督训练。

Result: 在ISPRS、LoveDA、OpenEarthMap上共9个跨域迁移：6个ISPRS迁移平均mIoU 68.61%，超此前最优2.69个百分点；LoveDA/OpenEarthMap三个迁移均最优，Rural-to-Urban 59.07%、Urban-to-Rural 47.95%、Urban-to-OpenEarthMap 49.38%。消融、特征可视化与空间可靠性分析验证了各组件有效性与互补性。

Conclusion: ASCoR通过语义引导与空间表示的非对称解耦、受限适配预算及可靠性驱动的有界融合，在保持预训练可迁移性的同时提升类别判别，实现遥感DG分割的SOTA性能。

Abstract: Source-only domain generalization (DG) for remote sensing semantic segmentation seeks reliable predictions in unseen domains without target-domain data during training. Although vision foundation models offer transferable knowledge, category recognition remains sensitive to source-specific visual cues, while task-specific adaptation may weaken pretrained transferability. We propose ASCoR, an asymmetric semantic-spatial framework built on SAM3. Its text-conditioned semantic branch uses class-name prompts to provide explicit category guidance. Probe-Once Dynamic Adaptive LoRA distributes a fixed adaptation budget across encoder blocks to limit source-specific specialization. A complementary task-specific dense branch learns multi-scale spatial representations using a separate frozen SAM3 image encoder. A lightweight reliability router integrates the two branches through bounded spatial correction based on local prediction reliability. All components are trained using source-domain supervision only. Experiments on the ISPRS, LoveDA, and OpenEarthMap benchmarks cover nine cross-domain transfers. ASCoR achieves a state-of-the-art mean IoU of 68.61% across six ISPRS transfers, outperforming the previous best method by 2.69 percentage points. It also achieves the best results on all three LoveDA/OpenEarthMap transfers, with 59.07%, 47.95%, and 49.38% mIoU on Rural-to-Urban, Urban-to-Rural, and Urban-to-OpenEarthMap, respectively. Ablation studies, feature visualizations, and spatial reliability analyses support the effectiveness and complementarity of the proposed components. Code: https://github.com/WangZitong42/ASCoR.

</details>


### [316] [Progressive Multi-Scale Super-Resolution via Cascaded Bicubic Anchors and Inter-Scale Mutual Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03390)
*Chun-An Lin,Yu-Chia Chang,Chi-Mao Fan,Yen-Chieh Ouyang,Tsung-Jung Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PMS-SR：一种在低分辨率网格上用Transformer骨干的渐进式单输入多输出超分网络，单次前向即输出x2/x4/x8，两阶段设计（尺度间残差级联 + 跨尺度互相精炼），以7.95M参数在五个基准上取得优于所有对比多尺度方法的PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超分网络针对单一放大倍数训练，获得多个输出分辨率需训练多个独立模型，参数与计算开销重复。作者希望用单一模型、单次前向同时输出多尺度结果，并让各尺度之间互相受益（尤其用高倍率特征反哺低倍率输出）。

Method: 1) Stage 1：在低分辨率网格上使用Transformer骨干，每个尺度预测为"上一尺度预测的bicubic上采样（detach）"的残差；采用Dirac与零初始化，使训练起点精确等于bicubic级联，每个head只补充上一尺度缺失的频带。2) Stage 2：跨尺度互相精炼，通过space-to-depth反馈、可变形对齐（deformable alignment）与逐像素融合实现双向耦合，使x8特征精炼x2输出。

Result: 在DF2K上训练，7.95M参数模型在五个标准基准上x4与x8的PSNR均高于所有对比多尺度方法；x8下全面超过专用x8的DRN-L，在Urban100上领先所有对比方法，其余数据集与最佳结果差距在0.13 dB内。相比三个单尺度SwinIR-M，参数量仅22%、在BSD100输入上快2.9倍，且输出一致性高出6.7–12.4 dB。

Conclusion: 单模型多输出超分在精度上可媲美甚至超过专用单尺度模型，同时显著降低参数与推理成本；尺度间残差级联与双向互相精炼是有效的设计范式。

Abstract: Super-resolution networks are trained for one magnification factor, so several output resolutions require several models. We propose PMS-SR, a progressive single-input multiple-output network that produces x2, x4, and x8 results in one forward pass, with a Transformer backbone on the low-resolution grid. In Stage 1, each scale is predicted as a residual over the detached bicubic upsampling of the previous scale's prediction. With Dirac and zero initialization, training starts exactly from the bicubic cascade, and each head adds only the band the previous scale lacks. In Stage 2, inter-scale mutual refinement couples the scales in both directions through space-to-depth feedback, deformable alignment, and per-pixel fusion, so x8 features refine the x2 output. Trained on DF2K, the 7.95M-parameter model achieves a higher PSNR than every compared multi-scale method at x4 and x8 on all five standard benchmarks. At x8, it outperforms the x8-specific DRN-L on all of them, leads all compared methods on Urban100, and is within 0.13 dB of the best result elsewhere. Against three single-scale SwinIR-M models, PMS-SR uses 22% of the parameters, runs 2.9x faster on BSD100 inputs, and its outputs agree 6.7-12.4 dB more closely. Code and pretrained models will be released at https://github.com/julian135707/PMS-SR upon acceptance.

</details>


### [317] [TaoMate-FVSR:Audio-Guided Facial Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03393)
*Xinyang Li,Zeran Xu,Qijun Gan,Chenwei Zhang,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出音频引导的人脸视频修复框架，在参考引导的视频流模型中联合恢复细节与音画同步，兼顾视觉配音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频中人脸小区域（嘴唇、牙齿）分辨率低；单纯超分虽能提升清晰度，却无法判断恢复的唇齿是否与语音一致，存在音画同步歧义。

Method: 基于参考引导的视频流模型：采用源-噪声混合（source–noise mixing）从部分保留的观测初始化重建；结构化参考注入分离外观锚点与连续运动上下文；训练同时包含退化图像/视频修复与在目标音频下对退化配音源的校正，并引入外观监督与同步监督。

Result: 在说话人头修复基准上，配对感知相似度与同步分数均为对比方法中最佳；消融考察音频条件、重建形式与参考注入；原生AIGC样例显示唇轮廓、牙齿结构及周边人脸纹理的恢复。

Conclusion: 音频引导的条件化可在提升人脸细节的同时保证唇齿与语音一致，并可扩展至音频替换下的视觉配音任务。

Abstract: AI-generated videos often depict people within detailed scenes while leaving their small facial regions poorly resolved. Visual super-resolution can sharpen these regions without resolving whether the recovered lips and teeth agree with the spoken audio. We present an audio-guided facial video restoration framework that addresses this ambiguity within a reference-guided video flow model. Source--noise mixing initializes reconstruction from a partially preserved observation, and structured reference injection separates appearance anchors from continuous motion context. Training combines restoration of degraded images and videos with correction of degraded dubbed sources under target audio. Appearance and synchronization supervision allow the model to recover facial detail and support visual dubbing when the audio is replaced. On talking-head restoration benchmarks, our method achieves the best paired perceptual similarity and synchronization scores among the compared restoration methods. Component comparisons examine audio conditioning, the reconstruction formulation, and reference injection. Native AIGC examples further illustrate the recovery of lip contours, dental structure, and surrounding facial texture in scene-level footage. injection. Native AIGC examples further illustrate the recovery of lip contours, dental structure, and surrounding facial texture in scene-level footage.

</details>


### [318] [TaoMate-HD: Progressive-Resolution Autoregressive Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03394)
*Qijun Gan,Chenwei Zhang,Xinyang Li,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma,Shen Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TaoMate-HD：面向高分辨率音视频流式生成的自回归模型，采用分块两阶段去噪（先低分辨率建结构、再高分辨率精修）与跨块历史传递，结合非对称文本条件与续写训练，显著提速并降低CER，支持小时级生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式数字人需在新文本到达时持续生成高细节音视频，但高分辨率去噪代价高，且文本更新易导致语音重复或中断，亟需在效率与跨文本更新的时间一致性上同时改进。

Method: 按短块自回归生成：每块先用两个去噪步在低分辨率建立粗结构，最后一步精修至高清，音频沿同一轨迹全程生成；各阶段将音视频预测作为历史传给下一块的对应阶段；文本更新时保留历史以推断已说内容；续写训练采用非对称文本条件，为生成与蒸馏设置不同文本窗口。

Result: 相同模型与硬件下相较直接高分辨率生成提速1.6倍；优化的阶段并行部署在串行渐进生成基础上再获1.9倍整体加速；续写训练使字符错误率相对基线下降11.18个百分点；支持小时级音视频生成。

Conclusion: 通过多分辨率分块去噪、跨块历史传递与续写训练，可在保证高分辨率质量与跨文本更新一致性的同时大幅提升流式数字人生成效率，并具备长时生成能力。

Abstract: Streaming digital humans produce detailed video while continuing speech as new text arrives. High-resolution denoising is costly, and text updates can cause repeated or interrupted speech. We present TaoMate-HD, an autoregressive model for high-resolution audio-video streaming. For each short segment, or chunk, two denoising steps establish coarse video structure at low resolution. A final step refines the video to high resolution, with audio generated throughout the same trajectory. Each stage passes its audiovisual predictions to the same stage of the next chunk as history. Across text updates, the model retains this history to infer what has already been spoken. Continuation training utilizes asymmetric text conditioning, with separate text windows for generation and distillation. Experiments show a 1.6x speedup over direct high-resolution generation with the same model and hardware. Optimized stage-parallel deployment provides a further 1.9x overall speedup over serial progressive generation. Our continuation training reduces character error rate by 11.18 percentage points relative to the baseline. The model also supports hour-long audiovisual generation. Our project page is https://taoliveaigc.github.io/TaoMate-HD.

</details>


### [319] [Image Guided Depth Upsampling using Anisotropic Total Generalized Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03399)
*David Ferstl,Christian Reinbacher,Rene Ranftl,Matthias Rüther,Horst Bischof*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于高阶正则化与各向异性扩散张量引导的凸优化深度图上采样方法，用原始-对偶算法实现高效并行计算，并在速度与精度上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kinect、ToF等现代深度相机能提供稠密高质量深度，但横向分辨率受限，因此需要将低分辨率深度图上采样至高分辨率。

Method: 将深度图上采样建模为凸优化问题，采用高阶正则化项；利用由高分辨率强度图计算得到的各向异性扩散张量进行引导；基于原始-对偶（primal-dual）形式推导数值求解算法，实现高效并行化，可达到每秒多帧的运行速度。

Result: 在广泛使用的 Middlebury 2007 数据集上，该方法在速度和精度两方面均明显优于现有最先进方法；同时提出具有高精度真值的新数据集，首次支持使用真实传感器数据对深度上采样方法进行基准评测。

Conclusion: 高阶正则化结合强度图引导的各向异性扩散张量的凸优化框架，配合高效原始-对偶并行算法，是解决深度图上采样问题的有效方案，并辅以真实数据基准数据集推动该领域评测。

Abstract: In this work we present a novel method for the challenging problem of depth image upsampling. Modern depth cameras such as Kinect or Time of Flight cameras deliver dense, high quality depth measurements but are limited in their lateral resolution. To overcome this limitation we formulate a convex optimization problem using higher order regularization for depth image upsampling. In this optimization an anisotropic diffusion tensor, calculated from a high resolution intensity image, is used to guide the upsampling. We derive a numerical algorithm based on a primaldual formulation that is efficiently parallelized and runs at multiple frames per second. We show that this novel upsampling clearly outperforms state of the art approaches in terms of speed and accuracy on the widely used Middlebury 2007 datasets. Furthermore, we introduce novel datasets with highly accurate groundtruth, which, for the first time, enable to benchmark depth upsampling methods using real sensor data.

</details>


### [320] [VidDub: Replacement-Aware Distillation for Two-Step Video Dubbing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03404)
*Yexin Liu,Yunlong Lin,Zehan Wang,Guangxin He,Xinli Xu,Jiajun Zha,Yaofu Liu,Yueze Wang,Bowen Ping,Yuanming Li,Amie Wang,Bernard Ghanem,Bing Li,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出替换感知的跨音频监督与两步扩散编辑器 VidDub，解决视频配音中源口型与目标语音冲突的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频配音编辑模型主要依赖原始配对音频的自重建训练，但当驱动语音被替换时，源口型成为冲突目标，导致唇形无法准确对齐目标语音。

Method: 提出替换感知的跨音频监督：配对音频样本做全帧重建；非配对替换音频样本则屏蔽源口型区域的重建损失，仅通过音视频同步约束监督口型，同时保持未遮蔽的面部与背景。基于离线配音可获完整源视频与目标语音的特点，构建全上下文扩散编辑器 VidDub，先训练替换感知教师模型获得准确唇同步，再用匹配轨迹回归将 CFG 引导轨迹迁移到两步（2 NFE）学生模型，并以分布匹配蒸馏恢复视觉质量。

Result: 在三个训练种子下，屏蔽冲突源口型重建使跨音频 Sync-C 提升 0.833；最终两步模型在四个基准上兼具强唇同步、优越视觉质量、身份保持与视觉语音识别表现。将发布 VidDub-Bench 数据集、固定配对与跨音频评测清单、预处理输出及统一评测代码。

Conclusion: 跨音频替换感知监督可有效消除源口型冲突，配合轨迹回归与分布匹配蒸馏，能在极少采样步数下实现高质量、高同步的视频配音。

Abstract: Video dubbing aims to synthesize lip movements that match target speech while preserving the speaker identity, head pose, and background of a source video. Existing editors are predominantly trained via self-reconstruction under original paired audio, even though the source mouth becomes a conflicting target when driving speech is replaced. We introduce replacement-aware cross-audio supervision: paired-audio samples use full-frame reconstruction, whereas unpaired replacement-audio samples mask out source-mouth reconstruction losses and supervise the mouth solely through audio-visual synchronization while preserving unmasked facial regions and background. Because offline dubbing provides the complete source video and target speech beforehand, we present VidDub, a full-context diffusion editor distilled into two denoising steps (2 NFE). Our replacement-aware teacher first learns accurate lip synchronization without regressing toward conflicting source mouth movements. Matched trajectory regression then transfers the teacher's CFG-guided trajectories to a two-step student, and distribution matching distillation recovers visual quality after step compression. We will release the curated training dataset, VidDub-Bench, fixed paired- and cross-audio evaluation manifests, preprocessing outputs, and unified evaluation code. Across three training seeds, masking out conflicting source-mouth reconstruction improves cross-audio Sync-C by 0.833; the final two-step model combines strong lip synchronization with superior visual quality, identity preservation, and visual speech recognition across four benchmarks.

</details>


### [321] [Circumventing Magnitude Collapse via Modular Two-Stage Open-Set Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03421)
*Laurin van den Bergh,Halil Bisgin,Andres Palechor,Mike Suter,Manuel Günther*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究开集识别（OSR）两阶段流水线中表征学习与后处理方法的交互，发现"幅度坍缩"失效模式，并提出简洁的K+1分类器+幅度感知后处理方法取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有OSR研究将表征学习与后处理割裂研究，二者交互未被探索；同时需考察数据规模与已知未知数据（known unknown data）使用的影响。

Method: 将OSR重新表述为模块化两阶段流水线，系统性地在不同数据集规模及是否使用已知未知数据的条件下，研究表征学习方法与后处理方法的交互效应。

Result: 发现幅度坍缩失效模式：利用已知未知数据的表征学习方法在小规模上表现优异，但在大规模下性能退化并不可逆地破坏判别信息；揭示模块交互何时带来增益、何时因幅度坍缩而放大退化。

Conclusion: 提出简单的K+1类分类器基线结合幅度感知后处理，规避幅度坍缩并达到SOTA；同时证明基于已知未知数据的小规模评测无法预测大规模性能，否定当前OSR研究的最佳实践。

Abstract: Open-set recognition (OSR) requires neural networks to classify known classes while rejecting unknown samples, which is critical for real-world deployment. So far, OSR research studied and developed representation learning and postprocessing methods independently and their interactions remain unexplored. In this paper, we reconsider OSR as a modular two-stage pipeline and present the first systematic study of interactions between representation learning and postprocessing methods across dataset scales and usage of known unknown data. First, we discover a failure mode we term \emph{magnitude collapse}, where representation learning methods that utilize known unknown data can suffer performance degradation at large scale and irreversibly destroy discriminative information, despite excelling at small scale. Second, we study the modular interactions and reveal when they can be leveraged for modular performance gains via two-stage processing and where interaction effects amplify performance degradation due to magnitude collapse. Third, we show how these findings can be used to achieve state-of-the-art performance with a simple $K+1$ class classifier baseline and magnitude-aware postprocessing while circumventing magnitude collapse. Finally, our results demonstrate that small-scale evaluations with known unknown data are not predictive of large-scale performance, invalidating current best practices in OSR research. Code available at https://github.com/TwoDigitsOneNumber/Two-Stage-OSR.

</details>


### [322] [Evaluating Depth-Augmented Visual Features for Cross-Dataset Lunar Traversability Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03430)
*Jan Baturo,Bartosz Ptak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出弱监督月球地形可通行性估计框架，基于稀疏成对标注训练，并系统检验深度派生几何信息（深度、表面法线及组合几何描述子）对视觉特征增强的效果；结果表明朴素几何融合无稳定增益，训练源域选择才是跨域迁移质量的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月面作业依赖自主移动，通信延迟与视线遮挡使连续遥操作不可行。现有深度学习方法多依赖地球模拟环境，与真实月面存在域间差异，且真实月面可通行性标签稀缺，难以全监督训练，因此需要弱监督方案并检验几何信息的实际增益。

Method: 构建基于稀疏成对标注的弱监督可通行性估计框架；将深度派生几何信息（深度图、表面法线、组合几何描述子）与视觉特征融合；在两个地球模拟数据集（域内）与嫦娥PCAM真实月面数据集（跨域）上进行对比评估，并在资源受限的边缘设备上部署评测。

Result: 与预期相反，朴素几何增强在域内与跨域设置下均未相对强视觉基线取得一致提升，效应量小于随机种子间方差；训练源域的选择成为迁移质量的主导因素，采用域相关源训练的模型在真实月面影像上的迁移效果显著优于几何融合带来的任何单模型改进。

Conclusion: 几何信息的简单拼接/融合并非关键，源域相关性对跨域迁移更为决定性；本文提供了受控的强基线，为未来月球感知系统中更具表达力的多模态融合架构设计奠定基础。

Abstract: Autonomous mobility is essential for sustained lunar surface operations, as communication delays and line-of-sight occlusions limit continuous teleoperation. Although recent traversability estimation methods increasingly rely on deep learning, the domain gap between terrestrial analogue environments and real lunar terrain can limit their generalisation, and real lunar traversability labels are scarce, making fully supervised training difficult. To address these challenges, we build a weakly supervised framework trained on sparse pairwise annotations and study whether augmenting visual features with depth-derived geometric information: depth, surface normals, and combined geometric descriptors. We evaluate the models in both in-domain and cross-domain settings using two terrestrial analogue datasets and the real lunar Chang'E PCAM dataset. Contrary to expectations, we find that naive geometric augmentation yields no consistent improvement over a strong visual-only baseline in either setting, with effects small relative to seed-to-seed variance. Instead, the choice of training source emerges as the dominant factor in transfer quality: models trained on domain-relevant sources transfer substantially better to real lunar imagery than the geometric fusion strategy improves any single model. Our evaluation runs onboard a resource-constrained edge device and provides a controlled baseline for designing more expressive multimodal fusion architectures in future lunar perception systems.

</details>


### [323] [Align Once to Explain: Feature Alignment for Scalable B-cosification of Foundational Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03438)
*Raphael Maser,Siddhartha Gairola,Sukrut Rao,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ALOE提出一次性、无标签的特征对齐方法，将基础视觉模型高效转换为内在可解释的B-cos变体，并在分类与稠密任务中保持与教师模型相当的泛化能力，同时给出良好定位的可解释解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础视觉模型性能强但黑箱难解释；从头训练B-cos模型代价高且不易迁移，因此需要一种低成本方式把已有基础模型转为内在可解释模型。

Method: 标签无关的一次性特征对齐：将预训练基础骨干（监督、自监督、视觉-语言范式）对齐到B-cos骨干；对齐后B-cos骨干作为下游任务的可直接替换模块，摊销可解释性成本；所需数据比从头训练少若干数量级。

Result: 分类上显著优于朴素B-cosification（DINOv3 ViT-B/16在ImageNet top-1提升约10.5个百分点）；线性探测、k-NN与零样本迁移在多数据集上与DINOv3、SigLIP2等基础骨干具竞争力；保留适合稠密预测的空间结构化特征，并产生定位良好、高度人类可解释的解释。

Conclusion: ALOE为将基础视觉模型转为内在可解释模型提供了高效、范式通用且数据经济的方案，在保持教师级泛化与下游迁移性能的同时获得可解释性收益。

Abstract: Foundational vision models have become the de facto standard for many vision tasks due to their strong performance. However, they are notoriously opaque and remain hard to interpret. We present ALOE (ALign Once to Explain), a one-time, label-free feature alignment approach that efficiently converts foundational vision models into inherently interpretable B-cos variants. Once aligned, the B-cos backbone is used as a drop-in replacement across several downstream tasks-amortizing the cost of interpretability. ALOE is robust across pre-training paradigms (supervised, self-supervised, vision-language) and can preserve teacher-level generalization with orders of magnitude less data than training from scratch. On classification, it strongly outperforms vanilla B-cosification (e.g., +10.5 p.p. top-1 on ImageNet for DINOv3 ViT-B/16), retains strong linear probing, k-NN, and zero-shot transfer competitive with foundational backbones (DINOv3, SigLIP2) across diverse downstream datasets. It also preserves spatially structured features useful for dense prediction, while yielding well-localized and highly human-interpretable explanations by design.

</details>


### [324] [Intent2CAD: How Semantic-Parametric Supervision Shapes Text-to-CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03449)
*Jinbo Yang,Mingyue Yuan,Shikai Jing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向设计意图的文本到CAD生成任务，将设计意图表示为可恢复的代码级信号（语义特征、可复用参数、轻量参数关系），通过构建IntentCAD-100K与IntentCAD-Edit-1K数据集，并采用两阶段保守提升流水线Intent2CAD，在受控微调下将IR从2.39%降至0.22%、API Recovery达99.96%，并提升零样本意图保持编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到CAD生成仅关注可执行几何，而实际CAD工作流还需生成程序暴露可复用结构以支持后续修改；作者希望在不改变模型架构与解码流程的前提下，研究监督表示的粒度如何影响CAD生成与零样本意图保持编辑。

Method: 定义意图导向的text-to-CAD生成，将设计意图编码为语义特征、可复用参数与轻量参数关系；构建100K配对文本-代码数据集IntentCAD-100K（由低层CAD构造轨迹转换为可执行CadQuery程序）及IntentCAD-Edit-1K评测集；提出Intent2CAD两阶段保守提升流水线：语义提升将可靠图元组改写为特征级操作，参数提升显式化轻量参数关系。

Result: 受控微调下，语义提升主要提升生成可靠性与特征恢复，IR由2.39%降至0.22%，API Recovery达99.96%；参数提升使可复用关系结构显式化，并进一步改善关系感知的零样本编辑性能。

Conclusion: 监督表示的粒度是影响文本到CAD生成质量与意图保持编辑能力的关键因素；通过语义与参数两级提升可在不改动模型架构的前提下同时获得高可靠生成与可复用、可编辑的结构化输出。

Abstract: Text-to-CAD generation aims to make CAD modeling more accessible by generating executable CAD programs from natural-language descriptions. However, practical CAD workflows require more than executable geometry: generated programs should also expose reusable structure that supports later modification. We introduce intent-oriented text-to-CAD generation, where design intent is expressed as recoverable code-level signals: semantic features, reusable parameters, and lightweight parametric relations. Rather than changing the model architecture or decoding procedure, we investigate how supervision representation granularity affects both CAD generation and zero-shot intent-preserving editing. To enable this study, we construct IntentCAD-100K, a 100K paired text--code dataset built by converting low-level CAD construction traces into executable CadQuery programs. We further construct IntentCAD-Edit-1K to evaluate zero-shot intent-preserving editing from the same representation perspective. Intent2CAD uses a two-stage conservative lifting pipeline: semantic lifting rewrites reliable primitive groups into feature-level operations, while parametric lifting exposes lightweight parametric relations. Under controlled fine-tuning, semantic lifting mainly improves generation reliability and feature recovery, reducing IR from 2.39% to 0.22% and achieving 99.96% API Recovery, while parametric lifting makes reusable relation structure explicit and further improves relation-aware zero-shot editing. Datasets and code are available at https://github.com/JinboYang94/Intent2CAD.

</details>


### [325] [Bg-Mix: On Using Image Backgrounds for Data Augmentation in Electron Microscopy Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03455)
*Ankur Srivastava,Joana Sarah Grah,Katharina Ladewig,Nils Körber*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Bg-Mix数据增强方法，将训练图像分解为前景与背景再重组成新图像，缓解EM病毒数据稀缺与标注稀疏问题，目标检测mAP提升+0.8至+3.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子显微镜（EM）是病毒学中可视化病毒颗粒的核心手段，但计算机辅助分析受限于EM病毒数据集规模小、标注稀疏，难以训练现成深度学习模型。作者希望利用样本中内容丰富的多样背景来弥补数据不足。

Method: 提出Bg-Mix：一种基于背景的数据增强方法。将训练图像分解为前景对象与背景两部分，随后将二者重新组合成新的增强图像，从而扩充训练数据多样性。

Result: 在两个数据集、两种架构上测试，相比基线，Bg-Mix使box mAP提升+0.8至+3.3个点，验证其有效性。

Conclusion: Bg-Mix既能提升目标检测模型性能，又能增加训练数据多样性，是应对EM病毒小样本、稀疏标注问题的有效增强策略。

Abstract: Electron microscopy (EM) serves as a cornerstone in virology enabling the visualization of viral particles and is widely used in research, diagnostics and morphological characterization of pathogens. Computer-assisted image analysis can help facilitate and speed up this labor-intensive method, however, typical EM virus datasets are small and sparsely labeled, resulting in a lack of readily available deep learning models due to training limitations. To overcome data shortages and leverage content-rich, diverse sample backgrounds, we employed background-based data augmentation. To this end, we propose Bg-Mix, a form of data augmentation method, which decomposes training images into foreground objects and backgrounds and subsequently rearranges both into novel augmented images. This not only enhances the performance of object detection models but also increases the diversity of the training data. Using Bg-Mix provides an increase between +0.8 and +3.3 box mean average precision (mAP) points over the baseline when tested across two datasets using two architectures, thereby demonstrating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [326] [Contrastive Adapter Training and Consensus Knowledge Distillation for Multi-Source-Free Domain Adaptation in Skin Cancer Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03528)
*Ali Abedi,Q. M. Jonathan Wu,Ning Zhang,Farhad Pourpanah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CAT-CKD，通过冻结 ViT 上的轻量 ConvPass 适配器与监督对比学习构建域不变特征空间，再以多源共识知识蒸馏进行免源数据的无监督域自适应，用于皮肤病变诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑色素瘤与良性痣视觉相似，CNN/ViT 在异构临床数据上性能下降；常规域自适应需访问源数据，受隐私限制不可行；现有 MSFDA 存在全骨干微调导致灾难性遗忘与小样本过拟合、仅在聚合阶段处理域偏移、缺乏共享域不变特征空间、依赖硬伪标签或置信度加权聚合引入噪声监督等问题。

Method: CAT-CKD 含两部分：(1) 对比适配器训练（CAT）：在冻结的 ViT 骨干内训练轻量 ConvPass 适配器，利用监督对比学习（SCL）在源域专属模型训练前建立共享域不变特征空间；(2) 共识知识蒸馏（CKD）：将多源模型 logits 聚合为共识监督信号，用 KL 散度在无标签目标数据上适配学生模型。仅需 4.3M 可训练参数。

Result: 在五个公开皮肤病变数据集上平均 AUROC 达 86.1%，优于现有 MSFDA 方法，且可训练参数仅 4.3M。

Conclusion: 以参数高效适配器+对比式域不变表征+共识蒸馏的组合，可在不访问源数据的约束下有效缓解域偏移与监督噪声，为皮肤病变跨域诊断提供高效方案。

Abstract: Skin cancer diagnosis, particularly differentiating melanoma from benign nevi, is challenging due to the visual similarity between lesions. Although convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and vision transformers (ViTs) have shown promising performance, their effectiveness often deteriorates on data from heterogeneous clinical sources. Conventional domain adaptation methods address domain shift but require access to source data during adaptation, which is often infeasible due to privacy regulations. Multi-source-free unsupervised domain adaptation (MSFDA) addresses this limitation by leveraging multiple labeled source domains to generalize to an unlabeled target domain without requiring access to source data. However, existing MSFDA methods rely on full backbone fine-tuning, leading to catastrophic forgetting and overfitting on small clinical datasets, and address domain shift at the aggregation stage without establishing a shared domain-invariant feature space. Furthermore, their reliance on hard pseudo-labels or confidence-weighted aggregation introduces noisy supervision signals under domain shift. To address these limitations, we propose CAT-CKD, consisting of two components: (1) contrastive adapter training (CAT), which trains lightweight ConvPass adapters within a frozen ViT backbone using supervised contrastive learning (SCL) to establish a shared domain-invariant feature space before source-specific model training, and (2) consensus knowledge distillation (CKD), which aggregates logits from multiple source models into a consensus supervisory signal and adapts a student model on unlabeled target data using KL divergence. Experiments on five publicly available skin lesion datasets show that CAT-CKD achieves an average AUROC of 86.1%, outperforming existing MSFDA methods while requiring only 4.3M trainable parameters. The code is available at https://github.com/A-Abedi/CAT_CKD.

</details>


### [327] [CoLa3D: Composable Latent 3D Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03533)
*Xudong Cai,Yongcai Wang,Deying Li,Chuanxia Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoLa3D is a simple and effective model that decomposes a 3D scene from a single whole-scene latent (from one image or an existing mesh) into a set of consistently positioned object meshes that reassemble into a coherent scene.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing approaches generate each object in canonical space and then glue objects together using fragile pose and scale estimation, which harms scene-level consistency.

Method: A lightweight, flexible, scalable query-based transformer operating in a shared whole-scene latent space; it accepts multiple query types (2D masks, 3D points, learnable queries) and learns to decompose the scene into objects within a compact latent space.

Result: State-of-the-art composable 3D scene decomposition on synthetic and real-world datasets, with substantially improved scene-level consistency and object-level quality, 2x faster inference, and under 1% of SAM 3D's training data; visible depth on par with MoGe2.

Conclusion: Latent-space whole-scene decomposition preserves accurate visible geometry while producing physically complete, decomposable object meshes, offering a superior alternative to canonical-space per-object generation.

Abstract: We introduce CoLa3D, a simple and effective model for composable 3D scene decomposition that, given a single whole-scene latent from either a single image or an existing scene mesh as input, directly outputs a set of object meshes with consistent spatial layout that can be assembled back into a coherent scene. CoLa3D introduces a novel paradigm for 3D scene decomposition within a shared whole-scene latent, unlike alternatives that perform per-object generation in canonical spaces and then glue the objects back together with fragile pose and scale estimation. The network is a lightweight, flexible, and scalable query-based transformer that accepts various types of queries, including 2D masks, 3D points, and learnable queries, and learns to decompose the scene into objects in a compact latent space. On both synthetic and real-world datasets, CoLa3D achieves state-of-the-art performance on composable 3D scene decomposition, substantially improving global scene-level consistency and object-level quality while running 2 times faster and using less than 1% of SAM 3D's training data. Visible depth on par with MoGe2 further confirms that latent decomposition preserves accurate visible scene geometry while producing physically complete, decomposable object meshes.

</details>


### [328] [PlantVLM-Edge: Quantized Vision-Language Models for On-Device Crop Health Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03557)
*Gaurab Wagle,Bishal Adhikari,Jiajia Li,Xiaolei Huang,Dong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PlantVLM-Edge——一个可完全在边缘设备上运行的量化视觉语言框架，用于对话式作物健康诊断，结合农业领域适配、训练后量化与离线on-device服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作物健康诊断需专家知识且难以随时获取；VLM虽在视觉理解与自然语言交互上表现优异，但其计算与内存开销大，难以部署在资源受限设备上。

Method: 框架与骨干网络无关：采用农业领域适配+训练后量化+边缘端服务与离线聊天界面；用两类不同规模/架构的VLM（Qwen3-VL-2B、LLaVA-1.5-7B）、四种量化方案、两个诊断领域（病害与虫害）进行验证，全部部署于NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin。

Result: 8比特量化对答案质量影响极小；4比特AWQ在两个领域均保留98.6%的全精度答案质量，同时将2B骨干权重内存降低49%；最紧凑配置仅需2.01 GiB权重内存，首token延迟约0.12 s，64 token诊断响应耗时0.78 s。

Conclusion: 在边缘设备上实现及时、准确的作物健康诊断是可行的，为下一代作物监测与决策支持系统奠定基础。

Abstract: Timely and accurate crop health diagnosis is essential for sustainable agricultural production, yet accurate diagnosis often requires expert knowledge that is not readily available. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in visual understanding, reasoning, and natural-language interaction, creating new opportunities for intelligent crop health assessment. However, deploying VLMs on resource-constrained devices remains challenging due to their substantial computational and memory requirements. This paper presents PlantVLM-Edge, a quantized vision-language framework for conversational crop health diagnosis that can run entirely on resource-constrained edge devices. PlantVLM-Edge combines agricultural domain adaptation, post-training quantization, and on-device serving with an offline chat interface, allowing growers to ask open-ended questions and receive diagnoses, symptom explanations, and management guidance without cloud connectivity. The framework is backbone-agnostic: we validate it with two different VLM types with varying scale and architecture (Qwen3-VL-2B and LLaVA-1.5-7B), four quantization schemes, and two diagnostic domains (plant diseases and insect pests), and we deploy all variants on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. Eight-bit quantization affects answer quality minimally relative to the full-precision model, and 4-bit activation-aware weight quantization (AWQ) retains 98.6% of full-precision answer quality on both domains while reducing the weight memory of the 2B backbone by 49%. In its most compact form, PlantVLM-Edge requires 2.01 GiB of weight memory, returns its first token in about 0.12 s, and completes a 64-token diagnostic response in 0.78 s. These results demonstrate that timely and accurate crop health diagnosis is practical on edge devices, providing a foundation for next-generation crop monitoring and decision support systems.

</details>


### [329] [3D-BBDA: Backpropagation-Free Black-Box Domain Adaptation for Robust 3D Point Cloud Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03588)
*Shivangi Rai,Kunal Jangid,Vaibhav Kumar,Vinod K Kurmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 3D-BBDA：一种无需反向传播、仅依赖黑盒输出的三维点云域自适应框架，通过伪标签引导的原型对齐与高斯扰动原型先验实现跨域识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维点云分类器在传感器变化、环境差异与采集伪影导致的域偏移下性能下降；现有自适应方法多需访问源数据或源模型内部参数，受隐私、许可与安全限制难以部署。

Method: 提出无反向传播的黑盒域自适应框架 3D-BBDA，仅用黑盒输出将源训练预测器适配到无标注目标数据：1）伪标签引导的原型对齐；2）加权预测模块融合源 logits 与对齐 logits 以降低伪标签噪声敏感性；3）当伪标签覆盖不完整时，由目标预测构建高斯扰动原型先验，作为缺失或不可靠类原型的回退机制以稳定原型记忆。

Result: 在四个损坏三维基准上稳定优于强基线；ModelNet-40C 上平均错误率：PointNet 32.08%、GDANet 29.62%，优于 Source-only（35.88%/34.03%）与 BFTT3D（33.78%/32.36%）。

Conclusion: 3D-BBDA 仅需单次前向、无梯度更新，适合实时、隐私保护的可迁移三维识别部署。

Abstract: Robust 3D point-cloud recognition is critical for robotics, mapping, and urban sensing, yet point-cloud classifiers often suffer performance drops under domain shifts caused by sensor changes, environmental variation, and acquisition artifacts. Many existing adaptation methods are difficult to deploy in practice because they require access to source data or the internal parameters of the source model, which may be restricted by privacy, licensing, or security constraints. We present 3D-BBDA, a backpropagation-free black-box domain adaptation framework that adapts a source-trained predictor to unlabeled target data using only black-box outputs. Our method performs pseudo-label guided prototype-based alignment and employs a weighted prediction module to fuse source logits with alignment logits, reducing sensitivity to noisy pseudo-labels. To stabilize prototype memory when pseudo-label coverage is incomplete, we construct a Gaussian-perturbed prototype prior from unlabeled target predictions and use it as a fallback for missing or unreliable class prototypes during alignment. Experiments on four corrupted 3D benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines. On ModelNet-40C, 3D-BBDA achieves mean errors of 32.08% (PointNet) and 29.62% (GDANet), outperforming Source-only (35.88% / 34.03%) and BFTT3D (33.78% / 32.36%). 3D-BBDA requires only a single forward pass with no gradient updates making it suitable for real-time, privacy-preserving deployment in transferable 3D recognition pipelines. Project page is available at https://visdomlab.github.io/3D_BBDA

</details>


### [330] [Structured Visual Landscape: Generating Preferred Representations in Biological and Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03597)
*Yusong Zhao,Edgar Y. Walker,Hao Wang,Mi Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Vis-Lens 框架，通过激活正则化的结构化视觉表征景观生成能激活指定神经元或脑区的自然图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经元对视觉刺激的响应，既有助于提升黑箱人工神经网络的可解释性，也有助于理解人脑的视觉表征；现有偏好图像生成方法在激活调制能力与图像真实性上存在不足。

Method: 构建结构化的视觉表征景观（structured visual representations landscape）并设计基于激活的先验，用来自脑区或网络单元的激活对其正则化；Vis-Lens 将变分自编码器（VAE）与扩散模型整合为图像生成框架，直接修改经激活正则化的潜码即可生成偏好图像，无需逐图迭代优化。

Result: 在人工神经网络和人脑视觉皮层（fMRI 数据）上验证有效；相较近期 SOTA 的偏好视觉表征生成方法，在激活调制能力和图像真实感上均有提升。

Conclusion: Vis-Lens 以潜码编辑替代逐图优化，为神经元与脑区的视觉偏好表征建模提供了一种高效、真实的生成式工具。

Abstract: Understanding how neurons respond to visual stimulus is an important question in both deep learning and neuroscience. It has significant implications for enhancing the interpretability of black-box artificial neural networks and for understanding the visual representation in the human brain. We propose a structured visual representations landscape and design an activation-based prior that allows us to effectively regularize the landscape with activations from brain regions or neural network units. Our model Vis-Lens integrates a variational autoencoder and a diffusion model into an image generation framework. It enables the generation of natural, realistic preferred images by directly modifying the activation-regularized latent codes without per-image iterative optimization. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework on both artificial neural networks and human visual cortex using functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data. Our framework improves activation modulation and image realism compared with a recent state-of-the-art method for generating preferred visual representations of these neural targets.

</details>


### [331] [Reviso: Not All Visual Evidence Deserves Equal Attention in Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03602)
*Tengfei Liu,Yang Shi,Yuran Wang,Yuqi Tang,Yizhuo Jia,Zhuoran Zhang,Qixun Wang,Shujie Li,Xuanyu Zhu,Xinyu Liu,Minghui Guo,Shixuan Liu,Haotian Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Reviso是一个"智能体式"视频描述框架，先由低帧率全局预览构建Context–Focus–Points分层观察计划，再按计划选择性回看关键片段、调用工具细查并动态更新计划，配合验证引导的轨迹构建与GRPO强化学习训练，在四个视频描述基准上优于开源基线，且生成的描述更利于下游视频问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型生成的视频描述常过度关注次要细节，而忽略关键上下文与核心事件。作者认为视觉证据的重要性取决于视频内容本身，并非所有视觉信息都应获得同等注意力，因此需要让模型学会"看什么"以及"如何收集相关视觉证据"。

Method: 1) 从低帧率全局预览构建分层Context–Focus–Points（CFP）观察计划，明确视频上下文、优先关注的关键方面、具体观察目标及对应工具；2) 依计划进行目标导向的主动感知，选择性重访时间片段并组合工具做细粒度检查，边解释证据边更新计划，融合全局与局部信息生成最终描述；3) 训练数据：验证引导的流水线，通过事实核验与教师引导精修构建高质量训练轨迹；4) 训练算法：GRPO，奖励包括描述质量的关键事实奖励、用于识别重要观察目标的Plan Alignment Reward（PAR），以及依描述质量调整观察成本惩罚的Unproductive Tool-Call Penalty（UTCP）。

Result: 在四个视频描述基准上，Reviso优于所评估的开源基线模型；其生成的描述在支持下游视频问答任务方面表现更好。

Conclusion: 结果表明，优先级引导的主动观察对视频描述任务具有有效性与潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can generate detailed video captions, yet they may overemphasize minor details while overlooking essential context and key events. We argue that not all visual evidence deserves equal attention, as the importance of visual information depends on the content of each video. Motivated by this observation, we propose Reviso, an agentic video captioning framework that learns what to observe and how to gather relevant visual evidence. From a low-frame-rate global preview, Reviso constructs a hierarchical Context--Focus--Points (CFP) observation plan that identifies the video context, prioritizes key aspects, and defines concrete observation targets and corresponding tools. Guided by this plan, Reviso performs goal-directed active perception by selectively revisiting temporal segments and combining tools for fine-grained inspection. It interprets the acquired evidence, updates its plan as needed, and integrates global and local information to generate the final caption. To learn this observation strategy, we develop a verification-guided pipeline that constructs high-quality training trajectories through factual verification and teacher-guided refinement. We further train Reviso using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), incorporating a key-fact reward for caption quality, a Plan Alignment Reward (PAR) for identifying important observation targets, and an Unproductive Tool-Call Penalty (UTCP) that adjusts observation-cost penalties based on caption quality. Experiments across four video captioning benchmarks show that Reviso outperforms the evaluated open-source baselines, while its generated captions better support downstream video question answering. These findings highlight the effectiveness and potential of priority-guided active observation for video captioning.

</details>


### [332] [Isekai3D SDG: A Platform for Diverse Synthetic Gameplay Visual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03626)
*Rafal Karp,Gene Liu,Shih-Hsin Li,Eric Courtemanche,Shengze Wang,Josef Spjut,David Pankratz,Mischa Kolbe,Michael Stengel,Sven Elflein,Adam Grygielski,Liwen Wu,Anjul Patney*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Isekai3D合成数据生成(SDG)平台，基于Unreal Engine插件与可复用资产，采用"世界模拟记录—回放捕获"两阶段流程，使同一段游戏过程可产出多种渲染配置下的视频、图像与深度图，并附带帧对齐的相机、角色和动作元数据，直接以训练友好格式上传。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉与世界模型、神经渲染、游戏智能体等研究需要多样化且带精确标注的合成3D游戏数据；传统采集流程需重复模拟、产生大量中间存储并依赖人工处理，效率低。

Method: 将可复用环境与角色资产同Unreal Engine插件结合的两阶段工作流：第一阶段进行世界模拟，记录世界状态、角色运动、动作及相机轨迹等回放所需信息；第二阶段回放该记录，在指定分辨率、帧率、渲染与相机配置下捕获视频、图像和深度图。模拟与捕获解耦，支持同一记录生成多套输出变体，视觉输出附带帧对齐的相机、角色与动作元数据。

Result: 平台可直接以训练友好格式上传生成数据，减少中间存储、准备时间与人工处理成本；同一段记录可低成本产出多种输出变体，并获得帧级对齐的多模态标注。

Conclusion: 该报告描述了Isekai3D SDG系统整体设计，包括平台资产、插件及其功能，以及它们如何协同生成多样化的合成游戏数据，为视觉、世界模型与神经图形等研究提供高效数据生成基础设施。

Abstract: We present Isekai3D Synthetic Data Generation (SDG), a platform for simulating and capturing synthetic 3D gameplay for research in vision and world models, neural graphics, gameplay agents, and other applications. The platform combines reusable environment and character assets with Unreal Engine plugins in a two-stage workflow. First, world simulation generates gameplay while recording the world state, character movements, actions, and camera tracks needed for replay. Second, the recorded simulation is replayed to capture selected outputs, such as rendered video, images, and depth maps, at a specified resolution and frame rate under selected rendering and camera configurations. Separating simulation from capture allows the same recorded gameplay to produce multiple output variants without repeating the original simulation. Visual outputs are accompanied by frame-aligned camera, character, and action metadata. The platform uploads generated data directly in a training-friendly format, reducing intermediate storage, preparation time, and manual processing. This report describes the design of our overall system, including the platform's assets, plugins, and their functionality, explaining how they work together to generate diverse synthetic gameplay data.

</details>


### [333] [EgoActionBench: Benchmarking Dense Action Captioning for Egocentric Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03646)
*Yujiao Shen,Jiahe Song,Haitian Li,Jingkang Yang,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EgoActionBench 基准，将第一视角连续操作分解为带时间定位、且标注左/右手/双手的原子动作，并配套检索式评测协议；20 个基线模型表现有限，经 SFT+RL 适配的 Qwen3-VL-4B 与 LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B 分别提升 15.9 与 23.2 分并超越全部开源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类数据学习是实现具身智能的可扩展路径，但需要模型从第一视角观测中准确理解细粒度人类动作。现有基准在动作原子性、时间精度与手-动作关联三方面存在不足，无法支撑面向具身智能的密集动作理解评测。

Method: 构建 EgoActionBench：含 1,407 个片段、41,132 条人工精修标注，将连续操作表示为时间局部化的原子动作，并关联左手、右手或双手。设计检索式评测协议：先检索时间上合法的候选，再按内容一致性选出预测，最后综合语义保真度、时间重叠度以及动作精确率与召回率打分，联合刻画"发生了什么、何时发生、由哪只手执行"。在 20 个基线与系统上评测，并对 Qwen3-VL-4B、LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B 两个骨干先做监督微调、再在自动标注训练片段上做强化学习。

Result: EgoAction 方法使两个骨干的 EgoActionScore 分别提升 15.9 与 23.2 分，两个适配后模型均优于所有开源基线；实验显示现有模型在动作原子性、时间精度和手-动作关联上存在明显局限。

Conclusion: 当前通用视频理解能力与具身智能所需的动作理解之间仍存在显著差距；EgoActionBench 提供了细粒度、时间精确且带手别关联的评测手段，验证了 SFT+RL 适配的有效性，并指明后续改进方向。

Abstract: Learning from human data offers a scalable path toward embodied intelligence, yet it requires models to accurately understand fine-grained human actions from egocentric observations. However, existing benchmarks remain limited in action atomicity, temporal precision, and hand-action association. We introduce EgoActionBench, a benchmark that represents continuous human manipulation as temporally localized atomic actions associated with the left hand, right hand, or both hands. EgoActionBench contains 1,407 episodes with 41,132 human-refined action annotations. To evaluate such dense predictions, we propose an evaluation protocol that retrieves temporally eligible candidates, selects a prediction by content agreement, and scores semantic fidelity and temporal overlap alongside action precision and recall, jointly capturing what happens, when it happens, and which hand performs it. We benchmark 20 baseline models and systems on EgoActionBench and further adapt two backbones, Qwen3-VL-4B and LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B, through supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning on automatically annotated training episodes. EgoAction improves EgoActionScore by 15.9 and 23.2 points on the two backbones, and both adapted models outperform all open-source baselines. Our experiments reveal substantial limitations of current models in action atomicity, temporal precision, and hand-action association, highlighting the gap between general video understanding and the action understanding required for embodied intelligence.

</details>
